om brahmasiddhivyākhyā śaṅkhapāṇikṛtā | brahmakāṇḍaḥ yadadhīnaṃ surendrāṇāmapyārambhasamāpanam | gaṇādhipatisaṃjñaṃ tadvastu me'stviṣṭakṛta sadā || prakaraṇārambhe vighnanivṛttaye parāmatra ca pratipādyatayābhimatāṃ devatāṃ stutipuraḥsaraṃ namasyati—ānandamiti | kāryārambhe P. 1-1. namaskārakriyāyāmavigītasadācāraḥ pramāṇam | na ca jaimininā namaskāro na kṛta iti vigānamāśaṅkyam ; na hyakaraṇaṃ vigānam, api tu nindāvādaḥ | tathā holākādīnāṃ niyataprācyādikartṛkāṇāmapi prāmāṇyamiti sthitam | sa cātra nopalabhyate | kṛta eva vā jaiminināpyanibaddhayā vācā manasā ca namaskāra iti viśiṣṭatvādanumirmīmahe | sa ca kāryārambhe tatsamāptiphalabhisaṃdhānenaiva śiṣṭairācaryate ; tena viśiṣṭaphalāyā eva smṛtestatphalaiva śrutistataḥ kalpyā ; ato na viśvajijñyāyena svargaphalaprasaṅgaḥ | namaskārācca dharmaviśeṣaḥ, tato vighnahetoradharmasya kṣayaḥ, tato hetvabhāvādvighnānutpadyau prāripsitakāryasamāptirityevaṃ kāryasamāptyarthatā namaskārasya | stutipūrvakaśca namaskāro vīryavattaraḥ ; atastathaiva kṛta iti | tatra brahmajñānāya prakaraṇamidamārambhaṇīyam ; tacca vedāntebhya eva siddhyati ; ataḥ kimanenetyāśaṅkya 'vedānteṣu' ityādinā 'ārambhyate' ityantena śrotṛjanapravṛttyarthaṃ prayojanamāha | vādivipratipattisamutthāprāmāṇyādisaṃśayatirohitaśaktayo vedāntā na brahmajñānaṃ kartumīśate ; anena tu prakaraṇena samastasaṃśayanirāse kṛte satyāvirbhūtaśaktayasta eva brahma jñāpayanti ; tataśca karmavidhīnāmiva dharmamīmāṃsā vedāntānāmapi brahmajñānaṃ kurvatāṃ vipratipattinirākaraṇadvāreṇedaṃ prakaraṇamaṅgam ; yacca pradhānasya phalaṃ tadevāṅgasya, darśapūrṇamāsa-
[p. 2]
phalamiva prayājādeḥ ; ato brahmajñānamasya prayojanam | tathā sati brahma viṣayaḥ, tena copāyopeyalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandha ityapyarthāt siddham; tataśca viṣayaprayojanasaṃbandhaiḥ saṃpannamidaṃ prakaraṇamārabdhavyāmetyabhiprāyaḥ | nanvatrāpi brahmamīmāṃsāśāstramaṅgamastyeva ; ataḥ kimanena punaruktaprāyeṇa prakaraṇena ? satya masti; kiṃtu tatra nānāpradeśe saṃkṣepataścārthasiddhaṃ ca siddhavacca yaduktaṃ tadatraikatra pradeśe vistareṇa sākṣāt sopapattikaṃ kathyata iti punarārambhaḥ | P. 1-3. tatrādyāṃ vipratipattiṃ tāvadāha—ke ciditi | kuta ityāha—ātmanaśca | vijñānātmā vā vedāntaiḥ pratipādyaḥ, paramātmā vā ; tatra vijñānātmanastāvadahaṃ pratyayasiddhatve sati teṣāṃ vedāntānāmanuvādakatvādabhāmāṇyam ; svaprakāśatve'pyātmanaḥ svataḥ siddhatvādanuvādakatvaṃ vedāntānāmaviśiṣṭam ; pramāṇāntarasiddhatvaṃ tu paramatopanyāsaprakramāduktam | paramātmanastu pramāṇāntareṇāsiddhatve saṃbandhinyagṛhīte saṃbandhagrahaṇānupapattestena saha tacchabdasya vācyavācakalakṣaṇasaṃbandhāgrahādapadārthatve satyavākyaviṣayatm ; yataḥ padārthā eva vākyārtha iti matam | tataśca vedāntānāmanavabodhakatvādevāprāmāṇyamityarthaḥ | athāhaṃ pratyayasiddhamapyātmānaṃ nirastasamastaviśeṣamavagamayatām “asthūlamanuvahastam” ityādivākyānāmananuvādakatvāt sthūlādipadānāṃ nañaśca brahmādipadānāṃ ca bṛhattvādyarthānvayena yathāyathaṃ svārthe saṃbandhagrahaṇasaṃbhavāt padārthatve sati vākyaviṣayatvaṃ ceducyate, tathāpi teṣāṃ pravṛttinivṛttyupadeśe'satyapuruṣārthatvādaprāmāṇyam | nanu paricchedalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyam, na tu puruṣārthopayogitvalakṣaṇam ; puruṣārthopayogināmapi ghaṭādīnāmaprāmāṇyādapuruṣārthānāmapyanapekṣitavastujñānānāṃ prāmāṇyācca ; asti paricchedakatvaṃ vedāntānāmiti kathamaprāmāṇyam ? ucyate—nānapekṣitavastujñānatulyamupaniṣadāṃ paricchedamātreṇa prāmāṇyam, kiṃ tu puruṣārthopayogitvena | na ca tulyam, sāṃpradāyikatvādadhyayanavidhiviṣayatvācca ; na hi vidhiḥ puruṣārthonupayogini puruṣaṃ prerayati ; puruṣārthopāyaṃ ca bodhayataḥ pramāṇasya puruṣārthopayogitā ; vyavahāraśca puruṣārthopāyaḥ ; sa ca pravṛttinivṛttyātmā ; te cennopadiśanni vedāntāḥ, naiṣāṃ yathāprāptaṃ prāmāṇyamastītyaprāmāṇyābhidhānam | kimarthaṃ tarhi tānadhyayanavidhiradhyāpayati ? japārthamiti brūmaḥ | nanvāpannaṃ tarhi
[p. 3]
teṣāṃ puruṣārthopayogitvam; bāḍhaṃ prameyatayā, na tu pramāṇatayā; pramāṇatayā ca puruṣārthopayogitvābhāvo'prāmāṇyopayogādiha pratipādyaḥ, na sarvathā | nanvadhyayanavidhirarthāvabodhārthaḥ, na japārthaḥ; satyam; pūrvoktayuktyā tu tadasaṃbhavāt vedānteṣu japārtho bhaviṣyati | nanvekasya vidheḥ dvyarthatvamayuktam; ayuktamapi vikalpavadagatyāśrayiṣyate | tasmādyathā vidhyarthavādamantrāṇāmadhyayanavidhiviṣayatvāviśeṣe'pi 'yacchakvanuyāt' ityupabandhādvidhistutismṛtayo yathāsaṃbhavamupayogaviśeṣāstathā vedāntānāṃ japa ityadoṣaḥ || anye tu kārye'rthe vedasya prāmāṇyaṃ manyamānāḥ 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' ityukte 'kathaṃbhūtaḥ' ityapekṣāyām 'itthaṃbhūtaḥ' ityapekṣitapratipattavyātmasvarūpasamarpaṇādupaniṣadāṃ kāryānupraveśāt prāmāṇyaṃ manyante; tathā sati ca viśiṣṭavidhyanupapattyā viśeṣaṇabhūtamātmasvarūpamapyaupaniṣadaṃ satsyati dravyadevatāviśiṣṭayāgavidhāviva dravyadevatam, apuruṣārthatvamadhyayanavidhivyarthatvaṃ ca na bhaviṣyati—iti vadantaḥ | teṣāṃ tu vyājamātramidam; paramārthatastu nopaniṣadāmātmasvarūpe prāmāṇyaṃ tadā sidhyati, atasparatvādupaniṣadām; 'evaṃbhūta ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti tadā vacanaṃ vyajyate, na 'evaṃbhūta ātmā' iti | nanu kathamanevaṃbhūta evaṃbhūtaḥ pratyetuṃ śakyate? na, adhyāropeṇāpi "vācaṃ dhenumupāsīta" itivadupapatteḥ | ata eva viśiṣṭavidherapi nātmasvarūpasiddhiḥ; dravyadevatamantareṇa tu yāgo na sidhyatīti yuktaṃ tadākṣepa iti vaiṣamyam | pratipattividhyasaṃbhavāccāprāmāṇyam | etacca vadante | tadāha—anye tviti | vipratipattyantaraṃ cāha | anye tviti | "eka P. 1-6. evāyam" "advitīyaḥ" ityādivacanāṃ mukhyārthaparigrahe sādhyasādhaneti kartavyatādibhedaviṣayatvena karmavidhivirodhāt nīlādibhedaviṣayatvena pratyakṣādivirodhācca deśakālajātyādyabhedena 'eko'dvitīyaḥ' ityupacāreṇocyata ityanye manyanta ityarthaḥ | upalakṣaṇārthāśraitā vipratipattaya uktāḥ; itarā api vakṣyamāṇanirākaraṇāt draṣṭavyāḥ | tadevaṃ vipratipattiṣu tāsāṃ nirāsāyedamārabdhamityāha—tannirāsāyeti kiṃ tadityāha—ānandamitīti | ānandamiti pratīkena prakaraṇamidaṃ nirdiśati; prakaraṇamityarthaḥ | tatrādyaślokasya tātparyamāha—parāmiti | yadyapyadvaitināṃ nirguṇaṃ brahma; ānandā- 1A
[p. 4]
dayaśca tasya svarūpameva, na guṇāḥ; tathāpi stutyastotṛbhedavadavidyāsiddhaguṇaguṇibhedamāśritya guṇasaṃkīrtanātmikā stutiḥ kṛtā | atastayā stutyā, kāyavāñcanasāṃ namratāsvarūpayā ca praṇatyā, dehendriyamanobuddhibhyaḥ parāṃ tairasaṃspṛṣṭasvarūpāṃ devatāṃ dyotamānāṃ svataḥ sphurantīṃ pūjayati; “namasaḥ pūjāyām” iti kyacaḥ smaraṇāt stutipūrvayā praṇatyetyuktaṃ bhavati | kāyavāṅmanaḥprahṛtālakṣaṇayeti ca praṇatimātrasyedaṃ lakṣaṇam | iha tu praṇativiśeṣa eva vāciko'bhipreta iti draṣṭavyam | kāyamanaḥpūrvaka eva vāyaṃ vāciko namaskāra iti granthakṛdvacanādevāvasyāmaḥ | tatrānandamityādinā stutirnama syāma iti praṇatiḥ kṛteti | namaskārārthaśredādyaślokaḥ tato namaskārapūrvatvātprakaraṇārambhasya tato bahiriti kathaṃ prakaraṇapratīkatvenānandamityucyata P. 1-9. ityāśaṅkyāha—prakaraṇārthamiti | ya evārthastutyarthaṃ uktā guṇāsta eva prakaraṇena pratipādyā ityarthāt prakaraṇārthamapi saṃkṣepataḥ sūcayati; ataḥ prakaraṇāntargatatvādyuktamasya prakaraṇapratīkatvamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu ‘vipratipattinirāsāyedaṃ prakaraṇam’ ityuktam; atasvannirāsopapattirevāsya pratipādyatvādviṣayaḥ, nānandādirityāśaṅkyāha—vipratipattīti | vipratipattinirāsopapatyabhidhānaṃ nādaṣṭārtham, kiṃ tvānandādirūpe brahmaṇi vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyapratipādanārtham; tadapyānandādirūpaṃ brahmatattvamiti pratipādayitum; ato bhavatyānandādirūpaṃ brahmāpi pratipādyatvādviṣaya ityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ vipratipattinirāsopapattirviṣayaḥ, kiṃ tvānandādirapītyapiśabdasyārthaḥ | idānīṃ prāthamyādānandādirūpaṃ vyākhyātukāmastatpūrvapakṣaṃ tāvat karoti— atreti | āhurityadhyāhāryam; manyanta iti vā vyavahitamapyapekṣayā'nuṣajanīyam | tataḥ kimityāha—rāgeti | itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ | rāganibandhanāyāśca pravṛtteḥ saṃsārahetutva “kāmātmatā na praśastā” “vicchenamiha vairiṇam” “yadā sarve pramucyante kāmā ye'sya hṛdi sthitāḥ | atha matyo'mṛto bhavati” ityādiśrutismṛtibhyo'vagatam; anumānācca; tathāhi— mumukṣo rāganibandhanā pravṛttirdharmiṇī, saṃsāravaijamiti sādhyo dharmaḥ, rāganibandhanapravṛttitvāt strībhogādipravṛttivaditi | kiṃ ca rāgato mumukṣupravṛttau yathācodito'dhikāryapi na labhyata ityāha—śāntasya nigṛhītāntaḥkaraṇasya, dāntasya nigṛhītabahiṣkaraṇasya, ātmani viṣaye darśanam “taramādeva-
[p. 5]
vicchānto dānta uparatastitikṣuḥ samāhito bhūtvā ātmanyevātmānaṃ paśyet” iti śrutyocyate | na cānandarāgāt pravartamānaḥ śānto bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu śāntatvaṃ cedanupapannaṃ pūrvaṃ dāntopanyāso'pi vyarthaḥ | na vyarthaḥ ; sa hi śāntatvasya śrautatvajñāpanārthaḥ ; anekapadopādāne hi śrutirbuddhau saṃnidhīyate ; ataḥ śāntatvasya śrautatvajñānaṃ bhavati | yadvā nigṛhītāntaḥkaraṇaścenna bhavati, tatastanmūlatvāt bahiṣkaraṇapravṛtterdānto'pi na syādeva ; śāntapraharṇaṃ tūpalakṣaṇārthaṃ mantavyam | nanu “vijñānamānandaṃ brahma” iti śrutasyānandasya prayojanavaśādutsargo na yuktaḥ ; na hi prayojanavaśālamāṇavyavasthocitā | bāḍham ; na prayojanavaśādānandaśrutimutsṛjāmaḥ ; kiṃ tu muktiśrutyantarabalādeva tāmanyathā nayāmaḥ ; na cātyantamutsṛjāma ityadoṣaḥ | nanvanānandātmake brahmaṇyapuruṣārthatvāt pravṛttirna syāt ; pravṛttau cāsya na necchāsti, anicchoḥ pravṛttyanupapatteḥ ; saiva ca rāgaḥ ; ataḥ kathaṃ śāntatā tavāpītyāśaṅkya paraḥ svapakṣe pravṛttimadhikāriṇaṃ ca śāntaṃ darśayati— tasmāditi ; yasmādānandarāgātpravṛttāvevaṃ doṣastasmādityarthaḥ | P. 1-16. sakalapādhyātmikādibhedabhinnaṃ duḥkhajātamatikrānte brahmattve tebhyaṃ eva duḥkhebhyaḥ udvignaḥ sukhebhyaśca hairaṇyagarbhebhyaḥ kṣayādidoṣaparibhāvanayā vigataspṛhaḥ pravartamāno mucyata ityarthaḥ | na hi duḥkhanivṛttīcchā rāgaḥ, kiṃ tu sukhecchā ; duḥkhanivṛttyarthamapi loke pravṛttirdṛṣṭā ; ataḥ pravṛttiradhikārī cāsmatpakṣe ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadi nānandātmakaṃ brahma kā tarhi “vijñānamānandam” ityādiśrutīnāṃ gatirityāśaṅkyāha—ānandeti | sakalasyādhyātmikādibhedabhinnasya trividhasyātikramamabhāvamāhuḥ gamayanti ; lakṣaṇayetyarthaḥ | sukhaṃ hi duḥkhābhāvāvinābhūtam ; yatnāpi yugapat sukhaduḥkhe tatrāpi sukhadvārajaduḥkhābhāvenāvinābhūtam | anupapannaṃ ca brahmaṇi pūrvoktayuktyā | tasmādānandaśrutiṣu lakṣaṇaiva nyāyyā | ke cittu sukhe duḥkhābhāve ca sukhaśabdasya prayogādakṣādiśabdavadanekārthatvamicchanti ; tadayuktam, ekārthatvenaivetaratra lakṣaṇayā prayogopapatterabhidhānaśaktikalpanānupapatteḥ | nirūḍhā caiṣā lakṣaṇā, nāsmatkalpitetyabhiprāyeṇāha—dṛṣṭo hīni | sukhaśabdasya duḥkhanivṛttau prayoge siddhe tatparyāyasyānandasyāpi tatra prayogaḥ siddho bhavatītyabhiprāyeṇa
[p. 6]
sukhaśabdaprayogo darśitaḥ | sukhasya vā vācakaḥ śabdaḥ sukhaśabdaḥ, P. 1-17. ānandaśabda eva draṣṭavyaḥ | matāntaramāha—saiveti ; duḥkhaniścirityarthaḥ ; manyanta ityadhyāhāryam | dṛṣṭo hi śuddhaḥ khādinivṛttau ‘sukhyaham’ iti sukhaśabdaḥ ; na ca tasya sukhyārthatvasaṃbhave gauṇatvamucitam ; ubhayasiddhā ca duḥkhaniścitaḥ, bhāvātmakasukhavādināpi tatra tatriścatteriṣṭatvāt ; ataḥ saiva sukhamastu, kiṃ dharmāntarakalpayā ? iti bhāvaḥ | nanvevamupalādā vapi duḥkhaniścatteraviśeṣāt sukhitvaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti ; sukhitvasyetyadhyāhāryam | kuta ityāha—pratyagiti | pratigato vyāvṛtta idamaṃśebhyo'cceti ceti pratyak anidaṃ cidātmā, tatra śrutiḥ yasyā duḥkhaniścetteḥ sā pratyagśrutiriti vaiyadhikaraṇye'pi gamakatvātsamāsaḥ | aśmādau ca jīvātmā nāstīti bhāvaḥ | mūrcchitādau pratyagśrutirapi duḥkhanivṛttiranupalabhyamānā sukhaṃ mā prasāṅkṣīdityupalabhyamānagrahaṇam | ‘idam’ iti buddhiścatteḥ pratigatā ‘aham’ iti buddhiścatteḥ pratyagvṛttiḥ, tata upalabhyamānāyā duḥkhaniścetteḥ sukhatvam ; na cāśmādeḥ ‘aham’ iti buddhirastīti vārthaḥ | nanu bhāvarūpaṃ sukhaṃ prasiddham ; kathaṃ niścitaḥ sukhabhityāśaṅkya pakṣāntaramāha—taditi | duḥkhaniścittiviśiṣṭasyātmana upalabdherbhāvarūpāyā eva vā sukhatvānnāśmādau prasaṅgaḥ ; tena duḥkhaniścirvā tadviśiṣṭātmopalabdhirvā sukhamastu ; sarvathā sukhaṃ nāma na dharmāntaramastīti bhāvaḥ | evamānandātmakatvaṃ brahmaṇa ākṣipya samādhatte—tāniti | tatra duḥkhābhāvasukhapakṣe pūrvamukte brahmaṇyānandaśabdo sukhya eva syāt ; tataśca gauṇatāpakṣo nāvatarediti | yadyapi pūrvapakṣe gauṇapakṣamupanyasya duḥkhābhāvasukhapakṣa uktaḥ, tathāpi bahuvaktavyatvānsukhyasiddhau ca gauṇatāyāḥ sunirākāryatvānsukhyapūrvakatvācca gauṇacintāyā duḥkhaniścittasukhapakṣameva tāvannirasyati—na tāvaditi | duḥkhābhāve hi sukhe naikasya bhāvābhāvāvekatra yugapat syātām ; bhāvarūpe tu tasmin dharmādharmayoriva viruddhayorapyekatra yaugapadyamupapadyate | dharmādharmayoścaikatrātmani sthitāviva janmanyapi yaugapadyamastyeva ; yatraikasmāt vyāpārādekasya nigraho'nyasya cānugrahaḥ | saṃtāpavataśca śītahṛde nimamānimagnārdhakāgradvayadvāre sukhaduḥkhe yugapaddṛśyete | ato na duḥkhābhāvaḥ sukhamityarthaḥ |
[p. 7]
na ca śabdakulībhakṣaṇe gandharasādijñānavadayaugapadye'pi nairantaryādyaugapadyaama iti vācyam; tatra rasādigrāhiṇāmindriyāṇāṃ bhinnatvādgutareṇa manasā yugapadadhiṣṭhānāśakteryuktamayaugapadyam; iha tu kṛtsnadehamabhivyāpyāvasthitena tvagindriyāvayavinaikena toyenārdhasaṃyoginā sakramanaḥsaṃyogādhiṣṭhitena cittena sitāsitajñānavadekaṃ śītoṣṇagrāhi jñānamupajanyate; tena ca sukhaduḥkhe yugapajjanyate; te ca manaḥsaṃyogāviśeṣādyugapadekatva jñāne pratibhātaḥ| tasmāt sūktam—yugapat sukhaduḥkhayordarśanāditi | atrāśaṅkayati— atheti | atha satyapi śītahṛde'nimagnārdhakāyadvāre duḥkham, itarasya P. 1-20. nimamārdhakāyadvārasya duḥkhasyābhāvāt sukhitvamityucyata ityarthaḥ | evamāśaṅkaya dūṣayati—kumbhīti | kumbhīpāke narake pacyamānasya puṃso rauravādinarakvāntaraduḥkhasya bhuktasya pradhvaṃsābhāvādabhuktasya ca prāgabhāvāt sukhitvaprasaṅgaḥ syāt | na ca tadiṣṭam; narake hi sukhāsaṃbhinnamaikāntikaduḥkham | tadidamalokamāgamamātrasujñeyamiti laukike viṣaye'tiprasaṅgadoṣamāha—eketi | ekendriyadvāraṃ duḥkhaṃ yasya puṃsaḥ sa tathoktaḥ | tasyendriyāntaradvāraduḥkhābhāvāt; sukhitvaprasaṅga ityanuṣajyate | hetvantaramāha—itaśceti | itaśca na duḥkhābhāvaḥ sukhamityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—aduḥkhasyeti | nāsti duḥkhaṃ yasya puṃsaḥ so'duḥkhaḥ, tasya karpūrādiviṣayaviśeṣeṇa saṃyogāt saṃvedyasya hrādasya sukhasyotpatterityarthaḥ| saṃvedyagrahaṇamanubhavasiddhatvānnāsau ‘nāsti’ iti vaktuṃ śakyata iti darśayitum | nāduḥkhasya duḥkhapradhvaṃsaḥ sukham, asmato dhvaṃsāyogāt; na ca duḥkhaprāgabhāvaḥ, tasya viṣayairajanyatvāt; asya ca viṣayajanyatvāt; tābhyāṃ sukhamanyadeva tatra saṃvedyata iti bhāvaḥ | yatra tarhi śudādiduḥkhamasti tatrānnapānādibhistatpradhvaṃsaḥ sukhaṃ kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkayāha—yatrāpīti | kuta ityata āha—anneti | surasāmbhaḥsuśītalānnapānopādānādityarthaḥ | nanvannapānaviśeṣopādānāt taduḥkhavigama eva bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkayāha—yaistairiti | yādṛśaistādṛśairasvādubhirapītyarthaḥ | nanu duḥkhavigamaviśeṣārthamevānnapānaviśeṣopādānaṃ bhaviṣyati; dṛṣṭo hi kāraṇaviśeṣāt kāryaviśeṣa ityāśaṅkayāha—na ceti | duḥkhābhāvasyāsiśayābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | bhāvarūpe tu sukhe tasyāsiśayavaccāt tadarthaṃ sukhasādhanaviśeṣāpekṣā yuktā | taduktam—sukhe tviti |
[p. 8]
P. 2-8. aduḥkhasyeti yaduktaṃ tatrāśaṅkayati—yadapīti | viṣayaprāptiṃ vinā kāma eva duḥkham, atastannivṛttireva sukhaṃ bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | taddūṣayati— tatreti; tasminnapi pakṣe | akāmasya avidyamānakāmasya puṃsaḥ pavanopanītācintitacampakagandhādiviṣayaviśeṣopabhoge sati sukhitā na syādityarthaḥ | mā bhavatviti cet tatrāha—bhavati ceti | madhyastho rāgavairāgyayormadhye vartamāna ityarthaḥ | kāmanivṛtteḥ sukhatvavyabhicārārthamakāmasyāpītyuktam, tatra codayati—syādetaditi | sukhayatīti sukho viṣayaḥ, ṇyantāddhātoḥ pacādyac | svapakṣe sādhutvamāha—kāmanivṛttyā tviti | abhiruciriti kāmasya vivaraṇam | yatrakāmasya viṣayaviśeṣayogāt sukhaṃ bhavati tatra tarhi kā gatirityāśaṅkyāha—tatreti | sahajo hi rāgaḥ sarvapuṃsāmasti, sa tu viṣayaviśeṣeṇāvirbhavatīti dhiyoktam—kāmābhivyaktāviti | siddhāntī pratyāha—tadapīti | nivarhaṇā hantāraḥ | bhavantītyadhyāhārtham | imamevārthamabhiyuktoktyā draḍhayati—uktaṃ hīti | tathā pātañjalenāpi vacasāṃ draḍhayati—bhogeti | yathā bhogābhyāso vivardhate viśeṣeṇa vardhate tathā rāgo'pītyarthaḥ | doṣāntaramāha—viṣayadoṣeti | kāmanivṛtteravastutvenāviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ | doṣāntaramāha—samāne ceti | ekasya prārthitārthalābhe prabhodo mahān bhavati, anyasya cārupa iti prabhodabhedo na syādityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—kāmanivṛtteriti | para idānīṃ viśeṣamāha—syāditi | yasya kāmo'tirikto mahān sa tannivṛttau sukhamanatiriktamapyatiriktamabhimanyate, na taccato'tiriktam, kāmanivṛtteraviśeṣāt; itaratra tvanatirikte kāme anyathā anatiśayitamityarthaḥ | tatra doṣamāha—tacceti | satyapi kāmātireke kleśāvāse'rthe sukhātirekābhāvāt, asatyapi kāmātireke'kleśādupagate sukhātiśayadarśanānna kāmātirekanibandhanaḥ sukhātirekābhimāna ityarthaḥ | kāmātirekāddhetoḥ prayāsaṃ kleśaṃ kurvāṇaṃ naraṃ na tathā labdho'rthaḥ prīṇayati, yathā vinā prayāsādupanato'prārthitaḥ prīṇayatītyanvayaḥ | imamarthaṃ lokasiddhenānubhavena draḍhayati—tathā hīti | anāśaṃsitamaprārthitamityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca kiṃ kāmābhāvamātraṃ sukham, atha pradhvaṃsaḥ? tatrādye pakṣe doṣamāha—
[p. 9]
kāmābhāvamātre ceti | akāmitaviṣayopabhoge bhogāt prāk kāmasya P. 2-23. prāgabhāvaḥ ; bhogāvasthāyāṃ paratra ca viṣayaprāptyuddhuddhasya kāmasya pradhvaṃsābhāvaḥ ; ataḥ kāmābhāvasyāviśeṣāt bhogāvasthātaḥ sakāśāt pūrvāpare avasthe na bhidyeyātām ; tasyāmiva tayorapi sukhī syāt ; na caivamastīti bhāvaḥ | dvitīye pakṣe doṣamāha—kāmadhvaṃsa iti | kāmadhvaṃse ca sukhe bhogāvasthātaḥ sakāśāt parāvasthā na bhidyeta ; ubhayatrāpi kāmadhvaṃsāviśeṣāt bhogāvasthāyāmiva taduttarakālamapi sukhī syādityarthaḥ | atha bhogāvasthāyāṃ sakāmaḥ pumān ; pūrvāparayoravasthayostvakāmaḥ ; tena kāmābhāvamātre sukhe bhogāvasthātaḥ pūrvāpare avasthe bhidyete ; kāmapradhvaṃse ca sukhe parāvasthābhidyete ; ataḥ pakṣadvayoktadoṣāprasaṅga ityucyate | tadayuktam ; evaṃ hyādye pakṣe bhogāvasthātaḥ pūrvāparayoravasthayoḥ kāmābhāvamātrātmakamukhasadbhāvāt sukhī syāt ; dvitīye ca pakṣe kāmadhvaṃsātmakamukhasadbhāvāt paratra sukhī syāt ; bhogāvasthāyāṃ tu kāmātmakaduḥkhasadbhāvāt duḥkhī syāt ; etaccānubhavaviruddham | tadetatsarvaṃ sāśaṅkramāha—bhogāvasthāyāmiti | prabuddhaḥ adhvastaḥ kāmo yasyasa tathā iti vigrahaḥ | pūrvāparayorityupalakṣaṇam, paratreti ca draṣṭavyam | akāmasyāpi viṣayopabhogāt kāmābhivyaktau tanniruttiḥ sukhamityuktam, taḥ yuktam ; yato'prāptaḥ sukhahetutvena smaryamāṇo viṣayaḥ kāmamuddhoṣayati, prāptaśca taṃ nivartayati ; prāptasyāpi viṣayasya kāmoddhoṣakatve tasyānivartakatvāt tato viṣayāt kāmasya nivṛttirna syāt ! ataḥ saiva nāsti ; kāsukhaṃ bhaviṣyatītyāha—aprāptaśceti | aprāpto'bhujyamānaḥ, prāpto bhujyamānaityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca yadi kāmanivṛttiḥ sukhaṃ tadā yatra kāmastatrāvaśyaṃ sukhena bhāvyam, utpattimattvena kāmanivṛtteravaśyaṃbhāvitvāt ; na caitadastītyāha— na ceti | kuta ityāha—manoratheti | manorathaśataprārthitasyāpi kasyacidviṣayasya kukalatrāderupabhogadaśāyāṃ duḥkhadarśanādityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca yatra
[p. 10]
gavādijātiviśeṣabhājāṃ vatsādīnāṃ kāmaḥ kāmādvā stanapānādau pravṛttirna 2. 3-5. syādityāśaṅkyāha—yā pīti | prāgbhavaḥ pūrvajanma, tatra bhavo'nubhavaḥ prāgbhavīyaḥ, tannivantyanetyarthaḥ | idamuktaṃ bhavati—anādau saṃsāre śataśo gavādidehānugṛhītātmanāṃ stanapānādisukhānubhavodbhūtastataḥ saṃskāraḥ, saṃskārācca gavādidehahetvadṛṣṭodvodhitā sukhasmṛtiḥ, tataḥ kāmapravṛttiḥ; atastatrāpi sukhānubhavapūrvaka eva kāmaḥ, kāmācca pravṛttiriti | taduktam—“taṃ vidyākarmaṇī” iti | yatra kāmastatra sukham, anyathā ya ekasya sukhaḥ sa sarvasya syāditi avyavasthoktā; tatrāha—yathā ceti | kāmo'pi na sarvasya sarvatra bhavati; kiṃ tarhi? kiñcijātīyasya kiñcidvayasaḥ kiñcidavasthasya kacidbhavati; yathā—gordhāsagrāse, yūnastraiṇe, rogiṇo'pathye; sukhamapi tathaiva jātyādibhedāt vyavasthāpyata iti nāvyavastheti bhāvaḥ | itaśca na kāmanivṛttiḥ sukham | api tu duḥkhaṃ yāvadityāha—api ca kecit pittāyupahatendriyā dūyante duḥkhena tapyante; kāmanivṛttau sukhe te'pi sukhinaḥ syuḥ; na caivamasti; ato na sā sukhamiti bhāvaḥ | kimiti dūyanta ityāha—tadabhāva iti; tadabhāve kāmābhāva ityarthaḥ | apara āha—na tarhīti | yadi dūyante na tarhi nivṛttakāmāḥ; na hyaniṣṭhe kāmanivṛttyā kecit dūyanta iti bhāvaḥ | siddhāntavādyāha—nivṛtteti | pittadoṣāt tiktatayāvabhāsamānaṃ khaṇḍādiviṣayaṃ prati nivṛttakāmā nivṛttarucayaḥ; rucitaṃ kāmayanta eva ‘kathamatra no ruciḥ syāt’ iti viṣayagrahaṇam | hetumāha—tasyeti | tasya viṣayasya, kutaścit pittādidoṣāt anupabhogyatvāt sukhahetutvādityarthaḥ | viṣayaṃ prati cenniṣkāmāḥ kutastarhi hetostadabhogyatvena dūyante? na hyaniṣṭhaviṣayānupabhoge duḥkhaṃ dṛṣṭamityabhiprāyeṇa hetuṃ paraḥ pṛcchati—kimiti | siddhāntī hetumāha—anubhūteti | pittādidoṣātpūrvamanubhūtasya khaṇḍādiviṣayājātasya sukhasya pittādiduṣṭendriyairidānīmaprāptestadanusmṛtyā te dūyanta ityarthaḥ | atrodāharaṇamāha—yatheti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | pratipuruṣavedyatvenānubhavasiddhatvādapratyākhyeyatvameva draḍhayati | yadi sukhaṃ vastubhūtamasti kimarthaṃ tarhi “anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmakhyātiravidyā”
[p. 11]
“ duḥkhaṃ janma tamaḥśvabhraṃ varṣabuddhudasaṃnnimam | nāśaprāyaṃ sukhādhīnaṃ nāśottaramabhāvagam | ” ityādivacobhiḥ vyāsapatañjaliprabhṛtibhirācāryaiḥ saṃsāre sukhābhāva uktaḥ ? kimiti vā kaiścitpaṇḍitairduḥkhanivṛttiḥ sukhamuktam ? ityāśaṅkyāha—P. 3-14. sukhasaṃgīti | viṣayasukhe'tyantamāsaktaṃ paramaśreyovimukhaṃ janamanukūlayituṃ tattvabhavadbhiḥ pūjyairācāryaiḥ sasvaṃ nāma vastu nāstīti varṇitamiti kalpayāma ityarthaḥ | prakṛtavicāraphalaṃ saṃkalpya kathayati—tadevamiti | siddhamityadhyāhāryam | idānīṃ yaduktamānandaśrutayo duḥkhābhāvaṃ lakṣaṇayāhuriti, taddūṣayati—sa ceti | ataḥ sa eva grāhya iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | nanu gaṅgāghoṣādivākyavadamukhyo'rthaḥ kiṃ na gṛhyata ityāśaṅkya viśeṣamāha—śabdeti | tatra pramāṇāntaragocaratvāt tathāpi syāt; iha tu tannāsti, śabdaikagocaratvādātmasvarūpasya; ato'tra śabdaśaktyanusāreṇaivārthe pratītiryuktā, na mānāntarānusāreṇa yathā gaṅgāśabdāt tīra ityarthaḥ | nanvanupapattyā lakṣaṇā bhavati; sā cātrāpyastītyuktam ‘ānandarāgāt’ ityādinetyāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | kuta ityata āha—na hīti | na hi tapasvī tapāṃtīcchan rāgītyucyate loka iti bhāvaḥ | ko nāma sa ityāha—avidyeti | asatye satyamityanātmanyātmeti jñānamavidyā, tayākṣiptaṃ kṛtamaśānteṣu rūparasādiguṇeṣvabhiniveśaṃ rāgamācakṣate dhīrā ityarthaḥ | avidyayā kṣetradharmādisukhādīnātmani manyamānasya taddhetuṣu rūparasādiṣvasatyeṣvavidyayā satyatayābhimateṣu yo'bhiniveśaḥ so'vidyākṣiptaḥ | vyatirekamāha—tacceti | tacce tu sacce tvānandātmakabrahmaṇi taddarśanasya vaimakhyāt saṃśayaviparyayamalavirahāt yā cittasya praśāntirabhīcchā sā na rāgapakṣe vyavasthāpyate dhīraiḥ | idamuktaṃ bhavati—na sarvavecchā rāgaḥ, kiṃ tvabhūtaviṣayeti | prasādo'bhirucirabhīccheti paryāyanirdeśaḥ, evaṃvidhaiḥ śabdailoke sā prasiddhā na rāgaśabdeneti dyotanārthaḥ | yathā tava noddegamātraṃ dveṣa ityāha—yatheti | tata iti, saṃsārādityarthaḥ | yoda punaruddhegamātraṃ dveṣaḥ syāt tatastavāpi muktirna syādityāha—anyatheti | tato duḥkhāddūṣaḥ, tannivṛttiḥ, dhanetyarthaḥ | evaṃ tāva-
[p. 12]
tattvabhūtanityaniratiśayānandecchā na rāgo bhavatītyuktam | adhunā bhavatu sā rāgaḥ, tathāpi na sā śāntatādiśrutyā niṣidhyate ; kiṃ tu tatparipanthitadarupakṣaviviṣayamutpannecchā ; yathā pramāṇāntaradṛṣṭe parame sārvabhaume rājyādyutkarṣe ye rāgiṇaḥ ; dṛṣṭaḥ parama utkarṣaḥ prakarṣo rāgitvena vā yeṣāṃ rāgiṇām ; yadvā parebhya utkarṣaḥ parotkarṣaḥ, dṛṣṭaśrāptau parotkarṣaśra, tatra ye rāgiṇaḥ ; teṣāmarthe kāmalobhādityāgātmaka indriyajayastatprāptyupāyatvenopadiśyate tathehāpi bhaviṣyatītyāha—api ceti | sarvarāgādiniṣedhe sārvabhaumarājyarāgasyāpi niṣedhāt tatrāpyapravṛtterupadeśvaryaiyarthyaṃ syāditi doṣāt yathā tatra samīhitāt sārvabhaumarājyāditarasmin tatparipanthini viṣaye abhiṣaṅga āsaktī rāgātiśayo niṣidhyate, tathehāpyuttamabrahmānandarāgāditarasmādanuttamaviṣayasukhāsaṅgādupanatādupasthitādapi śamavidhinā mumukṣornivṛttirupadiśyata ityāha—tatheti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—brahmānandavarjamitaratra rāgaḥ śamavidhinā niṣidhyate ; tena brahmānandarāgādāpa mumukṣupravṛttau na śamavidhivirodha iti | tathā cānyairapi sūribhiridamuktamityāha—tathā coktamiti | atyantakāmātmatā na praśastā ; na ca mumukṣorapyatyantamakāmitāsti ; tasyāpi hi muktau kāmo'stītyarthaḥ | evaṃ śamavidhivirodhe parihṛte paraḥ punarapi brahmaṇyānandamākṣipati—syādetaditi | yadyānandaḥ saṃvedyaḥ tataḥ kartuḥ saṃvedituranyat karma saṃvedyamiti hetoḥ dvaitaprasaṅgaḥ ; ātmani kriyāvirodhāditi bhāvaḥ | itaśca dvaitaprasaṅga ityāha—kartriti | kriyāgarbhatvāt karmakartṛsaṃbandhasya kriyāyāśca karaṇamantareṇābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | kartṛkarmabhāvena cānandabrahmaṇorbhede ghaṭapaṭhayoriva sāmānādhikaraṇyamapi na syādityāha—ānandamiti | atha ‘śuklaḥ paṭhaḥ’ itivadānandavadbrahmānandamityucyate, tato dvitīyena tadbrahmāvādadvitīyaṃ brahmeti na yujyate ; tataścādvaitaśrutivirodha ityabhiprāyeṇāha—tadvattayeti | athānando'saṃvedya ityucyate tatrāha—asaṃvedana iti | “vijñānamānandam” iti śrutāvityarthaḥ | kathaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkyāha—puruṣārtheti | atra samādhānamāha—ucyata iti | phalaṃ pramāṇaphalamanubhavaḥ, kartā ca sannidhestasyaivānubhavinā ātmā, tadvadityarthaḥ |
[p. 13]
imamevārthaṃ phalasadbhāvapradarśanapuraḥsaraṃ prapañcayati—tathā hīti | paraśośchidivat P. 4-3. pramāṇādarthāntaraṃ vā phalamastu | “yadābhāsaṃ prameyaṃ tat pramāṇaṃ phalate punaḥ | bhāhakākārasaṃvittyotprayaṃ nātaḥ pṛthak kṛtam |” iti bauddhamatenānarthāntaraṃ vāstu, sarvaśca sarvaparīkṣakairamyupagamyate ; na tu tadviśeṣacintopayujyate ; ato na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | pramāṇaśabdasya bhāvakaraṇavyutpattibhedābhiprāyeṇānarthāntaramityuktamityanye | tasyānavabhāsaṃ tāvanniṣedhati—na ceti | kuta ityāha—taditi | taccennāvabhāti, tataḥ śaśaśṛṅgavannāstyeva ; ato na tatkṛtaṃ saṃvedyatvaṃ bhāvānāṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ ; yadā pradīpakarapaṃ nendriyakarapam | ataḥ satyapi tasminnanavabhāsamāne tatkṛtaṃ saṃvedyatvaṃ bhāvānāṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | kasmāt punarajñātameva phalamindriyavadarthaṃ na saṃvedayati ? vaiṣamyāt ; indriyaṃ hi phalaṃ janayati ; janakaṃ cājñātamapi bhavati yathā bījamaṅkurasya ; phalaṃ tu phalāntaraṃ na janayati, anavasthānāt ; atastadeva ghaṭasyānubhavaḥ | sa cet tadanuviddhaghaṭapratibhāse bhātyeva | anyadeva ‘ghaṭo'yam’ iti jñānam, anyacca ‘ghaṭo'nubhūyate’ iti ; ato ‘ghaṭo'yam’ iti ghaṭa evāvabhāti, na ghaṭānubhava iti cet ; naivam ; evaṃ hi ghaṭe bhāti ‘ghaṭānubhavo mamāsti, na vā’ iti pṛthak jijñāsā syāt ; na ca tathāsti ; tasmādanubhavo ghaṭa evāvabhātīti | kiṃ caivaṃ satyanubhavo'nubhavāntareṇa bhāvāt ; tathā sati cānavasthā syāt | kiṃ ca tadanubhavāntaram, yato'nubhavo bhāti ? ātmamanaḥsaṃyogajaṃ pratyakṣamiti cet ; na tāvadyugapatkālam, jñānānāmayaugapadyeṣṭeḥ ; nāpyanubhavāt pūrvottarakālam, pratyakṣasyātītānāgatānubhavaviṣayatvāyogāt ; anumeyatvamapi liṅgābhāvāt pratyākhyeyam ; na tāvadarthaḥ sattāmātreṇa liṅgam, ajñātasyāliṅgatvāt ; jñātaśret, na ; saṃbandhānavasāyāt ; na hyajñāte'nubhave sambandhini tasya liṅgena saha saṃbandho jñātuṃ śakyaḥ | jñāte cet jñāyate, kena jñāte ? na tāvadanenaiva liṅgena, itaretarāśrayāt ; na liṅgāntareṇa, turudoṣatvāt liṅgāntaravaiyarthyācca | tasmāt jñātena cedanubhavena saha liṅgasya saṃbandhajñānamiṣyate,
[p. 14]
tataḥ svataḥ saṃvediteneti balādāpatitam; ataḥ siddho naḥ pakṣaḥ; tadā cānumānamapyaphalam | yadapyuktaṃ—“jñānaṃ tajjñātatāvaśāt” iti, tadapyayuktam; jñātatā hi jñānasya jñeyena saṃbandhaḥ; taduktam—“kuttaddhitasamāsebhyaḥ saṃbandhābhidhānaṃ tvataśbhyām” iti | na ca jñāne saṃbandhinyajñāte jñātatā jñātuṃ śakyata ityaliṅgam | athārthasthaṃ prākaṭyaṃ jñātatā, tadapyajñātamaliṅgam; jñātaṃ cet, travāpyarthavat prākaṭyantarāpattāvanavasthā | svataḥ siddhaṃ cet, anubhava eva tathāstu, kiṃ tena? arthasthaṃ ca prākaṭyaṃ jñāne'tīte'pyanuvartata pāke'tīta iva pākajarūpādi; tadvadeva ca sarvapuruṣān pratyaviśiṣṭaṃ ca syāt, arthasthatvāt tasya | etena jñātatānyathānupapattyāpi nānubhavo'nubhūyata iti siddham | kiṃ ca jñāna evārthānubaddhe prakāśamāne'rthaḥ prakāśata ityucyate; na tvarthasya pṛthak prakāśo'sti, jaḍaprakāśāyogāditi vakṣyāmaḥ | kiṃ ca jñānaṃ jñānāntarāprakāśyam, tatsajātitvāt; yat yatsajāti tat tasyāprakāśyam | tasmādanubhavaḥ svato bhāsamāna evārthaṃ bhāsayati, nendriyavadabhāsamāno'pi; dīpavat jñātaḥ svataśca | tadabhāne'rthasyābhānamiti sūktam—tadasaṃvedyatva iti | atha saṃvedyaṃ phalamiṣyate tatrāha—na ceti | na ca phalaṃ saṃvedyaṃ saṃvittikarma; tadā hi tatra saṃvittyantaropalabdhiḥ syāt, na ca sāstītyuktam | tathā phale phalāntare tatrāpyevamityanavasthā ca syādityarthaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | evaṃ phalavaditi yaduktaṃ tat prapañcaca idānīṃ kartṛvaditi yaduktaṃ tat prapañcayati—yathā ceti | kuto na syādityāha— na hati | na tadātmasaṃbandhitvenārtho bhāyādityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha— na hati | ‘idam’ iti viṣaye ‘viditam’ iti phale ca nātmānavabhāsamāno ‘mayā’ iti vettṛtvenānusaṃdhātuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ | tatra ca ko doṣaḥ syādityāha—ananusaṃdhāna iti | svaparavedyayoḥ ko'tiśayaḥ? na ko'pītyarthaḥ | astu tarhi saṃvedyaḥ; tadapi netyāha—na ceti | kuta ityāha— karmatva iti | ātmanaḥ saṃvittikarmatve svātmani kriyāvirodhāt saṃvettā tato vyātiriktaḥ syāt; ata ātmā tvanātmā syāt, saṃvedyatvāt, ghaṭavat; anavasthā ca syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvātmā nedaṃtayārthavadavabhāti, kiṃ tu ‘aham’ ityātmānameva vettṛtayā vetti; ataḥ kuto vettṛvedyayorvyatireka- P. 4-6.
[p. 15]
prasaṅgaḥ ? pratītisiddhāttvaikasya viruddho'pi karmakartṛbhāvo bhāvapyati | syādevaṃ yadyahaṃ pratyaya evaṃ tadā syāt; sa eva tvātmani saṃvedye durlabhaḥ; tathā hi—ghaṭādivat ‘ayam’ iti pratyayaḥ syāt, na ‘aham’ iti; ato'haṃ pratyayaparyantaṃ evāyamākṣepaḥ; tataśca na tadbalāt vettavedyayoravyatirekaḥ, nāpyavirodhaḥ | nanvasti tāvat ‘aham’ ityucchekhaḥ; nāsāvanyathā karṣu śakyaḥ | yadi na śakyaḥ, śṛṇu tarhi tasya gatim—na khalu ‘aham’ iti pratyayaḥ, kiṃ tu ahaṃkāra eva svataḥ siddhānityacaitanyenāvadyotitastadupahite cātmani svato'vabhātyapi; tenaiva ‘ātmā pratīyate’ iti bālānāṃ tatra pratyayabhramaḥ | tataśca na svātmani kriyāvirodhaḥ; na cāvirodhenopapattau virodhakalpanocitā | ahaṃkāraśca kevalakartṛśaktiḥ, na karma mātrayāpi sphurati | ataḥ kutastadavacchinnasya svasaṃvedyatā? ataḥ svasaṃvedyatvaṃ ghaṭavadātmano na syāt, api tu tato'nyasya tatsaṃvedyurghaṭeta | evamātmanaḥ saṃvedyatve'haṃ pratyayavedyatvaṃ balādāpatitam; tatra ca vedyurvedyayovyatireka iti sūktam—vyatirekaprasaṅgāditi | evaṃ saṃvittikarmatvāsaṃbhavamupajīvyātmanaḥ svābhimataṃ svaprakāśatvamāha—tasmāditi | svaprakāśatvaiva P. 4-11. tasyātmanaḥ saṃvedyatā, na saṃvittikarmatetyevakārārthaḥ | saṃvedyatetyavivakṣitakarmatvanirdeśaḥ | na cārthasaṃvittāvātmā bhāsate, atatkarmatvāt | yat yatra saṃvittāvakarma, tat tatra na bhāsate nīlamiva pītasaṃvidi | evaṃ phalavat kartṛvacceti dṛṣṭāntadvaye svaprakāśatvamupaprādya dārṣṭāntike yojayati— tatheti | na tāvadānando na pratibhāti; pratibhāne'pi na saṃvittikarmatayā pratibhāti, phalavat kartṛvat svaprakāśatvenopapatteḥ | ato nāpuruṣārthatvam; nāpi karturanyat karmeti dvaitaprasaṅgaḥ; na ca kriyākaraṇādyapekṣetibhāvaḥ | brahmaṇaśca svaprakāśatvam “ātmajyotiḥ samrāṭ” “na hi draṣṭṛṣṭerviparilopo vidyate” ityādiśrutisiddham | ‘svabhāvaḥ’ ityānandasya svaprakāśatve hetuḥ; svaprakāśabrahmasvabhāvatve tadavyatirekādānando'pi svaprakāśa eva yukta iti bhāvaḥ | nanvānando brahmaṇo guṇaḥ, na svabhāvaḥ | naitat, “ānandaṃ brahma” iti sāmānādhikaraṇyāt | nanu guṇaguṇinorapi śuklapaṭādiṣu taddṛṣṭam; na, vaiṣamyāt; śuklādayo hi guṇavacanāḥ; te guṇamuktvā
[p. 16]
guṇini vartantām; ānandādayastu niṣkṛṣṭaguṇasthāḥ, te na guṇini vṛttimarhanti; na hi bhavati 'candanaṃ gandhaḥ' iti; taduktam—“na kadācitrayogo'sti candanaṃ gandha ityayam” iti | na ca guṇinyavṛttasya guṇaśabdasya guṇisāmānādhikaraṇyamavakalpate | yathā cāsmatpakṣe tadbhavati tathā vakṣyām ityuparamyate | nanu svaprakāśaśredātmā, suṣuptaḥ kiṃ na prakāśate ! kasya prakāśatām ? yadi svasya, tadiṣṭameva naḥ; svasya svayamātmā nityaṃ prakāśata eva; “na hi draṣṭurdṛṣṭirviparilopo vidyate” iti śrutiḥ | atha jīvānām, na; te hyavidyākāryabuddhyādyapādhikalpitabhedāḥ svāpe saṃhatākhilakāryāyāṃ tattvamācchādya bījamātratayā sthitāyāmavidyāyāṃ saṃskāramātraśeṣāḥ kathamupalabhantām ! ato'yamadoṣaḥ | nanu svaprakāśatve nityapa. 4-18. dṛṣṭerbrahmaṇo darśanapratiṣedho nāvakalpa ityāśaṅkyāha—keneti | ‘kam’ iti dvāśikarmaṇo dṛśyajātasyāyamākṣepaḥ, na dṛśerevetyarthaḥ | etadeva śrutipaurvāparyeṇa draḍhayati—tathā hati | dṛśikarmaṇo dṛśyajātasya vilayanimittaka eva pratiṣedha ucyata ityarthaḥ | yadā asya viduṣaḥ sarvaṃ dṛśyajātamātmaivābhūta nātmavyatiriktamasti tadā kaṃ paśyet ? na kamapīti vākyārthaḥ; ‘nanu paśyet’ iti tena dṛśyadṛṣṭiratra niṣidhyate, nātmadṛṣṭirityarthaḥ | tatra kecit ānandaśabdasya brahmaṇi duḥkhābhāvaparatvapratipādanāya sukhavācitvaṃ niṣektuṃ punaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante—anya iti | dharmān vibhajati— bhāveti | tatrābhāvarūpāṇāmaduṣṭatvamāha—tatreti | tānudāharati—yatheti | hetumāha—na hati | nivṛttiravastutvāt na dvaitamāpādayati, ato nādvaitaṃ hantītyarthaḥ | ‘ekam’ ityātmanāmanātmanāṃ cārthānāṃ bhedasya nānātvasya nivṛttiḥ, ‘ajam’ ityudayasya janmanaḥ, ‘amṛtam’ iti vyayasya vināśasyeti vivektavyam | ānandastu yadi bhāvarūpo'bhyupagamyate, tato'dvaitavidhātaḥ syādityāha—ānandaśreti ! nanu dharmadharmiṇorbhedādadvaitavidhātaḥ syāt; sa eva tatra nāsti, tayorbhede gavāśvavat dharmadharmitvāyogāt; ato nādvaitavidhātaḥ syāditi codayati—syādetaditi | tad dūṣayāt—taditi | dharmadharmiṇoratyantābhedo'pi dhārmiṇa iva svāpekṣayā dharmadharmitvānupapatterityarthaḥ | yasmādātyantikabhedābhedābhyāṃ vyāvṛtto dharmadharmibhāvaḥ, tasmāt kathaṃcidvighno
[p. 17]
dharmo'bhyupagantavya ityāha—tasmāditi | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | P. 4-23 ātyantikabhedābhāvaparatvaṃ ca śruterekādvitīyaivaśabdaiḥ punaḥ punarabhidhānāt draṣṭavyam | nanu cānandavijñānayorna dharmadharmibhāvaḥ, kiṃ tvekameva brahma vijñānānandarūpadvayātmakam ; ato nādvaitaśrutibādha ityāśaṅkyāha—evamiti | yo'pyekasya brahmaṇo vijñānānandarūpadvayaṃ pratijānīte, tasyāpi rūpadvayasya mitho rūpiṇaśca sakāśādvedābhedābhyupagamādātyantikabhedābhāvaśrutirna yujyata iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ | evaṃ sukhavācinyānandaśabde doṣamuktamupajīvya paraḥ svapakṣaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati—tasmāditi | vijñānātmanaiti | vijñānaikarūpatvānna vijñānānandākhyarūpadvayabhedakṛto dvaitaprasaṅgo doṣo'stītyuktam | duḥkhābhāvopādhiriti cābhāvasyāvastutvāt bhāvarūpānandapakṣa iva na dharmadharmibhedakṛto'pi sa doṣo'stītyuktamiti mantavyam | nanu na vijñānānandayodharmadharmitvam, nāpyekamanekātmakaṃ vastu, kiṃ tvekamevedaṃ tattvaṃ vijñānānandaśabdadvayenocyate ; ato na dvaitaprasaṅga iti siddhāntimatamāśaṅkyāha— na ceti | kuta ityāha—śabdeti | ekārthatve hi paryāyatvaṃ syāt ; na ca paryāyāṇāṃ saha prayogo dṛṣṭo yuktaśceti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca loke vijñānānandaśabdayoraparyāyatvaṃ prasiddham ; na ca tadvācyayorekatvaṃ ghaṭapaṭayoriva yuktamityāha—kathaṃ ceti | tadevaṃ vijñānānandayorabhedo na ghaṭate ; bhede ca tayoradvaitavādheti sthite paraḥ svapakṣamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | anyatrāstu nāmānandaśabdasya sukhavācitvam, brahmīṇa tūktayānupapattyā duḥkhābhāva eva lakṣyaḥ ; ata eva 'brahmaṇi' ityuktam | atra siddhāntamāha—atreti | viśiṣṭasya trailokyavilakṣaṇasya hrādātmakasya sukhasvarūpasya, na tu taddharmiṇaḥ, candraprakāśopamasya prakāśaviśeṣasya vijñānānandaśabdadvayena pratipādanānnādvaitaśrutibādhadoṣa ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—vijñānamevānandam, na taddharmaḥ ; nāpyekaṃ dvirūpam ; ato nādvaitavādheti | yattūktamaparyāyaśabdānāmekārthatvaṃ na dṛṣṭamiti tadvacabhicārayituṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | aparyāyatvamabhidheyabhedāt, abhedārthatvaṃ ca lakṣyamāṇasyaikatvādityavirodhaḥ | ko'sāvityāha—prakāśa iti | nanu yadīmāvaparyāyau, tato bhinnārthau ; ato nalipyalādivadviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvaṃ 2
[p. 18]
syāt; ataḥ prakarṣaviśiṣṭaḥ prakāśaḥ pratipādyaḥ syāt; tataśca vijñāpa. 5-8. nānandayostattulyatve viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhedāt dvaitāpattirityāśaṅkyāha—na hīti | 'śabdadvayopāyaḥ pratīyate' ityantagatena saha saṃbandhaḥ | tataścāyamartho bhavati— na prakāśarūpādviśeṣyāt viśeṣaṇarūpatayānyo bhinnaḥ savitari prakarṣo'smācchabdadvayāt pratīyate, nāpi viparyaya iti | kiṃ nāma pratīyata ityata āha—api tviti | ayamāśayaḥ—yadā kaścit pṛcchati 'kaḥ savitā?' iti, tadā praśnānurūpatvāduttarasya prakāśaviśeṣa eva savitṛstvalakṣaṇaḥ pratipādyaḥ; na prakāśaḥ prakarṣaviśiṣṭo viparyayo vā, praśnānurūpatvāt; ataḥ savitṛstvalakṣaṇa eva tadā tātparyam; yatparaśca śabdaḥ sa śabdārthaḥ | tataśca śabdadvayenāvivakṣitasvārthena svārthadvāreṇa tadevaikaṃ lakṣyata iti siddhamaparyāyatvamekārthatvaṃ ceti | imamarthaṃ dārṣṭāntike'pi yojayati—tatheti | ānandabhedaḥ ānandaviśeṣaḥ sukham, svaprakāśaṃ vijñānameva vā sukharūpaṃ brahmeti śabdadvayena gamyate; na punarvijñānānandayorguṇaguṇibhāva iti bhāvaḥ | nanu vijñānānandayorabhede tacchabdayoḥ paryāyatvāt saha prayogo na syāt; prakṛṣṭaprakāśādau punarabhiṣeyabhedādupapadyate saha prayogaḥ; svārthadvayadvāreṇa ca lakṣaṇā; na ceha svārthadvayānvayo'sti; na cāsati svārthānvaye lakṣaṇā bhavati | na ca lakṣyameva lakṣaṇam; tatra cāparyāyayorlakṣaṇayaikārthatvamuktam; na ca vijñānānandayorabhede satyaparyāyatā; tataḥ sarvathā dṛṣṭāntavaiṣamyam | athaitassarvadoṣajihāsayā vijñānānandaśabdayorabhiṣeyabheda iṣyate, tato "vijñānamānandaṃ brahma" iti guṇaguṇitvaṃ balādāpannam, dvaitaprasaktiśca syāt | aśrocyate—atrāpyavidyākalpito'bhiṣeyabhedo'sti | avidyābhūmāveva hi lokasyāvidyāvataḥ śabdārthabhedavyutpattiriṣyate, na taccabhūmāvadvitīyāyām; tatra vijñānānandau pṛthak prasiddhau | sā cānayoravidyātmikā bhedaprasiddhiḥ "ekamevādvitīyam" ityanayā śrutyā bādhyate | anayaiva ca taccamupadiśantyā śrutyā sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ prayogadvāreṇa bodhyate; yathā "taccamasi" iti jīvaparamātmanoḥ; na hi tatra tadarthaviśiṣṭastvamarthaḥ, tvamarthaviśiṣṭo vā tadarthaḥ pratipādyaḥ | kiṃ tvadidyāsiddhārthabhedābhyāmeva tattvaṃ padābhyāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyabalādvedabādhenābhinnaṃ taccam | ataśca yathā tatrāvidyāsiddhā-
[p. 19]
rthabhedāt taccamoraparyāyatvam, atha ca tābhyāmavidyāsiddhārthabhedābhyāmekamalaukikaṃ taccaṃ gamyate—na tvabhidhīyate, alaukikatvādevātmanaḥ śabdaprastinimittajātyādicatuṣṭayavirahācca—evamatrāpi ; tataśca na dṛṣṭāntavaiṣamyam, na ca sahaprayogadoṣaḥ, na ca dvaitaprasaktiḥ, avidyātmakatvādabhidheyabhedasya ; na hyavidyātmako bhedo dvicandrādau dvaitamāsañjayati | kalpitasya kathamupāyatvamiti cet, atroktaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | nanvevaṃ sati prasiddhasyārthasya bādhe kathamasati svārthānvaye lakṣaṇā bhavati ? bhavatīti brūmaḥ, yathā “gāvo vā etatsatramāsata” ityasati gavāsane stutirlakṣyate ; tathā coktam— “gavāṃ satrāsanaṃ yadvadasat stutyaṅgatāṃ gatam | jñānānandau tathaivāṅgaṃ svasthātasukhasaṃvidaḥ ||” iti | nanu yuktaṃ tatra, taccamarthayorabhedaparatvādvākyasya ; idaṃ tu brahmasvarūpaparaṃ vākyam, nānandavijñānayorabhedaparamapi, ubhayaparatve vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt ; ataḥ kathamatra tayorabhedapratītiḥ ? na, arthasiddhatvāt ; yathā khalu kriyāvidhipare'pi vākye dravyaguṇayorekakāryaniyamaḥ, syādevamatrāpyekapratibaddhatvādubhayoranyonyaniyamaḥ | sa cānandaśabdasya niṣkṛṣṭaguṇavācinaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapatteruktatvān guṇisthāruṇādiśabdavadguṇaguṇibhāvenāvakalpata ityarthāt tādātmyena vyavatiṣṭhate—vijñānamevānandaḥ, ānanda eva vijñānamityanayorabhedasiddhiriti sarvamaduṣṭam | yadvā na prakarṣo nāma guṇaḥ, guṇa'pi bhāvāt ‘prakṛṣṭagandhaṃ candanam’ iti ; “dravyāśrayyaguṇavān guṇaḥ” iti hyuktam | ata eva na dravyāntaram | na ca karma, karmaṇi bhāvācca ‘prakṛṣṭagatiraśvaḥ’ iti | jātitvādi tu tasya dūranirastāvakāśam | tasmāt prakarṣo nāma taccato vyaktiviśeṣa eva kaścit, kalpanāmātreṇa tu bhidyate | sa ca vyakteśca viśeṣaḥ svata eva, na prakarṣayogāt ; prakarṣasyevāprakarṣāt, mithaścānyathānavasthānāt | nanu cireṇa prakarṣāpāye'pi candanagandhasyānuṣṭhasetano'nyaḥ prakarṣaḥ ; maivam ; gandho'pi tatra na prāktanopagata eva ; anya eva tūdakādisaṃparkāt kutaścidvipannasya candanasya gandhapariṇāmaścireṇa jāta ityavehi | tadevaṃ kalpanayārthabhedādaparyāya- 2A
[p. 20]
tvaprasiddhiḥ ; atha ca taccataḥ prakāśaviśeṣa eva savitari prakarṣaḥ ; sa ca śabdadvayena pratīyate, ekaikasmādapratīteriti 'na ca prakṛṣṭaprakāśaśabdayoḥ' ityādi sūktam | evamānandaśabdasya duḥkhābhāvopādhitvaṃ duḥkhābhāvārthatvaṃ ca nirasyedānīmānandasya "eṣo'sya paramānandaḥ" iti yā paramatā śrutā, tāmapi je "sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkham" iti viṣayasukhopāyastrakcandanādi-pāratantrarūpāyāstaducchedarūpāyāśra duḥkhitāyā abhāvādaupacārikīmāhuḥ, tān paramānandaśabdasya paramatāmiti | pramāṇāntaragocaro hi tadanusāreṇa varṇyate, śabdaikagocarastu śabdaśaktyanusāreṇa grahītumucita iti bhāvaḥ | kuta ityāha—ja hīti | virodhyannasargāditi ; ānandavirodhino duḥkhasyābhāvādityarthaḥ | prakaraṇārthamupasaṃharati - tasmāditi | ātmaprakāśeti svaprakāśatvena vijñānarūpatām, prakṛṣṭeti ca mukhyāṃ paramatām, ānandasvabhāveti ca duḥkhābhāvasya bhāvatvānupapatterānandasya bhāvarūpatvamabhedaṃ ca sūcayatīti vivektavyam | bhāvarūpānavacchinnānandasvabhāvatve ca brahmaṇo yat "etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrāmupajīvanti" iti laukikaṃ sukhaṃ paramānandasya mātretyuktaṃ tadapi ghaṭata ityāha—evaṃ ceti | mātrā ekadeśaḥ, alpo bhāga iti yāvat | kuta ityāha—avacchedāditi | bhāvo hyanavacchinno viṣayairavacchinnālpīkartuṃ śakyate yathā ākāśaṃ ghaṭādibhiḥ, nābhāvaḥ khapuṣpādyabhāvavat | taduktam—duḥkhanivṛttāviti | bhāvarūpānandasya svabhāvatve hetvantaramāha—itaśceti | paramapremaviṣayatvaṃ hetuṃ śrutyā sādhayati—śrūyata iti | na kevalaṃ śrūyate, anubhavāṃtteddhaṃ ca tadityāha—pratīyate ceti | anubhavamāha—tathā hīti | ātmāśīriti ; ātmanyāśaṃsetyarthaḥ | tāmāha—meti | tataḥ kimityāha—sāpīti | na kevalaṃ śrutiḥ, api tu sāpyātmāśīḥ ityapiśabdārthaḥ | tathāpi kimityāha—premā ceti | duḥkhāt dveṣaḥ, sukhaduḥkhobhayarūparahite copekṣaṇamiti vivekaḥ | ātmanaḥ sukhātmakatvānubhave matāntaramāha—anye tviti | suṣupte satyanantaraṃ pratibuddhasya parāmarśādityanvayaḥ | kathaṃ punarasmāt parāmarśādanubhavasiddhirityata āha - na hīti | anubhavapūrvakatvāt parāmarśasya tataḥ sa kalpyata ityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca sarvathānanubhave 'asvāptam' ityabhyupagamo'pi na syādityāha—suṣupteti | 'na syāt' ityanuvartate | nanve-
[p. 21]
vamastvanubhavaḥ ; ‘sukhānubhavaḥ’ iti viśeṣaḥ kutaḥ ? duḥkhānubhava eva kiṃ na syādityata āha—na ceti | ‘syāt’ iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | P. 6-1. sukhaparāmaśīghriśiṣṭānubhavasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | nanvastu sukhānubhavaḥ, tathāpi kathamātmanaḥ sukhasvabhāvatvam ? pṛthageva sukhamanubhūyata iti kiṃ na syāt ? ityata āha—na ceti | tatra supupte sarvakaraṇoparamādātmano'nyannānubhūyate ; ataḥ sa eva sukhasvabhāvaḥ svato bhāsata iti tasya sukhātmatvasiddhiriti | imamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | “evamevemāḥ sarvāḥ prajā aharahargacchanti, ya etaṃ brahmalokaṃ na vindati” iti śrutiḥ | tadidamanye nānumanyante ; yataḥ parāmarśānyathānupapattyānubhavaḥ kalpyaḥ ; sa ca duḥkhābhāvanimitto'pyupapadyata iti ; tadāha—tatreti | tatra parāmarśaliṅgakasukhānubhavavādina evamāhuḥ—ātmanaḥ sukhātmatvādanyathātve asukhātmatve pramāṇāntarādavagate duḥkhābhāvanimittaḥ sukhaśabdo gauṇaḥ kalpyate, anyathā asukhātmatve tvanavagate mukhya eva yukta iti ; tadāha—tāniti | tadetaddūṣayati—tatreti | jīvasyaiva hi pratibuddhasya parāmarśādanubhavaḥ kalpyo nānyasya | na ca tasya tadānīmupasaṃhatākhilopādheḥ saṃskāramātraśeṣasyāvidyācchāditātmanaḥ suṣupta sukhopalambhaḥ saṃbhavati | tasmāt ‘sukham’ iti parāmarśamātramidam | tacca sukhe śruddhuḥkhādinivṛttau duḥkhoparame cobhayatrāpi dṛśyate ; upapadyate ca sukhāvinābhāvena duḥkhoparamo'pi | tataśca na tataḥ sukhānubhavarūpasyārthasya prasiddhiḥ | tadāha—tatreti | athavā ‘sukhamahamasvāptam, na mayā kiñciccetitam’ iti cobhayathā vyapadeśāt svāpe sarvathā ajñānam ; sukhaparāmarśastu duḥkhoparamanimitta ityevaṃ vyapadeśadvayasyopapatteśca na vyapadeśamātrāt sukhānubhavasiddhirityathaḥ | nanu duḥkhoparamo'pi svāpe na jñātaḥ ; satyam ; pratibuddhastu sukhamanusaṃdhāya duḥkhoparamamavagacchati | nanu duḥkhānanubhavo'pi tadā na jñātaḥ kathamanusaṃdhīyate ? yadi duḥkhamanvabhavat, tato duḥkhamasmaripyat ; na caivam ; ato ‘na duḥkhamanvabhavam’ ityevaṃ duḥkhānubhavābhāvamanusaṃdhata ityadāpaḥ | idānīmekapadaṃ vyākhyātuṃ tadyāvartyamātmanāmātmadṛśyayordṛśyānāṃ cānyonyabhedaṃ parābhimataṃ tāvadupanyasyati—atreti | kaścit sukhī kaścit
[p. 22]
duḥkhīti bhogavyavasthā, kaścinmuktaḥ kaścit saṃsārīti ca vibhāga ātmanāṃ bhede ghaṭate ; anyathaikasmin sukhini mukte ca sarve sukhino muktāśca syurityātmanāṃ bhede yuktiḥ | dṛśyaṃ vinā cātmano dṛkchaktiraphalā syāt | P. 6-8. ātmānaṃ tayā drakṣyatīti cet, tatrāha—svātmanīti | svavyāpāravyāpyatayā svasya karmatvaṃ virudhyata ityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca darśanavyāpyaṃ hi dṛśyam ; darśanaṃ ca kāryatvāt samavāyikāraṇaṃ draṣṭāramanumāpayatītyevaṃ dṛśyena draṣṭuranumānam ; ato'sti draṣṭuranyadṛśyamiti ātmadṛśyayorbhede yuktiḥ | dṛśyasyetyādi dṛśyānāṃ mitho bhede yuktiriti vivektavyam | sukhādibhedavacca ca śabdādeściguṇajatvāt ; evaṃ ca sukhādilakṣaṇo'pi dṛśyabhedaḥ kaṭākṣita iti draṣṭavyam | yathā viruddhānāṃ sukhādīnāṃ bhedopalabdhiḥ, evaṃ viruddhānāmapi śabdādīnāmityarthaḥ | tadevaṃ bhede sati “eka evāyamadvitīyaḥ” ityātmaikatvaśrutimātmatvajāterabhedāddeśakālābhedācca, jagadekaśrutimapi sattārūpeṇaikatvato gauṇīm, “ātmaivedam” iti ca tādṛśyīt tacchabdaṃ ye manyante tān pratyucyate—ekamiti | atra śrutimāha—indra iti | mīyate'nayeti māyā buddhiḥ ; ataścendriyabuddhyātmā dvipadādirūpaḥ pratīyata ityarthaḥ ; ataḥ kathaṃ nānātvasya mithyātvamiti cet ; na, yaugikatvāt ; yaugiko hi māyāśabdo buddhau, rūḍhaśca mithyābhāve ; rūḍhiśca yogādbalīyasīti nyāyaḥ ; tasmādbuddhireva grāhyā | yadyapi buddhināmasu kvacitpāṭho'sti, tathāpīha na sā grāhyā ; yaddhi pratīyate tad buddhyaiva ; ato māyeti viśeṣaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | na ca sarvatra vede buddhivācī māyāśabdaḥ, “māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ viddhi” iti vyabhicārāditi | tataḥ kimityāha—tatreti | nānātve satyekatvamupacaritam ; asarve tu tasmin śrutyoktamekatvameva satyamāpadyate, nopacaritamityarthaḥ | ekatvopacāre śrutyantaravirodhamāha—tatheti | ‘nopacarita ekatve'vakalpata’ iti vakṣyamāṇena sarvatra saṃvandhaḥ | pūrvaṃ māyābhidhānenārthānāṃ nānātvaṃ niṣiddham, iha tu sākṣāditi vyaktatvābhidhānam | atrāhuḥ—ākṛteḥ padārthatvānāṃ śabdo nānātvamāha ; tataśca “asadevedam” ityataḥ prakṛte jagatkāraṇe nānātvaṃ niṣidhyate, na tu nānābhūtaṃ jagaditi | atra brūmaḥ—nānātvavācyapi nānāśabdo ‘vaṭānānā paṭaḥ’ iti-
[p. 23]
bat tadvati jagati vartate | tatra paṭhaśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt ‘śuklaḥ paṭaḥ’ iti vadvartate iti cet, atrāpi ‘kiñcana’ ityanirdhāritaviśeṣajagatsāmānādhikaraṇyamaviśiṣṭam | tasmānnānābhūtajagadeva niṣidhyate, na nānātvamiti siddham | na ca ‘ekasminnāyatane eka evādvitīyo bhoktā, na nānā’ ityayamasyārthaḥ kalpyaḥ, aśrutakalpanāprasaṅgāditi | tatraiva śrutyantaravirodhamāha—nānātveti | ekatve upacarite nānātvaṃ paramārthaṃ syāt; na ca P. 6-14. tasya nindā yukteti bhāvaḥ | tatraiva punaḥ śrutyantaravirodhamāha—bhedeti | bhedadarśanādanyasmin tadābhāse iva śabdo ghaṭate, na tu tatraivetyarthaḥ | na ca “bhedadṛṣṭiriva” iti “aṇvya ivemā dhānāḥ” iti vadavadhāraṇārthaṃ ivakāraḥ, nāsatvadyotanārthaṃ iti vācyam; bhramādityanupapatteḥ | ata eva ca bhedadṛṣṭe bhrāntitve sthite bhedasya mithyātvāt ‘nānā’ ityayamapīvakāro mithyātvadyotanārtho nāvadhāraṇārthaṃ iti siddham, anyathā tadviruddhārthaḥ syāt; “neha nānā” iti ca nānābhūtasya niṣiddhatvāt kāraṇaṃ vinā prasiddhatarārthatyāgāyogācca | tatra tu śrīliṅganirdeśāt kāraṇāntarādatikrama iti viśeṣaḥ | nānātvanivṛttiśruternaikatvopacāra iti pūrvamuktam; adhunā ekatvamupacaritamabhyupagamya tadā nānātvanivṛttiśrutirna ghaṭata ityāha—kāmamiti | bhāvikasyeti; tāttvikasyetyarthaḥ | nanu nānātvanivṛttirapi sattādirupābhede'nānātvādupacārādbhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya dūṣayati—sāpīti | nanu māstu prayojanam; tadupacāranimittaṃ sattādijātyabhedo bhavatu; kiṃ prayojanenetyāśaṅkyāha—satīti | nimittavadupacāre prayojanamapekṣyam | na hi stutiṃ prayojanamanapekṣya tejasvitānimittamātrabhāvādādityaśabdo yūpa upacaryate | na hi śrutiḥ sato nānātvasyābhāvaṃ niṣprayojanameva darśayatīti bhāvaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti | na kevalaṃ sa niṣprayojanaḥ, kiṃ tu prayojanavirodhī yāvadityāha—viparyayācceti | yat prayojanaṃ yathā copacārastadvirodhī, tadāha—bhabhyudayeti | sā nānātvanivṛttiḥ svargaḥ sādhyaḥ, yāgaḥ sādhanam, prayājādīnītikartavyeteni ca vibhāgaṃ nivartayantī tena vinānuṣṭhānābhāvādabhyudayavirodhinī; sarvatra viṣayeṣu ‘ātmaivedam’ itya-
[p. 24]
dvivacanāntena samāsaḥ | abhyudayavirodhitvaṃ prasiddhatvādupekṣya niḥśreyasavirodhitve hetumāha—tathā hīti | “nānyatra sarvasaṃtyāgānmokṣaṃ vindati mānavaḥ” iti sarvatyāgānmokṣaḥ śrutaḥ | na ca putrakalatrādāvātmābhimāne sati tyaktuṃ śakya iti mokṣo na syādityarthaḥ | ekatvopacāro'bhyupagamamātreṇoktaḥ, na tu śruterabhipreta ityabhiprāyeṇāha—tatheti | avadhāryate'nenetyavadhāraṇamevakāraḥ, advitīyaśabdaśravyaṃ, tābhyāṃ tasyaivārthasyābhedarūpasya punaḥ punarabhidhānāt sarvaprakāraṃ na kevalaṃ jātyādyātmanā api tu vyaktyātmanāpi bhedanivṛttiparatā śruterlakṣyata ityarthaḥ | atra hetumāha— abhyāse hīti | bhūyastvaṃ vaipulyamatiśayitatvam, sarvathā bhedasya gandho'pi nāstīti yāvat | eva ca bhedābhedavādino'pi śrutyaiva nirastā iti mantavyam | evamabhedaparādāmāyādātmādvaitaṃ prasādhya adhunā bhogavyavasthānādityātmanāṃ bhede hetuṃ paroktamanubhāṣya dūṣayati—yatviti | ātmaikatvepi buddhyādyupādhibhedena kalpitādvedāt svasya karakāpavarakabhedādalpatvamahattvavyavastheva bhogavyavasthāpi setsyatītyarthaḥ | na ceyamadṛṣṭakalpanā ; dṛśyate hi kalpitādvedād bhogavyavasthetyāha—tathā hīti | ekasmin ; ekadehagate jīva ityarthaḥ | na ca pādaśirasoreva tatra vedanetyāha—na hīti | nanu vibhoraṇārvātmano'navayavatvādastu kalpitanibandhanā bhogavyavasthā ; dehaparimāṇasya tu saṃkocavikāsavaccena sāvayavatvāt tadvedāt satyaiva setsyati ; ata āha—śarīreti | nātmāvayavā eva vedanābhājaḥ, kiṃ tu ātmāvayavyapi ; anyathā ātmāvayavino vedanā na syāt | ataśca ātmāvayavayorbhedādanyonyavedanānusaṃdhānāsaṃbhavāt pādaśirasorvedanayoranusaṃdhātaiko na syāt ; asti cāsau | na cāsau paravedanābhevānusaṃdhatte, ‘me vedanā’ ityasmādasaṃbhedāt | tasmādātmāvayavyapi vedanābhāk | na ca tasya kṛtsnadehavyāpinaḥ sarvatra vedanābhāktvam, api tu pādaśirasoreva ; tataśca na syādanavayavatvāt pādādyupalakṣitasvāvayavabhedakalpitabhedasyaiva vedanāvyavasthetyarthaḥ | atha nagnānāmapyanavayavātmā tadāyaṃ pādādyavayavābhiprāyeṇa grantho yojyaḥ | tathā
[p. 25]
ca pṛthaṅnirdeśābhiprāyo vācyaḥ | atra codayati—syādetaditi | P. 7-6. yathaikasmin dehe eka evātmā pādaśirasorvedanāmanusaṃdhatte, tathā dehadvaye'pyeka ātmā kiṃ nānusaṃdhatta ityarthaḥ | tiddhāntī tu tiddhāntāntaradūṣaṇaṃ nāma nigrahasthānamāha—anyāditi | ātmaikye dehadvaye'pyekadehavadanusaṃdhānaṃ syāt, na ca tadasti ; tasmānnātmaikyabhiti prakṛtasiddhāntadūṣaṇabhedevamityaparitoṣāt parihārāntaramāha—api ceti | yathaikasmin dehe śiraḥpādakalpitabhedo jīvapradeśo nānyonyasya vedanāmanusaṃdadhāti, tathaikasya paramātmanośā buddhyādyupādhikalpitabhedā jīvā apītyarthaḥ | paramātmanastvanusaṃdhānamasaṃbhavanirastam ; taccantaḥkaraṇadharmaḥ ; na ca tasya tadastīti bhāvaḥ | kalpitabhedanibandhanavyavasthāyāṃ dṛṣṭāntāntaramāha—tatheti | mukhādīnāṃ bhedābhāve'pi maṇikṛpāṇādau varṇasya iyāmādeḥ saṃsthānasya cāvayavasaṃniveśaviśeṣasya bhedena vyavasthānaṃ saṃdṛśyate tathātrāpītyarthaḥ | evaṃ bhogavyavasthāmupapādya imameva nyāyaṃ muktasaṃsārivyavasthāyāmapyatidiśannāha—evamiti | imamevārthaṃ dṛṣṭāntena draḍhayati —tathā hīti | yathaiko'pi jīvaḥ śiraḥpādādyupādhikalpitabhedapradeśadvāreṇa sukhādibhiryujyamāno yatra tairyuktaḥ tatra baddhaḥ tato'nyatra tairyukta ityavagamyate tathāsmākamapi praviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | atraiva dṛṣṭāntāntaramāha—tatheti | yathā malināmalinayordarpaṇayorekamapi mukhaṃ malinamamalinaṃ bhāti tathā malināmalinayorantaḥkaraṇayoreko'pyātmā duḥkhī sukhī ceti dvividhasaṃsārikathanam ; yathā vādarpaṇaṃ tadeva mukhaṃ taddoṣamuktaṃ tathānantaḥkaraṇātmā taddoṣaduḥkhādibhiryukto bhavatīti vyavasthāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | atra codayati—syāditi | kalpaneti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ | kalpanātvamasadarthatvaṃ pratipattuḥ pratyayasya dharmaḥ, na vabhtudharmaḥ ; ato na vastu vyavasthāpayitumalamityarthaḥ | yadvā pratīyate'nenetyantaḥkaraṇaṃ pratyayaḥ, tasya dharmo mithyājñānaṃ kalpanā ; na khalvityatra tu kalpanaiva pratyaya iti | kuta ityāha—na khalviti | na hi yadyathā jñātaṃ tattathaiva syāt ; na hi śuktī rūpyatayā jñātā rūpyameva syāt ; tataśca upādhibhedādātmā bhinno vijñātaḥ, na taccato bhinnaḥ syāt ; ato na bhogādivyavasthāti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca kalpitājīvātkāryamapi dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ ca na syāt kalpitābhibhāvādiva bhāṇavakādityāha—na ceti ||
[p. 26]
nanūktā śiraḥpādādibhedena kalpitādapi bhedāt vyavasthā ; satyamuktā ; P. 7-16. kiṃ tu bhrāntiḥ sā, na taccata ityāha—udāhateti| 'satyam' ityabhyupagame | siddhāntī tvāha atrāpīti | asmākamapi sarvaṃ vibhrama evetyarthaḥ | udāhatā tu vyavasthetyayabheva siddhāntaḥ | tatra 'satyam' iti pareṇa codite, atrāpi vibhramatvamiti punaḥ parihāra iti grantho yojyaḥ | yathoktaṃ kalpitāt kāryaṃ na dṛṣṭamiti tadvyabhicārayati—kalpito'pīti | kalpita iti ; asati daṃśe bhrāntyādhyavasita ityarthaḥ | na ca jñānaṃ tatra mṛtihetuḥ, na tvasatyamiti vācyam ; jñānamātrasya sarvatrāviśeṣāt | athāhidaṃśaviśiṣṭamityucyate, prāptaṃ tarhi yathā mṛtihetutvaṃ tathā kalpitatvamityadoṣaḥ | daṃśaḥ sarvathā kalpitaḥ, jīve tu bhedamātram ; ato na sarvathā sāmyamiti tadarthaṃ pratisūryamudāharati—pratisūryaka iti ; darpaṇādau kalpitabhedamātraṃ sūryapratibimbaṃ prakāśakāryāya kalpataṃ ityarthaḥ | tadevamātmabhedahetuṃ bhogādivyavasthāmātmābhede'pyupapādyādhunā draṣṭṛdṛśyayordṛśyānāṃ ca mitho bhedahetuṃ dūṣayitumanubhāṣate—yadapīti | tatrādyaṃ tāvaddhetumasiddhatvena dūṣayati—tadapīti | draṣṭṛvagamapuraḥsaro dṛśyāvagama, na tu viparyayaḥ ; yathā caitannirvahati tathoktam 'yathā ca kartaryasaṃvedye' ityatretyarthaḥ | dvitīyamapi hetumasiddhatvenaiva dūṣayati—dṛśyeti ; pramāṇasya pratyakṣāderdṛśyabhedaḥ grahaṇāsāmarthyādityarthaḥ | vakṣyata iti 'āhurvidhātṛ pratyakṣam' ityatretyarthaḥ | yo'pi bhinnadṛśyasadbhāvahetudṛcchakterthavacchaktaḥ, so'pi dṛśyābhāve'pi ghaṭana ityāha—dṛgiti ; ātmānameva tayā draṣṭā drakṣyatītyarthaḥ | nanu kartuḥ karmatvaṃ virudhyata ityuktamityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | yathā pradīpaḥ pradīpāntaranirapekṣaḥ svata eva prakāśate tathātmāpi ; tataśca na virodha iti bhāvaḥ | yadi nāma pradīpaḥ pradīpāntaranirapekṣaḥ buddhivedyastu bhavatyeveti abuddhivedyakaṃ svataḥsiddhaṃ pramāṇaphalaṃ dṛṣṭāntayati—pramāṇeti | 'na ca saṃvedyam' ityatroktamityarthaḥ idānīṃ tvapakṣe guṇamāha—api ceti ; draṣṭṛdṛśyayorekatva ityarthaḥ | kuta ityata āha—draṣṭuriti | tena tena dṛśyarūpeṇa vipariṇamāditi paramatam | vivartanāditi ; atadrūpasya bhrāntyā tadrūpādhyāsāditi tvamatam | krimatra
[p. 27]
viśeṣanirūpaṇena ? matadvaye'pi dṛksarūpeṇa draṣṭṛsaṃbandhādarthānāṃ dṛśyatvaṃ ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ | bhede tu tathostādātmyalakṣaṇasaṃbandharahitayorātmānātmanoranavayavatvādanyayogolakavacca parasparamasaṃsṛṣṭayorata evāsaṃbaddhayordraṣṭṛdṛśyabhāvo na syādityāha—nānātve tviti | kuto na syādityāha—na hīti | svato P. 7-24. jaḍatvādarthānāmiti bhāvaḥ | nanu sphaṭikopame cetasi indriyapraṇālyā saṃkrāntānāmarthānāṃ tatraiva saṃkrāntenātmacaitanyena saṃbaddhānāṃ tadṛśyatvaṃ ghaṭiṣyata ityāśaṅkayati—eketi | tadṛṣayati—neti | ekāntaḥkaraṇasaṃkrāntāyā api cito nārthasaṃsargakāluṣyamasti, viśuddhatvāt | na ca viśuddhāpi tatra saṃkrāntā arthākāreṇa pariṇamate, kauṭasthyenāpariṇāmāt | etacca saṃkrāntimapekṣyoktam ; taccatastu sāpi niṣkriyatvāccito nāsti, arthasya ca ; ataḥ kuto'rthasya tatsaṃbandhāccetyatvamiti bhāvaḥ | athocyate—na bāhyo'rtho dṛśyaḥ, kiṃtu buddhirevārthākāreṇa pariṇatā arthākāreṇa dṛśyā ; sā cātmacitaḥ saṃnidheḥ citicchāyāṃ vahati ; ato'rtho dṛśyata ityucyate ; na tu citeraśuddhatvaṃ pariṇāgitvaṃ saṃkrāntirveti ; tadāha—dṛśyeti | tadṛṣayati—atheti | acitirūpāyā buddheścitirūpatā cettacchāyatā, mithyā tārhi ; na ca mithyābhūtena citisaṃbandhena paramārthato dṛśyaṃ dṛśyate ; na ca paramārthenādṛśyamānaṃ dṛśyaṃ draṣṭṛrvā atiriktamastīti śakyate bhaṇitum ; ato draṣṭṛdvaitasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | yo'pi bhīmāṃsako manyate—dṛśyatayaiva vācyavadṛśyasya vyavasthā, na saṃbandhena ; vācyavācakavadraṣṭṛdṛśyabhāva evānayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ, nānya iti yāvat ; darśanaṃ ca ‘nīlamidam’ iti vyatiriktaṃ nīlamupasthāpayati, na ‘ahaṃ nīlam’ ityavyatiriktam ; ato draṣṭṛvyatiriktaṃ dṛśyamiti ; sa vaktavyaḥ—satyaṃ darśanaṃ vyatiriktavastuviṣayam, kiṃ tvekasyaivātmanastena tena nīlādinā vyatiriktarūpeṇa bhrāntyādhyavasāyādavyatireke'pi tadyatirekadarśanamiti | tatsarvamāha— yo'pīti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—darpaṇeti | ātmana iti ; ātmīyasya dehasyetyarthaḥ | tadeva prapañcayati—tathā hīti | yathā vimbāt prativimbamabhinnamapi bhinnaṃ pratyeti taddvityarthaḥ | nanu tatra vādhādastvevam, iha tu kiṃ nopapadyate, yenaivaṃ kalpyate ityata āha—citostviti | citerviviktamatadātmakatayā vā asaṃsṛṣṭaṃ tathā cetyata iti duravagamam ; ataścetyatvānupapadyaiva
[p. 28]
kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | vācyavācakayorabhidhānakriyāgarbhaḥ saṃbandhaḥ, citestu na kācit kriyāstīti vaiṣamyam | ātmāvyatireke ca dṛśyasya śrutirapyastītyāha P. 8-11 — evaṃ ceti | ātmavyatiriktasya sarvasyābhāvādātmaiva sarvasya tattvam ; ata ātmani jñāte sarvaṃ jñātaṃ bhavatīti śruterarthaḥ | na ca sadeva sarvamātmano bhinnābhinnam, tenābhedāṃśenātmani jñāte sarvaṃ jñātaṃ bhavatīti vācyam ; aśrutakalpanāprasaṅgāt, bhedāṃśena cājñānādaikāntikajñātatvānupapatteḥ, bhedābhedayośca nirākariṣyamāṇatvāditi | tasmācchobhanam 'ekam' ityuktamityupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | idānīm 'amṛtam, ajam' iti vyākhyātuṃ tadvyāvartya bauddhamatadvayamupanyasyan mādhyamikamataṃ tāvadāha—kṣaṇiketi | taduktam—“pradīpasyeva nirvāṇaṃ vimokṣastasya tāyinaḥ” iti | viṣayavāsanayā sahitasyāvidyādikleśasya samyagucchedanādityarthaḥ | viṣayavāsanocchedāt grāhyābhāvaḥ, tadabhāvācca grāhakābhāvaḥ ; nāgrāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ saṃbhavatīti vijñānoparamahetuḥ | mādhyamikasya śūnyalakṣaṇaṃ brahma ; atastatprāptireva brahmaprāptiḥ | imamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti saṃsārāt pretya jñānaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ | yogācāramatamāha—anye tviti ; samyagucchinnā sakalā viṣayādivāsanā yasya sa tathoktaḥ, tattvādityarthaḥ | vidhūto vigato viṣayākāreṇopaṣṭhavaḥ saṃsargo yasya ; ata eva viśuddhasya vijñānasyotpādaḥ, tallakṣaṇāṃ brahmaprāptimāhuriti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | nityātmavādināṃ tu doṣamāha—anādīti | janmanāśarahitatve kauṭasthye ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kro doṣa ityata āha—itīti ; itirhetvarthaḥ | tadarthānīti ; apanayopanayaprayojanānītyarthaḥ | apaneyopaneyābhāvameva prapañcayati—tathā hīti | kuta ityāha—avidyāyā iti | na tāvadavidyā nivartyā, vidyātmani tasyā abhāvāt ; na ca vidyopaneyā, tasyāḥ svabhāvasiddhatvāt | na cāvidyāsvabhāvaṃ brahma ; tatrāvidyāpanīyate iti vācyam ; nityasya hi brahmaṇaḥ svabhāvanāśe'nityatvaprasaṅgāt ; pūrvasyāvidyāsvabhāvasyātyāgāt, vidyāsvabhāvasya cānāveyātiśayatvenānāpateḥ vidyāvidyobhayasvabhāvatve ca vidyāyā avāptatvenānupaneyatvāt, avidyāyāśca tatsvabhāvatvenānapaneyatvādityarthaḥ | 'na kiṃcinnivartya'
[p. 29]
bhavāsavyaṃ vā' iti pakṣadvaye'pi parato'nuvartate | iti ye prāhuḥ tānpratyāha— P. 8-21. amṛtamajāmiti | tatra paroktaṃ tāvaddoṣadvayaṃ pariharati—na ceti | nanvanādiravidyā brahmavat tadanucchedyetyata āha—sarveti | sarvavādimiddhatvānnedaṃ vayamevopālabhyāḥ ; dṛśyata ca prāgabhāvasyānāderapyuccheda iti bhāvaḥ | para idānīmāha—neti | nityabrahmasvabhāvasyāvidyārūpasyānuccheda iti brūma iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | sa evāśaṅkya dūṣayati—atheti | tataśca dvaitaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | athaitadbhyādarthāntaramapi sā neṣyate, tato na sā svabhāvaḥ, na cārthāntaramiti nāstyeva ; ataḥ kiṃ nivartyamityāha—na ceti | paraḥ punarāśaṅkyati—atheti | nāvidyā brahmaṇaḥ svabhāvaḥ, tathāpi na dvaitaprasaṅgaḥ, grahaṇābhāvarūpāyā avastutvenānarthāntaratvāt ; na ca tata evānivartyā, sarvapramāṇānāṃ prameyāgrahaṇanidṛcyarthatvādityarthaḥ | tadanivartakatve satyapi pramāṇe prameyājñānamanuvartateti bhāvaḥ | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—tadapīti | kuta ityāha— tacceti | yasmāttaccāgrahaṇātmikāvidyā tadviruddhā taccagrahaṇātmikayā vidyayā nivartyā nānyena ; sā ca brahmasvabhāvatvānnityā ; ato nityanidṛttaivāvidyeti na tannivṛttyarthatvena śāstrādīnāmarthavatteti bhāvaḥ | atha brahmaṇo nāvidyā, kiṃ tu jīvānāṃ brahmaviṣayā ; sā ca teṣāṃ śravaṇādiprayatnalabhyayā brahmaviṣayayāgantukayā vidyayā nivartyate ; ato na nityamavidyānidṛttiprasaṅga ityucyate ; tadapi na, brahmavyatiriktajīvābhyupagame dvaitaprasaṅgāt ityabhiprāyeṇāha— na ceti | astu tarhi brahmaṇyevāvidyetyata āha—brahmaṇīti | dūṣaṇāntarābhidhitsayā punarupanyāsa iti draṣṭavyam | athaitaddoṣabhayādavirodhastayo riṣyeta tatrāha—avipratiṣedha iti | viruddhā hi satī vidyā tāṃ nivartayet, nāviruddhā, rasa iva rūpamiti bhāvaḥ | evamagrahaṇātmikāmavidyāṃ dūṣayitvā viparyayagrahaṇātmikāmidānīṃ dūṣayati—yasya tviti | brahmāsvabhāvatve'rthāntaratvādadvaitavidhāta iti bhāvaḥ | doṣāntaramāha—kasya ceti ; nirāśrayaviparyayagrahaṇāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | na tāvattadviparyayagrahaṇaṃ brahmaṇo'nyasya, tadabhāvān ; nāpi brahmaṇa eva, tasya vidyāsvarūpasya vipratiṣiddhāvidyāśrayatvāyogādityāha—brahmaṇa iti | athāvirodhastatrāha—avipratiṣedha iti |
[p. 30]
kena nivartyeta ? na kenāpītyarthaḥ | vidyāyā aviruddhatvenānivartakatvādanyasya ca tannivartakasyādṛṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | P. 9-11. atra siddhāntamāha—atrocyata iti | ‘na svabhāvo nārthāntaram’ iti svabhāvāsvabhāvapakṣoktadoṣāvanabhyupagamenaiva pariharati | yadi brahmaṇo na svabhāvaḥ, nāpyarthāntaramavidyā, nāstyeva tarhītyatrāha—neti | yadi na brahmaṇo bhinnā, abhinnā vā, na satī, nāpyasatī, kīdṛśī nāma sā ? ityata āha— evamiti ; īdṛśyeva sā, anavasthitaikarūpetyarthaḥ | na ceyamalaukikītyāha— avidyeti ; loka iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | evamādibhiḥ śabdairasau rūḍhā, na sadasadādiśabdairiti darśayituṃ viśiṣṭaparyāyanirdeśaḥ | tatra saccābhāve tāvat pramāṇamāha—svabhāvaśrediti ; tasyāḥ saccābhāve tāvadavidyātvānyathānupapattireva pramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ | tathā brahmasvabhāvatve satyatvāt brahmavadvādhānupapattirapi tatra pramāṇamūhyam | arthāntaratvābhāvastulyadoṣatvāt svabhāvānyatvapakṣatulyatvādvā na sa pṛthagukta iti | atyantāsaccābhāve tu vyavahārāṅgatvānupapattiḥ pramāṇam ; na hyasato vyavahārāṅgatvaṃ dṛṣṭamityabhiprāyeṇāha— atyanteti | upalakṣaṇaṃ cedam ; upalabhbho'pyasataḥ svapuṣpavannopapadyeteti so'pi pramāṇam | nanu vyavahārāṅgaṃ cet tato'rthakriyākāritvāt saccasya kathaṃ na satī ? maivam, dehātmabhāvasyāśeṣavyavahārāṅgasyāpyasaccāt | tajjñānaṃ tatra sadeva vyavahārāṅgamiti cet, tadapi na ; yato na jñānamātraṃ vyavahārāṅgam | nanvavidyā pramāṇena cet pratīyate, tato brahmavat satī ; no cet, svapuṣpavadasatī ; kathamanirvacanīyā ? maivam ; avabhāti hi sā ; na tu pramāṇena, śuktirūpyavat ; ata eva svapuṣpādviśiṣyate | na ca sadasatī, virodhāt ; ato balādāpannamanirvacanīyaṃ sadasaccābhyāṃ rāśyantaram | nanu na tadanyat kiñcidṛṣṭam | mā nāma dṛśyatām, tathāpyuktapramāṇabalāt siddhamanirvacanīyatvamavidyāyā nāpalapanīyam ; na hi bhuvo gandhavatvamanyatrādṛṣṭamiti tatra dṛṣṭamapyapalapyate | nanvanirvacanīyeti nirucyate, kathamanirvacanīyā ? maivam, sadasaccābhyāmiti viśeṣitatvāt | nanvavidyā cedupeyate, avaśyaṃ brahmaṇo bhinnā abhinnā vā syāt, rāśyantarāsaṃbhavāt | maivam, śuktirūpya-
[p. 31]
vādetyuktottaratvāt ; śuktirūpyaṃ hi na śukterbhinnamabhinnaṃ bhinnābhinnaṃ vā ghaṭate ; tasmādasti prakārāntarasaṃbhava iti | sarvamūrīkṛtyopasaṃharati—tasmāditi | P. 9-14. iyaṃ cedṛśī sarvaireva vādibhirabhyupeyā ; tena na vayamevopālabhyā iti yuktivalena sarvānabhyupagamayati—sarveti | etadeva prapañcayati—tathā hīti | sarvaśūnyavādināṃ mādhyamikānāṃ yādṛśaṃ jagadṛśyate tādṛśaṃ cet sat, tataḥ paramārthatvānnāvidyā ; athātyantamasat, tataḥ khapuṣpavat vyavahārāṅgaṃ na syāt ; nāvabhāseta | tathā jñānamātravādino yogācārasya bāhyārthāvabhāsānapahvanāttathā satye nārthāpahvanaḥ syāt ; atyantāsatye khapuṣpavadvyavahārāṅgatvam | bāhyārthavādino'pi patañjaleḥ “anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmabuddhiravidyā” itīyaṃ nityādibuddhirmithyeṣṭā ; sā pratibhāsamānanityatvādyarthasya satyatve na mithyā syāt ; asatyatve na tannivandhano vyavahāraḥ syāt ; ataḥ sarvairanirvacanīyaivāvidyābhyupetavyetyarthaḥ | nanu śuktirajatādivibhrame rajatamasat, na tu tajjñānam ; taccāvidyā ; ataḥ satyeva sā ; kathamanirvacanīyatā ? iti codayati—syādetaditi | siddhāntī tvāha—naitaditi | asatyavabhāsamāne'rthe, tadavabhāso'pi satyatvena durnirūpaḥ ; kiṃ tvavabhāsamātraṃ satyaṃ syāt ; tadavabhāsa ityapi bhrāntireva ; tasyaivābhāvāt ; na kevalamarthaḥ, api tvarthābhāso'pītyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ syāt—arthānubandharahitamanubhavamātraṃ no nityaṃ sadeva ; tasya ca bhrāntenārthānubandhenāvidyātvam, na svataḥ ; asatyārthānubandharūpeṇa na satīti svataḥ satyapi ‘na satī’ ityucyata iti | evamanirvacanīyatvamākṣiptaṃ samādhāya punarupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | tadeva draṣṭayati—ata iti | kuta ityāha— svabhāvāhānāditi ! yadyavidyā satsvabhāvā syāt, na svabhāvaṃ jahyādagnirivīṣṇyam | na ca tathābhūtasyānyathābhāvaḥ ; anyathābhūtasya cānyathābhāve'navasthā syāt ; ato na bādhakajñānenānyathākriyeta | athāsatī, tataḥ svata eva niścatatvānna tena nivartyetetyarthaḥ | evamanirvacanīyatvamavidyāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāpya ‘satye dvaitāpattiḥ, asatye brahmasvabhāvatve ca nivartanīyābhāvaḥ’ iti paroktaṃ doṣadvayaṃ pariharati—evamiti | idānīṃ kasyāvidyeti yaduktaṃ tadanubhāṣya tatrottaramāha—yatviti | atra paraśrodayati—nanviti | brahmaṇaśra nāvidyā, vidyāsvabhāvatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
[p. 32]
P 10-4. kuta ityāha—evaṃ hīti| ‘jīvenātmanā’ ityātmaśabdena brahmavacinā sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ śrūyate; na ca tayorbhede ghaṭapaṭayoriva tat syāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā “kṣetrajñaṃ cāpi māṃ viddhi” ityādivirodhaḥ, dvaitāpattiśca tayorbhede syādityūhanīyam | siddhāntavādī tvāha—satyamiti; na bhidyata ityanuvartate | kathaṃ nāma bhidyata ityāha—kalpanayeti; ayathārthabuddherityarthaḥ | paramārthenābhinnā api brahmaṇo jīvāḥ kalpanayā mithyābuddhyo vimbaprativimbavaccandravacca tato bhidyante; evaṃ ca bhedamātramatra kālpanikam, na jīvavastvevetyuktaṃ bhavati | ata eva “taccamasi” iti bheda eva bādhyate, na tu ‘taccamasi’ iti jīva iti | punaśrodayati—kasyeti | kasya kalpanā jīvaparayorbhediketyarthaḥ | tatra matabhedena parihāradvayaṃ vivakṣurekaṃ tāvadāha— atreti | yadyavidyā nāma vastu syāt tato nāsiddhā jīvavastusiddhaye'lam, jīvaṃ ca vinā na tatsiddhiḥ itītaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ syāt; māyāmātraṃ tu sā; ato na doṣa ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—na hīti | kuta ityāha— anupapannārthaiva hīti | anupapannaviṣayaiva sā māyā; upapannārthatve tu tasyā yathārthatvāt svarūpahānireva syāt; ato'nupapannārthatvaṃ tasyā bhūṣaṇam, na tu dūṣaṇamityarthaḥ | parihārāntaramāha—anye tviti | ahaṃkārādikalpanādatastadavacchinnastadguṇasāro ghaṭākāśa iva jīvaḥ, tatastatkalpaneti bījāṅkuravadanāditvaṃ mantavyam | kāraṇavidyā tvanādiḥ | anāditvameva svayūthayoktyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | avidyā upādānakāraṇaṃ yasya bhedaprapañcasya tadvādibhirityarthaḥ | tatrānāditvasya phalamāha—tutreti | tathā prasaṅgoktasyāprayojanatvasya phalamāha—aprayojanatvānneti; ‘bhidyamānasya prapañcasya sṛṣṭau kiṃ prayojanam?’ iti yaḥ paryanuyogaḥ tasyāvakāśo na bhavatītyarthaḥ | tatra paryanuyogaṃ tāvadāha—yadeka iti | yadyapi sṛṣṭeḥ prāgapyanugrāhyāḥ sabījāvasthā jīvāḥ santi, anyathā punaḥ sṛṣṭyabhāvaprasaṅgāt; tathāpyupādhilaye brahmaṇi līnā iveti yathādṛṣṭapṛthagbhūtānugrāhyābhāvauktaḥ | itaśca na parānugrahaḥ prayājenamityāha—duḥkhottaratvācceti | anugrahāddhi mukhinameva sṛjet, na duḥkhinam;
[p. 33]
duḥkhabahulā ca sṛṣṭirityarthaḥ ; taduktam—“sṛjecca śubhamevaikamanukampāprayojitaḥ” iti | na ca svopayogi kiñcitprayojanamastītyāha—neti | P. 10-18 āptakāmatvāditi ; “āptakāmaḥ ātmakāmaḥ” iti śruterāptāśeṣakāmyatvena kṛtārthatvādityarthaḥ | taduktam—“krīḍārthāyāṃ pravṛttau ca vihanyeta kṛtārthatā” iti | krīḍādirityādiśabdena kutūhalamucyate | evaṃ paryanuyogamuktvā tasyānavakāśatvamāha—tasyeti | kuta ityāha—na hīti | tadeva laukikadṛṣṭāntairdravyati—neti | prakṛtivikārapakṣokto'pi doṣo nāsmatpakṣamāskandatītyāha—tatheti ; vimalasyāpi vyomnastalamalinādibhramadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | avidyākṛtā sṛṣṭiraprayojanetyuktam ; idānīmabhyupagamyaiva krīḍārthā pravṛttiṃ pūrvoktadoṣābhāvamāha—api ceti | krīḍārthapravṛttiḥ ; krīḍā bhavatītyadhyāhāryam | ucchāsāditi ; protsāhādityarthaḥ | prārthaneti ; kāmaprārthanāvatā kṛtārthapravṛttireva krīḍocyate, na kāryārtheti bhāvaḥ | nanu sukhiduḥkhidṛṣṭe rāgadveṣahetutvena draṣṭṛtvādīśvarasya tadvatvena vaiṣamyaṃ syāt ; tathā duḥkhinaḥ sṛjato nairvṛṇyaṃ ca syādityata āha—na ceti | kuta ityāha—na hīti | māyāṃ karotīti māyākaraḥ śāmbarikaḥ, tasya saṃpūrṇamasaṃpūrṇāṅgaṃ ca rudrādiprapañcaṃ darśayato yathā na rāgadveṣau tatheti svapakṣe dṛṣṭāntaḥ | satyaprapañcavādipakṣe'pi tamāha—citreti | pustaṃ lekhyam ; ‘kṛtām’ iti kṛtraḥ kiṃvantāt ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanam | vikalāvikalatvamasaṃpūrṇasaṃpūrṇāṅgatvam | citrāṇītyupalakṣaṇam, lekhyamāpaṃ draṣṭavyam | pratikṛtiḥ pratirūpakam | parihārāntaramāha—karmeti | parjanyasthānīyo hīśvaraḥ sādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇam | sukhiduḥkhibhedastu svakarmabhedāddhījabhedādivāṅkurabhedaḥ | tasmānna tasya vaiṣamyādidoṣa ityarthaḥ | nanveva karmāśayaṃpāratanvyāt svātantryalakṣaṇamaiśvaryaṃ na syādityata āha—na ceti | etaduktaṃ bhavati— nānyāpekṣitvamātreṇānīśvaratvaṃ syāt ; na hi loke sevāviśeṣāpekṣaḥ phalaṃ prayacchan prabhuraprabhuḥ ; āśraya tadapekṣo'pi yathā phalaṃ dātuṃ prabhavatīti sa prabhuḥ, netaraḥ ; tathā karmāpekṣo'pi sa eva phalaṃ dātumīṣṭe netara iti tasyaiveśvaratvamiti | “puruṣasya ca śuddhasya nāśuddhā vikṛtiḥ” iti cādyaṃ 3
[p. 34]
māyikīṃ sṛṣṭimāśritya vikārapakṣānabhyupagamena pūrvaṃ parihatam; adhunā tadabhyupagame'pi naiṣa doṣaḥ, kimutāsmākaṃ māyāvādināmityabhiprāyeṇāha— P. 11-6. tatheti | prakṛtivikārayoratyantamavailakṣaṇye prakṛtimātravat prakṛtivikārabhāvabhedo na syāt; ato'sti tayorvailakṣaṇyam; evaṃ ca śuddhādapyaśuddhāvikṛtirbhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭaṃ cānyatrāpi prakṛtivikārayorvailakṣaṇyam, tadvadihāpi syādityabhiprāyeṇāha—dravāṇamiti | acetanacetanau cetanānadhiṣṭhitādhiṣṭhitau draṣṭavyau | idānīmadvaitimate punaśrodayati—nanviti | viśuddhasvabhāvāḥ; vimalavidyāsvabhāvā ityarthaḥ | siddhāntī tu śuddhādabhinnamapyaśuddhaṃ bhātyeveti darśayannāha—vārtamiti; asāramityarthaḥ; vārtātaāgataṃ vārtam; niṣpramāṇakamityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—na tāvaditi | kathaṃ punarvimbātpratibimbaṃ na bhinnam? sāvayavasyāvayavasaṃyogamantareṇānupapatteḥ | na na tasyāvayavā dṛśyante | na ca kalpyā, anyathāpi tatpratipatyupapatteḥ | sāvayavasya ca nirantare darpaṇe sthityanupapatteḥ; bimbarūpapratyabhijñānācca; sparśanena cānupalabdheḥ; sāvayavasya avayavatatsaṃyoganāśamantareṇānāśāt; asya ca bimbasaṃnidhimātreṇābhāsāt; tasmādvimbameva bhrāntyā bhinnaṃ bhātīti sthitam | nanu śuddhādabhinnasyāśuddhistaccato na ghaṭate; pratibimbe tu bhrāntiḥ setyāśaṅkya ihāpi bhrāntirityāha—vibhrama iti | bhrāntitvameva draḍhayati—anyatheti | taccatastu jīvānāmaśuddhasvabhāvatve śuddhirna syāditi 'svabhāvāhānāt' ityatroktamityarthaḥ | atra punaśrodayati—syāditi | aikātmyavādinastaddhyatiriktaāgantuko heturnāstīti bhāvaḥ | siddhāntī tvāha—kimatrāgantukenānyena hetunā? āgantuke hi pratibimbādibhrame so'pekṣyeta; anādistvayaṃ jīvabhramaḥ; ataścānādestaccāgrahaṇādevopapatterāgantukabāhyahetvanveṣaṇamayuktapityabhiprāyeṇāha—anādāviti | anāditvāccāvidyāpravṛttihetvabhāvacodyamapi parihatamityāha—tathā ceti | atra codayati—nanviti | hetucchedāddhi hetu maducchedo dṛṣṭaḥ; avidyā tvanāditvāt svābhāvikīti nirhetuḥ; ataḥ kathamucchidyetetyarthaḥ | siddhāntī tu svābhāvikyapyucchidyate tāvadavidyā; anyathā
[p. 35]
tanniṣṭanyarthāni śāstrāṇi vyarthāni syurityamiprāyeṇāha—sarveti | dṛśyate ca P.11-17. svābhāvikasyāpyuccheda ityāha—api ceti | parastu śyāmatādivaiṣamyamāpādayan codayati—nanviti | pratyayaḥ ; heturityarthaḥ | na vidyate āgantuko dvitīyo'rtho yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tadbhāvāt ; vilakṣaṇapratyayopanipātābhāvādityarthaḥ | kuto niṣṭhattiḥ ? avidyāyā ityadhyāhāryam | nanu mā mṛdāgantuko hetuḥ ; ātmano vidyātmakatvāt tatsamāvabhūtaiva vidyā avidyāṃ nivartayiṣyatītyata āha—na khalviti | vidyāvidye cedakatrātmanīcchāsi, tatastayoḥ svabhāvāvasthānādavirodhe na nivartyanivartakabhāvaḥ ; virodhe vā vidyayātmanyavidyā nityanirasteti tannibandhanatvābhāvānnityamuktaṃ jagat syādityarthaḥ | avidyāyā iti kākākṣivadubhayatra saṃbadhyate | tayeti ; vidyayetyarthaḥ | śravaṇādijanyamāgantukaṃ vidyāntaramavidyāṃ nivartayiṣyatīti cedata āha na ceti | āgantukasyāpi vidyāntarasya brahmasvabhāvatvāt naikātmyahāniriti cedata āha—āgantuketi | nānityasya nityasvabhāvatvaṃ ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | atraivārthaṃ paroktaṃ paṭhati—taduktamiti | atra siddhāntamāha—atreti | pratibimbadṛṣṭāntenoktamityarthaḥ | kasmānna brahmaṇa ityāha—tadbhīti | avidyānivartaka āgantuko'rtho'sya nāstītyarthaḥ | yadvā anāgantukārthamanādheyātiśayamityarthaḥ | tatra nityaprakāśatvena svābhāvikyavidyā nāstītyuktam ; atrānāgantukārthatvena cāgantukyapi sā nāstītyuktamiti vivekaḥ | vidyāsvabhāve brahmaṇyavidyākalluṣitatvaṃ nāstītyuktam ; yadi ca syāttato mahān doṣaḥ syādityāha—anyatheti | brahmabhūyaṃ gatasyeti ; brahmabhāvaṃ prāptasyetyarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityata āha—tatreti | brahmaiva saṃsarati mucyate ca, na jīvā iti ca teṣāṃ matam ; tadanubhāṣya dūṣayati—arthāt | brahmaiva ; na jīvā ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—yata iti | abhedadarśanādbrahmaṇi mukte, anyasya bhedadraṣṭurbaddhasyābhāvātsarvabhedapratyastamaye yugapat sarvamokṣaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | svapakṣamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | nanvastu jīvānāmavidyā ; tathāpyaikātmye vilakṣaṇahetvabhāvāt kathamavidyānivṛttiriti pūrvoktamāśaṅkya pariharati—teṣāmiti | vilakṣaṇasatyahetvabhāve'pi jīvānā 35
[p. 36]
mavidyākaluṣāṇāmavidyākalpitaśravaṇādilakṣaṇahetukavidyodayādavidyānivṛttirdhaṭata ityarthaḥ | vilakṣaṇaḥ pratyayaḥ kāraṇaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ, vilakṣaṇapratyayaśrāsau vidyodayaśreti karmadhārayaḥ; vilakṣaṇaśrāsau pratyayavidyodayaśreti vā | P. 12-8. nanu jīvānāmapi vidyā naitīrgaveyasti, ataḥ saivāvidyānivartikā, nāgantukī vilakṣaṇetyata āha—na hīti | avidyāmaya eva jīva ityucyata ityarthaḥ | nanu vidyātsabhāvād brahmaṇo jīvānāmabhedāt katha mavidyāmayatvamityata āha—avyatireke'pīti | yathā bimbaṃ mukhādyavadātaṃ tatpratibimbaṃ ca kṛpāṇādāvanavadātamevaṃ brahmaṇo vidyātmatve'pi jīvānāmavidyeti vyavasthetyarthaḥ | nanu na tāvadbrahmavidyānivartakam, nityanivṛttiprasaṅgāt; na cāvidyāntaram, avidyātvāviśeṣeṇāpratiyogitvāt; nivartakānivṛttiprasaṅgācca, nivartakāntarakalpanāyāṃ cānavatthānāt; na ca brahmāvidyābhyāmanyannivartakaṃ travāstītyabhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati—keneti | siddhāntī tu rajastvāviśeṣe'pi yathā katakarajo rajo'ntarapratiyogi tannivartakaṃ ca bhavati tathā avidyātvāviśeṣe'pi śravaṇādyavidyā prapañcāvidyāyāḥ pratiyoginī niva rtikā ca bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyeṇāha—śravaṇeti | tatra śravaṇamananayoḥ śābdavidyātpattikrameṇa, brahmacaryādeśca kalpamakṣayakrameṇa, dhyānādeścādvyātmaprakāśābhivyaktikrameṇāvidyānivṛttyupāyatvamiti vivekaḥ | śravaṇaṃ vedāntasya, mananaṃ tarkeṇa tadarthānusandhānam, dhyānābhyāsastadarthānucintanāvṛttiḥ | paraśca tulyajātīyasya śravaṇādeḥ pratiyogitvāsaṃbhavābhiprāyeṇa punaḥ pṛcchani— kathamiti | siddhāntī tu kimatra vaktavyam? avidyātvāviśeṣe'pi śravaṇamananapūrvako na karṇamūlopadeśapūrvako yo'yaṃ “sa eṣa neti neti” iti vīpsayā pratiṣiddhākhilabhidyamānaprapañce dhyānābhyāsaḥ so'ta eva prapañcadarśana pratiyogī; atastannivartayatītyāha—yo'yamiti | pratiṣiddho'khilabhedaprapañco yasmin sa tathoktaḥ | dehādīnāmātmatvapratiṣedho'yam, nākhilaprapañcapratiṣedha iti cet, na; dehāderaśrutatvādaśrutakalpanāpramāṇābhāvācca | tasmātpratiṣedhyāpekṣāyāmagṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvādakhilaprapañcapratiṣedha evāyamiti sūktam—pratiṣiddhākhilabhedaprapañca iti | nanvevamapi śravaṇāderbhedadarśananivartakasya nivartakā-
