ntarānupapatteranivṛttāvanirmokṣaprasaṅga iti parābhiprāyamāśaṅkya pariharāti—sa 1.12-14 ceti | sāmānyeneti ; aviśeṣeṇetyarthaḥ | ‘maḥ’ iti śravaṇādipūrvo dhyānābhyāsaḥ parāmṛśyate | etadevānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ prapañcayati—na hīti | yadi śrotṛśravaṇādibhedaṃ vihāya bhedāntarapravilāpakāḥ śravaṇādayaḥ syuḥ, tataḥ syādeṣa doṣaḥ ; te viśeṣeṇa sarvabhedān pravilāpayantastadantargatatvādātmānamapi pravilāpayanti ; tataśca na teṣāmanivṛttiḥ ; nāpi nivartakāntarāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ ; tataśca nānirmokṣadoṣa ityāha—tathā ceti | svaccha iti svabhāvanirmalatvamāha | pariśuddha iti ; apagatāgantukāvidyākāluṣya ityarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti ; yathā dravyaviśeṣasya katakādeścūrṇarūpaṃ rajaḥ kartṛ trākṣiptaṃ sat rajo'ntarāṇi saṃharat svayaṃ ca saṃhiyamāṇaṃ rajaḥ saṃparkakaluṣamudakaṃ karmamūtaṃ svarūpāvasthāṃ nayatītyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭāntamuktvā dārṣṭāntike'pi yojayati—evamiti | bhedadarśanatvena viśeṣābhāvāt tadvat śravaṇādigate ca bhede pravilīyamāne jīvaḥ svacche svarūpe'vatiṣṭhata ityarthaḥ | nanu brahmaṇo vibhakto jīvo'vidyāvilaye svarūpastho'pi tathaivāvatiṣṭhate ; ato na brahmībhāvo'sya syāt ; śrūyate cāsau—“brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati” ityāśaṅkyāha —avidyayeti | yadi brahmaṇo jīvastaccato bhinnaḥ syāt tataḥ syādevam ; kiṃ tvavidyayā ; ato vidyayāvidyānivṛttau yathābrahmarūpameva bhavatītyarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | bhede ; vidāraṇa ityarthaḥ | tadākāśa iti ; ghaṭākāśa ityarthaḥ | para idānīṃ vijātīyatvaprayuktamuṣṇaśītādiṣu nivartakatvaṃ dṛṣṭamityabhimānena punaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—syāditi | śravaṇādibhedena prapañcaḥ kathaṃ nivartate ? avidyātvāviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ | siddhāntau tu sajātīyasyāpi rajasaḥ pratiyogino rajontaranivartakatvaṃ dṛṣṭam ; vijānīyasyāpi rūparasāderapratiyogino na dṛṣṭam ; ataḥ śītoṣṇādāvapi pratiyogitvaprayuktameva nivartakatvam ; tacca śravaṇādibhedasyāvidyākṛtatve'pi vyaktamastītyāha—bhedeti | pratipakṣatvamāha—vyaktamiti | śravaṇādeḥ svayaṃ nivṛttau sajātīyasya ca nivartakatve dṛṣṭāntadvayamaparamāha—yathā ceti | yadyapi payo jāṭhareṇāgninā jīryati, tathāpi payontaramanapekṣya jīryatītyetāvatā sāmyena svayamityuktam ;
[p. 38]
na hi dṛṣṭāntena sarvathā sāmyaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | śravaṇādyavidyaivāvidyāṃ P. 13-2. nivartayatītīmaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—yathoktamiti | mantrārthaṃ spaṣṭayati— etaditi | vidyā-viśuddhavijñānaṃ nityam; avidyā śravaṇādi; te hyupāyopeyabhāvena sahite saṃbaddhe iti yathākramamabhisaṃbandha iti | nanūpeyā cedvidyā, sādhyā terhi ; na hyasādhyasyopeyatvaṃ ghaṭate; ataścānityatvamityāśaṅkya mantrasyottarārthaṃ yojayati—sādhyeti | tadyācaṣṭe—eṣa iti | nāvidyā vidyāṃ janayati, kiṃ tvavidyāniṣṭhastim; vidyāṃ tu vyanakti, tena vyaṅgyatathāpeyatvaṃ vidyāyāḥ, na janyatayā; ato nānityatvamityarthaḥ | etaccātmasvabhāvabhūtanityavidyābhiprāyeṇoktam | nanu “mṛtyuṃ tītvā” iti mṛtyuniṣṭhattiḥ śrūyate, nāvidyāniṣṭhattiḥ; kathamavidyā nivartyata ityucyeta ? ityāśaṅkyāha— mṛtyuriti | caturthaṃ mantrapādaṃ yojayati—tasyāmiti | ‘vidyayā’ iti ityaṃbhūtalakṣaṇe tṛtīyā | svarūpāvasthānāmityamṛtāvivaraṇam | nanvavidyā cedavidyāṃ nivartayati na tataḥ svarūpalabhāya prayatitavyamityāśaṅkyāha—sphaṭiketi; yathā sphaṭikamaṇerjapādyupādhyapanayāya prayatnādeva tannibandhanarāgatyāgāt prayatnāntaramantareṇa svarūpe'vasthānaṃ tadvadihāpītyarthaḥ | atra kecidāhuḥ—“kurvanneveha karmāṇi” ityato mantrāt karma prakṛtamavidyāśabdena vidyāparyudāsenocyate; tasmāt jñānakarmasamuccayaparo'yaṃ mantra iti | tadasat; ‘avidyā’ iti hi nanśrutiraparyantaṃ svaśaktyā vidyāviruddhamasamyagjñānamajñānaṃ cāha, nānyamātram | atra śrutiśca prakaraṇādbalīyatī | tasmānna jñānakarmasamuccayaparo'yaṃ mantra iti | mantrasyārthāntaramāha—anya iti | avidyā prapañcadarśanaṃ na vidyayā kūṭasthātmaprakāśena rahitamastītyarthaḥ | etadeva darśayati—tathā hīti | na jaḍasya svataḥ prakāśaḥ saṃbhavati; na ca parataḥ, svabhāvāhānāt; na cātmasthena prakāśāntareṇa svaprakāśena, avikāritvādātmanaḥ | na ca prakāśenāsaṃbaddhasya prakāśa iti bhāvaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | tathā tatheti; tena tena prapañcarūpeṇetyarthaḥ | etadeva śrutyā draḍhayati—yatheti | atra kaścidāha—
[p. 39]
sarvaśabdo'yaṃ prakṛtaviṣayaḥ ; prakṛtaṃ cātra “ na tatra sūryo bhāti na candratārakam ” ityataḥ sūryādi ; tenāyamarthaḥ—tasya brahmaṇo bhāsā prabhāveṇa sarvabhidaṃ sūryādi bhāti vidyotata iti, na tu tatprakāśena sarvaṃ jagatprakāśata iti | tadayuktam ; bhāḥśabdo hi prakāśavacanaḥ ; prabhāvaśaktyādivācitve satyalaukiko'rthaḥ kalpitaḥ syāt ; tasmāt tasya bhāsā prakāśena sarvaṃ prakāśata ityayamevārthaḥ | nanvevaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśātmakatvāt ‘tasya’ iti vyatireko na ghaṭate | ‘rāhoḥ śiraḥ’ itivadupacārādupapatteḥ | nanvevamapi prakṛtatvātsūryādireva brahmaprakāśyatvamuktaṃ syāt, na sarvasya jagataḥ | na ; tatsāmānyāditareṣu tathātvāt, brahmakṣatrādivadupalakṣaṇopapatteśca | sarvaśabdaśca svarasenāśeṣavacanaḥ ; sa svārthānupapattyā saṃkocyamānaḥ prakaraṇādibhirniyantyata ; yathā—‘sarvamannaṃ muktam’ iti | na tviha tatheti śrutasaṃkoco na yuktaḥ | uktaḥ para eva prakāśastathā tathā prakāśata iti sa kiṃ svabhāvātpariṇāmādvā ? netyāha—kiṃ tviti | atrārthe'bhiyuktavākyamāha—yatheti | P. 13-14. sarvaṃ darśanaṃ sarvaṃ jñānam ; anyūnaṃ saṃpūrṇam, paraprakāśasvarūpamiti yāvat | avikalpaṃ vidyadādivikalparāhitaṃ vidyadādirūpeṇa vikalpyamānamiva tu ayamāntaraḥ dehāntargato jīvo manyata ityarthaḥ yadvā vikalpo'sadarthajñānam, tanna bhavatītyavikalpam, asantaṃ vikalpamivāsadarthaṃ jñānamiva manyata ityarthaḥ | pūrvaṃ vidyāvidyayorupāyopeyabhāvena sahabhāva uktaḥ, iha tvavidyāyāḥ prapañcadarśanasya vidyānubaddhatveneti vivekaḥ | nanu jīvānāmavidyā, na brahmaṇa iti pūrvamuktam ; kathamidānīṃ tasyaivāvidyānubaddhatvamucyate ? pūrvaṃ brahmasvarūpājñātṛtvena jīvānāmavidyetyuktam ; adhunā tu niradhiṣṭhānavibhramānupapatterajñātvena śuktivat brahmaṇaḥ kāryāvidyānubaddhatvamucyata ityavirodhaḥ | evaṃ prapañcāvidyāyā nityavidyāyā sahabhāvamuktvā śravaṇādividyāyā avidyayā sahabhāvamāha—tatheti | vidyāvidyayoranyonyasahabhāva ityuktaṃ bhavati | ata evoktam—‘ubhayaṃ saha’ iti | kuta ityāha—śrotriti | evaṃ mantrasya pūrvārdhaṃ vyākhyāya tasyottarārdhaṃ vyācaṣṭe—tatheti | śravaṇādivibhāgāvidyayā nityavidyāgatāmavidyāṃ prapañcadarśanaṃ tīrtvetyarthaḥ | nanvavidyātve
[p. 40]
tulye ko viśeṣaḥ, ye^na^ sā jagato bhedadarśanamavidyāṃ bhāṣetatyāśaṅkya viśeṣa pa. 13-17. uktaḥ—vidyāpratyāsannayeti | aikātmyaśravaṇādividyāyāṃ tatsahabhāvena nityaprakāśarūpāyāṃ cābhivyañjakatvena śrotṛśravaṇādibhedāvidyā pratyāsanneti viśeṣaḥ | vidyālakṣaṇa iti ; nityaprakāśalakṣaṇa ityarthaḥ | tannivṛttāviti ; kāluṣyanivṛttāvityarthaḥ | atredānīmaikātmye bhedasyāsatyatvāt śrotrādibhedāśrayāḥ śravaṇādayo'pyasatyāḥ ; tataśca kathamavidyānivṛttikāryaṃ kuryuḥ ? iti paraśvodayati — syāditi | marumarīcikājalādau snānādikāryādarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | śravaṇāderasatyācca brahmapratipattirasatyā syādityāha—asatyācceti | vahnerityadhyāhatya pratipattipadenānuṣañjanīyam | siddhāntī tvasatyādapi satyaṃ kāryaṃ satyārthapratipattiśca dṛṣṭetyāha—ucyata iti | śāmbarikadarśitā ruyādimāyā prīteḥ, vyāghrādimāyā ca bhayasya nimittamiti vivekaḥ | satyapratipatterityatra nimittamityanuṣañjanīyam ; idamudāhṛtam | rekhā gavayādirūpeṇāsatyāpi rekhāsvarūpeṇa tāvat satyā ; śravaṇādyupāyāstu svarūpeṇāpyasatyā iti viśeṣaṃ darśayan paraḥ punaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—syādetaditi | siddhāntī tu rekhāsvarūpeṇa satyā api rekhāgavayādayo yena gavādirūpeṇa satyagavayādipratipādakāstadūpamasatyameva ; rekhārūpasatyatvaṃ tu satyagavayādipratipattikāryānupayogyakiñcitkaramevetyaviśeṣamāha—ucyata iti | yadi tu kenacidrūpeṇa satyatvamantareṇa na tuṣyati bhavān, tadapyastītyāha—api ceti | brahmaivāvidyayā śravaṇādirūpeṇa gṛhītaṃ śuddhabrahmaprāptāvupāya ityarthaḥ | rekhādayo'pi svarūpeṇa santo'pi varṇādināvidyādirūpeṇaiva varṇādīnāṃ gamakāḥ, na svarūpeṇeti sarvathā sāmyaṃ prakaṭayati—yatheti | atra paramatamupanyasya dūṣayati— yo'pīti | lokavirodhaṃ prakaṭayati—tathā hīti | asatyācca satyārthapratipattāvudāharaṇāntaramāha—tatheti | bahiḥ pratibimbalāñchitamādarśamupalambhya gṛhe bimbānumānaṃ na mṛṣetyarthaḥ | atraivodāharaṇāntaramāha—śabdācceti | nityatvaṃ sarvadā sattvam ; tacca dīrghatvāderdvikālatvādilakṣaṇasyāsatyatvadyotanārthamuktamiti mantavyam | evamasatyāsatyakāryārthapratipatterbhedenodāharaṇāni dattvā adhunobhayatrāpyekamudāharaṇamityāha—tatheti | satyapyahidaṃśe kasya-
[p. 41]
cinmaraṇamūrcchayoradarśanādanaikāntatvaṃ hetorāśaṅkya viśinaṣṭi—kālādīti | P.14-15. tenedṛśamidamanumāt ‘mariṣyatyayaṃ mūrcchā vāpatsyate, rāhoḥ paṅkto kāle valmīkādau deśe daṣṭatvāt pūrvadṛṣṭadaṣṭavaditi dṛṣṭāntaviśeṣaṇatve hetutvamunneyam | evaṃ paroktaṃ doṣaṃ svapakṣe parihṛtya parasmai cāpādayannāha—yasyaiveti | nanu jñānamevaitābhirapaneṣyate; ataḥ kathamapaneyābhāva ityāśaṅkyāha— kṣaṇikasyeti | jñānāpanayo hi tadvināśaḥ, sa ca tasya kṣaṇikasya svabhāvasiddha evetyarthaḥ | nanu pūrvamaśuddhasya jñānasya viśuddhiratiśayastābhirupaneṣyate; ataḥ kathamupaneyābhāvaḥ ? ityāśaṅkyāha—anadheyeti | dvitīye kṣaṇe jñānasya śuddhirjanyā, na ca tadastīti bhāvaḥ | yadvā dvitīye kṣaṇe kiñcidapaneyamupaneyaṃ vā; na ca kṣaṇikasya so'sti, svarasena vināśāt | kiṃ ca tathābhūtasyaivāsaṃbhavāt; kecidatiśayaṃ vikāramādhāya kiñcidupaneyamapaneyaṃ vā; na ca kṣaṇikasya kṣaṇikatvādevātiśayādhānamastītyarthaḥ | na caikasmin kṣaṇe'tiśayānatiśayau saṃbhavataḥ, virodhāt ityāha—ekasminniti | na caikasminneva kṣaṇe pūrvāparārdhabhedena tayoravirodhaḥ, ekasmin kṣaṇe pūrvāparārdhabhedāsaṃbhavādityāha—kṣaṇasyeti | yastu viśuddhāt kṣaṇāt viśuddhasyaiva kṣaṇāntarasyodayamāha tasyānyo baddho'nyo mucyata iti durghaṭamāpadyate | evaṃ saṃtāninyupaneyāsaṃbhavamuktvā prati saṃtatāvāśaṅkyati—yadīti | sthāyinī hi saṃtatiriti bhāvaḥ | taddūṣayati—tadasaditi | saṃsāriṇo mokṣe'dhikāraḥ; sa ca kartṛtvamoktṛtvalakṣaṇaḥ; na ca saṃtatistādṛśī, avastutvāt; ato'syā mokṣe'nadhikārādupaneyāpaneyāvakiñcitkarāviti bhāvaḥ | avastutvaṃ ca saṃtānasya saṃtānibhyo vyatirekānabhyupagamāditi draṣṭavyam | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—kathaṃ ceti; khapuṣpādāvadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | atraiva paramatamāśaṅkayati—atheti | etaddūṣayati—kalpitastahāti | svoktamāha—uktamiti | parasmādātmano bhinnatayā kalpito jīvaḥ, tadviṣayau bandhamokṣau, na parasmin paramātmani satya iti ‘tasmādavidyayā jīvāḥ’ ityādāvuktamityarthaḥ | evaṃ saṃsāramokṣāvapi kalpitaviṣayatvāt kalpitāvityuktaṃ bhavati | kiṃ cānā-
[p. 42]
dinidhanasyātmano muktisaṃsārī na saṃbhavata iti kṣaṇikapakṣo'bhyupagataḥ; taṃ cābhyupagamyāpi cedanādinidhanāyāḥ saṃtatereva tāviṣṭau, tata ātmanā P. 15-2. kimaparāddham, yena tasya tau neṣṭau? ityabhiprāyeṇāha—api ceti | anādinidhanatvameva saṃtaterupapādayati—tathā hīti | saṃtaternotpādaḥ, saṃsārasyānāditvāt; utpāde hi na so'nādiḥ syāt | avastutvācca saṃtaternotpādaḥ; na cocchedaḥ, avastutvādeva; na hyavastuno janmocchedau sta ityarthaḥ | itaśca noccheda ityāha—antyeti | anupapattimāha—sa hīti | apara āha—ārambhata iti | yogino'ntyakṣaṇasya sarvaviṣayatvena sarvajñajñānārthakriyākāritvānnāsattvam; ato na tadasattvāt pūrvakṣaṇānāmasattvam; ato na saṃtānābhāvaḥ | na ca tasyānucchedaḥ, saṃtānāntare sati svasaṃtatyucchedāditi bhāvaḥ | siddhāntī tu pūrvamapi hetuphalamāvānuvṛttāvekasaṃtatirāsīt; idānīmapi cedyogino'ntyakṣaṇasya sarvajñajñānasya ca hetuphalamāvo'sti, na tarhi saṃtānāntaratvam; na hi hetuphalaparaṃparāmantareṇānyadekasaṃtaterhetuḥ; ato na saṃtatyucchedaḥ; ataśca na tallakṣaṇo mokṣa ityabhiprāyeṇāha—satīti | paraḥ punarviśeṣamāha—neti | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti | yogijñānasaṃtatau carama ityarthaḥ | yadi nopādānaṃ kiṃ tarhi sa ityāha—ālambaneti | pratyayaḥ kāraṇam; viṣayarūpaṃ kāraṇamityarthaḥ | upādānaṃ tu samanantarapūrvabhāvi kāraṇamucyata ityāha—samanantareti | tatra sarvajñajñāne'nya eva svasaṃtānavartī pūrvakṣaṇaḥ, caramastu sarvajñajñānagrāhyatvāt viṣayarūpaṃ kāraṇamityāha— sveti | siddhāntī tu na tāvadyadātmakaṃ kāryaṃ tadupādānameva, nāpi yadāśrayaḥ; kṣaṇānāṃ parasparamanātmatvādanāśrayatvācca; yadi tulyajātīyamupādānam, prāptaṃ tarhi yogijñānaṃ sarvajñajñānasya jñānamātrarūpatayā tulyatvenopādānam; ato na saṃtānāntaratvamityabhiprāyeṇāha—yadīti | yadi tu yogisarvajñajñānayorasarvasarvaviṣayatvena viṣayabhedādanuvṛttajātīyatvam, ato na yogijñānamupādānam, ato vilakṣaṇasarvajñajñānotpattau yogijñānasaṃtānoccheda ityucyate; ato rūpajñānapravāhe sati yadā rasajñānaṃ bhavati tadāpi pūrvasaṃtānocchedādapavargaprasaṅgaḥ syādityāha—yo'pīti | atha tatra kathaṃci-
[p. 43]
dvoṣarūpatayā tulyatvamucyate, tadaviśiṣṭaṃ yogisarvajñānayorapi ; tataśropādānopādeyabhāvādekasaṃtānatvaṃ pūrvasaṃtānānucchedātpunarapyanirvāṇaṃ syādityāha — kathaṃciditi || P.15-16. idānīṃ kramaprāptaṃ vijñānapadaṃ vyākhyātuṃ tadvacāvatyaṃ paramatamupanyasyati— keciditi | ātmamanaḥsaṃyogajaṃ vijñānaṃ guṇo yasyeti vigrahaḥ | avijñānasvabhāvamiti ; svato jaḍamityarthaḥ | samyagutvātā unmūlitāḥ sakalā buddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmasaṃskārākhyā viśeṣaguṇā yasminniti vigrahaḥ | tasyeti ; ātmatattvasyetyarthaḥ | jñānasvabhāvo'pi cenmanaḥsaṃyogasāpekṣo jānāti tata eva tarhi jñānamutpattyate, tena dṛthā tatsvabhāvatvakalpaneti kaṇabhujo matam | kathaṃ svarūpāvasthā brahmaśabdenocyata ityāśaṅkya tasya pravṛttinimittamāha—sā hīti | dehādibhiranavacchinnatvaṃ bṛhattve hetuḥ ; bṛhattvācca brahmeti gīyate ; 'bṛha bṛhi vṛddhau' ityasmāt brahmaśabdavyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ | etacca śrautatvaleśādyotanārthamuktamiti mantavyam | jñānasvabhāvātmapakṣe tu doṣamāha—jñānasvabhāvatveti | caśabdastuśabdārthe | na tāvadasaṃnidhānāt sarvaṃ na budhyate, sarvagatasya sarvatra saṃnidhānāt | na ca sarvagato'pyasarvagatadehendriyādisāpekṣaḥ sarvaṃ na budhyate, nityatvājjñānarūpasya ; jñānotpattyarthaṃ hi dehādyapekṣyate ; nityatvādātmano jñānarūpaṃ nityam | ataśca saṃnihitavividhajñeyaviśeṣatvāt sarvamānmā budhyeta ; tataśca baddhasya muktasya ca yathādṛṣṭaniyatārthagocarāt saṃsārāt sarvadarśitvena bṛhattaraḥ saṃsāraḥ syādityarthaḥ | athaitaddoṣabhayāt na kiñcijjānātītyucyate, prāptaṃ tarhi jaḍatvam ; gateriva gamyamantareṇa jānātyarthasya jñeyaṃ vinānutpatteḥ | na cātmaiva jñeyaḥ, kartṛkarmatvavirodhāt | tadetat sāśaṅkramāha—athaiti | tadeva paramatamupanyasya tannivṛttaye vijñānapadaṃ yojayati—tāniti | paraḥ pūrvoktadoṣadṛṣṭiḥ pṛcchati—kuta iti | siddhāntī tu na vayaṃ prayojanabalāt citsvabhāvatvaṃ brūmaḥ, kiṃ tu śrutibalāditi manyamāna āha—śrūyata iti | nanu prāguktadoṣabalācchūterarthāntaratvaṃ kalpayiṣyāma ityata āha—na ceti | syādarthāntarakalpanā śruteḥ, yadyukto doṣaḥ syāt ; sa tu nāstītyarthaḥ |
[p. 44]
P.15-25. kuta ityāha—yatheti | yathā dāhako'pyagnirna sarvaṃ dahati, yathā ca darpaṇādiryadupanīyate tacchāyāmāpadya tadeva darśayati, upanāyakābhāve śuddhamavatiṣṭhate; tathā ātmāpīndriyādyupanītaṃ budhyate, na sarvam; muktaścopanāyakābhāvānna bāhyaṃ budhyata iti | adāhyamiti; ayogyamityarthaḥ | bhogāyatanaśarīrastha iti; anyatra sato'pi bhoganivṛttyarthaṃ viśeṣaṇam; muktau ca bhogābhāve upanāyakābhāvo heturiti mantavyam | nanu vahnyāderasarvagatasyāsaṃnihitadāhyādupanayanāpekṣā yuktā, na tu sarvagatasyātmano nityasaṃnihitasarvaviṣayasya; ataḥ sarvadarśitvaṃ muktasya ca bāhyaviṣayopalabdhiḥ syāditi codayati— syāditi | siddhāntī tu tavāpyeṣa doṣo'sti; ato yaste parihāraḥ sa no'pi bhāvapyatītyabhiprāyeṇa parasyāpi taddoṣamāsañjayati—ucyata iti | ātmamanaḥsaṃyogenātmanā ca jñānamupajanyate; sa cātmamanaḥsaṃyogaḥ sarvamanobhiḥ sarvātmanāṃ sarvagatatvenāviśiṣṭa iti sarvopalambhaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | nanu sarvātmanāṃ sarvamanobhiḥ saṃyogāviśeṣe'pi yo viṣayo yasya viṣayatve vartate sa eva tabhevāvabudhyata iti nāvyavasthā syādityāśaṅkyāha— mana iti | na hi viṣayāṇāmātmanaḥ prati svato viṣayatvam, api tu manaḥsaṃyogamuratena; ataḥ sarvasaṃyuktaiḥ sarvamanobhiḥ saṃyogāviśeṣāt sarve viṣayā viṣayatve'sya vartanta ityarthaḥ | atha sarvamanobhiḥ saṃyogāviśeṣe'pi svakarmanimittatvādupabhogasya svakarmopanītameva bhogyamupalabhata iti vyavasthocyate, tato'smākamapi jñānarūpo'pyātmā karmapāśavaśīkṛto'svatantrastadupanītamevārthaṃ budhyate, na sarvamiti vyavasthā setsyatītyāha—atheti | nanu janyajñānavādinaḥ karmavaśāt kacijjñānaṃ janyate kacinneti yuktā vyavasthā; nityajñānasvabhāvātmavādinastu kathaṃ karmanimittā vyavastheti vācyam; na hi tasya karmabhirniyatārthaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ janyate, nityatvāt; tajjanmani ca tatsvabhāvātmajanmaprasaṅgāt | na ca viṣayāntaraṃ pratibhāsyate, anityatvādātmanāśāpatteśca | tadā ca sarvānupalabdhiḥ prasajyate, na tu vyavasthā; na hyekasya viṣayabhedena nāśānāśau saṃbhavataḥ | ata eva na tiroṣīyate'pi | na cārthaṃ eva kaścidātmanaḥ sarvagatādapasāryate, paṭhādibhirivātriyate vā karmabhiḥ; yato'vyavasthā syāt | āpe ca karmanimittā cedvyavasthā, tato
[p. 45]
muktaya kṣīṇāśeṣakarmaṇaḥ sarvadarśitvaṃ durnivāram | ucyate—jñānamātmā, na jñātā; jñātṛtā ca tasya kartṛśaktyahaṃkāroparāgatibandhanā; ahaṃkāraśca bhogāyatanasthastatraiva jñātṛtāmavabhāsayati; tena vibhorapyātmano jñātṛtvaṃ prādeśikameva, na sārvatrikam; ato bhogāyatanastha evātmā jñātā, nānyatra sannapi; tataśca yadeva bhogasādhanaiḥ karmabhirbhogāyatanaṃ pratyupanītaṃ tadeva jñātuḥ saṃnihitamiti tadeva vetti, netaraditi vyavasthātiddhiḥ | nanu jñānarūpasyātmano vibhutvāt sarvajñeyasaṃbandho'sti | bāḍham | kimiti tarhi ‘sarvamidaṃ jāne’ iti na parāmṛśati? ajñātṛtvāt, jñāturbdhaye parāmarśo ‘jāne’ iti; jñātṛtvaṃ ca bhogāyatanastham, na sārvatrikamityuktam | ata eva muktasyāpi na bāhyaviṣayajñātṛtā; sā hi śarīrasthetyuktam | ato nāśarīrasya bhavitumarhati | tataśca muktau jñānamātratayāvatiṣṭhate, na jñātṛtayā; atastena saṃbaddhamapi jagattacca na jñeyatayā saṃbaddham; karturhi karmaṇā saṃbandhaḥ | nanu na jñānamapi jñeyaṃ vināsti; sakarmako hi jñānārtha ityuktam; ataḥ kathaṃ jñeyaṃ vinā muktajñānamavatiṣṭhate—iti cet, na; ādityaprakāśavadupapatteḥ; yathā svalvādityaprakāśaḥ prakāśyānapekṣatiddhiḥ, evaṃ jñānamapi jñeyānapekṣatiddhiḥ | nanu sakarmako jānātyarthaḥ | satyam; buddhivṛttaṃ tu tat; taddhi sakarmikā kriyā, sā karmapekṣati yuktam; nityaṃ ca jñānam, na kriyā; kṣaṇikā hi sā | kathaṃ tarhi tasyādhyastajñātṛbhāvasyāpi jñeyena saṃbandhaḥ? na; kriyāgarbho hi saḥ | sā ca buddherityuktam | na cānyakriyāgarbho'nyayoḥ karmakartṛbhāvo bhavati | satyam, anyatvāvagatau; aviveke tu tayoranyakriyāyā api tatkarmaṇyasya kartṛtā syādeva; yathā dehagatayā gatyā tadviviktasyāsmadarthasyātmano ‘grāmamahaṃ gacchāmi’ iti | tatkimidānīṃ dve jñāne? cideva svaprakāśā jñānam; tadavivekāsu loko buddhivṛttau jñānapadaṃ prayuṅkte | nanu lokaprayogagamyaḥ śabdārthaḥ; atastatra prayogāttadeva jñānapadārthaḥ syāt; na tu citsvaprakāśā | na, svayaṃprakāśajñānādhyāsenaiva buddhivṛttau prayogāt; yadadhyāsena yaḥ śabdo yatra prayujyate sa eva tasya mukhyo'rthaḥ, śuktāviva rajataśabdasya rajatam | kathaṃ punaravagamyate—adhyastasvaprakāśajñāne buddhivṛttau loke jñānapadaprayoga iti?
[p. 46]
‘ghaṭamahaṃ jāne’ iti jñānāvabhāsāt | na ca tadavabhāso'nyato'vakalpata ityuktam | na ca buddhivṛttameva svato'vabhāti, jaḍatvāt | na ca buddhivṛttasya svato jaḍasya ghaṭādivadarthaprakāśatvam | indriyaṃ tu prakāśakam, na prakāśa iti viśeṣaḥ | asti ca nityajñāne jñānaśabdo vede; yathā— “satyaṃ jñānamanantam” iti | na hi buddhivṛttasya kṣaṇikasyānantyamavakalpate; yogyatālakṣaṇo vā dṛśo dṛśyena saṃbandho nānyaḥ; “asaṅgo hyayaṃ puruṣaḥ” iti śruteḥ | darśanayogyatā cārthasya buddhyapārūḍhasyaiveti nānyatropalabdhiprasaṅgaḥ | tasmāt svaprakāśameva jñānaṃ mukhyam; nānyat | buddhivṛttaṃ tu āntamamukhyamiti siddham | tadetat svamate'pyupayujyata iti sāṃkhyamataprastāvenoktamityupasthite | P. 16-9. evaṃ paramatena vyavasthāmuktvā adhunā svamatenāha—api ceti | bhedadarśanamavidyā; tasyām ‘asmaivedaṃ sarvam’ ityabhedadarśanena vidyayā nirvartitāyāmātmamātrāvaśeṣe jagati dṛśyameva nāsti, kuto muktasya sarvadarśitvamityarthaḥ | baddhasya tu “eṣo'ṇurātmā cetastā veditavyaḥ” ityādiśruteraṇabuddhyavacchinnatvenāṇutvānniyatārthopanelavidyopanītārthamātradraṣṭṛtvācca sarvagatapakṣoktasarvadarśitvadoṣo dūrata eveti bhāvaḥ | atraiva dṛṣṭāntadvayamāha—draṣṭuriveti | tamevārthaṃ śrutidvayena draḍhayati—taduktamiti | draṣṭurātmano dṛśyatāyā iti vyadhikaraṇe ṣaṣṭhyau | evaṃ svapakṣamupapādya parapakṣe doṣamāha— vijñāna iti | kuta ityāha—na hoti; buddhyādiguṇarahitasya nityamanavabuddhyamānasya na sattvamasattvādviśiṣyata ityarthaḥ | na cātmābhāva eva puruṣārthaḥ, puruṣairanadhyamānatvādityāha—kaśceti || matāntaramāśaṅkya dūṣayati—syāditi | udvahanta ityatra svocchedāya yatamānā dṛśyanta iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | saṃsāriṇastu naivaṃvidhāḥ, yena svocchedāya yaterannityāha - na tviti | anevaṃvidhatvadyotanārthaṃ vividhetyādiviśe ṣaṇam | tataḥ kimityāha—tatreti | svapakṣe tu mokṣasyāniṣṭhanivṛttīṣṭaprāptibhyāgaikāntikaṃ puruṣārthatvamāha—yasya tviti |
[p. 47]
idānīṃ kramāgatamakṣarapadaṃ vyācaṣṭe—akṣaramiti | śabdātmatāmiti | P.16-23 yadāgamaikasamadhigamyaṃ yenākārādivarṇaviśeṣākhilaśabdārthaviśeṣānusyūtamokārasya śabdatanmātrarūpaṃ tadātmatāmāgamaikagamyātmana āhetyarthaḥ | nanvakṣaraśabdo'kārādivarṇopetaṃ viśeṣarūpamokāramāha ; tatkathamapetavarṇavibhāgamakhilaśabdārthānusyūtatvena sāmānyarūpamokāramāhetyata āha—viśeṣeṇeti | akṣaraśabdavācyenākārādiviśeṣarūpeṇa śrautreṇa sarvātmatve śrutivirodhāt atīndriyamokārasya pūrvoktaṃ sāmānyarūpaṃ lakṣyata ityarthaḥ | yadvā—akṣaraśabdo varṇātmakaṃ śabdaviśeṣamāha ; tatkathamapetaviśeṣaśabdamātrarūpatocyata ityāśaṅkyāha—viśeṣeṇeti | vyākhyāntaramāha—apariṇāmitvaṃ veti ; akṣaramityāhetyanuvartate | kathamakṣaraśabdena taducyata ityāha—pīraṇāma iti | pariṇāme pūrvadharmanivṛttirūpasya kṣaraṇasya bhāvāt, nāsti kṣaraṇaṃ cyavanamasya pūrvadharmāditi kṣaraṇavyudāsenāpariṇāmyucyata ityarthaḥ | tatra brahmaṇaḥ śabdātmatve pratyakṣāderaṃbhāvaṃ bakṣyamāṇaśrutīnāṃ pratīkopāsanāparatvaṃ ca manvānaḥ pṛcchati—kathamiti | siddhāntī tu nemāḥ pratīkopāsanaparāḥ śrutayaḥ, api tu brahmasvarūpaparā iti manvānaḥ śrutimeva pramāṇatvenodāharati—paraṃ cāparamiti | nanvokārābhidheyaṃ brahma ; taduktama—“tasya vācakaḥ praṇavaḥ” iti | tena tadabhidheyasya brahmaṇaḥ parāpararūpatātrocyate, nokārasyetyāśaṅkyāha— na ceti | nanu “varṇāt kāraḥ” iti vivakṣitaikasaṃkhyāt kathaṃ varṇasamudāyāt ‘om’ iti nāmnaḥ kārapratyaya ityata āha—avarṇāditi ; ekavarṇādanyasmādapītyarthaḥ ; tasmāt ‘varṇāt’ ityekatvamavivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | pratīkamālambanaṃ viṣaya upāsanābuddheḥ ; okāraviṣaye brahmabuddhiḥ kāryatyarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | devatāyā avyaktatvena sākṣāt pūjāyā asaṃbhavāt tallāñchane tasyā devatāyā lāñchanāni cihnāni śaṅkhacakrādīni yasmin dāruṇi śilāyāṃ vā tatra devatābuddhyā pūjāvidhānam, tathā sā pūjitā prasīdatīti kṛtvā yathā tathetyarthaḥ | yadvā omiti varṇabhedena nāmnā brahmaiva dhyeyam ; tathā tatprasīdatītyāha— anena ceti | evaṃ matadvayamupanyasya dūṣayati—ucyata iti | yatra
[p. 48]
dhyānāṅgatokārasya śrūyate, tatra bhavatu pratīkādyupāsanopadeśaḥ; yatra tu 'yajjīta' ityāderaśravaṇādaśrutakalpanāpramāṇābhāvācca brahmaṇaḥ sarvasya cokārātmatve vākyaṃ paryavasyati tatra na pratīkādyupadeśa ityarthaḥ | tadudāharati— P.17-11. yatheti | "tadyathā śaṅkunaikena sarvāṇi parṇāni saṃtṛṇṇāni, evamokāreṇa sarvā vāk saṃtṛṇṇā"; "oṃkāra evedaṃ sarvam" iti sopakramopasaṃhāraṃ vākyaṃ paṭhitam; yathā śaṅkunā patramadhyavartinā vaṃśakenaikena sarvataḥ prasṛtābhiḥ sirābhiḥ pratyekaṃ sarvapatrāṇi saṃtṛṇṇāni vyāptāni tathetyarthaḥ | nanu yadyapyokāreṇa sarvā vāco'nugatāḥ, tathāpi kathaṃ tāsāṃ tadātmatvam, tādātmye 'pi ca tāsāmarthamāgasyātādātmyāt kathamokārasya sarvātmatvam—ityāśaṅkya dvayaṃ saṃpādayati—atra hīti; sarvavāco'nugamenokārasya sarvavācāṃ tadrūpā tyāgādavyabhicāritvam; avyabhicāri vastunastaccam, na vyabhicāri śukteriva rūpyam; anyonyavyabhicāriṇaśca vācāṃ viśeṣāḥ, na tvokāra ityokāro vācastatvaṃ satyarūpam, na tadviśeṣā iti "sarvā vācaḥ saṃtṛṇṇāḥ" ityantena darśitatvāt sarvārthānāṃ ca savikalpakajñānairvāganuviddhānāmupalabdheḥ pūrvavadvāgrūpatvaṃ satyam; tataścārthānāṃ vāk taccam, vācaścokāra ityabhipretya "oṃkāra evedaṃ sarvam" ityupasaṃharati | sarvaṃ ca brahma; ata oṃkāro brahmeti bhāvaḥ | evamokārasarvātmatvamavivekavākyagamyamuktvā idānīṃ saviveke'pi tadgamyata ityāha—yatnāpīti | etena pūrvoktasarvātmatvenokāreṇetyarthaḥ | nanvarthavādatvāt stutyartho'yamupacāra ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | kuta ityāha—mukhyārthatva iti | arthavādā api guṇābhidhānalakṣaṇatvāt stuteḥ kecidarthamabhivadantaḥ stuvanti, evameva stutyavagamāt; tasya ca stute raveriva "naktaṃ dadṛśe" itivat pramāṇāntaravirodho nimittam; sa cennāsti tataḥ pratīyamānasyārthasyābādhitasyāpekṣitasya mukhyasya ca tyāgena nāśrutagauṇārthakalpanā yukteti bhāvaḥ | nanu sarvasya cedokārātmatvam, tataḥ pratyakṣādinā gṛhyeta; na caidam; ataḥ pratyakṣādyanupalabdhiviruddhatvāt kathamavirodha ityāśaṅkyāha—sarvasya hīti | na hi
[p. 49]
varṇātmanā śrautreṇa sarvasyokārātmatvam, kiṃ tu pratyakṣādyanavaseyenāgamagamyenātmanā; ato na pratyakṣādyanupalabdhivirodha ityarthaḥ | nanu śāstraikagamye'pi pratyakṣādyanupalabdhivirodho'styeva, kathamavirodha ityāśaṅkyāha—na hīti | P.17-17. pramāṇāntareṇa pratyakṣādinā, anavagamaḥ anupalabdhiḥ, pramāṇāntarasya śāstrasya, viṣayaṃ sarvasya okārātmatvaṃ dharmādikamiva nāpaharati na bādhate | na hi śāstragamyaṃ dharmādi pratyakṣādyanupalabdhito'sat bhavatītyarthaḥ | itaścārthavādoktamapi sarvātmatvamokārasya vivakṣitamityāha—tasmāditi | “tasmādvidvānetena paraṃ puruṣam” iti jñānino'dhikāraḥ śrutaḥ; jñānī ca pratyāsaceroṃkārasarvātmatvajñānī; taccāsya nānyataḥ pramāṇāt, anupalabdheḥ; ataḥ pratyāsacerarthavādājjātaṃ grāhyam; tasmādanantaramevārthavādavākyamokārasarvātmatve paryavasitam; na tadatikrameṇa gauṇaparyavasānam; tat punardharmānavidhyekavākyatāṃ nayati | tato'rthavādājjātokārasarvātmatvajñānasya puṃso dhyānopadeśo'yamiti siddhamokārasya sarvātmatvamiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣāntaramāha—athaveti | viśvā iti “seśchandāsi bahulam” iti selopaḥ; sarvāṇītyarthaḥ | tatra “viśvā bhuvanāni jajñe” iti bhogyavargasya vāgupādānatvena vāgātmatvamuktam; uttaratra tu vāgabhedāt bhoktṛvargasyeti vivekaḥ | vāgabhedaśca vācaḥ sarvātmatvena brahmatvāt; brahmaṇaśca jīvānāmabhedo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā vācaiva svayamātmanaḥ sarvātmatvamuktamiti sadṛṣṭāntamāha—tatheti; vasurudrādibhiḥ saha carāmi, teṣvanugatāsmītyarthaḥ | pūrvamokārasya sarvātmatvamuktam; adhunā tu vākśabdavācyasya śabdatattvasya sarvanāmarūpavikalpaviśeṣāspadasya sarvātmatvamuktamiti bhedaḥ | taduktam—“anādinidhanaṃ brahma śabdatattvaṃ yadavyayam |” iti | evamāgamabalena vācaḥ sarvātmatvamuktvā saṃpratyanumānabalenāha—api ceti | prakṛtirūpānvitā vikārā iti vyāptikathanam; vāgpānvitamiti ca pakṣadharmopanayaḥ; ata iti nigamanam | tadayaṃ prayogārthaḥ—jagaddharmi, vācaḥ pariṇāmo vivarto veti sādhyo dharmaḥ; tadajātiguṇabhūtena vāgpūpeṇānvitatvāt; yadyadajātiguṇabhūtayadrūpānvitaṃ tattatpariṇāmastadvivarto vā; yathā—ghaṭādayo mṛdūpānvitā mṛdaḥ pariṇāmāḥ, yathā vā vimbarūpānvitaṃ prativimbaṃ vimba- 4
[p. 50]
vivarta iti | vyaktayastu tajjātibhūtena gotvādinānvitā iti na tābhirvyabhicāraḥ; tathā guṇeṣvapi draṣṭavyam | śabdo'rthāvagame'bhyupāyamātram; ato'rthe śabdarūpa. 18-3. pānugatatvamasiddho heturityabhiprāyeṇa paraḥ pṛcchati—kathamiti | tadanugamaḥ bābhūpānvitatvapityarthaḥ | atrocaramāha—taditi | vāyūpānurāgeṇa yadvijñānaṃ tadvedyatvādarthānām; śabdānuraktārthajñānācchabdarūpatvamarthānām, yathā mṛdanuraktaghaṭādijñānānmṛdūpatvaṃ ghaṭāderityarthaḥ | parābhiprāyaṃ pariharati—tathā hīti | yadi cakṣurvadupāyamātraṃ śabdaḥ syāt, tataścakṣuṣīvāpratibhāti śabde'rthasaṃvedanaṃ syāt; na ca śabdānuṣṭherave ghaṭādyarthoṣṭhekho'stītyarthaḥ | nanu dhūmavattarhi jñātasyopāyatvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya tadvaiṣamyamāha—nāpīti | dhūmavadupāyatve śabdasya yathā ‘dhūmādagniḥ’ iti nyaṣikaraṇatayā pratītiḥ, na tu ‘dhūmo'gniḥ’ iti, tathā śabde'pi syāt; na ca sāsti, ‘ghaṭo'yam’ iti śabdābhedenārthapratīterityarthaḥ | yuktyantaramāha—itaśceti | na hi śabdayoḥ svataḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ; kiṃ tu arthagato'sau śabdayoḥ ‘nīlamutpalam’ iti iśyate; sa ca śabdasyārthābhede ghaṭate, nopāyatve; anyathā kākavattvordhvatvayoriva svato bhinnaṃ sthāṇuṃ gamayatāni syāt; kiṃ tu ‘ūrdhvatve sati kākavattvāt sthāṇuḥ’ iti vadvacaṣikaraṇābhyāṃ nīlotpalaśabdābhyāmarthāntarabhūtasyārthasya pratītiḥ syādityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca anyatra dhūmādāyupāyamātre na kevalaṃ pramāṇāntaropāye, dhūmopāye'pi yāvadagnau dhūmarūpeṇa niścayo na bhavati; iha tu pramāṇāntaropāye'pi śabdarūpeṇa niścayo bhavati; tasmādito'pi na dhūmādivadupāyatvenārthe śabdarūpapratyayaḥ, kiṃ tu śabdarūpatvādarthasyetyāha—api ceti | nanu tarhi śabdopāya evārthapratyayaḥ, na pramāṇāntaropāyaḥ; tatra tadānantaryāt tadbuddhirarthe bhrāntā kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkyāha—tadarśanāditi | pramāṇāntaropāye'rthe śabdarūpatvadarśanāt śābde'pi tathā śabdarūpa evārtha iti pratītau nopāyabhūtaśabdānantaryāt śabdātmakārthabhrāntirityarthaḥ | nanvastu savikalpakajñāne śabdānuraktārthajñānādarthasya śabdātmatvam, bālādijñāne tu nirvikalpake kathamityata āha—bālānāmiti | bālapravṛttirdharmiṇī, śabdānuviddheti sādhyo dharmaḥ, niścayapūrvatvāt asmadādi-
[p. 51]
pravṛttivat | bālādīnāṃ niścayo dharmī śabdānuviddhaḥ, asmadādisavikalpakaniścayavat | teṣāṃ ceha janmani śabdavyutpattivirahe'pi pūrvajanmavyutpannaśabdasaṃskārabhājāṃ saṃbhavati hṛdayasthaśabdānuvedhaḥ ; anuccāraṇaṃ tu karaṇapāṭavābhāvāditi parānupalambho'pi na doṣa iti bhāvaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha— tathā ceti | tacchabdābhyāṃ vācaḥ parāmarśaḥ | yadyapi vipariṇāmo'pi P.18-17. pūrvamuktaḥ, tathāpi vivartta evāsya svamatamiti sa evopasaṃhataḥ | itaśca śabdavivarto jagadityāha—api ceti | vyāvahārikā iti ; vyavahārāya prabhavanti, vyavahāramātrasiddhā ityarthaḥ | teṣāmeva tarhi śabdavivartatvamastu, netareṣāmityat āha—taditi | anye'pi śabdārthāḥ śabdavivartāḥ, śabdārthatvāt vidhyādivaditi prayogaḥ | tānudāharati—yatheti | vākyārthaḥ saṃsargaḥ, samūho vanādiḥ, asantaśca pratiddhā eva | tatra vidhiniṣedhayostāvacchabdavivartātmatvamāha—tatreti | 'pravṛttinivṛttī' itipadena pravartakanivartakau lakṣayati ; te eva puruṣavyāpārarūpeṇa kāryatayāvagate vidhiniṣedhāviti bhāvaḥ | nanu kārye pravṛttinivṛttī vidhiniṣedhau, na kālarūpāvacchinne ; ato na tridhā prayogasyāviśeṣa ityabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkayati—kārye iti | taddūṣayati— neti | kālatrayātirekeṇa kāryatvaṃ nāma caturthaṃ gatyantaraṃ na nirūpyata ityarthaḥ ; kāryatvamapi bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyadvartamānaṃ vā syāditi bhāvaḥ | upasaṃharati— tasmāditi | avastu kramiti ; avidhamānabāhyavidhiniṣedhavastukamityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti | tathāpi kimityāha—na ceti | prakṛtamupasaṃharati— tasmāditi | yadāpi pravṛttinivṛttī vidhiniṣedhāviti vyākhyānam, tadāpi kāryatvadharmopete pravṛttinivṛttī vidhiniṣedhau, na svarūpeṇa ; tena kāryatvameva prayojakamiti manvānaḥ pravṛttinivṛttyanuguṇamiti kāryatvameva bhedenāhetyavirodhaḥ | tathā 'nīlamutpalam' iti vākyārtho'pi saṃsargaḥ tadviśiṣṭo vā syāt ; tatra na tāvatsaṃsargaḥ, saṃsargivyatiriktasya tasyābhāvāt | katham ? tadvaccatireke tasya ghaṭapaṭayoriva saṃsargāntarāpacerabhavasthānāt ; vyatirekāvyatirekau tu na saṃbhavata iti vakṣyāmaḥ | avyatireke tu padārthā eva, na saṃsargaḥ ; ata eva na saṃsargī asaṃsargirūpātpadārthāt bhinno'sti, yo vākyārthaḥ syāt | tasmāt pratibhāmātramidam | na ca sā nirālambanā saṃbhavati ; na ca śabdoparāgarahitaḥ 4A
[p. 52]
P. 19-1. savikalpakaḥ pratyayo'sti ; tasmādvāktattvameva tathā tathā cakāstītyāha—evamiti | tathā vanādāvapyasati vastuni vastubuddhirbhavantī vāktattvamevālambata ityati diśati—evamiti | idānīṃ gānaśāstrānābhijñasya ṣaḍjādiṣu gapādiṣu ca nirvikalpakaṃ śabdānubedhaśūnyaṃ ye jñānamicchanti, tān prati—yadyapi ṣaḍjādiṣu ṣaḍjādiśabdābhāve'pi ‘gānamidam’ ityādisāmānyaśabdānurodho'sti ; yadyapi ca nirvikalpake'pi sūkṣmā vāgasti ityuttaramasti ; tathāpi tanmatamevopajīvya—sarvajñāneṣu prakārāntareṇa śabdānubedhamupapādayati—api ceti | atādṛktvamevāha—aviviktā hīti | tathā coktam “gānamātraṃ vijānanti tatrānādhikṛtāstu ye | vivekaṃ pratipadyante yeṣāṃ tassaṃskṛtā matiḥ || ” iti | śabdānuviddhā pratipattirvivekavatītyetallokavyavahāreṇa draḍhayati—tathā ceti | tataḥ kimityata āha—evaṃ ceti | śabdasaṃsparśe sati vivekavato'rthavodhasya vyavahāropayuktavivekavattvayotkarṣadarśanāt, tatpratisaṃhāre śabdābhāve saṃcetitānāmapyasaṃcetitakalpatvāt ; kutaḥ ? pathi gacchataḥ puṃsaḥ padmyāṃ spṛśyamānānāmapi gucchatṛṇādīnāmapakarṣāt ; ato'nvayavyatirekābhyāṃ vāprapādhīnameva citeśrititvamiti balādāpadyata ityarthaḥ | arthaprakāśātmakatvaṃ hi citeśrititvam, na vastumātratvam ; tacca śabdasaṃsparśe satyutkṛṣyate, tadabhāve cāpakṛṣyate ; tasmādvāprapādhīnaṃ citeśrititvaṃ nāma rūpam | matāntaramāha—vākśaktiriti ; vāco'rthaprakāśaśaktirvākśaktiḥ | vākśaktiścecitiḥ, kathaṃ tarhi vāgupasaṃhāre'rthaścetyata ityata āha—tatpratīti | tatrāpi citeścedasti, asti sūkṣmā vāk, citervākśaktitvāt | tataḥ kimityata āha—sarvatheti ; sarvathā vāgrapādhīnatve vākśaktitve cetyarthaḥ | hetau ‘iti’śabdaḥ | ‘mṛdaḥ’ iti vikāre, ‘candramasaḥ’ iti vivarte dṛṣṭāntaḥ | ataśca vāgātmakaṃ sarvam ; vākca “yathā śaṅkunā” iti śruterokārātmikā ; tenokāraḥ sarvaḥ ; sarvaṃ ca brahma ; ata oṃkāro brahmeti siddham |
[p. 53]
idānīmakṣarapadasyārthāntaraṃ vaktuṃ tadyāvatyaṃ paramataṃ vaktumupakramate—anya P. 19-13 iti | 'mṛdādidṛṣṭāntadarśanāt' ityekadeśena kṛtsno'numānaprayogaḥ sūcitaḥ ; so'yaṃ prayogaḥ—brahma kāryarūpeṇa pariṇamate, prakṛtitvāt mṛdādīva ghaṭādirūpeṇeti; prakṛtitva ca "yato vā imāni bhūtāni" ityādiśrutisiddham ; śrutau vā mṛdādidṛṣṭāntadarśanādityarthaḥ | evaṃ paramatamupanyasya tannirākaraṇārthatvenākṣarapadaṃ yojayati—taditi; tat brahmaṇaḥ pariṇāmitvam, apākīryate nirasyate; akṣaramityanenetyarthaḥ | parastu pūrvoktānumānabalena pṛcchati—kuta iti | siddhāntī tu śrutiviruddhamanumānamapramāṇaṃ manyamānaḥ śrutimāha— bhruva iti | atra paramatamāśaṅkayati—atheti | pariṇāmitve'pyātmano nityatā na vyāhanyate mṛda iva ghaṭādau, tattvāvighātāt tatsarūpatvāvināśāt ; ato nityatā tu tiḥ pariṇāmitve'pyupapannatvān pariṇāmitvānumānaṃ bādhata iti bhāvaḥ | etadevābhiyuktavacanena draḍhayati—yathoktamiti | yasmin kāryātmanā pariṇate'pi tattvaṃ kāryeṣu prathamijñānāt kāryarūpeṇa na vyāvartyate tadapi nityam; na kevalaṃ kūṭasthamevetyarthaḥ | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati— satyamiti | satyaṃ pariṇāmini nityatvamapyasti, kiṃ tvātmani tanna saṃbhavati; sa hi sarvātmanā pariṇamet, ekadeśena vā; tatra sarvātmanā pariṇatāvātmasvarūpasya vināśādanityatvaṃ syāt, ekadeśapariṇatau ca sāvayavatvānnityatvasya vyāghātaḥ syāt; yaddhi sāvayavam, tadavayavasaṃyogajatvāt kāryaṃ dṛṣṭam; yacca kāryam, tadanityaṃ dṛṣṭam; tathā cātmanaḥ sāvayavatve avayavānāṃ mitho'vayavinaśca bhedādekatvamadvayatvaṃ vyāhanyetetyarthaḥ | evaṃ pariṇāmapakṣo'nupapattimuktvā vivartapakṣe upapattimāha—tadetaditi | tadetatsarūpeṇa viśuddhatvaṃ prapañcātmanā cāviśuddhatvam, tathā prapañcanāśe'pi svato nityatvam, prapañcabhede'pyekatvaṃ cākāśakalpe viśuddhatvānavayavatvādibhirākāśopame avidyākalpitena prapañcena kalpitāvacchede brahmaṇi ghaṭate; nānyathetyarthaḥ | kathaṃ ghaṭate ? ityāha—kalpiteti | yathā kalpitāvacchedamapyākāśaṃ vastuvṛttenānavacchinnamastyeva, avacchedanāśādanityamapi kalpanayā tattvato nityam, avacchedabhedātkalpitabhedamapi vastuvṛttenaikam, tadaviśuddhyā
[p. 54]
vāśuddhamapi taccataḥ śuddham; tathā brahmāpi; na hi kalpanā vastu anyathābhāvaṃ nayatīti bhāvaḥ | athocyate—yadi kalpitāvacchedaṃ brahma, tataḥ kalpitānāmevaikadeśānāṃ pariṇāmo'stu; tathā sati ca nityatvamekatvaṃ ca na P.19-21. vyāhaniṣyate; pariṇāmastu setsyatītyāśaṅkya dūṣayati—atheti | kalpitasya asamyagucchādhyavasitasya ekadeśasya yaḥ pariṇāmaḥ, sa kalpanayaiva asamyagucchaiva syāt; na cāsamyagucchādhyavasitasya satyatvam; ato'pariṇāmitvameva brahmaṇa iti siddho naḥ pakṣa iti sūktam 'akṣaram' iti | idaṃ cākṣarapadasya vyākhyāntaraṃ na pūrvatrāparitoṣeṇa, pūrvatra dūṣaṇānabhidhānāt; tasmādāvṛtyā vṛttyantareṇa vā viṣayavyāptyarthamiti mantavyam | idānīmasarvapadaṃ vyākhyātuṃ tadvyāvartya satyaprapañcavādimataṃ tāvadupanyasyati— ke ciditi | ke cit “sarvagandhaḥ sarvarasaḥ sarvakāmaḥ sarvakarmā” ityādiśruteḥ satprapañcātmatāṃ brahmaṇo'bhyupagatavanta ityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ śrutitaḥ, arthāpattito'pyayamartho labhyata ityāha—evamiti | sarvātmatve sati ca viṣayāṇāmupabhogaḥ prakāśo ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | kathamityāha—prakāśeti | sarvātmake brahmaṇi prakāśasvabhāvasya brahmātmanaḥ svabhāvabhūtā viṣayāḥ prakāśātmakatvāt yuktaṃ prakāśaratnityarthaḥ | brahmaṇo'nyatve tu prakāśasaṃsṛṣṭasya svato jaḍasya ca viṣayasya prakāśo nādhakalpeta | tadevaṃ viṣayaprakāśo'sarvātmatve brahmaṇo'nupapadyamāno brahmaṇaḥ sarvātmatvaṃ gamayatītyabhiprāyeṇāha—anyatva iti | niṣprapañcabrahmaśrutayaśca “asthūlam” ityādyāḥ niṣprapañcamiti saprapañcaṃ brahmopāsyam—ityevaṃparāḥ, na tu prapañcātmatvapratyākhyānaparā iti bhāvaḥ | atra siddhāntamāha—tāniti | na sarvātmakaṃ brahma, kiṃ tu niṣkevalamadvayamiti yāvat | parastu tenaiva śrutyarthāpacyavaṣṭambhena pṛcchati—kuta iti | uttaramāha—asthūlamiti | uparāgastādātmyam | neyamupāsanāparā śrutiḥ, upāsteraśrutatvāt; aprakṛtatvādadhyāhārapramāṇābhāvāt; upaniṣadāṃ tu siddhe'rthe prāmāṇyāt; ato'sthūlādiśruterna sarvātmakaṃ brahma | nanu sarvagandhādiśrutirapyasti; tasyāstarhi kā gatiḥ? ucyate—sarvagandhādivikalpāspadasvāt ‘sarvagandhaḥ’ ityādyuktamityavirodhaḥ | viṣayopabhogopapattiśca
[p. 55]
vivartapakṣe'pyukteti na punaruktā | evaṃ pramāṇābhāvena viparyayapramāṇena ca prapañcasabhāvatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratiṣidhya, idānīṃ tadabhyupagame doṣamāha—sarveti | P. 20-2. sarvagandhādiviśeṣairaviyogādātmana, ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—na hīti | nanu sabhāvādāpi kiñcidviyojyate ; kathamiva ? ghaṭa iva pākenetyāśaṅkyāha —athāpīti | viyogo'pi bhavan katipayaireva prapañcaiḥ syāt, na sarvaiścailokyavartibhiḥ ; prapañcaprabandhasya kṛtsnasya prapañcasyānucchedāt ; ataśca nityasaṃsāritā syādityarthaḥ | nanu pralaye sarvocchedo dṛṣṭaḥ ; naivam, ātmantikalayābhiprāyatvādasya ; sa hi muktyupayogī ; netaraḥ, punaḥ sṛṣṭeḥ | prapañcaprābalyasyānucchedaṃ prakaṭayati—sarvayeti | sabhāvasyāviyoge viyoge ca, bubhukṣādiprapañco'syātmana ātmā sabhāvastvatpakṣe ; na ca bubhukṣādiprabandhasya sarvajantugatasya bubhukṣāderucchedaḥ kenacidiṣyate, ekamuktau sarvamuktiprasaṅgādityarthaḥ | bubhukṣāderādhyātmikasya puruṣaviṣṇavakasya pratyāsannamatiśayitaṃ bandhahetutvamiti viśeṣataḥ parigrahaḥ | tatra sarvātmakaikātmavādinastava mokṣo na syādityāha—tatreti | etena kalpitabhedasya jīvasyāpi brahma prāptasya muktiḥ pratyuktā | kasya nāma nirmokṣaḥ syādityata āha—ātmeti | ātmabhedavādinastu kasyacidātmano bubhukṣādiprapañcocchedānmuktasaṃsārasya vibhāgaḥ syāt ; na tvasya sarvātmatvamiṣṭamityarthaḥ | prakṛtamupasaṃharati— tasmāditi | yadi na brahmasabhāvabhūtaḥ prapañcaḥ, kīdṛśastarhītyata āha— avidyeti | ayamāśayaḥ—taccataḥ prapañcātmatve brahmaṇaḥ prapañcasya bahudoṣaduṣṭatvāt taddoṣakalpitatvādamedhyādīnāṃ durgandhādiprapañcena nityaduḥkhitvaṃ durnivāraṃ syāt ; akalpamānandaikarasaṃ ca tacchrūyate, tadvyāhanyeta | vikāradoṣāste na prakṛtiṃ spṛśantīti cet, tatkimamedhyādiduṣṭe ghaṭe mṛdamaduṣṭāṃ manyase ? yenaivam | atha bhinnābhinnatvāt prapañcasya bhedābhedena na taddoṣalepa iti cet, na ; bhedābhedena tadāpatterduṣṭamaduṣṭaṃ ca brahma syāt ; atyantaviśuddhaṃ ca tacchrūyatam | avidyākrīḍite tu prapañce taiṣa doṣaḥ ; na khalvavidyākrīḍitena doṣeṇa tattvamupalipyate gaganamiva malinatādinā | tasmādatyantaviśuddhaṃ brahmacchatā avidyāpakṣa evopāsya iti |
[p. 56]
P. 20-8. atredānīṃ śūnyavādī pratyavatiṣṭhate—yadīti | yadi paramārthaṃ tat brahma “tatsatyam” iti śruteḥ, avidyākrīḍite ca prapañce tacchūnyataiva satyāḥ ataḥ saivāstu brahmetyarthaḥ | etamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | tatra siddhāntamāha—tatreti | pūrvoktāsarvatvaviruddhaṃ sarvatvamapi bhramāpanusṛtye vivṛṇoti— etaditi | brahma sarvotprakaraṃ na bhavatītyuktam; idānīṃ tu sarvaṃ brahmātmakam, tenātmanā sat, na tu śūnyamityavirodha ityarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha—nityeti | sarvaśūnyatve niṣprapañcalakṣaṇasya mokṣasyāyatnasiddhatvānmokṣārthayorupadeśapravṛttyorānarthakyaṃ syādityarthaḥ | paramatamāśaṅkayati—nityeti | yathā tava nityamuktasyātmano'vidyānibandhanaḥ saṃsāraḥ, tathā mama śūnyatāyā bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | uttaramāha—yadīti | kuta ityāha—muktāvapīti | muktāvapi grahaṇābhāvasya tulyatvāt tadvadava saṃsāre'pi na kiñcit prakāśeta, tasyāvastutvāditi bhāvaḥ | atha viparyayagrahaṇamavidyā, tatastasya sattābhyupagamānna sarvaśūnyatetyāha—atheti | śrutivirodhaśca syādityāha—teneti | ‘na syāt’ ityadhyāhāryam | śrutyaivāyaṃ pakṣaḥ svayaṃ niṣiddha ityāha—kathamiti | śrutivirodhaśca svāvaṣṭambhenoktaḥ; na tu bauddhasya śrutiprāmāṇyaṃ siddham | sarvaśūnyatāyāṃ prapañcavibhramasya niradhiṣṭhānatvena niṣkāraṇatā syāt; na cākāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ bhavatītyabhiprāyeṇa śrutyarthamevopapādayati—bhāvo hīti | rajjvādiradhiṣṭhānaṃ yathāvatsvarūpeṇānavabhāsamānamadhyastāvidyamānasarpādirūpaṃ prakāśate; śūnye tvadhiṣṭhānābhāvāt ka kasyādhyāso'dhyāropaḥ ? tadabhāve ca kiṃ prakāśatām ? prakāśate ca prapañcaḥ; asya niṣkāraṇataiva syādityarthaḥ; na hi niradhiṣṭhāno bhramo dṛṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ | itaśca na śūnyatvamityāha—vijñānamiti | na hi śūnyasya vijñānānandarūpatā saṃbhavati; tasmānna śūnyarūpaṃ brahma; kiṃ tu sarvasyāvidyākalpitasyātmā nijasvarūpamityarthaḥ | idānīṃ kramāgatamabhayapadaṃ vyākhyātuṃ tadvyāvatyāṃ bhayāśaṅkāmāha—ke ciditi | sukhasya duḥkhenāvinābhāvānnāsti kāpyavasthā duḥkhasaṃsparśarahitā; ato muktāvapi duḥkhabhayamastīti ke cinmanyante | kathamavinābhāva ityāha—kevaleti; duḥkharahitasyetyarthaḥ | akaivalyamupapādayati—na hīti | nanu
[p. 57]
sukhasya duḥkhāvinābhāvo vācyaḥ—‘na hi kaścit sukhyeva’ iti; ‘duḥkhyeva’ iti tu prakṛtānupayogi; na hi duḥkhasya sukhenāvinābhāvo'tra prakṛtaḥ| ucyate—siddhasya sādhyatvenopanyāso dṛṣṭāntārthaḥ| vāśabdo'yamivārthaḥ; taduktam—“ivavāśabdānupamānārthau” iti| tenāyamarthaḥ—yathā sukhaṃ vinā na kaścit duḥkhyevopalabhyate, tathā duḥkhenāpi vinā na kaścit sukhīti| itaśca na duḥkharahitāvasthāstītyāha—duḥkhavat iti| atra P. 21-1. dṛṣṭāntamāha—tāpavat iti| tena muktau sukhaṃ duḥkhasahacaritam, sukhatvāt asadādisukhavaditi| tatra siddhāntamāha—tatreti| abhayaṃ vivṛṇoti—sarveti| hetumāha—asaktāditi| śrutimāha—abhayamiti| anumānametadāgamaviruddhamiti bhāvaḥ| śrutyarthaṃ yuktyā draḍhayati—tathā hīti| bhayamiti; duḥkhabhayamityarthaḥ| duḥkhavat eva sukhaṃ sukhatve'vatiṣṭhata ityapyuktamayuktamityāha—na ceti| yadi duḥkhābhāvaḥ sukhaṃ syāt, tataḥ syādevam; bhāvāntare tu sukhe duḥkhābhāve'pi tat syādevetyarthaḥ| abhayapadasyārthāntaramāha—apara iti| tatra bhayaṃ tāvadabhayapadaparyudasyamāha—iheti| tadupapādayitumanekavidhasaṃsārapradarśanamukhena saṃsāraprakārabhedaṃ tāvadāha—iheti| iha saṃsāre vijñānātmāno jīvā brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādvibhaktā vā syuḥ, avibhaktā vā| yadā ca vibhaktāstadāpi dvayī gatiḥ—ātmanaḥ sakāśāt brahmaṇaiva vā vibhajyeran; kimarthamityata āha—bhogārthamityādi; yadvā na bhogādyartham, kiṃ tu svabhāvāt; svabhāvo'yaṃ brahmaṇo yajīvān vibhajatītyekā gatiḥ| gatyantaramāha—avidyeti; avidyānibandhano vā vimbaprativimbavadvibhāgaḥ| pakṣāntaramāha—vijñānātmāna iti| tatrāpi gatidvayamāha—teṣāmiti| dehādikarmaṇaḥ ‘ahaṃ karomi’ ityātmanyadhyāsaḥ karmāvidyā| tatrānantarapakṣe tāvadāvṛttibhayaprasaktimāha—tatreti| draṣṭurātmano dṛśyasya ca viṣayākārapariṇatāyāstriguṇātmikāyāḥ prakṛteḥ yā draṣṭṛdṛśyayogyatā tayā dṛśyān paśyan vijñānātmā jīvo badhyata iti yadyucyate, tatastathonityayogyatāyā nityatvenānapāyāt punarāvṛttibhayaprastyeve-
[p. 58]
tyarthaḥ | atha prakṛtiḥ puruṣasya bhogakāryāya viṣayākāreṇa pariṇamate; tacca tathā saṃsārāvasthāyāmeva kṛtamiti muktasya punarna karoti; ato nāsti punarāvṛtti- P.21-14 bhayamityāśaṅkayati—atheti | atra doṣamāha—sakṛditi | sakṛcchabdādimupanīya kṛtakṛtyā prakṛtirna punarātmanastadupanayet; ataḥ saṃsāriṇo'pi sakṛcchabdādyupalambhaḥ syādityarthaḥ | athānantavikārā prakṛtiḥ sarvavikārarūpeṇātmanopalabdhā satī punaḥ śabdādiviṣayākāreṇa na pariṇamate; sakṛcchabdādyupalabdhau vikṛtaśabdādirūpāntaropalabdhaye punaḥ śabdādyākāreṇa pariṇamata eva; ataśca punaḥ śabdādyupalabdhirghaṭata ityucyate; tato'nantavikāratvādeva prakṛtipuruṣavivekavajñā muktena sarvātmanopabhukteti taṃ prati punarviṣayākāreṇa pariṇamet; atastasyāpi punarviṣayopabhogalakṣaṇasaṃsārabhayaṃ syādeva; tadetadāha—atheti atha pūrvoktā yā avidyā tatpūrvatayā ātmanyadhyastaṃ karmabandhaheturityucyate, tataḥ saṃsārasyānāditvena karmaṇāmānanyāt bahutarakarmakṣaye'pi karmaśeṣāṇāṃ muktasyāpi saṃbhavāt punarāvṛttibhayamasyeva | anantaraphaladāni karmāṇi phalamanantaraṃ datvā nivṛttāni; ataḥ kutasteṣāṃ muktau saṃbhava iti cet, na; teṣāmaniyatavipākakālatvāt; aniyatavipākasya phaladānasya kālo yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ; tataśca bahutarakālātikrame'pi svasahakāriṇaṃ kālaṃ prāpya kadācinmuktamapi badhnīyuḥ; tataśca pralayāvasthāta iva muktyavasthāto'pi punarāvṛttiḥ syāt; tadetadāha— avidyeti | avidyānibandhane'pi bandhe punarāvṛttibhayamāha—avidyeti | na tāvanmuktasya hetvabhāvāt prayojanābhāvādvā nāvidyā pravartate, yato nirhetukā niṣprayojanā ca sā; na tasyā hetuprayojanāpekṣāsti; tataśca kadācinmuktamapi punarbadhnīyādityarthaḥ | na cedamasaṃbhāvanīyam, suṣupte brahma prāptasya tataḥ punarāvṛttidarśanādityāha—tathā ceti | atrāvidyāstīti cet, muktāvapi tayā bhāvyamityuktam; ataḥ sāmyam | brahmaprāptiśca suṣuptasya “sati saṃpadya na viduḥ” “sati saṃtpasyāmahe” ityādiśrutyokteti draṣṭavyam | tathā svatantre'pi brahmaṇi svato bhinnānāmabhinnānāṃ vā jīvānāṃ vibhajya bhogakrīḍāvibhūtirupāpanasvabhāvāruthairhetubhirdehendriyabhogānāṃ hetāvabhyupagamyamāne punardehādisaṃbandhabhayamastītyāha—svatantre tviti | kṛta ityāha—
[p. 59]
svatantro hīti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—laukika iti | atha īśvaraḥ karma-P.21-24 sāpekṣaḥ na tvasya bandhane svātantryam; karmasu vā nāsya svātantryam, yena tānyāhūya punarbadhnīyāt; ato na svātantryarūpāpanāya muktau punarbadhnātītyucyate; tathāpyanādau saṃsāre karmaṇāmānantyāt aniyataphalapariṇatikālatvācca kalpaśatātikrame'pi svaphalapariṇāmakālaṃ prāpya kadācit tāni muktaṃ badhnīyurityastyevāvṛttibhayamityāha—atheti | nanu pūrvapūrvajanmāni kṛtaḥ karmāśayaḥ karmasaṅghāto maraṇenābhivyakteḥ samanantarajanmani phalāni dattvā kātsnyena nivartate; tataśca pratijanma samāptatvānna karmaṇāmānantyam; ataḥ samanantarapūrvajanmakṛtasya karmāśayasyāntyadeha eva kātsnyena nāśānna punarbandhasaṃbhāvanetyāśaṅkyānantye hatumāha—yata iti | eko bhava ekajanma, tatra bhavatītyaikabhavikaḥ | kuta ityāha—na hīti | na hi maraṇamātramevābhivyañjakam phalaṃ dātumuddyotakam, kiṃ tu deśakālādapi; tena satyapyanantaraṃ maraṇe svadeśakālāvapekṣamāṇāni karmāṇi cirataramapyanuvartanta iti naikabhavikatvamityarthaḥ | kathaṃ punarbandhaṃ maraṇamātramabhivyañjakamityāha— tathā hīti | maraṇamātre hyabhivyañjake tadanantarameva sarvakarmāṇi yugapat phalāni dadyuḥ; na caivam, yata ekasminnapi dehe tāni svaṃ svaṃ deśaṃ kālaṃ vāpekṣya krameṇa phalāni dadati; ato na teṣāṃ maraṇamātramabhivyañjakam, api tu deśādyapīti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca maraṇamātre'bhivyañjake pūrvajanmakṛtāni viruddhadevanaraniryaktyāvarajātibhoganimittāni karmāṇi maraṇānantaraṃ yugapat svaphalāni dadyuḥ; tatra yugapadviruddhānekadehopādānāsaṃbhavādekenaikasmin dehe kṛte'nyeṣāmāvāpagamanaṃ—tatrānupraviśya svaphalajananaṃ—syāt; tadapi nāsti; viruddhajanmabhogānāmekatra janmani samavāyāsaṃbhavādityāha—viruddheti | āvāpāpagamanāsaṃbhavāditi vā pāṭhaḥ; tatra copanayāpanayāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | garbhamṛtyūnāṃ ca tatra janmanyakṛtakarmaṇāṃ na maraṇābhivyañjakaṃ dehādikāraṇaṃ karmāstīti muktiḥ syādityāha—garbheti | tena naikabhavikaḥ karmāśayaḥ; ataścānādau saṃsāre karmaṇāmānantyānmuktasyāvṛttiphalamastītyāśaṅkya tannivṛttyarthamabhayapadaṃ yojayati—tatrocyata iti | kuta ityāha—parā hīti |
[p. 60]
P. 22-8. parā prakṛṣṭetyarthaḥ | kimiti nāstītyāha—na tāvaditi | nanu karmaṇāmanantatvāt svaphalaikavināśyatvāccākṛtabhogānāṃ vināśāsaṃbhavāt kimiti karmanimittaḥ saṃsāro na syādityata āha—vidyayeti | na tāvatphalenaiva karmaṇāṃ nāśa iti niyamaḥ, prāyaścittenāpi tannāśāt; nāpyānantyādanāśaḥ, vidyāyā ānantyadagdhṛtvāditi bhāvaḥ | na ca prāyaścittaṃ naimittikamātram, etadapi bhogenaiva kṣīyata iti vācyam; “dvādaśābdena godhatī tasmātpāpātpramucyate” iti smṛtivirodhāt | nanvāgamatiddhatvādastu prāyaścittasya pāpakṣayahetutvam, vidyāyāstu kathamanantakarmakṣayahetutvamityāśaṅkyātrāpyāgamamāha—uktaṃ hīti | evamadṛṣṭaśaktyā prāyaścittavadvidyāyāḥ sarvakarmakṣayahetutvaṃ paramatenoktvā adhunā svamatena dṛṣṭaśaktyā tadāha—atha veti | avidyāhetukaḥ kartṛkarmādivibhāgaḥ, tasya vidyayā nivṛttau kutastasya punarāvṛttibhayaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | avidyānibandhane'pi bandhe muktasya punaḥ saṃsārabhayaṃ nāstītyāha—nāpīti; punaḥ saṃsāra iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | kuta ityāha—taditi; ‘tat’ ityavidyāṃ parāmṛśati | kimiti tatpravṛttiheturnāstītyāha—anādiriti | kasmātpunaranādihetuṃ nāpekṣata ityāha— prāgiti | yadi nāmāvidyānāditvādahetukā, tataḥ kimityāha—vidyayeti | parvā tāvadanādiravidyā muktasya vidyayā hatā; itarā ca nirhetukatvānna hetumantareṇotpattimarhatītyarthaḥ | nanu suṣuptavaducchinnāpi punarutpattyata ityata āha—suṣupte tviti | suṣupte prapañcātmanā vistāramātramavidyāyā nivṛttam; svayaṃ tu sā ātmatattvāgrahaṇātmanā vikṣepasaṃskārātmanā cāvasthitaiva; ataḥ prabodhe punaḥ prapañcamādarśayati; muktasya tu sarvathocchinneti viśeṣaḥ | yadi tu suṣupte'pi sarvathocchinnā syāt, na mokṣāt suṣuptaṃ vibhajyetetyarthaḥ | suṣupte cedavidyā nocchinnā, kathaṃ tarhi “tatrāharaharimāḥ prajā brahmalokam” ityādau brahmaprāptyabhidhānamityata āha—vikṣepa iti | yadyapi tatra kāraṇāvidyāsti, tathāpi kāryāvidyānivṛttisāmānyād gauṇaṃ brahmaprāptyabhidhānamiti bhāvaḥ | svātantryayogyatāpakṣayostu punarāvṛttyāśaṅkā tayoranabhyupagamenaiva pratyuktena punarna pratyuktā | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi |
[p. 61]
kena punaḥ pramāṇenāsya prathamaślokoktasyādvayātmatattvarūpasyārthasyāvagamaḥ ? P.22-19. na tāvat pratyakṣeṇa, tasyābhedaviparītanīlapītādibhedaviṣayatvāt | nāpyanumānena, tasya liṅgiliṅgādibhedagrāhakapratyakṣapūrvakatvāt; tadabhāve cābhāvāt abhedapratyanīkatvāt | nāpyupamānena, tasya bhedādhiṣṭhānasādṛśyaviṣayatvena bhedasāpekṣatvāt | anumānopamāne bhedasāpekṣe, na tu bhedaṃ sākṣāt gṛhītaḥ; pratyakṣaṃ tu gṛhadāpi vastūpasarjanaṃ gṛhṇāti, na tu tameva sākṣāt | arthāpattiḥ punarabhedaviṣaye bheda eva sākṣāt pramāṇaṃ sutarāṃ pratipakṣabhūtā; yato na bhedamantareṇa pramātṛprameyādibhedavyavahāraḥ kvacidupapadyate; apekṣitaśrāsau; anyathā taṃ vinā nirbhedabrahmāvagamo'pi na syāt | abhāvo'pi na bhāvarūpe brahmaṇi tāvat pramāṇam | na ca prapañcābhāve, tasya pramāṇapañcakābhāvarūpasya pratyakṣādiṣu satstasaṃbhavāt | āgamasya bahuvaktavyatvāt pūrvamupamānādyabhāva ukta iti draṣṭavyam | evamāgamasyāpi; yasya tāvadāptaḥ praṇetā, sa pramāṇāntarādhigatavastuviṣayatvāt tadagocare brahmaṇi na pramāṇam | nāpi vedaḥ, tasya vidhiniṣedhātmakatvāt; vidhiniṣedhayośca siddhe brahmatattve'saṃbhavāt | na ca vidhiniṣedhāvanapekṣya siddhe vastuni śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ yuktam; tathā hi—ghaṭo'yamitivat siddhānuvādatve pramāṇāntarāpekṣā syāt; na ca tadalaukike brahmaṇi saṃbhavatītyaprāmāṇyameva syāt | itaśca siddhe'rthe na vedasya prāmāṇyam; yato loke śabdasya vācakasāmarthyāvagamaḥ, tatra ca kāryaparatayaiva kāryānvayitvārthe padāni prayujyante, tathā prayojanavaccāt; anyathā niṣprayojanāni syuḥ | kiṃ ca na siddhānuvādāt śabdasya vācyavācakasaṃbandhāvagamaḥ, kiṃ tu pravartakādvākyāt; yataḥ 'pikamānaya' ityukte preṣyasya pravṛttyārthapratyayaṃ vinānupapadyamānayā tasyārthapratyayamanumāya śabdānantaryāt tannimittāntarādarśanācca 'śabdenāyaṃ kṛtaḥ' iti tasya tatra sāmarthyamavagamyate; tataśca pravṛttisaṃbandhena vyutpannaśaktayaḥ śabdā na siddhaṃ brahmāvagamayitumalamiti na tatra pramāṇam | kiṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa siddhatve śabdasyānuvādakatvādaprāmāṇyam, asiddhatve natarām; ajñātasya hi brahmaṇaḥ padena saṃbandhājñānādapadārthatve vākyārthatvāyogāt | katham ? padārtha eva padārthāntaraviśiṣṭo vākyārtho bhavati; brahmapadārthastvanyena pramā-
[p. 62]
ṇenātyantamadṛṣṭo'navagatasaṃbandhatayā padādanavagamyamānaḥ padārthasaṃsargātmake vākyārthe guṇatvena pradhānatvena vā nānupraveśamarhati | tasmānna śabdapramāṇakaṃ brahma | api ca nirastasamastaviśeṣaṇaṃ brahmatattvaṃ pratītigocara eva na bhavati | katham ? sarvā hi pratītiḥ 'evaṃprakāramidam, nānyaprakāram' ityevaṃ vastu vyavacchindatī pravartate, anyathā pratītireva na syāt; nirastasamastaprakāre tu brahmaṇi kathaṃ pratītiḥ syādityevaṃ pramāṇābhāvenādvayaṃ brahmākṣipya tatra P.23-18. pramāṇamāha—āmnāyata iti | 'nirūpaṇam' ityatra pracakṣata ityanuvartate | yattāvaduktam—siddhānuvāditve vedasya pramāṇāntaratāpekṣatvaṃ syāditi, tattāvannāstīti niyogakāṇḍe vakṣyāmaḥ | yaccoktam—kāryaniṣṭhānyeva laukikavākyānīti, tadapi naikāntikam; akāryaniṣṭhānyapi hi priyākhyānāni priyamākhyāyate yaistāni 'putraste jātaḥ' ityādīni vākyāni pravṛttinivṛttiprayojanaśūnyāni loke santi; na ca tāvatā niṣprayojanānyeva, pitṛsukhotpādaprayojanatvāt | na ca tatra 'sukhī bhava' iti pravṛttyupadeśaḥ kalpyaḥ, jñātaputrajanmavastusāmarthyādeva sukhitvasiddherupadeśānapekṣatvāt | atha matam— asti tāvattatra pituḥ sukhe pravṛttiḥ; tatraiva vacatastātparyam; atastadapi pravṛttiniṣṭhameva | satyam; 'pituḥ sukhamutpannatām' ityabhisaṃdhānena prayuktatvādvākyasyotpattirūpeṇāsti tatra tātparyam; na tu tato vākyātpradhānatayā 'sukhapravṛttiḥ pratīyatām' ityevaṃ pratītirūpeṇa, tadvācakapadābhāvāt; apadārthasya cāvākyārthatvāt; yataḥ pratītiḥ siddhārthaniṣṭhaiva | etaccābhyupetyoktam; na tu sukhe pravṛttirastīti vakṣyati | na ca pitṛsukhotpādaprayojanavacce sati siddhārthaparyavasitasyaiva śabdasya "pūṣā prapiṣṭhabhāgaḥ" itivat pravṛttiparyanto vyāpāraḥ śakyaḥ kalpayitum; tena siddhārthaniṣṭhameva tadvākyam, na pravṛttiniṣṭham | api ca pravṛttiniṣṭhatve'pi na tāvat putrajanmani sukhopāye puruṣā'pravṛttaḥ pravartyaḥ, tasya siddhatvāt; nāpyupeye sukhe upāyājñānena pūrvamapravṛtta upāyajñāpanadvāreṇa pravartyaḥ; vākyārthajñānottarakālīne hi svavyāpāre puruṣaḥ pravartyaḥ; na ca sukhasya siddhya vyāpārāntaramasti, 'putraste jātaḥ' iti vākyārthajñānādeva tadutpatteḥ | etena
[p. 63]
durjanavacāṃsyapi vyākhyātāni | tathā bhramamītasya bhramāpanucaye 'rajjuriyam', P. 24-4. na tarpaḥ' iti taccākhyānaṃ svarūpaniṣṭhameva dṛśyate | na ca tatrāpi 'mā bhaiṣīḥ' iti niyogaḥ kalpyaḥ; tatra hi niyogo bhavati, yatra tajjñātvā tatsāmarthyāt tatpūrvayā cecchayā puruṣaḥ pravartate nivartate ca; atra rajjutaccajñānamātrādabhayaheto rajjusarpasatyatvābhimānasyābhāvādeva niyogamicchāṃ vānapekṣamāṇasyaiva bhayanivṛtternārtho niyogena | ka ca puruṣo niyojyaḥ? na tāvadbhayanivṛttau, niyogasyāpravṛttapravartakatvāt, bhayanivṛttau ca svata eva sargādivat pravṛttatvāt; nāpi tadupāye, rajjutaccajñānena tasya 'rajjuriyam' iti śabdādevotpatteḥ; śabdārthajñānottarakālīnasya ca vyāpārasyānyatra vidhinibandhanatvena dṛṣṭatvāt | evamatividūratvenāsaṃbhavaddhānopādānamatyantadūradeśavṛttāntākhyānavākyamapi svarūpaniṣṭhameva | na cānarthakam, kutūhalināmautsukyanivṛttyarthatvāt | yatrāpi hānopādānasaṃbhavaḥ, yathā 'eṣa pratirodhakavānadhvā' 'nidhimāneṣa bhūbhāgaḥ' iti, tatrāpi nidheḥ pramāṇāntarāvagatamupakārakatvaṃ smṛtvecchayā pravartate, pratirodhakasya cāpakārakatvaṃ smṛtvā dveṣeṇa nivartate; śabdastu bhūbhāgasvarūpamātropakṣīṇa eva, na hānopādāne vadati | nanu buddhipūrvakārī vaktā pravṛttinivṛttyarthapreva vākyaṃ prayukte, naivamevādṛṣṭāya vā; tataśca tadapi pravṛttinivṛttiniṣṭhameva; kevalamidaṃ tatra prayogārhamapyālasyādinā na prayujyate, yat—'gṛhāṇeto nidhim' 'mā vānenādhvanā gaccha' iti; svarūpaparatve'narthakametadvākyaṃ syāt | maivam; yathānarthakyaṃ svarūpe, tenāśrutapravṛttinivṛttiparyantaṃ vākyavyāpāraṃ kalpayasīti, mā tarhi kalpaya, svarūpaniṣṭhasyāpyasya pratyakṣādivat svarūpasamarpaṇadvāreṇa pravṛttinivṛttyaṅgatvopapatteḥ; pratyakṣādayo hi siddhārthaparicchedoparatavyāpārā leśato'pi pravṛttinivṛttī na gocarayanti, atha ca tadaṅgatvaṃ na jahati; tathā śabdo'pīti na doṣaḥ | pratyakṣādau vastumātramabhisaṃhitam, na pravṛttinivṛttī; idaṃ tu pravṛttinivṛttī abhisandhāya vākyaṃ prayuktam; tena te evātraṃ śabdārthāviti cet, maivam; na hi yadabhisaṃhitaṃ tacchabdārthaḥ, kiṃ tu yatra śabdasya vācakasāmarthyam | na ca
[p. 64]
P. 25-3. ‘nidhimān’ iti śabdasya ‘gṛhāṇa’ ityatra sāmarthyam | yadi tvabhisandhānamātreṇa śabdārthatvaṃ syāt, tadā nidhisādhyamupabhogādyapi tasya vakturabhisaṃhitamiti tadapi śabdārthaḥ syāt | kiṃ ca yadā ‘ghaṭe pravartiṣye, sarpānnivartiṣye’ iti cābhisandhāya tadvastu pratyakṣādibhirjijñāsyate, tadābhisaṃhite api te na śabdārthaḥ | yadi vā abhisandhānamātreṇa nidhivākye pravṛttirathaḥ syāt, tataḥ śabdaśakterekarūpatvāt sarveṣāṃ pravṛttibuddhireva syāt ; nopekṣābuddhiḥ ; asti ca sā kasyacit ; ato na pravṛttiḥ śabdārthaḥ | tena siddhārthaniṣṭhameva tadvākyamiti na loke sarvaṃ vākyaṃ kāryaniṣṭham | yadapyuktam—pravartakavākyāddhi pikānayanādivyavahāraḥ, tato loke vācyavācakasaṃbandhāvagamaḥ, tena pravṛttiniṣṭhataiva sarvaśabdānāmiti ; tadapi na, vartamānāpadeśādapi saṃbandhāvagamadarśanāt ; tathā hi—‘devadattaḥ kāṣṭhairodanaṃ pacati’ ityukte vyutpannādipadārthastathā vyutpannatṛtīyārthaśrāvyutpannakāṣṭhapadārtho yatpāke karaṇaṃ paśyati tatkāṣṭhapadārtha iti vyutpadyate | tathā harṣādiprayojanebhyaḥ ‘putraste jātaḥ’ ityādibhyastattvākhyāyakavākyebhyaḥ siddhārthebhyo'pi pravartakavākyādiva harṣādinimitteṣu putrādiṣu bhavati putrādiśabdavācyatve vyutpattiḥ | yathaiva hi pikānayanādivyavahāraviṣayaṃ pravṛttiviśeṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadanyathānupapattyā pravṛttiviśeṣajñānaṃ tannimittaniyogajñānaṃ vā bālena preṣyasyānumīyate—śabdānantarabhāvāt, kāraṇāntarasya cādarśanāt, dṛṣṭatyāgenādṛṣṭakalpanānupapatteḥ, śabdasyaiva tatra jñāne sāmarthyaṃ kalpyata iti kāryavākyārthavyutpattiḥ, tathā pitrādermukhaprasādādinā liṅgena harṣādyupalabhyākāraṇakakāryānupapattyā harṣādinimittajñānamanumīyate ; tacca śabdānantaryāt, anyasya hetoradarśanāt, śabdena kṛtamiti śabdasya tatra harṣādinimittajñāne jananasāmarthyakalpanā bhavati | tadevaṃ ‘harṣanimittaṃ kiñcidasyānena śabdenoktam’ iti sāmānyato'vagate, atha kiṃ taddharṣanimittamiti viśeṣavāñchāyāṃ svasantānajñātaharṣanimittabhāvasyāvyutpannaputraśabdavācyatvasya vyutpannaśabdāntarasya putrajanmanastasya vākyopāstsyaiva pituḥ saṃvandhitayāvagatatvāt, anyasya ca harṣanimittasyānupalabdherabhāvāt, dṛṣṭahānenādṛṣṭakalpanānupapatteśca ‘putrajanmanyevedaṃ vākyamāsena prayuktam ; anena ca pitrā tadevāsmādvākyāt pratī-
[p. 65]
tam' iti pārśvastho'vadhārayati | tadevaṃ prāguktaprakāreṇāptakṛtavākyaprayoga- P. 25-19 putrajanmagocarapitṛpratyayābhyāmasmin putrajanmākhyārthaṃ vākyasya pratipādanasāmarthyaṃ pārśveṣvaḥ pratipadyate | tataścāsti siddhārthādapi vākyāt vyutpattiḥ ; ato na kāryavākyādeva vyutpattiḥ ; ato na tadvalena kāryaikaniṣṭhatā sarvaśabdānāmiti siddhe'pyarthaṃ na prāmāṇyaṃ vyāhanyate | yadyapi padāni vyutpattimapekṣante, na vākyam, abhinavaviracitādapi vākyādarthapratīteḥ ; tathāpi vākyaprayogādvyutpattiḥ, na padamātraprayogāditi padamevābhipretya tatra vākyasya sāmarthyaṃ pratipadyata ityuktam | adhunābhyupetyocyate—bhavatu vā sarvavacasāṃ pravartakatā, tata eva vācyavācakasambandhāvagamaḥ, tathāpi vyutpattivākya eva pravartake vidhāyakapadamapoddhṛtya tadvyatiriktānāṃ padānāṃ ka tātparyam ? śaktiḥ kiṃ padārthamātre ? kiṃ vā kāryasaṃsarge ? kiṃ vā kāryeṇākāryeṇa cākāṅkṣitayogyasannihitena padārthāntaramātreṇa saṃsarge ? itīdaṃ vicāryam | tatra teṣu pakṣeṣu madhye padānāṃ sārthamātraparatve'ṅgīkriyamāṇe'yaḥśalākākalpāḥ sarve'pi padārthāḥ parasparamasaṃspṛṣṭāḥ syuḥ ; tataśca saṃsargarūpavākyārthapratyayābhāvaḥ syāt ; asti cāsau | kiṃ ca padārthamātrapratipatterniṣprayojanatvāt padaprayogavaiyarthyaṃ ca syāt | yasmādetaddoṣadvayamasmin pakṣe bhavati tasmādetaddoṣadvayaparihārāyānyonyākāṅkṣitayogyasannihitena kāryeṇākāryeṇa vārthamātreṇa vyatiṣaṅgaḥ anyonyasaṃsargaḥ kalpanīyaḥ ; tāvatā cānyārthamātravyatiṣaṅgeṇa padaprayogavākyārthapratyayayorupapattau satyāṃ prayoktṛpratipatternirākāṅkṣānna kāryarūpapadārthaviśeṣavyatiṣaṅge pramāṇamasti ; svārthamātraparyavasāne hi padānāṃ prayogapratyayāvanupapadyamānau tadatikramya padārthamātrasaṃsargaparyantatātparyaśaktiṃ gamayataḥ ; tau ca tāvataivopapattau na kāryātmakārthāntarasaṃsargaparyantatāṃ gamayitumalamiti bhāvaḥ | itaśca—yo hi kāryātmakārthāntaravyatiṣaṅgaṃ kalpayati, kāryasyāpyarthāntaratvāt kalpayatyasāvarthāntaravyatiṣaṅgam ; tenobhayavādisiddhatvādarthāntaramātravyatiṣaṅga evāśrayi tavyaḥ, na kāryavyatiṣaṅgaḥ ; vimatyadhikṛtvāditi bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca kāryasaṃsargo vyabhicarati, kāryasyākāryeṇa saṃsargat ; arthāntaramātrasaṃsargastvavyati . 5
[p. 66]
cārī ; tasmāt sa evāśrayitavyaḥ | evaṃ cetkāryākāryātmakārthāntaramātrasaṃsarge padānāṃ tātparyaśāktiravadhṛtā, tathā sati yatra loke vyavahārāya padaṃ prayujyate vede ca, tatra tena saṃsargaḥ ; yatra tu vedānte kāryaṃ na śrūyate, tatra vināpi tena padārthānāṃ parasparasaṃsargādanyonyaviśiṣṭasiddhārthapratyayasiddhiriti siddhaṃ siddhe'pyarthe śabdasya prāmāṇyam | ataśca loke vyutpattivākye dṛṣṭo'pi kāryavyatiṣaṅgo na kāryamātrānvaye śabdatātparyaśaktiniyāmakatvenāśrayitavyaḥ | etaccāvaśyaṃ tvayāpyaṅgīkartavyam, anyathā yadyokte dṛṣṭaṃ tacca sarvaṃ padaśaktiniyāmakatvenāśrayite, tato loke vyutpattivākye vaktṛvivakṣāparatvaṃ padārthānvayasya dṛṣṭamiti vede vakturabhāvena vivakṣābhāvāt vidhāyakebhyo'rthapratītirna syāt ; asti ca sā | tasmāt kāryavyatiṣaṅgo vyutpattāvatantramityakāryeṇāpi padārthānāṃ saṃsargāt siddhaṃ siddhe'pyarthe śabdasya prāmāṇyam | api ca sarveṣāṃ padānāṃ padārthānāṃ kāryeṇaiva saṃsarge'ṅgīkriyamāṇe'ruṇādīnāṃ parasparaṃ krīṇātinā ca kriyākārakalakṣaṇaḥ saṃsargo na syāt ; tatrāruṇādikārakaviśiṣṭakrayaviṣayo niyogo na pratīyeta, kiṃ tu krayamātraikapadārthasādhya eva sa syāt | atha matam—kārakaviśiṣṭadhātvarthaviṣayo hi niyogaḥ pratīyate, na dhātvarthamātraviṣayaḥ ; na ca kārakasaṃsargamantareṇa dhātvartho viśiṣṭo bhavati ; tena viniyogaṃ prati na pratyarthī, api tvarthī niyogaḥ ; tataśca niyogānvaye'pi padārthānāṃ na parasparānvayavyāghātaḥ | tatrocyate— niyogācca yāvatpūrvabhāvī tarhi viniyogaḥ | parastu bhavatu pūrvabhāvī viniyogaḥ, tathāpyasti tāvanniyogānvaya iti pṛcchati—kimato yadyevamiti | pa. 26-8. siddhāntī tu svābhiprāyaṃ prakaṭayati—evaṃ sati asti niyogātiriktapadārthānvaye'pi padasya tātparyasāmarthyam ; ataḥ siddho naḥ pakṣaḥ | nanu niyogānvayānāmanyonyamanvayaḥ, na tu tannirapekṣāṇām ; ato niyoganibandhana evāsāviti na tena vinopaniṣatsu padānāmanvayaśaktiriti ; tatrāha— niyoganirapekṣameva padānāmanyonyānvayasāmarthyam | kutaḥ ? pūrvaṃ parasparānvitānāṃ satāṃ padānāṃ paścānniyogenānvayāt | ayamāśayaḥ—viśiṣṭena hi viṣayeṇa niyogasyānvayastvayeṣṭaḥ ; na cāsau kārakairananvito viśiṣṭo bhavati ; na cāviśiṣṭo niyogenānvayite ; aviśiṣṭasyāviṣayatvāt, aviṣayasya ca
[p. 67]
niyogenānapekṣaṇām, apekṣānibandhanatvāccāsya ; tasmānniyogānvayātpūrvaḥ padānāmanvaya iti | niyogānvayanirapekṣa eva cet, vināpi tenopaniṣatsu siddhe'pyarthe'nvayaḥ siddha iti | nanu yadi nāma niyogānvayapūrvaḥ padārthānvayo na bhavati, tathāpi niyogākāṅkṣānibandhano'sau ; sa hi viśiṣṭaviṣayasiddhaye padārthānāmanyonyānvayamākāṅkṣate ; anyathā kāryānvayaśūnyaḥ padārthānvayo'pi vyarthaḥ syāt, ekavākyatā ca na syāt | evaṃ cedupaniṣatsu na niyogena vinānvayasiddhirityāha—na ca niyogākāṅkṣānibandhanaḥ padārthānāṃ parasparasaṃsarga P. 26-10 itīmamarthaṃ vakṣyati niyogakāṇḍe | yaccoktam—brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntarāsiddhatve saṃbandhāgrahādapadārthatve satyavākyatvamiti, tatrocyate—pramāṇāntareṇānadhigatamapi brahma tata eva brahmāntaryāmyādisvaśabdenānadhigatavācyatvasaṃbandhamapi śakyam “asthūlamanaṇvahasvam” ityādiśabdena nirūpayitum | katham? ucyate— ‘sthūlo'ham’ ‘aṇuraham’ ‘hasvo'ham’ ityavidyādhyastasthūlādiviśeṣapratiṣeṣadvāreṇa | na ca yena śabdena tathā pratipādyate tasyāpyasaṃgatidoṣastulya iti vācyam, sthūlādiviśeṣaśabdānāṃ nañaśca sena senārthena viditasaṃbandhatvāt | na ca brahmāntaryāmyādiśabdavadasaṃgatidoṣo'sti | tena svasiddhasya jīvasya sthūlādiviśeṣaniṣedhamukhenāsthūlādiśabdānāmasti vigataniḥśeṣadoṣatattvābhiṣānaśaktiriti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca śrutyāpi ‘asthūlamanaṇvahasvamadīrghamalohitam’ ityādyayā itthameva sthūlādiviśeṣaniṣedhamukhenaiva tadbrahma sarvaviśeṣātigaṃ sarvān sthūlatvādiviśeṣānatikrāntamupadiśyate ; nāsmābhiḥ svotprekṣyedamuktamiti bhāvaḥ | ukte'rthe ślokārthaṃ yojayati—etatkathayatīti | etacca caturthe kāṇḍe vakṣyata itīha na prapañcitam | itīti samāptau | asyaiva ślokasyānyo'rtha ucyate—yadapyuktaṃ pūrvapakṣe pratyastamitasakalaviśeṣaṃ brahma pratipatterevāviṣaya iti, tatrocyate—viśeṣanivṛttyaiva tannirviśeṣaṃ vastu śabdena pratipādyate | idamuktaṃ syāt—dvividhaṃ hi pratipādanaṃ dṛṣṭam; ekaṃ viśeṣamukhena, yathā ‘ayaṃ kuṇḍalī, sa kaṭhakī’ iti; ekaṃ ca viśeṣaniṣedhamukhena, yathā ‘nāṅgadī na makuṭī, sa na kaṭhakī’ iti | tataśca yadi nāma virviśeṣaṃ tattvam ‘itthaṃbhūtam’ iti viśeṣamukhena vaktuṃ na śakyate, tathāpyavidyādhyastaviśeṣaniṣedhamukhena pratipādayiṣyate pratipattyate ceti | atra 6A

P. 26-16. dṛṣṭāntamāha—suvarṇaṃ tattvavaditi | tatprapañcayati—na hīti | suvarṇatattvaṃ piṇḍarucakādisaṃsthānaviśeṣairya upalabdhaḥ—uparāgaḥ, saṃmiśratvam, apṛthagbhāvaḥ—tena rahitaṃ rucakādivilakṣaṇaviśeṣaśāli, tebhyaḥ pṛthak na dṛśyate iti | na ca rucakādaya eva suvarṇatattvamiti vācyam, rucakatyāgena saṃsthānāntare svastike'pi bhāvāt; suvarṇatattvasya hi rucakābhede ca rucakavat tadapi svastike na bhavet; asti ca; tasmāt tataḥ pṛthak | evaṃ svastike'pi draṣṭavyam | evaṃ suvarṇatattvaṃ rucakādibhyo'nyadapi pṛthak na dṛśyate; atha ca tādṛśamapi tanna svastikaḥ, rucake bhāvāt; na rucakaḥ; svastike bhāvāt; ato'nyaditi vivekabuddhyā ‘na tadbuddhako na svastikaḥ’ iti rucakādibhedāpohadvāreṇa rucakādiviśeṣaniṣedhe tebhyaḥ sarvāntaraṃ suvarṇatattvaṃ svayaṃ pratīyate parasmai ca pratipādyate, tathā brahmāpītyarthaḥ | na dṛṣṭaḥ saṃsthānabhedopalabdhāt viveko yasyeti vigrahaḥ | etacca gotvādāvapi tulyam; udāharaṇamātraṃ suvarṇam | imamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—sa eṣa iti | asminnevārthe'bhiyuktavākyadvayaṃ paṭhati—tatheti | uktamiti vākyaśeṣaḥ | sthūlādyākārāṇāṃ viśeṣāṇāmupasaṃhāre vilaye ante avasāne yat vyavatiṣṭhate tatsatyamityarthaḥ | tathā parairapyuktam—paramārthato niṣprapañcameva brahma; tadavidyayā prapañcasyādhyāropeṇa tadapavādana tanniṣedhena ca prapañcyate vyākhyāyata ityarthaḥ | evaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prapañcavilayadvāreṇāmnāyagamyatvamuktvā āmnāyaviśeṣanirdhāraṇāya idamidānīṃ vicāryate—kiṃ kṛtsna eva svarūpakāryaniṣṭha āmnāyo bhedaniṣedhadvāreṇādvayaṃ brahma gamayati, uta kaścidasyāmnāyasya vedāntākhya ekadeśa iti | tatra tasmin saṃdehe kecidāhuḥ—sarvatrāmnāye kacit kasyacidvedasya pravilayo gamyate | tena tadadvāreṇa kṛtsna evāmnāyo brahma gamayatīti bhāvaḥ | tad darśayati—yathā “sargakāmo yajet” ityāmnāyāt śarīrasyātmatvapravilayo gamyata iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | katham? ucyate—atra hi vākye dehasya lokāntaramananuvartamānasya jaḍasya ca janmāntarīyasvargopabhogasāmarthyābhāvāt tadyatirikto janmāntarānuvartī cetanaśca svargopabhogasamarthyo'dhikārī gamyate; sa eva catvāmeti dehātmavilayo gamyata iti
[p. 69]
pūrveṇaivānvayaḥ | tathā “godohanena paśukāmasya” ityatra godohanasyākriyātmano'nanyotpādanasvabhāvasya sākṣātpaśusādhanatvāsaṃbhavāt kriyāṃ svāśrayamapekṣamāṇasya prakaraṇāt dārśapaurṇamāsikīṃ praṇayanakriyāmāśritya paśusādhanatvamityarthāt darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛtasyaivādhikārādadhikārī bhinno niṣedhyate | P. 27-4. nanvevamādiṣu bhavatu bhedavilayaḥ, vidhiniṣedhacodanāsu tu pravṛttinivṛttimātraphalāsu tadasaṃbhavānna kṛtsnānnāyo bhedapravilayapara ityāśaṅkya tatrāpi bhedapravilayamāha—tatheti | tathā vidhiniṣedhacodanāsvapi svābhāvikīnāṃ rāgadveṣanibandhanānāṃ bāhyapravṛttīnāṃ niṣedho gamyata ityanvayaḥ | tatra niṣedheṣu sākṣādeva ‘nedaṃ kuru’ iti pravṛttyantaraniṣedho'vagamyata ityāha— niṣedheṣviti | nanvevamastu niṣedheṣu ; vidhiṣu tu pravṛttimātraphaleṣu kathamityata āha—vidhiṣviti | vidhiṣu tu “sandhyāmupāsīta” iti pravṛttyantaraniyogena tatkālabāhyapravṛttiniṣedho'vagamyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati—ubhayatrāpi bāhyapravṛttivilayo na viśiṣyate; ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—yanniṣedheṣu sākṣāt, vidhiṣu punararthāditi | lokadṛṣṭe cedaṃ prakāradvayamityāha—loka iti | loke'pyanabhipretāt pathaḥ sakāśāt ‘anenādhvanā mā gaccha’ iti sākṣādvā nivāraṇaṃ dṛśyate, mārgāntaropadeśena vā vivakṣitānabhimatamārganiṣedhenārthāt | tadevaṃ vidhiniṣedhacodanāsvapi rāgādinibandhanapravṛttivilayo gamyata iti sthitam | evaṃ caiṣa guṇo bhavati—rāgādinibandhanaisargikapravṛttirūpabhedavilayadvāreṇa mukṣukṣoḥ śāntatādadhikāriviśeṣaṇamupapādayanto dṛṣṭenaivopakāreṇāvadhātādivat sannipatyopakāritayā karmavidhayaḥ “ātmā jñātavyaḥ” ityātmajñānādhikāropayoginaḥ syutthidṛṣṭakalpanāgauravaṃ na bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | śrutyāpi rāgādinibandhanāśeṣabāhyapravṛttinivṛttiradhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ darśitamityāha—tathā hīti | śāntasya samāhitasya cātmani viṣaye darśanamupadiśyate—“tasmācchānto dānta uparatastitikṣuḥ samāhito bhūtvā ātmanyevātmānaṃ paśyet” ityādiśrutyā | tatra śānta ityantaḥkaraṇapūrvakarāgādijapravṛttinivṛttiradhikāriviśeṣaṇamuktam; dānta iti vahiḥkaraṇapūrvakarāgādijapravṛttinivṛttiḥ | anye tu viparyayamāhuḥ—avaśyaṃ caitadadhikāriviśeṣaṇamāsyeyam, yataḥ samartho'dhikārī bhavati; taccātmajñānaṃ śāntadāntasamāhitasyaiva śakyam,
[p. 70]
netarasya ; tena sa evādhikārī, natara iti bhāvaḥ | kutaḥ P. 27-10. punaritarasyātmajñānamaśakyamityata āha — na hīti | viṣaye ramaṇīyatayābhimukhamākṛṣyamāṇo viṣayasyopāye yā pravṛttiḥ tatra kṛtaṃ ceto yena tatprasaktamanā yaḥ, sa yasmānna śaknotyātmani viṣaye samādhānaṃ kartum, na vāstamāhitaḥ śaknotyātmānaṃ vettum, ato'samarthatvānnāsāvadhikārīti bhāvaḥ | yaḥ punā rāganibandhanābhyo naisargikībhyaḥ pravṛttibhya uparataḥ śānto dānto'ta eva niyatamānaso nirastavāhvāntaravikṣepaḥ sa śaknotyātmani samādhātumityātmajñānasamarthatvādātmadarśane'dhikriyate | tasmānnaisargikapravṛttiniṣedhaparatve karmavidhayo dṛṣṭenaiva jñānādhikāropayogino bhavantīti tatparā eva te yuktā iti kecit manyante | anye tvanyathā ātmajñānādhikāropayogitāṃ karmavidhīnāṃ manyante ; kathaṃ tat ? ucyate — anavāptakāmaḥ, kāmyanta iti kāmāḥ svargādayaḥ, anavāptāḥ kāmā yena so'navāptakāmaḥ ; kāmopahatamanāḥ, kāmanā kāmaḥ viṣayasukhābhilāṣaḥ, tenopahatamasvasthīkṛtaṃ mano yasya sa paramādvaitadarśanayogyo na bhavatīti na tatrādhikriyate ; na khalvayogyo'dhikāraṃ pratipadyate | yaḥ punarāśrānādārabhya sahasrasaṃvatsarākhyasatraparyantaiḥ karmabhirupanītāśeṣakāmyairavāptāśeṣakāmyatayā kṛtakāmanibarhaṇaḥ, kṛtaḥ kāmasya viṣayasukhābhilāṣasya nibarhaṇaṃ vināśo yasya sa prājāpatyāt prajāpateridaṃ prājāpatyaṃ padaṃ tataḥ paramavāptāśeṣakāmyatayā anupahatamanāḥ sannadvaitamātmānaṃ pratipadyate | evaṃ kāmanibarhaṇadvāreṇa karmavidhīnāmātmajñānādhikāropayoga ityanye manyante | evaṃ caiṣa sarvatrānnāye vākyārtho jñātaḥ — sahasrasaṃvatsarāntaṃ karmajātamātmani jijñāsunā kāryamiti | ubhayorapi cānantarayoḥ karmavidhīnāmātmajñānādhikāropayogitvapakṣayoḥ kṛtsna āmrāya ātmajñānaikakāryaparyavasāyī, ātmajñāne ekasmin kārye paryavasyati samāpyate ; tatpara iti yāvat | na bhāvāntarakāryaparyavasāyītyekaprahaṇam | tatrādye pakṣe vivakṣitāvāntaraniyogaphalānāṃ karmaniyogānāmātmajñānaparamaniyogānvayaḥ, dvitīye tvavāntarakarmaniyogaphaladvāreṇeti vivekaḥ |
[p. 71]
matāntaramāha—anyeṣāṃ darśanamiti | pṛthakkāryā eva, mokṣaphalāt P. 27-17 jñānāt pṛthakkāryāḥ pṛthakkalāḥ ; pṛthakkaraṇā vā, karaṇaṃ kāryaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ ; svapradhānā iti yāvat ; te pṛthakkāryā eva santo mumukṣuṃ puruṣamātmajñānādhikāramavatārayanti ; adhikārayogyaṃ kurvantīti yāvat | dvikarmako'yaṃ nirdeśaḥ ‘ajāṃ grāmaṃ nayati’ iti yadvat | kutaḥ ? anapākṛtaṛṇatrayasya, anapākṛtamṛṇatrayaṃ yena, tasya tatrātmajñāne'nadhikārāt | kutastasyānadhikāraḥ ? “ṛṇāni trīṇyapākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet” iti manuvacanāt | ṛṇatrayaṃ ca śrutyaivoktam—“jātastribhi ṛṇai ṛṇavān jāyate brahmacaryeṇarṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ” iti | nanu kāmanivarhaṇapakṣe'pi pṛthakkāryatvaṃ karmavidhīnāmastyeva ; ataḥ ko'sya pakṣasya viśeṣaḥ ? ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ—tatra hi na phalaṃ phalāyoktam, kiṃ tu kāmāvāptyā tatkāmanivṛttāvakāmasyātmajñānādhikāralabhāya ; tena tatrāvāntarakāryamavivakṣitam ; iha tu vivakṣitāvāntararūpāpākaraṇakāryā eva karmavidhaya- “bṛhaspatisaveneṣṭvā vājapeyena yajeta” iti vadātmajñānādhikāropayogina iti na doṣaḥ | matāntaraṃ cāha—anye tviti | śrutasargādiphalasaṃprayuktānāmata eva prayojanāntaranirapekṣāṇāmapi “tametaṃ yajñena dānena tapasānāśakana vividiṣanti” iti vidinā saṃyogapṛthaktvena jñānenāpi pṛthak sambandhāntaraśruteḥ “ekasya tūbhayatve saṃyogapṛthaktvam” iti nyāyena “svādiro yūṣo bhavati” “yūṣe paśuṃ badhnāti” “svādiraṃ vīryakāmasya” itivat nityanaimittikakāmyānāṃ sarvakarmaṇāmevātmajñānādhikārānupraveśaṃ viniyogādevānye tvāhuriti sambandhaḥ | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | viśeṣaśrātra pakṣe jñāne karmānvayasya viniyogitvam | tathā manovikṣepanivṛttidvāreṇa karmaṇāmātmajñānādhikārānupraveśitvaparaṃ śrutyantaramāha—yena keneti | matāntaramāha—anye tviti | saṃskṛto hi puruṣa ātmajñānādhikārayogyo bhavatītyarthaḥ | atrārthaṃ smṛtidvayamāha—mahāyajñairiti | mahāyajñaiḥ brahmadevapitṛmanupyabhūtavajñaiḥ itarayajñairiyaṃ tanuḥ brāhmī brahmaṇyadhikṛtā, ye yā
[p. 72]
brahmavidyāyām “tadadhīte tadveda” ityaṇo vidhānāt, kriyata ityarthaḥ | tanuriti tātsthye upacārādātmocyate | sa hi brahmajñāne'dhikārī brahmavicca na tanuḥ, jaḍatvāt | yasya puṃso garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇānnaprāśanacaulopanayanavedavratacatuṣṭayastrānasahacāriṇīsaṃyogapañcamahāyajñāṣṭakāpārvaṇaśrāddhaśrāvaṇyāgrahāyaṇīcaitrāśvayujyākhyasaptapākayajñasaṃsthābhyādheyāgnihotradarśanurṇamāsacāturbhāsyāgrayaṇeṣṭinirūḍhapaśubandhasautrāmaṇyākhyasaptahaviryajñasaṃsthāgniṣṭomātyagniṣṭomokthavaṣoḍaśivājapeyātirātrāptoryāmākhyasaptasomasaṃsthā ityete catvāriṃśatsaṃskārāḥ, aṣṭau ca dayākṣāntyanasūyāśaucānāyāsamaṅgalākārpaṇyāspṛhākhyāśrātmaguṇāḥ, sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ brahmaṇā hiraṇyagarbheṇa sahaikatvaṃ sālokyaṃ samānalokatāṃ ca gacchati ; tatra pralaye pare brahmaṇi līyata iti kramamuktiṃ darśayati | taduktam— “brahmaṇā saha te sarve saṃprāpte pratisañcare | parasyānte kṛtātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ padam ||” iti | P. 28-4. matāntaramāha—anye tviti | etadeva puruṣasaṃskārakatvaṃ viparītaṃ yatkarmaṇāmātmajñānādhikāraṃ pratyuktam, tadātmajñānasyaiva karmādhikāraṃ prati varṇayanti | tadevāha—ātmajñānamityādinā | dehavyatiriktanityakartṛpoktrātmavid eva svakarmasvadhikāraḥ, netarasya | tathā coktam—“na hyanadhyātmavit kaścit kriyāphalamupāśnute” iti | tena karmasūpayokṣyamāṇasya karturayaṃ saṃskāra iti bhāvaḥ | matāntaraṃ ca—anye tviti | sādhyasādhanādidvaitaviṣayaṃ karma, “ekamevādvitīyam” iti cādvaitaviṣayamātmajñānam ; ato'nayoḥ parasparavirodhādasaṃbandha evetyanye manyante | evaṃ saptapakṣānupanyasyādyaṃ pakṣaṃ dūṣayati—tatreti | kalpa iti kalpanetyarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—na hīti | karmādītyādiśabdena godohanādidravyavidhayo'bhimatāḥ ; yadvā karmaśabdena pravṛttilakṣaṇo dharma uktaḥ, ādiśabdena tu nidyattilakṣaṇaṃ tapaḥ ; tadvidhayaḥ ; te svavākyasamadhigatasvargādikāryāḥ,
[p. 73]
svavākyasamadhigataṃ svargādi kāryaṃ phalaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ ; ata eva kāryāntaram ātmajñānākhyaṃ kāryaṃ nāpekṣante, svavākyaśrutaphalairākāṅkṣyāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvevamastu karmavidhīnāmanapekṣatvam ; ātmajñānavidhistu sādhanatvena tānapekṣiṣyate ; bhavati caikāpekṣayāpi saṃbandha ityāśaṅkyāha—nāpīti | P. 28-9. tatraivaṃ satyubhayornirākāṅkṣyayorākāṅkṣālakṣaṇaparasparasaṃbandhābhāvāt kuta ekakāryatvenaikādhikārakatvaṃ karmajñānavidhyorityarthaḥ | atra paramatamāśaṅkyati— atheti | svavākye'navagataphalāḥ karmavidhayaḥ phalākāṅkṣāyāmātmajñānavidhyadhikāramanupravekṣyantīti bhāvaḥ | nanu “svargakāmo yajeta” iti śrūyate, tataḥ kathamanavagataphalatvamityāśaṅkyāha—svargādīnāmiti | satyaṃ svargādi śrūyate ; kiṃ tu prītikṛtacandanādidravyaṃ tat ; yacca dravyaṃ, tat ‘bhūtabhavyasamuccāraṇe bhūtaṃ bhavyāyopadiśyate’ iti karmaṇi guṇatvenāpradhānatvena sādhanatayeti yāvat, abhisaṃbandhyate ; na pradhānatayā phalatveneti bhāvaḥ | yadvā svargakāma iti puruṣaviśeṣaṇatvena svargādīnāṃ guṇatvenābhisambandhāt na phalatvamityarthaḥ | siddhāntī tu sopahāsamāha—svargeti | tatra hi prītiḥ svargaḥ, na dravyam ; prītyarthaṃ ca sarvam, na prītiranyārthā ; ataḥ sā phalatvena pradhānatayā saṃbandhyate, na sādhanatvād guṇatvena puṃviśeṣaṇam | api ca svargādi kāmyamānatayā pradhānamityevaṃ sthitam ; tadapyanena svargādiguṇatvavādinā nāvabuddham ; ato'smai tadadhikaraṇaṃ vyācakṣītetyarthaḥ | api ca sarvavidhiniṣedhānāṃ nāma śabdaḥ, rūpam arthaḥ, tadubhayātmakaprapañcavilaya eva kāryaṃ tadapavargitve tatparyavasāyitve'bhyupagamyamāne vidhiniṣedhānāmanyaparatvena niṣpramāṇakatvāt, vihitapratiṣiddhayorarthānarthasādhanatvaṃ na syāt ; tatra śra prāṇināṃ ya ime svargādayaḥ abhyudayāḥ, ye ca narakādiṣvapi vinipātāḥ, te vihitapratiṣiddhakarmanimittā na syuḥ ; ataścākarmanimittāḥ santa ākasmikā akāraṇakāḥ syuḥ ; na cākāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | yadi tvabhyudayanipātā ākasmikā bhaveyuḥ, tato'pavargo'pi tathā tadvadevākasmikaḥ syāt ; tataśca tadupāyopadeśakasya śāstrasya vaiyarthyaṃ syāt ; tadāha—tatheti | itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ |
[p. 74]
atha matam—yadā kadācit kaścit kvacidvakti ‘nagaraṃ gaccha, tatra tāvadamuktaṃ grāmaṃ gaccha, tato'muktam’ iti; atra yathā tattadbhāmaprāptikāryāṇāmapi taddṛṣṭāreṇa mārgagrāmagamanopadeśānāṃ nagaragamanopadeśānupraveśitvam, evaṃ karmavidhīnāṃ svavākyasamadhigatasvargādikāryāṇāmapi svargādikāryadṛṣṭāreṇa jñānādhikārānupraveśitvāt bhinnakāryatve'pyekādhikāryatvaṃ bhaviṣyati; tadāha P. 28-14 — atheti | svargādilobhāt karmavidhiṣu pravṛttau bāhyapravṛttivilayāt svargādikāryasya dvāratvaṃ mantavyam | tat dṛṣṭāntaṃ vaiṣamyeṇa dūṣayati—tadasaditi | tatra hi nagaraprāptirvakturabhilaṣitā, na tu mārgagrāmaprāptiḥ; ato'nīhitagrāmaprāptyupadeśānāṃ yuktamīhitanagaraprāptyupadeśānupraveśitvamityarthaḥ | kasmātpunarmārgagrāmaprāptiranīhitetyata āha—na hīti | nagaraprāptau hi parābhipratipuruṣārthaprāptirbhavati, na mārgagrāmaprāptau; tena nagaraprāptireva vakturīhitā, na mārgagrāmaprāptirityarthaḥ | ato mārgagrāmopadeśāḥ puruṣārthasākāṅkṣatvāt yatra nagaragamanopadeśe puruṣārthaprāptiḥ, tamanupraviśanti; tadāha—ata iti | karmavidhiṣu naivamityāha—na tvevamiti | svargādīnāṃ svavākyeṣu śrutatvāditi bhāvaḥ | na ca śrutānāmapyapuruṣārthatvam, tat kutaḥ karmavidhiṣu puruṣārthalābha iti vācyamityāha—svargādīnāmiti | na hi puruṣārtho nāma jātyā kaścidasti, kiṃ tu yat puruṣeṇārthate sa puruṣārthaḥ; svargādayaśca tathā; ataste puruṣārthā iti na karmavidhiṣu puruṣārthalābhaḥ | tathāpi kimityāha—tatra ceti | tatra tasmin svargādipuruṣārthalābhe sati karmavidhīnāṃ mokṣātmakapuruṣārthāntaraṃ prati nairākāṅkṣyāt kathamanyasminnātmajñānavidhāvanupraveśa ityarthaḥ | evaṃ puruṣārthakāryavaccena dārśanikadṛṣṭāntayorvaiṣamye ukte paraḥ punaḥ sāmyamāpādayati—atheti | ‘imaṃ grāmaṃ gaccha, tatrāyaṃ guṇo yatte mṛṣṭānnādi sampatsyate’ ityupadiṣṭastatra bhato labdhamṛṣṭānnaścānenaiva krameṇottarottaragrāmaguṇapradarśanenābhimatanagarapradarśaṃ pralobhya yadā kaścit nīyate, tadā pūrvagrāmopadeśāḥ prāptābhimatakāryāḥ, prāptaṃ labdhamabhimataṃ puruṣārtharūpaṃ mṛṣṭānnādi kāryaṃ yaiste tathoktāḥ, svavākye labdhamṛṣṭānnādipuruṣārthanirākāṅkṣā iti yāvat; te yathā tannirākāṅkṣāśca nagaragamanavidhyantarānupraveśinaśca, tathā karmavidhayo'pi svargādikāryanirākāṅkṣā api
[p. 75]
jñānavidhimanupravekṣyantīti bhāvaḥ | taddṛṣayati— yuktamiti | etadeva vivṛṇoti P. 28-22 — pramāṇāntareti | etaduktaṃ bhavati— dadyapi pūrvagrāmopadeśāḥ phalavatpūrvagrāmaprāptiparāḥ śabdavṛttānusāreṇa pratibhānti, tathāpi 'nagaraprāptāvasya vakturidamabhimataṃ phalam; anenaiva cābhiprāyeṇa tattadbhāmanagaragamanamupadiṣṭavān' iti pramāṇāntareṇa pratipadya pūrvagrāmopadeśānāṃ nagaropadeśānupraveśaṃ pratipadyate; tena pramāṇāntare sati bhāvāt tasyāyaṃ prasādaḥ, na śabdavṛttasyeti | na kevalaṃ pramāṇāntare sati bhāvāt, asati cābhāvādapyevamityāha— itaśceti | pratigrāmaṃ vaktuḥ śrotuśca satyāṃ mṛṣṭānnādiprayojanaprāptau yadā vakturnagaragrāmagamanābhiprāyaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa nāvagamyate, tadā pūrvagrāmopadeśān pūrvārthān pūrvo'rtho mṛṣṭānnādi prayojanaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ tān pūrvārthāneva pārśvasthā udāsīnāḥ pratipattāraśca manyante; na tu nagaragrāmaprāptyarthān; nagaropadeśaṃ ca svaniṣṭhaṃ manyante | naiṣāmekārthatvam | yadi tu śabdavṛttānusāreṇa pūrvopadeśānāmuttarārthatvaṃ syāt, tatastasya niyatarūpatvāt tatrāpi syāt; na tvevamasti; tasmāt pramāṇāntaraprasādaḥ sa iti bhāvaḥ | nanu pūrvapūrvagrāmaprāptiṃ vinottarottaranagaraprāptyabhāvāt tadarthā api kiṃ na syurityāśaṅkyāha— yadyapīti; nopakāramātreṇa śabdamaidamarthaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atra nyāyasiddhadṛṣṭāntamāha— dravyārjanādīnīti | yathā yajanādidravyārjananiyamavidheradravyasya kratorasiddheḥ kratuvidhyupakāre satyapi na śabdaṃ kratvaidamartham, tadvadityarthaḥ | ādiśabdenādhānādividhergrahaṇam | evaṃ dṛṣṭānte śabdato nirākāṅkṣāṇāmapi pramāṇāntarādhīnaṃ vidhyantarānupraveśitvamupapādya karmavidhiṣu tadasaṃbhavamāha— śabdavṛtteti | śabdavyāpārānusāreṇeha karmavidhiṣu tātparyaṃ yuktam; na pramāṇāntarabalena, vaidike'rthe tadasaṃbhavāt | śabdavṛttaṃ ca pratividhyarthaṃ paryāptam, na jñānavidhiparyantaṃ yāti; ato na tadanupraveśa iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca mārgagrāmopadeśānāmapi na paradeśaprāptiparatā; ato dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ; yato'vagate'pi paradeśagamanābhiprāye 'taccedaṃ cāsyābhipretam' iti mārgagrāmaprāptiparatvābhimānenaiva mārgagrāmagamanamṛṣṭānnādiphalaprāptiśca bhavati nagaraprāptiparatve tu teṣāṃ mārgagrāmaprāpteravivakṣitatvāt nagaraprāptereva vivakṣitatvānmārgāntareṇāpi gacchet | na ca niyogataḥ phalaprāptiḥ syāt,
[p. 76]
