anyapareṣu phalaśruteḥ “dravyasaṃskārakarmasu parārthatvāt phalaśrutirarthavādaḥ syāt” P. 29-6. ityarthavādatvāditi | tadetatsarvamāha—api ceti | imameva nyāyamupajīvyāha—taditi | yadi vidhiniṣedhānāṃ jñānakāryamākṣaparatvaṃ syāt, tato na te sargādikāryāḥ syuḥ; ataśca jantūnāmabhyudayavinipātā nirnimittāḥ syuriti bhāvaḥ; sargādiḥ kāryo yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ | yadyapi karmavidhīnāmuttaro vicāro'nuvartate, tathāpi tulyadoṣatvādupakrame copanyāsādiha niṣedhānāṃ nikṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ | sargādītyādiśabdena narakādyapi grāhyam | nanu pradhānopakāralakṣaṇaṃ prayājādikāryaṃ yathā pradhānakāryasargādiparamapi nāvivakṣitam, tathā sargādyapi kāryamātmajñānakāryamākṣaparamapi nāvivakṣitaṃ bhaviṣyati; tataśca vidhiniṣedhā jñānakāryaparā api sargādikāryāḥ kiṃ na syurityāśaṅkyāha—svasviti | prayājādeḥ kāryasya svayamapuruṣārthatvāt puruṣārthakāryānupraveśo yuktaḥ; na tu sargādikāryasya, svayaṃ puruṣārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ | evaṃ tāvacchrutasvargādinirākāṅkṣāḥ karmavidhayo nātmajñānādhikāramanupraviśantīti sthitam | idānīṃ yaduktam—dṛṣṭenaiva karmavidhaya ātmajñānādhikāropayogina iti, taddṛṣayati—kathaṃ ceti | atra parābhiprāyamāśaṅkyati—yadīti | yadi tāvadgaṇadveṣādiprayuktadṛṣṭārthapradṛttipratibandhadvāreṇa samāhitatvamāpādayantaḥ karmavidhayo dṛṣṭenātmajñānādhikāropayogina ityucyata ityarthaḥ | taddṛṣayati—bhavatviti | pratiṣedhānāṃ rāgādiprāptapravṛttinivṛttyarthatvādbhavatu jñānādhikāraṃ prati dṛṣṭopakāritvam, na ca karmavidhīnāmityarthaḥ | atra hetumāha—na hīti | parisaṃkhyāyāṃ hi rāgatastatra cānyatra ca prāptau “pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyāḥ” “śaśakaḥ śākhakaḥ” iti śaśakādivyatiriktamanuṣyādipañcanakhabhakṣaṇanivṛttiparaṃ vākyam | tathā “brīhibhiryajeta” iti yāgāpekṣadravyamātraprāptau tadantargatatvena brīherapi prāptatvādravyāntaranivṛttiparaṃ vākyam | karmavidhayastu na niyāmakāḥ, na parisaṃkhyāyakāḥ; kiṃ tu vidhāyakā eva; ato nānyanivṛttiparā ityarthaḥ | kuta ityata āha—atyantamiti | na
[p. 77]
prāpto'rtho viṣayo rāgādinā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ, karmaviṣayo'nyapadārthaḥ | na hyabhihotrādau rāgādibhiḥ pravṛttiḥ prāpteti bhāvaḥ | kasmātpunaratyantamaprāptārthā api karmaviṣayo niyāmakāḥ parisaṃkhyāyakā vā na syurityatra hetumāha—prāptārtho hati | rāgādibhiḥ prāpto viṣayo yasya vidheḥ sa 1, 29-14. prāptaprāpakatve vaiyarthyādanyanivṛttiphalo vijñāyate; karmaviṣayastvatyantamaprāptaṃ prāpayanta eva saprayojanā iti na vaiyarthyadoṣādanyanivṛttiprayojanā vijñāyanta ityarthaḥ | taduktam— “vidhiratyantamaprāpte niyamaḥ pākṣike sati | tatra cānyatra ca prāptau parisaṃkhyeti kīrtyate” || iti | yadyapi nāma karmaviṣayo na rāgādijapravṛttinivṛttiphalāḥ, tathāpi rāgādijavaidhapravṛttyostulyakāryatvalakṣaṇena virodhena vaidhapravṛttyā rāgādijapravṛtternivṛttirbhaviṣyati; tathā hi—yadeva rāgādijapravṛtteḥ sukham, tajjyotiṣṭhomādivaidhapravṛtterapi; tacca vaidhapravṛttita eva siddhamiti kṛtakāryatvādrāgādijapravṛtternivṛttiriti yadyucyeta tadapi netyāha—na ceti | kuta ityāha—aniyateti | aniyatakālaṃ phalaṃ yāsāmiti vigrahaḥ | aniyatakālaphalā naiyogikyaḥ pravṛttaya iha vāmutra vā phalapradāḥ; ato'dṛṣṭārthāstāḥ; rāgādiprayuktāstu svābhāvikyo rāgādeḥ puruṣasvabhāvājjātāḥ pravṛttayo dṛṣṭārthāḥ | dṛṣṭārthatvenaihikaphalatvaṃ lakṣayanti; aihikaphalā ityarthaḥ | tataśca bhinnakālakāryatvāt tayoravirodha iti na naiyogikyā pravṛttyā rāgādijapravṛttervādhaḥ | yadvā abhyārhitatvād dṛṣṭārthayaivādṛṣṭārthāyā bādhaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu sargādiphalamāmuṣmikameva; tatkathamaviśeṣeṇoktam— aniyatakālaphalā iti ? vidhiśaktimapekṣyedamuktam, na vastunaḥ śaktim; na hi vidhiriha vā amutra vā sarga ityāha, kālaviśeṣānirdeśāt; vastuśaktistvepā—yadiha sargo bhoktuṃ na śakyata ityamutraiva syāditi | yadvā upalakṣaṇamaniyatakālatvam, āmuṣmikaphalatvamapi draṣṭavyam; tatrāpi kālabhedādāvarodha eva | viparīto vā bādhaḥ | kiṃ ca yadi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthapravṛttyostulyakāryatve virodhaḥ syāt, tataḥ sevāsāṅgahaṇyorapi syāt; na caiva-
[p. 78]
P. 29-17. mastītyāha—na ceti | sāṅgahaṇyā grāmakāmeṣṭeradṛṣṭārthāyā dṛṣṭārthāyāśreśvarasevāyā ekagrāmakāryopāyatve'pi sati na kaścidvirodhaḥ, phalamūrmārthina ubhayatra pravṛttisaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | viruddhapādapi sāṅgahaṇī sevām, yadi tadvidhiśāstramanyanivṛttau pramāṇaṃ bhavet; ‘sāṅgahaṇī’ kāryā ityetāvati tu tatpramāṇam, nānyanivṛttau; ato na tayorvirodha ityāha—etāvatīti | tatraivamavirodhe sati yugapat krameṇa vā phalamūrmārthinaḥ phalabahutvārthinaḥ sevāsāṅgahaṇyāvanutiṣṭhataḥ ko virodha ityāha—tatreti | tataśca yathā tatra tulyakāryatve'pyavirodhaḥ, tatheha naiyogikarāgākṣiptapravṛttyorapi tulyakāryatve'pyavirodhānna mitho bādhyabādhakabhāva iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca naiyogikapravṛttibhyo rāgādyākṣiptapravṛttinirodhe'bhyupagamyamāne niyoganiṣṭhā api pravṛttayo nirudhyeran; ataśca iṣṭavighātakāryyaṃ pakṣa ityabhiprāyeṇāha—api ceti | atra hetumāha—anārjitadhanasyeti | na upārjitaṃ dhanākhyaṃ sādhanaṃ yena tasyānārjitadhanasya, ata eva yāgādisādhanapaścādidravyeṇa vikalpya hīnasya puṃso'śaktatvenānadhikārāt tāsāṃ niyoganiṣṭhapravṛttīnāṃ dravyasādhyānāmasaṃbhavāt avaśyaṃ dhanārjanaṃ kāryam; tacca rāgaprayuktam, na tu kratuprayuktamiti karmakāṇḍe siddham; tatra naiyogikapravṛttīnāṃ rāgākṣiptapravṛttinirodhakatve dhanārjanamapi nirudhyeta; ataścāsādhanasya naiyogikapravṛttyasaṃbhavāt tā api virudhyeranniti bhāvaḥ | dūṣaṇāntarāyopakramate—api ceti | dṛṣṭārthāyāḥ strīsaṃbhogādipravṛtteradṛṣṭārthāyāśca jyotiṣṭomādipravṛttestulye sukhopāyatve rāgaprayuktatve na kaścidviśeṣaḥ; jabhe api sukharāgaprayukte ityarthaḥ | tulyatvameva darśayati— tathā hīti | itiśabdaḥ svarūpanirdeśārthaḥ | sargakāmaśabdena “tasya lipsārthalakṣaṇā” iti nyāyena svarge rāgataḥ prāptāṃ pravṛttimanūya tatra jyotiṣṭomādisādhanaviśeṣo vidhīyata ityarthaḥ | yadi nāma dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthapravṛttīnāṃ rāgaprayuktatvaṃ tulyam, tathāpi kimityata āha—tathā ceti | tathā tulye rāgaprayuktatve sati ca rāgākṣiptasukhasādhanādiprapaścābhiniveśe tulye ko viśeṣaḥ, yena naiyogikī ātmajñānaṃ pratyanuguṇā bhavati, aparā
[p. 79]
tu dṛṣṭārthā ātmajñānavirodhinītiḥ na ko'pyastītyarthaḥ | aviśeṣe hetumāha—kāmeti | kāmaḥ sukhābhilāṣo rāgaḥ, tenākarṣaṇaṃ manaso P. 22-24. dṛṣṭārthāyāmadṛṣṭārthāyāṃ ca pravṛttāvaviśiṣṭam; dṛṣṭārthapravṛttivadadṛṣṭārthāyāmapi svargādisukhakāmākṛṣṭaṃ mano'samāhitaṃ nātmajñānakṣamam; ataḥ sāpi tadvirodhinīti bhāvaḥ | atha svargasya guṇatvenānvayānna jyotiṣṭomādeḥ kāmyasvargopāyatvam; ato na tatra kāmākṣepo manasa ityasti viśeṣa ityāśaṅkayati—atheti | tatra dūṣaṇamāha—varṇitamiti | jyotiṣṭomāderakāmopāyatve jantūnāmabhyudayavinipātā ākasmikāḥ syuriti varṇitamityarthaḥ | doṣāntaramāha—uktaśceti | svargasya sādhyatvenānvaya iti ṣaṣṭhāye jaimininoktaśca nyāyaḥ pratyuddhiyeta; ucchabda uddhārārthaḥ; pratiśabdaśca tatpratipakṣārthaḥ; adhaḥ kriyetetyarthaḥ | pūrvāparaviruddhaścāyaṃ pakṣa ityāha—tulyeti | dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthapravṛttyostulyakāryatvena virodhāt yā pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthapravṛttyornirodhāśaṅkā kṛtā, sāpi jyotiṣṭomāderakāmopāyatve tulyakāryatvābhāvāt dūre nirastāvakāśā syādityarthaḥ; dūranirasto'vakāśo yasyā iti vigrahaḥ | prakārāntareṇa karmavidhīnāmātmajñānānuguṇyaṃ dṛṣṭenāśaṅkayati—atheti | kāmasyāprāptau kāmānukūlaḥ puruṣo'samāhitatvānnātmajñānakṣamaḥ; karmavidhibhiḥ kāmyeṣu prāpiteṣu kāmanāḥ pravilīyante; tena kāmyaprāptidvāreṇa karmavidhayaḥ kāmān kāmanāḥ pravilāpayanta ātmajñānādhikārānuguṇyaṃ bhajanta ityarthaḥ | pravilīnakāmasya jñānotpattidvāreṇa muktirityatraiva śrutimāha—yathoktamiti | tad dūṣayati—tadapyasaditi | kuta ityāha—yata iti | sādhu brūte bhavān, yadi kāmaprāptyā tatkāmanāḥ pravilīyante; na tu tatprāptyā tāḥ pravilīyanta ityarthaḥ | kena tarhi tat pravilīyanta ityata āha—api tviti | viṣayāṇāmanityatādidoṣaparibhāvanā anucintanam, tato bhavatīti dāṣaparibhāvanodbhūtena prasaṃkhyānena vivekena vilaya ityarthaḥ | kāmyasaṃsparśastu manaso viparyayakārītyāha—kalayeti | mātrayāpi cetkāmyairmanaḥ saṃspṛśyate tato hyiyata eva tadatyantamākṛṣyata eva tairhāribhiḥ manoharaṇaśallaiḥ; na tu tebhyo niruttakāmaṃ mano bhavatītyarthaḥ | tadevābhiyuktavākyābhyāṃ draḍhayati—
[p. 80]
P. 30-6. uktamiti | na kevalaṃ dharmavidhayaḥ kāmyavilayahetavo na bhavanti, pratyuta viparyayakāriṇa ityāha—anupāyatvāditi | karmavidhiṣvasatsu kāmopāyabhūtayāgādyajñānādanupāyatvādapi tāvadayaṃ pumān kāmebhyo nivarteta; upaśāmyatyanenetyupaśamaḥ, nikhilasyāvidyādeḥ cheśasyopaśamaḥ, tadrūpaṃ cātmajñānamāśrayet; karmavidhiṣu tu satsu tairdarśito vividho yāgadānādirūpāyo yasya satathāvidhaḥ pumān tāneva kāmyān prakṛtihāriṇaḥ prakṛtyā svabhāvena mano hartuṃ śīlān, prakṛtiṃ ca svabhāvaṃ puṃso hartuṃ śīlān, api tu taṃ vikṛtaṃ kartuṃ śīlānabhiniviśeta teṣveva sutarāṃ baddhagraho bhavet; teṣāṃ kāmānāṃ ca pravilayaḥ, pravilīyate'neneti pravilayaḥ, tadrūpāccātmajñānādiṣṭavidhātakatvenodvijetetyarthaḥ | nanu viṣayasukhamanityamalpaṃ duḥkhānuṣaṅgi ca; jñānaphalaṃ ca paramānandastadviparītaḥ, “paramo hyeṣa ānandaḥ” iti śruteḥ; tena tatraivābhilāṣaḥ prāptaḥ; sa ca viṣayānandābhilāṣamucchetsyati; tena satsvapi karmavidhiṣu tatphalakāmebhyo nivarteta, na teṣvabhiniviśeta; na cātmajñānādudvijetetyāśaṅkyāha—śruto'pīti | viṣayānando hyasakṛdanubhūtaḥ; yaścānubhūtaḥ sa eva cittamākarṣati; na tu śrutimātragamyaḥ, ananubhūtatvāditi bhāvaḥ | prāgeveti; kimutetyarthaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | ato na kāmapravilayadvāreṇa dṛṣṭenaiva karmavidhīnāṃ jñānavidhyadhikārānupraveśaṃ iti siddham | yadyapi kāmapravilayapakṣo dvitīyaḥ, tathāpi karmāvadhīnāṃ dṛṣṭenaivopayoga ityetatpakṣadūṣaṇaprasaṅgenehokta ityadoṣaḥ | yadapyuktaṃ sarvatra vede kacit kasyacidvedasya vilayo gamyata iti, tadapyanubhāṣya dūṣayati—yadapīti | 'uktam' iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | kathamasadityāha—anidamiti; "sargakāmo yajeta" iti vākyasyeti śeṣaḥ | tadeva vivṛṇoti—na hīti | yatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārthaḥ; idaṃ tu sargakāmavākyaṃ na dehātiriktātmataccāvabodhaparam, śrutahānyaśrutakalpanāprasaṅgāt; api tu yāganiyogaparam; ato'traiva tat pramāṇam; na pūrvatriti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadyapi dehātiriktamātmataccaṃ na śrutam, tathāpi dehasyānityasya
[p. 81]
jaḍasya ca janmāntarīyasvargopabhogāśakterarthādidaṃ dehātiriktamātmatattvamavagamyate; bhavati cānyaparādarthagamyo'pi vedārthaḥ; yathā “upari hi devebhyo dhārayati” iti hetuparāduparidhāraṇavidhiḥ; tataśca dehātmavilayo jyotiṣṭomādividherartha ityabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkayati—atheti | dūṣayati—tadapīti | P. 30-16. tadapi hastini pratyakṣeṇa dṛṣṭe hastipadena hastino'numānaṃ yathā vyarthatvādanapekṣitam, tādṛśamityarthaḥ | nanu tatra pratyakṣasiddho hastī, na tviha tathā dehātmavilaya ityāśaṅkyāha—sākṣāditi | satyamātmajñānavidhirdehātmavilayamapekṣate, sa tu pratyakṣaśrutilabdha iti na tamarthalabdhamapekṣate; “yaścārthādartho na sa śabdārthaḥ” iti ca nyāyaḥ; tataścārthagamyo dehātmavilayo na jyotiṣṭomādividhiṣu vedārthaḥ; dhāraṇe punaraprāptasyānuvādāsaṃbhavāt tadvidhiriti vaiṣamyamiti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca arthasiddhamapi cedvedārthaḥ syāt, tato dehātmatve dehasyānityasya janmāntarīyaphalabhoktṛtvāsaṃbhavaṃ manvānasya tatkāmanayā bhoktṛtvābhimānena ca yo manaso granthiḥ sa śithilībhavedapi; dehātirikte'pyātmani vedārthe yathā so'rthagamyo vedārthastathā tasya nityasya janmāntarīyaphalabhogaṃ manvānasya puṃsaḥ tatkāmādigrantheḥ kāṭhinyaṃ dṛḍhatvaṃ yadarthāt sidhyati tadapi vaidikaṃ syāt; tataśca jyotiṣṭomādividhayo viraktātmajñānavidhipratikūlatvānna tadekavākyatāṃ yāyurityabhiprāyeṇāha—arthācceti | vedadarśitavividhopāyatvena vā yat kāmādigrantheḥ kāṭhinyaṃ tadvaidikaṃ syādityarthaḥ | cakāro dūṣaṇasamuccaye | evamādyapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā kāmanibarhaṇadvāreṇa karmavidhīnāmātmajñānānupraveśapakṣaṃ dvitīyaṃ dūṣayati—dvitīyo'pīti | “na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānāma” ityādinā varṇitādeva kāmyānāṃ kāmanāvicchedasāmarthyabhāvāt svavākyopāttasvargādikāryanirākāṅkṣāṇāṃ karmavidhīnāmātmajñānavidhyanupraveśe varṇitādeva pramāṇābhāvāt dvitīyo'pi pakṣo'samañjasa iti vyavahitenānvayaḥ | kiṃ ca karmavidhīnāṃ sahasrasaṃvatsarāntānāṃ jñānavidhiśeṣatve aṅgapradhānayorekādhikāratve sati jñānādhikṛtenaikenaiva mumukṣuṇā kṛtsnakarmopasaṃhāraḥ kartavyaḥ; tataśca sarvakarmaṇāṃ jñānena samuccayaḥ, ekena ca kartropasaṃhāraḥ syāt; sa cāśakya ityāha—ekādhikāratva iti | ekasyādhikāro yeṣviti vigrahaḥ | itiḥ samāptau | 6
[p. 82]
idānīmuddeśakramaprāptānanantarapakṣānanādṛtya bahuvaktavyatvādātmajñānasya P. 81-1. kartṛsaṃskāratayā karmāṅgatvapakṣaṃ dūṣayitumanubhāṣayati—ye'pīti | pūrvaṃ karma jñānāṅgamityuktam, adhunā tu jñānaṃ karmāṅgamiti viparyayārthaḥ | taddūṣayati— tairiti | tairapi jñānasya karmabhiḥ saṃbandhe śrutyādīnāmanyatamaṃ pramāṇaṃ vācyam, tacca durvacamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu brīhiprokṣaṇādisaṃskāravat kartṛsaṃskāratvātmajñānaṃ karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyata ityāśaṅkya taddaiṣamyamāha—na tāvaditi | yathā tatra karmabhiḥ saṃbandhe pramāṇamasti na tathāleti śeṣaḥ | tatra prokṣaṇādau tāvat karmasaṃbandhe pramāṇamāha—tatra hīti | tatra hi brīhisvarūpasya karmaṇyanupayokṣyamāṇasyāsādhanībhūtasya prokṣaṇena na kaścidartha iti brīhisvarūpaprokṣaṇasyānarthakyādanyatra sādhanībhūtaṃ brīhiśabdo lakṣayati; tatrāpi prakaraṇagṛhītasya prokṣaṇasyāprakṛtakarmāpūrvasādhanasaṃbandhe prakaraṇabādhaḥ syāditi prakaraṇāt prakṛtakarmāpūrvasādhanamiti gamyate; tadevaṃ prakṛtakarmāpūrvasādhanatvalakṣaṇadvāreṇa brīhiśabdaḥ prokṣaṇasya prakṛtakarmāpūrvasaṃbandhaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ | nanu parṇamayītvavadātmajñānamasatyapi prakaraṇe karmaṇā kimiti na saṃbadhyata ityāha— nāpīti | yathā “yasya parṇamayī juhūrbhavati” ityatra karmasaṃbandhaḥ, tathātreti vākyaśeṣaḥ | kuta ityāha—tatrāpīti | tatrāpi juhvādidravyamavyabhicaritakarmasaṃbandhaṃ na vyabhicaritaḥ karmasaṃbandho yasya tat tathoktam; karmasamavetameva hi juhvādidravyam, nānyatra tat; ata evāsatyapi prakaraṇe karmopasthāpayatītyarthaḥ | nanu yadi nāmaivaṃ tathāpi parṇamayītvasya kena pramāṇena karmasaṃbandha ityata āha—tatreti | tatra tasmin sati, tatra vā parṇamayīvākye vākyenaiva karmasaṃbandhaḥ; parṇamayītvasyeti śeṣaḥ | taddhiḥ vākyaṃ kratvavyabhicaritasaṃbandhajuhvanuvādena parṇamayītvaṃ vidadhat kratumanupraveśayatīti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ dṛṣṭāntadvaye karmasaṃbandhahetumuktvā dārṣṭāntike tadasaṃbhavamāha— ātmajñānaṃ tviti | nātmajñānaṃ prakaraṇe śrutam, karmakāṇḍabahirbhāvājjñānakāṇḍasya; nāpyavyabhicaritakratusaṃbandham, laukike'pi karmaṇyātmanaḥ kartṛtvasyeṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tena pramāṇābhāvādātmajñānasya kartṛsaṃskāratayā karmasaṃbandho vaktuṃ na śakyata ityāha—teneti | ātmajñānasya karmasaṃbandhābhāve
[p. 83]
yadabhimataṃ siddhaṃ bhavati, tadāha—tathā ceti | ātmajñānasya P. 31-7. karmasaṃbandhābhāve sati ajñāte pārārthye “dravyasaṃskārakarmasu phalaśrutirarthavādaḥ syāt” iti nyāyena “na ca punarāvartate” ityādiphalaśrutirarthavādinī na bhavatītyataḥ pṛthakphalatvāt pṛthagadhikāratvamiti ‘jñānakarmaṇorviparyayeṇaikādhikāratvam’ iti yaduktaṃ tannirastaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | nanu syādevaṃ yadīyaṃ phalaśrutiḥ syāt; iyaṃ tu “ghṛtakulyāḥ kṣaranti” itivat vartamānāpadeśatvāt svargakāmādivaktāmopabandhābhāvācca svarasataḥ phalaśrutirna bhavati; tataśca yathā rātrisatre “pratitiṣṭhante” iti ‘pratitiṣṭheran’ iti vipariṇamayya phalaṃ kalpitam, tathā kalpanīyam; tacca phalākāṅkṣāyāṃ satyāṃ rātrisatravat kalpanīyaḥ; na ca dṛṣṭe phale sati phalākāṅkṣā bhavati; asti cātmajñānasya dehāntarabhogyaphaleṣu karmasu pravṛttirdṛṣṭaprayojanam; na hi nityātmajñānaṃ vinā sā syāt; ato na vipariṇamayya phalaṃ kalpyamityabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkayati—atheti | tasmāt yathādhyayanavidhidṛṣṭārtho nārthavādādikaṃ phalaṃ prārthayate, tathātmajñānavidhirapītyāha—tasmāditi | ataḥ pṛthakphalābhāvācca karmādhikārādātmajñānavidhāvadhikārāntaramiti siddhamekādhikāratvaṃ jñānakarmaṇorityāha—tatheti | itirhetvarthaḥ | tadetaddūṣayati—tadapyasaditi | kathamasadityāha—yata iti | yasmāt karmavidhyapekṣitāt kartuḥ bhoktṛśrātmano'nya evākartṛbhoktṛrūpa aupaniṣada upaniṣadekagamyaḥ puruṣo vedānteṣu jijñāsyate | na ca tasya jñānaṃ karmaṇi pravṛttirheturityarthaḥ | kutastaddheturna bhavatītyāha—na hīti | kartā bhoktā ca karmasu pravartate; tena tajjñānaṃ karmapravṛttirhetuḥ, na punaropaniṣadātmajñānam; na hi tasyopaniṣadātmanaḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtve sta ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ punastasya kartṛtvabhoktṛtve na sta ityata āha—evamiti | ‘nāśrāti’ na bhuṅkte, ‘anaśnan’ abhuñjāna iti ca bhoktṛtvaniṣedhaḥ | upalakṣaṇamidam; “niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyam” iti “nāyaṃ hanti na hanyate” iti ca kartṛtvaniṣedho draṣṭavyaḥ | evaṃ vedāntavedyasyātmanaḥ karmaṇyapravṛttimuktvā yastu karmaṇi pravartate kartā bhoktā ca, na sa vedāntavedya ityāha—yastviti | 6A
[p. 84]
evaśabdo'vadhāraṇārthaḥ | yastu kartā bhoktā jīvaḥ, sa eva pratyakṣasiddhaḥ 'aham' ityaparokṣatayā vyavahāre svataḥ siddhaḥ, na paramātmā; tena so'dhigatatvāt na vedāntaśabdagamya ityarthaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | jīvaparamātmanorabhede paramātmani śabdagamye jīvo'pi śābda eva; ataḥ kathaṃ na śabdagamya ityuktamiti bhāvaḥ | ekatvameva śrutyā draḍhayati— P.31-18. evamiti | "anena jīvenātmanānupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi" iti śrutiḥ 'jīvenātmanā' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyāt 'vyākaravāṇi' iti cottamapuruṣaprayogājīvātmanorabhedamāha; tatkathamanayorbheda ityarthaḥ | siddhāntī tayostāttvikamabhedamabhyupagamya śabdagamyāgamyatve vyavasthāmāha—satyamiti | tasyaiva tu jīvasyaikatvenābhinnasya bimbapratibimbādivat kalpitabhedasya kartṛ-śaktyahaṅkārāvidyānuviddhaṃ bimbasyeva darpaṇādyanuviddhaṃ śyāmādirūpaṃ jīvākhya-mekarūpamasti; tacca kartṛtvāt karmaṇāṃ pravṛttihetuḥ, pratyakṣasiddhatvānna vedāntamapekṣate; tena tacchabdagamyaṃ na bhavati | yattu tasyaivāvidyānubaddhaṃ rūpaṃ svayaṃprakāśaṃ sarvakartṛtvādivibhāgaśūnyam, tat tādṛśaṃ lokavyavahāreṣva-siddhatvācchabdājjñātumiṣyate; tataśca siddhaṃ jīvaparayorabhede'pi paraḥ śabda-prameyaḥ, na jīva ityarthaḥ | na ca parajñānasyaiva vedāntajasya karmasu pravṛttidṛṣṭaṃ prayojanamityāha—tacceti | tacca vedāntavedyaṃ rūpaṃ karmapravṛttervirodhi; kathaṃ tajjñānasya karmapravṛttidṛṣṭaṃ prayojanaṃ syādityarthaḥ | virodhitvaṃ prakaṭayati—tathā hīti | brahmānandādi-rūpamātmānaṃ jānataḥ puṃsaḥ pravṛttau prayojanaṃ sāmarthyaṃ ca nāstī-tyarthaḥ | vāśabdaśrārthe | kutaḥ punarbrahmātmavidaḥ prayojanasāmarthyaṃ na sta ityata āha—āptakāmatvāditi | tatrāptakāmatvaṃ niṣprayojanatve hetuḥ | sādhanādyupāyābhāvaśca pravṛttāvasāmarthyaṃ hetuḥ | āptakāmatve ca pūrvoktaṃ paramānandarūpatvaṃ hetuḥ, sarvānandānāṃ tatrāntarbhāvāt | advayatvaṃ ca karma-sādhanetikartṛvyatādyupāyābhāve heturiti vivekaḥ | evamavidyānubaddhaṃ rūpaṃ karma-pravṛttihetuḥ, na tvaupaniṣadam; ato na tajjñānaṃ karmāṅgamiti sthitam | nanvaupaniṣadātmajñānameva karmāṅgatvena śrutyā coditam, tatkathamucyate na
[p. 85]
tatkarmāṅgamitīti paraśrodayati—syādetaditi | yadi nāmaupaniṣadātmajñānaṃ P. 31-24. dṛṣṭena na karmāṅgam, tathāpi prayojanādivadadṛṣṭena bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | nanu pravṛttau satyāṃ prayojanādivadadṛṣṭārthatvaṃ syāt ; aupaniṣadātmavidastu niṣpayojanatvāvirupāyatvācca karmaṇi pravṛttireva nāstītyuktam ; ataḥ kutraupaniṣadātmajñānamadṛṣṭenāṅgaṃ bhaviṣyatītyucyate ? etābhya eva vakṣyamāṇaśrutibhyaḥ paramānandādvayaśrutirupacaritārthe kalpyeteti paro manyate ; tāḥ śrutīrudāharati—yadeveti | ‘vidyayā’ ityātmavidyokteti parābhiprāyaḥ | tathā “yo vā etadakṣaramaviditvā gārgyasmin loke juhāṃti dadāti tapastapyati bahūni varṣasahasrāṇyantavānevāsya sa loko bhavati” itiśrutyā brahmavidyārahitatayā yāgādikarmaphalāntavaccapradarśanadvāreṇa kevalakarmanindayā “na hi nindā nindyaṃ nindatum, api tu ninditāditarat praśaṃsitum” iti vidyāsahitakarmapraśaṃsā kṛtā | praśaṃsā ca vidhānāyeti brahmavidyāyā yāgāṅgatvaṃ darśitam | tathā “taṃ vidyākarmaṇī samanvārabhete” iti saṃśabdasya sahārthatvāt brahmavidyākarmaṇoḥ samārambhe sahabhāvo darśitaḥ | ekaprayoge ca kāryārambhe sahabhāvo bhavati | na cāsau darśapūrṇamāsavat, samapradhānayoḥ samudāye phalāśravaṇāt, yāgādibhyaśca phalaśruteḥ ; yāgādayaḥ pradhānam, sāmarthyāt ; vidyā aṅgam, “phalavatsannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgam” iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ | siddhāntī tvāha—pūrvamiti | pūrvaṃ yatprathamamupanyastaṃ “yadeva vidyayā karoti” iti vacanam, tattāvat prakṛtā yā udgīthavidyā udgīthe sāmabhaktāvakṣaropāsanalakṣaṇā tadviṣayam, na brahmavidyāviṣayam ; ato na tasyāḥ karmāṅgatvaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ | ‘vidyā’ iti sāmānyaśabdo'pi prakramaviśeṣāt viśeṣe vartate iti vadituṃ prakṛtaprahaṇam | tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ | kathamudgīthavidyā prakṛtetyata āha—omiti | tathā “yadeva vidyayā” ityataḥ pareṇāpi “yo vā etadakṣaramaviditvā” ityanena kṣayiphalatvena karmanindayā akṣarajñānastutiḥ kriyate, na tu brahmavidyāyāḥ karmāṅgatvajñāpanam | nanu kevalakarmanindayā vidyāsāhityaṃ vidhitstimiti gamyate | maivam ; evaṃ hi parastāt vidyāsahitaṃ karma saṃstuyāt ; iha tu “yadetadakṣaraṃ gārgyaṃ viditvāsmācchoktāt preti sa brahmaṇaḥ” ityādinā kevalākṣarajñānaṃ stūyate ; tasmā-
[p. 86]
dakṣarajñānastutāvevāsya tātparyam, na vidyākarmaṇoḥ sāhityavidhāvityadoṣaḥ | P 32.5. tathā vidyākarmaṇoranvārambhe'pi sāhityaṃ nāstītyāha—vidyeti | bhedeneti vibhāgenetyarthaḥ | vibhāgamevāha—vidyāvantamiti | nanu saṃśabde sahārthaṃ jīvati kathamevaṃ vyākhyāyate ? vidyākarmaṇorekatvasāhityāsaṃbhavāditi brūmaḥ | kathamasaṃbhavaḥ ? ucyate—na tāvadaparavidyākarmaṇoranvārambhe sāhityam, anyonyanirapekṣatvāt | anyonyanirapekṣatvaṃ tu pṛthak phalaśruteḥ, jyotiṣṭomāmihotrādivat ; ekaphalatve hi kāryārambhe sāhityaṃ syāt yathā darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ | paravidyā tu karmaparipanthinī kathaṃ karmaṇā samuccayeta ? na ca sā janmāntarārambhikā, apavargaphalatvāt | tasmādvibhajyaivāparavidyākarmaṇoranvārambhakatvam | taduktam—“vibhāgaḥ śatavat” iti | saṃśabdaścāvivakṣitasahārthaḥ samyagarthānuvādaḥ | ataḥ sūktam—bhedeneti | prakaraṇārthamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | evamapīti ; viparyayeṇāpītyarthaḥ | yadvā na kevalamātmajñānasya dṛṣṭārthatvena, api tvevamapi śruticoditasya tasyādṛṣṭārthatayāpītyarthaḥ | evaṃ jñānaṃ karmāṅgamiti viparyayapakṣaṃ nirasya pakṣāntaraṃ dūṣayitumanubhāṣate—ye'pīti | asaṃbandhameva manyanta ityanantareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | kathaṃ tayorvirodha ityata āha—dvaiteti | sādhanādidvaitaviṣayaṃ karma, dvaitarahitātmaviṣayaṃ tvātmajñānamiti hetorvirodhādananvayaḥ | itiśabdo hetyarthaḥ | dūṣaṇamāha—teṣāmiti | kuta ityāha—pramāṇādīti | yadi dvaitaviṣayaṃ karmeti virodhādadvaitajñānena saha na saṃbadhyate, tataḥ pramāṇaprameyapramātrādidvaitamapi na tena saṃbadhyeta ; na ca pramāṇādyupāyasaṃbandhamantareṇa jñānamutpadyate ; tataścādvaitajñānasyānutpāda eva syādityarthaḥ | athocyate—pramāṇādivibhāga upāyaḥ, upeyaṃ cādvaitajñānam, tayośca paurvāparyādayogapadyam ; anyathā savyadakṣiṇaśṛṅgavadupāyopeyabhāva eva na syāt ; ataḥ kālamedena tayoravirodhādanyonyasaṃbandhe nādvaitajñānasyānutpādadoṣa iti ; tadāha—atheti | yadi pramāṇādivibhāgādvaitapratipattyoravirodhaḥ, tato jātāyāmapi tasyāṃ pramāṇādivibhāgena tathaivānuvartitavyam ; ato nādvaitaprāptiḥ ; ataścānirmokṣaprasaṅga
[p. 87]
ityāśaṅkyāha—pravilīyata eveti | advaitapratipattau satyāṃ pravilīyata eva P. 32-10. sarvapramāṇādivibhāgaḥ, ato nānirmokṣaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | yadyadvaitajñāne sati pramāṇādidvaitaṃ pravilīyate, prāptaṃ tarhi tayośchāyātpādivadvirodhaḥ anupāyatvaṃ ca pramāṇādivibhāgasya ; na hi pravilīyamānasya kvacidupāyatvaṃ dṛṣṭam ; ato nāvirodhaḥ, nāpyupāyatvaṃ pramāṇādivibhāgasyetyāśaṅkyāha— na ceti | tatrāvirodhe tāvaddhetumāha—upāyasyeti | na pravilāpyapravilāpakatvamātreṇa virodhaḥ, kiṃtu kālabhedena ca, tādṛśayorapi chāyātapayorbhinnakālayoravirodhāt | iha ca tayoḥ kālabhedaḥ ; tasmādavirodha iti bhāvaḥ | upāyatve hetumāha—vadā ceti | jāte brahmaitajñāne pramāṇādidvaitaṃ pravilīyate, na pūrvameva ; pūrvaṃ ca tasyopāyatvam, tadā ca tadapravilīnamevetyarthaḥ | itaśca nānupāyatvamityāha—bheda iti | advaitajñānasya tāvat kāryatvādavaśyaṃ kāraṇena bhāvyam ; na cādvaitameva brahma tasya kāraṇam, tasyākārakatvāt, nityaṃ ca kāryātpattiprasaṅgāt ; ataḥ pāriśeṣyāt pramāṇādidvaitena bhāvyamiti bhāvaḥ | yadvā bheda evānyo'pīti sāmānyādyabhedapratipattāvupāyo dṛṣṭaḥ, tadvat pramāṇādibhedo'pi bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ paramatamāśaṅkya tadevopajīvya karmajñānayorasaṃbandhahetuṃ paroktaṃ virodhaṃ pariharati—neti | yadi pramāṇādidvaitaṃ tūpāyatvādvinnakālamiti na tenādvaitajñānasya virodhaḥ, evaṃ tarhi karmabhirapyupāyatvādeva bhinnakālairma tasya virodhaḥ ; tataśca na tasya tairasaṃbandha iti bhāvaḥ | para idānīṃ karmaṇāmanupayogenopāyatvamevākṣipan saṃbandhamākṣipati—syāditi | bhavedetakarmaṇāmupāyatvaṃ tena ca saṃbandhaḥ, yadi teṣāmupayogaḥ syāt ; sa eva tu brahmaṇo nityatvenāsādhyatvānna vidyate | na ca kāryasiddhāvanupayuktasyopāyatvamasti ; na cānupāye'pekṣā ; na cānapekṣitasya saṃbandha iti bhāvaḥ | karmaṇāmanupayoge śrutimudāharati—śrūyata iti | asyārthaḥ—akṛto'kāryo mokṣo brahmasvarūpaṃ kṛtena karmaṇā nāsti na bhavati na sidhyatīti | nanu mā bhavatu brahmaṇyasādhye karmaṇāmupayogaḥ, tajjñānotpattau tu pramāṇādivibhāgasyeva kena vāryata ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | hetumāha—jñānasyeti | jñānaṃ nāma pramāṇādhīnam, na karmādhīnam ; anyathā
[p. 88]
karmāpi saptamaṃ pramāṇaṃ syāt ; ataḥ pramāṇasyaiva jñānotpattāvupayogaḥ, na karmaṇāmityarthaḥ | nanu yadi nāma karmaṇāṃ jñānotpattāvupayogo nāsti, P. 32-16. jñānakāryotpattau tu sahakāritvenopayogo bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | kuta ityāha—jñānasyeti | kāryaṃ hi saṃbhūya kurvataḥ sahakāritvam, yathā kṣityāderaṅkuram ; na cātmajñānasya svaviṣayaparicchedādanyat kāryamastītyarthaḥ | nanvastyeva jñānasya kāryāntaraṃ mokṣaḥ ; tathā ca jñānānmokṣaḥ śrūyata ityāśaṅkyāha—na tasyeti | mokṣo nāma brahmasvarūpam, tacca nityamiṣṭam ; taccetkāryaṃ syāt, tato'nityaṃ syāt | athavā mokṣaśret kāryaḥ syāt, tatastasyānityatvānmuktasya punarāvṛttiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu yadi nāma mokṣaḥ sākṣātsādhyo na bhavati, tathāpi bandhahetupradhvaṃso jñānasya sādhyo bhaviṣyati ; atastatra karmaṇāṃ sahakāritvenopayoga upapatsyate ; pradhvaṃsasya ca kāryasyāpi nityatvādanityatvadoṣo'pi na saṃpatsyata ityabhiprāyeṇa para āha—bandhahetviti | nanu mokṣaḥ puruṣārthaḥ, na bandhahetuvicchedaḥ ; sa cet jñānasādhyaḥ syāt, tato jñānamapuruṣārthaphalamanupādeyaṃ syāt ; kutaśca tadā mokṣaḥ syāt ? na hi tasya jñānādupāyāntaraṃ śrūyata ityāśaṅkya sa evāha— itīti | itiśabdo hetau ; yasmāt bandhahetuvicchedo jñānasya sādhyaḥ, tasmāt tasmin bandhahetau jñānādvicchinne tasya bandhahetorabhāvānmucyate ; tataśca jñānasya mokṣaphalatvānnāpuruṣārthaphalatvam, na ca nirupāyatvānmokṣāsaṃbhava ityabhiprāyaḥ | tadetad dūṣayituṃ pṛcchati—ka iti | itara āha—avidyeti | taddūṣayati—na tarhiti | vidyāyāḥ pṛthak vyatirikto nāvidyāvicchedo vidyāsādhyo'sti, yatra karmaṇāṃ sahakāritvamiti bhāvaḥ | kimiti nāstītyāha—yata iti | agrahaṇamavidyā, tacca grahaṇaprāgabhāvaḥ, tasya ca nivṛttigrahaṇātmakavidyotpāda eva, saprāgabhāvavimardātmakatvāt bhāvotpādasya ; ato nāvidyānivṛttirvidyayā pṛthak sādhyā, yatra karmaṇāṃ sahakāritvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | atra codayati—syāditi | bhavatvagrahaṇalakṣaṇāyāmavidyāyāṃ vidyotpāda evāvidyānivṛttiriti pṛthak sādhyābhāvaḥ, viparyayajñānalakṣaṇāyāṃ na
[p. 89]
vidyotpāda eva viparyayaniṣṭhattiḥ ; atastanniṣṭhattirvidyāyāḥ pṛthak sādhyā ; ataśca tatra karmaṇāṃ sahakāritvenopayogo'stīti bhāvaḥ | kimiti punaragrahaṇaniṣṭhattivat viparyayajñānaniṣṭhattirapi vidyotpāda eva na syādityāśaṅkya vaiṣamyamāha—na khalviti | agrahaṇaṃ hi prāgabhāvaḥ, tasya bhāvotpāda eva P. 32-21. nivṛttiryujyate ; viparyayajñānaṃ tu vidyāto bhāvāntaram ; na ca bhāvāntaraṃ vidyā viparyayajñānasya bhāvāntarasya nivṛttirityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—na hīti | yadi parasparābhāvā bhāvā bhaveyuḥ, tato bhāvāntaraṃ bhāvāntarasyābhāvaḥ syāt ; na tvevamasti ; tataśca bhāvādbhāvāntarābhāvaḥ pṛthak sādhyo'sti ; tatra ca karmaṇāmanupraveśo ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ | kuta ityāha—abhāvatveti | abhāvasvabhāvatve hi bhāvānāṃ bhāvasvabhāvatvābhāvādabhāvyaḥ syāt ; tataśca sarvaśūnyatā syāditi bhāvaḥ | bhodaka evāśaṅkyati—yadīti | na khalva tattvaṃ gṛhṇato viparyayajñānaṃ bhavati ; tena tattvāgrahaṇaṃ viparyayajñānasya nimittam ; atastattvajñānātmikayā vidyayā nivṛtte nimittanivṛttyaiva viparyayajñānaṃ svayameva nivartiṣyate | ato bhāvāntaratve'pi na tannivṛttirvidyayā pṛthak sādhyā, yatra karmaṇāmanupraveśaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati— tacceti | syādevam, yadyagrahaṇaṃ kasya cinnimittaṃ syāt ; na tvetadasti ; abhāvo hyagrahaḥ, tanna kasya cinnimittam ; ato na viparyayasyāpītyarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—mūrcchādīti | yadyagrahaṇaṃ viparyayanimittaṃ syāt, tato mūrcchādiṣu tadastīti viparyayajñānaṃ prasajyeta ; na caivamastītyarthaḥ | yadyagrahaṇamanimittaṃ viparyayasya, kiṃ tarhi tasya nimittamiti pṛcchati—kiṃ tarhīti | uttaramāha—anādīti | anādiraprayojanā yā anirvacanīyāvidyā, sā viparyayāvidyāyā nimittamityuktam ‘amṛtamajam’ ityetadvyākhyānāvasara ityarthaḥ | na ca tasyāmapi ko heturiti vaktavyam, anāditvādityāha—tatra ceti | yadvā tatra ca tasyāmanādyavidyāyāṃ satyāṃ viparyayasya ko heturiti yaḥ paryanuyogaḥ sa niravakāśa ityarthaḥ | yadi tvanādyavidyātiriktaṃ viparyayasya nimittamicchasi, tadapi śakyaṃ darśayitumityāha—viparyāseti | prapañcagrāhiṇo viparyayajñānasya prapañcasaṃskāro nimittam | tasya tarhi kiṃ nimitta-
[p. 90]
mityucyate ! tasyāpi viparyayajñānameva nimittam | evamubhayoranāditvena bījāṅkurādivat kāryakāraṇabhāvena vyavasthānānna nirnimittatvādidoṣa ityarthaḥ | tadevaṃ nāgrahaṇanivṛttyā viparyayasya svayaṃ nivṛttiḥ ; ato vidyayā socyate ; tena tatra sādhyasya samyagjñānasya sahakāryapekṣā syāt ; satyāṃ ca tasyāṃ karmaṇāmabhisaṃbandha iti phalamupasaṃharati | tadetadasaṃbandhavādī dūṣayati— P. 38-4. etacceti ; tadapyasāramityarthaḥ | kathamasāramityāha—na svalpiti | svalpaśabdaḥ prasiddhidyotanārthaḥ | prasiddhamidaṃ loke, yat śuktikādau mithyājñānapūrvakasamyagjñāne jāte mithyājñānanivṛttaye samyagjñānotpādāt pṛthak yatnaṃ na kurvanti ; na ca sahakāri vā samyagjñānādanyat kiñcit karmādhipekṣante, yena karmaṇāṃ sahakāritvenānupraveśaḥ syādityarthaḥ | nanu dātreṇeva lavanāya samyagjñānena viparyayanivṛttaye kimiti na prayatante, kimiti vā taddeva sahakāri nāpekṣante ityāśaṅkyāha | yadi hi dātrādiva lavanaṃ samyagjñānāt viparyayajñānanivṛttiḥ pṛthak kāryā syāt tato dātreṇeva lavanāya samyagjñānena viparyayanivṛttaye pṛthak prayateran, sahakāri vānyadapekṣeran ; na tvetadasti ; na hi dātramiva samyagjñānamutpādya paścānmithyājñānanivṛttaye vyāpāryate | kiṃ tarhi ? tadutpāda eva mithyājñānanivṛttiḥ ; yato'vidyāvirodhino bhāvāntarasyotpāda eva pūrvapradhvaṃsaḥ, ataḥ kiṃ pṛthak prayatnenetyarthaḥ | bhāvāntaramātrasya pūrvapradhvaṃsatve ghaṭabhāve mṛdaḥ pradhvaṃsaḥ syāditi virodhigrahaṇam | bhāvo hyanyābhāvaḥ, anyābhāvo na bhāva iti pūrvoktaḥ sarvābhāvaprasaṅgadoṣo'pi nāstīti bhāvaḥ | nanu na virodhibhāvāntarotpāda eva pūrvapradhvaṃsaḥ, kiṃ tu śūnyaṃ tadityāśaṅkyāha—na śūnyamiti | śūnyavastu na kiñcidityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—anyatheti | yadi pradhvaṃsaḥ śūnyaṃ syāt, tatastasya śaśaviṣāṇavadakāryatvācca tatra hetukṛtyamastītyahetumān syāt ; mudgarādihetukaśca sa prasiddha iti bhāvaḥ | nanu bhavatu virodhibhāvāntarotpāda eva pūrvapradhvaṃsaḥ, vidyā tu jñānatvena sajātitvānna mithyājñānavirodhinītyāśaṅkyāha—virodhinī ceti | ayamāśayaḥ—na vijātitvasajātitve virodhāvirodhayornibandhanam, tatra vijātyorapi rūparasayorekatrāvirodhāt ; sajātyorapi ca virodhāt, yathā viṣaṃ viṣaṃ śamayati, payaḥ payo
[p. 91]
jarayatīti ; tasmāt yadyasya nivartakaṃ tat tasya virodhi ; nivartikā ca vidyā mithyājñānasya ; tasmādvirodhinīti | tataḥ kimityāha—taditi | P. 33-8. yadā virodhibhāvotpattireva pūrvapradhvaṃsaḥ vidyā ca virodhinī, tadā vidyotpattireva mithyājñānapradhvaṃsa iti vidyotpattau mithyājñānaṃ naṣṭameva bhavati ; ato dātreṇaiva lavanāya vidyayā mithyājñānanivṛttaye na pṛthak prayatante, na sahakāri cāpekṣanta iti na viparyayajñānanivṛttyarthaṃ jñānasya karmasaṃbandha iti bhāvaḥ | athocyate—syādetat, yadyavidyā bandhahetuḥ syāt ; kiṃ tu karmāṇi bandhahetavaḥ | na ca tadvirodhinī vidyā, kiṃ tvavidyāvirodhinī ; ato na vidyotpattireva karmadhvaṃsa iti pṛthagvidyāsādhyaḥ ; atastatra sahakāritvena karmāpekṣayā karmānupraveśa ityabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkayati—athocyeteti | tadūṣayati—tacceti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—karmāṇyavidyāvilasitānyavidyāyā nivṛttau nimittanivṛttyā svayameva nivartante, na tu tannivṛttiḥ pṛthak sādhyā, yatra karmāṇi sahakāritvāpekṣyet | avidyāyā mūlatvādyāvadavidyā tāvat ‘karmedam, idaṃ ca phalam’ iti vibhāgavyavahāramātram ; tasya vyavahārasya pramṛṣṭāḥ nirastāḥ aśeṣāḥ prapañcabhedā yasminstat tathoktam ; ata eva viśuddhaṃ ca tat vijñānaṃ ca tasyodaye kutaḥ saṃbhava ityarthaḥ | mātragrahaṇamaparamārthatvadyotanārtham | imamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | viparyayasaṃśayābhyāṃ karmānapekṣasamyagjñānanivartyābhyāṃ saha karmaṇaḥ samyagjñānanivartyatvena tulyavat pāṭhāt karmānapekṣasamyagjñānanivartyatvamavasīyata ityarthaḥ | hṛdayagranthiḥ viparyāso mataḥ | prakārāntareṇa jñāne karmaṇāmanvayamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—atheti | jñāne hi saṃśayaviparyāsāveva male, nānyat ; te ca pramāṇajanite jñāne'saṃbhavaniraste ; tena tannivṛttyarthamapi karmāpekṣā vṛthetyarthaḥ | punarapi prakārāntareṇa jñāne karmānvayamāśaṅkyati—atha matamiti | śabdajanitādbrahmajñānādanyadapi vigalito'paścaṣṭo vibhāgodvāhaḥ prapañcabhedāvabhāso yasminstat, ata eva sarvavikalpātītaṃ viyadādijagadvikalparahitaṃ taccaṃ brahmataccaṃ yasminstat, tādṛśaṃ pratyakṣaṃ jñānamithyate ; tasyaiva ca brahma gocaro
[p. 92]
viṣayaḥ, na vedāntaśabdajanitasya vibhaktānāṃ nānābhūtānāṃ padārthānāmanyonyasaṃsargastamudgrahītuṃ śīlaṃ yasya tasyetyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—advaitaṃ brahma tu vigalitadvaitavikalpenaiva pratyakṣajñānena jñātuṃ śakyate, na tu śābdajñānena ; taddhinānāpadārthasaṃsargarūpavākyārthaviṣayatvena dvaitaviṣayatvānnādvaitaṃ brahma gamayatīti | P. 33-17. tataḥ kimityāha—tadutpattāviti ; nimittasaptamī “carmaṇi dvīpinaṃ hanti” itivat | karmānvayaprastāve tulyanyāyatvena prasaṅgādupāsanādyanvayo'pi darśitaiti draṣṭavyam | upāsanamanudinamanucintanam | ādiśabdena brahmacaryādigrahaṇam | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—ka iti | asya pratyakṣajñānasya śābdajñānāt ko viśeṣaḥ, yena hetunā jāte'pi śābdajñāne tat pratyakṣajñānaṃ prārthayate tadarthaṃ ca karmāṇyapekṣyate ; “sa eṣa neti netyātmā” “eka evāyamādvitīyaḥ” ityādiśābdajñānasyāpyadvaitaviṣayatvāviśeṣānna ko'pi viśeṣastasyāstīti bhāvaḥ | itaraḥ śābdajñānāt pratyakṣasya viśeṣamāha—spaṣṭābhatvamiti | tat dūṣayati—neti | astu pratyakṣasya spaṣṭābhatvam ; tathāpi na tadarthanīyam | spaṣṭābhatvasyaivānupayogādityarthaḥ | anupayogamāha—jñānaṃ hīti | jñeyaprāptaye jñānamapekṣyate, na ca sā śābdajñānānna bhavatītyataḥ kiṃ spaṣṭābhatvena ? ato na tadarthaṃ pratyakṣaṃ prārthanīyamiti bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ tarhi sarvasyāḥ pramāṇāntarajāyāḥ pramiteḥ pratyakṣaparatvena pratyakṣamarthyatām ; tadetadāśaṅkyati—pramiteriti | pratyakṣaṃ paraṃ pradhānaṃ yasyā iti vigrahaḥ ; pratyakṣaparyavasāyitvādityarthaḥ ; tadarthyata iti vakṣyamāṇenānvayaḥ | kutaḥ pramiteḥ pratyakṣaparatvamityata āha—tatreti | tatra tasmin pratyakṣe sati pramātuḥ nairutsukyādityarthaḥ | tathā hi—dhūmādinā agnyādi dūre pramitamapi pramātā punaḥ pratyakṣeṇa pramitsati ; evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—naitaditi | na hi sarvā pramitiḥ pratyakṣaparā, vaidikyā dharmādipramityā vyabhicārāt ; agnyādāvapi vyavahārāya saṃnidhānaṃ pramātāpekṣate, na tu pratyakṣapramitim ; daivāt tatsamīhitasya sā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | tathā brahmasaṃnihite sūryagatyādau na sā bhavati, bhavati ca pramāturnairutsukyam ; ato na pratyakṣaparā pramitiḥ, kiṃ tu prameyādhigamamātraparā | tatrādhigate prameye kimaparamākāṅkṣyate ? na kiṃcidityarthaḥ | nanvadhigate'pi prameye pramāṇāntaramākāṅkṣipyata ityāśaṅkya dūṣayati—pramāṇāntaramiti | pramāṇāntaraṃ hi
[p. 93]
prameyasiddhyarthamākāṅkṣyate, nāṭyāya ; prameyaṃ ca pūrvapramāṇādeva siddhamiti pramāṇāntarākāṅkṣā vyarthaivetyarthaḥ | na vyarthā, siddhasyāpi hi prameyasya punaḥ siddhyarthamapekṣā bhaviṣyatīti cet ; maivam ; siddhasyāpi punaḥ siddhyā cetprayojanam, tadā pūrvapramāṇādeva sā setsyati ; tathāpi pramāṇāntarākāṅkṣā vyarthaiveti | tadetatsāśaṅkyāha—punariti | etacca punaḥ siddhyākā P. 33-22. ikṣāmabhyupagamyoktam ; paramārthatastu sakṛtsiddhyaiva pramāturnairākāṅkṣyāt punaḥ siddhyākāṅkṣāyāṃ na kaściddheturastītyāha—siddhasyeti | nanvāgamasiddhasyāpi prameyasyāgamādupāyāntaraṃ pratyakṣamastīti tataḥ punaḥ siddhyapekṣā bhavati | tataśca kathamucyate heturnāstīti ? yāvadupāyāntarasadbhāvo heturityāśaṅkya dūṣayati—upāyāntaramiti | pramāṇāntaraṃ tarhyanugrāhyatayāpekṣyatām ; prameye tu pūrvasiddhyāt pramāturnairākāṅkṣyameva | na ca prameye siddhe pramāṇāntaraṃ svato'pekṣyam ; prameyasiddhyarthatvāt tadapekṣāyā iti bhāvaḥ | atha siddhasyāpi priyasya punaḥ siddhau prītiviśeṣo bhavati ; priyaścātmā—“tadetat preyaḥ putrāt” iti śruteḥ ; tena prītiviśeṣaḥ punaḥ siddhyapekṣāheturityāśaṅkya nirasyati—prītīti | yadi jñātasyāpīṣṭasya punarjñānāt prītiviśeṣo bhavati, tathāpi pūrvapramāṇajādapi punardarśanāt sa siddhyatīti na prītiviśeṣasiddhyarthaṃ pramāṇāntaramapekṣaṇīyamityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca yadyāgamajñātamapi prītiviśeṣārthaṃ pratyakṣeṇa punarjñijñāsyeta, tataḥ pratyakṣadṛṣṭamapyanyairanumānādibhirjñijñāsyeta, tatrāpi prītiviśeṣasaṃbhavādeva teṣāṃ viśeṣābhāvādityāha—pratyakṣadṛṣṭamiti | na ca teṣāmaniścāyakatvāt tairna jñijñāsyata ityāha—niścāyakatvamiti | ‘niścāyakatve’ iti pāṭhe tu niścāyakatve viśeṣābhāvamuktvā tatra heturuktaḥ—itareṣāmiti | niścāyakaṃ hi pramāṇaṃ bhavati ; tānyapi ca pramāṇam ; ato niścāyakānītyarthaḥ | pramāṇabhāvāditi ; pramāṇatvādityarthaḥ | athocyeta—saṃnikṛṣṭaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣam, saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ ca hānopādānayogyam ; anumānādi tu viprakṛṣṭaviṣayam, na tena vyavahartuṃ śakyam ; tenetaraiḥ pramitamapi pratyakṣeṇa pramitsyate, na pratyakṣapramitamitarairiti ; tadāśaṅkyati—hānādīti | hānādiyogyo viṣayo yasya tat tathoktam | saṃnikṛṣṭo'rtho viṣayo yasya tat ; saṃnikṛṣṭārthatvāditi hānādiyogyaviṣayatve hetuḥ | tannirasyati ; yadi saṃnikṛṣṭo'pekṣita
[p. 94]
starhi taddhetureva prameyasya samīpānayanaṃ pramāturvā tatsamīpagamanapaddeyatām, na tu pramāṇam; taddhi pramitaye'pekṣyate; sā ca pūrvapramāṇādeva siddhetyarthaḥ | prakṛte cātmani prameye naiva saṃnikṛṣṭāsaṃnikṛṣṭalakṣaṇo viśeṣo'sti, yena saṃnikarṣārthaṃ pratyakṣamapekṣyate; ātmano nityasaṃnikṛṣṭatamatvādityabhiprāyeṇāha— P. 34-4. prakṛte ceti | atha matam—sāmānyaviṣayāḥ śabdādayaḥ, viśeṣaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣam; tena śabdāt sāmānyataḥ pramitamapi viśeṣataḥ pramātuṃ pratyakṣamapekṣyata iti | tadyuktam; evaṃ tarhi śabdena hetunānadhigatameva viśeṣarūpaṃ pratyakṣaviṣayaḥ syāt; tataśca siddho naḥ pakṣaḥ; na siddhasya punaḥ siddhañcapekṣāyāṃ heturasti; tadetatsarvaṃ sāśaṅkumāha—sāmānyeti | itara idānīṃ tathaiva pratyavāteṣṭate—bāḍhamiti | asmākamapyabhimatametat, yacchabdenānavagataviṣayaṃ pratyakṣamiti; atastadarthaṃ karmopāsanādyapekṣā ghaṭiṣyata ityāśayaḥ | uktamiti | 'tasya ca brahma gocaraḥ, na śabdasya' ityatroktamityarthaḥ | tadetat dūṣayati—yadīti | yadi śabdena hetunā viśeṣato'nadhigataṃ brahma, kathaṃ tarhi tasmin brahmaṇi viśeṣarūpeṇopāsanā pravartatām? na hyajñātamupāsituṃ śakyamiti bhāvaḥ | na ca śabdādhigataṃ sāmānyarūpamupāsiṣyate, viśeṣarūpaṃ cāparokṣaṃ bhaviṣyatīti vācyam; anyasminnupāsite'nyasya sākṣātkaraṇāsaṃbhavādityāha—na ceti | kiṃ ca sāmānyamātraviṣayāḥ śabdā ityapyasiddham, apahatapāpmānandādiviśeṣasyāpi tataḥ pratīteḥ; yadi tvapahatapāpmānandādipuruṣārtharūpaṃ śabdānna gamyeta, tataḥ kasya rūpasya sākṣātkaraṇāya "yajñena dānena" iti śrutyā yajñādividhānaṃ syāt? api tu na syādevetyabhiprāyeṇāha—kasya ceti | yathā śabdādviśeṣarūpe jñāte yajñādividhānaṃ ghaṭate, yathā cājñāte na ghaṭate tadubhayamāha—śabdeti | ayamarthaḥ—yadi śabdenānandādirūpabrahmasvabhāva ātmā darśitaḥ syāt, tata ānandādipuruṣārthāvāptaye sādhanāpekṣāyāṃ yajñādīnāṃ vidhānamavakalpate; śabdatastvānandādau puruṣārtharūpe'nadhigate, tadviparīte'puruṣārtharūpe cātmanyadhigate yajñādividhirasadartho'vidyamānārtho vyarthaḥ syāt; tasmādānandādiviśeṣarūpamapi śabdādavagamyata iti bhāvaḥ | śabdenopadarśitaṃ niratiśayānandāpahatapāpmādirūpaṃ yasya brahmaṇaḥ tatsvabhāvasyātmana ityarthaḥ | tatra niratiśayānanda itīṣṭaprāptirūpapuruṣārtha uktaḥ |
[p. 95]
apahatapāpmeti cāniṣṭhaniṣṭhattirūpa iti vivekaḥ | aviditaṃ puruṣārtharūpaṃ dhasyetyātmānyapadārthaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | P. 84-12. apahatapāpmādiguṇakabrahmasvabhāve ātmani nirvicikitsāt niḥsaṃśayāt śabdāt pramāṇāt prakaṭatāṃ gate nāparaṃ pratyakṣamapekṣaṇīyamasti ; ataḥ śābdameva jñānamapavargaheturityabhiprāyaḥ | nanu śābdameva jñānaṃ cedapavargahetuḥ, tatastasminnutpanne saṃsāradharmaiḥ śrutpipāsādibhirnirvartitavyam ; te tvavagate'pi “tattvamasti” iti śabdāt ‘brahmaivāham’ iti brahmātmatve, ajñāvasthāyāmiva dṛśyante ; atastannidṛcchaye'nyat sākṣātkāri jñānamapekṣyamiti codayati—nanviti | yadyapi “tattvamasti” vākyamātmano brahmabhāvaṃ vadati, na tu brahmaṇa ātmabhāvam ; tathāpyarthāt so'pyukta iti brahmātmabhāva ityuktam | dūṣayati—neti | dehendriyadharmāste lākṣādharmā iva maṇau mithyājñānenātmanyadhyastāḥ, na taccataḥ | atastasmin samyagdarśanaparāhate kutastannimittānāṃ dharmāṇāmātmani saṃbhava ityarthaḥ | avagato brahmaṇa ātmabhāvo yeneti vigrahaḥ | ata eva viditātmayāthātathyo viditamātmano yāthātathyaṃ paramārtharūpaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | na kevalamuparpattiḥ, śrutirapyatrārthe'stītyāha—śrūyate ceti | na hi saṃsāradharmānuvṛttau brahmavido brahmībhāvaḥ śrutyukto ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ | na ca te brahmaṇa eva dharmāḥ, yena brahmavido'pyanuvarterannityāha—na ceti | puṇyapāpanimittāste'pahatapāpmani brahmaṇi na saṃbhavantītyarthaḥ | pāpmeti puṇyamapyucyate | atraivārthe śrutyantaraṃ cāha—tatheti | paramānandamadvayamātmānaṃ cet vijānīyāt, tataḥ kimicchan kasya ca putrādeḥ kāmāya śarīropādhikena duḥkhena śarīramanusaṃjvaret saṃtapyediti viduṣaḥ saṃsāradharmātikramamāha | śrutyantaraṃ cāha—tatheti | vāśabda evakārārthe | nanviyaṃ śrutiḥ śarīrapātādūrdhvamaśarīrasya priyāpriyāsparśamāha, na tu jīvataḥ ; jīvata evātmavidaḥ saṃsāradharmaprasaṅgaśroditaḥ, sa tu saśarīra eva ; kathamaśarīraśrutyocyata ityāśaṅkyāha—mithyeti | na tvaruvātmanaḥ karmanimittaḥ saṃsāraśarīrasaṃbandhaḥ, “niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyam” iti śruterniṣkriyatvāt,
[p. 96]
putrādiṣviva cedaṃpratyayaprasaṅgāt ; kiṃ tu yo'yamanātmani śarīre ‘aham’ iti mithyātmakatvābhimānaḥ satyatvenābhimataḥ, tannimittaḥ | tasmin śarīrasaṃbandhe dehātiriktātmatattvadarśanāt nimittanivṛttyā nivṛtte saṃskāramātreṇa satyāmapi śarīrābhāsānuvṛttau jīvata evāśarīratvamityarthaḥ | P. 34-22. evamaśarīratvaṃ vyākhyāya tatra śrutyarthaṃ yojayati—tatreti | tatra tādṛśyaśarīratvaṃ priyasyeṣṭasyāpriyasya cāniṣṭasyāsparśaṃ ātmanyākhyāyate ; anayā śrutyeti vākyaśeṣaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | vyatirekamāha—yastviti | tatheti, sāṃsārikadharmabhāgityarthaḥ | itiḥ pūrvapakṣasamāptau | ‘syādetat—asādhyatvāt brahmaṇo na karmaṇānupayogaḥ’ ityārabhya nānāprakārakarmajñānasaṃbandhaṃ nirasya punaḥ pratyakṣānubhavasiddhyarthaṃ karmopāsanādisaṃbandhamāśaṅkya ‘śābdajñānādeva niḥśreyasam, na pratyakṣānubhavāt ; ato na tadarthamapi karmopāsanāpekṣā’ iti siddhāntamāha—alocyata iti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | āptavacanena viniścitaṃ dikcandrayostattvaṃ yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ puṃsāṃ dvicandradigviparyāsādayo yathānuvartanta iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | dārṣṭāntike yojayati—tatheti | nirvicikitsādāmnāyādavagatamātmatattvaṃ yena tasya puṃso'nādimithyādarśanasyābhyāsena paunaḥpunyenopahitasya sahitasya svata eva ca bhavataḥ saṃskārasya sāmarthyānmithyāvabhāsasyānuvṛttirasti ; atastannivṛttaye'sti śābdajñānādanyadapekṣaṇīyam | yadapekṣyaṃ tadāha—tacceti | mithyādarśanabādhakasya tattvadarśanasyābhyāsaḥ saṃskāradārḍhyakaratvena loka evānvayavyatirekasiddhaḥ | yajñādayaśca “tametaṃ yajñena” ityādiśabdapramāṇakā ityarthaḥ | tatrābhyāsasyopāsanāparaparyāyasyopayogamāha—abhyāso hīti | tattvadarśanābhyāsaḥ saṃskāraṃ draḍhayati ; dṛḍhībhūtaḥ saṃskāraḥ pūrvamithyādarśanajaṃ saṃskāraṃ pratibadhya svakāryaṃ tattvadarśanākhyaṃ saṃtanoti pravāhayati ; tataścātmasvarūpāvirbhāva iti bhāvaḥ | karmaṇāṃ copayogamāha—yajñādayaśceti | atraiva matāntaramāha—śreya iti | upayujyata ityadhyāhāryam | atra hetumāha—nityānāmiti |
[p. 97]
tadevaṃ mithyāvabhāsanivṛttyarthaṃ karmopāsanānupraveśe darśite śābdajñānamālavādau punaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—syādetaditi | moktṛtvābhimānāt P. 35-8. bhogopakaraṇeṣu svakucandanādiṣu rāgaḥ; tataḥ śubhā jyotiṣṭomādilakṣaṇā aśubhā ca brahmahatyādilakṣaṇā pravṛttiḥ; tataḥ punaḥ śarīragrahaṇamityevaṃ moktṛtvādyabhimāno bodhātpūrvaṃ pramāṇatvena gṛhītaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ; sa śābdenābhoktṛtvādirūpātmatattvāvabodhena bādhito'nubartamāno'pi na pūrvatvapravṛttiṃ prasūte; na hi sūryodayāvagataprācīdiktattvo diṅmūḍho'pyudīcīṃ vyavaharati, kiṃ tu prāmāṇikatattvaniśrayānurūpameva; tenotpannātmatattvabodhasya karmopāsanādau pravṛttireva nāsti; ato na mithyāvabhāsānuvṛttinivṛttaye karmopāsanādyapekṣyamiti bhāvaḥ | tadetaddūṣayati—ucyata iti | mithyā artho yeṣāṃ te mithyārthāḥ, mithyārthā iva mithyārthāḥ; na tu mithyārthā eva, tattvaniśrayānāṃ mithyārthatvānupapatteḥ; mithyāviṣayajñānavat svānurūpaṃ vyavahāraṃ na pravartayanti; mithyājñānameva tu bādhitamapi svānurūpaṃ vyavahāraṃ pravartayatītyarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | yathā diṅmūḍhatyāprabodhitadiktattvasyāpyananusaṃhitāsavacasastatra niśrayā api mithyārthā bhavantīti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | kuta ityāha— prāgiveti | dṛṣṭāntāntaramāha—tatheti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | saṃskārasya hetorabhibhavocchedamukhena hetumato mithyāvabhāsasya nivṛttirabhipretā | imamarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | “ śrotavyaḥ ” iti vedāntaśravaṇādutpannaśābdatattvadarśanasyāpi “ nididhyāsitavyaḥ ” ityuttarakālamabhyāsa ucyata ityarthaḥ | tathā damādi vidhīyana ityāha—dameti | vyatirekamāha— anyatheti | śābdajñānādeva sāṃsārikadharmanivṛttau dhyānādyupadeśo vyartha eva syādityarthaḥ | punaḥ paramatamāśaṅkayati—syādetaditi | brahmacaryādyupakaraṇādāmnāyāt tattvajñānaṃ bhavati; tena śābdajñānasiddhyartha eva brahmacaryādisādhanopadeśaḥ, na vyartha ityarthaḥ | taddūṣayati—tadasaditi | śabdamātrādeva śamādinirapekṣāt śābdyāḥ pratipatterutpattiḥ; ato na tadutpattaye śamādyapekṣā; na cānapekṣitamupakaraṇaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | enadeva yuktyopapādayati—na hīti | sādhanaviśeṣebhyaḥ śamādibhyaḥ prāgāmnāyo nāvācakaḥ, śabdārthasaṃbandhasyautpattikatvāt | na ca saṃbandhajñānavadvācakasyāpi śamādya- 7
[p. 98]
pekṣocitā ; na hi saṃbandhajñāneneva śamabrahmacaryādibhirvinā śabdādarthapratītirna jāyata iti bhāvaḥ | na ca vācyam—yadi nāma prāk sādhanaviśeṣebhyo nāvācaka āmnāyaḥ tathāpi puruṣamātravākyavadaniścāyakaḥ ; tena niścayārthaṃ śamādapekṣiṣyata iti ; yataḥ puruṣāśrayāṇāṃ doṣāśaṅkānāmapauruṣeyavede nāsti P. 35-20 saṃbhava ityāha—nāpīti | yadi punarapauruṣeyo'pyāmnāyo na niścāyakaḥ syāt , tadāmnāyopadiṣṭeṣu śamādisādhaneṣvapi niścayābhāvāt pravṛttirdurlabhā syādityāha—anyatheti | nanu yadyāmnāyo niścāyakaḥ , tato ‘brahmāham’ ityapi tato niścayo jātaḥ ; tathā sati ca kutaḥ saṃsāradharmānuvṛttiḥ ? na hi taccaniścaye jāte mithyādharmānuvṛttiryuktā | yattu diṅmūḍhādyudāhṛtam, tatra vismṛtataccasya prāgiva pravṛttiryuktā ; na tviha tathetyāśaṅkayodāharaṇāntaramāha—api ceti | raṅge nāyyasthāne bharatā naṭā rāmādayo bhūtvā praviṣṭāḥ sītādiviyogāt duḥkhena rudantaḥ ‘neme rāmādayaḥ, kiṃ tu naṭāḥ’ iti taccaṃ jānatāmapi prekṣakāṇāṃ śokaṃ mahajjanayanti ; tathā vyāghrībhūya gayam, kvaciddharṣam ; evamātmataccaniścaye'pi sāṃsārikadharmānuvṛttirapi bhavatītyarthaḥ | udāharaṇāntaramāha—niścite ceti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | yasmādātmataccaṃ jānatāmapi mithyāvabhāsāḥ śokabhayādīn saṃsāradharmānanuvartayanti, tasmāttannivṛttaye niścito brahmaṇa ātmabhāvaḥ ātmaccaṃ yena tenāpi puṃsā śokādisaṃsāradharmanivartakasākṣātkārajñānasādhanāni śamādīnyapekṣitavyānītyarthaḥ | viditātmayāthātathyasyāpi dṛṣṭārtha ivādṛṣṭārthe'pi karmaṇi pravṛttisiddheriti bhāvaḥ | na caivaṃ sati yajñādikāraṇasāpekṣatvāt mukteḥ kāryatā syāditi vācyamityāha—yatheti | na khalu yajñādibhiḥ sākṣānmuktiḥ kriyate, kiṃ tvavasthāviśeṣo'bhivyajyate ; tataścāmnāyāt pramāṇādātmatattvābhivyaktau yathā mukterna kāryatā, tathā yajñādisādhanebhya ānandāvasthābhivyaktiviśeṣe'pi sākṣātkārarūpe | nanu śabdajñānāccenna muktiḥ kiṃ tu yajñādyapekṣāt, kathaṃ tarhi jñānānmuktimupadiśantyaḥ “brahma veda brahmeva bhavati” ityādyāḥ śrutayo vyākhyeyā ityāśaṅkayāha—śrutayasviti | prathamaṃ śabdādvijñāyātma-
[p. 99]
tattvaṃ tasyānucintanamabhyāsaḥ, tasya pariniṣpattau satyāṃ yā vigalitasakalaśokādisaṃsāradharmasākṣātkārijñānāvasthā tadviṣayā ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—“brahma veda” iti vidinā na śābdajñānamabhidhīyate, kiṃ tu sākṣātkāri jñānameveti | yadvā śābdameva jñānaṃ vidinocyate ityetadāha— śabdasādhaneti | śabdaḥ sādhanaṃ yasyeti tathoktam | śabdasādhanaṃ ca tat P. 36-2. jñānaṃ ceti vigrahaḥ | tat jñānaṃ viṣayatvenāpekṣanta iti tadapekṣāḥ śrutayo bhavanti tatharthaḥ | nanu śābdajñānamātrānna muktirityuktam, muktyanuguṇaṃ ca jñānaṃ vidinocyate ; tatkathaṃ śābdajñānamucyata ityāśaṅkyāha—tadvetutvāditi | śābdajñānahetutvāduttarasya sākṣātkārijñānasyetyarthaḥ | yadyapi śābdajñānaṃ na sākṣātmuktisādhanam, tathāpi muktyātmakasākṣātkārijñānahetutvāt pāraṃparyeṇa muktihetutayā vidinocyata iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ karmasākṣātkārajñānayorupāyopeyalakṣaṇasaṃbandhapradarśanena jñānakarmaṇorasaṃbandhapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā ṛṇāpākaraṇapakṣamidānīṃ dūṣayitumanuvadati—yadapīti | ityuktamiti vākyaśeṣaḥ | tadūṣayati—tadapīti | apākṛtaṛṇasya mokṣādhikāra iti yaduktaṃ tadapi na niyogato niyamenetyarthaḥ | kuta ityāha— āśrameti | yadyapākṛtaṛṇasyaiva mokṣe'dhikāraḥ, tato gārhasthyaṃ vinā ṛṇatrayāpākaraṇāsaṃbhavāt gṛhasthāśramamātramupadiśet ; tataścāśramavikalpaśravaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt ; ato nāyaṃ niyamaḥ—yadapākṛtaṛṇasyaiva mokṣe'dhikāra iti | kiṃ punastadāśramavikalpasmaraṇamiti tadāha—tasyeti | na kevalaṃ smṛtiḥ, api tu śrutirapyāśramāntarasthasyāpi mokṣe'dhikāramāhetyāha—yadi veti | tathā śrutyantarāṇyāha—enaditi | agnihotrādinityakarmabhirdevamṛṇaṃ prajayā paitryaṃ svādhyāyenārṣamapākriyate | tadyadyapākṛtaṛṇatrayasya mokṣe'dhikāraḥ syāt, tato mumukṣoragnihotrādikarmaprajāsvādhyāyānāṃ tyāgaśrutayo na ghaṭeranniti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca karmatyāgadarśanādityupalakṣaṇam, prajāsvādhyāyatyāgāvapi draṣṭavyau | yadyanapākṛtaṛṇatrayasyāpi mokṣe'dhikāraḥ, kā tarhi “ṛṇāni trīṇyapākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet” ityādismṛtergatirityāśaṅkya pūrvodāhataśrutyavirodhena tāṃ vyācaṣṭe—pratipanneti | pratipannaṃ prāptaṃ kāryatve- 7A
[p. 100]
nāṅgīkṛtaṃ gārhasthyaṃ yena tasyātmavidyāmupādāya tayaiva kṛtakṛtyo bhaviṣyā- P. 86-10 mīti manvānasyāt eva ṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ pratyanāṭṭatasyeti saṃbandhe ṣaṣṭhī, tatsaṃbandhitvenātmavidyotpattipratibandhutvaṃ darśayatīyaṃ vakṣyamāṇā smṛtiḥ | kasya pratibandhutvamityapekṣāyāmāha—avaśyakartavyatayā yadvihitamṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ tasya yadakaraṇaṃ tadeva nimittaṃ yasya pāpmanaḥ tasyeti kartari ṣaṣṭhī, vidyodayasyeti ca karmaṇi; tenāyaṃ smṛtyarthaḥ—gṛhasthaḥ san ṛṇāni trīṇyapākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet, anapākṛtarṇastu mokṣamicchan vihitākaraṇaniāmattapāpapratibaddhavidyatvāt na mokṣaṃ vindati; kiṃ tu tena pāpenādho gacchatītyarthaḥ | yadyapi smṛtāvavaśyaṃ gṛhastha iti viśeṣo nāsti, tathāpi pūrvoktaśrutivirodhāt kalpyaḥ; anyathā smṛtirapramāṇaṃ syāt | taduktam—“virodhe tvanapekṣaṃ syāt” iti | evamṛṇāpākaraṇapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā saṃyogapṛthaktvapakṣaṃ karmaṇāṃ sākṣātkārijñānopayogitvena siddhāntānuguṇamupapannamabhyupagacchati—idaṃ tviti | svavākyopāttasvargādikāryāntaranirākāṅkṣāṇāmapi karmaṇāṃ “tametaṃ vedānubandhanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti” iti vidyāsaṃyogasya pṛthaktvāt vidyāṅgabhāvo vidyāṅgatvaṃ yaduktamidaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | tu śabdaḥ pūrvoktapakṣebhyo viśeṣaṃ darśayati | evamaṅgabhāvaṃ prasādhyotpattau kārye comayathāpi dravyādiprayājādivadaṅgabhāvasya darśanāt viśinaṣṭi—so'pi karmaṇāṃ vidyāṃ pratyaṅgabhāvaḥ yathoktaprakāreṇa vidyotpattyarthatayā, na tu yathā darśapūrṇamāsādikāryaṃ svargādikaṃ pratyupayogena prayājādīnāṃ tathotparyaḥ | utpattiratrābhivyaktirabhipretā | kasmātpunaḥ kāryopayogenaiva karmaṇāṃ vidyāṅgatvaṃ na bhavatītyāha—vidyāyā iti | darśādau hi svargādi kāryamasti, tena yuktaṃ tadupayogena prayājādīnāmaṅgatvam; iha tu vidyāyāḥ sākṣātkaraṇalakṣaṇāyā na kāryāntaramasti | mokṣaḥ kāryaṃ iti cenna; tasyā eva mokṣatvāt; kāryatve cānityatvāt | nanvavidyānivṛttireva kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati | maivam | vidyodaya eva tannivṛttiḥ, na pṛthak; taduktam—“virodhibhāvāntarotpāda eva pūrvapradhvaṃsaḥ” iti | evaṃ pṛthaksaṃyogapakṣaṃ svasiddhāntānuguṇatvena yuktatvena cābhyupagacchedānīṃ ‘karmabhiḥ saṃskṛtasya jñānādhikāraḥ’ itīmamapi pakṣaṃ svasiddhāntānuguṇaṃ
[p. 101]
yuktameva manyamāna āha—saṃskārapakṣo veti | yuktamiti liṅgavyatyenānu- P. 36-16 vartate | kutaḥ punaḥ pramāṇādayaṃ pakṣaḥ sidhyati, yena yuktamityucyata ityāśaṅkyāha—smṛteriti | “yasyaite catvāriṃśatsaṃskārāḥ” ityādismṛterityarthaḥ | nanu yadi puruṣasaṃskārārthāni karmāṇi, na tarhi vidyopayogitvam; tacca pūrvamuktam; tatkathamityāśaṅkyāha—saṃskṛtasyeti | na hi saṃskāraḥ saṃskāryārthaḥ | kiṃ tarhi? saṃskṛtasya kāryāntarotpattyarthaḥ; taccātmasākṣātkāralakṣaṇā vidyetyarthaḥ | pakṣadvayamapi sūtrakāravacanena draḍhayati—taduktamiti | na kevalaṃ svārtha vidyā śamādyapekṣate, api tu brahmacaryādyāśramacatuṣṭayasaṃbaddhaṃ yathākramamagnisamindhanāgnihotratapaśraraṇadhyānādikarmāpi vidyāpekṣate, vihitatvāditi sūtrārthaḥ | atra pakṣadvaye'pi codayati—nanviti | dṛṣṭarūpo'bhyāsa upāyo yasya sa dṛṣṭopāyaḥ sākṣātkāraṇalakṣaṇavidyotpādaḥ; atra dṛṣṭaiva dṛṣṭopakārivetikartavyatā cittavikṣepahantrī śamadamādikāpekṣayatām; dṛṣṭopāye hi kārye dṛṣṭaivetikartavyatā dṛṣṭā; yathā amyupāye pāke'gnisamindhanādikā dṛṣṭarūpā; yajñādayastvadṛṣṭārthāḥ, tena vidyotpādena nāpekṣyā ityarthaḥ | cittavikṣepahantrīti śamādedṛṣṭopakāritāmāha | nanu yadi nāma śamādiritikartavyatā cittavikṣepahantrī, tathāpi vidyotpāde kathaṃ tasyā dṛṣṭopakāritvamityāśaṅkyāha—samāhitasyeti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—śamādibhiścittavikṣepavidhātāt samāhitācittaḥ puruṣo bhavati; samāhitacittasyābhyasyato jñānasya prasādaḥ praśāntirviśuddhatā bhavatītyarthaḥ | kasmātpunaryajñādayo nāpekṣyanta ityāśaṅkyāha—tairiti | yajñādibhirvināpyabhyāsenaiva jñānaprasādasaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | abhyupagamenaivottaramāha—satyamiti | etadeva draḍhayati—tathāceti | tairiti; yajñādibhirityarthaḥ | nanu pūrvaṃ yajñādervidhāṅgatvamuktam, adhunā vidyāyāstadanapekṣatvamabhyupagacchataḥ pūrvāparavirodha ityāśaṅkyāha—kiṃtviti | mukterekarūpatvāt sargādivat sādhanaviśeṣāt kharūpaviśeṣo na saṃbhavatītyataḥ kālakṛta evātra viśeṣo yukta iti bhāvaḥ | tameva viśeṣamāha—sādhaneti | yathā yathā kāraṇe viśeṣaḥ prakarṣo bhavati,
[p. 102]
tathā tathā sā vidyā śīghraṃ śīghrataraṃ ca vyajyate ; yathā yathā ca tadabhāvo viśeṣābhāvaḥ sādhanāpacayo bhavati, tathā tathā cireṇa ciratareṇa ca sā vyajyata ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—kevalabhyāsaśritareṇa vidyāmabhivyanakti, yajñādiṣvekaikasahitaḥ śīghram ; sarvasahitaḥ śīghrataramiti | P. 36-23 etadeva sūtrakāravacanena draḍhayati—taduktamiti | sūtrārthamāha—eṣa iti | karmabhirvināpyabhyāsalabhyāyāmapi karmāṇyapekṣyante vidyāyām, “yajñena” iti śravaṇādityarthaḥ | yathāntareṇeti nigadavyākhyāto dṛṣṭāntārthaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | “satyaṃ jñānamanantam” iti śrute-viśuddhajñānasvabhāvaṃ brahma, vidyāpi cābhyāsalabhyā viśuddhajñānasvabhāvaiva ; ato brahmaiva sā, na brahmaṇo'nyā ; dvaitaprataṅgācca | brahma ca nityam ; ata evākāryam ; tatra tasmin sati tatra ca nityākāryasvabhāve brahmaṇi kathaṃ kiñcit yajñādyapekṣyeta ? kāryaṃ hi sādhanaṃ tadanugrāhakaṃ cāṅgamapekṣate, nākāryamiti bhāvaḥ | pariharati—ucyata iti | upa samīpe dhīyate dhāryate ityupadhānaṃ japākusumādi, tena tirohitaṃ śuklarūpaṃ yasya sa sphaṭika-maṇirakāryo'pyupadhānasya japāderapakarṣaṇamapanayanamapekṣate śuklarūpasya vyaktaye yathā, tathā akāryamapi brahma avidyātirohitasvarūpābhivyaktaye yajñādīnmapekṣata ityarthaḥ | yadyapi sphaṭikamaṇiḥ kāryaḥ, tathāpi tadvyāpārakāryo na bhavatītyetāvataiva sāmyam | nanu kṣaṇikāḥ sarvabhāvāḥ ; tenāgnisaṃnidhānādiva kāṣṭhasya japādyupādhisaṃnidhānāt sphaṭikasya pūrvarūpaṃ naṣṭam, anyadutpannam ; upādherapagame ca punaranyadeva śuklaṃ rūpamutpannam ; tena kāryameva śuklaṃ rūpamupādhyapakarṣaṇamapekṣate, nākāryamiti viṣamo dṛṣṭānta ityāśaṅkyāha—na hīti | kasmātpunareva na bhavatītyāha—na hīti | anekairvisamāgai-visadṛśairlohitasphaṭikakṣaṇairvyavahitasya lohitakṣaṇebhyaḥ pūrvo yaḥ śukla-kṣaṇaḥ tatsabhāgasya tatsadṛśasya śuklakṣaṇasya punarutpattirakasmāt kāraṇaṃ vinā na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu pūrvakṣaṇa evottarasya hetuḥ ; tatkathamakasmādityucyate ? ucyate—yastāvadanantaro lohitakṣaṇaḥ sa na śuklaṃ kṣaṇamārabhate, visadṛśatvāt ; sadṛśārambhako hi pūrvakṣaṇaḥ ; tathā hi—ghaṭakṣaṇo ghaṭamārabhate, na paṭam | nanu visadṛśamapyārabhate, yathā kāṣṭha-
[p. 103]
kṣaṇo'ṅgārakṣaṇam | satyaṃ visadṛśahetupapāte ; visadṛśo hi tatra heturagnirūpahitaḥ ; tena visadṛśamārabhate ; na ceha tathāsti, visadṛśasya japāderapanītatvāt | nanvastu tarhi pūrvataraśuklakṣaṇo'ntyasya śuklakṣaṇasya hetuḥ | tadapi na śakyate ; bahubhirlohitakṣaṇairvyavahitaśriravinaṣṭo nottarasya hetubhavitumarhati | tadidamuktam—anekavisabhāgakṣaṇavyavahitasyeti | tatraiva dṛṣṭāntamāha—na hīti | tadeva sphaṭikadṛṣṭāntamupapādya dārṣṭāntike P. 37-8. yojayati—tasmāditi | nanu sphaṭikasya nijaṃ rūpaṃ sthitameva ; na tadarthamapakarṣaṇe puruṣaḥ prayatate, kiṃtu tajjñānāya ; tacca kāryam ; ataḥ kāryameva jñānaṃ tatrānyāpekṣam, nākāryaṃ sphaṭikarūpamiti punaśrodayati— syādetaditi | tadetat vikalpya pariharati—kiṃ punariti | vyavahārāya hi puruṣaḥ pravartate ; sa cārthena ; na jñānamātreṇa, mithyājñāne'pi prasaṅgāt ; tenārtharūpāya jñānamarthyate, na tu svataḥ ; tataśca jñānārtho vyāpāro'rthārthaḥ ; ataśca yadyapi jñānaṃ kāryam, tathāpi jñānārtho vyāpāro'rthenaivāpekṣito bhavati, na jñānena ; tasyārthapradhānatvādityarthaḥ | idamuktaṃ bhavati—arthasyaiveyamapekṣā jñānadvāreṇopajātā 'jānīhi, tato vyavahara' iti | tenākāryasyāpyanyāpekṣāsti, tathā ātmano'pi bhaviṣyatīti | nanu jñānaṃ cedārthārtham, tato nūnamarthe kaṃcidvikāramādhatte ; na hyarthe kiṃcidakurvatastādarthamavakalpate ; vikāraśca kāryaḥ ; tena kāryameva sāpekṣam, nārtha ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | syādeva yadi jñānena vikāro'rthe janyeta, na tvevamastītyarthaḥ | yadvā jñānena cedartho'bhivyajyate, nūnaṃ jñānādarthe kaścidvikāraḥ syāt, akiṃcitkarasyānabhivyañjakatvāt ; ataśca brahmaṇyabhivyajyamāne tathaiva vikārāpatteranityatvadoṣaḥ syādityāśaṅkya āstāṃ tāvat brahmaṇi, arthe'pi na jñānāt vikāro bhavatītyāha—na ceti | yadvā nanu jñānamarthaṃ vikṛtyābhivyanakti, itarathā sarvadhānanyathābhūtasya prāgivābhivyaktyayogāt ; tataśca yuktā tatrābhivyaktaye'nyāpekṣā ; brahma tvavikāryatvādabhivyaṅktumaśakyam ; ataḥ kathamarthavadabhivyaktaye'nyadapekṣyatāmiti dṛṣṭāntavaiṣamyamāśaṅkya sāmyapradarśanārthamāha—na ceti | yadvā nanvartho jaḍatvāda-
[p. 104]
nabhivyaktasvabhāvastajjñānena prāktavyamupajanaśyābhivyajyatām; abhivyaktaye cānyadapekṣyam; brahma punarātmajyotirnityābhivyaktasvabhāvaṃ kathamabhivyajyate, abhivyaktaye cānyadapekṣyate ityāśaṅkya sarvatraivābhivyaktirna pa. 37-14 tāttvikī, kiṃtu māyāmadhīti darśayitumāha—na ceti | yadi jñānādarthe prāktavyādivikāraḥ kaścidbhavet, tatastatrābhivyaktistāttvikī syāt; yadā tu naivam, tadā sarvatraiva sā māyāmadhī; tathā brahmaṇyapi kiṃ na syāt? na hi māyāyāṃ pūrvoktānupapattidoṣaḥ; anupapannādyaiva hi māyetyucyata iti bhāvaḥ | jñānādarthasya vikārābhāve hetumāha—tayoriti | nāsaṃbaddhaṃ hīndhanamamirvikaroti; asaṃbaddhaṃ ca jñānamāntaraṃ bahirarthena; ato nārthaṃ vikarotītyarthaḥ | athetarasaṃbandhābhāve'pi yogyadeśatālakṣaṇo'rthasya jñānena saṃbandho bhaviṣyatīti cedata āha—yogyeti | yogyadeśatā arthasyārjavenāvasthānam, tacca rūparasādau sapānāśraye sarvatra samānam; ato rūpajñānaṃ rūpa iva rasādāvapi prāktavyākhyāyāṃ vikāraṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | yadyapi asaṃniruṣṭasya vikārakatvamagnyādiṣu na dṛṣṭamityayamatra parihāraḥ, tathāpyamyupetya parihārāntaramuktamiti | kiṃca jñānaṃ cedarthe prāktavyaṃ janayet tataḥ kasyacidekasya jñānena jāte prāktavye arthaḥ sarveṣāṃ prakaṭaḥ syāt, yathaikasya pākena jātalohityo ghaṭaḥ sarveṣāṃ lohitaḥ, na pakṣareva, tadāha— sarveti | kiṃca vartamāne'rthe janyatāṃ nāma jñānena prāktavyam; atītānāgate tu liṅgādigamye na tatsaṃbhavaḥ, dharmiṇa evābhāvādityabhiprāyeṇāha— dhvasteti | evaṃ dṛṣṭāntasvarūpamupapādyopasaṃharati—tasmāditi | sphaṭikadravyaṃ hi lohitakambalenāvakuṇṭhanavat na japākusumena tirohitam; nāpi tatprabhayā, tasyā aprabhādravyatvāt; atha ca tirohitamivābhāti, na tu tattvatastattirohitam; ata evātirohitatvāt tattvato nābhivyajyate, tathāpyabhivyajyamānamivopadhānāpakarṣaṇaprayatnamapekṣate yathetyarthaḥ | evaṃ dṛṣṭānte siddhamarthaṃ dārṣṭāntike yojayati—tatheti | tathātmatattvamapi kṛṣṇakambaleneva nānādyavidyātirohitam, tathāpi svarūpānavabhāsāt tirohitamiva yajñādi-
[p. 105]
prayatnādabhivyajyata ivetyadoṣa ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—tirodhānamabhivyaktiḥ prayatnāpekṣā ceti tritayamapyetadātmano na tāttvikam ; api tu avidyā bhrama eva ; tattvatastu nātmā tirohito na ca nityābhivyaktasvabhāvatvādabhivyajyate, na ca prayatnamapekṣata iti | evam ‘āmnāyataḥ prasiddhim’ ityādinā brahmasiddhau pramāṇamuktvā adhunā tatprameyasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpamāha—saṃhṛteti | ślokārthaṃ vidyaṇeti— P. 37-19 yata iti | anyairvaihvāviddhiriti pūjārtham, na svamataṃ paryudasitum | saṃhṛtā upasaṃhṛtā akhilā bhedā viśeṣā yasmin sa saṃhṛtākhilabhedaḥ | sāmānyātmā sāmānyasvabhāvaḥ, sa ātmā varṇitaḥ | yathā hema kaṭakādibhedānāṃ viśeṣāṇāmupasaṃhāreṇa sūcitaṃ nirūpitaṃ teṣāṃ kaṭakādibhedānāṃ sāmānyaṃ tathetyarthaḥ | pārihāryaṃ kaṭakamucyate | “ sa eṣa mahān ” ityādinā brahmavidyābhiyuktagranthaṃ paṭhati | yadviśeṣāpoddhāreṇa pratīyate, tadviśeṣavat ; yacca viśeṣavat tat sāmānyam, yathā suvarṇatattvam ; ātmatattvamapi “ sa eṣa neti neti ” iti sarvaviśeṣāpoddhāreṇa pratīyate ; tasmāt tadapi viśeṣāpoddhāreṇa pratīyamānatvāt sāmānyamiti tātparyārthaḥ | ślokamanyathā vyācaṣṭe—ye veti | yadi nirviśeṣaṃ yat tat sāmānyaṃ na bhavediti nirviśeṣasyāsāmānyarūpatā sādhyate, tataḥ siddhasādhanam ; asmākamapyadvayavādināṃ viyadādiviśeṣāṇāmabhāve vastudṛṣṭyā nātmatattvasāmānyamiṣṭam | kathaṃ tārhi brahmavādibhiḥ sāmānyamuktamata āha—sāmānyaṃ tūktamupacārata iti | nanu kutaścit sādhāraṇāddharmādupacāro bhavati ; yathā tīvratvādabhiśabdasya māṇavake ; tadatra kuto dharmādupacāra ityata āha—viśeṣeti | suvarṇādau pratiddhāt sāmānyasya dharmāt viśeṣapratyastamayamukhena nirūpyamāṇatvādātmani sādhāraṇādupacāra ityarthaḥ | asmin vyākhyāne prasiddhasuvarṇatattvasāmānyasādṛśyapradarśanārtho'yaṃ śloka iti draṣṭavyam | atha nirviśeṣaṃ yatsāmānyaṃ parairiṣṭaṃ tanna bhavet, nirviśeṣatvāt śaśāviṣāṇavaddhiti tasyābhāva eva sādhyate ; tato viśeṣaireva hetoḥ vyabhicāro'naikānti-
[p. 106]
katā | nanu viśeṣāṇāmapyavāntarāḥ santi viśeṣāḥ, tataśca teṣu nirviśeṣapatvaṃ hetureva nāsti ; kasya vyabhicāra ityata āha—nirviśeṣairiti | yadyapi pūrvaṃ viśeṣāḥ, teṣāmapyanaṅgatvādye nirviśeṣā avaśiṣyante tairvyabhicāra ityarthaḥ | etaccocchlokamuktamiti na ślokayojanāyāmabhiniveṣṭavyam || iti śrībrahmasiddhivyākhyāne brahmakāṇḍavyākhyānaṃ śaṅkhapaṇinā kṛtam ||
[p. 107]
ām brahmasiddhivyākhyā śaṅkhapāṇikṛtā tarkakāṇḍaḥ āmnāyapramāṇakramadvayaṃ brahmeti brahmakāṇḍe vṛttam | tatrādhunā yat pratyakṣādiviruddhaṃ vaktavadapramāṇam, pratyakṣādiviruddhatvāt śilāñjavanādivākyavat; advayāmnāyo'pi vyāvṛttastvabhāvano'vagrāhakaiḥ pratyakṣādibhirviruddhaḥ; ato'pramāṇamityākṣipya pratyakṣādiviruddhatvāditi hetorasiddhatāmabhidhātuṃ pratyakṣavirodhābhāvaṃ tāvadāha—āhuriti | yadi pratyakṣam 'ghaṭaḥ paṭo na P. 39-3. bhavati' iti niṣekṭu syāt, tato bhedaviṣayatvādadvayāmnāyaṃ viruddhyāt; na tu tadviṣekṭu vipaścita āhuḥ; apitu vastustvarūpamātrasya vidhātu grāhakam; tena hetunā ekatve'dvayaviṣaye yo'yam 'eka evāyamadvitīyaḥ' ityādirāmnāyaḥ sa pratyakṣeṇa na viruddhyata ityarthaḥ | atredānīṃ pratyakṣādivirodhe'pi nāmnāyasya prāmāṇyaṃ vyāhanyate; pauruṣeyaṃ hi vaco vaktṛpramāṇādhīnaprāmāṇyaṃ tadvirodhe syādapramāṇam, nāpauruṣeyamanapekṣaprāmāṇyam; tathā satyapi pratyakṣādivirodhe yadi tadapramāṇaṃ tadā viparyaya eva kiṃ na syāt? na hi pratyakṣādirāmnāyasya ca tulye'napekṣatve kaścit viśeṣaheturasti, yena pratyakṣādivirodhādāmnāyo'pramāṇam, na tadvirodhāt pratyakṣādīti; ato vṛthaiva pratyakṣādyavirodhapratipādanaprayāsa iti tadvicārārambhamākṣipati — nanviti | nāpekṣitā straprāmāṇyāya pratyakṣādeḥ pramāṇāntarasya vṛttiḥ vyāpāraḥ yena sa tathoktaḥ | tadvirodhe iti pratyakṣādivirodhe ityarthaḥ | atraikīyamatena tāvatparihāramāha—tatreti | pratyakṣādivirodhe āmnāyasya daurbalyam; ataḥ pratyakṣādivirodhena tadaprāmāṇyaṃ prāptam; na tu tadvirodhe pratyakṣādirityāmnāyaprāmāṇyāya pratyakṣādyavirodhapratipādanamarthavat, na tu vṛthetyarthaḥ | kathaṃ daurbalyamityāha—sāpekṣatvāditi | sāpekṣatvamāha—tathā-
[p. 108]
hīti | paṭhitasiddhaḥ kaścidāgnāyaḥ śrautapratyakṣamapekṣate, tathācāreṣu kartṛsāmānyānubheyo'numānam, mantraliṅgādigamyaśrārthāpattim, prākṛtaśrāṅgavidheryikṛtānupamānam | evaṃ sarve evāgnāyaḥ pratyakṣādīnyapekṣate ; tadadhīnā hi P. 39-11. tasya svarūpasiddhirityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | teṣāmiti pratyakṣādīnāmityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—taditi | teṣāṃ pratyakṣādīnāṃ tatprāmāṇyasya vāgnāyenāpavādhane'bhyupagamyamāne satyāgnāyasvarūpasyaiva tāvat siddhirna syāt ; dūrataratvarthasya yata ityarthaḥ | na ca yathāgnāyaḥ pratyakṣādīnyapekṣate tathā tānyapīti, yena tulyabalatvaṃ syāt | ataḥ pratyakṣādisāpekṣatvādāgnāyastebhyo durbala ityāha—na tvevamiti | itaśca durbala ityāha—dṛṣṭavyabhicāratvāditi | yadeva yathāvidhamekadārthaṃ saṃvadati vākyam, tadeva tathāvidhameva punaranyadā visaṃvadati ; na hi duṣṭo'pi vaktā śabdaṃ kalādhmātādinā dūṣayati | pratyakṣe tu duṣṭendriyajaṃ vyabhicāri, netaraditi viśeṣaḥ | pratyakṣagrahaṇaṃ tūpalakṣaṇārtham, anumānādyapi draṣṭavyam | yadyapi vede vyabhicāro na dṛṣṭaḥ, tathāpi loke dṛṣṭaḥ śabdatvasāmānyāt tatrāśaṅkyata iti bhāvaḥ | itaścāgnāyo durbala ityāha—apiceti | tataḥ kimityāha—anavakāśāśreti | na tāvadātmā pratyakṣādīnāmavakāśaḥ, tasyendriyādyagocaratvāt ; tatra prapañco'pi cenna syāt tato nirviṣayatvādanavakāśāste syurityarthaḥ | śabdastu sāvakāśa ityāha—sāvakāśa iti | kathamityāha—gauṇeneti | etadeva spaṣṭayati—tathā hīti | bhinnamapi jagat sattādyātmanā ekatvāt “ekapevādvitīyam” ityupacaryata ityarthaḥ | prakārāntareṇa sāvakāśatvamāha—vivakṣitārthānāmiti | tadeva draḍhayati— upaniṣada iti ; idaṃ svavacanaṃ vedāntānāṃ japopayogitvamāhetyarthaḥ | itaścāgnāyo durbala ityāha—kiñceti | vyākulatvamāha—karmeti | karmaviṣayo hi yasmāt sādhyasādhanādibhedaṃ viṣayatvenāśrayante ; vedāntāstvabhedamadvaitamityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—paraspareti | ataḥ parasparavyāvātādeva tāvat vedāntā durlabhaprāmāṇyāḥ ; parasparamavyāhatam ata evāgnāyasvarūpasiddhyarthatvena vā sarvavādisiddhārthatvena vā pratiṣṭhitaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ yeṣāṃ pratyakṣā-
[p. 109]
dīnāṃ tairvirodhe tu sutarāṃ durlabhaprāmāṇyā ityarthaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | P. 89-21 yasmāt pratyakṣādivirodhe vedāntānāmaprāmāṇyaṃ prasajyate tasmādityarthaḥ | sati virodhe pratyakṣādīnāmānmāyavādhakatve hetvantaramāha—mukhyatvāditi | pratyakṣādīnāṃ prāthamyādityarthaḥ | prāthamyamāha—mukhyā hīti | jātamātrasya jantoḥ prāṇino mukhyāḥ pūrvabhāvinaḥ pratyakṣādayaḥ ; vyutpannapadārthasya ca prabuddhasya kasyacideva paścātkāla āgnāyaḥ pramāṇam | katipayapuruṣasamavetādāmnāyādaśeṣajantugatatvenāpi pratyakṣāderatiśayadyotanārthaṃ jantugrahaṇam | tataḥ kimityāha—sa iti | sa āgnāyaḥ taiḥ pratyakṣādibhiḥ pūrvabhāvitayānupajātavirodhitvena pratiṣṭhitārthaiḥ pratiṣṭhito bhedarūpo'rtho viṣayo yeṣāṃ tairapahato'panīto yathāśrūyamāṇo'dvaitarūpo viṣayo yasya sa tathoktaḥ, ata eva kalpanīyārthaḥ pratyakṣādivirodhena kalpanīyaṃ śrutādarthāntaraṃ yasya sa bādhyate ; ananugrāhyaścet tadā pratyakṣādivirodhāt śrutārthatyāgaḥ, arthāntaraṃ ca na kalpyata iti viṣayāpahāralakṣaṇa evāyaṃ bādhaḥ | athānugrāhyaḥ, tadā pratyakṣādyanusāreṇa pratyakṣādigṛhītadvaitāpārityāgena jātyātmanādvaitamityevamarthakalpanāmarhatītyarthaḥ | yadvā pratyakṣādi pratiṣṭhitārtham ; āgnāyastu pramāṇāntarānusāreṇa kalpanīyārthaḥ, ato'pratiṣṭhitārthaḥ ; tena durbalatvāt bādhyate, tadanusāreṇa vārthakalpanamarhatītyarthaḥ | evaṃ svarūpato mukhyatāmuktvārthato'pi pratyakṣādīnāṃ mukhyatāmāha—arthata iti | kathamityāha—padeti | padapadārthavibhāge jñāte satyānmāyārthasya paricchedaḥ, sa ca padapadārthavibhāgaḥ pratyakṣādiṣvāyatate pratyakṣādyāyattaḥ ; etaduktaṃ bhavati—pratyakṣāderviṣayaḥ padapadārthabhedaḥ pūrvabhāvī, āgnāyasya cābhedaḥ paścādbhāvī ; tena viṣayamukhyatvenāpi pratyakṣādermukhyatvāmiti | upasaṃharati—ata iti | yato mukhyatvena balīyasā pratyakṣādinā virodhe nāgnāyasya prāmāṇyaṃ syādata ityarthaḥ | matāntaramāha—anya iti | bukhyabalatvameva pūrvāktayā yuktyā nāsti, tathāpi pratyakṣādyāgnāyayorvirodhe dvaitādvaitayoḥ saṃśayaḥ syāt, na nirṇayaḥ | tena taccanirṇayāyāvirodha upapādyata ityarthaḥ | nanu ṣoḍaśigrahaṇādivat vikalpo bhaviṣyati, na saṃśaya ityāśaṅkyāha—vastviti ; kariṣyamāṇatvādanuṣṭhānaṃ vikalpyate, na tu siddhaṃ vastvityarthaḥ |
[p. 110]
P. 40-3. evaṃ matadvayena virodhaparihāraprayojanamuktvā svamatamāha—anyāditi | āmnāyabalavacce nyāyamupanyasyati—paurveti | “adhvaryu niṣkramantaṃ prastonā saṃtanuyāt, tamudgātā, udgātāraṃ pratihartā, taṃ brahmā, brahmāṇaṃ yajamānaḥ” iti somayāge anyonyahastanigraheṇa saṃnatānāmṛtvijāṃ yajñaśālāto niṣkramaṇaṃ śrutam | tatra ca naimittikaṃ śrūyate—“yadi prastotāpacchidyeta brahmaṇe varaṃ datvā sa eva pravartitavyaḥ; yadyudgātāpacchidyetādakṣiṇo yajñaḥ saṃsthāpyaḥ; yadi pratihartāpacchidyeta sarvavedasaṃ deyam” iti; apacchidyeteti viśchiṣyetetyarthaḥ | tata udgātṛpratihartvoḥ paurvāparyeṇāpacchedadvaye sati atulyabalatvādadakṣiṇasarvasvadakṣiṇayośca virodhādvikalpasamuccayayorasaṃbhavāt kiṃ pūrvaṃ balavat? athottaram? iti saṃśaye mukhyatvāt śrutiliṅgādivat pūrvabalīyastve prāpte pratyāha—“paurvāparye pūrvadaurbalyaṃ prakṛtibat” iti | śrutiliṅgādiṣu pūrvaṃ śīghramāvitvāt prāptaṃ sat atrāptameva paraṃ bādhate; tena tatra paurvāparyameva nāsti, parasyānutpatteḥ | iha tu paramapi pratyakṣaśrutiprāptam; ataḥ paurvāparye naimittikadvayasya pūrvasya daurbalyam, pūrvamanupamṛdya parasyānutpatteḥ; atastadeva bādhyate, prakṛtivrat; yathā prākṛtāḥ kuśā vikṛtāvatideśaprāptāḥ pratyakṣaśrutaiḥ śarairapodyante tadvadityarthaḥ | etadeva bhaṭṭavākyāt draḍhayati—pūrvābādheneti | nanu paurvāparye bhavatu pūrvadaurbalyam; iha tu tadeva nāsti, pratyakṣāmnāyayoranidaṃ prāthamyādityāśaṅkyāha—tathā hīti | sarvasya jantoḥ pratyakṣādinibandhano vibhaktasya bhinnasya vastunaḥ paricchedo nisargajo janmamātrasiddha iti bhedapratyayasya pūrvatvamuktam; nāyaṃ vibhaktavastutattvaparicchedastattvataḥ, kiṃtu māyāmātramiti dyotanārthaḥ kilaśābdaḥ | advaitāvagamastu vibhaktapadapadārthavastupratyakṣaparicchedāpekṣaḥ, ata eva tatpūrvakaḥ, ata evānisargajo'janmamātrasiddhaḥ, ata evāganturuttarakālabhāvī kasyacideva janakāderbhavati na sarvajantoriti advaitāvagamasya paratvamuktam | evaṃ dvaitādvaitajñānayoḥ paurvāparyamuktvā pūrvasya bādhyatvamāha—sa iti | āmnāyabalavacce hetvantaramāha—itaśceti; āmnāyo balavānityanuvartate | kuta ityāha—saṃbhavaditi; saṃbhavanto vicitrā nānābhūtāstibhirādayo vibhramahetavo yeṣu te tathoktāḥ | vigalitā-
[p. 111]
vigatā akhilā doṣāśaṅkā yataḥ sa tathoktaḥ ; asaṃbhavadoṣāśaṅkasyāmnāyasya saṃbhavadoṣebhyaḥ pratyakṣādibhyo balīyastvaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | naikarūpaḥ sarvatra bhrame bhramahetuḥ, apitu vicitraḥ ; tenendriyādidoṣāsaṃbhave'pi doṣāntaraṃ prapañcajñāne kalpayiṣyāma iti sūcanārthaṃ vicitragrahaṇam | tatrāmnāyasya vigatadoṣāśaṅkatve hetumāha—puruṣeti | nirdoṣāmnāyabalādeva pratyakṣādiṣu P. 40-8. doṣakalpaneti paścāduktamapyāmnāyanirdoṣatvaṃ pūrvamupapāditam | puruṣāśrayo hi śabde tarvo doṣaḥ ; sa tadabhāve kathamāmnāye saṃbhavedityarthaḥ | nanu pratyakṣādiṣvāpi doṣā na dṛṣṭāḥ ; yeṣu dṛṣṭāḥ na tāni pratyakṣādīni, kiṃtu tadabhāsāḥ ; na cānyatra dṛṣṭānāmanyatra pramāṇaṃ vinā kalpanocitā, atiprasaṅgāt ; atasteṣvanupalabhyamānavibhramahetudoṣakalpanāyāṃ kiṃ pramāṇamityāśaṅkya pramāṇamāha—śakyo hīti | ita eva nirdoṣatvasiddhaprāmāṇyādadvaitāmnāyāt dvaitagrāhiṣu pratyakṣādiṣu doṣasaṃbhāvanāniścayau śakyāvityarthaḥ | nanu timirādayo loke doṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ ; te ca pratyakṣādiṣu yogyānupalabdhivāritāḥ kathamāmnāyādapi kalpayituṃ śakyā ityāśaṅkyoktam— anādiriti | uktamidam—na hi sarvatraikarūpa eva doṣo bhavatīti ; tena timirādyasaṃbhave'pi avidyāsaṃskārākhyaṃ doṣāntaraṃ kalpayiṣyāma iti bhāvaḥ | avidyāsaṃskārākhyadoṣotpatteḥ prāk nirdoṣasya pratyakṣāderāmnāyena saha tulyabalatvaṃ prāptamityāśaṅkya viśinaṣṭi—anādiriti | dārḍhyamūcanārthaṃ vā— dvaitadarśanam, avidyāsaṃskāraḥ, tataḥ punardvaitadarśanamityevamanādināvidyāsaṃskārākhyadoṣeṇānubaddhatvāt nityamāmnāyāt pratyakṣāderdurbalatvamiti bhāvaḥ | avidyaiva vā saṃskāraḥ ; saṃskṛyate tathā ātmā atiśayamivāpadyata iti kṛtvā avidyāvāsanā vā avidyāsaṃskāraḥ | niścaya evātrābhimataḥ ; saṃbhāvanā tu doṣasaṃbhāvanāmātreṇāpi yāvatpratyakṣādīnyasaṃbhāvitadoṣādāmnāyāt durbalāni, kimuta niścitadoṣāṇīti prauḍhavādenoktā | saṃbhāvitasya ca sādhyatvāt saṃbhāvanākrameṇa niścaya evoktaḥ | taduktam— “saṃbhāvinaḥ pratijñāyāṃ pakṣaḥ sādhyeta hetunā” iti | ka punarāmnāyāt timiravyatiriktasyānāderavidyāsaṃskārasya vibhramahetutvaṃ prasiddham, yenātra
[p. 112]
P. 40-10. kalpita ityāśaṅkyāha—deheti | dehe ‘aham’ ityātmajñānaṃ dehātmābhimānaḥ ; tatra yathā dehātiriktātmāvabodhakādāmrāyādanādiravidyāsaṃskāra eva janmani janmani dehātmavibhramahetustathehāpītyarthaḥ | nanu ‘mama śarīram’ iti vyatirekapratyayāt dehātmābhimāne vibhramahetuḥ kṛṣṭaḥ, nāgnāyāt | maivam ; ‘mamātmā’ ityavyatireke'pi vyatirekapratyayadarśanāt nāyamaikāntiko hetuḥ | ata āgnāyādeva dehātmābhimāne'nādiravidyāsaṃskāro vibhramahetuḥ kṛṣṭa iti sūktam | avidyāsaṃskārabhramahetutvamātradṛṣṭānto'yam | etāvatā ca kathaṃ sarvajanīnaḥ prapañcapratyayo bhrama ityapi nirastam ; sarvajanīno'pi hi dehātmapratyayo vibhramo dṛṣṭa iti | kimiti punarāgnāyāt pratyakṣādiṣu vibhramaheturdoṣaḥ kalpyate, na tu pratyakṣādibalenāgnāya evetyāśaṅkyāha—na tvevamiti | na hi svabhāvataḥ śabdo duṣṭaḥ, kiṃtu puruṣadoṣeṇa ; tenāgnāye nirāśrayasya doṣasya śaṅkāpi kartumaśakyā, dūratastu niścaya ityarthaḥ | śaṅkāyāṃ tu doṣamāha—satyāmiti | satyāṃ vā doṣaśaṅkāyāmāgnāyaḥ pramāṇameva na syādityarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ pratyakṣādayo'pi sadoṣāḥ kathaṃ pramāṇam ? asti ca teṣu loke ‘pramāṇam’ iti prasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha—pratyakṣādīnāṃ tviti | tuśabdo viśeṣapradarśanārthaḥ | idamuktaṃ bhavati—dvividhaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ tattvāvedanalakṣaṇaṃ vyavahārāvisaṃvādalakṣaṇaṃ ca | tatra doṣavacce pratyakṣādīnāmapi tattvāvedanalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ satyaṃ nāstyeva ; vyāvahārikaṃ tvasti ; ato na prasiddhihāniriti | nanu doṣavaccena rajatādibhramavat vyāvahārikamapi prāmāṇyaṃ kathamityāśaṅkyāha—avidyeti | doṣanivṛttau hi vyavahārasaṃvādo bhavati | ayaṃ tvavidyādisaṃskāro'nāditvāt sthiraḥ pūrvavat vyavahāre'pyanuvartate | tena vyavahāraviparyayābhāvāt pratyakṣādīnāṃ vyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | nanu doṣavacce'pi cet vyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyam, aprāmāṇyaṃ tarhi ka bhaviṣyati ? asti ca rajatādibhrameṣu tatprasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha—yatra ceti | tena pratyakṣādeḥ rajatabhramasya ca doṣavaccāviśeṣe'pi laukikapramāṇāpramāṇaviveko ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ | nanu doṣavatāmapi cet pratyakṣādīnāṃ prāmāṇyam, tarhi śabde'pi doṣavati tathaiva bhaviṣyati ;
[p. 113]
ataḥ 'satyāṃ vā pramāṇameva na syāt' iti yaduktaṃ tadayuktamityāśaṅkya tadupapādayati—śabde tviti | tuśabdaḥ pratyakṣādibhyo viśeṣamāha | P. 40-13. saṃbhāvitadoṣe'pi yāvat śabde nobhayavidhaṃ prāmāṇyam, kimuta niścitadoṣe iti saṃbhāvitaṃ grahaṇam | nanu doṣavati śabde taccāvedanalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ mā bhavatu, vyāvahārikaṃ tu pratyakṣādivat kimiti na bhavatītyāśaṅkyāha— adṛṣṭārthatvāditi | adṛṣṭo'rtho viṣayo yasyeti vigrahaḥ | pratyakṣādiṣu vyavahāraviparyayādarśanāt vyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ yuktam; āmnāyasya tvadṛṣṭārthatvāt na tadyavahārāviparyayajñānamasti; ato na tasya vyāvahārikamapi prāmāṇyamityarthaḥ | itaśca sadoṣasya śabdasya na vyavahārāvisaṃvādalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyamityāha—doṣebhya iti | jñānakāraṇadoṣebhyo vyavahāravisaṃvādi jñānamanyatra rajatabhramādiṣu dṛṣṭam; ataśca śabde'pi cet doṣāḥ syuḥ, te'pi vyavahāravisaṃvādijñānahetavaḥ doṣatvāt timirādivaditi sāmānyato dṛṣṭena vyavahāravisaṃvādijñānahetavaḥ kalpyeran; tataśca kuto vyavahārāvisaṃvādalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyamityarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ tarhi pratyakṣādidoṣā api dharmiṇo vyavahāravisaṃvādijñānahetavaḥ doṣatvāt indriyādidoṣavaditi te'pi vyavahāravisaṃvādijñānahetavaḥ kalpyeran, tataśca pratyakṣādīnāmapi doṣavacce vyavahārāvisaṃvādalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na syāt, virodhādityāśaṅkyāha—pratyakṣādīnāṃ tviti | tuśabdaḥ śabdādviśeṣamāha; pratyakṣādīnāṃ tu vyavahāre saṃvādo dṛśyate; ataḥ pratyakṣāvirodhāt nāsmādanumānāt teṣu vyavahāravisaṃvādijñānahetudoṣaḥ śakyate kalpayitum | evaṃ hi sa kalpyeta yadi teṣu vyavahāre visaṃvādaḥ syāt, sa tu nāstītyuktam | ataḥ satyapi doṣe pratyakṣādīnāṃ vyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na virudhyate iti bhāvaḥ | yadi pratyakṣādiṣu vyavahārāsaṃvādahetudoṣā na kalpyate, kīdṛśastarhi, kutaśca sa tādṛśaḥ kalpyate— ityapekṣāyām, yādṛśo'sau yataḥ sa tādṛśaḥ kalpyate tadubhayamāha—taccadarśanasya tviti | “ekamevādvitīyam” • ityādivedāntājjāgamānasyādvaitatattvajñānasya pratyakṣādiprameyadvaitapratipakṣatvāt tadvādhakatvāt pratyakṣādiprameyadvaitasya yat tacca pāramārthikaṃ tatpratighātamātraheturevānādiravidyānubandhaḥ kalpyate, na 8
[p. 114]
vyavahārapratighātahetustimirādirityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—na hi pratyakṣādiṣu pramāṇāntaragamyo doṣaḥ ; kiṃ svāmnāyabalāt kalpyaḥ | atastadanusāreṇaiva sa kalpanāmarhati | āmnāyaśrādvaitaṃ darśayan pratyakṣādiprameyadvaitamātrapratipakṣo na vyavahāraṃ raṣṭaśati | tena tadanusāreṇa doṣaḥ kalpyamāno'nṛtadvaitajñāna-jananasamartho'vidyāsaṃskāra eva kalpyate, na vyavahāravisaṃvādikṣānajanana-samarthastimirādiriti | ato doṣavacche'pi pratyakṣādiṣu vyavahāravisaṃvāda-P.40-18 lakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ dehātmābhimānavat na virudhyate ityupasaṃharati—tata iti | śabdasya ca sadoṣasya prāmāṇyaṃ na syāditi yaduktaṃ tadapyupasaṃharati—śabdastviti | tatraiva pūrvoktāṃ yuktimapi smārayati—dṛśyate hīti | tacca-pratighātasya cetyatra 'śaṅkitatvāt' ityanuṣajyate | idānīṃ pūrvaprakṛtaṃ pratyakṣādi-virodhe śabdasya balīyastvamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | yo hi pratyakṣādivirodhe satyāmnāyasyāprāmāṇyamāha sa niyatamavirodhe satyadoṣatvena tasya prāmāṇya-mabhyupagacchati; tathā sati nirdoṣasyāmnāyasya prāguktayuktikalpitadoṣebhyaḥ pratyakṣādibhyo balavaccaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | idānīṃ pratyakṣādisāpekṣatvādāmnāyastebhyo durbala iti yaduktaṃ tadanu-bhāṣate—yacciti | tatra dūṣaṇamāha—tatreti | satyaṃ śabdasya pratyakṣādi-viṣayāpekṣāsti ; na tu sā pramitau svārthaparicchede, kiṃtu svarūpasiddhāvi-tyarthaḥ | etadeva draḍhayati—anyatheti | yadi tu pramitau śabdasya pratyakṣā-dyapekṣā syāt tadā anapekṣālakṣaṇatvāt prāmāṇyasya śabdaḥ pramāṇameva na syādityarthaḥ | yadvā yadi svarūpasiddhau śabdaḥ pratyakṣādi nāpekṣate, tato'-navabuddhaḥ pramāmakurvan pramāṇameva na syādityarthaḥ | yadi tāma pramitāvana-pekṣaḥ śabdaḥ, tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | kārye pramitilakṣaṇe śabdasya pratyakṣādyanapekṣatvāt na śabdaḥ pratyakṣādibhyo hīyate, prāmāṇyāviśeṣādi-tyarthaḥ | nanu kārye'napekṣo'pi śabdaḥ svarūpasiddhau pratyakṣādyapekṣate, na tu pratyakṣādi śabdam ; ataḥ kimiti tebhyo na hīyata ityāśaṅkyāha—pratyakṣādayo'pīti | svarūpasiddhau sāpekṣatvaṃ pratyakṣādiṣvapīndriyādyapekṣatvā-dasti ; na ca tadaprāmāṇyamāvahati ; yataḥ kārye sāpekṣatvaṃ sāmarthyamapakṛṣṭaṃ
[p. 115]
karoti ; apakṛṣṭasāmarthyaṃ cotkṛṣṭasāmarthyena bādhitamapramāṇībhavati | tena na sāpekṣatvamātramaprāmāṇyahetuḥ, api tu kārye sāpekṣatvam ; tacca śabdasyāpi nāsti ; ato na śabdasyāprāmāṇyamityabhiprāyaḥ | idānīṃ pratyakṣādyadhīnatvādānnāyastarūpasiddheravaśyamānnāyastarūpasiddhaye teṣāṃ prāmāṇyamanumantavyam, ataścānnāyavirodhe teṣāmeva balavatvamucitaniti tadanubhāṣate—yatviti | evamanubhāṣya dūṣayati—ko ceti | satyameṣāṃ P. 41-1. prāmāṇyamanumantavyam, kiṃtu dehātmajñānavat vyavahārāvisaṃvādalakṣaṇam, na tattvāvedanalakṣaṇamityarthaḥ | kaḥ punarviśeṣahetuḥ ? yena vyāvahārikameva prāmāṇyamanumanyate, na tu tattvāvedanalakṣaṇamityāśaṅkya viśeṣahetumāha—vyāvahāriketi | vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyopetebhyaḥ pratyakṣebhyaḥ siddhādānnāyāditi vyavahārāvisaṃvādasyānnāyasiddhyanuguṇatvenāvirodhādityabādhyatve hetuḥ, tadvirodhāditi ca tattvadarśanaśrutyā bādhyatve | anenedamuktaṃ bhavati—pratyakṣādiṣu tattvadarśanabādhyatvādvaiṣāṃ tattvāvedanalakṣaṇaprāmāṇyamānnāyenānumanyate ; vyāvahārikaṃ tu svānuguṇatvādabādhyam ; ato'numanyata iti viśeṣaḥ | nanviyamānnāyagrāhiṇī pratyakṣādipratipattirupāyatvādānnāyajanyayā nattvapratipattyāpekṣitā | yadvalena yatsiddhiḥ tattato balavattaramucitam ; taduktam—“yadvalenaiva yatsiddhiḥ kathaṃ tadvalavattaram” iti | tataśca pratyakṣādipratipattibhyaḥ kathamānnāyajanyāyāstattvapratīterbalīyastvam ? kathaṃ ca tadvādhakatvam ? virodhe hi bādhyabādhakabhāvo bhavati ; na copāyopeyayoḥ so'sti, anyathopāyopeyabhāva eva na syāt ; taduktam—“na cātmahetumevāsau siddhā bādhitumarhati” ityetat vyabhicāradarśanena dūṣayati—dṛṣṭaṃ ceti | pūrvasyāḥ pratipatteriti pañcamī ; parasyā iti ṣaṣṭhī | tadudāharati—yatheti | vanaspatipratipatteriti ; balavattvamityanuṣajyate | nanu hetutvenāpekṣitāyāḥ pūrvapratipattitaḥ parapratipatterbalavattvamudāhartavyam | na ceha vanaspatipratipattyā hastipratipattayo hetutvenāpekṣitā iti pramāṇamasti | ataḥ prakṛtānanurūpamudāharaṇamityāśaṅkya prakṛtatulyatvamudāharaṇe tāvatpratijānīte—apekṣitā hīti | hastipratipattaya iti ; bahīnāṃ tāsāṃ hetutvam, naikasyā iti darśayati | yatha 8A
[p. 116]
caitattathā vakṣyāmaḥ | nanvindriyasaṃnikarṣānantarabhāvāt tajjanyaiva vanaspatibuddhiḥ, na hastibuddhijanyā ; tataḥ kathaṃ tadapekṣā setyucyata ityāśaṅkyāha— P. 41-7. na tasyā iti ; vanaspatibuddherityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—āpāta iti | yadīndriyasaṃnikarṣamātrāt vanaspatibuddherjanma syāt, āpāte prathamameva syāt ;
