na ca bhavati ; ato nendriyasaṃnikarṣamātrāt tasyā janma, kiṃtu hastibuddhirapi hetutvenāpekṣiteti bhāvaḥ | nanu mā bhavatu kevalādindriyasaṃnikarṣāt tasyā janma, deśaviśeṣasahitāttu bhaviṣyati ; tathāpi kimiti hastibuddhihetutvenāpekṣyata ityāśaṅkyāha—na ca deśeti | tasyā janmetyanuvartate | kuta ityāha—taddeśasyeti | yatra dūrasthasya prathamamabhūtaṃ vanaspatijñānaṃ paścāt samīpamupasarpato bhavati tatra syādevam ; yatra tu taddeśasthasyaiva jāyate tatra na deśaviśeṣāt tasyā janmetyarthaḥ | sa eva deśo yasya puṃsaḥ sa tathoktaḥ ; yatra deśe vanaspatijñānamāpātotpannaṃ taṃ 'tat' iti parāmṛśati | yasmāddhastibuddhibhyaḥ prāk kevalāt deśaviśeṣasahitāccendriyasaṃyogāt vanaspatibuddhirna bhavati, hastibuddhyanantaraṃ ca bhavati, tasmādanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ hastibuddhisahitādindriyasaṃyogāt sā bhavatīti niścīyata iti siddhaṃ hastibuddhīnāmupāyatvamityupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | prācyaḥ pūrvabhāvī viparyāso bhrāntijñānaṃ tatrānugatā hastinameva punaḥ punaḥ gṛhṇatī matisantatiḥ buddhimālā tajjaḥ saṃskāraḥ sa eva sacivaḥ sahakārī yasyendriyasaṃyogasya tatkāritā sā vanaspatibuddhirityarthaḥ | yadā hi bhrāntyā hastinaṃ dṛṣṭvā āśrayīdinā praṇihitamanasaḥ kasyacit tameva hastinaṃ tena tena viśeṣeṇa punaḥ punaḥ paśyato hastivilakṣaṇaviśeṣaprabodhamukhenānabhisaṃhitameva vanaspatitaccajñānamudeti tadāsti hastinaḥ punaḥ punardarśanasya matisaṃtatināmno vanaspatitaccajñāne nimitta tvam ; tathāhi—mithyāhastinaṃ sakṛt jñātvā tamupekṣya viṣayāntarasaṃcāriṇo na tatra taccajñānamudeti | nanu manaḥpraṇidhānamevātra taccajñānanimittam ; evaṃ tarhi na tattaccajñānamabhisandhāya kṛtam ; api tu hastinameva punaḥ punarjñātum | nanvanyārthamapi praṇihitaṃ mano'nyat jñāpayiṣyati, yathā ghaṭāyonmīlitaṃ cakṣuḥ paṭam ; na tarhi ghaṭapaṭajñānavat yugapadekatra hastijñānavanaspatijñāne saṃbhavataḥ, virodhāt | ato manaḥ praṇihitaṃ taccajñānaṃ kurvadapi hastijñānasaṃtatipuraḥ-
[p. 117]
sarameva karotīti siddhaṃ hastijñānasaṃtaterapi taccajñānanimittatvam | nanu hastidharmavilakṣaṇadharmajñānāt taccajñānaṃ bhavati ; atastadeva tasya nimittam | satyam ; tadeva tu tena tena viśeṣeṇa punaḥ punarhastinaṃ nipuṇataramavalokayato bhavati ; na tu sakṛt taṃ jñātvopekṣya viṣayāntarasaṃcāriṇa ityuktam | tena na vilakṣaṇadharmajñānaṃ hastijñānasaṃtateravāntaravyāpāro'pi hetutvaṃ vyāhanti, sarvakārakāṇāmahetutvaprasaṅgāt ; taduktam—“svavyāpāravyavāyo hi sarvasminneva kārake” iti | tasmāt sarvamanākulam | atraivodāharaṇāntaramāha—evamiti ; ekatvādisaṃkhyābuddhayaḥ upāyāḥ yāsāmiti vigrahaḥ | viśvādi- P. 41-10 buddhīnāṃ ca balavaccamekatvādisaṃkhyābuddhīnāmavacchedakatvamabhibhūya dravyāvacchedakatvena svavyavahārapravartakatvāt draṣṭavyam | yadyavacchedakatvaṃ nāma saṃkhyāyāḥ svarūpaṃ tadabhāve mahāsaṃkhyāyāmavāntarasaṃkhyā svarūpavirahāt mithyaiva ; taduktam—“pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ mithyā” iti | evaṃ sthite saṃprati prasaṅgāt codyāntaramapi nirasyati—evaṃ ceti | evamityanantaroktaṃ hastivanaspatyādinyāyaṃ parāmṛśati | yadeke varṇayanti tadapāstamiti vakṣyamāṇenānvayaḥ | yadvarṇayanti tadāha—svayameveti | kathamityāha—nābheda iti | kuta ityāha—bhedopāyā iti ; na hi pramātrādibhedamantareṇābhedasya pratipattirastītyarthaḥ | tatra ko doṣa ityāha—tatreti | advaitaṃ svapratipattaye pramātrādidvaitamākṣipati ; na ca dvaitādvaite saṃbhavataḥ ; ataḥ parasparaṃ vyāghāta ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ punaretadapāstamityāha—yata iti | hetumāha—mithyeti | atra ca hastijñānānyevodāharaṇāni | kathamatyantāsacce bhedasyopāyatvamiti cedata āha—vyāvahārikamiti | evaṃ pratyakṣādivirodhe śabdasya balavaccamiti sthitam | itiḥ samāptau | yadaparamuktaṃ dṛṣṭavyabhicāritvācchabdasyeti taddṛṣṭayati—vyabhicāreti | śabdasya svayamaduṣṭasyāpi duṣṭataroccāryatvameva doṣaḥ, doṣāṇāṃ kāryaviparyayakāriṇāṃ vaicitryādini bhāvaḥ | athāduṣṭahetujanyasya pratyakṣasyāvyabhicāritvamucyate, tacchabdasyāpi samānamityāha—itarayoriti |
[p. 118]
yacca pratyakṣādibalavacce'navakāśatvaṃ kāraṇamuktaṃ tadapi dūṣayati— P. 41-17 tatheti | śābdapratyakṣayoḥ samānamityanuvartate | samānatvamāha—yadīti | vedāntārthatvena prasiddho'rthastasyāvakāśo vācyaḥ, nānyo hiṃsāniṣedhavidherivānavakāśayajñiyahiṃsāvidhibādhyasya bāhyahiṃsā | sa ca mukhyo vā gauṇo vā ; na tūbhau, vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | tadyadi mukhyo vedāntārthaḥ prasiddhaḥ, tato gauṇāvivakṣitārthate na sta eva ; kathamavakāśaḥ ? ato'navakāśatvamāgnāyasyāpi samānamityarthaḥ | atha satyeva dvaye jātyādyātmanaupacārikamekatvaṃ vedāntānāmarthaḥ syāt, avivakṣitārthatā vā japopayogināmityucyate, tato dvaye sati tadviṣayaiḥ pratyakṣādibhiḥ jātyādyātmanaupacārikaikatvavācināṃ japārthānāṃ vā vedāntānāṃ virodha eva nāsti vibhinnaviṣayatvādaviṣayatvādvā ; tatra balābalacintaiva nāvatarati ; virodhe hi sā bhavati | tasyāṃ ca satyāmekasya sāvakāśatvena daurbalyaṃ tadā bhavet ; kutastadityāha—atheti | pakṣadvaye'pi dūṣaṇāntaramāha—kathamiti | buddhakakṣyo'rtho'vakāśo bhavati | na ca mukhyena gauṇasya buddhakakṣyatvam, gauṇamukhyanyāyavirodhācceti bhāvaḥ | tathā vācyamarthaṃ prati vācakatvena śabdasya śeṣabhāvaḥ aṅgatvam autpattikaṃ svābhāvikaṃ “dṛṣṭo hi tasyārthaḥ karmāvabodhanam” “aviśiṣṭastu vākyārthaḥ” iti nyāyasiddham ; tat kathamavivakṣitārthatā syādityarthaḥ | athocyate yatra pramāṇāntarāvirodhaḥ, tatra mukhyamarthamucchacchacopacaritārthatvamavivakṣitārthatvaṃ vā na syāt ; iha tu bhedagrāhipratyakṣādipramāṇāntarairadvayāgnāyasya virodhāt mukhyārthāsaṃbhave kimiti tayoranyataranna syāt ; tadetadāśaṅkayati—pramāṇāntareti | taddūṣayati—vārtamiti ; vārtāgatametat, asāraṃ vetyarthaḥ | asāratvamāha—neti | svataḥ prāmāṇyasya sthitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | mukhyārthaparatāsvabhāvatvaṃ svarasam | pramāṇāntarānapekṣaprāmāṇyasya svarasātyāge hetumāha—taditi | pramāṇāntarāpekṣatvaṃ pramāṇāntarānuguṇatayā vṛttau hetuḥ hi yat ityarthaḥ | vyatirekamāha—nirapekṣasyeti | pramāṇāntaranirapekṣasya tu pramāṇasya svārthaṃ hātumādātuṃ vā viruddhatvāviruddhatvābhyāṃ pramāṇāntarānusaraṇe ko hetuḥ ? na kaścidityarthaḥ | nanu loke ‘agnirmāṇavakaḥ’ ityādau pramāṇāntarānuguṇatayā śabdasya
[p. 119]
pravṛttidṛṣṭā ; ato'trāpi tathaiva bhavituṃ yuktamityāśaṅkya tadvaiṣamyamāha— laukikā iti | laukikāstu śabdāḥ pramāṇāntaradṛṣṭe'rthaṃ prayujyante . tena P. 42-1. te pramāṇāntarāpekṣamarthamabhidadhatīti hetoryuktā teṣāṃ pramāṇāntarānusāriṇī vṛttirityarthaḥ | vyatirekamāha—tulye tviti | pratyakṣāmnāyayoḥ pramāṇāntarānapekṣatve tulye tadviparyayaḥ kiṃ na bhavati ? pratyakṣādīnyevānnāyānuguṇatayā kimiti na nīyanta ityarthaḥ | viruddhāvyabhicārihetudvayavadanyonyavyāghātādaprāmāṇyameva cobhayorapi kimiti na syāt ? “paurvāparye pūrvadaurbalyam” iti nyāyena rajatajñānasyeva śuktijñānena pūrvasya pratyakṣāderāmnāyena bādhaḥ kimiti na syādityarthaḥ | antya evātra pakṣaḥ siddhānto'bhipretaḥ ; pūrvotu sarvathā pratyakṣādyanuguṇatayāmnāyasya vṛttirna prāpnotītyevaṃparau prauḍhivāditayoktāviti mantavyam | dṛṣṭāntameva draḍhayati—na tvalviti | tvallaśabdaḥ prasiddhidyotanārthaḥ | prasiddhameva tadrajatajñāne yatpūrvavirodhāduttaramaviṣayamanyaviṣayaṃ vā na kalpyate, kiṃtu, pūrvasyaiva bādheti, tathehāpi bhavitumarhatīti bhāvaḥ | aviṣayamityāmnāyasyāvibakṣitārthatvapakṣasāmyārthamuktam | nanu pramāṇāntaravirodhe'pi cenmukhyamarthaṃ śabdo na jahāti, kathaṃ tarhi “āpo vai śāntāḥ” ityādiṣu guṇavādaḥ ? na hyatra pramāṇāntaravirodhādanyat guṇavāde nibandhanamasti ; tadetadāśaṅkyati—kathamiti | siddhāntī tvāha—yatreti | yadvākyaṃ yāvatpramāṇaṃ tasya tāvato yatra śrautairabhidheyabhūtairmukhyairarthairanyonyamananvayāt artho vākyārthābhūto viṣayo na niṣpadyate, tatra dvitīyasyā api vṛtterloke śabdānāṃ vṛttidarśanāt tayā śabdasya pramāṇasya viṣayalābha āśrīyate ; yathā “vetasaśākhayāvakābhiśrāgniṃ vikarṣati” iti vetasaśākhāvake vidhīyete ; “āpo vai śāntāḥ” iti cāpaḥ stūyante ; tatra vidhistutyorminnaviṣayatvena parasparamananvayāt vākyārthāsiddhau, asatyapi pramāṇāntaravirodhe śrutāmapāṃ stutimullaṅghyābhijanastutyā abhijātaḥ stuto bhavatīti vetasaśākhāvakāstutiparatvamevāśrīyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati—na vede pramāṇāntaravirodho gauṇatvahetuḥ, kiṃtu mukhyārthatve pramāṇaviṣayasiddhāvanupapattiḥ | nanu vidhistutyorekaviṣayatvamanyatra pratītam ; atastayorminnaviṣayatve'styevātrāpi pratītivirodhaḥ |
[p. 120]
satyam; vākyārthāniṣpattau tu sa hetuḥ, nāpāṃ śrutaśāntatāparatvotsarge; tadutsargastu vākyārthāniṣpattihetuka eva | nanu “ādityo yūpaḥ” ityādau pramāṇāntaravirodhādeva gauṇatvam; virudhyate hi pramāṇāntareṇānupalabdhyā yūpasyādityatā | na hi tatrāpi pramāṇāntaravirodho gauṇatāyāṃ hetuḥ, api tu pramāṇārthāniṣpattireva | sā hi veta-saśākhādau dṛṣṭaśaktiravyabhicāriṇī ca | yūpasyādityatāyāḥ paśuniyojana-vākyārthe'nanvayāt pramāṇārthāniṣpattirastyeva | vyabhicārī tu pramāṇāntara-virodhaḥ, vetasaśākhādāvasatyapi tasmin gauṇatāśrayaṇāt; avyabhicāri ca kāraṇamucitam | tasmādyūpādāvapi pramāṇārthāniṣpattireva gauṇatānimittamiti sthitam | dvitīyasyā dṛtteriti pañcasyau | vedānte tu tathā nāstīti na gauṇatvamityabhiprāyeṇa vyatirekamāha—yatra tviti | apāvartante; svarasā-trivartanta ityarthaḥ | atraiva prāguktāṃ yuktiṃ smārayati—na hīti | atraiva matāntaramāśaṅkayati—atheti | evamiti; āmnāyasya gauṇatva-mityarthaḥ | ubhayānugrahaṃ vivṛṇoti—evaṃ hīti | āmnāyasya gauṇatve jātyādyātmanā bhāvānāmabhedaḥ svataśca bheda iti bhinnaviṣayatvenāvirodhādu-bhāvapyanugṛhītau syātām; nāmnāyena pratyakṣāderatyantabādhetyarthaḥ | vyatireka-māha—anyatheti | yadi punarāmnāyo mukhyārthaḥ syāt tadānīmadvaitasya satyatvāt dvaitaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣādyatyantamapabādhitaṃ syāt, nobhayānugrahaḥ; na cobhayānugrahe satyekasyātyantabādhā yukteti bhāvaḥ | tadetat dūṣayati—naita-diti | kuta ityāha—pramāṇeti; ayamarthaḥ—pramāṇasāmarthyasyānusāreṇa prameye kācit kalpanā bhavati; yathā—brīhiyavaniyamātridhidvayasāmarthyānu-sāreṇa brīhiyavādau prameye vikalpakalpanā; yathā ca—“darśapūrṇamāsā-bhyām” iti dvivacanasāmarthyānusāreṇa samuccayakalpanā; yathā ca—uditā-nuditahaumādau śāstrāntarasāmarthyānusāreṇa caraṇabhedena vyavasthākalpanā | pramāṇasvarūpe tu svarasataḥ svabhāvenautpattikyā śaktyā yā mukhyārtha-siddhistāṃ parityajya nobhayānugrahāya gauṇatvakalpanāyāṃ kiñcitkāraṇamastīti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | prameya ityanuvartate | paroktaṃ hetuṃ nirāha— P. 42-5
[p. 121]
neti | itara āha—te eveti | ye eva pratyakṣādyāmnāyākhye P. 42-13. parasparaviruddhe pramāṇe te evobhayānugrahe pramāṇam, nāparaṃ pramāṇaṃ tadarthaṃ prārthayata ityarthaḥ | etadeva vivṛṇoti—yadīti | kuta ityāha—pramāṇatvāditi ; tasyāmnāyādeḥ svīyaṃ yatpramāṇatvaṃ tata eva, na pramāṇāntarabalāt, tenāmnāyādinā dvaitaṃ pratyakṣāderarthaḥ gauṇamadvaitamāmnāyasyārthaṃ ityevamartho vyavasthāpyate yata ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—evamavyavasthāpyamāne pratyakṣādyāmnāyau pramāṇameva na syātām, viruddhārthatvāt; pramāṇaṃ ca tau | ataḥ svataḥprāmāṇyabalenaiva viṣayavyavasthā ; ubhayānugrāhyatve te eva pratyakṣāmnāyau pramāṇe pramāṇamiti | ‘tayoḥ’ ‘tābhyām’ iti vaktavye sāmānyena nyāyotpādanārthaṃ ‘tasya’ ‘tena’ iti ca sāmānyābhiprāyeṇaikavacanaṃ kṛtam | siddhāntī tvāha—yatheti | pramāṇatvena cet tadarthaṃ vyavasthāpayati, tato yathābhūtasyārthasya tato'vagamyamānasya tatpramāṇaṃ tathābhūta eva vyavasthāpyaḥ ; evaṃ tadanugṛhītaṃ bhavati ; anyathā tathābhūtārthatyāge tu arthaviparyayāt bādhitameva tatsyāt | evaṃ cādvayāmnāyasyādvayarūpārthavyavasthāpane sati pratyakṣādigocarasya dvayasyāsaṃbhavāt kuta ubhayānugrahasaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | yathāpratīyamānādanyathā vyavasthāpane doṣāntaramāha—apramāṇikā ceti | kuta ityata āha—tata iti | apratīyamāno hyartho vyavasthāpyamāno na tataḥ śabdāt svarasena pratīyate ; nāpyanyataḥ, pramāṇāntarasyābhāvāt ; ato'pramāṇikaiva sārthavyavasthetyarthaḥ | atraiva doṣamāha—itaretareti | tameva spaṣṭayati—prāmāṇyāditi | svarasasiddhārthāśrayaṇe tu naiṣa doṣa ityāha—svaraseti | yadā hyapratīyamāno'rthaḥ kalpyate, tadā prāmāṇyabalena tatpratītiḥ kalpyā ; tatpratītyā ca tajjanakatvena prāmāṇyamiti itaretarāśrayaḥ syāt | svarasasiddhe tu mukhye'rthe gṛhīte svarasasiddhayā tatpratītyā prāmāṇyam, prāmāṇyāt tatpratītiḥ, tasyāḥ svarasata eva siddhatvādityarthaḥ | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi ; yasmādvirodhe nobhayānugraho yuktaḥ, tasmātpūrvasya pratyakṣāderāmnāyena bādha eva prāmāṇikaḥ, tathā pratīteḥ pūrvopamardena pratīteḥ, śuktirajatādau vā tathā pratīteḥ, nocarasyāmnāyasyopa-
[p. 122]
caritaviṣayāntarakalpanā pramāṇānusāriṇītyarthaḥ | atraiva dṛṣṭāntamāha— P. 42-22 na khalviti | yadapi pratyakṣādīnāmanavakāśatvamuktam, tadapyasiddhamityāha—na ceti | kathamanavakāśatvaṃ nāstītyāha—yā khalviti | ananyagatitvenānavakāśatvaṃ bhavati ; asti ca pratyakṣādīnāṃ rajatādivibhramavat bhrāntitvaṃ gatiḥ ; ato nānavakāśatvamityarthaḥ | vicchinnamapi niravakāśatvaṃ rajatādivibhramaprasaṅgena smṛtvedānīṃ nirastamityadoṣaḥ | rajatādivibhramabuddhyagatitvameva teṣāmāha—yadīti | teṣāmiti ; rajatādivibhramāṇāmityarthaḥ | māṇavakaḥ svarūpeṇa sanneva guṇayogamātreṇāgniśabdasyopacarito viṣayaḥ ; rajatādi svarūpeṇaivopacaritamityavāntaraviśeṣamātrapradarśanārthamuktam—kalpita iti ; kalpito buddhyāropita ityarthaḥ | pratyakṣādīnāmapi tathāvidha eva viṣayo bhavatīti vākyaśeṣaḥ | atha tasya rajatāderasaccādaviṣayā rajatādivibhramāḥ, tataḥ pratyakṣādayo'pi tathaiva bhaviṣyantītyāha—atheti | āgnāyasyopacaritārthatvamavivakṣitatvenāvidyamānārthatvaṃ vā gatiruktā ; sā pratyakṣāderapi buddhyeti darśayituṃ rajatādivibhramavat pratyakṣāderupacaritārthatvamavidyamānārthatvaṃ coktamiti mantavyam | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | yathāgnāyasyopacaritārthatvamavivakṣitārthatvaṃ vā gatiriti sāvakāśatvam, tathā rajatādivibhramavat pratyakṣādīnāmapīti na tairanavakāśatvenāgnāyabādheti bhāvaḥ | ubhayoriti ; pratyakṣāgnāyayorityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati matāntaramapi nirākṛtamityaha— eteceti | matāntaramupanyasyati—yadāhuriti | tathābhāvaḥ ; tathātvamupacaritārthatvamavivakṣitārthatvaṃ vetyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭānusāriṇī kalpanā yukteti parābhiprāyaḥ | kathaṃ tannirākṛtamityāha—pratyakṣādiṣviti | sāvakāśatvavat duṣṭatvamapi pratyakṣāgnāyayoraviśiṣṭam | ato na tato'pyāgnāyasyaivopacaritāvivakṣitārthatve iti bhāvaḥ | pratyakṣābhāse tadarśanamiti cet ; satyam ; mānāntaravirodhādeva tu tadābhāsatvaṃ tatra kalpitam, na tu pūrvam ; tathā śabde'pyamukhyārthatvamavivakṣitārthatvaṃ veti tulyatā |
[p. 123]
idānīṃ pūrvoktaṃ pakṣāntaraṃ dūṣayitumanukīrtayati—yacciti mukhyatvāt P. 48-5 prathamabhāvitvādanupajātavirodhitvena pratiṣṭhitaviṣayaiḥ pratyakṣādibhirbādhitaviṣaya āgnāyaḥ; ato na pramāṇamityarthaḥ; pratiṣṭhito niścalībhūto'rtho yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ | tat dūṣayati—ata iti; yat eva pratyakṣādīni pūrvabhāvīni, ata evāgnāyasteṣāṃ bādhaka ityarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—śuktijñānavaditi; yathā śuktijñānaṃ pūrvabhāvino rajatajñānasya bādhakaṃ tadvadityarthaḥ | nanu ca mukhyatvāt pratyakṣādyanusāreṇa tadavirodhyāgnāyaḥ; tasmādāgnāyasyārtho na kalpyata ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | atrāpi śuktijñānavadeveti dṛṣṭāntaḥ | hetumāha—anapekṣatvāditi | anapekṣatvameva vivṛṇoti—neti | pratyakṣādyadhīnaprāmāṇyaśredāgnāyaḥ syāt, tataśca prāmāṇyāya pratyakṣādyapekṣeta; na tvetadasti, apauruṣeyatayā tasya svataḥ prāmāṇyāt; ato na tadanusāreṇārthakalpanāmarhatītyarthaḥ | yadi ca pratyakṣādibhirāgnāyasya saṃbhūyaikapramitikāryakāritā syāt, tato'pi kāryaṃ prati sāpekṣatā syāt; tataśca viruddhārthayoḥ saṃbhūyaikakāryakāritāsaṃbhavāt pūrvānurodhenottaraṃ nīyeta; na tvetadastītyāha—na tairiti | yadi vā aśvapratigraheṣṭyādivākyagatapadavadanayorekavākyatā syāt tato'pyevaṃ syāt, na tvetadapyastītyāha—nāgnāyasyeti | ubhayatrāpi hetumāha—paraspareti | yadvā 'nāgnāyasya' ityasaṃbhūyakāritve hetuḥ; ekavākyatvamekatātparyatvam; tasmin sati saṃbhūyakāritvaṃ bhavati | na cāgnāyasya pratyakṣādeśca tadasti, ubhayatra parasparanirapekṣabuddhyutpādāditi grantho yojyaḥ | kimevaṃ sati nyāyyamityata āha—tatreti | tatra tasmin parasparānapekṣabuddhyutpāde sati parabalīyastvaṃ nyāyyam, rajataśuktijñānādau tathā dṛṣṭatvāt; na tu pūrvānusāreṇottarasyārthakalpanetyarthaḥ | etadabhiyuktavacanena draḍhayati—yathoktamiti | ka tarhi mukhyabalīyastvamityata āha— yatna tviti | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ; yaṃtra tu vākyaviśeṣe bahūnāṃ padārthānāmekavākyatvena saṃbhūyaikavākyārthajñānakāritvam, tatra pūrvaṃ virodhinaḥ padasyānuccaritatvādanupajātavirodhitayā mukhye prathame pade svarastavṛttyā mukhyayā dṛśyā sthite sati parāṇyuttarāṇi padāni pūrvapadāpekṣitārthasamarpaṇānupapatteḥ
[p. 124]
prathamayā mukhyayā dṛcyā pūrvapadaikavākyatāṃ pratipattumaśaknuvanti dvitīyayā P. 43-15 gauṇyā vṛcyā pūrvapadāpekṣitamarthaṃ samarpayanti ekavākyatāsāmarthyāt ekavākyatābalena, anyathā anyonyamananvitārthānāṃ padānāmekavākyataiva na syāt—ityanena hetunā tatra mukhyasya pūrvasya balīyastvamityarthaḥ | yathā—“prajāpatistapo'tapyata | tatastapastepānātrayo vedā ajāyanta” ityupakramya “uccai ṛcā kriyate, uccaiḥ sāmnā, upāṃśu yajuṣā” ityante śrūyate | tatra saṃśayaḥ—kimṛgādiśabdā yathālakṣitaṛgādijātivacanāḥ, kiṃ vā ṛgvedādivacanāḥ iti ; tatra mukhyatvādṛgādijātivacanā iti prāpte satyupakrame vedasaṃkīrtanāt tasmiṃścānupajātavirodhitayā mukhyārthaṃ vyavasthite tadapekṣitamarthamṛgādiśabdā mukhyayā dṛcyā samarpayitumaśaknuvanto dvitīyayā vṛcyā vedavacanā iti siddhāntitam | yatturum—karmavidhayaḥ sādhyasādhanetikartavyatādibhedaparāḥ ; vedāntāśrābhedaparāḥ ; tenānnāyaḥ svayameva parasparavyāhato durlabhaprāmāṇyaḥ ; ataḥ pratyakṣādivirodhe sutarāṃ tasyāprāmāṇyaṃ sūcitamiti, tadanubhāṣate—yacciti | evamanubhāṣya dūṣayati—tatreti | yatparaḥ śabdaḥ, tatra śabdaḥ pramāṇam, na ca karmavidhayo bhedasadbhāvapratipādanaparāḥ ; kiṃtu vyavahārasiddhaṃ bhedamupāśritya ‘idaṃ sādhyamanena sādhanenetthamanayotikartavyatayā sādhayet’ ityaṃśatrayaviśiṣṭapuruṣahitabhāvārthavidhipradhānāḥ | tena na te bhede pramāṇamiti taiḥ sahābhedapramāṇānāṃ vedāntānāṃ kuto vyākulatvamiti bhāvaḥ | atra paraśrodayati—syādetaditi | yadyapi śabdo na bhedaparaḥ kiṃtu bhāvārthavidhiparaḥ, tathāpi bhedamārthamākṣipati ; na hyasati bhede sādhyādyaṃśatrayavibhāgo'sti | na ca tasminnasati tādviśiṣṭasya bhāvārthasya vidhirupapadyata ityarthaḥ | tatpariharati—ucyata iti | satyaṃ hitānuśāsanaṃ bhedasattāmākṣipet yadyaṃśatrayamantareṇa tanna syāt ; yathā śyenādividhiṣu “na hiṃsyāt” iti pratiṣedhāt sādhyāṃśābhāve'pi bhavati, tathā sarvatra karmavidhipvaṃśatrayābhāve'pi bhaviṣyati ; ato na bhedamākṣipantīti bhāvaḥ ; nanu śyenādiṣu hiṃsā sādhyā ; na ca sā svapuṣpavat nityaṃ nāstyeva, kṛtāyāḥ sattvāt |
[p. 125]
na ca na prītikarī, na ca na kāmyate; ataḥ kathaṃ teṣu sādhyābhāva ityāśaṅkyāha—tathā hīti | yo hyanatikrāntapratiṣedhaśāstraḥ, tasya P. 43-20. nidhidattvenānarthodayā hiṃsā na prītikarī na kāmyā; na ca sādhyāṃśaḥ; atastaṃ prati sādhyāṃśābhāva ityarthaḥ | anartha udīyate udgacchatyasyā iti anarthodayā | atha tīvrakrodhākrāntacittatayā samuddhatena tamasā tirohitārthānarthavivekajñānasyāt evānarthāmapi hiṃsāmarthatvena paśyato'tikrāntapratiṣedhaśāstrasya kasyacidevaṃbhūtasya puṃso hiṃsā sādhyā bhavati; itthaṃ śyenādiṣu sādhyāṃśasaṃbhava ucyate; tathā jyotiṣṭomādividhiṣvapyanādyavidyāvatāmavidyākalpitāstrayo'pyaṃśāḥ saṃbhavantīti tānavidyāsiddhānevāśritya teṣāmavidyāvatāmarthena hitānuśāsanamupapadyata iti na tenāṃśatrayavibhāgasaccākṣepaḥ; tadetadāha—atheti | vidyāvatāṃ tadyavidyākalpitāṃśatrayasaccāt kathaṃ karmavidhaya ityāśaṅkyāha—pratibuddhāstviti | sākṣātkṛtabāhyātmatattvāstvaṃśatrayamasat ātmānaṃ vā akartāraṃ manvānāḥ karmavidhibhirnānuśiṣyante; tena tān prati kimaṃśatrayasaccenetyarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | nānuśiṣyanta ityanuvartate | krodha evārātiḥ krodhārātiḥ, sa nirjito yaiste tathoktāḥ | upasaṃharati—tadevamiti; tat tasmāt evaṃ pūrvoktena prakāreṇa vyavahārataḥ vyāvahārikapramāṇebhyaḥ pratyakṣādibhyaḥ siddho yo'śatrayabhedaḥ sa evāśrayo yeṣāṃ karmavidhīnāṃ teṣu yadyapyaṃśatrayabhedapratītirasti tathāpi teṣāṃ karmavidhīnāṃ bhedaparatvābhāvāt na tatra bhede pramāṇamityarthaḥ | nanu pratīyate cet kathaṃ na tatra prāmāṇyamityāśaṅkya na pratītimātreṇa prāmāṇyam, api tu tātparyata iti darśayituṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha—vṛttānteṣviti | vapottvananādivṛttānteṣu “prajāpatirvapāmudaksvitvadat” ityādīnāmarthavādānāṃ pratītimātreṇa yathā na prāmāṇyaṃ tathā karmavidhīnāṃ vāṃśatrayabheda ityarthaḥ | yadā cāmrāyo na bhede pramāṇaṃ na ca bhedasattāmākṣipati, tathā sati nāmnāyasya parasparavyāhatiriti na tayā tasya durbalatvamityabhiprāyeṇāha—tathā ceti | evamāmnāyaparasparavyāhatiṃ parihṛtya pratyakṣādīnāmeva durbalatvasiddhaye tāmāpādayati— api ceti | kuta ityāha—vādhyeti | kutretyāha—rajateti |
[p. 126]
aindriyakamātrajñānābhiprāyeṇātra parasparavyāhatiruktāḥ na tu rajatajñānaṃ pratyakṣam, apitu tadābhāsaḥ | saṃprati bhedābhedavādinaṃ prati pratyakṣābhipra. 44-4. matayoranyonyavyāhatāvudāharaṇāntaramāha—bhedābhedeti | sarvatra jātiguṇādiṣu bhedābhedau pratyakṣāviṣṭau; tau ca parasparaviruddhau; atastadviṣayatvena pratyakṣasyaiva vyāhatatvāt durbalatvaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | evamanumānādiṣvapyūhyam | tadevamānmāyabalavacce sthite pratyakṣādivirodhe'pyānmāya eva pramāṇamiti vyarthatvāt tadvirodhe parihāralakṣaṇaṃ nārambhaṇīyamiti prāptam; tathāpi yathārabhyate tadāha—tadevamiti | ānmāyaḥ pratyakṣādivirodhe satyupacaritārthaḥ, śabdatvāt, laukikaśabdavaditi sāmānyato dṛṣṭānumānābhāsena bhrānto yo nāma mandadhīḥ pratyakṣādivirodhe ānmāyasyopacaritārthatvaṃ manyate tatpratibodhanāya pratyakṣādivirodho nirasyata iti vakṣyamāṇenānvayaḥ; na tu vivekinaṃ prati | kathaṃ punaranumānābhāsaḥ? ucyate— laukikaṃ vacaḥ pratyakṣādyadhīnaprāmāṇyamiti tadvirodhe yuktamupacaritārthatvam | ānmāyastu nirapekṣaprāmāṇyaḥ san kimiti pratyakṣādivirodhe'pyupacarito bhavati | tena pratyakṣādyadhīnatve sati pratyakṣādivirodhaḥ śabdasyopacaritārthatve nimittam, na kevala ityanumānābhāsatvam | prakārāntaramāha— yo veti | sarveṣāmavidyāvatāṃ sarvadā bhāvādāddiśābhededaṃ niścitatvena rūḍhaṃ nirūḍhatvena nibiḍam atyantaṃ gāḍham, durucchedamiti yāvat; tadbhāvastattā tayā; 'nirūḍhinibiḍatayā' iti pāṭhe tu nirūḍhyā nibiḍamiti samāsaḥ, nirūḍhiśca niścitatvena rūḍhireva | kasya nirūḍhanibiḍatayetyapekṣāyāmāha— bhedadarśanāndhakārasyeti | bhedadarśanamevāndhakāraḥ, taccāprakāśasāmyāt; tasya nirūḍhinibiḍatayā lokavacasāṃ ca pratyakṣādibhirbādhadarśanāt pratyakṣādīnāmeva balavaccaṃ yo vā manyate tatpratibodhanāya pratyakṣādivirodho nirasyata ityarthaḥ | nanu mandadhiyā bhrāntena ca varākeṇa pratyakṣādivirodhadūṣitasyānmāyasya na kācit kṣatiḥ; ataḥ kimiti tannirāsāya prayatna ityāśaṅkyāha— na hīti | sarpāpekṣayā mandatripeṇa dṛśyikena dṛṣṭo yo na bhriyate so'pi yathā cikitsyate tathā mandadhiyā bhrāntena ca kenacit pratyakṣādivirodhadūṣito'pyānmāyāścakitsanīyaḥ; tasyāpi virodhākhyadoṣanirāsaḥ kārya
[p. 127]
ityarthaḥ | 'mriyate' iti pāṭhe tu mandadhiyoktatvāt tadukto doṣo'vicikitsya ityāśaṅkya mandaviṣeṇa dṛśyikena daṣṭo yo deśaviśeṣe mriyate sa yathā cikitsyate tathāmnāyo'pi yo mandadhiyā dūṣito mriyate bahubhirmandabuddhibhirviṣṭāvito'prāmāṇyamṛtyumaśnute so'pi cikitsanīya iti yojyam | evamavirodhalakṣaṇārambhaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya 'āhurvidhātṛ' iti ślokaṃ vyācaṣṭe—tatreti | tāneva trīn pakṣān vidyaṇoti—vastviti | pratyakṣaṃ P. 44-10 kiṃ 'ghaṭo'yam' iti vastusvarūpamātraṃ vidhatteḥ kiṃ vā 'paṭo'yaṃ na bhavati' iti vastvantaraṃ vyavacchintiḥ atha gṛhṇāti vyavacchinti ca ? yadāpyubhayaṃ karoti tadāpi kiṃ yugapat vidhivyavacchedau karoti ? atha vidhāya vyavacchintiḥ uta tadvyavacchidya vidhatteḥ iti | tatra teṣu pakṣeṣu madhye anyavyavacchede ubhayasmin vā pratyakṣavyāpāre abhyupagamyamāne, bhedaḥ pratyakṣagocara iti hetorāmnāyasya bhavati tena virodhaḥ | vastusvarūpavidhimātre kasyacidvastvantarasya vyavacchedena rahite pratyakṣavyāpāre sati na bhedaḥ pratyakṣasya gocaraḥ | kasmātpunarvyavacchedaśūnye vidhimātre pratyakṣavyāpāre bhedaḥ pratyakṣagocaro na bhavatītyāśaṅkyāhaṃ—na hīti | naḥ hi paṭhamavyavacchindat pratyakṣaṃ ghaṭasya tato bhedaṃ gṛhṇātiḥ, ato vyavacchedaṃ vinā na bhedasya siddhiḥ prasiddhiḥ jñānamityarthaḥ | yadi nāmaiva tataḥ kimityatrāhaṃ—vidhimātreti | vastusvarūpamātragrāhakaṃ ca pratyakṣam, nānyavyavacchedakam; ato'sya bhedaviṣayatvābhāvāt nānenābhedāmnāyasya virodha ityarthaḥ | atra paraḥ pṛcchati—kathamiti | kathaṃ vidhātṛmātraṃ pratyakṣam, na vyavacchedakamityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—ucyata iti | yaducyata ityuktaṃ tadāha—labdheti | ślokaṃ vidyaṇoti—na tāvaditi | nirākariṣyamāṇavidhipūrvakavyavacchedapakṣāpekṣayā tāvacchabdaḥ | na tāvadvyavacchedamātraṃ pratyakṣavyāpāraḥ, nāpi yugapadyavacchedavidhī, nāpi vyavacchedapūrvaṃ vidhānam; yataḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhe viṣaye tatsiddhameva niṣidhyate | niṣedhasvarūpamāha—
[p. 128]
P. 44-20. nedamiha nāyamayamiti | ‘nedamiha’ iti saṃsarganiṣedhaḥ, ‘nāyamayam’ iti tādātmyaniṣedha iti bhedenopanyāsaḥ | ubhayatrāpyudāharaṇamāha—siddha iti | pramāṇāntarasiddhe bhūtale tatsiddho ghaṭo niṣidhyata iti ‘nedamiha’ ityasyodāharaṇam | gavi siddhe siddho'śvo niṣidhyata iti ‘nāyamayam’ ityasyodāharaṇam | kutaḥ punaḥ pratiṣedhe viṣayapratiṣedhyayoḥ prasiddhirapekṣyata ityapekṣāyāmāha—neti | na hi pratiṣedho nāma svatantraḥ, kiṃtu kasyāpi kutracit; ato yāvatpratiṣedhyapratiṣedhaviṣayau na jñāyete tāvat pratiṣedha eva na ghaṭate yata ityarthaḥ | atra codayati—kathaṃ tarhīti; yadi siddhe viṣaye siddhaṃ niṣidhyate, kathaṃ ‘pradhānaṃ nāsti’ ‘khapuṣpaṃ nāsti’ iti ca niṣedha ityarthaḥ | kasmātpunaratra niṣedho nāvakalpata ityata āha—na hīti | deśaviśeṣe kālaviśeṣe hi siddhe sarvo niṣedho dṛṣṭaḥ; na ceha tau staḥ; tena viṣayastāvadatra siddho nāstīti bhāvaḥ | kuta ityāha—ātyantikatvāditi; yatra kvacit sato'nyatra niṣedhaḥ, tatra deśakālaviśeṣaviṣayau bhavataḥ; ayaṃ tvātyantikaḥ ‘sarvatra nāsti’ iti niṣedhaḥ, na punaḥ ‘iha nāsti’ iti deśaviśeṣe kālaviśeṣe vā | atastāvadatra viṣayau na saṃbhavata ityarthaḥ | na kevalamatra viṣayo nāsti, pratiṣedhyo'pi siddho nāstītyāha—nāpīti | nāpi kutaścit pramāṇāt pradhānāderghaṭādivat kvacit siddhirityarthaḥ | hetumāha—atyantātsvāditi | khapuṣpasya sarvajanīnamatyantāsaccam | pradhānaṃ tu sāṃkhyānāṃ sat, na tu tairniṣedhyam; vādāntarasya tu niṣedhyam, tasya ca pratiṣedhyamasiddham | anenaiva ca bhedenodāharaṇadvayamuktam | tatraikīyamatena parihāramāha—tatreti | yadāhustadāha—kheti | pradhānapratiṣedhagataparihārāpekṣayā tāvacchabdaḥ | pramāṇāntarasiddheṣu khādiṣu tadvidhāḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhā eva puṣpādayo'tra niṣidhyanta iti nātra pramāṇāntarasiddhaviṣayapratiṣedhyāsiddhirityarthaḥ | tathā ‘pradhānaṃ nāsti’ ityatrāpi na viṣayapratiṣedhyayorasiddhirityāha—pradhānamiti | jagatastāvadutpattimakhvāt kāraṇaṃ siddham | pradhānaṃ ca saccarajjñyastamorūpam; kāryakāraṇayorabhedāt tatkāryaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohātmakaṃ sāṃkhyairiṣṭam |
[p. 129]
punaḥ 'pradhānaṃ nāsti' ityukte siddhe jagatkāraṇe anyatra siddhā sukhaduḥkhamohātmatā nivāryate | tathā kāryakāraṇayoratyantamabhedādavibhaktakāryatvaṃ satkāryatvaṃ ca mṛdvaṭādau 'mṛdayaṃ ghaṭaḥ' ityādipramāṇasiddhaṃ pradhānaniṣedhena vāryate iti nātrāpi viṣayapratiṣedhyayorasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ; avibhaktaṃ saṃbhinnaṃ kāryaṃ yasmāttat tathoktam, tasya bhāvastaccam | atraiva matāntara māha—anye tviti | sādṛśyavāsanānyāyābhāsavākyābhāsādeḥ kutaści P. 45-1. nnimittāt buddhau labdharūpāṇāṃ buddhyākārāṇāṃ satāṃ khapuṣpādīnāṃ bahirvedavabhāsamānānāṃ bahirniṣedhaḥ kriyata ityanye varṇayantītyarthaḥ | ityabhavarṇyamāne tu pratiṣedha eva na siddhyatītyāha—anyatheti | anyathā evamavarṇyamāne sarvathā aprāpte khapuṣpādau daivaniṣiddhe kaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ ? na ko'prītyarthaḥ; na hyaprāptaṃ pratiṣidhyate; prāpte ca nātyantiko niṣedhaḥ syādityarthaḥ | tadevaṃ siddhe viṣaye siddhameva niṣidhyata iti yaduktaṃ tadupapannam | siddhaṃ siddhe niṣidhyata ityatraivopapattyantaramāha—api ceti | yadi pratiṣedhe viṣayapratiṣedhyau ghaṭapaṭau nāpekṣyete tato 'nāsti' ityevamaviśeṣeṇa niṣedhaḥ syāt; na 'ghaṭe paṭo nāsti' iti | tataśca sarvatra sarvasyāviśeṣeṇa niṣedhāt sarvaśūnyatā pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ syāt; na bhedaḥ, bhedidvayābhāvā diti bhāvaḥ | nāpekṣitau viṣayaniṣedhyau yena sa tathoktaḥ | devāyatto ye niṣedhaḥ sa kīdṛśo bhavatītyapekṣāyāmāha—yastviti | 'idamato bhinnam' iti hi bhedo gṛhyate; sa ca tādṛśo bhedadvayasya pramāṇataḥ siddhimapekṣataṃ ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti | sā bhedayoḥ siddhirvidhānāt tatpramāṇena vastusvarūpagrahaṇādyate na ghaṭata iti vyavacchedāt prāgvidhāna meṣitavyamityarthaḥ | itaśca prāgvidhānameṣitavyamityāha—vidhipūrvaka iti; sarvavādibhistathaivāṅgīkaraṇādityarthaḥ | itirhetau, ślokārthaparisamāptau vā | labdharūpe pramāṇataḥ siddhasvarūpe kañcit bhūtalādau kiñcit ghaṭādi tādṛgeva pramāṇataḥ siddhasvarūpameva niṣidhyate yataḥ, ato vidhānamantareṇa niṣedhasya na saṃbhava iti ślokārthaḥ | astu tarhi vidhipūrvo vyavaccheda ityata āha—nāpīti | kuta ityata āha—yata iti | ekapratyakṣavijñānavyāpārabhūtayorvidhivyavacchedayoḥ 9
[p. 130]
paurvāparyalakṣaṇaḥ kramo yuktyā na saṃgacchate na yuktimān, jñānasya kṣaṇikatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tacca pratyakṣaṃ vijñānaṃ saṃnihitajaṃ saṃnihite viṣaye jāyate | tena tadanyāmarśi na jāyate; tataḥ saṃnihitāt anyat asaṃnihitaṃ parāmraṣṭuṃ śīlaṃ yasya tat tadanyāmarśi tathābhūtaṃ na jāyata P. 46-11 ityarthaḥ | ślokaṃ vivṛṇoti - na khalviti | kuta ityāha -- kṣaṇikatvāditi; jñānasyetyadhyāhāryam | kasmāt punaḥ kṣaṇikasya vyāpārayoḥ kramo na yujyata ityata āha -- kramavatoriti ! yataḥ kramavatorvyāpārayormadhye yaḥ paścāttano vyāpāraḥ sa tasya jñānasya vyāpārā na syādityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha -- vyavadhānāditi | paścāttano vyāpāraḥ pūrvabhāvinā vyāpāreṇa vyavahito na kṣaṇikasya syāt, tasya tadānīṃ naṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | itaśca na vidhipūrvo vyavaccheda ityāha -- api ceti | arthaparicchedarūpāyā buddherjanmaiva vyāpāro na kāṣṭhādīnāmiva janmātirekeṇa vyāpārāntaramasti | taduktam—“tena janmaiva viṣaye buddhervyāpāra iṣyate” iti | tathā “na hi tatkṣaṇamapyāste jāyate vā pramātmakam” iti ca | arthāvagraharūpāyā iti viśeṣaṇamantaḥkaraṇanivṛttyartham | tataḥ kimityāha—sā cediti | sā buddhirarthavidhānarūpodayā yadi tadā vidhirevāsyā buddhervyāpāro na vyavaccheda iti kuto vyāpāradvayasaṃbhavaḥ, yena kramaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | arthavidhānarūpa udayo janma yasyā iti vigrahaḥ | cecchabdo ‘yadi vedāḥ pramāṇam’ itivadgṛhavyaḥ | nanu kimityarthavidhānarūpodayā sā bhavati? vidhivyavacchedobhayarūpodayaiva tāvat kiṃ na syāt? ityāśaṅkyāha yaugapadyeti | nobhayarūpodayā sā, yaugapadyasyānantarameva niṣedhāt | ato vidhānarūpo dayaiva sā buddhiriti na tato vyavacchedaḥ | tasmānna kramaḥ, dvayorhi kramo bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | nanu dve tarhi buddheḥ kramavatī janmanī bhaviṣyataḥ; tatraikaṃ vidhānarūpam, aparaṃ vyavacchedarūpamityasti kramasaṃbhava ityāśaṅkyāha—utpannāyā iti | anmiśca pakṣe buddherjanmadvayena vidhivyavacchedayorekasya vijñānasya karmaṇerjanmarūpayorvyāpārayorvā kramaḥ paurvāparyaṃ yuktyā na saṃgacchate, jātasya punarjanmāsaṃbhavāditi ślokārthaṃ yojyam | ślokasyottarārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe—api ceti | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti;
[p. 131]
pratyakṣamityanuvartate | kimityanavabhāsamānaṃ vyavacchettuṃ pratyakṣamaśaktamityata āha—anavabhāsamāne hīti | tatra pratyakṣe ghaṭādau vyavacchedye'navabhāsa- P. 45-16 māne ‘atra bhūtale nāsti ghaṭādi’ iti vyavacchedyaviśeṣarāhitaṃ vyavacchedamātraṃ syāt, na tu ghaṭādervyavacchedyaviśeṣasya kasyacidyavacchedaḥ syāt; dṛśyate cāsau | atha vyavacchedyaṃ vinā vyavacchedānupapattyā vyavacchedyaṃ kalpyate, tatrodgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt bhūtale sarvasya pratiṣedhaḥ syāt; na caitadastītyarthaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | tataḥ kimityāha—na ceti | ayaṃ ca doṣaḥ pūrvāddiṣṭeṣu caturdhvapi pakṣeṣu samānaḥ | kimarthaṃ tarhīddoṣanyastaḥ? sarvārthaṃ uktaḥ sarvaiḥ saṃbhantasyata iti | atra punaśrodayati— kathamiti | yadi na niṣedhapuraḥsaro vidhirekajñāne, yadi vā asaṃnihitaniṣedhyāvabhāsaḥ pratyakṣe nāsti, tadā ‘śuktikeyam’ iti bādhakaṃ pratyakṣaṃ nopapadyata ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—tatreti | tatra bādhake pratyakṣe pūrvarajatārthaniṣedhapuraḥsaro'nyasyāḥ śuktirvidhirekajñāne'bhyupagamyate | na hyaniṣedhyarajataṃ śuktividhiravakalpate | tathā asaṃnihitasya rajatasya niṣedhyasyāvabhāsaścābhyupeyate, tadanavabhāse tanniṣedhānupapatteḥ | tasmāt na pūrvo niṣedhaḥ, na caikajñānavyāpāre kramaḥ, na cāsaṃnihitāvabhāsaḥ pratyakṣe iti yo'yamanantarokto niyamaḥ sa nāsti, vyabhicārāt; yato bādhake pratyakṣe tritayamapyetadastītyarthaḥ | tadetat siddhānto dūṣayati—naitaditi | asāratvamāha—tatrāpīti | tatrāpi bādhakapratyakṣe ‘rajatamidam’ iti pūrvajñānavihite rajatādau pratiṣedhye, tadīyenaiva ‘idam’ ityaṃśenānidhīritaviśeṣe saṃnihitārthasāmānye viṣaye vihite niṣedho vidhipūrvaka evetyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—atrāpi viṣayapratiṣedhyavidhipūrvaka eva pratiṣedha iti ‘na pūrvo niṣedhaḥ’ ityasya tāvatra vyabhicāra iti | nanu ‘nedaṃ rajatam, śuktiriyam’ iti niṣedhapūrva eva vidhiravagamyate; tatkathamucyate ‘na niṣedhapūrvako vidhiḥ’ itītyāśaṅkyāha—śuktiketi | virodhino rajatasya niṣedhaḥ pūrvo yasmāditi vigrahaḥ | tathā satyapi ca nāsmadabhimataniyamanāśa ityāha—vidhīti | ‘na pūrvo niṣedhaḥ’ ityanena viṣayaniṣedhyavidhipūrvatā niṣedhasya niyamenocyate; na ca tadvidhipūrvatvābhāvo niṣedhasya 9A
[p. 132]
nivartyate | yadyapi śuktividherniṣedhapūrvatā, tathāpi rajataniṣedhasya rajatedamaṃśasāmānyavidhipūrvatvādavyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ | etacca paramatenoktam | siddhānte tu na rajataniṣedhaśuktividhyoḥ kramo'sti, 'kramaḥ saṃgacchate yuktyā' ityanena vicāritatvāt; vakṣyati ca—'na ca tatraikajñānasya kramavadyāpāratā' iti | evaṃ vidhipūrvaka eva niṣedha ityasyāvyabhicāramuktvā adhunā naikajñānavyāpāre krama ityasya bādhakapratyakṣeṇa yo vyabhicāra uktastaṃ P. 45-25. pariharati—na ceti | tatreti bādhakajñānaṃ parāmṛśati | kuta ityāha— ubhayeti | śuktividhirajataniṣedharūpaṃ citrameva bādhakajñānaṃ jāyate; janma ca tasya vyāpāra ityuktam | na caikam, utpannasya punaranutpatteḥ; anekatra ca kramo bhavati; naikatra | tena na yugapadeva vidhiniṣedhau tatkaroti | ato naikatra jñāne kramavadyāpāranetyarthaḥ | na ca pūrvasiddhaviṣayaniṣedhyābhāvādubhayarūpotpattyasaṃbhava iti vācyam, 'idaṃ rajatam' iti pūrvajñāne tayordarśitatvādityāha—pūrvajñāneti | yathā caitattathoktam | idānīṃ pratyakṣe nāsaṃnihitāvabhāsa ityuktavyabhicārārthaṃ bādhakapratyakṣeṇāsaṃnihitāvabhāsa iti yaduktaṃ tatpariharati—pūrvajñāneti | 'idaṃ rajatam' iti jñānenāvabhāsite vyavacchedye rajatavyavacchedasya praśnettarnāsaṃnihitarajatāvabhāsaprasaṅgaḥ pratyakṣa ityarthaḥ | kathamanyaprāpitamanyo niṣedhatīti cet, yathā ṣoḍaśavidhiprāpitaṃ tanniṣedhavidhiḥ | evaṃ tarhi smṛtiprāpitapratiyogivyavacchettṛtvāt pratyakṣasyāsaṃnihitārthāvabhāsitve'pi na doṣaḥ | naivam; abhinnaṃ cet smṛtyā prāpitaṃ na pratiyogi; bhinnaṃ cet itaretarāśrayam | nanu pratyakṣaprāpite'pi pratiyoginyetadūṣaṇamastyeva | astu; kā no hāniḥ? kimarthaṃ tarhi noktam? dūṣaṇāntaravibhavāt; pratyakṣameva vā prāpakaṃ vyavacchedakaṃ ceti yo manyate taṃ pratyasaṃnihitānavabhāsakatvaṃ doṣa ukta iti | nanu jñānānāmanyonyanirapekṣāṇāmutpatteḥ kathaṃ pūrveṇāvabhāsitaṃ vyavacchedyamanantareṇa vyavacchidyata ityāśaṅkyāha—pūrvāpekṣasyeti | syādeva yadi pūrvāparayorjñānayoratrānapekṣatā syāt; saivāsiddhā; bādhakaṃ hi bādhyamapekṣyaivotpadyate, anyathā bādhakatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ | pūrvamapekṣata iti pūrvāpekṣi | ata eva tad dvirūpamutpadyate; anyathetarajñānavadekarūpamevodiyādityāha—pūrveti | dvirūpaṃ vidhi-
[p. 133]
niṣeṣarūpam | ekarūpaṃ vidhimātrarūpamityarthaḥ | kiṃca yena vādinā P 46-4. sarvavyavacchedo nirākṛtāḥ, tena rajatādivyavacchedo'pi pakṣīkṛtaḥ; na ca pakṣeṇaiva vyabhicāro yukta ityāha—api ceti | atha rajatādivyavacchedo loke siddha iti tena vyabhicāra ucyate, tato grāmādīnāmanyonyavyavacchedo yo nirākāryatvena pakṣīkṛtaḥ sa kiṃ na lokasiddhaḥ? atastenaiva vyabhicāraḥ kiṃ nocyate? sa cedvicāryamāṇo na ghaṭate, tathā rajatādivyavacchedo'pīti lokasiddhirakiṃcitkaraivetyabhiprāyeṇāha—siddhaḥ sa iti | matāntaramāha—yacciti | ekasya nīlasya vidhinīlaikasattādarśanamevānyasya pītādivyavacchedaḥ; na tu pratyakṣe vyāpāradvayamasti, yena kramo yaugapadyaṃ vā syāt | atastathorasaṃbhave'pi siddhaṃ pratyakṣasya niṣeṣṭṛtvamiti kathamucyate ‘na niṣeṣṭṛ’ iti? siddhaṃ ca niṣeṣṭṛtvena bhedaviṣayatvāt pratyakṣasyāmnāyaviroddhṛtvamiti bhāvaḥ | etadeva vivṛṇoti—tathā hīti | nīlanirvikalpakadarśanaṃ hi pītādiviviktaniyatanīlākāramutpannam; na punaḥ pītādyaviviktanīlākāram | atastadyathaiva nīlaṃ rūpaṃ vidadhāti, tathaiva nīlākāraikapratiniyamāt tato'nyat vyavacchinati | tena vidhirevānīlavyavaccheda iti bhāvaḥ | nanu ‘idaṃ nīlam’ ityanyadeva nīlajñānam; anyacca ‘nedamanīlam’ iti pratiddham; ataḥ kathaṃ nīlajñānamevānīlavyavaccheda ityāśaṅkyāha—tadini | tasya nirvikalpakadarśanasya yatsāmarthyaṃ niyataikākāratvaṃ tenānādivāsanāvaśāt pratibhāvitau janitau ‘idam, nedam’ iti vikalpau bhāvābhāvavyavahāraṃ pravartayata ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—satyaṃ jñānadvayamidam; savikalpakaṃ tu tat | yattu nirvikalpakaṃ tathormūlabhūtaṃ tatpratyakṣam | tatra ca vayamekavidhirevānyavyavaccheda iti brūma iti | kasmātpunareva vikalpyata ityata āha—na hīti | anyasaṃsargo'syāstītyanyasaṃsargi, anyasaṃsargi na bhavatītyananyasaṃsargi; etacca ‘ekākārapratiniyamāt’ ityasya vivaraṇam; tathā ca vakṣyati—‘tatpratiniyamo hi tato'nyasyāsaṃsargaḥ’ iti |
[p. 134]
P. 46-11 evaṃ bauddhamatamupanyasyātivyāptyavyāptibhyāṃ dūṣayati—atrocyata iti | ekasya vidhānamevānyasya gocaro'nyasya pratiṣedho na bhavati | kuta ityāha—mā sma bhūdaviśeṣeṇeti | yadyekavidhereva tato'nyasya pratiṣedhaḥ, tato dṛśyādṛśyayoraviśeṣeṇa pratiṣedhaḥ syāt; sa mā bhūdityarthaḥ | smeti pādapūraṇārtham | evamativyāptidoṣamuktvā avyāptidoṣamāha—mā neti | ekabuddhisevināmekabuddhigocarāṇāṃ citre nīlapītādīnāṃ miśro bhinnānāṃ pratyekamekabuddhyabhāvānmā na bhūdanyonyavaccheda ityarthaḥ | ślokārthaṃ vivṛṇoti—naiketi | kuta ityāha—tatpratiniyamo hīti | tasya nīlādenīlādibuddhāveṣa pratiniyamo yaḥ, tato nīlāderanyasya pītādestatrāsaṃsargo'nanupraveśaḥ | tathā sati cāsaṃsargāt pītādivyavacchede'bhyupagamyamāne tasyāsaṃsargasya dṛśyādṛśyayorupalabdhiyogyānupalabdhiyogyayoraviśeṣāt tayorubhayorapi vyavacchedaḥ syāt; na ca so'stīti bhāvaḥ | atredānīmiṣṭa eva dṛśyādṛśyayorvyavacchedaḥ; tena neyamaniṣṭāpattiḥ; aniṣṭāpattiśca doṣa iti manyamānastasya matamāśaṅkayati—atheti | ubhayorvyavaccheda ityanuvartate | etadupapādayati—tathā hīti | tathā hi nīladarśanaṃ nīlaikākāratayā ‘nīlātmakamevedam’ iti niyacchat yāvanto bhāvā asya nīlasyānātmāno'svarūpabhūtā dṛśyāścādṛśyāśca tān sarvānaviśeṣeṇaivāpākaroti vyavacchinnatītyarthaḥ | etadeva draḍhayati—tathā ceti | dṛśyāḥ pītādadho'dṛśyāḥ paramāṇvādayo yāvanto nīlādanye, te sarve'sya nīlasyānātmāno bhaveyuḥ, ekasyānekātmavirodhāt | tena tān sarvānevātadātmanastadātmaniyamāt darśanamapākarotīti bhāvaḥ | yadyanye'nyasyātmāno na bhaveyuḥ kīdṛśāstarhi bhaveyurityapekṣāyāmāha—taddeśeti; tasya deśakālau yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ; anye anyasya deśakālā bhavanti, na tu tadātmāna ityarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha— rūpādivaditi | yathā rūpeṇa sahaikadeśakālā rasādayaḥ, taddhadityarthaḥ | atra hetumāha—avirodhāditi | rūpasya hi rasātmakatvaṃ virudhyate, na tvekadeśakālatvamityarthaḥ | yadi dṛśyādṛśyāvaviśeṣeṇa darśanamapākaroti, kimarthaṃ
[p. 135]
tadvipalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptirnāma viśeṣo vyavacchedyaṃ pratyāśrīyata ityāśaṅkyāha— tatreti | tatra tasminnanyeṣāṃ taddeśakālatve satyupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptirnāma yo P. 46-19 viśeṣaḥ sa yasyaiva taddeśakālasyopalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptirasti tasyaiva taddeśakālasya vyavacchedo netarasyetyavadhāraṇārthaḥ, na punarātmavyavacchedāvadhāraṇārthaḥ; sa punaranupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasyāpi bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atra hetumāha—saṃbhavatīti | taddeśakālo'nātmā astvarūpabhūto'nupalambho yo bhāvaḥ sā'ta evānyopalabdhāvanupalambhamāno'pi saṃbhavati; ato na tasya vyavacchedaḥ, kintu upalabdhiyogyasyaiveti yuktaṃ tatropalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptirviśeṣasyāśrayaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—rūpeti | yathā rūpopalabdhyānupalambhastaddeśakālo rūpasthānātmā raso rūpopalabdhāvanupalambhamāno'pi saṃbhavatīti na rūpapratiniyamādrūpopalabdhyā rasavyavacchedastathetyarthaḥ | yastu tadātmā sa tena saha tulyopalambhayogyato na tadupalabdhāvanupalambhamānaḥ saṃbhavati | tenānupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāpto'pi sa vyavacchedya iti na tadarthamupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptirviśeṣa āśrayaṇīya ityabhiprāyeṇāha—na tulyeti | anupalambhamānaḥ saṃbhavatītyanuṣajyate | tulyā upalambhayogyatā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | kasmānna saṃbhavatītyata āha—niyameneti | tulyopalambhayogyataścedasti tato yena saha tulyopalambhayogyatastadupalabdhau niyamenopalambhate, itaravat upalambhamānavat; anyathā tulyopalambhayogyata eva sa na syāt, na cedupalambhate tato na saṃbhavatyeveti bhāvaḥ | tadevaṃ taddeśakālavyavacchede dṛśyatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ; tadātmavyavacchede tu dṛśyamadṛśyaṃ cāviśeṣeṇa darśanamapākarotīti bauddhena svamate pratiṣṭhāpite siddhāntavādyāha—satyamiti | taddeśakālavyavacchede dṛśyaṃ vyavacchidyate nādṛśyamiti, tathā taddeśakālavyavacchedāvadhāraṇāya dṛśyatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ na tadātmavyavaccheda ityayaṃ vibhāgaḥ satyamasti; nedānīṃ yo darśanasyaikākārapratiniyamaḥ, tasyānyavyavacchede sāmarthyaṃ syādityarthaḥ | nanu bhūtalapratiniyamasya dṛśyaghaṭādivyacchede sāmarthyamasti; ataḥ kathamucyate nedānīṃ darśanapratiniyamasya sāmarthyamitītyāśaṅkyāha—tathā hīti | rūpe rūpaviṣaye yadarśanaṃ tadyathā rūpavidhimātropakṣayāt tatraiva paryavasitaṃ yatra deśe
[p. 136]
kāle rūpaṃ tatra na rasaṃ vyavacchinatti, tathā bhūbhāgadarśanapratiniyamo'pi na dṛśyaṃ vyavacchindyādityarthaḥ | darśanapratiniyamasya cedanyavyavacchede sāmarthyamasti, rūpadarśanasyāpi syāt | atha tatra rūpavidhimātra eva pramāṇavṛtteḥ paryavasānādanyavyavacchedaparyantaṃ sāmarthyaṃ nāsti, tato bhūtale'pi | darśanapratiniyamasya tatra cātra ca na kaścidviśeṣo'stīti bhāvaḥ | atha vidhimātraparyavasāyinyapi pramāṇavyāpāre svācchandyenānyavyavaccheda icyate, P. 47-2. tato dṛśyasyādṛśyasya ca sarvasyaiva vyavacchedaḥ syādityāha—vyavaccheda iti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | darśanapratiniyamasyāsāmarthyamupapādya ‘ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavati’ ityādau tadātmavyavacchede'pi yojayannāha—tathā ceti | tatra ceddhetyantaramupāsyamihāpi tata eva vyavacchedaḥ setsyati, na darśanapratiniyamāditi bhāvaḥ | tata iti; darśanapratiniyamādityarthaḥ | yadi tvekavidhiranyaṃ vyavacchindyādapi, tataḥ svavidheyābhāvavyavacchedarūpatvāt svavidheyābhāvaṃ vyavacchindyāt; na tu ‘nīlaṃ pītaṃ na bhavati’ iti nīlātmanaḥ pītamātmāntaraṃ yattasya sattāṃ vyavacchindyādityāha—kāmamiti | vidhinīlādidarśanam; vidheyo nīlādiviṣayaḥ | yadyapi vidhividheyasattāmava-bhāsayati na tu vidheyāsattvavyavacchedam, tathāpi vidheyameva vyavacchedamantareṇa sattāṃ nāvakalpata iti nāntarīyakatvādvidheyāsattvavyavacchedarūpatvamuktam | bhāvasyābhāvābhāvarūpatvādvā vidheyameva vidheyāsattvavyavacchedo nānyo vidheyā-kārasya vidhiriti manvānena taduktamiti draṣṭavyam | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—taddeśeti | yathā nīladarśanamanupalabhyasya paramāṇvādenīlasya deśe kāle ca na sattāṃ vyavacchinatti tadvadityarthaḥ | nanu yadi darśanaṃ dṛśyāsattvaṃ vyavacchinatti, prāptaṃ tarhi dṛśyasya jagataḥ sattvam | ataḥ pratyakṣe bheda-magṛhṇatyapi bhedamātraṃ na syāt; dṛśyaṃ tu jagat pramāṇasiddhatvāt kena vāryate? ato nādvaitasiddhiḥ | ucyate—bhedābhāve jagadekaṃ tattvaṃ syāt; tadapi na dṛśyam, tasya jaḍatvena svato bhedābhāvena vānyatā'siddheḥ | ataḥ svataḥ siddha ātmā, tadekaṃ tattvamiti siddhamātmādvaitam; abhyupa-gamavādamātraṃ vā tat | na hi vidhividheyāsattvaṃ vyavacchinatti, asatyapi vidheye svaksarpādau vidherdarśanāt | vibhramaḥ sa iti cet, idaṃ nāma kim?
[p. 137]
yathā cāsya vibhramatvaṃ tathoktamāmrāyabalavattvapradarśanapradeśa ityuparamyate | athocyate -- naikasya bahava ātmānaṃḥ saṃbhavanti ; ata ekākāraniyamaṃ darśanamanekātmānaṃ vyavacchinatīti, tadāha -- naikasyeti | atrottaramāha -- P. 47-6. anyata iti | ekavidhirevānyavyavaccheda iti te pakṣaḥ ; sa virodhāt vyavacchede sati na te sidhyediti bhāvaḥ | nanu cānātmavyavacchedena naḥ kāryam ; sa cedvirodhāṅgavati, bhavatu ; tāvatā bhedaḥ sidhyatyeva ; kiṃ no darśananiyamavyavacchettṛtvagraheṇetyāśaṅkya virodhādapi na vyavaccheda ityāha -- asatīti | anyonyavyavacchinnānāṃ hi bahutvaṃ bhavati ; vyavacchedaśca vimatyadhikaraṇabhāvāpanno nādyāpi sidhyati | ato'sati vyavacchede kuto bahutvam ? satyapi bahutve bahutvaikatvayorasati vyavacchede dvitīyasya virodhino'bhāvāt kuto virodhaḥ ? atha vyavacchedāt virodho virodhācca vyavaccheda ityucyate, tathā sati itaretarāśrayatvaṃ syāt | ato virodhādapi vyavacchedo nāstīti bhāvaḥ | athavā asati vyavacchede kuto bahutvaṃ tadabhāvācca kuto virodha iti yojyam ; tadā ca vāśabdaścārthaṃ | upasaṃharati -- tasmāditi | idānīṃ 'mā na bhūt' iti ślokapādaṃ vyācaṣṭe -- api ceti | ekadarśanabhājāmiti ; ekena citrajñānena gṛhyamāṇānāmityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha -- eketi | nīlādīnāmekatra citrajñāne prameyatvena saṃbandhādekatra nīle pīte vā citrajñānasyāniyamāt tanniyatākārajñānasāmarthyānna bhedasiddhirityarthaḥ ; tatra cedanyad bhede pramāṇam, tadeva sarvatra syādityakāraṇaṃ darśanapratiniyama iti bhāvaḥ | evamekavidhirevānyavyavaccheda iti mate niraste bhāvāvyatiriktabhedavādī codayati -- nanviti | prakṛtyaiveti ; svabhāvenetyarthaḥ | tadvidhāniti ; bhinnasvabhāvānityarthaḥ | nanu vyāvṛttirvyāvṛttimato'nyā ; tena vyāvṛttimadvahaṇe'pi na gṛhyata ityāśaṅkyāha -- na hīti | vyāvṛttitadvatoranyatve'navasthā syāt ; ato vyāvṛttisvabhāva eva bhāva iti bhāvaḥ | kimevaṃ sati syādityatrāha -- tatreti | kuta ityāha -- yaditi | vyatirekamāha -- anyatheti ; yo yatsvarūpaḥ sa cet tena svarūpeṇa na pratīyate, tataḥ svarūpāpratītyā pratīta
[p. 138]
eva na syādityarthaḥ | evaṃ sāmānyena nyāyaṃ vyutpādya prakṛte yojayati— P. 47-14. vyāvṛtteti | yathāyathamiti ; svena svena rūpeṇāsaṃkīrṇena darśaneṣu nirbhāsate yata ityarthaḥ | siddhāntī tvatrāpi pūrvoktadoṣamatidiśannāha—atrāpīti | pītādibhirasaṃsṛṣṭasya nīlasya nirbhāsanāt pītādyasaṃsṛṣṭasya bhāvatve nīlasyāmyupagamyamāne, yathā hṛdyairghaṭādibhirasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ bhūtalaṃ nirbhāti tathā paramāṇvādibhirapyadṛśyaiḥ ; atastathorubhayorapi vyavacchedaḥ syādityarthaḥ | atraiva dūṣaṇāntaramāha—api ceti | bhedo vastuno rūpaṃ svarūpaṃ vastvavyatiriktaṃ na bhavati ; kutaḥ ? vastūnāṃ bhedābhāvaprasaṅgāt | atha vyatiriktaṃ tadā tenārūpeṇa vastuno'svarūpeṇa svarūpeṇa vastuno bhinnatvaṃ na ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | ślokaṃ vivṛṇoti—na vyāvṛttiriti | kuta ityāha— seti | sā vyāvṛttiryasya yebhyastadanekādhiṣṭhānā dvitvādivat pratijñāyata ityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ pratijñāyata eva, pratīyate'pi tathaivetyāha—pratīyate ceti | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | kuta ityāha—naikasmāditi | yadekasmādabhinnasvabhāvaṃ na taddhinnaṃ yathā tadevaikam ; ekā ca bhedipratiyoginorvyāvṛttiḥ ; atastadabhedāt tayorapi bhedo na syādityarthaḥ | atha svoktivirodhadoṣabhayāt vastubhyo vyatiriktā vyāvṛttiriṣyate, tato vyāvṛtteravastusvarūpatvāt svarūpeṇa bhāvānāṃ bhedo na syāt ; ataścaikabheva taddhya syādityabhiprāyeṇāha—atheti | pūrvārdhasya vyākhyāntaramāha—apara iti | nīlapītayoritaretarānātmatvādabhāvasvarūpā bhedaḥ ; sa cedvastunaḥ svabhāvaḥ, tadā vastūnāmabhāvātmanāṃ pratijñānāt bhedasyabhāvātmanaḥ pratijñānāt vastūnāmabhāvaḥ syādityarthaḥ | atra vyākhyāne tadabhāva iti tacchabdena vastu parāmṛśyate | tasyaiva vyākhyāntaramāha—apara iti | taṃ prakāramāha—bhedaścoditi | kuta ityāha—jedeneti | bhedo hi dvaidhībhāvātmā ; tatsvabhāvatve na vastuna ekatvamityarthaḥ | nanu paramāṇubhyo prinnamabhinnaṃ vā naikaṃ vastu samasti ; tena paramāṇava evādharottarabhāvena saṃhatā vastu ; tacca paramāṇūnāmanekātmakatvādanekātmakameveṣṭam | ato bhedasvabhāvatve vastuno naikavastvabhāvaprasaṅgo'smākamaniṣṭhāpattiriti bauddhamata-
[p. 139]
māśaṅkyāha—paramāṇuriti | paramāṇurapi bhinnatvāt bhedena svabhāvabhūtena P. 48-2. dvidhā kṛto naikaḥ syādityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | ekābhāve tadabhāvādekaikasamuccayarūpo'neko'pyasya vastuno nātmāvakalpata ityarthaḥ | na kevalamekaḥ, na ko'pītyarthaḥ | tadevamekamanekaṃ ca vastu na ghaṭate | tṛtīyaśra prakāro nāsti | ato niḥsvabhāvatvāt vastuno'bhāvaprasaṅga ityabhiprāyeṇāha—tatreti | arūpeṇeti ślokārthasyānyā vyākhyārabhyate— anyeti | tāmāha—vyatirekiteti | yo'yamanantarokto bhedo vastuno vyatirekto'vyatirekto veti vikalpaḥ, tasmin pratyavatiṣṭhante kecidityarthaḥ | pratyavasthānamāha—vastviti | vastuśūnyaḥ pratyayo vikalpaḥ, tena viracitaṃ śarīraṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | etadeva prapañcayati—tathā hīti | taditi vastūcyate; tasya bhāvastacca vastustvarūpatvam; vastuno'nyatvenānanyatvena vā niścitya vastumaśakyastaccānyatvābhyāmanirvacanīyaḥ | nanu yadi bhedo vastuno na bhinno nāpyabhinnaḥ, nāstyeva tarhi śaśaviṣāṇavat; kathaṃ vyavahāraṃ pravartayatītyata āha—anādīti | anādiryo bhedavikalpaḥ, tajjā vāsanā upādānakāraṇaṃ yasya vikalpasya tena viracitaṃ śarīraṃ yasya bhedasya sa tathoktaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—śaśaviṣāṇaṃ na pratibhāti; bhedastu taccato'sannapi vāsanāvaśāt pratibhātīti vyavahārahetuḥ; na tu bhedo nāma paramārthato vastvasti, yasya taccamanyatvaṃ vā vicāryata ityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—tatreti | na vidyate paramārthato bhinnatvaṃ dvicandrasyeva nāvakalpata ityarthaḥ | etadeva vivṛṇoti—yadīti | arūpa ityasya vivaraṇaṃ niḥsvabhāva iti | yadi bhedo vastudṛtyā nāsti, kiṃtu kalpitarūpaḥ, tataḥ paramārthato bhedābhāvānna vastuno bhinnatvaṃ paramārthataḥ syāt | na hi kalpitena mithyābhūtena svabhāvena dvitveneva candramasaḥ pāramārthikī tadvattā yujyate; kiṃtu kalpitaiva syāditi tvayāpīṣṭaḥ | tadetadasmābhirucyamānaṃ kimiti bhavān nānumanyate | vayamapyetadeva brūmaḥ—na bhedo nāma paramārthato'sti; kiṃ tvavidyāvilasitametadvinnatvamityarthaḥ | bhāvata iti; sadrūpatayā, paramārthata iti yāvat; bhāvebhyo vānya ityanyārthayoge pañcamī | sarve evāyamartho'sya ślokasya tantreṇa anyakṛto'bhimata iti na vyākhyāvikalpabhramaḥ |
[p. 140]
P. 48-16. pūrvoktamanubhāṣya tatra kiñcidāśaṅkayati—yaduktamiti | bhedātmatve bhedasvabhāvatve vastuno bhedenaikatvasya virodhāt ekavastvabhāvaḥ ; tadabhāvāccaikaikasamuccayarūpasyetarasyānekātmakasyāpi vastuno'bhāva iti hetorekatvānekatvayoranupapatteḥ tṛtīyaprakārābhāvācca vastvabhāva iti yaduktaṃ tatra para āhurityarthaḥ | yadāhustadāha—parāpekṣamiti | ghaṭavastu parataḥ paṭāt bhinnam, na tu svataḥ ; svayaṃ tvekameva ; ato bhedena vastvekatvasyāvirodhānnaikavastvabhāvadoṣa ityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—tatreti | puruṣasyeyaṃ pauruṣeyī ; tāmapekṣāṃ vastu yasmānnānuvartate, nāpekṣānurūpaṃ vastvityarthaḥ ; tasmāt vastuno bhedasvabhāvadoṣaḥ pūrvoktayuktyā tadavastha evetyarthaḥ | yadāpekṣā vastudharmaḥ syāt tatastaddharena vastu bhinnaṃ vyavasthāpyeta, na tvetadastītyāha—nāpekṣeti | kuta ityāha—na tviviti | svallaśabdaḥ prasiddhidyotanārthaḥ ; svahetubhya eva yatsvabhāvaṃ vastu tatsvabhāvavutpadyate ; anyathā tadvastveva na syāt ; ato na svabhāvavyavasthitaye tasya vastvantarāpekṣā yujyata iti prasiddhametadityarthaḥ | svahetubhiḥ prāpita udayo janma yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni | tatrārthātpuruṣasya yaḥ pratyayaḥ, tasya dharmo'yamapekṣā nāma ; puruṣo hi bhinnaṃ pratiyan pratiyoginamapekṣya pratyeti ; tadāha—tatreti | tataḥ kimityāha— na ceti | kuta ityāha—svahetubhya iti ; svahetvadhīnā vastusvabhāvavyavasthitiryata ityarthaḥ | puruṣapratyayānuvidhāyitve tu vastūnāṃ yathā yathā vastuni puruṣaḥ pratyeti tathā tathā syuḥ ; ataḥ śuktī rajatatayā puruṣeṇa pratītā rajatamapi syāt ; ato na vastusvarūpaṃ vyavatiṣṭheta ; na caitadasti | ato na puruṣapratyayānurūpyeṇa vastusvarūpavyavasthetyāha—ata iti | vastusvarūpānurūpaiva tu puruṣapratyayasya vyavasthocitā, vastupūrvakatvāttasyetyāha— vyavasthiteti | evaṃ bhinnasvabhāvaścet ghaṭaḥ svahetubhistatsvabhāva eva janita iti na pratiyogyapekṣāṃ pauruṣeyīmanuvartata iti prakṛte yojyam | kiñca yadi paṭhāpekṣo ghaṭasya bhedaḥ, tataḥ paṭhasyāpi ghaṭāpekṣo bheda itītaretarāścayaḥ syādityāha—paraspareti | imameva ślokārthaṃ punaranyathā vyācaṣṭe—
[p. 141]
athaveti | bhedapratiyoginorekaścedbhedastadavyatirekī tata ekasmāt P. 49-1. bhedādavyatirekāt bhedavattayorapyekatvāt bhedābhāvaprasaṅga iti 'na bhedo vastuno rūpam' ityava. yaduktam, tatrāhuḥ pare—syādevaṃ yadyeko bhedo'nekāśrayaḥ syāt; sa tu naikaḥ, rūpavirodhāt; bhedo hi prativastu vaiviktyamāpādayan prativastu viviktasvarūpa evocitaḥ; kutastasyaikatvam? yadi caikaḥ syāt tata ekadharmayogāt tannimittamekatvaṃ vastūnāṃ syāt, na nānātvam | athavā bhedaikatve tadavyatirekāt taddhedeva vastūnāmekatvaṃ syāt | tasmāt pratibhāvaṃ bhedo bhidyate | ghaṭasya paṭāt paṭhasya ca ghaṭādanyonyaṃ bheda ityarthaḥ | atra siddhāntī pṛcchati—kathamiti | yadyekaikāśrayo'nyo'nyo bhedaḥ kathaṃ tarhi bhedasyānekāśrayatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ satsyatītyarthaḥ | tatra paraṃ āhuḥ— apekṣāta iti | ekatra vartamāno'pi dvitīyamapekṣata ityanekāśrayatvaṃ bhedasya, na tu naikatra vartamānatvādityarthaḥ | ekatraiva cedvartate bhedaḥ, kathamanyamapekṣate? na hi ghaṭasyaṃ śauktyaṃ paṭhamapekṣata ityāśaṅkyāha—tathā hīti | bhedo hi kasyacit kutaścidbhavati | ataśca yato bhavati taṃ pratiyoginamapekṣya tasya svarūpaṃ vyavasthitam | tenaikasthasyāpi yuktānyāpekṣetyarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti | tathā ca sati yathāyathaṃ svīyasvīyabhedena pratibhāvaṃ bhinnena pratiyogyapekṣeṇātmabhūtena bhāvāvyatirekiṇā bhinnāḥ sarve bhāvāḥ; tena pratibhāvaṃ bhedānyatvāt na tadekatvena bhedavyati rekāt bhedavadbhedināṃ bhedābhāvadoṣaprasaṅga iti tātparyārthaḥ | atra dūṣaṇamucyate—pauruṣeyīmiti | tadyācaṣṭe—puruṣo hīti | puruṣo hyekamarthaṃ mṛtpiṇḍaṃ gṛhītvā paraṃ cakrādyapekṣate, tāvatā ghaṭādikāryasiddheḥ | bhāvāstu svahetubhyaḥ kenacidrūpeṇa hīnāśredātmānaṃ labheran, tatastadrūpalābhāyānyadapekṣeran; teṣāṃ tu svahetubhyaḥ prāptasaṃpūrṇarūpāṇāṃ kimarthānyāpekṣetyarthaḥ | apara āhuḥ—nanviti | svabhāva eṣa bhedasya, yadekastho'pi dvitīyaṃ pratiyoginamapekṣate; kiṃ kurva ityarthaḥ | nanu śauktyādīnāmayaṃ svabhāvo na dṛṣṭa ityāśaṅkyāha—nāneti | tacce; svarūpa ityarthaḥ | sādhyakoṭinikṣipto bhedaḥ pratiyogyapakṣitayā sarvajanasiddha iti tamevodāharati— yatheti | atra siddhāntavādyāha—na tarhīni | yadi bhedārupo bhāva-
[p. 142]
svabhāvo'nyamapekṣata na tarhi svahetubhyaḥ saṃpūrṇasvabhāvā bhāvā jātāḥ ; kiṃtu bhedākhyena svabhāvena vikalā ityabhyupeyam ; anyathānyāpekṣānupapatteḥ ; na caitadviṣṭamityarthaḥ | kiṃca bhāvasvabhāvo bhāvahetubhya eva bhāvasamakālameva jāyate, yathāgnerīṣṇyam ; bhedastu bhāvahetubhyo hetvantarāt pratiyogino bhāve jāte paścājjāyamāno na bhāvasvabhāvaḥ syāt ; ataḥ svabhāvabhūtā bheda P. 49-11. itīyaṃ pratijñā te hīyenetyāha —hetvantarāditi | atra punaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate —syādetaditi | syādetat tvadīyaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ yadi bhedarūpavikalo bhāvaḥ svahetubhyo jāyeta ; sa tu bhedādibhiḥ sarvarūpairavikalaḥ saṃpūrṇa eva svahetubhyo jāyate | ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—yadrūpāntarasya pratiyogyanapekṣasvabhāvatvam ; bhedākhyaṃ tu rūpaṃ pratiyogisāpekṣam, yataḥ pratiyogyanapekṣaṃ kadācidapi na dṛṣṭaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | iti hetvarthaḥ | kuta ityāha —tasyeti | tasya bhedākhyasya rūpasya tadvidhatvāt pratiyogisāpekṣasvabhāvatvādityarthaḥ | atastena bhedena tadvidhenaiva svabhāvenaiva saha bhāvo jāyata iti hetorna bhedena vikalarūpasya prādurbhāvo janma, yena tvadukto doṣaḥ syādityarthaḥ | atra dūṣaṇamāha —atreti | bhavatu tadvidhasya bhedasahitasyaiva bhāvasya prādurbhāvaḥ ; pratiyogyapekṣāyāstu arthaḥ prayojanaṃ vaktavyam ; na ca te tadastīti bhāvaḥ | kuta ityāha —yasya hīti | yadāyattaṃ hi yatra kiṃcidbhavati tat tadapekṣam, anyat tannirapekṣamitīyaṃ sāpekṣanirapekṣayorvyavasthā ; nānyetyarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha —tatreti | na tāvadbhedasyotpattiḥ pratiyogyāyattā, yenāsyotpattyarthaṃ bhedastamapekṣata ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha —sveti | svahetubhya evāvikalasya bhedasahitasyaiva bhāvasya prāmutpatterityarthaḥ | athodakāharaṇādyarthakriyā ghaṭādebhedasya pratiyogyāyattetyucyate, tadapi netyāha —neti | kuta ityāha —taditi | paṭādipratiyogyasaṃnidhāne'pi ghaṭasyārthakriyā dṛśyate | sā yadi pratiyogisāpekṣā syāt, tadasaṃnidhāne na syāditi bhāvaḥ | bhedapratītiḥ pratiyogyāyattetyāśaṅkayati —pratītiriti | etadeva draḍhayati —tathā hīti | yasya yato bhedaḥ pratīyate tatpratiyoginamanapekṣya bhedo na pratīyate yataḥ, atastatpratītiḥ
[p. 143]
pratiyogyāyattetyarthaḥ | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—neti | evaṃ cettarhi P. 49-21. bhedākhyo bhāvasvabhāvo na pratiyogyapekṣaḥ ; kiṃtu tasmin bhede pūrvamavasthite bhedākhyasya vastunaḥ sthiteruttarakālā tatpratītiḥ pratiyogisāpekṣetyāpannam | ato bheda eva pratiyogisāpekṣa iti pratijñātaṃ hīyata iti bhāvaḥ | na ca pratītau sāpekṣāyāṃ tatprameyaṃ vastu sāpekṣaṃ syādityatra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | vyavasthitāśra te sahetubhyo labdhātmānaḥ, ata evānapekṣasvabhāvāśreti vigrahaḥ | vyavasthitau anapekṣaḥ svabhāvo yeṣāmiti vā | nanvastu bhedapratītireva sāpekṣā, tadarthameva svato nirapekṣo'pi bhedaḥ pratiyoginamapekṣiṣyata ityāśaṅkyāha—itaretareti | yato bhedena pratītādāsya bhedaḥ pratyetavyaḥ, tasyāpi tatastathetītaretarāśrayaḥ | ato bhedasya pratītirapi parasparāyattā itaretarasāpekṣā na ghaṭata ityarthaḥ | nanu vastumātrādagṛhītabhedādbhedaḥ pratyeṣyate ; ato netaretarāśraya ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | hetumāha—ekasminniti | yadyevaṃ syāt svato'pi svasya bhedaḥ pratīyeta ; na caivamastīti bhāvaḥ | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | asyaivānyā vyākhyā ; tāmāha—neti | hetumāha—apekṣika iti ; pratiyogyapekṣayā yataḥ sa bhedaḥ pratīyata ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha— sa ceti | apekṣā hi puruṣasya pratyayaviśeṣaḥ ; na ca taṃ varatvanuvartate ; na ca puruṣapratyayamanu vastuvyavasthetyarthaḥ | hetumāha—vyavasthiteti | yathā vastu hi puruṣapratyayo yuktaḥ ; anyathā nirālambanaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | itiḥ ślokārthaparisamāptau | nanu yadyapekṣikaṃ na vastuno rūpam, kathaṃ tarhi pitṛtvādayo vastuno rūpam ? te'pi hyāpekṣikā eva ; na hi te putrādyapekṣayā vinā bhavantītyataste yathāpekṣikā api vastuno rūpaṃ tathā bhedo'pītyabhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati—kathamiti | na ca khapuṣpavadasantaḥ ; na ca gandharvanagarādivadasatyā ityāha—na ceti | astāṃ vyavahāra eva nāsti ; asatyena tu visaṃvādī ; pitṛtvādayastu vyavahārasya hetutvena loke siddhā, sa cāvisaṃvādīti nāsanto nāpyasatyā ityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—ucyata iti |
[p. 144]
yadi pitṛtvādayo'pyapekṣikāḥ, te'pi tarhi bhedavadvastuno rūpaṃ mā bhūvan, asatyā eva santu; kā no hāniḥ? na ca teṣāmavisaṃvādivyavahārahetutvena siddhatvādbhedādviśeṣaḥ; bhedo'pyevamavisaṃvādivyavahārahetutvena pitṛtvādivadvastuvadvā kiṃ na loke siddhaḥ? api tu siddha eva; ato nāyamapi viśeṣa ityarthaḥ | P. 50-6. evaṃ pitṛtvādibhedaporaviśeṣamuktvā saṃprati viśeṣamāha—api ceti | pitrādau viṣaye putrādyapekṣā jananādinā bhavati | hrasvadīrghayorapyanyonyāpekṣā, ekasyāḥ deśavyāptikriyāyā nyūnādhikabhāvaviśeṣeṇa bhavati | na ca bhede tādṛśo'pekṣāhetuḥ kaścidastīti bhāvaḥ | etadyācaṣṭe—vyavasthite iti | vyavasthite autpattike anyonyanairapekṣyeṇa sthite, na tu vastusthityuttarakālamanyāpekṣāprabhave pitṛputrayorjanyajanakaśaktī pitṛputrayorabhidheye; yathāsaṃkhyamatrāvivakṣitam | etacca śaktiḥ kārakamityabhipretyoktam | śaktimatkārakamiti pakṣe'pi sarvamūhyam | kathaṃ tarhi tayoranyonyāpekṣitvamityāśaṅkyāha—te tviti | janyajanakayoḥ kartṛkarmakārakaśaktī samānaśrāptau jananākhyo vyāpārastadviṣaye sati tā pitṛputraśabdau āskandataḥ viṣayīkurutaḥ | te vā śaktī kartṛbhūte tau śabdau karmabhūtau āskandataḥ svavācakatvena svīkuruta ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yadaikasyāṃ jananakriyāyāmekaḥ puruṣaḥ kartā, aparaḥ karma, tadā tau pitṛputraśabdavācyau bhavataḥ; nānyatheti | kutaḥ? svabhāvabhedāt; īdṛśa eva tayoḥ śabdayoḥ svabhāvaviśeṣo yenedṛśa eva viṣaye vartate, netaratretyarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha—ata iti | samānatvaṃ hi kriyāyā dvayorbhavati; ato yasya kriyā yena samānā tasya tatrāpekṣā bhavatītyarthaḥ | uktamarthaṃ prakṛtopayogitayā spaṣṭayati—etaditi | pitṛputraśabdayorarthastatra pitrādau sāpekṣaḥ, na tu janyajanakaśaktyākhyaṃ vastu; tattu pūrvaṃ vyavasthitamevetyuktam | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yadā janyena samānajanikriyo janako bhavati, tadā pitṛśabdārtho bhavati; nānyadā | ataśca yena sā kriyā samānā tajjanyamepakṣya janakaḥ pitṛśabdārtho jāta iti śabdārthaḥ sāpekṣa ityucyate | ataḥ śabdamatraṃ
[p. 145]
tvatra sāpekṣaṃ na vastviti | apekṣāhetuśca tatra pivādau sādhāraṇī janakakriyā vidyate, yadyapi janyajanakaśaktyākhyavastunī vyavasthite anyonyanirapekṣe ityarthaḥ | na dvitīyena vinā sādhāraṇyaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | bhedetu vaiṣamyamāha—bhāveti | vastutattvagata iti; na śabdagata ityarthaḥ; tena P. 50-12. vastveva tatra sāpekṣamiti bhāvaḥ | hastādāvapi yadvastu yāvatparimāṇaṃ tattāvatparimāṇamanyanirapekṣameva svahetubhyo jātam | śabdapravṛttimātraṃ tu sāpekṣam; apekṣāhetuścaikajātīyāyā deśavyāptikriyāyā nyūnādhikabhāvaḥ; yadapekṣayā yasya deśavyāptikriyādhikā tat tato dīrghamityucyate; yā yasyonā taddhastvamiti | yā yāvaddeśaikasya vyāptiḥ, anyasya na tāvaddeśātadūnam; itaradadhikamiti nyūnādhikavivekaḥ; tena nyūnādhikayoranyonyāpekṣitādoṣo nāsti | yadi jananakāryaviśeṣāt pitrādāvapekṣā iṣyate, vastubhedo'pi tarhi kāryasyaiva bhedādiṣyatāmiti paraḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—yadīti | kiṃca evaṃ sati yadyapi pratyakṣaṃ vastuvidhimātravyāpāraṃ na vyavacchinati na tadbhede pramāṇam, tathāpi kāryabhedādanumāsyāmahe; ato na bhedasyāpramāṇatvamapītyāha— kiṃceti | vidhimātramanyavyavacchedarahitaṃ vyāpāro yasyeti vigrahaḥ | vipakṣādavyāvṛttau heturagamaka iti tato vyāvṛttimāha—naikatva iti | anvayastvadvaitinaṃ pratyasiddho na ca bauddhairiṣṭa iti nokta ityadoṣaḥ | atra hetumāha—kāraṇeti | tadvivṛṇoti—taditi | siddhāntī tu kāryabhedo'pyasmākamasiddhaḥ; atastena na bhāvabhedānumānam | naikasyāneke svabhāvāḥ, tadbhede bhedaprasaṅgāditi bhedāt bhedaprasādhanamaśobhanamityāha—tadidamiti | yadapyuktaṃ kāraṇaikatve kāryabhedo nāvakalpata iti, tadapyuktam, ekatve'pi tadarśanādityāha—api ceti | dāhādikāryabhedāt 'dāhakaḥ pācakaḥ' iti vyapadeśamātraṃ bhidyate, na tu vastvityarthaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | arthakriyākṛte bhede aṅgīkriyamāṇa ityanvayaḥ | nanvalāpi rūpādisamudāyamātraṃ vahniḥ; na tvavayavyeko bhinno'bhinno vā saṃbhavati; ato rūpādiguṇabhedādeva kāryabheda ityāśaṅkayati—atheti | siddhāntī tu bhavatvevam; tathā- 10
[p. 146]
pyuṣṇasparśāttāvadekasādāhapākakāryabhedo'sti ; ato P. 51-1. vipakṣādavyāvṛttirhetostadavasthaivetyāha—kāmamiti | na ca pākadāhayorbhedo'siddha iti vācyamityāha— tau ceti | yadyapi vahnisaṃyogavikriyā pāko'pīti tadvikriyātvasāmānyamubhayatrāpyekamasti, tathāpyavāntarabhedastayorlokaprasiddhatvānnāpalapanīya ityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ lokasiddhamekasāt kāryabhinnatvam, api tu vādibhirapi sarvairidamiṣṭamityāha—karmeti | muktasyeṣorgatikarma pūrvāparadeśābhyāṃ vibhāgasaṃyogayorvegākhyasya saṃskārasya heturasamavāyikāraṇam ; ātmā cecchāsukhādīnāṃ hetuḥ samavāyikāraṇamiti kaṇabhujeṣṭam | tathā saccādayaḥ pratyekamabhedā avidyamānabhedāṃśā aṅgalādhavārthaprakāśādīnāṃ hetava iti yojyam | kvacidabhedāditi pāṭhaḥ ; tatrāyamarthaḥ—na ca te bhinnā iti vācyam ; kutaḥ ? pratyekaṃ sattvarajastamasāmabhedāt | ataścaikasādapi kāryabhedo'stīti bhāvaḥ | tatra sattvaṃ lādhavādīnām, rajaḥ pravṛttiduḥkhānāṃ heturiti kapilasyeṣṭam | tathā rūpādayaḥ pratyekaṃ svagocarāṇāṃ jñānānāṃ hetava ālambanapratyayāḥ rūpaṃ rūpakṣaṇānāṃ raso rasakṣaṇānāṃ copādānapratyaya iti bauddhamatam | nanu nārthakriyābhedamātrāt bhāvabhedaṃ brūmaḥ ; api tu yato yasya bhedaḥ, tatrādṛṣṭatvenāsaṃbhāvanī yārthakriyā, tata iti paraśrodayati—nanviti | atraivodāharaṇamāha—yatheti | yathā badhire satyapi cakṣuṣi śabdabuddhiradṛṣṭatvenāsaṃbhāvanī ; tataścakṣuṣo bhinnaṃ śrotraṃ kalpyata ityarthaḥ | nanvevamanyatra kathaṃ bhedasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha—prāyeṇeti | parasparāsaṃbhavīni kāryāṇi yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ ; sarvatraiva prāyeṇānyakāryamanyatra na dṛśyate ; ato bhedasiddhirityarthaḥ | śaraśṛṅgayorviśaraṇaikakāryayornivṛttyarthaṃ prāyagrahaṇam | kathaṃ tarhi tayorbheda ityāśaṅkya viśaraṇaikakāryatve'pi prāyeṇa parasparāsaṃbhavīni kāryāntarāṇi tayorapi sannīti prāyeṇeti punaryojyam | siddhāntī tvāha— atheti ; anyakāryasyānyatrāsaṃbhavaḥ kathaṃ pratyetavya ityarthaḥ | para āha— nanviti | taddūṣayati—neti | advaitināmekatraiva sarvaṃ kāryaṃ dṛśyate ; ato nānyakāryasyānyatrādarśanamasti | tatra sati bhede anyakāryamanyatra na dṛśyata iti vācyam | evaṃ ca bhede sati kāryādarśanam, adarśane sati bheda itītaretarāśrayaḥ syāt | ato na kāryabhedāt bhāvabhedānumānamityarthaḥ | atha satyapi paṭhe ghaṭakāryasyādṛṣṭasya paścāttaddheto-
[p. 147]
ghaṭasya bhedo'numīyate ; tayorabhede hi paṭhakāryavat ghaṭakāryamapi pūrvameva dṛśyeteti paramatamāśaṅkayati—atheti | taddūṣayati—taditi | ekasyāpi P. 51-10 puruṣasya krameṇānekabhojanādyarthakriyāṃ kurvataḥ pūrvamadṛṣṭā kriyā paścādṛśyate ; na ca tasya bheda ityarthaḥ | nanvatyantamabhede puruṣasya tasya sarvārthakriyāsamarthatvāt samarthasya ca kṣepāyogāt yugapat sarvakāryadarśanaṃ syāt ; ataḥ kasyacit kāryasya pūrvamadarśanaṃ paścācca darśanaṃ pūrvāvasthāto'viśeṣānna syāt ; ato'sti pūrvasāt kaścidviśeṣo yadāgame pūrvamadṛṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ paścāt dṛśyate ; ato naikāntena bhedaḥ, nāpyabheda ityāśaṅkayati—syāditi | atrottaramāha—na tarhīti | yadi viśeṣamātrabhede'pi kāryabhedo ghaṭate, na tarhi kāryabhedo vastvabhedaṃ bādhate ; kiṃtu kāryabhedāt vastvabhedānupamadena viśeṣamātrabhedo'numīyeta, abhinne'pi vastuni tāvataiva kāryabhedopapatteḥ | ato yaduktaṃ kāryabhedādvastubheda iti tadevaṃ sati vighaṭitamityabhiprāyaḥ | nanu viśeṣamātrabhede'pi nādvaitasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha—tacceti | satyaṃ viśeṣamātraṃ bhinnamicchāmaḥ ; kiṃtu na tastatyam ; api tu kālpanikam ; taccāvastutvānna dvatamāvahatīti bhāvaḥ | nanu satyameva tat kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkyāha—vastviti | vastuvṛttyā satastasya viśeṣamātrasyāyogādaghaṭamānatvādityarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha—tacceti | tadbhāvastaccam abhinnatvam ; vastuto viśeṣāṇāṃ bhinnatvābhinnatvayorasaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | te cedvastuno na bhinnāḥ, tato vastveva param ; na te pṛthak santi | atha bhinnāḥ, na tarhi vastunaḥ, asaṃbandhāt | na hi bhinnayostādātmyamarbudahemanayorāva | nāpi samavāyaḥ ; so'pi hi samavāyibhyāṃ bhinno'bhinno vā syāt ; yadyabhinnaḥ, na pṛthagasti ; bhinnaścet, na tayoḥ syāt, asaṃbandhāt ; tasyāpi tābhyāṃ samavāyāntarakalpane cānavasthā | bhinno'pi samavāyastābhyāṃ svata eva saṃbandhaścet tāveva tathā stām ; kiṃ tena ? asaṃbandho hi saṃbandhamapekṣate, na saṃbandhaḥ, pradīpa iva pradīpāntaramiti cenna ; samavāyinorasau saṃbandho nātmanaḥ ; tenātmanaḥ samavāyibhyāṃ saṃbandhāya samavāyāntarāpekṣā yukteti yuktānavasthā | pradīpopekṣataṃ cakṣuritaravatpradīpamapyupalambhayatīti yuktā tatra pradīpāntarānapekṣeti | kiṃca saṃbandhaścetsaṃbandhāntaraṃ nāpekṣate, 104
[p. 148]
saṃyogo'pi nāpekṣeta samavāyam ; nacaivaṃ vaiṣamyam | kiṃca samavāyaḥ svasaṃbandhibhyāṃ samavāyenānyena saṃbadhyate, saṃbandhatvāt, saṃyogavat | evaṃ so'pyanenetyanavasthā | kiṃca iha pratyayahetuḥ samavāyaḥ ; na ca gotvādiṣvihapratyayo'sti | na hi 'iha gavi gauḥ' iti pratyayaḥ, api tu 'gaurayam' iti sarveṣām | ataḥ samavāyo nāstīti na so'pi vastuviśeṣayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ | nāpi saṃyogaḥ | so'pi hi saṃyogibhyāmabhinnaśretrāsti ; bhinnaśret kena tābhyāṃ saṃbadhyeta ? samavāyeneti cet, sa nirastaḥ | na ca svatantraḥ saṃbhavati | tasmāt so'pi nāsti | tena saṃyogo'pi na vastuviśeṣayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ | ato vastuno bhinno'pi viśeṣo na bhavatīti sūktam | evaṃ vastvekatve'pi kālpanikaviśeṣamātrabhedāt kāryabhedaṃ sthiravādinaḥ P. 51-15 pratyāpādya kṣaṇikavādinaṃ prati viśeṣeṇa tamāha—yastviti | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | yastu kṣaṇikatvavādī taccamevābhedameva saccādyātmanā viśeṣasya na manyate—viśeṣā evātyantavilakṣaṇāḥ kṣaṇikaṃ vastu, na tvekajātyādi sthāyi kiñcidastīti manvānaḥ—tasya kāraṇasvabhāvānuvartī kāryasvabhāva iti vrīhikṣaṇānāṃ tatsāmānyarūpeṇāsatyabhede tebhyaḥ kathamekarūpavrīhyakurākhyakāryotpādaḥ | athātyantabhinnānāmapi teṣāṃ kalpitājjātirūpeṇābhedāt kāryatulyatācyate, evaṃ tarhi kāraṇaikatve'pi kalpitādeva bhedāt kāryabhedasiddheḥ kāryabhedasiddhaye kāraṇabhedakalpanā gurvī dṛṣṭaivetyarthaḥ | niranvayo vināśo yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ | kṣaṇikavādināṃ ghaṭanāśe ghaṭatvasya ghaṭāntare mṛdaśra svakapālādāvanvayo nāstīti niranvaya ityucyate | atha bhinnebhyo'pi cakṣurviṣayasamanantarapratyayebhya ekajñānotpatterna kāryābhedāya kāraṇābhedo'pekṣyate, tenātyantabhinnebhyo'pi kṣaṇebhyastulyarūpaṃ kāryamutpattyata ityucyate, tathā sati kāryasvabhāvasya kāraṇasvabhāvānuvartitvavyabhicārāt yathā bhinnādapyekarūpaṃ kāryaṃ tathaikasmādapi kāryabhedopapatterna kāryabhedasiddhaye kāraṇabhedaḥ kalpyaḥ ; tadetadāha—atheti | yadapi bhinnānāmekakāryatve cakṣurādyudāhataṃ tadapi tasyaivāsiddham ; tathāhi—cakṣuṣo rūpagrahaṇaniyame, samanantarajñānasya jñānātmatve, viṣayasya ca tadākāratve vyāpāra iti vadata
[p. 149]
cakṣurādikāraṇavyāpārāṇāṃ bhinno viṣayastenaivoktaḥ; tasmānna tatrāpi kāryabheda ityāha—api ceti | kāryasāmyasya kāraṇābhedānapekṣatve doṣāntaramāha— P. 51-21. yadi ceti | tarhi brīhyaṅkurānmṛtpracchannasya brīheranumānaṃ na syādityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—bhinneti; yavakṣaṇādapi brīhyaṅkurasyotpattisaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | etadevopapādayati—hetviti | kāryasāmye hetusāmye cennopayujyate, tato yayaikasādvīhikṣaṇādatyantabhinnādvīhikṣaṇāntarāt brīhyaṅkuro bhavati tathā yavakṣaṇādapi syāt, abhedakalpanāṃ vinā tayoraviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ | tatrāvaśyaṃ kāryasāmyāya hetvabhedakalpanā tvayāpyāstheyeti tulyatvamāvayorityuktaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati—tatreti | atra paraśrodayati—syāditi | yadarthakriyākāri, tat sat; netaracca śaśaśṛṅgādi | bhedo'pi cedarthakriyāhetuḥ, na tarhi kalpitaḥ; kiṃtu sannevetyarthaḥ | siddhānto tvāha—tulyamiti | abhedo'pi tava kāryatulyatvaheturna kalpitaḥ syādityarthaḥ | atha mataṃ nārthakriyābhedamātrādvastubhedaṃ brūmaḥ; kiṃtu viruddhārthakriyābhedāt; na hyasāvekatra saṃbhavati; tadāha—yo'pīti | etadeva prapañcayati—tathā heti | tasyottaramāha—tasyāpīti | nanu viruddhārthakriyābhedādbhāvabheda uktaḥ; na ca dāhapākau viruddhau; ataḥ kathamidamuttaramityabhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati—kathamiti | siddhānto tvasyottaratvamupapādayati—idamiti | yadi tāvadekatra jananamaraṇādīnāmasaṃbhavo virodhaśabdasyārthaḥ, so'dvaitināmasmākamasiddhaḥ; asmākaṃ hi viruddhaṃ ca sarvamekatrātmani samavetam | atha mitho vyavacchinnatā virodhaḥ, jananamaraṇādayaśca tathetyucyate, tato'nyato vastubhedasiddhiḥ | ekasyāpi vahermitho vyavacchinnayordāhapākayordarśanādityarthaḥ | na ca tayormitho vyavacchinnatvamasiddham, nānātvena tasmiddheḥ | atastathābhūtāccedvastubhedaḥ, tato'nekarūparasādiṣarmakapṛthivyādyapi naikaṃ syādityarthaḥ | atha harṣaḥ śokābhāvātmakaḥ, śokaśca harṣābhāvātmaka ityayamanayorvirodhaḥ; tatra tau cedekatra yugapat syātām, tataḥ śokasya bhāvaḥ, tadabhāvātmakasya harṣasya bhāvāccābhāvaḥ; tathā harṣasyāpi bhāvaḥ, harṣābhāvātmakasya śokasya bhāvāccābhāvaḥ; na caikatraikasyaikadā bhāvābhāvau saṃbhavataḥ;
[p. 150]
atastayorīdṛśādvirodhāt tadāśrayamūtabhāvabhedo'vasīyate ; evaṃ jananādibhyo'pīti cet ; tadasat ; evaṃ hi parasparābhāvātmakatvāddharmaveva bhāvarūpī na staḥ ; kutastayorvirodhādbhāvabhedāvagatiḥ ? tathā khaṇḍapādiṣu P.52-13. harṣaśokayorabhāvādanyonyābhāvātmakayostayoḥ prasaṅgaḥ | tadetatsarvamāha—atheti | atha vadhyaghātakabhāvo'nayorvirodha ityucyate, tadapi na ; yato jāto ghātako vadhyaṃ hanti, na ca bhinnādhikaraṇe ityato bhūtvaiva vadhyena caikādhikaraṇe vadhyaṃ ghātako hanyāt | tataścaikādhikaraṇayoreva vadhyaghātakabhāvāt na tato'dhikaraṇabhedāvagatiḥ | athāpi syāt, tathāpyekādhikaraṇatvadarśanānnādhikaraṇa-bhedānumānamavyabhicāram, ekādhikaraṇayorapi vadhyaghātakayordarśanādityāha—atheti | na vidyate vyabhicāro'syeti vigrahaḥ | atha bhāvābhāvarūpī dharmo virodhinau ; yathā—nityatvānityatve mūrtatvāmūrtatve ca ; tayoreka-dharmatvāsaṃbhavāddharmibhedo'vasīyata ityucyate, tadapi na ; na hi yo dharmo yatra vartate sa tamavaśyaṃ vyāpnoti ; na hi rathā rathasaṃyogā vā nabho vyāmuvanti pṛthivyādīṃśra, rathānāmavacchinnatvāt avacchinnarathāśrayatvena tatsaṃyogānāmapyavacchinnatvāt | yathā rathādayastatsaṃyogāścaivamanye'pi ye'vacchinnāḥ | ye ca nityānityatvādayo dharmoḥ, te nānavacchinnāḥ | ata evānantātmākhyamarthamāśrayanto'pi na taṃ vyāmuvanti | tataśca teṣāṃ nityatvātmakabhāvatadabhāvau na virudhyete | ato na bhāvābhāvayorvirodhāt taddharmiṇo bhedāvagatiriti | tatsarvamāha—atheti | nanu saṃyogāderavyāpitvādekatra vyomni bhāvābhāvau yuktau ; nityatvaṃ tvātmano vyāpi ; tataśca tadabhāvasyānityatvasya tadekāśrayatvāsaṃbhavādātmano bhinnaṃ kṣityādyāśrayaḥ pratīyata eva | atra brūmaḥ—syādevaṃ yadyanityatvaṃ nāma dharmastaccato'sti ; māyāmātraṃ tu tat kṣityādirūpeṇātmani kalpitam | na ca māyāyāṃ virodho'sti ; na hyekasya candramaso dvitvaṃ virudhyata iti bhāvaḥ | matāntarabhāśaṅkayati—yastviti | vyatirekamāha—ekatva iti | avyavasthāmāha—tathā ceti | anyatheti ; yadi tilāt tailameva syāt, na dadhi, yadi caikasmin naśyatītarastra naśyet tadetyarthaḥ | atra paravacanaṃ paṭhati—taditi | sarvaṃ sarvatropayujyeteti ; sarvaṃ payastilādi sarvatraiva
[p. 151]
tailadadhyādiṣu hetutvenopayujyetyarthaḥ | evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—tasyāpīti | P. 58-7. yadi kāryanānātvaṃ vyavasthā, tayā tilānāṃ payasaśra bhedaṃ pratijānāti, tadedemevottaramityanvayaḥ | nanu kṣīrād dvyo janma nānyata itīyaṃ vyavasthoktā, na kāryanānātvamātram ; ataḥ ko'yamanukopālambhaḥ ? satyamuktam ; kiṃtu tilapayasoranyatve satīyaṃ vyavasthā, vyavasthayā ca tayoranyatvasiddhiritītaretarāśrayagrastatvādayuktā | ato vyavasthāṃ vadatā kāryanānātvameva vācyamityabhipretyedamuktamityadoṣaḥ | atha pūrvoktāmeva vyavasthāmāha, tatrocyate— atheti | vyavasthā hi bhāvānāmanyatvasādhanam | siddhaṃ ca sādhanaṃ bhavati, nāsiddham | tadyadi payaso dvyo janma nānyata iti vyavasthāyā lakṣaṇaṃ syāt, tato yadanyatvaṃ sādhyatvenāsiddhaṃ tat sādhanabhūtāyā vyavasthāyā lakṣaṇamanupraviśet | na ca tadyuktam, sādhyatvenāsiddhasyānyatvasyānupraveśe tasyā vyavasthāyā evāsiddhiprasaṅgāt | tataścetaretarāśrayaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | tasmāt dadhijananaśaktistadabhāvaśra vyavasthā | tatastilapayasorbhedo ganyate | na hyakatra viṣaye dadhinyekasya payasaḥ śaktyaśaktī yujyete | ataḥ payasaḥ śaktiḥ, tilānāmaśaktiriti tayorbhedasiddhiriti vācyamityāha—tasmāditi ; yasmātpūrvoktā vyavasthā durghaṭā, tasmādiyaṃ vācyetyarthaḥ | siddhāntī tvāha—sa ceti | ayaṃ ca heturanantarameva 'ato naikasya bhāvābhāvavirodhaḥ' ityatrāsmābhirnirastaḥ | ato nedamapi vyavasthālakṣaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | pūrvoktameva bhāvābhāvayorekatrāvirodhaṃ dṛṣṭāntena draḍhayati—yatheti | yathā dīrghakālā devadattādayaḥ parimitakālairbālyayauvanādibhiḥ saṃbadhyamānāstatkāle taddharmāṇaḥ paścādataddharmāṇo bhavanti na virodhasthānatvaṃ bhajante, tathā parimitadeśaiḥ rathatatsaṃyogādibhiriva vipuladeśāḥ pṛthivyādayaḥ, anantāśrātmādayo'nityatvādibhiḥ saṃbadhyamānā na virodhapadabhāja ityarthaḥ | atrāśaṅkyati—kathamiti | dharmasya dharmiṇo'vyatirekāttasyāpyanantatvamucitamiti bhāvaḥ | ata evāvyatirekapakṣānabhyupagamena tāvaduttaramāha—vyatireka iti | yadyanantādavyatirekto dharmaḥ syāt, so'pi tadvadevānantaḥ syāt | vyatireke tu tasya mithyātve vā anantasyāpyantavaddharmāsaṃbhāvanā nāstītyarthaḥ | avyatirekapakṣe'pi sā nāstītyāha—
[p. 152]
P. 53-16. avyatireke'pīti | dīrghakālādavyatiriktā dharmāstadvadeva dīrghakālā na, punarmitakālā bhavanti; atastathaivānantasyāpyantavān dharmo bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | yastu kṣaṇikavādī dīrghakālamarthaṃ na pratijānāti taṃ prati dṛṣṭāntāsiddhau tena dṛṣṭāntena naikatra bhāvābhāvayoḥ samāveśaḥ sidhyatītyāśaṅkya taṃ pratyekatra bhāvābhāvayoḥ samāveśaṃ darśayituṃ pūrvoktameva ślokārthaṃ punayojayati—yastviti | tadviṣṭuṇoti—eka iti | eko vahnikṣaṇaḥ kiñcideva tṛṇataṇḍulādi dahati pañcati ca, na jalādi; tathā ca tṛṇādīn prati dāhakaḥ pācakaśra, jalādiṣu na tathā, na ca bhidyate; tasmādekatrāpi śaktyaśaktyādilakṣaṇabhāvābhāvadharmadarśanānna tato bhāvabhedāvagatirityarthaḥ | asmin pakṣe kvacit dāhapākayorbhāvaḥ kañcidabhāva ityayaṃ vibhāgo vyākhyeyaḥ | nanu viṣayabhedādatrāvirodho yuktaḥ; śaktyaśaktī tvekaviṣayeṇaikasya viruddhe ityāśaṅkya tadeva ślokārthaṃ punaranyathā yojayati—tatheti | eko'pi vahnikṣaṇa ekasminneva dāhye pākaye ca kiñcit dāhaṃ pākaṃ ca karoti; tena dāhakaḥ pācakaśra; tato'nyanna karotītyadāhako'pācakaśreti 'na ca bhidyate' ityanuvartate | ayameva ca dāhapākavibhāgo yojyaḥ | evaṃ kṣaṇavādinameva prati sāśaṅkramekatra samāveśaṃ darśayitumudāharaṇāntaramāha—tatheti | bauddhā abhimanyante—bījamaśaktaṃ cet kṣityādisaṃyoge'pi nāṅkuraṃ janayet sikatāvat; śaktaṃ cenna kṣityādyapekṣeta; kṣityādyāhitaviśeṣaṃ janayatīti cet, na tarhi svataḥ śaktam; aśaktaṃ cāhitaviśeṣamapi na janayati, duṣṭabījavat | api ca viśeṣe sati bhāvādaprati ca bīje satyapyabhāvāt sa hetuḥ syāt, na bījam | na ca sa bhinno'bhinno bhinnābhinno vā saṃbhavati | tasmātsadṛśakṣaṇaparaṃparāprāptāḥ kṣitibījādikṣaṇāḥ pratyekaṃ śaktā apyekamevāṅkuraṃ kurvanti, na pratisvamanekān | nanu kṛtasya karaṇābhāvāt yadekena kṛtaṃ tasyānyena karaṇaṃ na syāt; syādevaṃ yadi kramaḥ syāt; saha kriyāyāṃ tu kṛta ityeva nāsti | yasyāpi sāmagrī janikā tasyāpi sā ekamevāṅkuraṃ janayati, nāṅkurāntarāṇi | tathāca satyekasyāpi janakatvamajanakatvaṃ ca
[p. 153]
‘na ca bhidyate’ ityatrāpyanuvartate | itirudāharaṇasamāptau | janakatva-P. 58-21. majanakatvamiti ; jananaśaktiraśaktiścetyarthaḥ | nanvekatraikasya śaktyaśaktī virudhyamāne tilapayasorbhedaṃ gamayata ityuktam | tatraiva vyabhicāro vācyaḥ | na ceha tathā ; tathāhi—bījānāmekatraivāṅkure śaktiḥ ; na ca tatrāśaktiḥ ; api tvanyatra ; ato vaiṣamyānna bījādivyabhicāra ityāśaṅkayati—atheti | anyatrāśaktistu bhinnaviṣayatvādevāviruddheti bhāvaḥ | siddhāntī tu vākchalena sāmyamāha—anyamiti | bhavatvekatraiva śaktiḥ ; tathāpyatītamapyekamanāgataṃ ca ; ataścaikatraiva śaktirekatraivāśaktiriti śaktyaśaktyoḥ samānaviṣayatvātsāmyabhevetyarthaḥ | atha kāryagamyatvācchakteranantarotpanna evāṅkure teṣāṃ śaktiḥ ; nātītānāgatayoriti na ca tatraivāśaktiḥ ; tilapayasorbhedakatraiva dadhi śaktiraśaktiśca ; tataśca vaiṣamyādvījādibhirna vyabhicāra iti punarāśaṅkayati—atheti | tatrottaramāha—abhede'pīti | etaduktaṃ bhavati— yattāvahadhi payaso jātaṃ tadabhedāt tilebhyo'pi jātameva ; ataśca yathā payasastatra śaktireva nāśaktiriti na śaktyaśaktyorvirodhaḥ ; tathā tilānāmapi ; ato na tatastayorbhedāvagatiḥ | atha ca dadhi cettilānāṃ śaktiḥ ; dadhyantaramapi janayet ; na ca tathā ; ato'śaktirapyastīti śaktyaśaktyorvirodha ityucyate ; tatra ; payoja eva hi dadhi tilānāṃ śaktirna dadhyantare ; bījādīnāmivātītānāgatayoḥ ; ataśca yatra śaktistatrāśaktireva nāsti ; kayā virodhaḥ syāt ? yā tvanyatrāśaktiḥ ; sā vibhinnaviṣayatvādavirodhinīti na śaktyaśaktyorvirodha iti | ekatra cecchaktiranyatrāpi tulyajātīye syāt ; atastadapi tilā janayeyuriti cet tannetyāha—na hīti | matāntaramāśaṅkayati—atheti | viniyogabhedamāha—dadhi hīti | taddūṣayati—tadapīti | anaikāntikatvamāha—ekatve'pīti | tadeva prapañcayati—tadeveti | ekamapi vastu sahakārisahitaṃ kāryaṃ viniyujyate na kevalam ; ataścaikatrāpi viniyogāviniyogasaccenānaikāntikatvāt na vastubhedāvagatihetutvamityarthaḥ | atha sahakārisaṃnidhānopādhibhedāt bhinnameva vastu kevalāt ; ato nānaikāntikatvamityāśaṅkayati—atheti | siddhāntī
[p. 154]
P. 54-8. tvāha—aupādhika iti | yadi sahakārisaṃnidhānopādhibhedādbhedaḥ, aupādhika stārhaiḥ ; yaścaupādhikaḥ sa pratīyate param, na vastu saṃspṛśati, vimbaprativimbayoriva ; ato'paramārthaḥ ; aparamārthaśca bhāvānāṃ bhedo'smābhirabhyupeyate | ato'smatpakṣamevāśrito'sīti bhāvaḥ | kṣaṇikavādinastu kevalāt payaḥkṣaṇāt sahakārisahitaḥ payaḥkṣaṇo'tyantabhinnaḥ ; tathā sikatāpīti sāpi dadhni viniyujyeta | athādṛṣṭaśaktitvāt sikatā dadhni na viniyujyate, tato navo'pi payaḥkṣaṇaḥ pūrvamasattvānna dṛṣṭaśaktiriti na viniyujyeta | na caikajātimukhena sa dṛṣṭaśaktiriti vācyam, jātestairanabhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ | athānādivāsanākalpitaikajātimukhena dṛṣṭaśakteḥ payaḥkṣaṇāt navasyābhedāttasmin dṛṣṭaśaktāvayamapi dṛṣṭaśaktirityasya viniyogasiddhiḥ, na sikatāyā vijātīyakṣaṇāyā ityucyate, tataḥ parasyāpyabhedavādino'vidyākalpitādbhedāt payaḥsikatayorviniyogāviniyogau setsyata iti viniyogāviniyogavyasthayā bhāvabhedakalpanā vyarthaiveti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ bhedābhedavādinaṃ pratyāha—yo'pīti | yathā bhedābhedau viruddhau dharmāvekatra bhavataḥ, tathā janakatvādyapīti na tatastilapayasorbhedasiddhirityarthaḥ | janakatvājanakatvābhyāmityupalakṣaṇam ; śaktyaśaktyādayo'pi sarve draṣṭavyāḥ | idānīṃ sarvaṃ pūrvoktamarthamupajīvya svapakṣamupasaṃharati—taditi ; tasmādityarthaḥ | sahaḥ ; kṣama ityarthaḥ | ślokārthaṃ vidṛṇoti—ekatreti | ‘arthakriyākṛte bhede’ ityata ārabhya yāvaduktaṃ tatsarvam ‘evam’ ityupajīvitam | tadeva ‘ekatra’ ityādinā ‘saṃbhavāt’ ityantena sukhasmaraṇāyānukīrtitam | ‘vyavasthāyāśca’ ityatra ‘anaikāntikatvāt, asiddhatvādvā’ ityanuvartanīyam | imameva svapakṣaṃ śrutyā draṣṭavati—tadetaditi | śrutiṃ vidṛṇoti—etaditi | “yadbhūtam” ityatra ‘yadeko'pi’ ityanuvartate | “ato jyāyān” ityāśaṅkitottaraṃ yojayitumāśaṅkāṃ tāvadāha—pūrveti | “sahasra—” ityādau pūrvasmin ṛgdvaye nirdiṣṭānāṃ viruddhatvenābhimatānāṃ dharmakāryavyavahārāṇābheko'pi san kathamāśraya ityarthaḥ | tatraikasya sahasraśīrṣatvamadṛṣṭatvādviruddham | sarvabhūmivyāpinastu daśāṅgulatvamanyāyyam |
[p. 155]
atrottaramāha—ata iti | ‘ataḥ’ itīdamā saṃnihitamucyate | tacca P. 55-5. pratyakṣataḥ, śabdato vā | tatra pratyakṣamāśritya ‘ataḥ pratyakṣābhimatāt’ ityuktam | śabdamāśritya ca ‘puruṣa evedamiti sarvaśabdanirdiṣṭāt, utāmṛtatvasyetyanena vā nirdiṣṭāt’ ityuktamiti vivektavyam | kathamanena devādiprapañcasya nirdeśaḥ !
