ucyate—martyasya devāderamṛtatvasyeśāna ityarthāt devādiprapañcasya nirdeśaḥ | pratīkanirdeśo vāyam—“yadatrenātirohati” ityanena devādiprapañcasya nirdeśaḥ ; yaddhi yasyādanīyaṃ tattasyānnam ; devānāṃ cāmṛtādyannamiti | tataśca yadatrenātirohati prādurbhavati ca tasya ceśāna iti devādiprapañcanirdeśaḥ | medeneti ; itthaṃbhūtalakṣaṇe tṛtīyā ; itthaṃbhūtādityarthaḥ | sarvaṃ cedaṃ prapañcādityasya viśeṣaṇam | dṛḍhatara iti ; sarvathātiśayito'nanta iti yāvat | anena jyāyāniti “dṛḍhasya ca” ityanena jyādeśa iti darśayati | tadevamanante'sminnekasminnapi puruṣe paricchinnānāṃ viruddhānāmapi nityānityatvādīnāṃ samāveśo na na yujyate | ato'nantato bhāvabhedāvagatirityabhiprāyeṇāha—teneti | etadevānantatvaṃ śrutiḥ prakārāntareṇa darśayatītyāha—tadeveti | tāṃ vyācaṣṭe—kṛtsno'pīti | viśvā iti ; “śerundasi bahulam” iti śerlopaḥ | pādaḥ caturtho bhāgaḥ ; kanīyān arūpa ityarthaḥ | bhāga iveti ; nirbhāgasya tattvato bhāgābhāvādavacchinnaprapañcopādhikalpitaḥ sa iti darśayitumivaśabdaḥ | kathaṃ prapañcāvacchinnasya bhāgasyālpatvaṃ puruṣasya ca jyāyastvamityapekṣāyāmuttaramṛkpādaṃ yojayati—tathā hīti | pādā bhāgāḥ ; trayaḥ pādā asyeti vigrahaḥ ; rūpamanyapadārthaḥ | tritvena ca pādānāmekasmāt pādādvaipulyato'nantatvamabhipretam | athavā amṛtatvamavināśitvam ; taccājatvādidharmopalakṣaṇārtham | ajasyaiva hi tat, na janmavataḥ | tadayamarthaḥ—aparimitam, ata evānantam, maraṇādayo bhūtadharmāḥ yasya prapañcasya tena śūnyam, asya puruṣasya rūpaṃ divi pradyotāvasthāyāṃ viśuddhacaitanyāvasthāyāṃ pramodāvasthāyāṃ nityaniratiśayānandāvasthāyāṃ sūryamaṇḍale vā svargasthāne vā vartate iti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—prapañcasyāvacchinnatvāt saprapañcaṃ brahmarūpamarūpam, aprapañcaṃ tvaparimitam ; ato'sya viruddhāviruddhadharmakārya-
[p. 156]
vyavahārāśrayatvamucitamiti | nanvanantaṃ sarvatra vartate ; tatkathaṃ sūryamaṇḍale P. 55-12 yusthāne vetyuktamityāśaṅkyāha—tatra hīti | tadvivṛṇoti—taddhīti | upalabdhiḥ upāsanājñānamabhipretam | ‘ekasyaiva’ ityādiślokamanyathā vyācaṣṭe—apara iti | kalpaḥ arthaḥ | ghaṭanākāryabhedāt pūrvoktayā vā vyavasthayā kalpite'pi tvayā kāraṇayostilapayasorbhede, tvāṃ pṛcchāmaḥ—tilakāryāt tailāt kāryāntaraṃ dadhi kimiti payaso bhavati ? dvābhyāṃ saṃbhūyaikameva kāryaṃ kimiti na kriyate ? athāpi tilapayasī pṛthak kāryaṃ kurutaḥ, tathāpyekajātīyaṃ kiṃ na kurutaḥ ? tadā ca kāryāntaraśabdo bhinnajātīye draṣṭavyaḥ | kimiti cobhayamubhābhyāṃ na kriyate ? so'yaṃ saṃkaraḥ | payasastailaṃ tilebhyo dadhīti viparītā vā vyavasthā kiṃ na syāt ? payasaḥ sakāśāt tilasikatayorbhedāviśeṣe'pi kimiti vā tilebhyastailam, na sikatābhyaḥ ? iti | kiṃca dṛṣṭatvādapi bhinnānāmekakāryatvamekajātīyakāryatvaṃ vā yuktam ; dṛṣṭaṃ hi cakṣurādīnāmekakāryatvam, ekajātīyaśarādikāryatvaṃ ca śaraśṛṅgādīnām | tilapayasorbhede'pi kimiti tilebhyastailam, payaso dadhi, na tu viparyayaḥ ? iti viparītavyavasthāvivaraṇamātram | atha matam—acintyebhyo bhāvasāmarthyebhyaḥ kāryabhedaḥ kāryavyavasthā ; satyāṃ ca tasyāṃ na viparyayaḥ setsyatīti ; tato varamekasyaiva so'stu sāmarthyātiśayaḥ, yadekasmādapyanekaṃ vyavasthitamaviparītaṃ ca kāryaṃ bhavatīti ; ekakāraṇamātreṇa ekasya vā sāmarthyātiśayamātreṇa kāryabhedādisiddherapramāṇikā bhāvānāṃ gurvīṃ bhedakalpanetyarthaḥ | acintyebhya ityasya vivaraṇam—acintyānīti | ‘kasmādbhavanti, kathaṃ bhavanti’ ityevamupapattitaścintayituṃ na śakyante, kiṃ tu kāryadarśanaistatsāmarthyānāṃ vyavasthotrīyate | kāryaṃ tu tilapayasorbhinnaṃ vyavasthitamaviparītaṃ ca dṛśyate | atastathaiva tāni vyavasthitānīti bhāvaḥ | etaccāvidyātmikāṃ śaktimabhipretyoktam | na taccato bhinnā abhinnā bhinnābhinnā vā śaktirasti | ekasmādapyanekaṃ kāryaṃ dṛṣṭam ; yathā—vahnerdāhapākāvityuktam ; tatra codayati—syāditi | tatpariharati—tatreti | śaktīnāṃ bhede'pi
[p. 157]
vaherekatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvekasmādapi vaheḥ kāryabhedaḥ śaktibhedanibandhanaḥ ; na cābhedavādināṃ śaktibhedo'sti | naivam ; asmākamapi māyātmakaśaktibhedasyeṣṭatvādityadoṣaḥ | atha satyapi vahau mantrādyupahatāyāṃ śaktau kāryādarśanācchaktīnāmeva kārakatvamabhipretam ; tāśra bhinnāḥ ; tena kāraṇabhedādeva tatra kāryabheda ityāśaṅkayati—atheti | atrocyate—yatheti | P. 56-1. rūpamiti ; atra 'abhinnam' ityanuvartate | ślokārthaṃ viṣṭaṇoti—ekasyeti | ekasya vaheranekadāhādikāryasaṃbandhavirodhāt śaktibhedaḥ kalpyata ityarthaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | kiṃca ekasmādvaheranekāḥ śaktayaśredavyatiriktāḥ, tato vahireko'nekaśreṣṭavyaḥ | tatra vaherekatvaṃ tadgatenaivānekatvena cenna virudhyate, natarāṃ kāryagatena | ataśrekasmādapi kāryānekatvasaṃbhavāt na kāryānekatvena kāraṇabhedasiddhirityabhiprāyeṇa sopahāsamāha—api ceti | athaikasmādvahervyatiriktāḥ śaktayaḥ, kathaṃ tasya tadāśrayatvamiti vācyam | tena saha tāsāṃ saṃśleṣastadāśrayatvamiti cet, sa kiṃ tādātmyalakṣaṇaḥ ? uta nairantaryam ? na tāvattādātmyalakṣaṇaḥ, bhinnānāṃ tadayogāt | tādātmye hi tayorekatvameva syāt, na bhedaḥ | nairantarye ca saṃśleṣa āśrayatve, dāhaśaktikavahisaṃnikṛṣṭasya kāṣṭhasyāpi tannairantaryamastīti tadāśrayatvaprasaṅgaḥ | na hi yaddvyasya saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ tattacchakterasaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ; na hi cakṣurdravyasya saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ taddūpasyāsaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ; anyathā rūpopalabdhireva na syāt | evaṃ ca vibhūnāṃ sarvaiḥ śaktimadbhirarthaiḥ saṃnikarṣāt sarvaśaktitvaprasaṅgaḥ | tasmāt śaktīnāṃ kaṃcidupakāraṃ kurvato vaheranekaśaktyāśrayatvaṃ vācyam | sa ca śaktīnāṃ bhedāt pratiśakti bhinnaḥ | tadevamekasmādyānekopakārasiddhiḥ, tathānekakāryasiddhirapi syāditi na kāryabhedāt kāraṇabhedasiddhiriti | tadetatsarvamāha—atheti | idānīṃ prakaraṇārthaṃ saṃkalpyoktvā matāntaramavatārayati dūṣayitum— evamiti | yadeva kāryabhedādivipakṣavyāvṛttimukhena bauddhānāmanumānamuktaṃ tadevānyeṣāmanyathānupapadyamānamarthāpattiriti matvoktam—arthāpadyā veti | anyonyābhāvo hi bhedaḥ ; na ca so'kṣagocaraḥ, bhāvamātragrahaṇayogyatvādindriyāṇām | na ca liṅgagamyaḥ, pramāṇāntarāgocare saṃbandhinyabhāve
[p. 158]
liṅgini, tena saha kasyacillaṅgasyādṛṣṭasaṃgatitvāt | arthāpattirapi payaso dadho janma nānyata iti vyavasthāhetukāḥ sā cetaretarāśrayatvadoṣagrastatvājñānyonyābhāvarūpe bhede pramāṇam | tasmāt bhāvopalambhahetubhyaścakṣurādibhyaḥ pramāṇāntaraṃ pramāṇānutpādalakṣaṇaṃ, vidhāyakādvā bhāvajñānāt pramāṇāntaraṃ vyavacchedakatvena 'nāsti' iti jñānamanyonyābhāvāyāśrayitavyam ; tadadhīnā hi bhāvānāmanyonyamasaṃkarṇiteti ye manyante tān pratyucyate— P. 57-3. anyonyeti | pramāntareti ; pramāśabdaḥ karaṇasādhano bhāvasādhano vā ; tathāca darśitam—'sadupalambhahetubhyaḥ' ityādinā ; viṣayapratiṣedhyayorbhedādvinā 'asmin bhūtale nedaṃ ghaṭādi' 'nāyaṃ ghaṭaḥ paṭaḥ' iti cobhayavidhā niṣedhadhīnaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanvanyonyābhāvo bheda iti pareṇoktam ; sa ca tādātmyābhāvo nādhārādheyābhāvaḥ ; tatra 'nāyamayam' itimātraṃ vācyam ; na 'nāsminnidam' iti | satyam ; na vyartham ; dṛṣṭāntārthaṃ hi tat ! ślokaṃ vyācaṣṭe—neti | khaluśabdaḥ prasiddhidyotanārthaḥ | kuta ityāha— nānapekṣiteti | na hi yatra yo niṣedhyate tāvanapekṣya 'nedamiha' 'nāyamayam' iti niṣedho bhavati ; bhavan vā 'na' ityetāvanmātraṃ syāt ; tataścāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt sarvaśūnyatāmāpādayati ; na bhāvaviśeṣayoranyonyābhāvarūpaṃ bhedamiti 'api cānapekṣitaviṣayaniṣedhyavyavacchede śūnyatā pramāṇārthaḥ' ityatroktam | etacca pareṣāmapi saṃmatamiti gṛhītvā ityādinā darśitam | tadevaṃ viṣayaniṣedhyapratītau siddhārthā yadi sā tayoranavagatabhedayorvastumātratvena syāt, tadā 'idaṃ niṣedhyam, viṣayastvayam' iti vibhāgābhāvānniṣedha eva na syāt | vastumātraṃ hyanavagatabhedaṃ yadi viṣayaḥ, niṣedhyaṃ na syāt | atha niṣedhyaḥ, tato viṣayo na syāt | viṣayābhāve ca 'ghaṭo nāsti' iti ghaṭasyātyantiko niṣedhaḥ syāt, nānyatra siddhasya deśaviśeṣe ; na kvacitsiddhasyātyantiko niṣedho yujyate ; na cātyantamasiddhasya, aprasakte ; etacca 'anyathā daivaniṣiddha kaḥ pratiṣedho'prāptarūpe ?' ityādinoktam | vyavacchedāvadhiḥ vyavacchidyate asminniti viṣayo'bhipretaḥ | atha na vastumātrasya viṣayaniṣedhyatve, kiṃtu vastuviśeṣayoreva yayoḥ kayościdityucyate, tataḥ sarvasya sarvatra niṣedhaḥ syāt, viśeṣā-
[p. 159]
navadhāraṇāt | tasmāt bhinnasvabhāvena viśiṣṭau ca viṣayaniṣedhyau pratipadya pūreṇa niṣedhaḥ pratipattavyaḥ ; evaṃ hi sa saṃbhavet ; anyathā viṣayaniṣedhyayorvibhāgāpratipattau na saṃbhavet | viśiṣṭayośca tayoḥ pratītau kvacideva niṣedhyata iti niyamaḥ syāt | anyathā sarvaḥ sarvatra niṣedhyetetyuktam | kiṃca pratiyogī niṣedhyaḥ ; na cānavagatabhedaḥ sa bhavati ; anyathā svasya svayaṃ sa syāt | yadvā anavagatabhedaḥ sāmānyamātreṇāvagataḥ pratiyogī bhavati | tataḥ sthāṇāvaviśeṣeṇa pratīte puruṣo'pyūrdhvatāsāmānyena pratītaḥ pratiyogī syāt ; atastasyāṃbhāvapratītiḥ syāt | na caivam | ataśca bhinnau niṣedhyaviśeṣau buddhau svīkṛtyābhāvaḥ pratipattavya iti siddham | tathāca loke'pi tatpratītistathaiva pratyātmamupalakṣyate | tathāca sati viṣayaniṣedhyayorbhedajñāne satyabhāvapratītiḥ, tasyāṃ ca satyāṃ tayoranyonyābhāvātmakabhedapratītiritītaretarāśrayatve syādityarthaḥ | idānīmitaretarāśrayatvamucchlokamāha—apiceti | nānupalabdhimātrāt P. 67-20 vastvabhāvajñānam, suṣupte'pi prasaṅgāt ; kiṃtu upalabdhisādhanasākalye satyanupalabdhyā | na hi suṣupte'nupalabdhilakṣaṇaḥ svarūpāvaśeṣaḥ kaścidasti, yasya sāmarthyātiśayāt cakṣurādiriva rūpāderabhāvasya bodhakaḥ ; kiṃtu pramāṇasāmagrīvaikalpena tatrānupalabdhiḥ | avaikalpe tu prameyābhāvamantareṇa tadasaṃbhavānnāntarīyakatvena sā vastvabhāvaṃ gamayati | evaṃ ca dhūmādivat jñātā sā gamikatyāpannam | sā ca dvividheṣṭā—pratiyogyupalabdhirūpeṇātmano'pariṇāmo vā, tato'nyasya vā viṣayasya jñānam ; taduktam—“sātmano'pariṇāmo vā vijñānaṃ vānyavastuni” iti | tatra yadyanyavastujñānamanupalabdhiḥ, tato nāptiddhe vastubhede sā sidhyati | atastajjñānāya vastubhedo jñātavyaḥ | na ca vācyam—niṣedhyajñānādanyat jñānamanupalabdhiḥ ; ato jñānabhedo'tra gamyate, na vastubhedaḥ ; tatkathamucyate ‘nāptiddhe vastubheda’ iti ? | yato jñeyānumeyaṃ tajjñānam, ato bhedo'pi tasya jñeyabhedānumeya ityarthaḥ | jñeyavastubhedo vijñātavyaḥ ; tatastu bhedaṃ vinānyatvātiddherbhede jñāte'nupalabdhijñānam, tasyāṃ ca jñātāyāmanyonyābhāvātmakabhedajñānamitītaretarāśrayatvaṃ syāt | athātmano'pariṇāmo'nupalabdhiḥ, sāpi nātmanaḥ
[p. 160]
sarvavijñānaśūnyatārūpā stimitāvasthā, sarvāpratipattau kasyacinniyatasya paṭāderabhāvāt | tasmāt ghaṭādibhyo bhinne paṭādau jñānapariṇāme satyeva tadvitre ghaṭādau jñānapariṇāmo'bhāvajñānaheturiti vācyam ; na caiṣo'nupalabdherviśeṣo ghaṭapaṭayorbhedajñānāt prāk sidhyati | tataśca pūrvavaditaretarāśrayatvam | nanu pratyakṣeṇaiva bhedo gṛhyate ; tathāhi—nirvikalpakapratyakṣasya sāmānyamātraviṣayatāmapākurvatā vārtikakṛtā “tadayuktam” ityādyuktam | nanu “vastvasaṃkarasiddhiśca tatprāmāṇyamupāśritā” ityapi tenoktam ; bāḍham ; saṃyogasamavāyātmakāsaṃkarābhiprāyaṃ tu tat—iti ye tvāhuḥ, te prāgeva ‘āhurvidhātṛ pratyakṣam’ ityatra nirastāḥ | ye'pi kāṇādā pūrvamāhuḥ—bhūtalādivastugrahaṇapratiyogismaraṇarūpayetikartavyatayā'nugṛhītādakṣāt saṃyuktaviśeṣaṇasaṃnikarṣeṇa labdhaṃ janma yeta tat pratyakṣāntaramabhāvagrāhakam | na ca vācyam—akṣajaṃ taccedviṣayajñānavat prathamameva syāt, na paścāt ; pratiyogismaraṇavyavahite cākṣavyāpāre jātaṃ kathaṃ tadakṣajam ? iti ; yato yathā śabdasmaraṇapekṣitvāt savikalpakaṃ nirvikalpakāt paścādbhāvi, yathā ca śabdasmaraṇavyavadhāne'pi tadanugṛhītādakṣādutpatternānakṣajam ; tathā ‘nāsti’ ityapi jñānaṃ pratiyogismaraṇāpekṣitvāt paścādbhāvi, pratiyogismṛtivyavadhāne'pi ca tadanugṛhītādakṣāpa. 58-14 dutpatteścākṣajamiti, tān pratīdameva vaktavyam—anyonyasaṃśrayāt iti | pramāṇāntaram ; pratyakṣāntaramityarthaḥ | pratyakṣeṇāpi viṣayaniṣedhyau bhedena gṛhītvā tayoranyonyābhāvo grahītavyaḥ ; tataścābhāvagamya iva pratyakṣagamye'pyabhāve tulyamitaretarāśrayatvamityarthaḥ | itaścānyatrānyābhāvajñānāsaṃbhava ityāha—kiñceti | mīyamānamekaṃ sanmātrarūpaṃ yeṣāṃ bhāvānāmiti vigrahaḥ | tadvidyuṇoti—apiceti | yathāpramāṇaṃ hi prameyavyavasthā ; tacca ghaṭapaṭau sanmātrarūpatayā pramiṇoti, na viśeṣarūpatayā ; ataḥ sanmātrasvabhāvāveva tau, na bhinnasvabhāvau ; tataśca na tayoritaretarāśrayatvam ; ataścetaratvenābhimate ghaṭe netarasya paṭasya
[p. 161]
pramānutpādo'nupalabdhiḥ, dvayoḥ sanmātraikarūpatvāt; ekopalabdhau cetarasyāpi tena rūpeṇopalabdhatvāt; ato netaratretarābhāvajñānaṃ saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | atra sattāpahavavādī codayati—nanviti | ekarūpapratyabhijñānāt P 58-22. govyaktiṣu gotvamupeyate | na ca yathaikāṃ gāṃ dṛṣṭavato gavāntare tadasti, tathā sarṣapaṃ dṛṣṭavato merau | tasmānna sattāsāmānyamasti | kathaṃ tarhi atyantabhinneṣvananteṣvekamālambanaṃ vinā 'sat sat' iti śabdapratyayoranuṣṭattiḥ? pācakādivadupādhiyogena bhaviṣyati | kaḥ punarupādhiḥ? pramāṇaviṣayatā | yo hi pramāṇaviṣayaḥ, taṃ lokaḥ 'san' ityācaṣṭe, nātadviṣayaṃ svapuṣpādītyarthaḥ | taddūṣayati—naitaditi | na svalla dvayoranyonyapratiṣedhamantareṇa bhedapratītiḥ; na kevalo vidhisamakālo vidhitaḥ paścādvā pratiṣedhākhyaḥ pramāṇavyāpāraḥ saṃbhavati | na ca vastuvidhirevānyapratiṣedha iti 'labdharūpe kacit' ityādau varṇitam | ato na bhedaḥ pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ | tadyadyabhinnamapi rūpaṃ na gṛhyeta, nirviṣayameva pramāṇaṃ syāt | ato'śeṣabhāvagatamabhinnamekaṃ rūpameṣṭavyam | tacca naḥ sattā | saiva ca pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ; na bhedāḥ | tena bhedā eva pramāṇaviṣayatayā śabdavācyā ityapi pratyuktam | yattu merusarṣapayoḥ 'sat' iti pratyabhijñānaṃ nāstītyuktam, tadasiddham; astyeva hi sadrūpatayā tatrāpi pratyabhijñānam | atha satyapi sadrūpānugame vailakṣaṇyānnetyucyate, tarhi yādṛśamekasyāṃ gavi vyaktaṃ na tādṛśamekāṃ gāṃ dṛṣṭavato gavāntare, vyaktibhedāditi tatrāpi na syāt | athāsti tatra tāvat satyapi vyaktibhede 'sa evāyam' iti pūrvāparānusaṃdhānam, na tu sataḥ sarṣapasya sadantare merau; sattāvādinaḥ 'so'yam' iti pūrvāparānusaṃdhānādekatvam, nābhinnarūpasaṃvittiḥ; tena merusarṣapayoḥ satyabhinnarūpasaṃvidanugame'pi 'so'yam' ityanusaṃdhānābhāvānnaikatvamiti; tadidamayuktam; anusaṃdhānaṃ vināpyabhinnarūpasaṃvittita eva prathamamekavyaktidarśane tadekatvaniścayāt | anusaṃdhānaṃ hi deśakālabhedena punardarśane bhavati; na ca prathamadarśane so'sti | tasmādabhinnarūpasaṃvittita evānekatvaikatvaniścaya iti siddham; tathā mahiṣīmaṇḍalāvyāhṛtāt 'gauḥ' ityabhinnarūpānnirvikalpakapratyayātprathamādeva gomaṇḍale govyaktīnāṃ deśabhede'- 11
[p. 162]
P.59-14. pyekagotvaniścayasyeṣṭatvāt ; taduktam—“nirvikalpaka—” ityādi | na hyatrāpyanusaṃdhānamasti ; taddhi dṛṣṭasya kālabhedena punardarśane bhavati ; gomaṇḍale ca vyaktīnāṃ deśabhede'pi na kālabhedaḥ | tasmādatrāpyabhinnarūpasaṃvittita evābhedādhyavasāyaḥ | api ca yadyanusaṃdhānādekatvam , tato yathā maṇikaṃ dṛṣṭvā maṇikāntare'nusaṃdhānaṃ na tathā śarāvaṃ dṛṣṭvā ; tatra mṛjātirapi na syāt | atha maṇikamiva śarāvamapi dṛṣṭvā astyeva maṇikāntare'nusaṃdhānam ; ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—yanmaṇikayormaṇikāvayavasāmānyānāṃ bhūyastvādutkaṭam , maṇikaśarāvayostu mṛtsāmānyamātrānugamādanutkaṭam ; ato'sti mṛjātiriti ; evaṃ tarhi yathā maṇikaśarāvayoranutkaṭamanusaṃdhānaṃ tataśca mṛjātisiddhiḥ , tathaiva sarṣeṣu dṛṣṭvā merāvapi sarṣeṣatvarūpeṇānanusaṃdhāne'pi sadrūpeṇānutkaṭamanusaṃdhānamasti ; ataḥ sajātirapi siddhā | ataḥ sūktam—‘mīyamānaikarūpeṣu’ iti | etadeva paramatenākṣipya samādhatte—bhedeti | bhidyate iti bhedo viśeṣarūpaṃ vyāvṛttam ; abhedastadviruddhamanuvṛttaṃ sāmānyarūpam ; tadubhayāvabhāsinī ‘san’ iti ‘ghaṭaḥ’ iti dve vijñāne staḥ | ‘san’ iti sarvatrānuvṛttam , ‘ghaṭaḥ’ iti ca paṭādibhyo vyāvṛttam | tathā ‘gauḥ śābaleyaḥ’ ityādyapi draṣṭavyam | yadi tu dve vijñāne neṣyete tato dvirūpo vyavahāro na syāt | asti cāsau ; tathāhi—vatsau mātaramanvicchan sāmānyabuddhyā gogaṇamabhidhāvati , viśeṣabuddhyā ca svamātaram | atastato'nuvṛttavyāvṛttākāre dve jñāne | jñānādhīnā cārthavyavasthā | anyathā hyabhedo'pi tava na syāt ; so'pi jñānabalenaiva tvayābhyupeyate | tasmād dvirūpameva jagat , naikarūpamiti cet ; tanna ; na hi jñānamātrādarthatattvavyavasthā , alātacakrādijñāneṣvabhāvāt | tasmājjātamapi jñānaṃ yuktyā virudhyate , na vā ; ata eva pramāṇotpannaṃ na veti parīkṣyam ; na jñānamityevāstheyam | yadvā yuktyā virudhyate na veti yuktivirodhāvirodhe parīkṣā ; pramāṇotthamiti ca pramāṇasāmarthyāsāmarthyaparīkṣeti viśeṣaḥ | nanu kathaṃ pratyakṣaṃ yuktyā bādhyate ? satyam , na bādhyeta yadi tat pratyakṣaṃ syāt ; pratyakṣābhāsarūpaṃ tu taditi ‘āhurvidhātṛ pratyakṣam’
[p. 163]
ityādāvevāveditam | ataśca svapnadṛśyālātacakrādijñānavat jāgradṛśyālātacakrādijñānaṃ yathā sadapi na taccānugamanimittaṃ tathā bhedajñānamapi | nanu 'na bhedaḥ kevalaḥ, na vidhisahitaḥ, na vidheḥ paścāt pramāṇasya vyāpāraḥ' ityuktanyāyena pramāṇādanyato'pi bhedajñānasyodayo na bhavatītyucyate; ko vānyathāha ? vayamapi bhedamapahuvānā bhedajñānānudayameva brūmaḥ; na hi paramārthato bhedāvagamo'sti; yadi sa syāt, tato nirviṣayasya jñānasyāsaṃbhavāt kathamasmābhirbhedo'pahūyeta ? apahṛve ca prāguktahetubhiḥ kathaṃ bhedāvagamaḥ ? na hyavagamyaṃ vināvagamo ghaṭate, sakarmakatvādavagateḥ | nanu yadi bhedatadavagamau na staḥ, tarhi yadidaṃ jagadbhirbrahmaṃ bhāti tatkimucyate ? avidyā | tāmeva paryāyāntareṇa vyanakti—vibhrama iti; ajñānamavidyā, tadupādāno vā P. 60-7. vibhrama ityarthaḥ | sa cānirvacanīyaḥ; tathāhi—yathāvabhāsaṃ vedyasadbhāve na vibhramaḥ, kiṃtu samyagjñānaṃ syāt | asati ca svapuṣpavadavagamo'nupapannaḥ | tasmādvibhramaḥ sadasaccābhyāṃ niścitya loke nocyate | ato'nirvacanīya evāsau, itthaṃbhūtasyaiva sarvapravādeṣu siddhatvāt | etacca 'sarvapravādibhiścetthamiyamāsyeyā' ityatroktam | uktamapyanirvacanīyatvaprasaṅgāt punaruktam | athavā tatrārthavibhramasyānirvacanīyatvamuktam; iha tu jñānavibhramasyeti viśeṣaḥ | tadā ca jñeyaṃ vinā jñānaṃ nāsti, tat kimidaṃ yatsarvasya bhedajñānamavabhāsate ? iti praśno yojyaḥ | vedyasadbhāve iti cāvivakṣitakarmanirdeśaḥ | avabhāsasadbhāve; jñānasadbhāva ityarthaḥ | tadevaṃ jñānadvaye satyapi kiṃ tadyuktyā virudhyate naveti parīkṣyam | tatra ca bhedajñānaṃ na pramāṇasāmarthyārthamiti 'āhurvidhātṛ' ityatra 'kramaḥ saṃgacchate' ityatra ca yuktyā virudhyata ityāveditamasmābhiḥ | ato na bhedaḥ paramārthata iti bhāvaḥ ! kiṃtu itopi tat jñānadvayaṃ parīkṣyamityāha—apiceti | tāṃ kalpanāmāha—jñānabhedāditi | anuvṛttavyāvṛttajñānabhedāt yathājñānaṃ ca vastuvyavasthiteḥ sāmānyavyaktivibhāgena vastudvayamāhuḥ kāṇādāḥ, ye jātivyaktyoratyantabhinnayoḥ samavāyarūpaṃ saṃsargamāhuḥ | yadvā jñānadvayasya 'san ghaṭaḥ' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyenopapatteḥ, atyantabhede ca tadasaṃbhavāt sāmānya- 114
[p. 164]
viśeṣātmakamekaṃ vastu; yathāhuranekāntavādino bhāṣṭāḥ, ye jātivyaktyorekāntena na bhedo nāpyabheda ityāhuḥ | yadvā vyaktaya eva vastūni; na sāmānyaṃ vastu | jātirūpeṇābhedaḥ | sa ca bhinnābhinnajātyasaṃbhāvāt sarvapuṃsāṃ svābhāvikatvāt vyaktigatātyantikabhedaniścayāsāmarthyādanādidhāsanāvaśāt vyaktyupādāno vyaktiviṣaya eva kalpanājñānagocaraḥ | na cedamadṛṣṭam; dṛṣṭā hi bhinneṣu taruṣu 'vanam' ityabhedakalpaneti bauddhāḥ | ekamadvayaṃ vā taccam | tasya deśakālādyanavacchinnasya ata evānantasya tena rūpeṇāvidyayā niścetumaśakteravidyāyāśrānāditvāt anādayo'napekṣitāgantukakaraṇādidoṣāśrābhedopādānāstatprabhavatvāt tadviṣayāśra tatraivotpattebhedakalpanāḥ | nedamadṛṣṭam; dṛṣṭā hi taraṅgabhedādabhinne candramasi bhedakalpaneti svamatam | tadevaṃ vā ivipratipatteḥ saṃśaye nāparīkṣya taccaniścaya iti satyapyanugrahacyāvṛttākāre jñānadvaye parīkṣā kāryatyarthaḥ | tatra parī| '60-19. kṣāyāṃ prāptāyāmāha—na tāvaditi | na tāvatsāmānyaviśeṣākhyamatyantabhinnaṃ vastudvayam, aikātmyena jātivyaktyorabhedena 'san ghaṭaḥ' iti jñānasyānupapatteḥ | na hyatyantabhinnayorghaṭapadyoriva sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dṛṣṭam | samavāyākhyasaṃbandhakṛtaṃ tadaikātmyajñānamiti cet; tanna; tatrāpi tayo rūpabhedasyāparicyuteranapāyāt saiva bhinnarūpayoraikātmyajñānānupapattirityarthaḥ | etadvyācaṣṭe—neti | na tāvadanugrahacyāvṛttaprakhyayorbhedāt nīlapītavadatyantabhinnau sāmānyaviśeṣau dve vastunī iti yojyam | atra hetumāha— samāneti | saṃbandhasyāpratibhāsanāditi; yatra bhinnayorviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ, tatra matubarthaḥ saṃbandho'vabhāti; yathā—'daṇḍī' iti | na tatheha 'sattāvān ghaṭaḥ' iti tatsaṃbandhasya pratibhāsanamasti; kiṃtu 'san ghaṭaḥ' ityaikātmyasyetyarthaḥ | yadi nāma samānādhikaraṇatveneme prakhye bhavataḥ, tathāpi kimityata āha—na ceti | sāmānyaviśeṣau dve cedvastunī bhinne syātām, tatastayoḥ saṃbandhe satyapi 'san ghaṭaḥ' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na syāt, yataḥ saṃbandhena sāmānyaviśeṣadvayaṃ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyadvayagrāhiṇā buddhidvayena ekayā dā viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaviśeṣyagrāhiṇyā buddhyā 'daṇḍī puruṣaḥ' itivat viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvena gṛhyeta, na punaraikātmyena | iha ca 'san
[p. 165]
ghaṭaḥ' ityekātmyena pratītiḥ sarvajanasaṃvedyā | kiṃca vipratipatteśca sāmānyaviśeṣayorbhedena pratītiḥ syāt ; tataḥ pratītisiddhatvāttayorbhede sāṃkhyā na vipratipadyeran ; vipratipadyante ca ; tasmādito'pi tayoraikātmyena pratītiḥ | api ca tayorbhedena pratītau sāmānyapratyakṣe sati viśeṣapratyakṣābhimāno na syāt ; apitu dhūmadṛṣṭāvivāgrāvavinābhāvāt parokṣābhimānaḥ syāt ; na caivam ; ato'pi tayoraikātmyena pratītiḥ | sā ca tayoḥ saṃbandhe satyapi na syāt ; kiṃtu daṇḍivadviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvena syādityuktam | atra codayati—syāditi | yadi saṃyogādaikātmyapratīti P. 61-2. brūmaḥ, tato daṇḍivat syāt ; vayaṃ tu samavāyāddhama ityarthaḥ | tasya ko viśeṣa ityatrāha—sa hīti | sa hi bhinnayorapi vastunornairantaryarūpatvāttayorbhedaṃ tirodhāyābhedaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ | taddūṣayati—tanneti | hetumāha—samavāye'pīti | samavāye'pi saṃbandhatvādvinnayorbhavati, naikasya | yadi tu tayorbhedo na bhāyāt, tato bhinnāśrayatvātsaṃbandho'pi na bhāyāt | ataḥ samavāye'pi saṃbandhe tayorbhedena bhāvyam, tena samavāyinoḥ svarūpabhedasya tābhyāmanapāyāt | tathāca naikātmyena buddhiryujyate | asti ca sā | atastadviruddhaḥ prakhyābhedaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣavastunorbhedaṃ vyavasthāpayituṃ nālamityarthaḥ | evaṃ prakhyābhedasāmānādhikaraṇyapratyayayorbhedamāśritya pūrvasya pareṇa viruddhatvamuktam | yadā tvekaiva prakhyā dve vastunī samānādhikaraṇatvena bodhayati, tadāha—svayamiti | svayaṃ vā vyāhatatvāt prakhyābhedo na vastubhedavyavasthāyai bhavati | vyāhatatve hetumāha—eketi | sāmānyena prakhyābhedasya sāmānādhikaraṇyenotpatterityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yadyanuvṛttavyāvṛttaprakhyā ghaṭe paṭaprakhyeva vaiyadhikaraṇyena syāt, tato na svayaṃ vyāhatā syāt ; yadā tu sāmānyaviśeṣau na bhedena darśayati, tadā viruddhabhedābhedopadarśitvāt svayameva vyāhateti | atra codayati—nanviti | atrottaramāha—bhedeti | tasyārthaḥ—atha sāmānyaviśeṣayoratyantabhede'pi bhedatirodhānasāmarthyaṃ samavāyasyābhyupeyate, evaṃ tarhi ekasyaivātmanastasāmarthyaṃ kiṃ nābhyupeyate ? yataḥ sāmarthyaviśeṣāt nānārūpeṇa tadavabhāsata iti | vṛttirnigadavyākhyātā |
[p. 166]
P. 61-16. na hyasti viśeṣa ityuktam; tatra codayati—nanviti | agnyayoguḍādiṣu saṃsargasya bhinnayoragnilohaguḍakayorbhedatirodhānasāmarthyaṃ dṛṣṭam; tathāhi—‘agnirayam’ iti lohaguḍakaṃ pratiyanti | tena dṛṣṭatvāttatrābhyupeyate; naikasya nānāvabhāsasāmarthyam, adarśanāditi paroktaviśeṣamāśaṅkaya siddhāntī punaraviśeṣamāha—dṛṣṭa iti | bhinnayorbhedatirodhānasāmarthyaṃ nāma dharmaḥ saṃsargasya dṛṣṭa ityasti viśeṣa iti yadyucyeta, tatrocyeta—nāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ; ekamapi hi citraṃ rūpamekā ca buddhirnānāvabhāsate yatra ityarthaḥ | tadvivṛṇoti—dṛṣṭamiti | na ca vācyaṃ citraṃ nāma paṭasya rūpāntaraṃ nāstyeva, tantūnāmeva tu nīlapītādirūpāṇi samāhitāni parasparamilitāni citramityucyate iti; yato nīlādirūpāṇāṃ tantuṣu samavetatvāt paṭastarhi nīrūpaḥ syāt | nīrūpatve ca rūpalakṣaṇasya viṣayasaṃskārātiśayaviśeṣasyābhāvādvāyuvadacākṣuṣaḥ syāt | svayamarūpasyāpi paṭasya rūpiṣu tantuṣu samavāyādekatvādisaṃkhyāgatyādikarmaṇoriva cākṣuṣatvaṃ syāditi cet; tanna; yasmādevamuktaṃ vaiśeṣikatantre—‘mahati dravye tryaṇukādau viṣaye yā upalabdhiścākṣuṣī sā anekadravyasamavāyāt rūpācca’ iti | tatra paramāṇoḥ rūpavato'pi cākṣuṣatvanivṛttyarthamanekadravyasamavāyādityuktam | anekatvaṃ cātra bahutvamabhipretam; tena dvyaṇukasyāpyacākṣuṣatvamuktaṃ bhavati | anekadravyasamavetasyāpi vāyoścākṣuṣatvanivṛttyarthaṃ rūpādityuktam | yadvā anekadravyasamavāyāditi mahattvopalakṣaṇam; tena mahattvādityarthaḥ | tatra mahattvena paramāṇudvyaṇukayornivṛttiḥ, rūpādityanena ca vāyavāderiti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—guṇakarmādernīrūpasyāpi dravyasamavāyācākṣuṣatvam, na dravyasya; tasya tu rūpādeva cākṣuṣatvam | ataḥ paṭasya citrarūpānabhyupagame tasyācākṣuṣatvaprasaṅga iti | kiṃca svayamarūpasyāpyasti ceccākṣuṣatvam, tato ghaṭāderapi rūpistvāvayavasamavāyena cākṣuṣatvopagateḥ svayamarūpatvaṃ syāt | atha dravyasyārūpasya cākṣuṣatvaṃ neṣṭamiti ghaṭādernīrūpatvaprasaṅgaḥ, tataścitre'pi paṭe tathaiva syāditi so'pi nārūpaḥ | ato'sti citraṃ rūpam | taccaikamapi nīlādiprabhedenāvabhāsata ityekasya nānāvabhāsaḥ | tathā nagarabuddhetvailokyabuddheśca bhedābhāve'pi svata ekatve'pi gṛhaprāsādādibhedena nānāva-
[p. 167]
bhāsitvaṃ dṛṣṭam | paratantre'pyetatsiddhamiti darśayituṃ viṣayākāra ityādyuktam | ca ca vācyaṃ buddherākārā nānāvabhāsante, na buddhiḥ; ato naikaṃ nānāvabhāsata iti; yasmādekabuddhitattvāvyatirekātparasparamapi teṣāṃ tattvato bhedo nāsti, api tu bhrāntiḥ | kimuta buddheḥ sakāśāt | tasmādbuddhireva nānāvabhāsata iti | buddhyavyatirekaśca pareṣāṃ svatantre siddhaḥ | atha matam—yeṣu nīlapītādayo guṇāḥ samavetāḥ, teṣveva paṭāvayavī; tatra citraguṇasya samavāyo yaḥ, sa eva nīlādibhiḥ saha citraguṇasyetyekārthasamavetasamavāyalakṣaṇaḥ saṃsargaḥ | tata eva nīlādigataṃ nānātvaṃ citre pratibhāti | tenaiṣo'pi saṃsargasya mahimā | atastanmahimaiva jātivyaktī bhinne apyabhinne pratibhāsyata iti cettatrāha—na ceti | P. 62-7. saṃbandhapūrvakaḥ saṃbandhaḥ saṃsargo na bhavati; kutaḥ? vyavadhānādityarthaḥ | nanu vyavadhāne'pi saṃsargo bhavati; yathā—dravyasaṃyuktasya cakṣuṣo rūpeṇetyata bhāha—saṃśleṣo hīti | saṃśleṣo hi saṃsargaḥ; tasmācca parasyāyogolakasya parasyāgre rūpeṇāvabhāso bhavati; na yataḥ kutaścit saṃbandhāt | sa ca saṃśleṣo'tyantamavyavadhāne yujyate; naikavyavadhāne'pi | tantugatasya nīlādirūpasya paṭagatasya citrarūpasya ca tantupaṭadvayena vyavadhānāstutarāṃ saṃśleṣo na yujyata ityarthaḥ | vyartho ceyaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣayoratyantabhedakarupanetyāha—kiṃcetti | ‘gāṃ śuṅkāmānaya’ iti jātiguṇopādhau vyavahāre vyavahārāṅgabhūtā sarvā dhīrte yathārthā yadā, tadā sāmānyaviśeṣayorbhāvayorbhedakarupanā vyarthetyarthaḥ | tadvyācaṣṭe—yasyeti | yasya sāmānyaviśeṣayoratyantabhedavādinaḥ ‘śuṅko gauḥ’ ityādyā buddhayo vyavahārāṅgabhūtā dravyeṣu guṇajātyupadhānena tadanurañjanena tadvyāvāpatyā tadabhedena bhavanti, tasya tā buddhayo na yathāvastu; anyathābuddhaya ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ? jātiguṇābhyāṃ dravyasyārthāntaratvāt bhedāt; bhinnaṃ vastu; buddhayaścābhedena; ato na yathāvastu tā ityarthaḥ | tulyanyāyatvādguṇopanyāsaḥ, vyāvṛttabuddhipradarśanārtho vā | tataḥ kimityāha—tathā ceti; tathā sati bhinnavastunibandhano vyavahāro na syādityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—
[p. 168]
P. 62-16 buddhyeti | jñātaṃ hi vyavahārāṅgaṃ bhavati, nājñātam | ajñātaṃ ca bhinnaṃ vastu; tato na vyavahārāṅgamityarthaḥ | tathābhāvāditi; vyavahārāṅgatvadityarthaḥ | tathāpi kimityāha—evaṃ ceti | satyapi jātiguṇabhyāṃ dravyāṇāṃ bhede, buddhimātraparigatena jātiguṇabhyāmabhinnaivārthena 'gauḥ' ityanuvṛttaḥ 'śuklaḥ' iti vyāvṛtto vyavahāro hṛśyate; ato vastubhedasya vyavahārānupayogādabhedasyaiva ca tadupayogāt vyartho vastubhedābhyupagama ityarthaḥ | itaśca vyarthaḥ—yato vyāvahārikabuddhīnāṃ yathārthatvāya vastubhedaḥ kalpyate; tasmin kalpite'pi 'śuklaḥ' ityādivyāvahārikabuddhīnāṃ jātiguṇopādhitvāt tadabhedenotpatternāyathārthatvaparihāraḥ; ato vyathā vastubhedakalpanetyarthaḥ | parastu vastubhedakalpanāyāḥ sārthatvamāha—syāditi | sthāṇau puruṣabuddhiḥ śuklau rajatabuddhirūddhvatārūpasāmānyahetukā dṛṣṭā | tasmāt 'gauḥ' śuklaḥ' ityabhedabuddherayathārthāyā api hetunā bhāvyam | yadi ca dve vastunī syātāṃ tataste sāmānādhikaraṇyatayā pratibhātaḥ | nāsati vastubhede anuvṛttavyāvṛttarūpagrāhi vyāvahārikamayathārthamabhedajñānaṃ syāt; hetvabhāvāt; ato vastubhedakalpanāyā vyavahārānaṅgatve'pi vyavahārāṅgabhūtā yathārthasāmānādhikaraṇyabuddhibījatvānna vyarthatvamityarthaḥ | taddūṣayati—ucyata iti | nirbījā cenna bhrāntiḥ, avidyābījā bhaviṣyati; alaṃ vastubhedakalpanayetyarthaḥ | evaṃ saṃsargavādino mataṃ dūṣayitvā anekāntavādino mataṃ dūṣayitumavatārayati—astviti | sāmānyaviśeṣātmakamekaṃ vastvastvityarthaḥ | asya matasya pūrvasmādatyantabhedapakṣācadgatadoṣābhāvena viśeṣamāha—evamiti | sāmānyaviśeṣayoratyantabhede 'gaurayam, śuklo'yam' ityaikātmyaprakhyā nāvakalpata ityuktam; tattāvadiha nāsti, vastunaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣātmakatvenābhedapratipacyupapatteḥ | yadapi samavāyākhyasaṃbandharūpasyārthasya bhedāntardhānasāmarthyaṃ kalpitam, tacca saṃbandhavastuno bhedaviṣayatvāt viparītabhinnayorahetutvādvā vastuviparītam; tadapi na bhavati ekasyādhyātmatvena vastunaḥ svayamevābhedāt | tathā vastuno dvyātmakatve
[p. 169]
‘gauḥ śuklaḥ’ ityādivyavahāro vastunibandhano bhavati ; tena ‘vyavahāre paropādhau’ iti vyāvahārikajñānānāṃ yadayathārthatvamuktaṃ tadapi na bhavati | tasmātsādhurayaṃ pakṣa ityastu sa eva | virodhamāśaṅkayati—syāditi | P. 63-7. virodhamāha—ekamiti | ekaṃ tadviparītamanekamityarthaḥ | prakārāntareṇa virodhāt dvayorekatvasya duravagamatvamāha—api ceti | tatpariharati—neti | yat pramāṇena pratīyate tadaviruddham, yathā rasabati rūpam ; tato'nyadviruddham, yathoṣṇe śaityam | tasmādyadyathā pramāṇena pramīyate tattathaiva yuktam | tatra pramāṇamanāhatya cedvirodho'bhidhīyate, tato virodhāvirodhavyavasthā nirhetukaiva syāt | tasmādyat pramāṇena pramīyate tadaviruddham ; pramīyate caikaṃ vyātmakam ; tathā hyanuvṛttivyāvṛttirūpeṇa buddhibhedāt vyātmakatvam, sāmānādhikaraṇyena cāvagamādekatvamityarthaḥ | tadevamanekāntavādimatamavatārya dūṣayati—tadidamiti | hetumāha—yat iti | vyātmakavastujñānamapramāṇam, vipratiṣiddhārthatvāt, saṃśayajñānavadityarthaḥ | svarūpaviparyayo'tra vipratiṣedhaḥ, na sahānavasthānam ; sa cāstyekatvānekatvayoriti na hetvasiddhirāśaṅkyā | yadi tvekasya dvyātmakatvajñānaṃ vipratiṣiddhārthamapi pramāṇaṃ syāt, tatastulyatvāt saṃśayajñānamapi pramāṇaṃ syāt ; tataśca saṃśayyamāno'pyartho dvyātmā syāt ; na tvetadiṣṭamityabhiprāyeṇāha—anyatheti | atra hetumāha—dvyātmeti | yathā ‘gaurayam’ iti jñānamanuvṛttavyāvṛttākāram, tathā saṃśayajñānamapi sthāṇupuruṣadvyātmakākāram ; yathā ca tatra sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam, tathā saṃśaye'pi tadākārayoḥ sthāṇupuruṣayoḥ | ato dvyātmakavastutulyatvāt saṃśayaviṣayo'pi dvyātmā syādityarthaḥ | atrātulyatvamāśaṅkayati—atheti | yathā sāmānyaviśeṣavatyarthe ‘gauśrāyaṃ śāntyeyaśrāyam’ iti samuccayapratītiḥ, na tathā saṃśaya ityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—mā bhūditi | para āha—so'pīti | na hyekameva vastu pakṣe sthāṇuḥ pakṣe puruṣa ityupapadyate, tasya vyavasthitaikarūpasya svahetuta utpatteḥ ; taduktam—“na hi vastu vikalpyate” iti | siddhāntī tu yadi vastuno vikalpo'nupapanna iti neṣyate, ekasyāpi tarhi dvyātmatā
[p. 170]
virodhādanupapanneti sāpi na syādityarthaḥ | atrāpi svarūpaviparyayo virodhaḥ, na sahānavasthānam, tasya sādhyatvāt | tadevaṃ sati yajñavati P. 63-20. tadāha—tatreti | aikādhikaraṇyādityasyānantaram 'apramāṇam' ityanuvartanīyam | nanu saṃśayajñānaṃ na virodhādapramāṇam; api tu sthāṇupuruṣayoranyataranirṇayajñānena bādhanāt | tacca sāmānyaviśeṣapratīternāsti | ataḥ satyapi virodhe bādhakābhāvāt sā pramāṇameva | ataḥ saṃśayavadvipratiṣedhāt sāmānyaviśeṣajñānamapramāṇamityuktamayuktamityabhiprāyeṇa codayati—syāditi | siddhāntī tvāha—yatreti | yadi saṃśayasya nirṇayena bādhanānna prāmāṇyam, evaṃ tarhi yatra pāśupatanikhātapṛthivīmadhyopalakṣaṇakīlakanirdeśāt 'madhyam, amadhyaṃ vā' iti saṃśayastatraikasya vikalpyamānadvyātmataiva syāt; na caivamiṣṭam | ato virodhādevāprāmāṇyam | evaṃ cet tato'satyapi bādhane sāmānyaviśeṣapratīteḥ saṃśayavadvirodhādapramāṇyamiti sūktamityarthaḥ | evaṃ viruddhātmatvena sāmānyaviśeṣajñānasya saṃśayavadpramāṇyamuktam | idānīmekatvānekatvajñānayorviruddhārthayorekenaikanirākṛtau na dvyātmatā; parasparanirākṛtau tu saṃśayatulyatvamityāha—api ceti | rajatajñānasya śuktijñānena bādhāyāmekasya rajatajñānasya śuktijñānena balīyasā nirākaraṇāt bādhanādaprāmāṇyaṃ yathā, tathā ekatvānekatvajñānayorvirodhe'pi dvayorekasya vā jñānasyāprāmāṇyam | kutaḥ? viruddhayorekānekatvarūpayormahaṇe viruddharūpa grāhiṇorjñānayoranyānyanirākaraṇāt | tatra yadyekaṃ balavat tato rajatādijñānavadekasya nirākaraṇe parasmāt śuktijñānādiva niścayaḥ; tatraikameva vā vastu syāt, dvyātmakaṃ vā; na tvekaṃ dvyātmakaṃ ca | viruddhāvyabhicārihetudvayavat tulyabalayoḥ parasparanirākṛtau tu bādhāyāmekatvadvyātmakatvayorniścayābhāvādāpannaṃ saṃśayatulyatvaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣapratipatteḥ | tasmādekāntena naikaṃ nāpyanekaṃ vastu, kiṃtu ekaṃ dvyātmakaṃ cetyetadvāhi jñānaṃ suniścitamiti svapakṣavarṇanamātramidam; na bhūtaṃ tathetyarthaḥ | itaśca naikaṃ dvyātmakamityāha—api ceti | ślokaṃ vyācaṣṭe— avaśyamityādi | avaśyamid jātivyaktyādipadārthānāṃ bhedābhedavyavasthāṃ
[p. 171]
kurvatānena sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ pratyekaṃ bhedābhedayorlakṣaṇaṃ tāvadavaśyaṃ vācyam | pratyekaṃ ca tayoḥ rūpavirodhāvirodhāvanyonyābhāvatadviparyayarūpau bhedābhedalakṣaṇe ; yato rūpavirodhādanyonyābhāvarūpādviśeṣāṇāmanyonyabhedaḥ, tadviparyayācca teṣu bhinneṣu sāmānyamabhinnam | tatraitādṛśi bhedābhedalakṣaṇe yadi sāmānyaviśeṣayorapi virodhā'nyonyābhāvarūpaḥ, tato viśeṣāṇāmiva nānyonyabhekatvam | atha sāmānyaviśeṣabuddhyoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyenotpattiḥ, ato na tau viruddhatvabhāvāvityavirodhena tayorekatvamucyate ; tato rūpavirodhasya bhedalakṣaṇasyābhāvādasati bhede bhedādhiṣṭhānadvitvābhāvādyathā sāmānyabhāgo viśeṣabhāgaśrāntirbhaktaḥ pratyekamavāntaravibhāgarahito na dvirūpaḥ, tathā sāmānyaviśeṣayorapi rūpavirodhābhāvādasati bhede bhedādhiṣṭhānadvitvābhāvāt naikaṃ dvyātmakam | sāmānādhikaraṇyabuddheriti rūpavirodhābhāve hetuḥ | P. 64-15. yadītyādinā cāyabhevārthaḥ saṃkṣipya spaṣṭīkṛta iti | atha matam—yadi rūpavirodhādanyonyābhāvalakṣaṇānnānātvaṃ tadviparyayācaikatvaṃ syāt, tataḥ sāmānyaviśeṣayo rūpavirodhābhāvādanyatarāṃśavat dvitvaṃ na syāt ; na caivam ; kiṃtu yatra dvirūpā buddhistatra nānātvam, yatra caikarūpā tatraikatvam | ataśca sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ 'gaurayam' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratīterasatyapyanyonyābhāvalakṣaṇavirodhe'dvirūpabuddhivedyatvādekatvam ; ato vastuno dvirūpabuddhivedyatvāt dvyātmateti ; tatrocyate—aikānyeti | aikānyabuddhirekarūpā buddhiḥ ; tataḥ sāmānyabhāge viśeṣabhāge cedekatvam, evaṃ tarhyasāvekarūpā buddhirvastuni dvirūpe nāsti cet tataḥ kuta ekatvam ? athāsti, tata ekarūpabuddhibodhyatvādaṃśavadekatvaṃ syāt ; dvyātmatā kathamityarthaḥ | dṛttirvyaktārthā | atha matam—buddhirūpabhedaḥ parasparānātmatvaṃ ca dvayamapi bhedalakṣaṇam | tatra gavāścādīnāṃ dvayamapyastīti bhinnatvameva | sāmānyaviśeṣayostu satyapi buddhirūpabhede sāmānādhikaraṇyabuddhyā parasparātmatvena pratīteḥ parasparānātmatvaṃ nāstīti na bhedaḥ | ato naikatvavidhāta iti ; tadapi na ; yato yadi sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ parasparātmatvena pratīteḥ parasparānātmatā nāsti, evamapi
[p. 172]
tayoḥ samuccitena bhedalakṣaṇena virahādeśavat dvyātmatā na syādityuktaṃ 'dvyātmatā katham' ityatra | P. 65-9 atha matam—buddhirūpābhedaḥ parasparātmapratipattiśca dve pratyekamabhedalakṣaṇe ; tadubhayābhāvaśca samuccito bhedalakṣaṇamiti | tataḥ sāmānyāṃśasya viśeṣāṃśasya ca buddhirūpābhedādabhedalakṣaṇāt pratyekamekatvam | ubhayostu sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ parasparātmatvapratipatterabhedalakṣaṇādanyonyamekatvam | viśeṣāṇāṃ punarabhedalakṣaṇadvayābhāvāt bheda eva | tataśca sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ svarūpato dvitve'pi pūrvoktābhedalakṣaṇādekatvamapīti siddhaṃ dvyātmakamekaṃ vastrivati | tadayuktam ; yasmādevamapi parasparātmatvapratipattāvabhedalakṣaṇe sati ata eva cobhayābhāvarūpe bhedalakṣaṇe cāvidyamāne, sāmānyaviśeṣayodvyātmatā na syāt | kiṃca naikasyābhedasya buddhirūpābhedaparasparātmatvapratītisaṃjñake dve lakṣaṇe yukte | kutaḥ ? yasmāddhakṣaṇābhedaḥ sarvatra lakṣyeṣvamedaṃ vyavasthāpayati ; yathā—anumānalakṣaṇābhedaḥ sarvānumānānāmanumānatvābhedam ; lakṣaṇabhedaśca tadbhedaṃ vyavasthāpayati yathānumānārthāpatyoḥ | tatra lakṣaṇabhedādabhedasya lakṣyasya nānātvaṃ yuktam ; tathā satyapyabhedasya lakṣyasya yadi tattvamābhinnatvam, tadā lakṣaṇabhedābhedayoratantratvāt tadabhede nānātvamapi syāt ; tato lakṣaṇaṃ vyarthameva syāt | itiḥ samāptau | itaśca sāmānyaviśeṣayorekatvamayuktamityāha—api ceti | yadi tayorabhedaḥ syāt, tata ekabuddhacaivetarasya gṛhītatvāt tayoranyatarasmin gṛhīte paragrāhiṇo buddhacantarasya vilakṣaṇaviṣayasya vyarthatvamāpatet ; yathā—sāmānyāṃśe viśeṣāṃśe vā svabuddhyā gṛhīte tadvyāhṛṇāya buddhacantarasya | atha bhinnābhinnatvānnānātvamapi tayorastīti naikagrahaṇe'parasya grahaṇamiti buddhacantarasyārthavattocyate, tata ekatvamastīti vyarthatā kiṃ na syādityarthaḥ | bhedeṣviti ; viśeṣeṣvityarthaḥ | yathā sāmānyaviśeṣabhāgayoriti ; yathā sāmānyāṃśe viśeṣāṃśe vā svabuddhyā gṛhīte na tadvyāhṛṇāya vilakṣaṇaṃ buddhacantaramarthyate tathā sāmānye gṛhīte viśeṣagrahaṇāya na buddhacantaramarthyate, tayorabhedāt | evaṃ viśeṣe'pi gṛhīte na sāmānya ityarthaḥ | idānīmabhedādekayā buddhyobhayorbhrahaṇameva yuktam, na nānātvādagrahaṇa-
[p. 173]
mityāha—autsargika iti | bījābhāve'ṅkurasya prāgabhāvaḥ siddho hetu-P. 66-2. nirapekṣo'nāditvāt | tasyāṅkuraprāgabhāvasya bījabhāve'ṅkurabhāvo'pavādaḥ ; tena hi vyāvartyata ityarthaḥ | evaṃ sāmānyenoktvā prakṛte yojayati— tatreti | tatra sāmānyaviśeṣayorabhede'dhigamahetoḥ sadbhāvādekayaiva buddhyā tayoradhigamasya phalasyāpavādabhūtasya bhāvo yuktaḥ, nautsargikasyobhayādhigamākhyaphalābhāvasya | ekatve hi tayorya ekādhigamahetuḥ, asti sa evetarasyāpīti naikatve'nyatarādhigamaheturnāstīti śakyaṃ vaktum | ata ubhayādhigamābhāvamautsargikamapodyobhayādhigamenāpavādabhūtena bhāvyamityarthaḥ | atha bhedo'pyastītyekatvābhāvādanyatarādhigamasya phalasya heturnāsti, tena hetvabhāvādautsargika ubhayādhigamaphalābhāva ityucyate ; tathāpyasti tāvadubhayādhigamahetuḥ, tayorekatvādekasyāpi vidyamānatvādityarthaḥ | tataḥ kimityata āha—tatreti | ubhayādhigamahetorekatve sati bhāvādubhayādhigamaphalasya bhāva ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha—tasyeti ; tasya hetubhāvasya kāraṇatvādityarthaḥ | nanu tayorbhedādanyatarādhigamahetorabhāvo'pyastīti tataḥ kimiti yathoktaphalabhāvo na syādityāśaṅkyāha—na tviti | na tu hetvabhāvaḥ | phalaprāgabhāvasya kāraṇam, abhāvayorhetuphalabhāvādarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | kathaṃ tarhyasau syādityata āha—svabhāveti | svabhāvasiddho hi phalaprāgabhāva autsargikaḥ ; nāsau hetumapekṣata ityarthaḥ | tasyāpavādamāha— tasyeti | tataḥ kimityata āha—sa ceti | sa ca sāmānyaviśeṣayorekatvāddheturubhayoradhigamāyāsti | tenaikabuddhyetarasyāpi buddhatvāt tayorabhede buddhyantaravaiyarthyaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ | yadyapi tayorbhede heturnāsti, tathāpyabhede tāvadastīti tāvacchabdaḥ | nanu bhedo nāstyapi ; ato hetvabhāvāt phalābhāvaḥ kiṃ na syādityata āha—asaccamiti | abhāvasyākāraṇatvādityarthaḥ | itiḥ samāptau | vyātmakamekaṃ varityati pakṣe dūṣaṇāntaramāha—api ceti | na sāmānyamātraṃ dvyātmakasya vastunaḥ ātmā svarūpam ; na viśeṣamātram, vastudvayaprasaṅgāt ; kiṃtu citraḥ śabdaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣābhayarūpaḥ sa vastuna
[p. 174]
ātmā kharūpam | tasya ca tādṛśavastvantare'nanvayādanugamābhāvāt dvyātmakamekaṃ vastviti śabdāntareṇātyantabhedavāda evāśrita ityarthaḥ | śabdāntarāditi lyablepi hetau vā pañcamīti vyākhyātam | P. 66-16. nanu sāmānyāṃśasya vyavatyantare'nuvṛtternātyantabhedavādaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkyāha—anuganturiti | vyaktiṣvanugantuḥ sāmānyamātrasya dvyātmavastupakṣe na vastusvabhāvatvam; ataścāvastvetat kalpitarūpamiti na tadanugame vastuno'nugama iti bhedaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | atra codayati—syāditi | yadyapi na vastu; vastvaṃśatvaṃ tu na sāmānyasya vāryate, anyathā dvyātmataiva na syāt; ato na tatkalpanāviṣeyamiti na bhedavādaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | yadi tu tanneṣyate tato nirvījāvakalpanāpi na syādityāha— anyathati | siddhāntī tvāha—ka iti | bhedaprasaṅga iti | sāmānyaviśeṣau dve vastunī syātāmityarthaḥ | nāpyaṃśo vastuno'nyat svayaṃ vastvityāha—nāpāti | atha na vastvevāṃśaḥ, nāpi vastuno'nyaḥ, svayaṃ vastvityucyate, tataḥ pariśeṣāt kalpanāviṣaya eva syāt; taccānyatvābhyāṃ vastuno bhinnābhinnatvādanirvācyo yata ityarthaḥ | atra paraśrodayati—nanviti | nāṃśaḥ aṃśino'tyantabhinno nāpyabhinna ityarthaḥ | atra hetumāha—ubhayatheti | bhede'pyaṃśo na bhavati, yathā ghaṭasya na paṭaḥ; nābhede'pi, yathā ghaṭasya ghaṭaḥ | ato nāṃśaḥ aṃśino bhinno nāpyabhinna ityarthaḥ | nanu tattvātaccābhyāmanirvācyatvādavastu tarhītyāśaṅkyāha—nāpīti | ata eveti; aṃśatvādeva; na hyavastunaḥ aṃśatvaṃ dṛṣṭam; na hi svapuṣpādyavastu kasyacidaṃśo bhavatītyathaḥ | tataḥ kimityata āha—tatreti | tasya tadavyatirekādityabhedābhiprāyam | taddūṣayati—naitaditi | yadi sāmānyāṃśasya viśeṣāntare'nugamādvastuno'nugama ucyate, tato viśeṣāṃśānanugamādananugamaḥ kasmānna syādityarthaḥ | nanu sāmānyāṃśena vastuno'nugamo'pyastīti nātyantabhedavādaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkya vastuno'nanugamamāha—api ceti | vastunaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣadvayarūpatvādekasya viśeṣāṃśasya vyavatyantareṣvabhāve vastunasteṣvabhāvāt sāmānyānugame'pi na
[p. 175]
vastuno'nugama ityarthaḥ | etadeva sādhayati—tathā hīti | samāhāraḥ P. 67-2. samuccayaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | evaṃ viśeṣāṃśānugamādvedavādaprasaṅgamāpādya adhunā sāmānyāṃśānugamāmyupagame satyabhedavādaṃ prasaṅgayati—yadi veti | sāmānyāṃśānugamāttasya vastvavyatirekādvastvanugame'bhyupagamyamānaṃ sati, advaitavādaḥ prasajyeta ; tathā hi—gaustāvat svayaṃ gauḥ, so'śvasya dravyatvādisāmānyāṃśānugamāt aśvavastuno'pyanugamādaśvo'pi syāt ; tataśca gaurgauraśvaḥ ; evamaśvo'pyaśvo gauśca ; tataśca gorgautvamaśvatvaṃ ca, aśvasyāpyaśvatvaṃ gotvaṃ ceti so'yaṃ tadbhāvo'nyabhāvaḥ, tābhyāṃ gavāśvayorabhedaḥ | evamanyeṣāmapi bhāvānām | ato vacanavyaktyantareṇādvaitamevoktamityarthaḥ | tadbhāvānyabhāvābhyāmiti ‘tadatattvataḥ’ ityasya vivaraṇam | etāvatā ca tadatattvāṃ pratyekaṃ tvasya saṃbandhaḥ, pañcamyantāttasilityuktam | taccātacca tadatadī, tadatadorbhāvastadatattvam, tata iti vyutpattiḥ | nanu satyamadvaitam ; kiṃtu bhāvānyabhāvābhyāmabheda iti tvayaivoktam ; ataśca gauraśvasyābhāvaścet tato dvaitamapyasti ; tena dvaitādvaitātmakaṃ sarvam ; tacceṣṭamevāsmākamityabhiprāyeṇa codayati—satyamiti | siddhāntī tvāha—naitaditi | lokasiddhamanyatvamabhipretya parapratipādanārthamanyābhāva ityasmābhiruktam | taccatastu gavāśvayoranyonyamabhede kuto'nyatvam ? yato dvaitamapi syādityarthaḥ | nanu gavāśvayorabhede ‘gauścāśvaśca’ iti bhedenābhidhānaṃ na syāt ; ato bhedo'pyastītyāśaṅkyāha—gauśceti | abhidhānabhedo'pi lokasiddha eva parapratipādanārthamuktaḥ ; na tu gavāśvayoḥ pratyekaṃ gavāśvatvena tasya kāraṇamastītyarthaḥ | nanu yadyapi sāmānyāṃśānugamādabhedaḥ, tathāpi viśeṣāṃśasya vyāvṛtterbhedo'pyasti ; ato dvaitādvaitātmakaṃ jagat syāt ; tacceṣṭamevāsmākamiti codayati—nanviti | siddhāntī tu dūṣayitumidaṃ vikalpayati—atheti | ubhayarūpaṃ vastu ; ato'sya viśeṣavyāvṛttito vyāvṛttatvāt viśeṣāntare'nugamo nāsti ; tadudāharati—aśvavastu govastu na bhavatīti | athāsti vastuno vastvantare'nugamaḥ, tadudāharati—aśvo'pi gaurbhavatīti | tatrādye
[p. 176]
P. 67-19. pakṣe doṣamāha—tatreti | tatra tayoḥ pakṣayormadhye vastuno vastvantarānanugamapakṣe tāvat vastuno vastvantare'nanugamādanugacchataḥ sāmānyāṃśasya kevalasya svayamavastutvāt prāguktanyāyena kalpanāgocaratvāt bhedavādaprasaṅga ityuktaṃ ‘tasyānanvayataḥ’ ityatra | atha viśeṣavyāvṛttau satyāmapi tadvastu vastvantare'nugatam, tadudāharati—bhavatīti | tatra gavāśvayorabheda eva, kuto'nyatvasiddhirityarthaḥ | yadi matam—divyatvādirūpeṇāśvo govastu ; ananugataṃ ca viśeṣarūpeṇa, ato ‘nātyantabhedasyābhedasya vā prasaṅga iti ; ‘bhavati cāśvo gauḥ’ ityaśvasya gorūpatvaṃ vadatā dravyatvādinānugatatvamuktam ; ‘gaurgaureva’ iti gorgorūpeṇāśve'nanugatatvamuktam | kathaṃ punagorgorūpeṇāśvādyāvṛttatvamityata āha—bhavatyeveti | yadyaśvasya gobhāve gorviśiṣṭaṃ svaṃ gorūpaṃ na bhavet, tato'śvānna vyāvartate ; yatastu bhavatyeva, na bhavati tatra gavi govastunastasya svarūpābhāva iti yāvat, tadā tasya svarūpeṇa vyāvṛttatvādanyatvasiddhiriti | ‘yadi matam’ ityādinā paramatamāśaṅkya vikalpya dūṣayati—atheti | athetyākṣepe ; yadvovastu svarūpeṇāśvādyāvṛttaṃ tatkathaṃ kiṃprakāramiti pareṇa vaktavyam | kiṃ yadvaurbhavatyevāśvarūpeṇa ? na bhavatyapi ? etaduktaṃ bhavati—kiṃ gaurasādhāraṇagorūpa eva, utāśvarūpo'pi ? yadi gaurgorūpa eva, tato'śvo'pyaśvarūpa eveti bhedavādaḥ | atha gauraśvarūpo'pi tato gaurgavāśvarūpo'pi, aśvo'pi gavāśvarūpa ityabhedavādaḥ | nanvevamabhede satyapi viśeṣāntareṇāśvādanyatvaṃ govastu vyavasthāpayiṣyāmaḥ ; ataśca bhedo'pyastītyāśaṅkya prakṛtaṃ bhedābhedavādaprasaṅgaṃ saṃkṣepeṇapapādayati— sarvatheti | evaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣātmakamekaṃ vastviti mataṃ dūṣayitvā dravyaparyāyātmakamekaṃ vastviti mate'pi tadūṣaṇamatidiśati—dravyeti | ślokaṃ vyācaṣṭe— ye'pīti | mṛdo ghaṭādayaḥ, suvarṇasya rucakādayo viśeṣāḥ | paryāyeṇa jāyamānāḥ paryāyā ucyante | apāyina ityavasthitamṛdādidravyādvedamāha | avyatirekā ityabhedam ; nānti vyatireko yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ ; bhinnābhinnā ityuktaṃ bhavati | etadevopapādayati—na ceti | yadi rucakādayaḥ
[p. 177]
suvarṇadravyameva syuḥ ; tatasteṣāṃ nāśe suvarṇamapi naśyet | ato rucakanāśe vardhamāne nāvatiṣṭhet ; na caivam, tatrāpi pratyabhijñānāt | na ca dravyādvidyate ; bhedenānupalabdheḥ, dravyasyaiva rucakādirūpeṇopalabdheḥ | tadevaṃ dravyaparyāyayoginnābhinnatvāt tadubhayātmakamekaṃ vastu, na dravyamātraṃ paryāyamātraṃ vā | dravyamātre hi vastuni rucakāderabhāvāt tadutpāde tadvināśo ca tadarthino harṣaśokau na syātām | paryāyamātre ca vastuni tadutpāde vināśaḥ ca hemo'pyutpattivināśānmadhyasthatā na syāt | dvyātmake tu vastuni harṣādikamapyavakalpate | tasmāt dvyātmakamekaṃ vastviti ye manyante—dravyaparyāyārthakāḥ dravyaparyāyarūpo'rtho vastu yeṣāṃ te, teṣāmapi nītirenaivātyantabhedābhedavādaprasaṅganirākaraṇaprakāreṇa nirākṛtā | etadeva prapañcayati—tathāhīti | avasthitaṃ hi dravyamucyate ; tadyadi P. 68-17. rucakavardhamānakavastunorhemāṃśānābhedādabheda ucyate, tata ekameva vastu ; tacca anityamavasthitamiti dravyamātraṃ jagadityeṣā nītiḥ syāt | atha hemarucakādyubhayarūpatvāt vastuno rucakasya vardhamānakādvyāvṛttyā vastvapi vyāvṛttam, yattu hemāṃśamātraṃ vardhamānake'pyanuvṛttaṃ na tadanuvṛttyā vastuno'nuvṛttiḥ, tasya kevalasya tvayamavastutvāt ityucyate ; tataḥ paryāyamātraṃ jagaditīyaṃ nītiḥ syāt, yataḥ parasparavyāvṛttāḥ paryāyā ucyante, na tu dravyam ; yato'vasthitaṃ dravyamucyate ; na ca paryāyamātre kiṃcidvastvavasthitamasti, teṣāmutpattivināśasaṃbandhāt | yā tu hemāṃśamātrasya rucakanāśe'pi vardhamānake'vasthānakalpanā, sā kalpanaiva ; yato dravyaparyāyarūpaṃ citraṃ vastu ; hemāṃśamātrasya ca na tathātvamityarthaḥ | atraiva dūṣaṇāntaramāha—api cati | dravyāt paryāyāṇāṃ bhede dravyasya sthitatvāt paryāyayośra nāśotpādāt tadarthināṃ madhyasthatāśokaharṣavyavasthā ghaṭet | abhede tu tadeva tiṣṭhati tadeva jāyate tadeva vinaśyatīti sthitijanmanāśānāmekatraiva samavāyaḥ syāt ; tataśca dravyārthino dravyasya sthitatvāt madhyasthatā syāt ; na syācca, rucakavardhamānakanāśotpādayostadabhedāt dravyasyāpi nāśāt utpāde ca sthitadravyābhāvāt ; ata eva harṣaśokau ca syātām ; yadi tu dravyamaghatiṣṭhata eva tato madhyasthatveva 12
[p. 178]
syāt ; yadā tvabhedādutpadyate naśyati ca, tadā śokāditritayamapi syāditi durghaṭamāpadyate | tathā vardhamānakanāśe'pi tadarthinaḥ śokāditritayaṃ syāt ; tathā rucakotpāde'pi tadarthinastritayaṃ syāt | atha dravyāt paryāyāṇāṃ mithaśca bhedo'pyasti ; ato na pūrvoktāśaṅkādyanavasthādoṣa ityucyate ; tato bhedābhedavādināmabhedo'pyastīti prāgukto doṣaḥ kiṃ na syāt ? atha satyamastyabhedaḥ, kiṃtu nāsāvatyantam ; tena tadāśrayaḥ śokādisaṃkaradoṣo na bhavati ; tato bhedo'pi nātyantamiti tadāśrayā śokādivyavasthā na P. 69-13 syādityāha—atheti | kiṃca sa viśeṣa ucyate, yo'nyatra nāsti ; anekāntavādinaśca yadi kvacidaikāntikaṃ syāt tata ātyantikatvaṃ viśeṣa-ṇābhedaṃ pratyucyate ; na ca tasya sarvatrānekāntamicchataḥ kiṃcidaikāntataḥ ; ataḥ sarvasyaiva nātyantikatvam, yadabhedaṃ prati viśeṣeṇa śokādyasaṃkarā-yocyata ityāha—na ceti | nanu sārvatrike'pyanekāntavāde nātrāpi saṃkaradoṣa ityāśaṅkānekāntavādameva dūṣayati—tathā ceti | tathā ceti dūṣaṇopanyāsopakrame | sarvabhāvānāṃ sarvaprakāreṣu yadyanekāntastataḥ pravṛtti-nivṛttirahitaṃ jagat syādityāha | hīśabdaḥ kaṣṭārthaḥ | asāvatathaiveti ; abhimatasyāsādhanamityarthaḥ | ślokamanyathā vyācaṣṭe—atheti | sarvatrānekāntavādino mama harṣādyapyasti, nāsti ca | ataḥ prītimadhyasthatāśokāḥ syurna syuriti yat durghaṭatvamuktaṃ tadiṣṭameva ; evaṃ na mama durghaṭamityāśaṅkācocyate—naikānta iti | śeṣaṃ sugamam | evaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣau dve vastunī dvyātmakaṃ caikaṃ vastviti pakṣadvaye dūṣite, bhedāḥ satyāḥ tadutthā abhedakalpanā viparyayo veti pakṣadvaye'va-śiṣṭe'nyataranirṇayārthamāha—tatreti ; tayoḥ pakṣayorityarthaḥ | ślokārtha ucyate—bhedo hi pratiyoginamapekṣate ; ato bhedagrahaṇapuraḥsaro bhedo grāhyo grahītuṃ śakyo na bhavati, anyonyāśrayatvāt | na cāgṛhītasya tasyābhedakalpanopādānatvam ; na hi bhedanāgṛhītau śāvaleyavāhuleyau gaurgoḥ ityabhedakalpanāṃ pramukhāte | tasmādagṛhīto bhedo nābhedakalpanā-hatyuriti | abhedajñānaṃ tu nābhedamapekṣate ; nāpi bhedam, svarūpahāna-
[p. 179]
prasaṅgāt | ataḥ śakyajñānatvāt svarūpamātreṇa jñātaḥ sa bhedakalpanopādānaṃ bhavitumarhatīti | dṛṣṭaṃ caikasya bhedakalpanopādānatvamityāha—yatheti | P. 70-16. yathaiko'vayavī avayavakalpanāheturityarthaḥ | nanvavayavebhyo bhinnasyāvayavino'saṃbhavādavayavopādānaivāvayavikalpanā, na tvavayavyupādānāvayavakalpanā ; ato bhedopādānaivātrāpyabhedakalpanetyāśaṅkyāha—na tatreti | bhedaśabdo'tra bhidyamānānavayavānāha ; abhedaśabdaśrābhidyamānamavayavinam | hetumāha—bhedo hīti | yathā ghaṭāvayavī kālpaniko'satyaḥ, tadvat kambugrīvādayo'pi svāvayavāpekṣayāvayavitvādasatyāḥ syuḥ | ato bhedaḥ paramāṇuṣveva niravayaveṣvavatiṣṭhate | ata eva paramārthato bhinnāḥ syuriti yāvat | na ca ta evābhedakalpanopādānam, atīndriyatvenāgṛhītānāṃ kalpanopādānatvāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | naikajñānaṃ cotpattaye bhedamapekṣate ityuktam ; tatra codayati—nanviti | anusaṃdhānaṃ pratyabhijñāpratyayaḥ ; sa ca deśakālabhede ‘so'yam’ iti pūrvāparānusaṃdhānātmako bhavati, nānyathā deśakālabhedamantareṇa | pūrvāparabhāvābhāvāt kiṃ paraṃ kena pūrveṇa sahābhedenā'nusaṃdhīyeta | tasmādabhedajñānamapi deśakālabhedasāpekṣamiti bhedatulyadoṣamityarthaḥ | tatrottaramāha—pratyuktamiti ; nedānīmabhinnadeśakālo'rtha ekaḥ syādityatra pratyuktamityarthaḥ | taduktameva smārayati—neti | ekārthābhāve cā'nusaṃdhānamiti vyarthaṃ syādityāha—asatīti | ekasya hyarthasya deśādibhedādāśaṅkitabhedasyābhedamāpādayadanusaṃdhānamarthavat syāt ; ekābhāve tu tadanarthakameva syādityarthaḥ | api ca sarvajanasiddhatvādapyabhedopadāno bhedo yuktaḥ, na tu viparyaya ityāha—api ceti | savikalpakāt prathamabhāvinirvikalpakajñānena vastumātramabhinnamekamālocyate bhedādisamastavikalparahitaṃ gṛhyate ; tataḥ paścāt ‘gauḥ śucchaḥ’ ityādibhedavikalpo bhavatīti sarvajanasiddham | ataścābhedopādānaiva bhedakalpanā yuktetyarthaḥ | dvitīyārdhamāśaṅkottaratvena vyācaṣṭa—syāditi | viśiṣṭarūpopagrāhīti ; bhinnarūpopagrāhītyarthaḥ ; yathā vikalpabuddhāviti vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntaḥ | yastvityādi ; asyārthaḥ—na brūmaḥ pratyakṣe'- 12A
[p. 180]
vabhāsate bhado nopalakṣyate ceti, yena virodhaḥ syāt; apitu sarvebhyo ghaṭasvarūpādanyebhyaḥ paṭādibhyo vyāvṛttaṃ ghaṭaṃ spaṣṭamevāvabhāsayati pratyakṣam; ato na virodha iti yo brūte, tasya 'āhurvidhātṛ' ityādinaivoktamuttaramityarthaḥ | kiṃca yadi sāmānyaviśeṣau bhinne dve vastunī na saṃbhavata ityuktam, na caikaṃ dvyātmakam, tadā anyatarasya mithyātve kalpye bhedajñānasyaiva P. 71-8. mithyātvamucitaṃ nābhedajñānasyetyāha—kiṃceti | 'idaṃ rajatam' ityādibhrāntiṣu 'idam' ityanavagataviśeṣavastumātrāvalambi jñānaṃ pratiṣṭhitamavyabhicāri; ataḥ paścāt bādhadaśāyāmapi 'śuktiśakalamidam' itīdamaṃśasya pratibhānāt; bhedeṣu tu viśeṣeṣu rajatādijñānasyāpratiṣṭhitatvaṃ vyabhicāritvaṃ dṛṣṭam; dṛṣṭavyabhicāritvasyaiva ca vyabhicārakalpanā labdhī, tasya vyabhicārayogyatāyāḥ kṛṣṭatvāt; itaratra vastumātrāṃśe tu sā kalpyatvāt gurvī; tasmādihāpi bhedajñānameva mithyā, nābhedajñānamiti bhāvaḥ | 'indriyadhiyāmapi' ityuktam; tasya vyāvartyaṃ vaktuṃ pūrvapakṣopakramamātraṃ karoti—syāditi | anuvidhāyitvādapītyapiśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ; atra dvicandrādyudāharaṇam | kiṃca vyāvṛttāḥ sarvabhāvā iti vadatā vyāvṛttaśabde'nyonyābhāvasya pravṛttinimittatvāt anyonyābhāvasvabhāvāstadviśeṣaṇā veti vācyam; na ca tadubhayamapi saṃbhavatītyāha—anyonyeti | tatra tasminnanyonyābhāve upādhau viśeṣaṇe'bhyupaganyamāne tathā bhedarūpeṇa pratīyantāṃ bhāvāḥ, na svarūpato bhinnāḥ syurityarthaḥ | anyonyetyādyuktam; tatkṛta ityāśaṅkyāha—ekasinniti | yadyekaṃ bhāvarūpamitaranirapekṣaṃ syāt tato'paraṃ bhāvarūpaṃ vyavatiṣṭheta; yadā tu sarvamevābhāvarūpaṃ tadaikamasminnasatyaparaṃ tadabhāvarūpaṃ na syāt; tasminnāsati tanna syāditītaretarāśrayaṃ syādityarthaḥ | atadātmanaśrayatthātpratītāvupahito maṇirudāhāryaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | arthasiddhiriti; svarūpamevānyonyābhāvaḥ, nānyo'stītyarthaḥ | siddhāntī tvāha—na ceti |
[p. 181]
kiṃcānyatvānmukhasaṃnidhau mukhāntarāṇyutpadyante, nānyathā | tena mukhamapi kāraṇam | kāraṇānurūpaṃ ca kāryamucitam | tatra tatsamānaparimāṇānyeva tāni yuktāni ; na caitat, kṛpāṇādāvalpatarāṇāmupalabdheḥ | avidyānyatve tu teṣāmalpatānupapattiradoṣaḥ | etaccopalakṣaṇam ; anye'pi doṣāḥ prāguktā eva | tadarthaṃ prayogaḥ—viśvabhedo'yamabhedopādāna iti sādhyo dharmaḥ, bhedatvāt, maṇikṛpāṇādiṣviva mukhabheda iti | paro'pīdānīmanumānabalenaiva pratyavatiṣṭhate—syāditi | tadidamanumānam—abhedo dharmī, bhedopādāna P. 72-8. iti sādhyo dharmaḥ, bhedatvāt, yathā 'vanam' ityabhedastarubhedopādāna iti | taddūṣayati—neti | yadi yāvānabhedaḥ sa sarvo bhedopādānatvānmithyā, tataḥ paramāṇurapyeko na syāt ; tadabhedo'pi bhedopādāna eva syāt ; tathā tadaṃśatadaṃśo'pītyanavasthā syāt ; tataśca na kiñcidekaṃ syāt | ataścaikaikaparamāṇusamuccayarūpastadabhedo nāstyeva ; kuto 'vanam' ityabheda-kalpanā syāt ? atha dūramapi gatvā kiñcidekaṃ tattvamabhedopādānatva-rahitamiṣyate, tatastenaiva heturanekānta ityarthaḥ | tadabhāvata iti ; ekaika-paramāṇusamuccayarūpasya sthūlasya vṛkṣādirūpasya bhedasyābhāvata ityarthaḥ | bhedasya mithyātve anumānāntaramāha—api ceti | bhedo dharmī, mṛṣeti sādhyo dharmaḥ, 'idam' iti 'tat' iti 'arthaḥ' iti 'vastu' iti 'sat' iti ca pratyekamabhedenānugatatvāt ; yo ya eva sa mṛṣā, yathā candramasastaraṅgabhedādyo bhedaḥ sa pratyekaṃ 'candraḥ' ityabhedānuviddho mṛṣetyarthaḥ | tarubhedo 'vanam' ityabhedānuviddho'pi na mṛṣā ; atastena vyabhicāro mā bhūditi pratyekamityuktam | kiṃca kalpanālāghavādapyabhedopādānā bhedakalpanocitā, na tu viparyaya ityāha—api ceti | na tu minnānām ; mahimetyanuṣajya kalpya ityadhyā-hāryam | nanu tvayaikasya nānāvabhāsasāmarthyaṃ kalpyam, na mayā ; ato mamaiva lāghavamityāśaṅkaya āvayoḥ sāmarthyakalpanā tulyā, tathāpi tvayā bahūnāṃ bahūni sāmarthyāni kalpyāni, mayā tvekasyaiva sāmarthyātiśaya iti
[p. 182]
P. 72-25. sapakṣe kalpanālāvavamupapādayitumupakramate—idamiti | bhedavādino'tyantabhinnāsu vyaktiṣu kathaṃ 'gaurgoḥ' ityabhedāvabhāsaḥ ? na hi nirnimittaṃ jñānaṃ bhavati | na ca bheda evābhedajñānasya svarūpeṇa nimittam ; 'gāmānaya' ityukte ca yāṃ vyaktimānayati punarukte tatsāmānyamevānayati, na yāṃ kāṃcidaśvādivyaktimapi ; so'yamatyantabhede kathamanuvṛttāvabhāso vyavahāraśca ? atha govyaktīnāṃ sādṛśyādanuvṛttāvabhāsavyavahāraḥ, tadayuktam ; yataḥ sādṛśyamapyavayavasāmānyānvayena bhavati, na cātyantabhedavādinaḥ so'sti | atha govyaktīnāmanyonyavyāvṛttirna gṛhyate, gṛhyate'śvādivyaktibhyaḥ ; tena govyaktiṣu bhinnāsvapyanuvṛttāvabhāsavyavahāraḥ, tadaviśeṣe'pi nāśvādivyaktiṣu saṃbhavati ; bhavati ca satyā api govyaktīnāṃ vyāvṛtteragrahaṇaṃ guḍyāpravālarāgayoriva sūkṣmatvenetyucyate ; tadasat ; yatastava vyāvṛttirnāma bhāvānāṃ svabhāva eva, na tato bhinnā ; tatra tasyā agrahaṇe tatsvabhāvānāmapyagrahaṇaṃ syāt ; anyathā tatsvabhāvataiva na syāditi bhāvaḥ | atha nirvikalpakatvena pratyakṣamaniścayātmakam ; savikalpakaṃ tu niścayātmakam ; taccāpratyakṣam | ato niścayapratyakṣayoḥ svarūpabhedaḥ | tena govyaktiṣu pratyakṣāsu tatsvabhāvabhūtānyonyavyāvṛttirapi yadyapi pratyakṣā, tathāpi tanniścayābhāvādanuvṛttāvabhāsavyavahārau bhavata iti cet ; tadasat ; yato vyāvṛtteraniścaye gosāmānyanibandhana eva vyavahāraḥ syāt, na viśeṣāntaravyāvṛttaḥ ; sa ca dṛśyate ; tathāhi—'kālākṣīmānaya' ityukte gavāntaravyāvṛttāmeva gāmānayati ; na ca tadvadyāvṛtteraniścaye yujyate | tasmānnaiṣo'pi pakṣaḥ sādhuḥ | athaikā govyaktiryāmarthakriyāṃ dohanādilakṣaṇāṃ karoti tāmevāparā ; tataśca yathaikā aśvādibhyo'tatkāribhyo vyāvṛttā tathā parāpītyatatkārivyāvṛttestulyatvāt govyaktiṣvatyantabhinnāsvapyekarūpāvabhāsavyavahāro bhavatīti cet ; tadapi na śobhanam ; yato yaikasyā govyakterarthakriyā na saivāparasyāḥ ; nāpi tajjātīyā, tairjāteraniṣṭhatvāt | ato govyaktīnāṃ naikārthakriyākāritvam | ataśca yathaikasyā govyakteratadarthakriyākāriṇo vyāvṛttiḥ evaṃ govyaktyantarādapi, tasyāpi tata iti kathamatatkārivyāvṛttistulyā ? kiṃca vyāvṛttirvastunaḥ svabhāvaścet,
[p. 183]
vastūnāṃ bhede kathaṃ sā tulyāḥ athāsvabhāvaḥ, tadā tādātmyādisaṃbandhābhāvāt kathaṃ sā vastūnāṃ syāt ? tataśca tato'pyekameva tattvaṃ syāt, na bhedaḥ | athānādivāsanānibandhano bhinnāstvapi vyaktiṣvekarūpāvabhāsaḥ ; tataśca tadanurūpo vyavahāraḥ, na tu vyaktigataḥ kaścidviśeṣo'pekṣyat ityucyate ; tadapyayuktam ; vyaktigataviśeṣānāśrayaṇe hi govyaktiṣveva ‘gauḥ’ ityekarūpāvabhāso bhavati, nāścavyaktiṣvityayaṃ niyamo na syāt ; asti ca | tasmānna vāsanāmātranibandhano'yamabhedāvabhāsaḥ | na ca vāsanānāṃ bhedāt kācideva vāsanā kāścideva vyaktiṣvabhedāvabhāsaṃ karoti, na sarvāstiti vācyam ; sā hi pūrvānubhavajaṭṭasaṃskārānuvedharūpatvāt pūrvānubhūtāstveva vyaktiṣvabhedāvabhāsaṃ kuryāt, na bhinnāsu | sādṛśyāttatrāpi karotīti cet ; na, tasya nirastvāt | tataḥ kāścidvacaktayaḥ kasyacidabhedāvabhāsasya prakṛtyaiva samarthāḥ, na sarvāḥ sarvasyeti tāsāṃ sāmarthyātiśayāḥ kalpyāḥ | bhinnānāṃ vyaktīnāṃ bhedāvabhāsahetava eva sāmarthyātiśayā yuktāḥ ; ime tvabhedāvabhāsasya hetava iti svabhāvaviparītā ityucyante | P. 73-11. tatra teṣāmanantāni vastūni, anantāśca sāmarthyātiśayāḥ kalpyā iti kalpanāgauravam | mama tvekaṃ tattvaṃ kalpyamiti kalpanālāghavam | tasmādekasyaivāstu mahimā yannānevāvabhāsate ; na tu bhinnānām, yadabhedena bhāsanta ityarthaḥ | yathāvastu vyavahāro yuktaḥ ; tatrābhinne tattve ghaṭapaṭādibhedavyavahāraste kathamiti cedata āha—bhedeti | yathāvabhāsaṃ hi vyavahāraḥ, na yathāvastu, rajjusarpāvabhāse palāyanadarśanāt | avabhāsate ca bhedaḥ | ato'vabhāsānurūpā bhedavyavahārāḥ setsyanti ; yathā—bhedavādināṃ bhinnāsu vyaktiṣu ‘gauḥ’ ityabhedavyavahāraḥ | na kevalaṃ teṣām ; apitu jātivyavasyoratyantabhinnayoḥ samavāyākhyasaṃsargavādināmapi ; kaṇabhujāṃ jātervyaktibhyo'rthāntaratvāt ‘gotvavartī vyaktimānaya’ iti yathāvastu bhedāvabhāsena na vyavahāraḥ ; kiṃtu ‘gāmānaya’ ityabhedāvabhāsenaiva viśeṣeṣu sāmānyavyavahāraḥ | tathā ye'pi vyaktibhyo jātimanarthāntarabhūtāṃ vadanti bhāṭṭāḥ, teṣāmapi dvyātmakaṃ vastu | na ca tasya tādṛśasya śabdasya vastvantare'nugamo'sti | yasya tvasti sāmānyāṃśamātrasya, tadekamavastveva | tataśca
[p. 184]
yo'yaṃ ‘gaurgauḥ’ ityanugatarūpo vyavahāraḥ, sa yathāvabhāsameva na yathāvastviti ‘tasyānanvayataḥ’ ityatra darśitam | itiḥ tarkakāṇḍīyanyāyoktisamāptau | P. 78-50. anantaroktamarthaṃ ślokena saṃgṛhṇāti—yatheti | bāhyāḥ ; bāhyāthavādino vedāntavivekabāhyā vādinaḥ | itiḥ tarkakāṇḍagranthasamāptau | iti śrībrahmasiddhivyākhyāne tarkakāṇḍavyākhyānaṃ śaṅkhapāṇinā kṛtam ||
[p. 185]
**niyogakāṇḍaḥ**
idānīṃ kāṇḍāntaramārabhate—evamiti | vṛttavartiṣyamāṇasaṃkīrtanaṃ kāṇḍayoḥ saṃgatipradarśanārtham | sthite bupacaritārthatvanirāsenopaniṣadāṃ mukhyārthatve, ko'sau mukhyo'rthaḥ ? svarūpaṃ kāryaṃ vā iti cintāvatarati, nānyathā | kāryaṃ evārthaṃ vedasya prāmāṇyamiti ; sāmānyadvāreṇa P. 74-8. vedāntagatānāmupaniṣadāmapi kārye'rthaṃ prāmāṇyamityabhiprāyaḥ | pratijñātamarthamṛṣijuṣṭatvena prasādhayati—evamiti | tadvacācaṣṭe—codaneti | mantrārthavādopaniṣadāṃ codanaikavākyatvena [a]pṛthagvedatvācodanātmaka eva kṛtsno vedaḥ | tena tadarthaṃ eva vedārthaḥ, nāparaḥ kaścidastīti | nanvanena codanāpramāṇako vedārtha iti yāvadityuktam ; tataḥ kimityata āha—codanā ceti | codanā hi pravartakaṃ vacanam ; na ca sā vāyuvat pravartayati ; | katu pravṛttihetubhūtamarthamavagamayantī | pravṛttihetuḥ svakāryam ; tathāhi—iṣṭasādhanamapi ‘mamedaṃ kāryam’ ityapratipadya na kaścit pravartate | tasmāt kārye'rthaṃ vedaḥ pramāṇamityarthaḥ | nanu bhavatvevamanyatra ; vedānteṣu tu kaḥ kāryavidhiḥ, yatra teṣāṃ prāmāṇyam—ityāśaṅkyāha—vedāntānāmiti | ‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti pratipattikartavyatāpramāṇam ; tatsevināṃ pratipattikartavyatāyāṃ pratipattavyatvenānugatamanvitaṃ yat siddharūpamātmatattvaṃ tadviṣayapratipattikartavyatāmukhenāvabodhakatvam, na tu svatantrāṇām | ataste'pi kāryaniṣṭhā evetyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—ātmapratipattikartavyatāvidhyapekṣitapratipattavyātmasvarūpasamarpakatvena vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyamiti | etadeva bhāṣyoktyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | yadi kārye'rthaṃ vedaḥ pramāṇaṃ kathaṃ tarhi mantrārthāḥ sopaniṣatkāḥ siddhamarthamāhurityāśaṅkya “codanā hi” ityādyuktam | ayamarthaḥ— mantrādīnāṃ vidhyekavākyatvādekaiveyaṃ tāvatī codanā | sā ca kāryaṃ
[p. 186]
gamayantī tatparatayā bhūtādikamarthamekadeśena brūte, na svaniṣṭhatvena | tenopaniṣado'pi pratipattikartavyatāparā eva, na siddhārthaparā iti ye manyante P.74-10. tān pratyucyate—tisra iti | śabdāt vedāntākhyāt yadātmajñānaṃ tat ‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti vidhinā na vidhīyate; yasmādadhītavedasya śabdasāmarthyādeva karmāvabodhavat tadātmajñānaṃ bhavati; ataḥ kiṃ tatra vidhineti bhāvaḥ | nanu pramāṇāntarajaṃ jñānamupekṣya kimiti śābdameva jñānaṃ na vidheyamatroktamityāśaṅkyoktam— brahmaṇo hīti | śeṣaṃ sugamam | karmāvabodhavaditi dṛṣṭāntitam; tatra vidhyanapekṣāmāha—svargeti | “svargakāmo yajeta” ityasmin śābde vākyārthabodhe nānyo vidhirapekṣyate, tadvidhivākyārthabodhe'pi vidhyantarapekṣāyāmanavasthāprasaṅgāt | na ca yo'yaṃ ‘yajeta’ iti vidhiḥ sa evāpekṣyate, tasya karmaṇi vyavasthiteḥ; karma hi tena vidhīyate, na tu tadbodha ityarthaḥ | atra caudayati—nanviti | yathā svargakāmādivākyānāmadhyayane “svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ” iti vidhirapekṣyate, svādhyāyādhyayanavidhyadhyayane tu na vidhyantaramapekṣyata iti nānavasthā; tathaiṣāmarthabodhe'pi vidhyapekṣāstu; tadvidhyarthabodhe tu na vidhyantaramapekṣyate; ato nānavasthā bhavatītyarthaḥ | na ca vedārthajñānapravṛttau vidhirnāstītyāha—asti ceti | taddūṣayati— viṣama iti | vaiṣamyamāha—apravṛtto'pīti | yadyadhyayanavidhiḥ svayamadhīto vākyāntarāṇyadhyāpayati, tatastadadhyayanamapyadhyayanavidhyantarādhīnamityanavasthā syāt | yatastu svayamapravṛtto'pyadhyayanavidhyadhyayane puruṣaḥ puruṣāntarādhītādadhyayanavidhestasyānyeṣāṃ ca vākyānāmadhyayane pravartate, tena tatrānavasthā na bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanvanyenādhītādadhyayanavidheranyasya kathaṃ pravṛttiḥ? adhītaṃ hi vākyaṃ pravartayati, nānadhītam, agṛhītasya pravartakatvāyogādityata āha—śākheti | nādhyayanaṃ pravṛttau tantram, apitu grahaṇam; tacca śravaṇamātrādapi bhavatyeva; yathā svayamanadhītādapi śākhāntaroktādaṅvākyāt tadadhyāyibhyaḥ śrutāt tadaṅkopasaṃhāraḥ prayogeṣu kriyata ityarthaḥ |
[p. 187]
nanu puruṣāntarāṇāmapi kathamadhyayanavidhivākyādhyayanamityāśaṅkyāha—yatheti | P. 76-16 evaṃ svargakāmādivākyādhyayane nānavasthādoṣa ityuktam | tadarthabodhe tu “vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ” iti vidhyantarāpekṣā saṃbhavati | yatastadapyanavabuddhārthaṃ na svargakāmādivākyārthajñāne pravartayitumalam , atastadartho'pi vidhyapekṣaḥ | na ca sa eva svārthabodhe pravartayati, anavabuddhārthasya pravartakatvāyogāt ; tadarthabodhāt pravṛttiḥ, pravṛttasya ca tadarthabodha iti itaretarāśrayaṃ hi syāt | ataśca “vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ” ityasyārthabodhe vidhyantaramapekṣyam , tatrāpyevamityanavasthā syāditi mūkam—‘viṣama upanyāsaḥ’ iti | yaccoktam—‘asti vidhiḥ’ iti, tadapyayuktam ; yato'pūrvārthaprāpako vidhirbhavati ! ayaṃ tvadhyayanavidherdṛṣṭārthatvena nyāyaprāptānuvādaka ityāha—na ceti | abhyupagamyāsya vidhitvaṃ vedārthabodhe ‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti pṛthagvidhyanapekṣāmāha—atheti | nanu svādhyāyādhyayanavidhyākṣiptaṃ cet vedārthajñānaṃ “vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ” ityanenocyate, tato'dhyayanavidhiprāptānuvāda evāyaṃ syāt ; kathaṃ vidhyupagamaḥ? ucyate—vidhyākṣiptamapi vidhīyate ; yāgavidhyākṣiptamiva vrīhyādi | vilambī hyarthākṣepaḥ, śīghrabhāvinī śrutiḥ ; sā arthākṣepavyāpārāt prāgaprāptameva vidhatte | phalaṃ tvanyanivṛttiḥ ; arthākṣepe hyanyadapyākṣipyeteti na doṣaḥ | karmavidhivākyavadityuktam ; tadvivṛṇvan tatra punaḥ ślokaṃ yojayati—yatheti | vidhau satyapi cedvidhyantaramapekṣyate tatastatrāpyevamityanavasthānāt svargakāmādivākyeṣu “vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ” iti sāmānyavidhinārthabodhe pravṛttisiddhestadarthaṃ yathā nānyo vidhirapekṣyate—na ca svargakāmādivākyagataḥ, tasya karmaviṣayatvāt—evaṃ vedānteṣvapi vākyasāmarthyādeva tadarthabodhotpatteḥ ‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti vidhirnāpekṣitavya ityarthaḥ | nanu karmavidhivadatrāpi sāmānyavidhisiddhatvameva vācyam ; tatkimidamuktam—‘vākyasāmarthyāt’ iti? ucyate—vedānteṣu ‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti viśeṣavidhivādī sāmānyavidhestadanyaviṣayateti manyate ; atastadabhiprāyeṇa sāmānyavidhisiddhārthatā nāstīti ‘vākyasāmarthyāt’ ityuktam | sāmānyavidhiśeṣatve'pi copaniṣadāṃ siddhārthatā na syāditi taccheṣatvamapyanicchatedamuktamityadoṣaḥ |
[p. 188]
P. 76-3. itaśca śabdāt yadātmavijñānaṃ tanna vidhīyata ityāha—itaśceti | vidheyāvagamāduttarakālīnā yā pravṛttiḥ, saiva vidheḥ phalam, na tu śabdaprabhavo yo vidheyabodhaḥ sa ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? anyonyasaṃśrayāt— śabdārthabodhe sati pravṛttiḥ, pravṛttasya ca śabdārthabodha ityātmāśrayatā | yastu taccāvabodhaparādvedāntavākyāt taccāvabodhaḥ, sa na śabdārthabodhāt paraḥ uttarakālīnaḥ ; kiṃtu sa evātmā | ato na 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidheḥ phalamityarthaḥ | itaśca śabdajñānamavidheyamityāha—apiceti | kiṃca śabdājjāte'rthabodhe na ṣuruṣaḥ 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidhinā pravartyaḥ | sādhye hi pravartyate, na siddhe | atha śabdo'rthasyānavabodhaka eva, tena tajjñāne pravartyaḥ, tatrāpi śabdārthabodhasyāsaṃbhavāt sutarāṃ na pravartanīyaḥ | na hyasaṃgate viṣaye kaścitpravartyate | śabdājjāte'rthārthabodhe tanniścaye pravartyate iti cet, ata āha—śabdeneti | na niścaye'pi pravartyaḥ ; so'pi hi śabdajanyo vā syāt, pramāṇāntarajanyo vā ; na tāvacchabdādartha taccāvabodhe tasmādava yo niścayaḥ, tatra pravartyaḥ ; sa hi śabdena prathamameva kṛtaḥ ; kiṃ tatra vidhinā ? atha śabdādavabuddhe tacce anyasmātmāṇāntarādyo niścayastatra vidhinā pravartyaḥ, tato'nyasya pramāṇāntarasya vācchanamapekṣā syāt | ataśca śabdastatra niścayaheturna syāt | na caitadiṣṭamityarthaḥ | idānīṃ vikalpāntaraṃ karoti—atheti | upaṣṭhavo'vagama ityarthaḥ | bhūtārthavaiyarthyamatātparye hetuḥ ; atātparyasyāpi darśanāditi sāṃśayikatve | tadbūṣayati—tadvārtamiti ; tadasāram | kutaḥ ? yato lokavedayoḥ śabdānāmarthaparatvam "aviśiṣṭastu vākyārthaḥ" iti nyāyenautsargikam | nimittāntarāttu śabdasvarūpapradhānatvam ; ata evāvivakṣitārthatvaṃ tadapavādaḥ ; yathā—'gavityayamāha' iti 'iti'śabdāt, yathā japamantreṣu japasyoccāraṇātmakasyoccāryamāṇaśabdasvarūpaniṣṭhatvam | na ca vedānteṣu tannimittāntaramastīti bhāvaḥ |
[p. 189]
itaśca vedāntānāṃ vivakṣitārthatvāya 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidhirnāpekṣyata ityāha—hṛṣṭeti | karmavidhivākyādhyayane svādhyāyavidheyā P. 77-10 dṛṣṭārthatā, sātra vedāntādhyayane na bhidyate, na viśiṣyate, samāneti yāvat | ataḥ svādhyāyavidheṣṭeṣṭārthatvenaiva karmavākyavadvedāntānāmapyavivakṣitārthatā nivṛttaiva; kiṃ pratipattividhinā tadartheneti bhāvaḥ | itarathā yadi tu vedāntādhyayane dṛṣṭārthatā na syāt, tataḥ 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidhyarthena saha vidhyarthaḥ kṛtvā vedāntārtho'vivakṣitaḥ syādityarthaḥ | phalāntarakalpaneti; viśvajihvayāyena sargo rātrisatranyāyena dadhimadhukulyādi vā phalāntaramuktam; tathā vedāntānāmavivakṣitārthānāmadhyayanātphalāntarakalpanā, tatrāpyātmāvabodhasya karmāvabodhavaddṛṣṭatvādityanvayaḥ | nanu karmavākyānāṃ pradattinivṛttyupayogenārthavaccādhuktaṃ vivakṣitārthatvam; na tu vedāntotthāyāḥ siddhārthapratipatteḥ, pravṛttinivṛttyanupayogena vaiyarthyāt | ataḥ svādhyāyavidhidṛṣṭārthatā tu bhidyata ityāśaṅkyāha—na ceti | kiṃca pratipattividhāvapi vaiyarthyasya tulyatvameva | tacca pratipattividhau tulyamityatra vakṣyata ityāha—pratipattīti | 'bhavet' ityādiślokārdhaṃ vyācaṣṭe—avaśyamiti | avaśyaṃ caitadevaṃ vijñeyaṃ yat svādhyāyavidheṣṭeṣṭārthatvena vedāntānāmapi vivakṣitārthatā | anyathā 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidhāyakena saha kṛtsnasya vedāntasyāvivakṣitārthatā syāt | kutaḥ? yataḥ pratipattividhāyakaśabdanibandhanamityareṣāṃ vedāntānāṃ vivakṣitārthatvam | vidhāyakasya tu na vivakṣitārthatvasya nibandhanaṃ heturasti | ato vidhāyakasyāvivakṣitārthatvānna tadvalenetareṣāṃ vedāntānāṃ vivakṣitārthatvamiti saha vidhāyakena sarve vedāntā avivakṣitārthāḥ syuḥ | atha vidhāyakaḥ śabdaḥ prakṛtyā śabdasyārthaparatvasvabhāvatvena dṛṣṭārthatvena ca vivakṣitārtha ityucyate, tata itare'pi vedāntāstathaiva vivakṣitārthā bhaviṣyantīti kiṃ vidhāyakenetyarthaḥ | athāpuruṣārthasādhane vidhiḥ pravartayituṃ nālamiti vidhyadhīnaṃ puruṣārthatvam; ataḥ śabdotthasyātmajñānasya puruṣārthatvasiddhyartham 'ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vidhiripyate, tadapi netyāha—jñānasyeti |
[p. 190]
aśobhanatvamāha—śeyeti | jñānasya jñeyaprāptirdṛṣṭaṃ phalam | na dṛṣṭaphale vidhiḥ, dṛṣṭasyānvayavyatirekasiddhatvena vidhyanapekṣatvāt | mokṣo jñānasyādṛṣṭaṃ phalam, tadartho vidhirbhaviṣyatīti cet ; tanna ; na hi sa sādhyaḥ ; kutaḥ ? taccacyuteḥ, mokṣatvahānāt | ātyantikī hi saṃsāranivṛttirmokṣa ucyate ; tasya kāryatve ghaṭādivadvināśāt punaḥ saṃsāraḥ syāt | ato mokṣasyātyantikasaṃsāranivṛttilakṣaṇaṃ mokṣatvaṃ hīyeta | nanu ghaṭādināśavatkāryo'pi mokṣo na naṅkṣyati ; naivam, bhāvasya kāryasya nāśenāvinābhāvāt ; bhāvaśca mokṣaḥ, niratiśayānandaiścaryalakṣaṇatvāt | nanu ātyantikī saṃsāranivṛttirmokṣa ityucyate ityuktvā, kathamāha bhāvarūpo mokṣa iti ? ucyate—mokṣe sā bhavatītyupacārāt saiva mokṣa ityuktamityavirodhaḥ ; mokṣe vā sā śrutyādibhirucyate ityarthaḥ | nanvāgāmidehendriyabuddhisaṃyogābhāvo mokṣaḥ ; sa ca kāryo'pyabhāvatvāt ghaṭābhāvavat naṅkṣyati | naivam ; prāgabhāvo hi saḥ ; na cāsau kāryaḥ, anāditvāt ghaṭādiprāgabhāvavat | nanu bandhahetudhvaṃso mokṣaḥ ; sa ca ghaṭadhvaṃsavat kāryo'pi nānityaḥ | satyam ; kiṃtu asau vidyāyā na pṛthak | ato na tatkāryam | yasmādavidyottho bandhaḥ, vidyaiva ca tadvirodhinī tadabhāvaḥ, na pṛthak ; pṛthakca kāryaṃ bhavati | athaveti ‘taccacyuteḥ’ ityasyāparā vyākhyā | ātmanaḥ svarūpasthitilakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ śrutyoktaḥ ; sa cetkāryaḥ syāt, evaṃlakṣaṇānmokṣatvāccyaveta ; na hi kāryaṃ nityasyātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | kāryatve mokṣasyānityatvāt punarāvṛttiḥ syāt | apunarāvṛttiśrutiviruddhatvāt ‘yat kāryaṃ tadanityam’ ityanumānamapramāṇam ; tena kāryatve'pi mokṣasya nityatvāt yaduktaṃ kāryatve vināśāt tasya punaḥ saṃsāra iti tadayuktamityanubhāṣate—yacciti | taddūṣayati—dṛṣṭeti | dṛṣṭā sarvakāyāṇāmanityatvādvināśitā ; mokṣasya kāryatve'pyapunarāvṛttiśrutibalānnityatvamāśrayeta, yadi śruteranyā gatirna syāt ; asti tu setyarthaḥ | tāmanyāṃ gatimāha—yadā tviti | anavayaveneti ; niḥśeṣata iti yāvat | avidyā punarāvṛttihetuḥ ; tasyāṃ vidyayā niḥśeṣato gatāyāṃ hetvabhāvādeva
[p. 191]
punarāvṛttyabhāvo'pītyarthaḥ | tamarthavādenānuvadatyeṣā 'na ca punarāvartate' iti vartamānaśrutiḥ | ato na tatprāmāṇyādanantamokṣaphalatvaṃ pratipattiviṣe rityarthaḥ | arthavādatvamevāsyāḥ śruterdraḍhayati—tathāhīti | vartamānāpa- P. 78-19 deśo hi prārabdhāparisamāptamāha, nāgāmi sādhyamiti bhāvaḥ | yadi ca svargakāmādivat kāmopabandhaḥ syāt, tato'pi sādhyaṃ kāmyate na nityamityapunarāvṛtteḥ sādhyatā gamyeta ; na tu so'pyatvāsti | tasmādarthavādo'punarāvṛttiśrutirityarthaḥ | nanvarthavādādapi phalakarūpanā dṛṣṭā ; yathā— rātrisatrādau pratiṣṭhāderityata āha—arthavādāditi | satyam ; anirjñātapārādhyeṣu sā | nirjñātapārādhyeṣu tu “dravyasaṃskārakarmasu phalaśrutiḥ parārthatvādarthavādaḥ syāt” iti nyāyaḥ paṭhitaḥ | nirjñātapārādhye ca jñānaṃ jñeyataccāvabhāsena dṛṣṭenārthena nirākāṅkṣam | ato'tra na sā yuktetyarthaḥ | nanu jñeyasyātmanastaccāvabhāso'pi svayamapuruṣārthaḥ ; ato'styeva puruṣārthākāṅkṣā ; tat kathaṃ nairākāṅkṣyamityata āha— puruṣārthateti | matāntaramāśaṅkayati—anya iti | vidhervinā kāryaśūnyaṃ bhūtārthaṃ yadvacaḥ, tat bhūtasyārthasya pramāṇāntaragatatvādanuvādakaṃ syāt ; anuvādaśca pramāṇāntaragocare bhavati ; atastadanuvādakaṃ vacaḥ pramāṇāntarāpekṣaṃ sat anapekṣatvalakṣaṇaprāmāṇyamativartate, na pramāṇaṃ syāt | vidhyarthe tu kārye pramāṇāntarāviṣaye yat pratiṣṭhitaṃ paryavasitaṃ vacaḥ tat pramāṇāntarānapekṣaṃ sat pramāṇatvaṃ labhate ; yathā agnihotrādikarmavākyam | ataśca yadyapyātmataccāvabodhāt parā uttarakālīnā paṭhattirvidhiphalamatra nāsti, tathāpi vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyārthaṃ pratividhirāśrayitavya ityarthaḥ | śeṣaṃ spaṣṭam | atrocyate—pramāṇeti | pramāṇāntareṇa yātaḥ prāpto'rtho yasya vacasaḥ tattathoktam, tadvāvastattā ; tathā bhūtaḥ siddho'rtho yasya vacasaḥ tadvūtārtham, tadvāvastattā | pramāṇāntaraprāptārthatā pramāṇāntarajñātatvena jñāpitvaṃ kimanuvādatā ? uta bhūtārthatāmātram ? tatra pramāṇāntaralabdhārthatā cedanuvādatābhipretā, tato yadapauruṣeyaṃ vedāntavacaḥ pramāṇāntarajñātatvena na bodhakaṃ,
[p. 192]
na tadanuvādakam ; ato nāpekṣam ; ato nāpramāṇam | pauruṣeyaṃ hi pramāṇāntaralabdhārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | P. 79-22 atha bhūtārthatāmātramanuvādatā, tatrāha—sāpekṣatāyā iti | smṛtau darśanāditi ; smṛtau pramāṇāntarasaṃsparśasya pramāṇāntarāpekṣāhetutvena darśanādityarthaḥ | etadevāha—sā hīti | sā hi smṛtiḥ ‘sa ghaṭaḥ’ iti pūrvādhigatameva ghaṭaṃ ‘saḥ’ iti sarvanāmnā pūrvādhigataparāmarśinollikhantyādhigamāntarasaṃsparśārthā jāyata iti hetostasyādhigamāntaramapekṣata ityarthaḥ | adhigamāntareṇa saṃbhinnaḥ saṃspṛṣṭo'rtho viṣayo yasyā iti vigrahaḥ | apauruṣeye siddhārthe'pi vacasi sāpekṣatvaheturnāstītyuktam ; tatra para āha—syāditi | pratyakṣādipramāṇāntaraṃ siddhaviṣayam | ato vaidikasyāpi siddhārthasya vacaso viṣaye pramāṇāntarasya saṃbhavāt tadapekṣā yuktetyarthaḥ | siddhārthe pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave'pi kimiti tadapekṣata ityata āha—tathāhīti | pramāṇāntarasaṃvādāpekṣāyāṃ ca nairapekṣyalakṣaṇaṃ vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | yadā tu pramāṇāntarāgocare kāryarūpe pratipattividhyarthe paryavasyanti vedāntāḥ, tadā tatrāsaṃbhavati pramāṇāntare na kutaścitpramāṇāntarādvisaṃvādāśaṅketi na saṃvādāya pramāṇāntaramapekṣaṇīyam ; ato nairapekṣyalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ sidhyati | pramāṇāntaraviṣaye hi tatsaṃvādavisaṃvādau syātām, nānyatreti bhāvaḥ | atrocyate—na ceti | śabdaviṣaye pramāṇāntarasya saṃbhavamātreṇa pramāṇāntarāpekṣā śabde na yujyate | kutaḥ ? yatastadā tayoḥ pramāṇāntaraśabdayoḥ, śabda eva sāpekṣo na pramāṇāntaramityatra viśeṣo na gandhetetyarthaḥ | śabdaḥ pramāṇāntarasāpekṣo loke dṛṣṭaḥ, na pramāṇāntaramityasti viśeṣaḥ ; tasmācchabdasyaiva sāpekṣatā, na pramāṇāntarasya adṛṣṭatvāt iti yadyucyeta, tataḥ kārye'pi pramāṇāntarāgocare śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ loke na dṛṣṭamiti tatrāpi buddhavākyavat sutarāṃ śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ na syāt | tadidaṃ sarvamāha—yadīti | natarāmityuktamatiśayārthamāha—pramāṇāntareti | pramāṇāntaraviṣaye hi tatsaṃbhavādapi tāvacchabdasyāptavākyavat prāmāṇyaṃ
[p. 193]
syāt | pramāṇāntarāgocare tu kārye pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavāt buddhavākyavat tatprāmāṇyaṃ durlabham | atha pauruṣeyatvakāritā lokavacasāṃ pramāṇāntarādhigataviṣayatā, na śabdasāmarthakāritā ; śabdasya tu svataḥ pramāṇāntarāgocara etārthaṃ sāmarthyam ; tena pramāṇāntarāgocare'pi kāryaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na durlabhamityucyate, tanna ; yadi śabdaḥ pramāṇāntarānapekṣasāmarthyastarhi tulyasāmarthyatvānna śabda eva tadviṣaye pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave pramāṇāntarasāpekṣaḥ syāt, apitu pramāṇāntaramapi tadviṣaye śabdasāpekṣaṃ syāt | kimataḥ ? yadyevamidamato bhavati pramāṇāntarasya śabdasāpekṣatvenāprāmāṇyam ; aprāmāṇye ca sati tatsaṃvādavisaṃvādānupayogānna śabdasya tadapekṣā yujyate | pramāṇasāpekṣaṃ na pramāṇaṃ bhavati | śabdastu na pramāṇam | ato na tadapekṣayā pramāṇāntaramapramāṇam ; apitu pramāṇameva, anapekṣatvāditi cet . tatrocyate—śabdasyaivāprāmāṇyaṃ kṛta iti | para āha—pramāṇāntarāpekṣatvā P. 80-22. diti | siddhāntī tvāha—pramāṇāntarasyāpi prāmāṇyaṃ śabdānapekṣatvāt ; tadanapekṣatvaṃ ca śabdasyāprāmāṇyāt | ataḥ śabdāprāmāṇye tadanapekṣatvāt pramāṇāntarasya prāmāṇyam ; tatprāmāṇye ca tadapekṣatvāt śabdasyāprāmāṇyamitītaretarāśrayaṃ syāt | śabdasyāprāmāṇyāt pramāṇāntarasyā'napekṣatvaṃ prasādhayati—pramāṇe hīti | yadi śabdaḥ pramāṇaṃ syāt tataḥ pramāṇāntarasya viṣaye śabde saṃbhavati pramāṇāntaramapi śabdasāpekṣaṃ syāt, tatsaṃbhava iva śabdaḥ | tasmācchabdasyāprāmāṇyāt pramāṇāntarasyānapekṣatvamiti yuktamitaretarāśrayatvam | na ca pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave śabdaḥ sāpekṣaḥ, na tu śabdasaṃbhave taditi viśeṣo'stītyarthaḥ | yadi ca siddhārthaṃ śabde pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavamātreṇa tatsāpekṣatvaṃ syāt, evaṃ sati pratyakṣānumānayorapi sparśanakampādiliṅgayorekatra vāyāvanyonyagocare pravṛtteḥ parasparasāpekṣatvamaviśeṣaṃ śabda iva prasajyate | tataśca tayorapyaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt | tathendriyāntaraviṣaye yadīndriyaṃ pravartate tadapi sāpekṣaṃ syāt | taduktam— pratyakṣānumānayorityādinā | satteti ; rūpādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ sattāguṇatve pañcendriyagrāhye ; dravyaṃ tu ghaṭādi tvakcakṣurgrāhyamiti vivekaḥ | atha śabdasyaiva pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave sāpekṣatvam, na pratyakṣānumānayorityucyate, tato 13
[p. 194]
viśeṣaheturvācyaḥ ; na ca so'sti | atha śabdaḥ pramāṇāntarādhigataviṣayo loke dṛṣṭaḥ ; ato'smādviśeṣācchabda eva pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave sāpekṣaḥ, na pratyakṣānume iti cet, uktamatrottaram 'pramāṇāntarasyāviṣaye tarhi natarām' P. 81-11. ityatra | uktamarthaṃ prapañcayati—yadīti | yadi pauruṣeyāpauruṣeyaśabdamātradharmo'yaṃ pramāṇāntarādhigatārthatvaṃ nāma, tataḥ pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavādeva buddhādivākyavadaprāmāṇyaṃ śabdasya syāt ; pramāṇāntarasaṃbhava eva tvāptavākyavat prāmāṇyaṃ syāt | ataśca vedāntānāṃ siddhārthatve pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavādaprāmāṇyam, kāryārthatve tu pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavātprāmāṇyamityuktamayuktamiti bhāvaḥ | atha pauruṣeyaśabdadharmaḥ syāt, tato'pauruṣeyasya vedāntaśabdasya pramāṇāntarādhigatārthatvaṃ nāstīti tasya siddhārthatvena saṃbhavatpramāṇāntaraviṣayasyāpi na sāpekṣatvam | ato nāprāmāṇyam | saṃbhavat pramāṇāntaraṃ yatra tat saṃbhavatpramāṇāntaram, tadeva viṣayo yasyeti samāsaḥ | pauruṣeyatvanimittaṃ hi sāpekṣatvam | apauruṣeyaśabde satyapi saṃbhavatpramāṇāntaraviṣayatve nimittanivṛttau nivartate, na tu śabdatvasāmānyāt prasajyata iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃca yadyanyatra dṛṣṭamanyatrāpi kenacit sāmānyena syāt, tataḥ svareṇa putrānumānaṃ viśeṣataḥ pratyakṣādhigataviṣayaṃ dṛṣṭamityanumānatvasāmānyāt sāmānyato dṛṣṭasyāpi deśāntaraprāptyā sūrye gatyanumānasya pratyakṣādhigataviṣayatā syāt | ṣaḍjādivivekaviṣayaṃ ca pratyakṣaṃ gānaśāstrapūrvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti ghaṭādipratyakṣamapi tathā syāt | na caivam | ato yathā tatrānyatra dṛṣṭapanyatra na bhavati, evaṃ pauruṣeye sāpekṣatvaṃ dṛṣṭamapi nāpauruṣeyasya bhūtārthasyāpi bhavitumarhanti | yadi pauruṣeye yadṛṣṭaṃ tadapauruṣeye'pi syāt, tataḥ pauruṣeyaṃ pramāṇāntarādhigatārthaṃ pramāṇaṃ dṛṣṭamityapauruṣeye pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavāt prāmāṇyaṃ na syādityuktaṃ natarāmityarthaḥ | 'mānāntarasyāviṣaye vidhyarthe tu pratiṣṭhitam | anapekṣatvaṃ pramāṇatvamaśnute' ityuktam ; tatra dūṣaṇamāha—aniceti | yadi niyoganiṣṭhatve tatra pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavādanapekṣatvena śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ bhavatītyucyate, tadoṣadhapānasikatābhakṣaṇaniyogayoḥ pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavāttadanapekṣatvena tadvākyayoḥ prāmāṇyaṃ syāt ; na caitadiṣṭam, ekatrānuvādakatvāt aparatra ca nirbhūlatvenāprāmāṇyādityarthaḥ | atra laukikavaidikaniyogavākyayoḥ parābhipretaṃ
[p. 195]
viśeṣamāśaṅkayati—nareti | tadviṣṭatam—athetyāditā | sādhyasādhanabhāva P. 81-22 iti viniyogavivaraṇam ; sa ca jvaranāśauṣadhapānayoḥ svargasikatābhakṣaṇayośca draṣṭavyaḥ | yo veti ; viniyogasyārthāntarakathanam | tatra niyojyo jvaraviyogakāmaḥ, viṣayaḥ pānam, niyogastu 'pibet' iti liṅghyaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yadi nāmedaṃ niyoge'napekṣaṃ vacaḥ, tathāpi viniyoge sāpekṣatvādapramāṇam | vede tu viniyoge'pi na sāpekṣatvamityanapekṣatvāt prāmāṇyamiti | evaṃ pareṇa lokavedaniyogavākyayorviśeṣa ukte, siddhāntī pratyāha— nṛbuddhīti | hanteti tarhyaye | yathā tvamāttha, itthamevaṃ tarhi puruṣabuddhipūrvatraiva vacasaḥ pramāṇāntaravyapekṣāyā nibandhanaṃ kāraṇam, tadbhāve loke sāpekṣatvāt tadabhāve vede nirapekṣatvāt ; na tadviṣaye pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavaḥ kāraṇam, yataḥ pramāṇāntarābhāve'pi laukikaniyogavākye sāpekṣatā dṛśyata ityarthaḥ | punaratraiva viśeṣāntaramāśaṅkayati—atheti | tat tasmāt vede na pramāṇāntaravyapekṣā, loke tu tadvyapekṣeti yojyam | yadvaco yatra paryavasitaṃ tasya pramāṇāntaragocaratvāgocaratvābhyāṃ tat sāpekṣamanapekṣaṃ ca yuktam, nānyatreti bhāvaḥ | siddhāntī tvāha—neti | yatheti vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntaḥ | na śabdamātrakṛto'yaṃ vibhāga ityuktam ; tatrāśaṅkayati—atheti | pramāṇāntarādarthaṃ jñātvā tatra puruṣo vacaḥ prayuṅkte | tena tat pauruṣeyaṃ vacastanniṣṭhaṃ pramāṇāntaragocaraviniyoganiṣṭham | itarattu apauruṣeyam anyathā niyoganiṣṭhatvena sthitamityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—śabdasāmarthyena niyoganiṣṭhataivautsargikī | pauruṣeye tu viniyoganiṣṭhatā tadapavādaḥ, na tu śabdasāmarthyakṛta evāyaṃ vibhāga iti | vaidikaṃ tviti ; vaidikaṃ tu śabdasāmarthyena niyogapradhānam, niyogasya kāryatvādityarthaḥ | aboddhyate—mānāntareti | saṃbhavi anyat pramāṇaṃ yatra sa saṃbhavyanyapramāṇakaḥ | tadyamarthaḥ—hanta tarhi mānāntarasāpekṣatvāt asya puruṣavacasaḥ saṃbhavyanyapramāṇako viniyogo viṣayaḥ ; na punaḥ asmān saṃbhavyanyapramāṇa- 18A
[p. 196]
