P. 83-10. katvāt asya puruṣavacaso mānāntare vyapekṣeti | tadvidhasyeti ; saṃbhavatpramāṇāntarasya viniyogasyetyarthaḥ | uktamarthaṃ sopapatikamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | mānāntarāt arthaṃ gatvā jñātvā parasya śroturarthapratipattaye puruṣo vacaḥ prayuhyakte | tena tadviri puruṣavacasi pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavāt puruṣavacasaḥ pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavedo bhavāti | ato na pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavātpuruṣavacasaḥ pramāṇāntaravyapekṣetyarthaḥ | pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatvena puruṣavacasaḥ saṃbhavatpramāṇāntaro viniyogo viṣayo jātaḥ, na tu saṃbhavatpramāṇāntaraviṣayatvenāsya mānāntaravyapekṣetyuktaṃ bhavati | tatreti ; puruṣavacasītyarthaḥ | apekṣāphalamāha—yatreti | saṃbhavatyasyetyasyāsaṃbhavadupalambhabuddhādivākyamadṛṣṭaviṣayaṃ vyatirekaḥ, na cānupalambhya ityasya ca saṃbhavadupalabdhikramapi dṛṣṭaviṣayamanāptavākyaṃ vyatireka iti vivakaḥ | svoktamarthamāvāryasaṃmatyā draḍhayati—apauruṣeyeti | tena yata eva pauruṣeyāpauruṣeyatvanibandhane sāpekṣatvānapekṣatve, tenaiva hetunetyarthaḥ | anapekṣavyapekṣatvayornimitte iti ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ | yataḥ pauruṣeyatvanibandhanaṃ sāpekṣatvam, ato puruṣabuddhijaṃ yadvedāntavākyaṃ tadbhūtārthamapi sat na sāpekṣam ; ato nāpramāṇam | atastasyānapekṣatvena prāmāṇyasiddhyarthaṃ kāryaparatvakalpanā vyartheti bhāvaḥ | kathamautapattikatvenānapekṣatvamityuktamata āha— autpattike hīti ; nitye hi śabdārthasaṃbandhe sati apauruṣeyatvāt pramāṇānapekṣatvaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu satsaṃprayogasūtreṇa pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavo'pyuktaḥ ; tato'saṃbhavo'napekṣatve heturiti siddhyarthatve pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavāt sāpekṣatvenāprāmāṇyaṃ mā bhūditi tadasaṃbhavāya kāryārthatvaṃ vedāntānāmavaśyamāśrayaṇīyamityabhiprāyeṇa codayati—nanviti | siddhāntī tu satyamuktaṃ pramāṇāntarā saṃbhavaḥ ; kiṃtu pramāṇāntare sati codanaivaityavadhāraṇaṃ na syāt ; atastadarthaṃ sa uktaḥ ; na tvanapekṣatvena codanāprāmāṇyārthamityāha—satyamiti | yadi pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave'pi codanāprāmāṇyaṃ syāt, tataḥ kiṃ nirākaraṇena ? tena sāpekṣatvena codanāprāmāṇyavyāghāto mā bhūditi pramāṇāntaranirākaraṇaṃ kṛtam ; ato yuktaṃ siddhyarthānāṃ ca vedāntānāṃ saṃbhava-
[p. 197]
tpramāṇāntarārthatvena sāpekṣatvādaprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavāya kāryaparatvamāśrayitavyamityabhiprāyeṇa paraḥ pṛcchati—atheti | siddhāntī tu P. 84-8. pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavāt sāpekṣatvaṃ mā bhūtviti nedaṃ tannirākaraṇasya prayojanam ; apitu dharmatattvajñānam ; sāpekṣatvamapauruṣeyatvādeva dūranirastāvakāśamityabhiprāyeṇāha—dharmeti | kuta ityata āha—pramāṇeti | pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave hi buddhādipratyakṣaṇocaraśraityavandanādirapi dharmaḥ syāt ; tatrāgnihotrādireva dharmatattvamavadhāritaṃ na syādityarthaḥ | kiṃ tenāvadhāriteneti cet, ata āha—taditi | ayaṃ yatna iti ; pramāṇāntarānirākaraṇayatna ityarthaḥ | atha codanāprāmāṇyārtha eva sa kiṃ na syādityata āha—codaneti | codanāprāmāṇyamapi dharmatattvanirṇayārthamevānviṣyate | ataḥ sa eva pradhānam, na codanāprāmāṇyam | na ca pradhānārthatve saṃbhavatyapradhānārthatociteti bhāvaḥ | athavā pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave viruddhārthamapi kadācitsyāt ; yathā agnīṣomīyahiṃsāderadharmatve vihārapraveśādeśca dharmatve buddhapratyakṣam ; tatra ca durbalatve codanāyāstena bādhā syāt, tulyabalatve ca saṃśayaḥ saṃbhavet ; tena bādhasaṃśayotthāprāmāṇyanivṛttaye pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavaḥ pratipādyate satsaṃprayogasūtreṇa ; na tu pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave sāpekṣatānibandhanāprāmāṇyanivṛttyarthamityāha—athaveti | nanu yadi virodhipramāṇāntare saṃbhavati śabdo na pramāṇaṃ syāt ; tatprāmāṇyāya tarhi virodhinaḥ pramāṇāntarasyābhāvo'pekṣaṇīyaḥ ; ataśca sāpekṣatvaṃ tadavastham | bhūtārtheṣu ca vedānteṣu yeṣu pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavānna tadabhāvaḥ, teṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na syādityabhiprāyeṇa codayati—nanviti | siddhāntī tu kāmamapekṣyatāṃ virodhipramāṇāntarābhāvaḥ, tatrāpi na tāvacchabdasya sāpekṣatvam ; pramāṇāntarāpekṣatvaṃ hi sāpekṣatvam ; na ca pramāṇāntarābhāvaḥ pramāṇāntaram, anavabodhakatvādityāha—kāmamiti | yadi ca bhūtārtheṣu virodhipramāṇāntarasaṃbhavamātreṇāprāmāṇyaṃ syāt, tataḥ pratyakṣādāvapi bhūtārthe tatsyādityāha—virodhīti | kiṃca virodhipramāṇāntara saṃbhavādaprāmāṇyaṃ kāryaniṣṭhatve'pi vedāntānāmaparihārthamityāha—apiceti | kuta ityāha—kartavyeti | kāryaniṣṭhatve'pi vedāntānāṃ pratipattikartavyatāpekṣitapratipattavyātmasvarūpabhūtārthapratipatteriṣṭhatvāt, tatra ca pramāṇāntaravirodha-
[p. 198]
saṃbhāvanā yato'stītyarthaḥ | evaṃ tāvat ‘na tu tadabhāvo'pekṣaṇīyaḥ’ iti paroktaṃ dūṣitam | adhunā parābhiprāyamanubhāṣya tatra pūrvoktaṃ doṣa-manukīrtya tamupajīvya ‘bhūtārthamapi’ ityupasaṃhāraparaṃ ślokārthaṃ yojayati— P. 84-19 bhūtārthasyeti | teneti ; pareṇetyarthaḥ | kilaśabdo'rucidyotanārthaḥ | kārya-niṣṭhatve tu svatantreṇetyatra ‘arthaṃ pratipādayati’ ityanuṣajyate | tatra gocaratvāt ityantena parābhiprāyānubhāṣaṇam | ‘uktena’ ityādinā ‘na ca saṃbhava-mātraṃ sāpekṣatvam’ ityantenoktadoṣānukīrtanam | bhūtārthamapītyādinā copa-saṃhāra iti nivekaḥ | idānīṃ vedāntānāmavidhiśeṣatve kriyāṃ vinā padārthasaṃsarga eva na syāditi paroktaṃ doṣamāśaṅkayati—atheti | kriyayā vinā padārthā na saṃsargabhājo bhavanti ; tasmāt ‘satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma’ ityādipadārthānāṃ tava saṃsargo na syāditi bhāvaḥ | taddūṣayati—kimiti | kriyāṃ vinā cenna saṃsargaḥ, astu tarhi kriyā ; kimāyātaṃ vidheḥ ? yena tadanupraveśo vedāntānāṃ varṇyata ityarthaḥ | nanu sāpyaśrūyamāṇā vedānteṣu durlabhetyata āha—sāpīti | sāpi kriyā astyādirūpā ka na sulabhā ? apitu sarvatraiva sulabhā ; yatrānyat kriyāpadaṃ na śrūyate, tatra “astirbhavantīparaḥ prayoktavyaḥ” iti nyāyenāstikriyā sulabhaivetyarthaḥ | ‘astiḥ’ iti dhātunā tadarthaṃ lakṣayati ; “yajātastu dravyadevatākriyam” itivat | ādiśabdena yathāsaṃbhavamanyāḥ kriyā gṛhyante ; yathā ‘dvāraṃ dvāram’ ityatra ‘apātriyatāṃ, saṃtriyatām’ iti | vedānteṣvastikriyaiva vivakṣitā | etadvivṛṇoti—yadītyādi | ākhyātapadārthaḥ kriyā, tāṃ vinā na prātipadikārthānāṃ saṃsargaḥ ; yataḥ kriyāprātipadikārthānāmanyonyamaruṇaikahāyanīnyāyena saṃśleṣahetuḥ kṛtaḥ | prātipadikārthānāmanyonyasaṃsargabhāve kriyāyāḥ sākāṅkṣatvāt ; kriyā hi sādhyā, prātipadikārthaśca siddhaḥ ; sādhyaṃ ca siddhamapekṣate ; tacca viśiṣṭaṃ padārthāntarasaṃsargeṇa viśiṣṭaṃ bhavati, ekahāyanī-vāruṇinnā | tenānyonyaṃ nānyārthā kriyā saṃsargamākāṅkṣate ; ākāṅkṣā-hetukaśca padārthānāṃ saṃsargaḥ | tena kriyā teṣāṃ saṃsargahetuḥ | atastathā vinā na bhūtārthasaṃsargasiddhirityarthaḥ | ataḥ ko doṣa ityata āha—ata
[p. 199]
iti | na śabdagocara iti ; na padārtha ityarthaḥ | aviditasaṃgatitve P. 85-8. brahmaṇo vācyasya pramāṇāntareṇāvagamo hetuḥ | tatra siddhāntyāha—evamiti | padārthasiddhiriti ; brahmapadārtho'bhipretaḥ | kaścidartha ityarthaśabdaḥ prayojanārthaḥ | sā cāstyādikriyatvanvayaḥ | atra hetumāha—astirityanena | yatrānyakriyāpadaṃ na dṛśyata iti nyāyo darśitaḥ | itirhetau ; asmācyādityarthaḥ | bhavantī vartamānārthā vibhaktiḥ | tadevamastikriyālābhe tannivandhana eva vedānteṣu padārthasaṃsarge na doṣa ityāha—tatreti ; astikriyālābhe satītyarthaḥ | nanvastyarthaniṣṭhaṃ cedvākyam , kathaṃ svarūpapratipattiparam ? na hyekaṃ vākyamubhayaparaṃ yujyate, vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | ato'styarthaniṣṭhatve vedāntānāṃ na tataḥ satyānantādirūpasya viśiṣṭasya brahmavastunaḥ siddhirityāśaṅkyāha—sattāyā iti | 'devadattaḥ pacati' iti kartā kriyāśeṣaḥ ; kriyaiva tu sādhyatayā pradhānam | satyajñānānantarūpaṃ brahmāstīti tu brahmapradhāna evāstyarthaḥ , na pākavatpradhānatayā pratīyate | nanvatra dvitīyā nāsti ; kathaṃ kārakapradhānatvata āha—prakṛtyaiveti | svabhāva eṣa sattāyāḥ satpradhānatā | kiṃca 'brahmāsti' iti sattāviśiṣṭaṃ brahma pratīyate ; ato viśeṣyatvādapi svatastasya pradhānatetyarthaḥ | atra sattāsāmānyāpahavavādina āhuḥ—pramāṇāvagamyataiva sattā, na tato'nyeti ; tadāha—atreti | tatra hetumāha—tathāhīti | viparyaya iti ; pramāṇānavagamya ityarthaḥ | nanvayaṃ sadasadvibhāgahetuḥ kiṃ na syādityata āha—na khalviti | nanu kimityanyo heturna lakṣyate ? yāvatārthakriyābhāvābhāvāveva hetū bhaviṣyataḥ ; tathāhi—yadarthakriyākāri tatsat, anyattu nṛśṛṅgādyasadityucyata ityāśaṅkyati—artheti | taddṛṣayati—neti | arthakriyābhāvābhāvāvapyarthakriyāgrāhiṇaḥ pramāṇasya bhāvābhāvābhyām , nānyataḥ | evaṃ cedvastusadasattvamapi tābhyāmevāstu ; kimarthakriyābhāvābhāvābhyām ? iti bhāvaḥ | nanu arthakriyābhāvābhāvāvapyarthakriyābhāvābhāvanivandhanau bhaviṣyataḥ ; na pramāṇabhāvābhāvanivandhanāvityāśaṅkyāha—tathāhīti | tadviparyayāditi ; pramāṇābhāvādityarthaḥ | evaṃ pramāṇavipayatāṃ sattāṃ prasādhya prakṛte yojayati— tathāceti | tathāca vedāntānāmastyarthaniṣṭhatve pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatvenā-
[p. 200]
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt prāmāṇyāya teṣāṃ kāryaniṣṭhatvameṣṭavyamiti bhāvaḥ | taddūṣa- P. 86-18 yati—atreti | itiśabdo buddhiśabdau nirdiśati ; tenāyamarthaḥ— ‘asti’iti buddheḥ śabdasya ca mānāvagamyatvaṃ na ca viṣayo matam ; na mānāvagamyatā satteyarthaḥ | ‘asti’ ityavivakṣitakālanirdeśaḥ, ata eva abhūt, asti, bhaviṣyatīti buddhiḥ | jāte hi māne mānaviṣayatā, na pūrvareva ; ataśca na mānaviṣayatvam ‘asti’ iti buddherviṣayaḥ | tato dhūmādibhyo vahnyādau jāte māne paścāt ‘asti’ iti buddhiḥ syāt | sā tu svayaṃmānaiva ‘asti’ iti jāyate | ato mānaviṣayatvādanyā vastusvabhāvabhūtā sattāstīti bhāvaḥ | ‘asti’buddhāveva dūṣaṇopapattau bhūtabhaviṣyadarthabuddhyupanyāso vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇārthaḥ | atha māne jāte paścāt ‘asti’buddhirbhavatītyucyate, tatrāha—bhidyeteti | pramāṇāduttarakāle ‘asti’ iti buddhirbhavatīti na vidhā bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamānaviṣayatvena bhidyeta, api tu vartamānaviṣayaiva syāt | yadyapi vastu trikālam, tathāpi pramāṇasaṃbandhastriṣvapi vartamāna evetyarthaḥ | vāśabdaḥ pakṣāntaravikalpārthaḥ | nāstīti buddheriti ; ‘asti’ iti buddheḥ tatpramāṇāvagamyatvaṃ na viṣaya iti yojyam | pūraviśeṣāditi ; setubhimittapūraniṣṭhasyarthaṃ viśeṣagrahaṇam | meghodbhaviśeṣāditi ca virodhivāyusaṃyuktameghanivṛttyartham | vaiyākhyāditi ; dhāṣṭarcādityarthaḥ | atha svātmasaṃbandhitayā yā pramāṇaviṣayatā, saiva dhūmādiliṅgatrayajena pramāṇena gṛhyate ; ato'styarthabuddheḥ pramāṇaviṣayāyā api nottarakālatā, na ca mite syādityucyate ; tathāpi trikāleṣvartheṣu tatpramāṇāvagamyatayā vartamānatvādastyarthabuddhirna tridhā bhidyeta | kiṃca itthaṃ sati svaviṣayatayaivā styarthaḥ pramāṇena gṛhyata ityastyarthaniṣṭhe'pi vacasi pramāṇāntarāpekṣā na syāt | ato yaduktam—tathā cāstyarthaniṣṭhaṃ vākyaṃ pramāṇāntarāpekṣamiti, tadayuktamiti | tadetat sarvamāha—atheti | atha svaviṣayatā liṅgena pramāṇena na gṛhyate, kiṃtu pramāṇāntaraviṣayatā ; kiṃ tat pramāṇāntaramiti cet, pratyakṣamiti hyamaḥ | tacca trikālam ; tathāhi—bhūte bhūtaṃ kasyacitpratyakṣam, anāgate anāgatam
[p. 201]
vartamāne vartamānam | ataḥ pratyakṣasya traikālyāt tadviṣayatāpi trikāleti ‘abhūt, asti, bhaviṣyati’ iti tridhā bhedopapattiḥ | pramāṇāntarāpekṣatvāccāstyarthaniṣṭhatve vedāntānāmapramāṇyaprasaṅgāt kāryaniṣṭhatvameṣṭavyamiti cet; ucyate—tadasāram | evaṃ hi sati nityānumeye paramāṇavapratyakṣe pramāṇāntaraviṣayatā nāstīti sthūladarśanena paramāṇurnānumīyeta | tathā mantraliṅgānumeyā viniyojikā śrutirapyatrodāhāryā | kiñcaivaṃ sati na kevalaṃ vedāntānāmeva sāpekṣatvādaprāmāṇyam, apitu liṅgasyāpi dhūmādeḥ syāt; ato'numānamapi pramāṇaṃ na syāt; tadetadāha—anyeti | atrāśaṅkayati— P. 86-18. tatsiddhāṃreti | tata eva dhūmāderliṅgāt tatsiddheḥ pramāṇāntarasya siddheḥ prasiddheḥ jñānāt liṅgasya nairapekṣyaṃ yadītyarthaḥ; saṃvādakapramāṇāntarānapekṣāṃ hi nairapekṣyaṃ vyāharanti | iha tu pramāṇāntaraṃ na saṃvādāyāpekṣyate, apitu pramīyate | tatpramitau ca liṅgaṃ nirapekṣameva | taddvāreṇa cāptavākyavadarthasiddhiḥ | ataḥ prāmāṇyaṃ liṅgasyetyabhiprāyaḥ | vecchabdaḥ pādapūraṇārthaḥ | yadvā tata eva vettatsiddhiḥ, tathā sati na nairapekṣyaṃ yadīti yojyam | atrottaramāha— neti | itthaṃ yannairapekṣyaṃ tadastyarthaniṣṭhe'pi vede rājadaṇḍairlaguḍairvāpi na nivāryate | vedānteṣvapyetat samānam | teṣāmapi hi pramāṇāntarasya tataḥ siddheḥ tatpramitidvāreṇa cārthasiddheḥ prāmāṇyamapratihatamityarthaḥ | tatra paro vedāntaliṅgayorvaiṣamyamāha—tatsaṃbhava iti | laiṅgike nadīpūradhūmādiliṅgagranthe dṛṣṭyādāvarthe tatsaṃbhavaḥ pramāṇāntarasya saṃbhavaḥ | tena tatra tatpramitidvāreṇārthasiddheryuktaṃ prāmāṇyam; na tvatra vedāntaikavedabrahmaṇīti yat, tadasamañjasam; kutaḥ ? brahmaṇyapi sākṣātkaraṇābhyupagamāt sākṣātkāri jñānamasti | atastatpramitidvāreṇārthasiddhau vedāntānāmapi prāmāṇyamapratihatamiti bhāvaḥ | evamiti; pramāṇāntarapramitisiddhestatpramitidvāreṇa vārthadvāreṇa vā tatprameyasiddherityarthaḥ | arthe tasyeti; tasya liṅgasyārthaṃ viṣaye pramāṇāntarasya pratyakṣasya saṃbhavādityarthaḥ | buddhādivākyānāmatīndriyārthopadeśināmiti vyavahitenānvayaḥ | ‘evamayaṃ puruṣo veda’ iti pauruṣeyavākyatvenaucityaprāptamanumitam, na ca buddhavākyeṣu jñānaniṣṭhāsti, atīndriye'rthe jñānasyāsaṃbhavāt | ato jñānaniṣṭhānāmiti jñānasyāsaṃbhavāditi cānayorna
[p. 202]
virodhaḥ | nanu brahma sākṣāccet bhāti, kathaṃ tarhi ‘āmrāyataḥ prasiddhiṃ pa. 87-12. ca’ ityatrāmrāyaikapramāṇakaṃ taducyata ityāśaṅkyāha—avidyāvatāmiti | yadyapyavidyāvatāmapi ātmā sākṣādbhāti, tathāpi na brahmarūpeṇa ; api tvahaṃkārānubahena jaivarūpeṇa | vidyāvatāṃ tu nirmuktāhaṃkārādigranthiḥ sākṣādbhātīti viśeṣaḥ | nanu pramāṇaviṣayatā sattā ; nityātmaprakāśāvirmāvaḥ pramāṇam | na hi tatpratyakṣam, anindriyajatvāt ; anumānādi tu dūranirastāvakāśam | na ca brahmaṇastadviṣayatā ; bhinnayorhi viṣayaviṣayibhāvo bhavati ; na ca brahmatatprakāśayorbhedo'sti, dvaitaprasaṅgāt | ucyate— pramāṇaviṣayatāṃ sattāmicchatāpi pramāṇaphalamanubhavaḥ saṃdiṣṭaḥ | na sa pramāṇaviṣayaḥ, svayaṃprakāśatvābhyupagamāt | atastasya sattā na syāt | na caivamiṣṭam | ataḥ pratibhāsamānatāmātraṃ pramāṇaviṣayatā parasyeṣṭā, na tu viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ | tādṛśī ca sā brahmaṇyapyastīti siddhāntī manyate | yattu nedaṃ pratyakṣam, anindriyajatvādiyuktam ; tatra brūmaḥ—na ‘indriyajaṃ pratyakṣam’ iti pratyakṣalakṣaṇam ; apitu sākṣātpratītiḥ ; anyathā anubhavo'pratyakṣaḥ syāt | sā ca brahmaṇyapyastīti na doṣaḥ | yadvindriyajaṃ sūtrakṛtoktam, tadvadviviṣaye sākṣātpratītistīndriyajā | tena dharmādau buddhāderbhāvanābalajā na yujyate | ato dharme codanaiva pramāṇamiti darśayitumiti sarvamanākulam | yattṛktam—pramāṇāntarasaṃbandhasya traikālyāt ‘abhūt, asti, bhaviṣyati’ iti traividhyamiti, tadapyayuktamityāha—apiceti | yadi mīyamānatā sattā, tataḥ pramāṇāntaramapi mīyamānatvāt sadeva ; sat vartamānam ; atastattveva traikālyamasiddhamiti samudāyārthaḥ | idānīṃ dūṣaṇāntarābhidhitsayā vikalpayati—apiceti | pramāṇasaṃbandha iti ślokasthayogavivaraṇam | tadviparyayo'satteti ; vartamānapramāṇāsaṃbandho'sattetyarthaḥ | anāśritakālaviśeṣamityatra ‘pramāṇasaṃbandhaḥ sattā, tadviparyayo'sattā’ ityanuṣajjanīyam | tadā ca pramāṇamātrāyogo viparyayo'bhimataḥ | tatrāye tāvat pakṣe doṣamāha—tatreti | mate iti ; sattāsatte samuccitya dvivakṣanam | pūrvapramitaṃ hi smaryate ; ato'sya vartamānapramāṇa-
[p. 203]
saṃbandhaḥ svayamapramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | P. 88-1. siddhāntāha—satyamiti | nāstīti syāditi ; smṛtau vartamānapramāṇayogābhāvādityāśayaḥ | dvitīye pakṣe doṣamāha—pramāṇeti | anāśritakālaviśeṣapramāṇayogamātraṃ cet sattā syāt, tato bhūtenāpi pramāṇayogena pūto'pi ghaṭaḥ saṃprati san syāt | tataśca naṣṭo'pyanaṣṭa iva madhudhāraṇādikāryaṃ kuryāt ; na caivam | ato na pramāṇayogamātraṃ sattā | naṣṭānaṣṭatvena tayorviśeṣāt, nānaṣṭavadvṛṣṭasya kāryakaratvaprasaṅga iti cet, tanna ; yadi pramāṇayogāyogau vihāya naṣṭānaṣṭatvābhyāṃ viśeṣaṃ brūṣe, sa eva tarhi naṣṭānaṣṭatvalakṣaṇo viśeṣaḥ ‘avinaṣṭaṃ sat, naṣṭamasat’ iti sadasadvyavahāraheturastu ; yathā tatra ‘anaṣṭātkāryaṃ, na naṣṭāt’ iti kāryavyavasthāyām, na tu pramāṇayoga ityarthaḥ | etadeva lokato draḍhayati—taditi ; naṣṭānaṣṭatvākhyaṃ viśeṣaṃ tadā parāmṛśati, eṣeti ca sadasadvyavahāravyavasthām | upacaratīti ; upadiśati jānāti vā, carergatyarthasya jñānārthatvāt | kiṃca pramāṇayogamātraṃ cet sattā, tadā pūrvapramitamapi saṃprati sadeveti nedānīṃ tasya sadasattvayoḥ punarjijñāsā syāt, asti ca sā | ato'pi na pramāṇayogamātraṃ sattetyarthaḥ | idānīṃ pakṣāntaramāśaṅkya dūṣayati—atheti | atha pramāṇayogyatvamarthadharmaḥ sattā ; tacca naṣṭādarthāccyutam ; dharmināśe naṣṭamiti yāvat ; ato na naṣṭasya pramāṇayogyatvamiti tasya sattvaprasaṅgaḥ | smaryamāṇe ca kvacideva pracalitaṃ dharmināśena naṣṭam, na sarvatra | ato na sarvasya sattvaprasaṅga ityucyate, tadapyamundaramityarthaḥ | tatra ‘naṣṭācca taccyutam’ ityanena naṣṭasya sattvaprasaṅgaḥ, pramite jijñāsānupapattiśca pratyuktau | ‘smaryamāṇe’ ityanena ca vartamānapramāṇayogasattāpakṣe sarvasmaryamāṇasattvaprasaṅgaḥ pratyukta iti vivektavyam | asundaratvamāha—abhyupagateti | abhyupagatahānamāha—tathāhīti | pramāṇasadasadvābhopādhiḥ sadasadvyavahāraḥ, na tu vastudharmo 'rthātmikā sattāstīti parasyābhyupagamaḥ ; sa pramāṇayogyatāmarthagatāmarthātmikāṃ sattāmicchatā parityakta ityarthaḥ | arthasvabhāva iti ; arthātmikā, na
[p. 204]
pramāṇayogātmikā ; arthasya vā svabhāva ityarthaḥ | kiṃca evaṃ sati P. 88-20 vedāntānāmastyarthaniṣṭhasāpekṣatvadoṣo'pi nāstīti vaktumupakramate—pramāṇeti | ‘pramāṇayogyaḥ’ iti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ, yogyatetyarthaḥ | dharmadharmiṇorabhedavivakṣayā cedamuktam | yadvā pramāṇayogyatārūpo'pyarthasvabhāvaḥ prameyatvāt pramāṇayogya ityarthaḥ | nanu pramāṇayogyatā sattetyākhyāyate ; atastaniṣṭhatve'pi vedāntānāṃ pramāṇāntarasaṃsparśāt sāpekṣatā tadavasthaivetyabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkyāha—tasya tviti | tasya tvarthasvabhāvasya kathanopāya eva yat pramāṇasaṃvandho nāma ; yogyatā tu pramāṇādvahirevārthātmikā, na tu pramāṇa yogātmikā ; ato na tasyāṃ pramāṇasaṃsparśo'stītyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati— yogyatā sadartha ityukte yā kācit yogyatā mā pratīyatāmiti tadviśeṣapratipattaye pramāṇayogyatetyākhyāyate ; na tu pramāṇaṃ yogyatāśarīrāntarbhūtam, yena yogyatāyāṃ sadarthaṃ pramāṇamupādhiḥ syāditi | yadviśeṣapratipattaye yat saṃkīrtyete tadeva tacchabdapravṛttādupādhirbhavatītyatra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | nīlajñānamupādhiryasyeti vigrahaḥ | paroktadoṣābhāvamidānīmāha—tathāceti | sa ityarthasvabhāva ucyate | atra paraścodayati—syāditi | siddhāntī tvāha—mā bhūditi | mā bhūdekā sattā, nāsmākametat sādhyaṃ yadekā sattetyarthaḥ | kiṃ nāma sādhyamityata āha—sarvatheti | ekā bhavatu sattā, anekā vā ; sarvathā arthātmikāṃ sattāmastiśabdo'bhidhatte, na ‘san pramāṇayoganimittaḥ’ iti etanna sādhyamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ | kimini punaḥ pramāṇayoganimittatvābhāvamātraṃ sādhyate, na sattaikatvamityata āha—pramāṇeti | pramāṇayoganimittatve sanśabdasya, astyarthaniṣṭheṣu vedāntavākyeṣu pramāṇasaṃsparśyarthaḥ syāt ; tathā sati ca teṣāṃ sāpekṣatā syāditi pramāṇayoganimittaḥ sanśabdo bhavatītyetanna sādhyam | anekatā tu sattāyā na keciddoṣamāvahatīti naikatā sādhyetyarthaḥ | etaccābhyupagamadoṣoktam | paramārthatastu, yadekā sattā nepyate tadānantairmārgaiḥ saṃvandhagrahaṇāsaṃbhavāt anantaśaktikarūpanāprasaṅgācca na teṣu ‘sat’ ityekaḥ śabdaḥ pravartate ; tasyādekā sattaiṣṭavyetyarthaḥ | nanu bhinneṣvapyabhinnaśabdapravṛttirdṛṣṭā ; yathā—
[p. 205]
govyaktiṣu gośabdasya daṇḍiṣu daṇḍaśabdasya vibhītakādiṣu cākṣaśabda syetyāśaṅkya, satyaṃ dṛṣṭvā, kiṃtu tribhiḥ prakāraiḥ ; iha tu sattāmekāṃ vinā teṣāmeko'pi prakāro nāstīti hṛdi kṛtvā tatprakāratrayaṃ tāvadupanyasyati— tathāhīti | minnaṃ vibhītakṛtvādijātitrayaṃ nimittaṃ pravṛttau yasyeti vigrahaḥ | P. 89-6. tatra svabuddhyā-rajyate yena viśeṣyaṃ taddhi viśeṣaṇam ; upalakṣaṇaṃ tu cihnamātram ; taccātītamapi bhavati | na ca pramāṇayogyeṣvastiśabdapravṛttāveṣāmanyatamaḥ prakāro'stīti darśayituṃ sādhāraṇaśabdaṃ tāvannirasyati—na tāvaditi | anantāśca te arthasvabhāvā iti vigrahaḥ | hetumāha— saṃbandheti | saṃbandhino gṛhītvā hi saṃbandho grāhyaḥ ; na cānantā grahītuṃ śakyāḥ saṃbandhinaḥ | atastaistacchabdasya saṃbandhagrahaṇāsāmarthyamityarthaḥ | akṣādiṣu tu sādhāraṇaśabdatvaṃ yuktamityāha—parimiteti | vibhītakṛtvādijātiviṣayāḥ, tadupalakṣitā vākṣādiśabdāḥ | tena tatra parimitajātitrayeṇa taddāreṇa vā anantābhirapi taddhyaktibhiḥ saṃbandhagrahaṇasaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | nanu mamāpi dravyatvādijātitrayopalakṣitānāmanantānāmapyarthānāṃ sacchabdaḥ sādhāraṇo bhaviṣyati, kiṃ sattayeti codayati—syāditi | etacca tripadārthasāṃkarī satteti dṛṣṭyoktam | taddūṣayati—tanneti | kathamityāha— na kileti | na hi sarṣapaṃ dṛṣṭavato merau ‘tadevedam’ iti parāmarśo'stīti sattāpahave yā yuktiḥ, sā dravyatvādyapahave'pi tulyā yana ityarthaḥ | kiṃca jātyādiṣvapi sacchabdo'sti, sa dravyatvādinimittatve na syādityāha— apiceti | nanvaupacārikasteṣu sacchabda ityāha—na teṣviti | hetumāha—pratyaya iti | yatra hi pratyayavailakṣaṇyaṃ tatrānyaśabdo'nyatropacaritaḥ syāt ; yathā agniśabdo māṇavake | jātyādiṣu ‘sat’ iti pratyayasyāvailakṣaṇyam ; ato nopacāra ityarthaḥ | taduktam—“jātyādiṣvapi sadbuddheḥ sattā tajjātiṣu sthitā” iti | kiṃca śauryādisāmānyāt siddhādiśabdānāmupacāro dṛṣṭaḥ ; na ca dravyāditrayasya jātyāditraye kiñcidekaṃ sāmānyamasti ; ato nopacāra ityāha—na ceti | athāsti sāmānyamekam ; tataḥ siddhamasmadiṣṭamityāha—satīti | tulyatvāditi ; dravyādiṣu-ca ‘sat’ iti pratyayāviśeṣādityarthaḥ |
[p. 206]
prakārāntareṇa sacchabdasya sādhāraṇanāmāśaṅkayati—atheti | kaṇasugādipravaktṛṇāṃ pravādeṣu pravacaneṣu sarve padārthāḥ parimitaiḥ kaiścit ṣaḍbhiḥ pañcabhirvā dharmaiḥ ṣaṭpañcādiprabhedena saṃgṛhītāḥ | tataśca ṣaṭpañcādiparimitadharmopalakṣaṇānāmanantānāmapyarthānāṃ ‘san’ iti sādhāraṇaśabdo bhaviṣyati, yathā vibhītakatvādijātitrayopalakṣaṇānāmanantānāṃ vyaktīnāmakṣaśabdaḥ ityarthaḥ | tatra guṇavaccaṃ dravyāśrayatvaṃ nirguṇatvaṃ saṃyogavibhāgayoranapekṣakāraṇatvam anuvṛttabuddhihetutvam atyantatryāvṛttabuddhihetutvam ihapratyayahetutvamityetairdharmaidravyādiṣaṭpadārthāḥ kaṇasukpravāde saṃgṛhītāḥ | evamanyeṣvapi pravādeṣu vācyam | taddūṣayati—tadeti | ayuktatvamāha—saṃgraha iti | na hi ‘ṣaḍ gāvaḥ’ ityekena gotvena vinā ṣaḍ govyaktayaḥ saṃgrahītuṃ śakyāḥ | tatra sattānamyupagamādyadi sacchabdatāyāṃ saṃgrahaḥ—kriyate—ṣaṭprabhedaḥ sacchabdārtha iti, tataḥ sacchabdārthasya ṣaṭsaṃkhyātvamuktaṃ syāt, na tu padārthāntaratvam | tat kṛtsnaṃ jaganna saṃgṛhītaṃ syāt ; yathā ‘navavidho gośabdārthaḥ’ ityukte gośabdārthasya navavidhatvamuktam, na punargośabdārthādanye na santīti | tatraitāvatprabhedā eva jagatyarthāḥ, nānye ityeṣārthatattvāvagatiḥ pravādebhyo na syāditi kṛtsnajagatsaṃgrahāya pravṛttāḥ pravādāstadabhāve vyarthāḥ syurityarthaḥ | para āha—nanviti | sacchabdasya sādhāraṇatvābhāvastava sādhyaḥ, na pravādānāmarthavattā | ato yadi nāma te'pyarthāḥ, tathāpi tadupadarśitaiḥ parimitairdharmaiḥ sacchabdasādhāraṇatvopapattena tavābhimatasādhyasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | siddhāntāntaradūṣaṇākhyaṃ nigrahasthānaṃ cedam | siddhāntī tvāha—neti | sādhyamāha—kiñciti | pravādinaḥ pravaktāraḥ kaṇādādayaḥ | hetumāha—yata iti | yathā ‘ṣaḍ gāvaḥ’ iti naikamupasaṃgrāhakaṃ gotvaṃ vinā ṣaṭsaṃkhyāniveśaḥ, tathā pañca ṣaḍityādipadārthasaṃgrahe'pi naikaṃ sāmānyaṃ vinā pañcaṣaḍādisaṃkhyāniveśaḥ | tasmādekaṃ kiñcitsāmānyaṃ pravādinaḥ pratiṇdyante; tacca sattetyarthaḥ | bauddhānāṃ vijñānavādināṃ rūpavijñānavedanāsaṃjñāsaṃskārākhyāḥ pañca padārthāḥ ; kaṇasujo dravyādayaḥ ṣaṭ ; ādiśabdena sāṃkhyādyuktapañcaviṃśatyādayo draṣṭavyāḥ | nanu yadyekamupasaṃgrāhakaṃ vinā na saṃkhyāniveśaḥ, tarhi pramāṇayogyatāyāmarthakriyāsāmarthye caikasminnupasaṃgraho'stu, kiṃ sattayetyāśaṅkayati—atheti |
[p. 207]
evamāśaṅkya dūṣayati—tābhyāmiti | yadi ca P. 89-25. jagadvartyakapramāṇayogyatvamarthakriyāsāmarthyaṃ vopasaṃgrāhakamiṣyate, tato varaṃ tābhyāmeva sacchabdārtha upalakṣyatām, prat pramāṇayogyamarthakriyāsamarthaṃ vā tat sacchabdārtha iti ; te eva sacchabdapravṛttau nimittamiti yāvat | tataśca parimitadharmopanyāso vyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca sacchabdasya sādhāraṇatāpi na syādityāha—tatreti | yo bhinnanimitto bhinneṣvartheṣvekaḥ śabdaḥ sa sādhāraṇaḥ, yathā akṣādiḥ | eke eva tu jagadvartinī pramāṇayogyatvārthakriyāsāmarthye nimitte iti sacchabdasya sāmānyaśabdataiva syāt, na sādhāraṇatā | atha te pratyarthabhinne, na tarhi upasaṃgrāhake ; yat ekamupasaṃgrāhakamiti bhāvaḥ | sacchabdasya sādhāraṇaśabdatāyāṃ doṣāntaramāha—bhinneti | yathā ‘akṣamānaya’ ityukte vibhītakatvādijātīnāṃ nimittabhūtānāṃ bhedāt kena nirmitenāya pravṛtta iti saṃdeho bhavati, tathā ‘san ghaṭaḥ’ ityatrāpi syāt, na sattākhyaviśeṣapratītiḥ ; na ca so'sti, ‘śuklaḥ paṭaḥ’ itivat ‘san ghaṭaḥ’ ityatra pratipattunairākāṅkṣyādityarthaḥ | nanu sattāyāmapi dravyaṃ guṇaḥ karma veti, tathā rūpamarthaḥ san vijñānaṃ veti saṃśayādastyevākṣāditukhyatvamityāśaṅkyāha— yastviti | satyamastyayaṃ saṃśayaḥ, kiṃtu vādināṃ, na tu laukikānām ; ato laukikāḥ sacchabdapravṛttāvabhinnameva nimittaṃ pratipadyanta ityavagacchām ityarthaḥ | kiṃca sādhāraṇaśabdāt śabdotthaḥ ‘ko'syārthaḥ’ iti saṃśayo bhavati ; yathā akṣādeḥ | jātiśabdārthamasaṃśayaṃ pratītya ‘kiṃtvarūpo'yam’ ityarthotthastadviśeṣasaṃśayaḥ ; yathā gośabdādgotvaṃ pratītya vyakterbhinnamabhinnaṃ veti vādinām | sacchabdasya tu ‘ko'syārthaḥ’ iti na saṃśayaḥ ; kiṃtu sattākhyamarthaṃ pratītya paścāt ‘dravyaṃ guṇaḥ karma vā’ iti tadantaraviśeṣe | tasmādeṣa sādhāraṇaśabda iti | tadāha—athacciti | nātraśabdāt saṃśayaḥ ; kiṃtu arthottha iti | atra hetuṃ pṛcchati—kuta iti | uttaramāha— viśeṣa iti | ‘dravyaṃ guṇaḥ karma vā’ iti sattāyāṃ ye viśeṣā vādivipratipattiviṣayāḥ, tatpratītiṃ vināpi ‘san ghaṭaḥ’ iti ghaṭasattāpratītimātreṇaiva pratipattenairākāṅkṣyāt nātra śabdāt saṃśaya ityarthaḥ | nanvarthotye'pi saṃśaye tadviśeṣākāṅkṣāstyeva ; atha kathaṃ nairākāṅkṣyamityata āha—
[p. 208]
P. 90-6. yatreti | anyatheti; yadi tatrāpi sādhanaviśeṣākāṅkṣā syāt, tatastadviśeṣākāṅkṣāyāmanavasthā syāt; ato dūramapi gatvā yasya kasyacidupādānānnairākāṅkṣayeṇa bhavitavyam; tadvaramādāveva tadastīti bhāvaḥ | kva tarhi sākāṅkṣatvamityata āha—yatra liti | yatra tu ‘aśvamānaya’ ityakṣaśabdādanekārthāt ‘ko'syārthaḥ’ iti saṃśayo bhavati, tatrārthasyaivānavadhāraṇādapravṛttiriti bhavatyarthaviśeṣākāṅkṣā | sacchabdāttu ‘ko'syārthaḥ’ ityākāṅkṣā nāstyeva; apitu ‘kiṃsvabhāvo'syārthaḥ’ iti vaiṣamyamiti bhāvaḥ | upasaṃharati—naditi; tasmādityarthaḥ | evamiti anantaroktaṃ yuktiprakāraṃ nirdiśati | sacchabda ityadhyāhāryam | idānīṃ viśeṣaṇapakṣaṃ dūṣayati—viśeṣaṇamiti | nanu pramāṇaṃ pramātṛdharmaḥ kathamarthasya viśeṣaṇam? abhyupagamavādo'yamityadoṣaḥ | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—apiceti | pramāṇaphalādanubhavādatītameva pramātṛvyāpārarūpamanumīyate; tatra kutaḥ pramāṇaviśiṣṭatārthasyetyarthaḥ | atra hetumāha—neti | atītatvenāsaṃnihitaṃ pramāṇākhyaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘san ghaṭaḥ’ ityanubhavasamaye pramāṇasyātītatvānna tadviśiṣṭaḥ ‘dhaṭaḥ san’ iti bhāyādityarthaḥ | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—na ceti | ciranikhātā nidhayo yeṣviti bahuvrīhiḥ | ayaṃ ca pramāṇabhāvaniścaye hetuḥ | vyadhikaraṇe vā saptabhyau | ‘santi’ ityatra ‘nidhayaḥ’ iti prathamāntaṃ kṛtvā yojyam | upasaṃharati— tasmāditi | sadartha iti; sacchabdasyārtha ityarthaḥ | idānīmupalakṣaṇamapi pramāṇaṃ na yuktamityāha—nāpnoti | hetumāha— pramiteti | yataḥ pramitanaṣṭe sacchabdo loke na prayujyate, yataśca ‘asti, nāsti’ iti smarthamāṇe saṃdeho'stītyarthaḥ | nanvatītatvādeva pramāṇasaṃbandhasya pramitanaṣṭe sacchabdāprayogasaṃdehāvupapatsyete ityāha—chatteti | atītamapyupalakṣaṇaṃ śabdapravṛttau naṣṭam | tataścātītasyāpi pramāṇatvopalakṣaṇatvāt saccameva syāt, nāsaccam | smarthamāṇe ca saccaniścaya eva syāt, na saṃśaya ityarthaḥ | vṛttaḥ pramāṇasaṃbandho yasyeti bahuvrīhiḥ | nanu mā bhavatu pramāṇamekamupalakṣaṇaṃ vā, pramāṇayogyatvameva tadvividhyati; tathāpi sattā na
[p. 209]
sidhyati | kathaṃ ca viśeṣaṇamapyekaṃ pramāṇamityuktam ? taddhi pratiprameyaṃ bhinnamucyate ; na pramāṇayogyatvaṃ nāma kaścidasti | ataḥ pāriśeṣyāt pramāṇameva viśeṣaṇamupalakṣaṇaṃ vāsyeyamiti manyate | pramāṇayogyatāṃ vinā kathamarthasya prameyatvamiti cet ; na, pramāṇasāmarthyādevātītānāgatavadupapatteḥ | na hyatītānāgatayorasatoḥ pramāṇayogyatvamasti, asati dharmiṇi dharmānupapatteḥ | ataśca pramāṇayogyatvameva viśeṣaṇamastu, kiṃ sattayetyuktam | kiṃca sattāpahvanyāyena pramāṇayogyatvamapyekaṃ tairneṣṭam, teṣāmānantyena ekaśabda-pravṛttāvanimittatvāt | yadapi pratiprameyaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhinnaṃ na tvekamityuktam, tadapi pācakādivadupapatterayuktamityuktam | idānīmarthakriyāyāḥ sacchabdapravṛttau viśeṣaṇatvopalakṣaṇatve nirasyati— yo'pīti | yadarthakriyāvat tat sat | tatrāṅkurajanmanaḥ prāk bījaṃ P. 90-19. ‘sat’ iti nocyeta | atha bījapratyakṣe bījamapi janakam, anyathendriyasāmarthyāviśeṣānna tanniyatārthaṃ syāt ; ato'pi svaviṣayapramāṇajananamevārthakriyā ; tato bījaṃ ‘sat’ iti vyapadeśyata iti cet, tanna ; evaṃ hi pramāṇajananākhyāmarthakriyāṃ vijñāya paścāt ‘asti’ iti buddhiḥ syāt, na pramāṇādeva ; na caivam ; vijñāyamānameva bījam ‘asti’ iti jñāyata ityarthaḥ | atha nirvikalpaṃ pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam | ‘asti’buddhistu śabdānuvedhāt savikalpikā | nirvikalpapūrvakaṃ ca savikalpakam | ataḥ pramāṇottarakāla eva ‘asti’ buddhiḥ, na pramāṇādevetyucyate ; evaṃ tarhi liṅgāddhūmāderagnyādau ‘asti’ buddhirna syādityarthaḥ | anumānaṃ savikalpakameveṣyate ; na tatra pṛṣṭhabhāvinirvikalpakamasti, yajjananenārthakriyayā paścāt ‘asti’ iti buddhiḥ savikalpikā syāditi bhāvaḥ | athocyate—yadyapyanumāne nirvikalpakaṃ neṣyate, tasya sattā cāstibuddhyā na gṛhyate ; tathāpi kāryeṇa kāraṇamanumeyam | ataśca ‘asyedaṃ kāryam’ iti nirjñātadhūmādikāryasaṃbandhamevāgnyādiliṅgena dhūmādinā anu paścādramyate | dhūmādikāryavattālakṣaṇaiva sattā dhūmādikāryavattayā gamyata ityuktaṃ bhavatītyatrāha—tathāhīti | kāryavattā sattā | sā cedagnyāderjñātaiva, kiṃ paramadhikamanumīyate ? kāryavattayaiva ca kāryavattāvagamyata ityaśobhanam, ekasya sādhyasādhanatvavirodhāditi bhāvaḥ | etenājñātaiva kāryavattā kāryavattayānumāsyata ityapi pratyuktam ; ·14
[p. 210]
jñātaṃ hi sādhanam, ajñātaṃ hi sādhyam | na caikameva jñātamajñātaṃ ca bhavati | nanu tavāpi 'asyedaṃ vyāpyam' iti jñātasaṃbandhameva liṅgena gamyata iti doṣo'styeva ; ucyate—mama sāmānyayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ, viśeṣataśca prameti naiṣa doṣaḥ | bauddhānāṃ tu na sāmānyamastīti viśeṣaḥ | śiśapātvāddṛkṣatvānumāne nirjñātakāryasaṃbandhatāpyastītyūhyam | kiṃca kṣaṇika-vādināṃ kāryakṣaṇakāle kāraṇakṣaṇamasat; ato'sya na kāryaviśiṣṭatā ; P. 90-24. ataśca na kāraṇasya sattā syādityabhiprāyeṇāha—kāryeti | athopalakṣaṇaṃ kāryavattā sacchabdapravṛttāvityucyate, tadapi na, pūrvoktadoṣādityāha—atheti | upasaṃharati—tasmāditi | yathā nīlādiśabdānāmartho'rthātmako yasya kāryaṃ yasya vā pramāṇaṃ sa nīlaśabdārtha iti kāryapramāṇalakṣyo na bhavati, kāryapramāṇayorapi tacca kāryapramāṇābhyāṃ lakṣyaṃ tayorapi tadanantarābhyāmityanavasthānāt ; apitu kāryapramāṇasaṃbandhanirapekṣatvahetubhyaḥ svarūpeṇa vyavasthitā yathāpramāṇamavasīyate—pramāṇamavabodhakamātramiti yāvat—tathā sacchābdo'pītyarthaḥ | yaduktamanavasthānāditi, tadvivṛṇvan svārthasya kāryapramāṇalakṣyatvābhāvaṃ draḍhayati—kāryeti | tasyeti ; vyavahāravibhāga-syetyarthaḥ | evaṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya svapakṣaṃ punarupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | evaṃ pramāṇayogyatā sacchābdārthaḥ naikā satteti pakṣe dūṣite, parāvṛtya pramāṇayogyatvasattāpakṣe punaḥ pratīkāramāśaṅkayati—atheti | yadi pramāṇa-yogyatvamarthasvabhāvaḥ sattā syāt, tato'styarthaniṣṭheṣvapi vedānteṣu pramāṇāntarānapekṣatvāt astyarthaniṣṭhaṃ sāpekṣamiti parābhimataṃ prakṛtaṃ hīyeta; yadi tu pramāṇahetusaṃbhavastadyogyatā tadā hetau satyavaśyaṃ pramāṇena bhāvyam ; atastasāpekṣatvānna prakṛtahānamiti bhāvaḥ | na caivaṃ nṛśṛṅgādīnāṃ saccaprasaṅga ityāha—na ceti | etāvatā ca pramāṇahetubhāvācca sadyavahāra ityuktam | nadīpūrākhyapramāṇahetusaddhāve vṛṣṭeratītāyā api saccaṃ syāditi yadyapyatra dūṣaṇamasti, tathāpyasya pūrvamuktatvāduttaravibhavācca dūṣaṇāntaramāha—taditi | bhūmau nirvātamiti bhūbhāgatirohitatvādindriyasaṃnikarṣasya pratyakṣaheto-rabhāvamāha ; niliṅgamiti anumānahetoliṅgasya ; avedyānupapannakāmiti
[p. 211]
arthāpattihetoranyathānupapannasya ; na vedyamanupapannaṃ yatreti vigrahaḥ ; tathā naṣṭadraṣṭṛkamiti āsāgamahetorāsasya | upamānasya ca P. 91-10. sādṛśyaviṣayatvātaddheturnidhisvarūpapratītau dūrāpeta eva | tadevaṃ sarvapramāṇayogyatāmatikāmati nidhidravyam ; na ca tadasat , apitu saṃśayitam | tvatpakṣe cāsat syāt , na saṃśayitamityarthaḥ | atha na pramāṇahetusadbhāvaḥ sattā ; apitu arthagata eva kaścitsamāvaviśeṣaḥ pramāṇaṃ prati yogyaḥ sattocyate, tadapi na, prakṛtahānādiyuktam | ślokamanyathā vyācaṣṭe—athaveti | pramāṇaviśiṣṭaṃ sadiyucyate | ataḥ pramāṇasadasadbhāvasaṃśaye pramāṇaviśiṣṭābhidhāyisacchabdānuviddhaḥ 'sadvā, asadvā' iti saṃśayo yujyate | nidhau tu sarvapramāṇābhāvaniśrayādasaracaniśrayaḥ syāt, na saṃśaya ityarthaḥ | itaśca na pramāṇayogyatā sattā, yato'sato'pyabhāvasya sāsti ; ataḥ sāpi na syādityabhiprāyeṇāha—apiceti | abhāvaprameyatve kecidvipratipannāḥ ; tadarthaṃ tanmeyatvamupapādayati—tathāhīti | cakāro dūṣaṇasamuccayārthaḥ ; apyartho vā—na sadeva, paramasadapi | yathā yena pramāṇaviṣayatvena sarvaśaktivirahitatvena vābhāvaṃ paro vyavasthāpayati, tathā tena rūpeṇa prameyam | kutaḥ? yatastathā tena rūpeṇāmitaṃ cet, kasmāttathā tadrūpaḥ ? na punaranyathā anyasvarūpa ityarthaḥ | etadeva vivṛṇoti—na ceti | ekapakṣānurāga iti ; tathābhāvātathābhāvayormadhye pramāṇaṃ vinā ekapakṣānurāgo na yujyata ityarthaḥ | abhāvasya prameyatve hetvantaramāha—apiceti | abhāvaśreṣṭha prameyaḥ, tataḥ kaṇṭhakābhāvamamitvā aparicchidya bhūtalādau caraṇaṃ nyasyate katham? na kaṇṭhakābhāvaparicchedāccaraṇanyāsaḥ ; apitu kaṇṭhakādanyasya bhūtalādeḥ paricchedāditi cedata āha—nānyeti | nānyasya bhūtalāderjñānāccaraṇanyāsaḥ, tasya kaṇṭhakasya bhāve'pyanyasya bhūtalādergrahaṇe caraṇanyāsaprasaṅgāt | bhūtalagrahaṇāppurastādapyanyasya kasyacidvahaṇasaṃbhavāt tatra caraṇanyāsaprasaṅgaśca | atha nānyagrahāt, kiṃtu kaṇṭhakasaṃvicyabhāvasaṃśrayanyāsa ityucyate ; tataḥ sūkṣmakaṇṭhakāderāpāte'grahaṇāt padanyāsaḥ syāt, na tu tajjijñāsā | ataḥ kaṇṭhakādisaṃvicyabhāvato'pi na pādanyāsaḥ | tasmādabhāvaparicchedādeva sa 144
[p. 212]
P. 92-14 ityarthaḥ | prayatnāditi ; niścayārthāt prayatnādityarthaḥ | 'jijñāsāpūrvakāt' ityādeḥ 'yujyate' ityantasya aparicchedapakṣe sūkṣmajijñāsā niṣphalā, abhāve tu paricchedye saphaleti tātparyārthaḥ | na ca kaṇṭhakāpariccheda eva jijñāsyaḥ, tasya paricchedāt prāk svataḥ siddhatvena jijñāsāvaikalyāt | kiṃca prameyaṃ jijñāsyam; aparicchedaśca paricchedābhāvaḥ; na cābhāvaste prameyaḥ; ataśca na jijñāsyata ityarthaḥ | atha matam—na kaṇṭhakādyaparicchedamātrāt padanyāsapravṛttiḥ, apitu kaṇṭhakādyapalambhahetuṣu cakṣurādiṣu satsu pramāṇaviṣayasya pramāṇaviṣayatāṃ gantuṃ yogyasya sūkṣmasya sato'paricchedāditi, tatrāha—tadasaditi | tadasat; yato'trāparicchede pramāṇasya na kaścidupayogo dṛśyate, tenādṛṣṭārthatvameva syāditi bhāvaḥ | kutra nāmaitadupayujyata ityāha—asaditi | hiśabdastuśabdārthe; abhāvabodhe tu pramāṇaviṣayatvamupayujyate | katham? ananyahetutvena | kaṇṭhakādyaparicchedāttadabhāvabodhaḥ | aparicchedaśca dvidhā bhavati—pramāṇahetuvaikalyahetukaḥ, kaṇṭhakādyarthābhāvahetuko vā | tatra yaḥ pramāṇahetuvaikalyahetuko'paricchedaḥ sa na sattābhāvaniścayahetuḥ, arthabhāve'pi tatsaṃbhavāt | yastu pramāṇahetupvavikaleṣu satsvaparicchedaḥ, so'rthābhāvaniścayaheturityevamupalambhahetuṣu satsu pramāṇaviṣayatvamabhāvabodha upayujyata ityarthaḥ | anyagrahaṇāccet pādanyāsaḥ, kaṇṭhakāderbhāve'pi bhūtalagrahaṇe syādityuktam; tatra paramatamāśaṅkayati—atheti | atha yatra bhūbhāgādau pādanyāsapravṛttiḥ, tatra bhūbhāgādau kevalasaṃvittitaḥ sā | ataḥ sā kaṇṭhakādisadbhāve kaivalyābhāvān bhavatītyucyate, tatrāha—jijñāseteti | tatredaṃ nirūpyam—kimidaṃ kaivalyaṃ bhūtalajñānasya bhūtalaikāvabhāsitvalakṣaṇam? uta jñānaviṣayasya bhūtalasya? yadāpi viṣayasya, tadā kiṃ svarūpeṇa tasya kaivalyam? uta viśeṣaṇayogena? tatra yadyādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadā yatra kaṇṭhakādau sūkṣmatvādabhāti bhūtalaikaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ bhavati, tatra saṃvitteḥ kaivalyāt pādanyāsaḥ syāt; pravartatetaiva tadarthaṃ vā puruṣaḥ | na ca bhūtalādiviṣayarūpameva svarūpeṇa kevalamiṣṭam, kaṇṭhakādimiśre'pi bhūtale tatsvarūpamanapetamiti pādanyāsaprasaṅgāt | atha viśeṣaṇayogena, tataḥ kaivalyaviśiṣṭasya bhūtalā-
[p. 213]
danyasya kaṇṭhakāderabhāvādanyadviśeṣaṇaṃ na bhavatīti kaṇṭhakābhāvaparicchedādeva pādanyāsa iti prāptamabhāvasya prameyatvamityarthaḥ | ekaviṣayapratiniyama iti ; P. 98. 9. etat jñānakaivalyavivaraṇam | viṣayāntarāsaṃsarga iti ca pratiniyamavivaraṇam ; bhūtalaikaviṣayatvamityarthaḥ | itaśrābhāvaḥ prameya ityāha—apiceti | kiṃ kṣuṇṇamiti ; ko'parādha ityarthaḥ | śloke dhīśrahaṇamupalakṣaṇam ; śabdo'pi draṣṭavyaḥ | dhīśabdaviṣaya eva vyavahāraviṣayaḥ ; atastaccirūpaṇenaiva nirūpita iti vṛttau pṛthak noktaḥ | athavā vyavahriyate'neneti vyavahatiḥ śabdaḥ | ślokaṃ vivṛṇoti— apiceti | yasyābhāvo na prameyaḥ, tena 'nāsti' iti buddhiśabdayorviṣayo vācyaḥ—kimālambanāvetāviti | tatra na tāvadbhūtalavastu svarūpeṇānayorviṣayaḥ, kaṇṭhakādimiśre'pi bhūtale 'nāsti' iti buddhiśabdayoḥ prasaṅgāt | nāpi kevalabhūtalavastu tayorviṣayaḥ ; tadā hi kaṇṭhakādyabhāvaviṣayaḥ syāt, abhāvādanyasya kaivalyākhyasya viśeṣaṇasyāsaṃbhavādityuktam—'nānyābhāvādviśeṣaṇam' iti | para āha—yannāsti nirūpākhyaṃ, tadeva gamyata iti vipratiṣiddham | ato 'nāsti' iti nābhāvaḥ pratīyate, kiṃtu 'nāsti' iti 'na pratīyate' ityayamasyārtha iti ; tatrocyate—pratītyabhāvastarhi 'nāsti' iti dhīśabdayorviṣayaḥ syāt ; tathā sati ca prameyābhāvena kimaparādam ? yena tamatilaṅghya pramāṇābhāvo'nayorviṣaya ucyata ityarthaḥ | paramatamāśaṅkayati—atheti | kaṇṭhakādeḥ agrahe ajñāne asadvahabhrāntiḥ abhāvagrahaṇabhrāntiḥ, tathā kaṇṭhakavyavahārābhāve cābhāvavyavahārabhramaḥ, na punaḥ abhāvagrahaṇābhāvavyavahārī sta ityarthaḥ | dṛṣṭaśrādarśane darśanabhrama ityāha—adṛṣṭāviti ; yathā ālokādṛṣṭau 'tamaḥ paśyāmi' iti tamodṛṣṭivibhrama ityarthaḥ | naiva stāṃ 'nāsti' iti buddhivyavahārāviti pareṇokte, kathamiti siddhāntinākṣipte, ucyata iti punaḥ pareṇa pratyuktamiti grantho yojyaḥ | nanu tamovastveva dṛśyamasti, na punarālokābhāvamātramiti dṛṣṭāntāsiddhimāśaṅkyāha—na hīti | yadi tamo dṛśyamasti, tato rūpavaccāccākṣupaṃ syāt ; ato'ndhena na dṛśyeta ; asti ca tasyāpi tadṛṣṭiḥ | ato bhrama evetyabhiprāyaḥ | tadṛṣyati—naitāditi |
[p. 214]
P. 94-10 kuta ityāha—itthamiti | bhāvādarśane abhāvadarśanamiti yathā tvamāttha, itthaṃ suṣuptimadamūrchāvasthāsu trailokyadarśanābhāvāt tadabhāvadarśanabhramaḥ syāt; na caivamasti | nanu suṣuptasyoparatakaraṇagrāmasya kathaṃ bhramaḥ? satyam, anyathākhyātiḥ karaṇajanyā; na tvakhyātiḥ | akhyātivādī ca bhavānityabhiprāyeṇedamuktam | nanu vivekākhyātirapi smaryamāṇagṛhyamāṇayorbhavati; na ca suṣuptasya tau staḥ; ataḥ kathamakhyātirūpo'yaṃ vibhramaḥ? ucyate—bhāvābhāvadarśanabhrame tau na sta ityanantaraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | tadevaṃ tāvapi vinā cedabhāvadarśanamatra bhramaḥ, tataḥ suṣuptasyāpi trailokyādarśanena tadabhāvadarśanabhramaḥ syāditi manvānena 'itthaṃ bhavet suṣuptyādau' ityatiprasaṅgāpādanaṃ kṛtamityadoṣaḥ | yaḥ kaścidapi na paricchinatti, na tasya bhramo yujyeta; tathāhi—yatra rajatamāropyate tamāropaviṣayaṃ śuktikām āropyaṃ ca rajatamajānāno na rajatabhramavān bhavati | tataḥ kimityata āha— nābhāveti | vyavahāro vyavahṛt; abhāvena hi sā viśiṣṭā vyavahṛt, abhāvavyavahāra ityarthaḥ; tadabhāvaśca na dhīpade na buddhiviṣayāvityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—bhāvavyavahārābhāve abhāvavyavahārabhramastvayeṣyate | tatra bhāvavyavahārābhāvaḥ śuktikāvadāropaviṣayaḥ abhāvavyavahāraśca rajatavadāropyaḥ | na cābhāvaprameyamicchatastau jñātau | ato bhramo na ghaṭata iti | 'nābhāvabhinnā vyavahṛt' ityupalakṣaṇam; abhāvabuddhirapi pūrvaślokanirdiṣṭā vaktavyā | ata evāha—na ca buddhyabhāva iti | anyatheti; āropaviṣayāropyājñāne tu yadi dvayorajñānaṃ tato'pratipattiḥ; athāropaviṣayamātrasya, tato rajatasmṛtiḥ; athāropyasya, tato'navadhāritaviśeṣapurovartidravyasāmānyapratītirityarthaḥ | atra cobhayājñānādapratipattireva syādityabhipretam; smṛtisāmānyapratipattī tu prasaṅgādukte | na ca buddhyabhāva ityādinā āropaviṣayājñānamuktam | nābhāvabuddhirityādinā cāropyājñānamiti vivekaḥ | abhāvabuddhiḥ abhāvavyavahāra ityabhāvo viśeṣaṇam; buddhyabhāvaḥ vyavahārābhāva ityatra tu viśeṣyaḥ | tena viśeṣaṇasyetyupalakṣaṇam, viśeṣyasyāpi draṣṭavyam | 'adṛṣṭau dhvāntadṛṣṭivrat' iti dṛṣṭāntaśca sādhyavikala ityāha—tama iti | tatra yacchāyāmiti paurāṇikīṃ dṛṣṭimāśrityoktam; taduktaṃ purāṇe
[p. 215]
“bhūtaguṇaśruteḥ” iti | atra bhūtaguṇatvaṃ bhūpāratantryamātramuktam, na tu chāyā guṇaḥ dravyāt pṛthagupalabdheḥ ; api tu dravyāntaram ; guṇavatī dvisā ; guṇavacca dravyam | guṇaśca mahadalpatvākhyaṃ parimāṇam | calā ca sā | ataḥ kriyāvaccādapi tat dravyam ; taduktam—“mahadalpaṃ calācalam” iti | sā dravyaṃ kiñcinnimittīkṛtya sūkṣmaiśrāvayavairdṛṣṭairārabhyate | kāṣṭhamivāśritya jvālā tejo'vayavaiḥ | ata eva dravyānugatā jvālā ! sā ca dravyam ; prabhāvasvācca | sūkṣmāstitve ca sthūlameva liṅgam, yathā pṛthivyādau | yathā vāyurarūpaḥ sparśavān, tathā chāyā rūpavatparaparśā, vaicitryādbhāvasya | ato bhāvasvarūpā sā | ata eva cākṣuṣeti | yasya tu matam—ālokābhāve bhāvāt tadbhāve cābhāvāt ālokābhāva eva chāyāstu, kiṃ kalpanāgauraveṇa ? mahatā ca taruṇā mahati bhūbhāge āloke'panīte mahatī, alpena cālpā | calā tāvat dravyeṇa ; yatra yatrāloko'panīyate, tatra tatra calantīva lakṣyate | na tu sā mahatī alpā calā ceti na tato dravyasiddhiriti—tanmatenālokābhāvameva vetyuktam | nanvevamandhasya chāyāmālokābhāvaṃ cāpaśyataḥ kathaṃ tamodṛṣṭiḥ ? ucyate—adṛṣṭarajatasyeva śuktāvadṛṣṭatvāttasyālokādaśane'prameye'pi sadṛśavastumātrapratibhānātmikā bhrāntirupapadyate | yaspunaḥ ‘āropaviṣayāropye nājānan rajatabhramī’ ityuktam, tadviśiṣṭarajatādivastubhramābhiprāyamityavirodhaḥ | idānīṃ pramāṇayogyatāṃ sattāmabhyupagamya, astyarthaniṣṭhatve'pi vedāntānāṃ na sāpekṣatādoṣa ityāha—astviti | bhavatu vā pramāṇayogyatā sattā ; P. 96-1. saivāstyarthaniṣṭhā vedāntānāṃ prameyāstu ; tathā tasmin mānayogyatve pramite, tadyogyasya vastuno brahmaṇaḥ sattvaṃ vedāntārtho bhavati | yogyatāpramāmukhena brahmasvarūpe vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyasiddhirbrahmasvarūpasiddhiśca bhavatītyuktaṃ bhavati | na caivaṃ pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatā doṣaḥ, kutaḥ ? pramāṇāntarasaṃbhedābhāvāt | yatra hi pramāṇāntarasparśena pramāṇāntarādhīnā arthasiddhiḥ, tatra sāpekṣatvaṃ doṣahetuḥ | atra tu ‘pramāṇayogyo'yam’ ityetāvanmātramavagamyate, na tu pramāṇāntareṇa saṃbhedaḥ saṃsparśaḥ ; na pramāṇāntarādhīnā arthasiddhiriti yāvat | ato na sāpekṣatā ; tathāhi—ye'pi pramāṇagrāhyatāṃ pramāṇasaṃbandhaṃ sattāmāhurna tato'nyām, te'pi antarā arthavibhinnatāṃ pramāṇāntara-
[p. 216]
saṃbhedaṃ necchanti ; yathoktam—‘pramāṇasya prameyakoṭipraviṣṭatvānnecchanti’ iti ; tairapi na pramāṇāntarasaṃsparśena pramāṇāntaramanveṣyam, kiṃmuta P. 95-5. ye'rthasvābhāvatāmarthasthāṃ yogyatāṃ sattāmāhustairityarthaḥ | tatprameyamiti ; tasyāḥ sattāyā avinābhāvena prameyamityarthaḥ | atra paraścodayati—kathamiti | yadā astyarthaniṣṭhaṃ vedāntavacaḥ pramāṇayogyatāmātre nivṛttavyāpāraṃ viśiṣṭasyānandādirūpayogyatāviśiṣṭasya yogyasya brahmaṇaḥ saccaṃ na bodhayati, tadā viśiṣṭārthasaccasiddhaye yatpramāṇayogyo'sāvarthastatpramāṇaṃ kathaṃ nāpekṣyam ? apitu apekṣyamevetyarthaḥ | siddhāntī tu pṛcchati—kimarthamiti | para āha—viśiṣṭeti | astyarthaniṣṭhaṃ vaco yogyatāmātraṃ pratipādyoparatavyāpāraṃ na viśiṣṭārthasaccamavagamayati ; atastadarthaṃ pramāṇāntaramanveṣyamityarthaḥ | punaḥ siddhāntī pṛcchati—kimiti | kiṃ punaviśiṣṭasyārthasya saccam, yasyāstyarthaniṣṭhena vacasānavagamitasyāvagamāya pramāṇāntaramanveṣyamityarthaḥ | para āha—pramāṇeti | siddhāntyāha— adhigatamiti | yadi pramāṇayogyatvaṃ viśiṣṭārthasaccam, evaṃ tarhi astyarthaniṣṭhatvādvacaso'dhigatameva tat ; yataḥ pramāṇayogyataivāstyartha ityarthaḥ | yadyapi yogyatāvinābhāvena yogyasya siddhirityuktatvānna tadarthaṃ pramāṇāntaramapekṣyamityuttaramasti, tathāpyuttaravibhavāduttarāntaramuktam | pramāṇāntarasaṃbhedābhāvādastyarthaniṣṭhānāṃ vedāntānāmapramāṇayadoṣābhāva uktaḥ ; adhunā tu pramāṇāntarasaṃbhedābhyupagame'pi na doṣa ityāha—pramāṇāntareti | nośabdo niṣedhārthaḥ | pramāṇāntareṇa bhinnaḥ saṃsṛṣṭo'rtho yasya vacasastadapi nāpramāṇam, apitu pramāṇameva, yathā—āptavākyam | nanu pramāṇāntarabhinnārthaṃ cet, tataḥ sāpekṣam ; yacca pramāṇam, tatkathaṃ sāpekṣaṇityāśaṅkyāha—nirapekṣamiti | yacchabdātpratīṣiṣitam, ‘idaṃ vastu mayā pramitam’ iti, ihāsminnarthe vakrabhiprāyarūpe śrotrā pramāṇāntareṇāpratīte śabdodevānyanirapekṣādavagamyamāne nirapekṣaṃ tadvaca ityarthaḥ ; tathāhi— pauruṣeyādvacanāt ‘evamayaṃ puruṣo veda’ iti pratītiḥ, na tu ‘evamarthaḥ’ iti | ‘pramitaṃ mayā’ iti vakturvākyena svapramāṇaprakāśanamuktam ; śrotā tu ‘pramitamanena’ iti budhyate | kva nāma tatsāpekṣamityata āha—
[p. 217]
artheti | arthamātre tadvacaḥ sāpekṣam | atra hetumāha—anyeti ; P. 95-18. vaktṛpramāṇadvāreṇārthaṃ bodhayati, na tu tannirapekṣamityarthaḥ | anyat vaktṛpramāṇaṃ svasiddhaye'pekṣata ityanyāpekṣaḥ, sa cāsāvarthaścetyanyāpekṣārthaḥ, tasya bodhanādanyāpekṣārthabodhanāditi | tadevaṃ vaktṛrūpalabdhipramāṇāntaranirapekṣameva tadvaco bodhayatīti tannirapekṣatvātprāmāṇyamaśnute, taddvāreṇa cārtha iti sthitam | nanvarthaṃ tatsāpekṣamityuktam; ataḥ kathaṃ tatra prāmāṇyamucyate ? abādhitārthatvāt arthaṃ tadavisaṃvādamātrābhiprāyeṇārthaṃ tatpramāṇayityuktamityadoṣaḥ | nanu yadi sāpekṣamapyabādhitārthaṃ bacaḥ pramāṇaṃ bhavati, tarhi caityavandanādipuruṣavacaḥ kathamabādhitārthamapi tatsāpekṣatvadoṣādapramāṇamucyata ityāśaṅkyāha— abādhitamiti | yadyapi tatra nārthaṃ bādhaḥ, tathāpi mūlabhūte vaktṛpramāṇe tadapabādhyate, tathāhi—na tadvacaḥ pratyakṣādimūlam, dharmasya tadagocaratvāt | na ca buddhasya manvāderiva vedasaṃyogo'sti | tadevaṃ pramāṇāṃśe tadvādhyate; tadabhāvāccārthe'pi | taduktam “puruṣāśaktitastatra sāpavādatvasaṃbhavaḥ” iti; puruṣasya dharmaṃ draṣṭumaśaktitaḥ tatra buddhavacanādau apavādaḥ aprāmāṇyaṃ tasya saṃbhava ityarthaḥ | tena vaktṛḥ pramāṇe'rthaṃ ca na tatpramāṇatāmeti | evamanāptavākye'pi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ yojyam | āptavākye tu tadvaiparītyena prāmāṇyamāha—āpteti | pramāṇāṃśa iti, arthaṃ tu sāpekṣamityuktaṃ bhavati | evamāptavākyeṣu pramāṇāntarasaṃsparśe'pi prāmāṇyamupapādya vedāntānāmapi pramāṇāntarasaṃsparśe satyapi tathaiva prāmāṇyamityāha—mayeti | yata evamāptavākye, tatastadvadeva vedāntā api mānāntaraṃ svaprakāśākhyaṃ pralīnajñānajñeyavibhāgam ‘idaṃ jñānam, idaṃ jñeyam’ iti vibhāgarahitam, ata eva alaukikaṃ laukikātpratyakṣāderjñānajñeyavibhāgopetādanyat pravedayantu | tatsiddhidvāreṇa cāptavākyavadarthasya brahmataccasya siddhiḥ | evaṃ pramāṇāntarasaṃbhede'pi vedāntānāmāptavākyavat na prāmāṇyavyāhātirityāśayaḥ | tatkiṃ vedapauruṣeyavacasoraviśeṣa eva ? na; pauruṣeye hi vacasi ‘mayedaṃ pramitam’ iti vaktṛvākyaprayogeṇa pratipāditaṃ pramāṇaṃ śrotā budhyate; vede tu vaktṛrabhāvāt ‘mayā’ iti saṃsparśarahitaṃ pramāṇāntaramātramavabudhyate | tadidamuktam—‘mayā’ iti saṃbhedarahitam iti |
[p. 218]
P. 96-22 evamabhyupagamavādaṃ samāpya prakṛtamupasaṃharati—evamiti | meyatā ityupalakṣaṇam, mānayogyatādyapi dṛṣṭavyam | atra paraḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate— nanviti | anyeti ; sāmānyarūpetyarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha—na hīti | pūrvaṃ vyaktyantare dṛṣṭasya punarvyaktyantare parāmarśaḥ pūrvāvamarśaḥ | dṛṣṭaṃ sat sarṣapādi yena tasya, sadantare merau na tathetyarthaḥ | na ca 'sat' ityekaśabdapravṛttyanyathānupapattyā ekā jātiḥ kalpyā, daṇḍyādivadanyathāpyupapatterityāha—eketi | pūrvanirastamapi daṇḍyāditulyatvaṃ sattāsadbhāvapramāṇābhāvāvaṣṭambhena punarupanyastam | varamevam, na tvapratīyamānasattākalpaneti bhāvaḥ | atrottaramāha—atreti | nuśabdaḥ saṃbodhane | yo'yaṃ sāmānyakalpanāhetustvayoktaḥ pūrvāvamarśaḥ, sa kaḥ? para āha—pūrveti | pūrvamekasyāṃ vyaktau buddheḥ rūpamākāraḥ, tasya vyaktyantare yo'nvayo'nugamo'nuvṛttiḥ— yathā prativyakti 'gaurgoḥ' iti buddhau gotvarūpasya—pūrvāvamarśa ityuktam, tatra doṣamāha—sadantareti | dṛṣṭasataḥ puṃsaḥ sadantare so'styeva bahūnāṃ rūpāṇāmananugame'pi kayācit khalu mātrayā kiñcinmātrarūpeṇa ; anyathā yathā sañco'sannirupākhyamatyantavilakṣaṇaṃ bhāti, tathā santo'pi mitho'tyantavilakṣaṇāḥ prakāśeran ; na caivam | khalviti prasiddhisūcanārtham | atha sarvātmanā tatra dhiyaḥ pūrvarūpānvayo nāstītyucyate, tatrāha—sarveti | sarvātmanā tu dhiyaḥ pūrvarūpānvayaḥ khaṇḍamuṇḍādivyaktiṣvapi nāstyeva ; tatra gotvādyapi sāmānyaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | yadi tu tāsu sarvātmanā rūpānvayaḥ syāt, tatastāsāmaikyameva syāt | ataśca gotvādisāmānyābhāvāpattiḥ syāt ; na hekasyāṃ vyaktau vyomnīva sāmānyamiṣyate, nānāvyaktyāśrayatvāttasya | khaṇḍamuṇḍayoriti vyaktivailakṣaṇyopanyāsaḥ sarvātmanā rūpānvayābhāvadarśanārthaḥ | khaṇḍamuṇḍayoryādṛśa udbhūtarūpānvayo na tādṛśaḥ satsadantarayoriti cet, na tarhāvāntarabhedamātreṇa satsadantarayoḥ saṃvedyarūpānvayasyāpahavo yuktaḥ ; anyathā devadattasya punardarśane yādṛśo buddherudbhūtarūpānvayaḥ, na tādṛśaḥ khaṇḍādivyaktiṣviti gotvamapyapahūyeta | vedyagrahaṇamapahave saṃvidvirodhapradarśanārtham | kiñca na kevalamekavyaktau yādṛśaḥ, tādṛśaḥ khaṇḍādivyaktipu buddhirūpānvayo bhavati ; apitu mṛtsāmānya-
[p. 219]
gocaro'pi maṇimallakādau sarvatra samāno buddhirūpānvayaḥ ; tathāhi— maṇikeṣu parasparaṃ yādṛśo rūpānvayaḥ prodbhūtaḥ, na tādṛśo dṛṣṭamallakasya maṇike ; na ca tāvatāvāntarabhedamātreṇa mṛjātirhīnaṃ maṇimallakādi, tasya mṛjātyātmanā kiñcittulyatayāvabhāsanādityarthaḥ | atha ‘so'yam’ iti pūrvāparānusaṃdhānaṃ pūrvāvamarśaḥ, atrocyate— pūrveti | svaṇḍamuṇḍavaditi vaiṣamyadṛṣṭāntaḥ | atha yadyapi dṛṣṭamaṇikasya P. 98-5. mallake ‘so'yam’ ityanusaṃdhānaṃ na bhavati, tathāpi vimūśataḥ ‘tajjātīyamidam’ iti paścādbhavati ; tena tatra mṛjātiriṣyate ; tato dṛṣṭasataḥ sadantare'pi tatsamānamiti sattāpi kiṃ neṣyate ? tadetadāha—vimūśata iti | nanu yadi maṇikeṣu maṇikatvavat maṇikamallakayorapi mṛjātirasti ; tatastulye sāmānyayoge maṇikeṣu ‘so'yam’ ityanusaṃdhānaṃ drāk, na maṇimallakayorityatra ko heturiti pṛṣṭhe uttaramucyate—sāmānyeti | taccamiti ; ‘so'yam’ ityabhedaḥ prakāśata ityarthaḥ | maṇikeṣu parasparaṃ mṛtvaṃ maṇikatvamavayavasāmānyāni ca bhūyāṃsyanuvṛttāni ; mallake tu mṛtvamātramityato maṇikeṣu pūrvāvamarśo drāk, na maṇikamallakayorityarthaḥ | ‘ekaśabdapravṛttiśca daṇḍyādivadupayatām’ ityuktam ; taddūṣayati— pramāṇamiti | pramitikāryānubheyapramāṇenāsaṃyojya prathamaṃ pūrvamevāstidhīyato jāyate, ato na pramāṇasaṃyojananimittā ; tannimittatve hi pramāṇe jāte tatsaṃyojya paścājjāyeta, na pūrvameva ; na hi daṇḍasaṃyojanātprāgeva ‘daṇḍī’ iti bhavati | ato na daṇḍyādisamānatetyarthaḥ | na cāstibuddhireva pramāṇam, astibuddherekasyā nimittanaimittikatvavirodhāt, svasaṃbandhasya ca svayamajñānāt prāyuktasamastadoṣaprasaṅgācceti bhāvaḥ | atra paro dṛṣṭābhiprāyaḥ pṛcchati—yadīti | yadi na pramāṇayogo'styarthaḥ, pramāṇayogatraikālyenārthasya kathaṃ traikālyam ‘abhūt, asti, bhaviṣyati’ iti ? ataḥ pramāṇayoga evāstyartha iti taddṛṣṭo'bhiprāyaḥ ! atrottaramāha—taditi | kālabhedenopādhinā kalpitabhedo'styarthaḥ traikālyena bhāti vyapadiśyate ca | tena pramāṇakālenopādhinā kalpito bhedo yasyāstyarthasya sa tathoktaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—devadatteti | yathā devadattasattā deśaviśeṣopādhikalpita-
[p. 220]
bhedā tatraiva nānyatreti deśabhedena vyavatiṣṭhate, tathā kālabhedopādhikalpitabhedā kālabhedenetyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—sattā hi jātiḥ ; jātiśca vyaktideśā pratīyate ; tena yatra deśe kāle vā pramāṇena vyaktiḥ pratīyate, tatra bhāti vyapadiśyate ceti traikālyopapattiḥ | evaṃ tāvat kriyayā vinā na saṃsargaḥ padārthānāmityabhyupagamya pūrvapa. 11-10. muktam ; adhunā tu tadapyasiddhamityāha—saṃbandheti | rājapuruṣayordvaimaphalayośca saṃbandhamātre'vasitam, ata evākriyamastyādikriyārahitaṃ vaca āhurityanvayaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | idānīṃ yadi nāma kriyārahitaṃ vākyaṃ nāsti, astikriyāyāṃ cāsteḥ pramāṇayoganimittavāt vedāntānāṃ pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatādoṣo bhavati, tathāpi janikriyāṃ kalpayiṣyāmaḥ ; janiśca na pramāṇayoganimittā ; ato na sāpekṣatādoṣaḥ, brahmasvarūpaṃ ca setsyatītyāha—janīti | īdṛśāccetanadivyāmūrtādirūpāt kāraṇādidaṃ agat jāyata iti janikriyāvasānājanikriyāniṣṭhādapi vākyādviśiṣṭaṃ cetanādirūpaṃ kāraṇaṃ śakyabodhamityanvayaḥ | śrutyupapannaścāyamarthaṃ ityāha—tathā ceti | ākāśādeveti ; brahmaṇa ityarthaḥ | vyuccaranti ; udgacchantītyarthaḥ | apiyanti ; vilīyanta ityarthaḥ | idānīṃ kimāyātaṃ vidherityuktamasahamāno vedānteṣu padārthasaṃsargārthaṃ vidhirāśrayitavya iti paraḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—nanviti | pramāntarādhīnaḥ saṃsargo yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | vidhyadhīna ātmasaṃśleṣo yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ | puruṣokteṣu yathā pramāṇāntarādhīnaḥ padārthasaṃsargaḥ, tathā darīyati—padānāmiti | ‘ghaṭena kūpājalamāhara’ iti padānāṃ racanā samabhivyāhāraḥ parasparānvitānāṃ sahoccāraṇaṃ vakturviśiṣṭārthavivakṣāṃ paripūrayet paripūrṇāṃ pratipādayet | na hi ‘āhara’ ityekapadāt sā pūrṇā pratīyate | yato vivakṣā pramāṇāntaragamye'rthe vyavasthitā, tena tatra pramāṇāntarādhīna eva padārthasaṃsargaḥ ; tathāhi—yatra pramāṇāntaravirodhaḥ, tatrāsaṃsargaḥ, yathā— jaradbhavādivākye | vede tu pramāṇāntarābhāvāt vidhirūpo yo'rtho niyogaḥ, tatsiddhaye | padārthasaṃsargaviśiṣṭārthaviṣayo hi vidhiḥ ; na ca padārthasaṃsarga-
[p. 221]
mantareṇa viśiṣṭaviṣayasiddhiḥ | ato veda vidhyāyatta eva padārthasaṃsarga iti vidhirāśrayitavyaḥ | nanu 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma' ityatra vinā vidhinā viśiṣṭārthāvagatirityata āha—tadabhāve sviti | saṃsarganimittasya P.101-11 vidherabhāve tu 'satyaṃ jñānam' ityādau padārthasaṃgaterabhāvāt viśiṣṭārthāvagatiryā, sā bhrāntisamānarūpetyarthaḥ | vivakṣitārthaniṣpattaya iti ; vivakṣito yo yāvānviśiṣṭaḥ sakārakakalāpo jalāharaṇādirarthaḥ, sa tāvān kathaṃ nāma saṃpūrṇaḥ saṃpādyatāmityarthaḥ | naikapadasādhyeti ; naikapadapratipādyo naikena padena pratipādyaḥ saṃpūrṇārthaḥ | siddhāntī tu bahuprakāraṃ vikalpya dūṣayati— atreti | yadi pramāṇāntareṇa śrotrā saṃsargasyāvagamāt pramāṇāntarādhīnaḥ sa ucyate ; tadasat, apūrvasyāpi pramāṇāntarānadhigatasyāpi tasya saṃsargasya śrotrā pratīteḥ | 'agniruṣṇaḥ' ityādibhyo'dhigato'pi gamyata iti tadapekṣayā apiśabdaḥ | anyatheti ; pramāṇāntarādhigata eva gamakatva ityarthaḥ | anadhigatamityādinā śroturanadhigatārthapratītimeva draḍhayati | nanu 'pramāṇāntaragamye'rthe vivakṣā ca vyavasthitā' iti pūrvaṃ viśiṣyaivoktatvāt ko'yamanuktopālambhaḥ ? ucyate—asmindūṣite tatra mā saṃkramiṣyatīti taddūṣaṇapuraḥsaramevāyaṃ dūṣita ityadoṣaḥ | athocyate—pramāṇāntareṇa saṃsargaśret pramīyeta, tataḥ syādvacanavaiyarthyam ; na tvevam ; kiṃtu saṃsargayogyatāmātraṃ pramāṇāntareṇa pramīyate kriyākārakaiḥ ; tāni ca tayā saṃsargayogyānīti ; tena loke pramāṇāntarādhīnasaṃsarga iti | tadayuktam ; vede'pi hi saṃsargayogyatā pramāṇāntarādhīnaiva ; tathāhi—'aruṇayaikahāyanyā somaṃ krīṇāti' ityaruṇimnaḥ karaṇakārakatvāt kriyayā saṃsargayogyatā | tathā sādhyaviṣayo niyogaḥ ; bhāvārthaśra sādhyaḥ ; ataḥ sa eva yogyatvānniyogaviṣayaḥ, na dravyaguṇau siddhatvenāyogyatvāditi pramāṇāntarāvagatayogyatāsāmarthyenaiva vyavasthāpyata iti vede'pi pramāṇādhīna eva saṃsargaḥ syāt, na vidhyadhīna iti bhāvaḥ | evamimaṃ pakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā adhunā pūrvapakṣopanyastaṃ pakṣaṃ dūṣayitumanubhāṣate—atheti | taddūṣayati—evamiti | 'ghaṭena kūpājjalamāhara' iti viśiṣṭārthavivakṣā 'āhara' ityekasmāt padānna pratīyate ; tato hi āharaṇakriyā pratīyate ; sā ca kiṃ kena kutaśretya-
[p. 222]
jñānāt vyavahārānupayogena niṣprayojanā | tasmāt viśiṣṭārthavivakṣāpramitirnāsaṃsṛṣṭeṣu padārtheṣu bhavati | sā padārthānāṃ saṃsargaprayukteti cet, P.101-21 ucyate—evaṃ tarhi vivakṣāprayuktatvāt tannibandhana eva padārthasaṃsargaḥ, na pramāṇāntaranibandhanaḥ | na hi pramāṇāntaraṃ tasyāvabodhakaṃ prayojakaṃ vā | yattu pramāṇāntarasiddhe vivakṣātaḥ pramāṇāntarādhīno loke saṃsarga ityuktam ; tatro'cyate—bhavatu pramāṇāntarasiddhe vivakṣā, na tu pramāṇāntarasya saṃsarga pratyupayogaḥ, vināpi pramāṇāntaramanāptavākye'pi padārthasaṃsargapratīteḥ | yattu pramāṇāntarādhīnaḥ saṃsarga ityatra kāraṇamuktam—pramāṇāntaravirodhe jaradravādivākye na saṃsarga iti, tatrocyate—kāmaṃ bhavatu tadvirodhānna saṃsargaḥ ; tathāpi tu na tataḥ saṃsargaḥ, vipralambhakavākye'pi tatpratītereva | na ca yadvirodhe yanna bhavati tat tadadhīnaṃ syāt ; na hyātapavirodhe na chāyeti sā tadadhīnā | kiñca pramāṇāntaravirodhe nāsaṃsarga ityapyasiddham, yato'nāptavākye tadvirodhe nāsaṃsargaḥ, apitu saṃsargaḥ pratīyate | ayaṃ tvāptavākyādasya viśeṣaḥ—yadatra pratīte'pi pramāṇāntareṇa bādhyate, na tu pratīyata eva | yadi tvanāptavākye saṃsargapratītireva na jātā bādhyate, tato'nṛtaṃ nāma vaco nānarthakādvidyeta ; ataśca 'anṛtam, anarthakam, satyam' iti vākyatraividhyaṃ lokasiddhaṃ bādhyeta | atha pramāṇāntareṇopalamyārthaṃ vivakṣitvā puruṣaḥ parasmai vākyena pratipādayati | tena pramāṇāntaropalabdhireva viśiṣṭāptavacaso viṣayaḥ | viśiṣṭārthaviṣayatayā ca sā viśiṣṭā ; na svataḥ, nirākāratvāt | viśiṣṭaścārthaḥ padārthasaṃsargasādhyaḥ ; taikapadasādhyaḥ, tasya sāmānyamātraviṣayatvāt | ataḥ sā viśiṣṭā nāsaṃsṛṣṭeṣu padārtheṣu vākyādavagamyata iti tatsaṃsargamākṣipati | vede tu pramāṇāntaropalabdheḥ padārthasaṃsargaprayojikāyā abhāvānna saṃsargasiddhiriti tatprasiddhaye vidhirāśrayitavya ityucyate ; tatra brūmaḥ—mā mūḍhede pramāṇāntaropalabdhiḥ, tathāpi tatra vidhirnāśrayitavyaḥ | kiṃ tarhi tatra padārthasaṃsargaprayojakam ? satyajñānānantabrahmarūpo viśiṣṭo'rtha eva | yadi puṃvacasaḥ pramāṇāntaropalabdhirartha iti sā tatra svapratipattaye padārthasaṃsargaṃ prayuktaṃ tato vedāntānāmānandādirūpaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ brahmārtha iti tadveva svapratipattaye vedānteṣu padārthasaṃsargaṃ prayokṣyate ; kiṃ vidhineti mātraḥ | atha
[p. 223]
lokāvagamyaṃ śabdasāmarthyam ; loke ca pramāṇāntaropalabdhiḥ padārthasaṃsargasya P.102-10 prayojikā dṛṣṭā ; ato vedānteṣu tadabhāvānna viśiṣṭo'rthaḥ padārthasaṃsargasya prayojakaḥ, tena vidhirāśrīyata ityucyate ; tato vedānteṣu vidhimattve'pi pramāṇāntaropalabdheḥ prayojikāyā abhāvāt vidherapi cālaukikatvāt saṃsargo na syāt | ato dṛṣṭyā teṣu vidhimattvakalpanākleśa ityāśayaḥ | atha śabdānāṃ padārthamātraparatve viśiṣṭārthapratyayānupapattyā padārthamātreṇa ca vyavahārābhāvāt tadarthaśabdaprayogānupapattyā ca yārthāpattistayā saṃsargamātraparatvaṃ śabdaprayogasya gamyate ; na tu pramāṇāntaropalabdhiprayuktasaṃsargaviśeṣaparatvam, vināpi tat saṃsargamātraparatvenāpi prayogapratyayayorupapatteḥ | ato vināpi pramāṇāntaropalabdhyā vidhimattve syādeva saṃsarga ityucyate ; tanna, loke pramāṇāntaropalabdheraprayojakatvāt, vināpi tayā śabdānāṃ saṃsargamātraparatvasāmarthyenaiva vedānteṣu viśiṣṭārthāvagatisiddheḥ | ataḥ kiṃ vidhinā ? atha na loke pramāṇāntaropalabdhiprayuktaḥ saṃsargaḥ, kiṃtu śabdānāṃ saṃsargaparatvakṛta eva ; satyatā saṃsargasya pramāṇāntarādhīnā ; tathāhi—yatrāpi śrotuḥ svapramāṇaṃ nāsti, tatrāpi vaktṛpramāṇaṃ vinā buddhādivākyeṣvasatyatvāt vaktṛpramāṇāntaramutpannena saṃsargaḥ satyatāṃ labhate ; tena pramāṇāntarāprayukto'pi pramāṇāntarādhīnasatyatva iti pramāṇāntarādhīno loke saṃsarga ucyata iti cet ; bhavatvevaṃ loke ; vede tvapauruṣeyatvādeva vaktṛpramāṇasaṃsparśābhāvāt svātantryeṇārthaniścayāt na satyatvārthamapi pramāṇāntarasyāpekṣā ; śabdādeva saṃsargasya satyatāvagamāt śabdanibandhana eva saḥ | evaṃ ca sati na saṃsargajñānārthaṃ na ca tatsatyatārthaṃ vedānteṣu vidhirupāsyata iti tātparyārthaḥ | idānīṃ bhavatu loke yathā tathā vā ; vede tu saṃsargasya vidhyadhīnatvaṃ yaduktaṃ tatkathamiti paryanuyujyate—kathamiti | vede padārthasaṃsargasya vidhyadhīnatvaṃ katham ? na hi saṃsargasāmānyasya tadviśeṣasya vā bodhako vidhiḥ | atha viśiṣṭaviṣayo vidhinaikapadārthaviṣayaḥ ; na ca saṃsargamantareṇa viśiṣṭaviṣayasiddhiḥ ; ataḥ saṃsargaṃ vinā nirviṣayo vidhirvṛthā syāt ; tena saṃsargamākṣipatīti yaducyate, tanna ; yato vidhipadādanyena padenodite ukte'pyarthe tulyamidaṃ dṛṣṭyātmya ; yato'nyo'pi padārtho viśiṣṭārthāvagatyā
[p. 224]
P.102-20 svasiddhaye prayujyate | atrākṣipyate—viśiṣṭā svārthāvagatiḥ kathaṃ nāma bhavatviti ; etadarthaṃ padena padārthasaṃsargaḥ pratipādyata iti yāvat | na ca padārthasaṃsargaṃ vinā viśiṣṭārthasiddhiḥ | tāṃ ca vinā nāpūrvārthapratītiḥ | padārthamātrasya saṃbandhajñānasamaye pūrvamavagatatvāt padāni saṃsargayuktārthābhidhānadvāreṇa saṃsargasya pramāṇam | na padaiḥ svārthamātraniṣṭhatayā svārthāḥ pratyāyyante ; kiṃtu viśiṣṭārthapratipattaye, tathā vyavahāropapatteḥ | evaṃ ca sati pratipadārthaṃ saṃsargākāṅkṣā | ato'nyo'pi padārthaḥ saṃsargaṃ vinānarthakaḥ san saṃsargamākṣeptumalamiti ko vidherviśeṣaḥ ? evaṃ ca vidhinā vinā vedānteṣu satyajñānādipadārthā eva saṃsargārthāḥ prayokṣyante, kiṃ vidhineti bhāvaḥ | loke buddhipūrvaprayuktānāmānarthakyamayuktam ; ataḥ saṃsargārthatā yuktā ; vede punarabuddhipūrvaṃ padārthānāmarthavacce pramāṇābhāvānna tathā | padārthaḥ saṃsargamanākṣipan yadyanarthako bhavati, bhavatu ; na tu saṃsargamākṣeptumalam | ataḥ saṃsargāya tatra vidhirupāsyata iti cet, tanna ; asti hi vede'pyadhyayanavidhirarthavacce pramāṇam | yaccoktaṃ loke buddhipūrvaprayuktānāmānarthakyaṃ mā bhūditi saṃsargāvedakatvamiti, tadasāram ; yato naiṣāṃ pramāṇabhāvaṃ brūṣe, tato vidhiḥ kasmāddhetoḥ naiva nānarthaḥ, tasyāpi vaidikatvāt padārthāntarabadarthavacce pramāṇābhāvāviśeṣāt | ataḥ so'pi na saṃsargaṃ prayujjītetyāśayaḥ | yadi ca saṃsargaṃ vinā buddhipūrvaprayuktānāmānarthakyam, bhavatu ; tathāpi padārthānāṃ saṃsargaprayuktasvārthābhidhānasāmarthyaṃ vinā niṣpramāṇakaḥ saṃsargaḥ pratipattumaśakyaḥ | apramāṇapratipattau hi apramāṇakatvāviśeṣāt yasya kasyacitsaṃsargasya pratipattiḥ prasajyeta, na viśiṣṭasya | tasmādavaśyamevamabhyupagantavyam—yatsaṃsargaprayuktasvārthāvabodhasāmarthyaṃ padārthānāṃ vyavahārādgamyata iti | evaṃ cet tato vedānte'pi satyānantajñānādipadārthānāṃ saṃsargakṣepakatvaṃ samānamiti kiṃ tadarthaṃ vidhinetyarthaḥ | atha vidhipadādaparamanyatpadaṃ pramāṇāntarasiddhe'rthe prayogi ; na tu vidhāyakaḥ śabdastathā, vidheḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaratvāt | atastadartho vidhāyakārtho vidhiḥ vidhāyakācchabdānmīyamāno yasmādananyaganyaḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaraḥ, tasmāt pramāṇāntarāvaśīlato'pūrvamapi saṃsṛṣṭarūpaṃ viṣayaṃ
[p. 225]
saṃsargapuraḥsaramākṣipet | itarastu pramāṇāntarasiddhaḥ padārthaḥ, yathā pramāṇāntarasiddhastathaivānuvādabhāganūdyamānaḥ pramāṇāntarāṅkuśavaśīkṛto nāpūrvaviṣayaṃ samākṣipet | ato vedānteṣvaśrūyamāṇo'pi padārthasaṃsargārthaṃ vidhirāśrayitavya iti cet, tadayuktam; evaṃ pramāṇāntarāgocareṇa vidhinā tacchabdasya P. 108.3. saṃbandhajñānaṃ nihūyet | nihave cāvyutpannasyājñātasaṃbandhasyāpi vidhāyakācchabdāt pravṛttiḥ syāt | na caivam | kiṃca evaṃ sati padaṃ saṃbandhajñānāpekṣitvarūpaṃ svadharmaṃ vyutkrāmet atikramet, vākyadharmaṃ ca tannairapekṣyalakṣaṇamāśrayet | anyathā saṃbandhajñānānihave tu ajñāne saṃbandhini vidhau tacchabdasya saṃbandhajñānāśaktervidheranyasiddhatā pramāṇāntarasiddhatāpadyeta | tathā sati ca padārthāntarebhyo'nuvādasāmyena vidhena kaścidviśeṣaḥ | ataśca yathā padārthāntaramanūdyamānatvānnāpūrvasaṃsargamākṣiptumalam, tathā vidhirapītyarthaḥ | pramāṇāntarāgocare niyoge saṃbandhajñānāsaṃbhava ityuktam; tatra paramatamāśaṅkayati—śabdāditi | na pramāṇāntarāstramite vidhyarthe saṃbandhajñānam, apitu vidhāyakācchabdādeva pramite tacchabdena saha vācyavācakasaṃbandhagrahaṃ yo'pi manyate tasyaivam ityanāgatenānveyaḥ | nanvevaṃ śabdājjñāte vidhyarthe saṃbandhajñānam | saṃbandhajñāne ca sati śabdājjñānamityanyonyāśrayaḥ syādityāśaṅkyāha—nānyonyeti | na cānyonyāśrayarūpam avadyaṃ doṣaḥ | kutaḥ? yato na bālasya svayaṃ pramite vidhyarthe saṃgatigrahaḥ | kathaṃ nāma? tadāha—pareṇeti | pareṇa prayojyavṛddhena śabdādavagate vidhyarthe bālena ca tasya prayojyasya 'gāmānaya' ityukte yā pravṛttiḥ, tathā 'nūnamasmādanena pravṛttihetubhūto'rthaḥ pratipannaḥ' ityevaṃ sūcite tatra vidhyarthe sukaraḥ saṃgatigrahaḥ saṃbandhajñānamityarthaḥ | tatra dūṣaṇamāha—tasyeti | yo manyate tasyaivaṃ prayojyapravṛttyā tadīyaviśiṣṭapratyaye'numeye tasya ca viṣayatayā viśeṣaṇatvena vā padārthāntaravadvidhyarthasyānubheyatve kathaṃ sa vidhyarthaḥ śabdaikagocaraḥ? atha tasyānumānasya śabdapūrvakatvāt śabdaikagocaro vidhyarthaṃ ucyate, tato'numānaikagocaraḥ kasmānna bhavati? yataḥ śabdajo'pi vidhyarthabodho'numānapuraḥsaraḥ; tathāhi— 15
[p. 226]
prayojyo'pi saṃbandhagrahaṇasamaye prayojyāntarapravṛttyanumitameva vidhyarthaṃ saṃprati śabdāt prabudhyate ; prayojyāntaramapyanyasmāt prayojyadṛddhāditi bījāṅkuravat śabdānumānayormadhye prathamamityekasyedaṃprathamatā na labhyate | tenādau śabdapṛrvakatvāt śabdaikagocara iti na vācyam | kiṃtu sarvasyaiva prayojyasya boddhuranumānaṃ śabdāt puraḥ sthitaṃ pūrvabhāvi ; yataḥ so'pi saṃbandhajñānasamaye pramitameva vidhyarthaṃ śabdāt budhyate | padārthāntarasyeva sa śabdaḥ śrutasya vidhyarthasya vidhāyakaḥ śabdo'nuvādakaḥ | ataḥ so'pi padārthāntaravadeva nāpūrvasaṃsargamākṣeptumalam, na ca śabdaikagocara iti bhāvaḥ | anumānāt budhyamāno bālaḥ śabdādbuddhā vidhyarthaṃ na budhyate, tenānumeya eva | nanu śabdādbuddhameva bālo'numinoti ; bāḍham ; kiṃtu prayojyena | na ca pratipannantare prayojye yaḥ śabdabodhaḥ, so'nyasya bālasyānumānikaṃ jñānamanuvādīkaroti | tenānumeyatvameva vidhyarthasya yuktam, na śabdaikagocaratvamityāśaṅkayati | kiṃca prayojyapravṛttyā pravṛttihetusāmānyamātre vyutpattirbhūtā ; atastadeva śabdārthaḥ syāt | atha sāmānyena vyavahārābhāvāt tasminviśeṣakalpyate, tathāpi laukikādeva śreyaḥsādhanatvāt siddhenīlaukiko niyogaḥ kalpayituṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ ! vaidikīṣu pravṛttiṣu laukikasya pramāṇāntaragocarasya pravṛttihetorasaṃbhavāt alaukikaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ kalpyata iti cet, naitat ; yataḥ 'ya eva laukikāḥ' iti nyāyena lokāvagatasāmarthyaḥ śabdo vede bodhakaḥ | tatra laukikīṣu pravṛttiṣu laukika eva pravṛttihetuviditasaṃgatirnīlaukika iti sa eva vede pratipattavyaḥ | asaṃbhave vā vede pravṛttihetorjñānasya mithyātvameva yuktam, na tu tasmādalaukikakalpanā vā | avyabhicārī śabdārthaḥ ; pravṛttihetuviśeṣaśca bahuvidhatvena vyabhicārādaśabdārthaḥ ; tena pravṛttihetuḥ sāmānyameva loke vede ca śabdārthaḥ | yattu vede viśeṣāntaramalaukikaṃ pratīyate, talaukikānāṃ pramāṇāntaragocarāṇāmapramāṇapūrve vede'saṃbhavāt ; na tu śabdādeva | tena na vede laukikaśabdārthatyāgadoṣa iti cet, evamapi śabdatvasāmānyaṃ vede śabdārthaḥ ; taloke saṃbandhajñānasamaye pramitamiti padārthāntaravat tasyānuvādatā na vyāvartate | athālaukikaviśeṣābhiprāyeṇa pramāṇāntaragocaratocyate, tadapi na ; yataḥ so'pītarāsaṃbhavena pāriśeṣyānumānasiddhaḥ | etaccābhyupagamyoktam | taccatastu
[p. 227]
pariśeṣyānumānamapyalaukike viśeṣe nāsti ; anyāsaṃbhave hi tatsaṃbhavati ; na cātra viśeṣāntarasyāsaṃbhavaḥ, śreyaḥsādhanatvasya laukikasya viśeṣāntarasya saṃbhavāt | atha lokavedavyavahārau dvāvapyanādī pṛthakpanthānau ; tatra vaidikīṣu P 105-21 pravṛttiṣu laukikapravṛttihetorasaṃbhavādalaukike pravṛttihetau vaidikādeva vyavahārāt saṃbandhajñānam | ato na lokāvagamyo vede liṅādyarthaḥ, kiṃtu anyathaiva | 'laukika—' iti nyāyavirodhaśrāddhavanīyādiśabdavat parihartavyaḥ | ato na laukikāsaṃbhave mithyātvam | na ca vaidikīṣvapi pravṛttiṣu pravṛttihetoḥ pravṛttyanumeyatvamaviśiṣṭamityanuvādatvānna vimucyata iti vācyam ; yato na tatra pravṛttyā pravṛttiheturanumīyate, apitu prayojyavṛddhasya tatpratyayaḥ pravṛttihetośca siddhistatpratyayasāmarthyena | sa ca prayojyasya pravṛttihetupratyayaḥ śabdottha iti pravṛttihetorniyogasya śabdaikagamyatvam | yaccoktam—laukikasya śreyaḥsādhanatvasya saṃbhavāt nālaukiko vidhyarthaḥ karaṇyata iti, tadayuktam ; yato laukikī śreyaḥsādhanatā pramāṇāntaragamyā, pramāṇāntaragamye liṅādeḥ prayogadarśanāt | laukikaprayogānusāreṇa ca vede śabdārtho'nusartavyaḥ | tatra śreyaḥsādhanatā cedvede liṅyarthaḥ syāt tataḥ pramāṇāntaramapi śabdārthaḥ syāt, tathaiva vyutpatteḥ ; na caivam ; apauruṣeyatvādvede pramāṇāntarābhāvāt | tenālaukika eva pramāṇāntaragocaro vede liṅyarthaḥ, nānuvādabhāk | ato'pūrvaṃ saṃsargamākṣeptumalamiti cenmatam, tatro'cyate—bhavatu vedavyavahārāt vede śabdārthayoranugamo'nvayaḥ saṃbandhajñānamiti yāvat, tathāpi na vidhyarthasya pramāṇāntaragocaratā vyāvartate ; tathāhi—alaukike vidhyarthe yadā liṅādiśabdasya vācakasāmarthyaṃ gamyate, tadā sa vidhyarthaḥ pramitaḥ, na vā apramitaḥ ? tatra pramitaścet, tato na tata eva liṅādeḥ śabdāt tasya tadaiva jñāyamānasaṃbandhatvena pūrvamajñātasaṃbandhasyābodhakatvāt | ato'vaśyaṃ pramāṇāntareṇa pramitiriti vācyam | ata āpannaṃ pramāṇāntaragocaratvam | atha na pramitaḥ, tato yadāpi śabdaḥ pramitaḥ, tadā vidhyartho na pramitaḥ ; tataśca dvayoḥ saṃbandhinorajñānāt tadāśrayaḥ saṃbandho jñātumaśakyaḥ | etaccābhyupagamyoktam | taccatastu na liṅādayaḥ kevalā vaidikāḥ ; tena teṣāṃ paśvādiśabdavallaukikādeva vyutpattiryuktā | 15A
[p. 228]
yo'pi manyate—śaktiḥ śabdasya saṃbandhaḥ | sā ca nārthe pravṛttihetau, tasya tatpratyayadvāreṇa pratīyamānatvena vyavahitatvāt | kiṃtu tatpratyaye ; sa hi śabdasya sākṣātkāryaḥ | sa eva ca prayojyavṛddhasya pravṛttyānumito na pravṛttihetuḥ | tenaiva ca liṅgadeḥ śabdasya saṃbandhajñānam | tena na pravṛttihetoḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratā | na ca saṃbandhajñānānupapattiḥ, pravṛttihetāva- P.106-15 pramite'pi tatpratyayena pramitena tadupapatteriti | sa idaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ—kiṃ pratyayamātre śabdasya sāmarthyaṃ gṛhyate? athālaukikapravṛttihetupratyaye? yadyādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadā tataḥ prayojyasya pratyayāttatpravṛttyānumitāt bālena yatkiñcitpratīyeta, nālaukikaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ | atha prayojyasya pravṛttihetupratyayaḥ, tatpravṛttyā laukikāsambhavena ca pāriśeṣyādalaukikārthaviṣayamanumāya tatra bālaḥ śabdasya sāmarthyaṃ pratyeti, tato viśiṣṭaprayojyapratyayānumitau tadviśeṣaṇasya pravṛttihetorapi pravṛttipāriśeṣyābhyāmanumitatvādāpannaṃ tasya pramāṇāntaragocaratvam; tathāhi—smṛtaṃ vā viśeṣaṇaṃ viśiṣṭapramitāvaṅgaṃ bhavati, yathā—‘abhimān dhūmāt’ ityādisaṃbandhajñānasamaye smarthamāṇo'mristadviśiṣṭaparvatapramitau; pramitaṃ vā, yathā—‘śuklo gauḥ’ iti śauklyaṃ tadviśiṣṭagopramitau | tadiha yadi pravṛttihetustatpratyayasya smṛtau yadi vā pramitau viśeṣaṇam, ubhayathāpi pramāṇāntaraviṣayo na śabdaikagamyaḥ, śabdasya tadānīmagṛhītasaṃbandhatvāt | tatra yadi smṛtyākṛṣṭaḥ pravṛttihetustatpratyayasya viśeṣaṇam, tataḥ smṛteḥ pramitijanyatvādavaśyaṃ pramāṇāntaraṃ smṛtisiddhye'nveṣṭavyam | ataḥ pramitatvānna śabdaikagamyatvam | atha pramitau viśeṣaṇam, tadāpi viśiṣṭānumitau viśeṣaṇasyāpyanumitatvāt pravṛttihetoḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratvam | atha matam—satyaṃ pratītālaukikapravṛttihetuviśiṣṭa eva prayojyapratyayastatpravṛttyā pāriśeṣyādanumānenānumīyate, tathāpi prayojyasya pratyaya evānumānasya pramāṇyaṃ na tadviśeṣaṇe pravṛttihetau; tasya tu tatra pratītimātram, na tu pramitiḥ; pramitistu tasya pravṛttyānumitāt prayojyapratyayādeva; sa ca śabda iti nānumeyatvam; taccapramitasya viśeṣaṇatvaṃ kimedamanyatra dṛṣṭam | dṛṣṭamiti brūmaḥ; yathā—āptavacanaṃ viśiṣṭe kṣattupratyaye pramāṇam, na pratyayamātre; viśiṣṭatā ca pratyayasyārthānubandhena, na svataḥ | sa cārthaḥ
[p. 229]
pratīta eva pratyayaṃ viśinaṣṭi | na cāptavacanapramitaḥ, tasya vaktṛpratyaya mātre prāmāṇyāt; arthasya ca tata eva vaktṛpratyayāduttarakāle pramiteḥ, tataḥ pūrvameva ca viśiṣṭapratyayapratīteḥ | tasmātpratītamātraḥ pravṛttihetu-statpratyayānumitau viśeṣaṇam, na smṛto na pramita iti | tadayuktam; P. 107-4 pañcavidhā hi pratītiḥ—pramāṇasaṃśayaviparyayavikalpasmṛtirūpā | tatra yayā pratītyā pravṛttihetuḥ pratītamātraṃ ucyate, tayāvaśyaṃ pramāṇādiṣu madhye'nyatarayā bhāvyam | tatra na tāvatsmṛtiḥ, prāganavagateḥ; avagataṃ hi smaryate | na saṃśayaḥ, ubhayakoṭisaṃsparśaśirahāt | na ca vikalpaḥ; sa hi vastu-śūnyam; taduktam—“śabdajñānānupānī vastuśūnyo vikalpaḥ” iti | tatra pravṛttihetupratītiścet tadviṣayavastuśūnyā syāt, tataḥ pravṛttihetorabhāvāttatpratyayasyāpi prayojyavṛddhagatasya bālenānumīyamānasya vastuśūnyatayā vikalpatvaṃ prasajyeta | tadā ca pravṛttihetu vastu nāstyeva; ko vidhyartho bhaviṣyati ? na ca viparyayaḥ; yatastatra pravṛttihetau yathārthatve prayojya-pratyayasyābhimate satyārthagrāhiṇī satī bālasya pravṛttihetuḥ pratītiḥ katha-marthaśūnyā syāt ? smṛtitvaṃ ca nirastam | pāriśeṣyāt pramāṇamityāpannā pravṛttihetoḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratā | yacca prayojyapratyayādvālasya pravṛttihetu-pramitiḥ; sa ca pratyayaḥ śabdottha ityananyapramāṇakatvamuktam; tadapyayuktam; prayojyavṛddhasya hi śabdotthaḥ, na bālasya | na cānyapratyayenānyo'vabudhyate | tasmādvālaḥ pratyayāntareṇa budhyata iti na śabdaikagamyatā vidhyarthasya | atha matam—na prayojyapratyayena bālo budhyate, apitu svapratyayenaiva; pravṛttyanumitaśabdotthaprayojyapratyayasāmarthyena tu tataḥ; tena tasya śabdaika-gamyatvamiti | tatra pṛcchāmaḥ—yadvālasya svaṃ jñānam, tat kṛtamat pramāṇam ? na tāvacchābdam; śabdajñānādvi yadarthajñānaṃ tacchābdam; na ca bālasya pūrvamavyutpannasya śabdajñānādarthajñānam, apitu prayojyasya | yacchabdātpravṛttiheturūpārthajñānaṃ bālena pravṛttyānumitaṃ tataśca bālasya jñānaṃ na śābdamiti tadgamyasya vidhyarthasyāpannaṃ pramāṇāntaragocaratvam | atha matam—na brūmo bālasya svajñānaṃ śābdamiti; kiṃtu yatprayojyasya pravṛtti-hetuviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ pravṛttyā bālenānumīyate, yanmukhena pravṛttihetuṃ bālaḥ pratyeti, tasya prāmāṇye sati tadviṣayapravṛttihetubālasya pratidhyati, nānyathā |
[p. 230]
tasya ca prāmāṇyaṃ nirdoṣācchabdāduddhavati | tena śabdādhīnastatra bālajñāne pravṛttihetuniścaya iti | yadyapi bālaḥ svajñānena prayojyapratyayaviśeṣaṇatvena pravṛttihetuṃ pratyeti, tathāpi nirdoṣaśabdabalena tatra niścaya iti śabdasya tatra pravṛttihetau prāmāṇyamucyate; na bālajñānasya, tasyāniścayakatvāditi | P.107-15 tadapyasāram; yataḥ śabdādyadarthajñānaṃ tacchābdam; na tu paramparayāpi yatra śabdādhīno niścayaḥ, tacchābdam | yaduktam—āptavākyāt vaktṛjñāne pratīte tata evārthasiddhiḥ, pratītimātreṇaiva vaktṛjñāne'rtho viśeṣaṇamiti; tadapyayuktam; yataḥ puruṣavacanamapi nārthaṃ na pramāṇam, na hyarthamapratītya jñānaviśeṣaḥ pratyetuṃ śakyaḥ | tasmādvaktṛjñānaviśeṣaṇatvenārthaḥ pratyetavyaḥ | viśeṣaṇaṃ ca smṛtaṃ vā, pramitaṃ vā | tatrārthasya pūrvamananubhūtasya smṛtyasaṃbhavāt pramīyamāṇaṃ evārtho vaktṛjñāne viśeṣaṇam | na ca tatprāmitiḥ śabdādanyena pramāṇena, tasyānupalabdheḥ | tasmācchabdapramāṇikaiva tatra pratītiriti nārthaḥ pratītimātreṇa viśeṣaṇam | na ca vaktṛjñānenaiva śābdena, parajñānena parasyāpratīteḥ | ataḥ puṃvacanamadṛṣṭāntaḥ | nanu puṃvacasi śābdena svajñānena cet śrotārthaṃ budhyate na vaktṛjñānena, kathaṃ tarhi ‘apica pauruṣeyādvacanāt’ ityādinā vaktṛjñānādhīnārthasiddhiāratyucyate ? tat vaktṛjñānamithyātve tadasaṃbhave vā caityavandanādivākyeṣu yā puṃvākyāt vaktṛjñānapratītiḥ sā bādhyate, vaktṛjñānapratītyabādhe ca sā na bādhyata iti kṛtvā; na punaḥ puṃvacaso'rtho na viṣayaḥ, kiṃtu vaktṛjñānena śroturarthapratītirityabhiprāyeṇa | yadi tu puṃvacaso'rtho na viṣayaḥ syāt, tataḥ puṃvacaso'rthaṃ sāpekṣatvam ‘arthamātre ca sāpekṣatvam’ ityatra yaduktaṃ tatra syāt | viṣaye hi sāpekṣanirapekṣate bhavataḥ, nāviṣaye | tathā coktaṃ vārttikakṛtā—“prāmāṇyasthāpanaṃ tu syādvaktṛdhīhetusaṃbhavāt” iti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—puṃvacaso'rthaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ svata eva; vaktṛdhīhetusaṃbhavena tu pratiṣṭhāpyeta | yadi tu vaktṛbuddherheturna saṃbhavet caityavandanādāviva buddhabuddheḥ, tato bādhyeta | tenābādhanamatra sthāpanamabhipretam, na tu prāmāṇyameva | tathā prāmāṇyasthāpanamityuktam, na tu prāmāṇyamiti | etacca prāmāṇyasthāpanaṃ vākyasya, na tu vaktṛdhiya eva | tena na vaktṛdhiyo'rthaṃ prāmāṇyam, apitu vākyasya | kiṃca “autpattikastu śabdasyārthena saṃbandhaḥ” ityartha-
[p. 231]
grahaṇādaparyante śabdasya vācyavācakayogyatālakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ, na jñānena | sa cāpramite saṃbandhinyarthe jñātumaśakya ityāpannaṃ vidhyarthasya pramāṇāntaragocaratvam | na ca vācyam—syādevaṃ yadi pramite vidhyarthe jñānādeḥ saṃbandho gṛhyata, kiṃtu pratītimātra iti ; yataḥ sā pratītirna tāvatsaṃśayaḥ, ubhayakoṭisparśavirahāt | tatra yadyarthavatī, tataḥ smṛtiḥ pramāṇaṃ vā syāt | P 108-11 tatra ca vidhyarthasya pramāṇāntaragocaratvamuktam | atha viparyayavikalparūpā nārthavatī ; tano vidhyartho nāstyeva ; kena liṅādeḥ saṃbandhaḥ ? kiṃca yadi śabdapratīteranyayāpyaprāmāṇikayā pratītyā vidhyarthaḥ pratīyeta, tataḥ śabdaikagamyatvātikramāt pramāṇāntaragamyatvamapyāśaṅkyeta | yadapyuktam—laukikaśreyaḥsādhanatā pramāṇāntaragocaratā ; tataḥ sā yadi liṅarthaḥ syāt, tataḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratāpi śabdārthānupraveśinī syāditi ; tadapyayuktam ; yataḥ tasmin sati śabdaḥ prayujyate, yadvā tasminnasati na prayujyate, ityetāvanmātreṇa na śabdārtho bhavati ; anyathā vṛkṣatve sati śiśapāśabdaḥ prayujyate, tasminnasati ghaṭādau na prayujyate iti tadapi śabdārthaḥ syāt | ka nāma dvitīyo'pi śabdārtho bhavati ? tathā satsu kṣitipavanādiṣu bījābhāve nāṅkurotpattiriti bījamapi heturgamyate | evaṃ yatraikasminprayojake śabdaprayoganimitte satyapi dvitīyasyābhāvānna śabdasya prayogaḥ, tatra so'pi prayojakaḥ prayoganimittaṃ gamyate, yathā gau(śo)ṇaśabde ; tatra hi satyapyaruṇatve gavādāvaprayogādaśvatvamapi prayojakamityeṣā tāvadanyatra sthitiḥ | iha ca liṅādau śreyohetau liṅādidarśanāt tadabhāve cāśreyohetoḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratve satyapyadarśanāt śreyohetutvaṃ tāvacchabdārthaḥ | yadyapi śreyohetau pramāṇāntaragamye liṅādedarśanam, pramāṇāntaragamye nṛśṛṅgādau pramāṇāntaragamyatvābhāvenādarśanam, tathāpi yadi śreyohetutve sati pramāṇāntaragamyatvābhāvādadarśanaṃ syāt tataḥ śoṇanyāyena pramāṇāntaragamyatvamapi śabdārthaḥ syāt ; na tvevamasti | kiṃtu yatra pramāṇāntaragamyatvaṃ nṛśṛṅgādau nāsti, tatra śreyohetutvamapi nāsti | tena ubhayābhāve liṅāderaprayogāt kiṃ śreyohetutvasya vyatirekādaprayogaḥ, uta pramāṇāntaragamyatvasyeti saṃdigdho vyatirekaḥ | tatra śreyaḥsādhanatāyāḥ pūrvaṃ prayojakatvasyāvadhāritatvāt tadabhāve tadabhāvanibandhana evāprayogaḥ, na pramāṇāntaragamyatvābhāvanibandhana
[p. 232]
P.109-1 iti niścīyate | tataśca śreyaḥsādhanatve sati prayogaḥ tadabhāve cāprayoga ityanvayavyatirekayorupapattī na pramāṇāntaragamyatvasya śabdārthatve kiñcitpramāṇamasti | ataḥ śreyaḥsādhanatāyāḥ śabdārthatve pramāṇāntaragamyatāpi śabdārthaḥ syāditi yaduktaṃ tannirastam | yadi punaryarthoktaprakāraṃ vinā anvayavyatirekamāleṇa śabdārthatvaṃ syāt, tataḥ sattāvṛkṣatvādiṣu satsu śiśapāyāṃ prayogāt tadabhāve ca nṛśṛṅgādāvaprayogāt sattādayo'pi śiśapāśabdārthāḥ syuḥ | atha nṛśṛṅgādau śiśapārthābhāvādevāprayogopapatterna sattāvabhāvasyāprayogahetutvam, ato na sattādeḥ śiśapāśabdārthatvamityucyate, tacchiṅgādāvapi samānam | yathā caitattathoktam | atha śreyaḥsādhanatā pramāṇāntaragamyā; sā cedvidhyarthaḥ syāt, tato vede pramāṇāntarābhāvāt prāmāṇyaṃ na syāt | ataḥ pramāṇāntarānavagamya eva vede vidhyartha ityucyate, tadasat | na hi ya ekatra yasya pramāṇasya gocaraḥ, so'nyatrāpi tasyaiva gocaraḥ, tatpramāṇābhāve vā pratīyamāno'pi mithyā bhavati; yataḥ samīpe vahnirindriyagocaraḥ, dūre tvanumānādigocaraḥ | na sa ekendriyagocara iti sarvatraiva tathā; na ca pratīyamāno vā mithyā | tena yadi nāma loke bhojanādāvitaraphale śreyaḥsādhanatā anvayavyatirekāvagamyā, tathāpi vyavahitaphaleṣu yāgādiṣvanvayavyatirekābhāvāt śabdaikagamyā vede bhaviṣyati | ko doṣaḥ? vaidikī śreyaḥsādhanatā pramāṇāntarānupalabdhyā bādhyate, yathā pratyakṣānupalabdhyā bādhyate śaśaśṛṅgamityayaṃ doṣa iti cet; nāyamapi doṣaḥ, yataḥ pramāṇāntaraṃ dṛṣṭaviṣayam | adṛṣṭaviṣayastu śabdaḥ | tenādṛṣṭaṃ pramāṇāntarasya viṣaya eva na bhavati | na ca yo yasyāviṣayaḥ sa tenānupalabdhyā bādhyate, yathā cakṣuṣānupalabdhyā rasaḥ | śaśaśṛṅgaṃ ca na kenāpi pramāṇena gṛhyate | śreyaḥsādhanatā tu śabdena gamyate | na caikapramāṇagamyamanyājñānādvādhyate rasavadeva | atha pramāṇāntaraviṣayā cet sādhanatā vede liṅyaḥ syāt, tataḥ pramāṇāntaraviṣaye pramāṇāntareṇa virodhāt vedasyāprāmāṇyaṃ syāt; asti ca yāgādau anantaraphalānupalabdhyestathaiva virodhaḥ; pramāṇāntarāviṣaye tu liṅye naiṣa doṣa iti pramāṇāntarāviṣaya eva vede vidhyartha āśrīyate, netara iti cet; tadasat; yato yadi vede phalasyānantaryaṃ coditaṃ syāt, tato'nantaraphalānupalabdhyā virodhaḥ syāt; na tvetadasti | taduktam—“ānantaryaṃ hyacoditam” iti |
[p. 233]
nanu yadyapyānantaryamacoditam; pramāṇāntaragamyatā ca na śabdārthaḥ; P.109-14 tathāpi pramāṇāntarasiddhā cet śreyaḥsādhanatā vede'pi liṅyaḥ, tato yādṛśī sā loke siddhā tādṛśyāśrayitavyā | sā cānantarabhāviśreyaḥ sādhanatā pramāṇāntarasiddhā | ato vede tādṛśyevāśrayitavyā; anyathā na śabdatā syāt | na ca yāgādāvanantarabhāviśreyaḥsādhanateti vede na sā śabdārtha iti cet, tatra brūmaḥ; yattāvaduktam—anantaraphalasādhanatā loke siddheti, tattāvanna; kṛṣyādiṣu vyavahitaphaleṣu vyabhicārāt | atha tatrāpi yadyapi sukhalābhādi vyavahitam, tathāpi bhūtaṃskārāṅkurajanmādyavāntarakāryamanantarameva bhavatītyucyate, tadyāgādāvapi saptānam | teṣvapi hi apūrvamanantaramutpadyata ityarthāpatyā siddham | taduktam— “ phalāya vihitaṃ karma kṣaṇikaṃ cirabhāvine | tastiddhirnānyathetyevamapūrvaṃ ca prakalpyate ||” iti | kiṃca yatra viśiṣṭā anantarā śreyaḥsādhanatā siddhā, tatra tādviśeṣaṇaṃ śreyaḥsādhanatāmātramapi siddham | ato yathā gotvaviśiṣṭādāṃ vyaktau gośabdaprayoge gotvameva tadviśeṣaṇaṃ tadarthaḥ, na tadviśeṣaḥ; tathā ihāpi śreyaḥsādhanatāmātraṃ liṅyaḥ, nānantaryaṃ viśeṣaḥ | tena yāgādāvanantaraphalādarśane'pi na kaścidviśeṣaḥ | tadevaṃ laukike'pi śreyaḥ sādhanatve pravṛttihetau liṅyaṃ doṣābhāvāt alaukikavidhyarthakalpanā vyarthaiva | kiṃca yathā mukhaprasādādinā kasyacit sukhasaṃvedane'nubhīyamāne, sukhamapi tadanumānaprameyaṃ bhavati, na tadarthaṃ pṛthak pramāṇamarthyate; tathā prayojyavṛddhapravṛttyā pravṛttihetupratyaye'nubhīyamāne pravṛttiheturapyanumeyo bhavati | ato na tasya śabdaikagamyatvam | yadāpīdaṃ darśanam—vaktṛjñānameva puṃvacaso'rthaḥ; tacca tasya vaktujñānamarthaṃ vinā na bhavatītyanupapattyā; yadvā āptajñānamarthānvitam, āptajñānatvāt pūrvadṛṣṭāptajñānatvāt ityanumānenārthasiddhiriti; tadapi prayojyapravṛttyā pramitaḥ pravṛttihetupratyayo na pravṛttihetumantareṇetyāptavacanārthasyeva pravṛttihetoranumeyatayā | ato na śabdagamyatvasiddhiḥ | anumeyatve iti vṛttau cānumānasāmyādarthāpattigamyatāpi draṣṭavyā | atha yataḥ pravṛttihetupratyayāt
[p. 234]
