pravṛttiheturanubhīyate, tasya liṅādiśabdagamyatvācchabdaikagamyatvaṃ pravṛttihetorucyate ; tadasat ; pravṛttihetupratyayasya śabdasādhanatve'pi pravṛttiheturanubheyaḥ, na sa tasya śabdo bhavati | anyathā āptavacane yadāptajñānaṃ tadyadākṣasādhanaṃ bhavati, tadā tadanubheyo'rthaḥ śrotuḥ pratyakṣapramāṇakaḥ syāt | na caivam | ato na pravṛttihetoḥ śabdaikagamyatvam | tasmāt 'tasyaivamanubheyatve' ityādi śobhanamuktam | P.110-9. atha pramāṇarūpeṇa sa vidhyarthaḥ svaśabdena liṅādinā prakāśitaḥ, pramāṇasya prameyamantareṇāsaṃbhavāt apūrvasya padārthasaṃsargasyākṣeptā saṃbandhajñānanirapekṣatvāt pramāṇāntaraṃ prati nirapekṣaśca kathyate | yadi hyasau gośabdavat saṃbandhajñānāpekṣaḥ syāt, tataḥ saṃbandhajñānasya saṃbandhijñānādhānatvāt saṃbandhijñānāya ca pramāṇāntaramapekṣate ; tatra ca yathāsiddhamanūya māno nāpūrvasaṃsargamākṣipet ; na tvetadasti ; kiṃtu nirapekṣaśca kathyate'nyapramāṃ prati ityāśaṅkayati—atheti | taddūṣayati—tathāpīti | yadyapi pramāṇātmā vidhyarthaḥ, tathāpi pramāṇāntareṇāsiddhe tasminnapūrvoktāvavyutpannapravṛttipadadharmavyatikramākhyau doṣau syātmā ; pramāṇāntaratviddhe ca tasminnanuvādatā anūdyamānatā | tacca yathāvagato'nūdyamāno nāpūrvasya saṃsargasyākṣeptā syāt | kiṃca yatra prameyasya vidhyarthasya pramāṇyaṃ tatra śruteḥ śrūyamāṇasya śabdasyaiva | tataśca vṛthā tvayaṃ vidhyarthaṃ ityarthaḥ | nanu śabde pramāṇe tasyānuvādakatvādapūrvārthāvagamo na syāt ; tena tadvācyo vidhyarthaḥ pramāṇamiṣyate ; naivam ; śabdaśredanuvādakaḥ, tadvācyaṃ vidhyarthākhyaṃ pramāṇamapi yathāvagamamanūdyamānaṃ kathamapūrvamarthaṃ saṃsargākhyamavabodhayet ? na ca—pramāṇamanūdyate, na tatprameyam ; tena tadapūrvamarthaṃ bodhayiṣyatīti vācyam | yataḥ pramāṇaṃ cedanūdyate nūnaṃ tatprameyamapyanuvaditavyam | na prameyasaṃsparśāhitā viśiṣṭapramāṇāvagatirasti | ataḥ pramāṇamanūdyamānaṃ yathāvagataprameyameva pratīyata iti nāpūrvaprameyākṣepasiddhirityarthaḥ | kiṃca eṣa vidhyarthaḥ kāryaṃ pramāṇam ? na tāvadyāgādau svaśabdako, svīyo yajatyādiśabdo yasyāsti sa svaśabdakaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati— yajatyādiśabdenaiva yāgādiruktaḥ ; kiṃ tava vidhyartheneti | na ca padārtha
[p. 235]
saṃsargo'pūrvastasya prameyaḥ ; yato laukike śabde 'brāhmaṇa, putrasta jātaḥ' ityādau avidhau vidhirahite'pi śrotuḥ apūrvaḥ padārthasaṃsargo'sti pratīyate P. 111-2 śrotreti yāvat | ato na vidhipramāṇako'sāviti bhāvaḥ | atha sargādikāmino niyogaḥ, kāmaṃ pratyanupāye yāgādau viṣaye nāvarkalpate | kartavye hi viṣaye kāmino niyogaḥ ; kāmopāyaśca kartavyo nānyaḥ | tatrākāmopāyo yāgādirna kāminā kṛtaḥ syāditi kāmopāyaḥ | na cāśakto'sau kāmopāyo bhavatītyevaṃ yāgādeḥ phalasādhanaśaktau niyogaḥ pramāṇamiti cettatrāha—śabdastatra phalasādhanaśaktau na duṣyati | 'yāgena sargaṃ sādhayet' iti śabde'pi yāgasya phalasādhanaśaktimavagamayitumalam | ataḥ kastasya doṣaḥ ? yena tamatilaṅghya vidhirāśrīyate | na ca—śabdaḥ pramāṇāntarāpekṣaḥ, tena nāpūrvaphalasādhanaśaktimavagamayitumalamiti vācyam, yataḥ pramāṇāntarāpekṣā vaidikasya śabdasyāpauruṣeyatvena pūrvameva 'apauruṣeyatā tena pauruṣeyatvameva ca' ityatra pratyuktā | na ca śabdagamye phalasādhanatve anantaraṃ phalādarśanāt anupalabdhyā virodha iti vācyam ; niyogagamye'pyasya virodhasya tulyatvāt | athānantaryasyācoditatvāt tatra virodhābhāvaḥ, sa śabde'pi pramāṇe tulya ityadoṣaḥ | anyo'rthaḥ—śabdaḥ tatra phalasādhanaśaktau na duṣyati | niyogātphalasādhanatvāvagamo na saṃbhavati | taccedaṃ vidhivivekabhāvanāvivekābhyāmavagantavyam | tatra hi—niyogo viṣayaniyojyamātrāpakṣo na phalasādhanāpekṣaḥ | adhikārī ca niyojyaḥ | na cāviśiṣṭo'dhikārī bhavatīti tatra sargakāmapadamadhikāriviśeṣaṇam | sargakāmanāyāśca jīvanabhedādivat nimittatvenāpyadhikāriviśeṣaṇatvopapatteḥ na niyogātsargasya sādhyatvaṃ yāgasya tatsādhanatvamavagantuṃ śakyata ityādyuktam | kiṃca phalasādhanaśaktiścetriyogasya prameyaḥ, nityeṣu tadabhāvāt prameyānupapattiḥ | tadevaṃ yasmānniyogasya pramāṇatvena viśeṣo nāsti, tasmāt padārthāntaraiḥ saṃbandhajñānāpekṣapratītibhiranūdyamānatvenāpūrvasaṃsargamākṣettumaśakteniyogasya tulyatvāt na niyogasyaiva viṣayasiddhyarthaṃ yā saṃsargakāṅkṣā tannibandhanaḥ padārthānāṃ saṃsargaḥ, kiṃtu sarve eva padārthāḥ svaśabdairanyonyasaṃbandhaṃ prati yadyogyaṃ kriyākārakādirūpaṃ tena prakāśitāḥ santaḥ saṃsargabhāginaḥ | yato viśiṣṭo-
[p. 236]
'rthaḥ kathaṃ nu nāma pratīyatāmiti tatra yuktā padānāṃ samabhivyāhṛtiḥ samuccāraṇaṃ na padārthamātrapratipādanaprayuktā, tanmātrasya vyavahārānaṅgatvāt | viśiṣṭatā padārthasya na saṃsargamantareṇa | tasmādevamapi sarve eva padārthāḥ saṃsargākṣepakā na niyoga eva | ato niyogābhāve'pi padārthānāṃ saṃsarga upapadyate | na saṃsargāya vedānteṣu niyogānupraveśaḥ kalpyata ityarthaḥ | P. 112-6. tatrāparaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—kathamiti | niyogaparatvena loke padānāṃ vyutpatteravaśyaṃ vedānteṣu niyoga upāsyata ityarthaḥ | tadyabhicārayati—naitaditi | svarūpaparādapi vyutpatteḥ naitadaikāntikamityarthaḥ | na cātrāpi ‘ānaya’ ityādiniyogaḥ kalpyaḥ; agrisatāmātrapratyayenaiva tadarthinaḥ svayameva pravṛttisiddherna vākyasya niyojanasāmarthyaṃ śakyaṃ kalpayitum | kiṃca padārthamātre padaprayogaviśiṣṭārthapratyayayoranupapattyā niyogānvayaḥ kalpyaḥ | tayośca yena kenacit padārthāntareṇānvaye'pyupapatteḥ na niyogānvaye pramāṇamasti | yadi ca loke niyogānvayo dṛṣṭa iti vede'pi tathāśrīyate tato loke pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvenānvayo dṛṣṭa iti vede'pi tathāsyāt | na ca tatra pramāṇāntaramasti | ato vedārthaḥ śabdapramāṇako na syādityuktam | atha pravṛttinivṛttī vākyasya prayojane, nānye | te ca kartavyatājñānādhīne | tena ca kartavyatājñānādvinā vākyasyānarthakyam | ato vedānteṣu vidhirupāsyata ityucyate; tatrocyate—‘ātmā pratipattavyaḥ’ iti pratipattividhau satyapyātmapratipatteḥ uttaratra pravṛttinivṛttyorabhāvāt vākyānarthakyaṃ tulyam | tayorabhāve'pi ātmasvarūpapratipattireva vidheḥ prayojanamiti cet, samānametat svarūpaniṣṭhe'pi vākye | nanu na pratipattividhau tulyamānarthakyam; yāvatā prajñākriyopāsanādyadhikāravidhibhirupāsyasyātmano viṣayabhūtasya svarūpāvagamamantareṇānupapadyamānaiḥ apekṣitamarthamupāsyamātmasvarūpaṃ samarpayati pratipattividhiḥ; ato nāsyānarthakyamiti | atrottaraṃ—samānametadityanuvartanīyam | svarūpaniṣṭhānāmapi vedāntānāmupāsanādiviṣayabhūtātmasvarūpasamarpakatvaṃ samānam | ataścaivamapi pratipattividhivaryartha evetyarthaḥ | “vijñāya prajñāṃ kurdhīta” iti prajñākriyā upāsana-
[p. 237]
kriyocyate; “sa kratuṃ kurvīta” iti kraturmanovyāpāraḥ; so'pyupāsanaiva; “ātmekhevopāsīta” ityupāsanā copāsanaiva | evamarthābhede'pi vidhibhedāt bhedenopanyāsaḥ kṛtaḥ | śloke tu ‘prajñākriyā’ ityasyopalakṣaṇārthatvāt kratukriyāpi draṣṭavyā | kriyeti vā kratukriyaiva pṛthak | sūtre'dhikāraśabdaḥ prajñādibhiḥ pratyekamabhisaṃbandhyate | mokṣārthī adhikriyate yeṣu vidhiṣu te adhikārāḥ, teṣāṃ siddhiḥ, tathā vyapekṣitamityanvayaḥ | vidhiniṣṭhatve'nyaparatvena tathā ātmasvarūpaṃ samarpayanti vedāntāḥ, yathā svarūpaniṣṭhatve ityabhiprāyeṇa vṛttau sutarāmityuktam | nanu yadi vedāntavacaḥ prajñādibhiḥ pratyekamabhisaṃbandhyate viṣayaṃ samarpayati, tataḥ prajñādiniyogānupraveśinastasya svarūpaniṣṭhatā hīyata iti codayati— hanteti | atrottaram—tulyamiti | yadi prajñādiniyogānupraveśānmatpakṣe P.112-21 svarūpaniṣṭhatā hīyate, tavāpi tarhi tatpravṛttividhivacaḥ prajñādiniyogānupraveśāt na svavākyagatapratipattiniyoganiṣṭhaṃ syādityarthaḥ | ‘samāptaṃ ca vākyaṃ vākyāntarānugam’ ityatra ‘cet’ iti ‘tulyametat’ iti cobhayamanuvartate | tenāyamarthaḥ—pratipattividhivākyaṃ svagataniyoge samāptaṃ tatparyavasitaṃ sat prajñādibhiḥ pratyekamabhisaṃbandhyate prajñādivākyenānvīyate; tena pratipattividhivākyasya svagataniyoganiṣṭhatā na hīyata iti cet, ucyate— svarūpaniṣṭhatve'pi vedāntavākyasya tulyametat yaduktaṃ tvayā | tasyāpi pratipattividhivākyavat prajñādiniyogānupraveśino'pi svarūpaniṣṭhatā na hāsyata ityarthaḥ | ‘so'yaṃ prākaraṇikaḥ’ ityādinā svaniyogaparyavasitasyaivāyaṃ vākyāntareṇānvaya ityetadeva vṛttau dṛḍhīkṛtam | prākaraṇiko hi saṃbandhaḥ svaniyogaparyavasitasyaiva bhavati, nāparyavasitasya; yathā—prayājādivākyasya darśādivākyaiḥ | aparyavasitasya tu vākyalakṣaṇe yathā arthavādānāṃ vidhibhiḥ | prākaraṇikaścāyaṃ pratipattividheḥ saṃbandhaḥ | tasmātsvārthaparyavasitamiti bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ sati niyogānupraveśaṃ vinā vṛthātvāpattervedāntānāṃ svarūpaniṣṭhānāmapi prayājādivākyavattriyogānupraveśastāvadasti | ato na niyoganirapekṣāṇāṃ teṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ tvadiṣṭaṃ sidhyatītyabhiprāyeṇāśaṅkayati—niyogeneti | atrottaramāha—na ceti | viṣayasamarpaṇārthaścet ātmatattvāvabodhavidhiḥ, tato vedāntā eva tat samarpa-
[p. 238]
yiṣyanti | kiṃ taccāvabodhavidhinā? ityevaṃ taccāvabodhavidhyaṃśabhāvastāvat uddhṛto nirākṛtaḥ | na tu paramārthato vedāntānāṃ niyogānupraveśitā, svarūpamātraniṣṭhatve vyutpattipratipattyoruktatvāditi bhāvaḥ | P.113-20 atra codayati—nanviti | “āgneyo'ṣṭākapālo bhavati” ityādirutpattividhiḥ yathā āgneyādikarmasvarūpabodhe vyavasthitaḥ karmasvarūpabodhārtho na pravṛttyarthaḥ, tathā ātmataccāvabodhavidhirapi taccāvabodhe vyavasthite, taccāvabodhamātrārtha evāstu, na pravṛttyarthaḥ | pravṛttistu “prajñāṃ kurvīta” ityadhikārāt adhikāravidhervatā ; yathā āgneyādau “darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeta” iti karmādhikāravidheḥ | yat eva pravṛttinivṛttirūpaṃ prayojanadvayaṃ vidheḥ, ato dvividhaṃ vidhimācakṣate dhīrā ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati— yathotpattividhiḥ karmasvarūpaṃ bodhayat nānarthakaḥ ; evamātmataccāvabodhavidhiḥ ātmasvarūpaṃ bodhayannānarthakaḥ | ato noddhartavya iti | utpattividhirajñātajñāpake, adhikāravidhirapravṛttapravartane iti vivekaḥ | utpattividhipūrvako'dhikāravidhiriti sūcanārtho'thaśabdaḥ | taddūṣayati—etaditi | tādṛśamiti ; pravṛttiḥ | atha sādhanasāmānye, itthaṃbhāvasāmānye'pi samānā ; yato yathā phalārthī tadupāyasāmānyamicchati, tathā tadanugrahakasāmānyamapi ; niranugrahasya sādhanasyāsamarthatvāt | athetthaṃbhāvaviśeṣo vidhīyate, tataḥ sādhanaviśeṣo'pi tathā vidhāsyata iti na tatrāpyarthalakṣaṇā pravṛttirityarthaḥ | -itaśca phalasādhane vidhāyakasya vyāpāra ityāha—apekṣiteti | apravṛtta- P.116-17 pravartanarūpo vidhiḥ | pravṛttihetuśca dharmaḥ pravartanā | sa ca dvividhaḥ— iṣṭatā, tatsādhanatā ca | yata iṣṭe tatsādhane vā puruṣaḥ pravartate, nānyatra | tatreṣṭatāyāmiṣṭaphaladharmabhūtāyāṃ jānātyevāsau ‘mayaitatkartavyam’ iti ; pramāṇāntarasiddhāyāṃ svayameva puruṣasya pravṛttiḥ, nāpravṛttapravartanātvam | ato na sā vidhiḥ | iṣṭasādhanatā tu yāgāderna pramāṇāntarāvaseyā ; apitu śabdaikagamyā | teneṣṭasādhane śabdādeva pravṛttiḥ, nānyata iti tasyā eva pravṛttihetutvam | teneṣṭasādhanataiva śabdapramāṇikā satī vidhiriti gīyate | nanvastu iṣṭasādhanaṃ pravṛttihetuḥ, sa ca vidhiḥ | anuṣṭhānaṃ tu kathaṃ tadviṣayamavagamyate ? P. 119-1 mate'pi vidyānantyamāha—kṣaṇiketi | atra siddhāntamāha—na ceti | ślokārthamupapādayituṃ mokṣasvarūpanirūpaṇāya pṛcchati—kaḥ punariti | tatra na tāvadanāgatadehādyanutpādo mokṣaḥ, tasya prāṇabhāvatvenāsādhyatvādityāha— yadīti | aśarīraṃ vāveti vāvaśṭhaddo'vadhāraṇārthaḥ | tadevamasaṃbhavādasminyakṣe niraste sāretaravibhāgāya anekānmokṣapakṣānupanyasyati—atheti | caitragrāmayoriveti; gatipūrvā saṃyogalakṣaṇāṃ brahmaprāptimāha | āyanniti; gacchannityarthaḥ | sa yāvaditi; manasaḥ kṣiprasyaiva brahmaprāptyarthamādityalokaṃ gacchato yoginaḥ kṣipratāmāha | etadvai khakhiti mārgastutiḥ | arciriti arcirādimārgakathanam | madhukṛto madhumakṣikāḥ | vidhūyeti apanīyetyarthaḥ | nirañjana iti; dehādivyañjanarahita ityarthaḥ | yogavyāpravaditi; yathā yogabalena prāptāṣṭaguṇaiśvaryā vyāghrarūpeṇātmānaṃ pariṇamayanti, tadvadityarthaḥ | ‘brahma saṃpadyate’ ityatra darśanādityanuvartate | rāgādyapakarṣaṇa iti; japādyupādhikahipatarāgāpanayane; rajyate aneneti vā rāgaḥ japādireva, tadapakarṣaṇa ityarthaḥ | tatra pṛthaksvarūpasya gatipūrvakabrahmaprāptipakṣaṃ tāvat dūṣayati—tatreti | hetumāha—sarveti | caitragrāmayoḥ prāptiryuktā; na tu jīvabrahmaṇoḥ, brahmaṇaḥ sarvagatatvāt; sarvagatasya ca nityaprāptatvāditi bhāvaḥ | sarvagatatvaṃ śrutidvayena ghaṭayati—taditi | “tadantarasya sarvasya tadu sarvasyāsya bāhataḥ” iti śrutiḥ “nityaṃ vibhum” iti ca śrutiḥ brahmaṇaḥ sarvagatatvamāhetyarthaḥ | ‘vibhum’ ityasya vivaraṇaṃ sarvagatam iti | vividhabhāvādvā vibhurityapaunuruktyam | kiṃca grāmādvinnasya caitrasya grāmaprāptigatipūrvakā yuktā | jīvastu brahmaṇo nānyo brahmaiva san | tasmādadhyasya gatihetukā na brahmaprāptirityāha—ananyatvācceti | ananyatve hetumāha—taditi | “ātmāni vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati” iti śrutiḥ jīvabrahmaṇorananyatve ghaṭate, nānyatve | na hyanyasminvijñāte'nyadvijñātaṃ bhavati | ato'nanyatvam | tathā “tattvamasi” iti tvamiti jīvamuddiśya tadarthādbrahmaṇo'nanyatvamuktam | tathā “anena jīvenātmanā”
[p. 245]
iti ātmaśabdena pratyagātmadṛttinā svātmavācinā jīvo nirdiṣṭaḥ | na cānyatsvātmā bhavati | tathā “yo'nyāṃ devatāmupāsta anyo'sāvanyo'hamiti na sa veda” iti jīvaparayorbhedadarśanaṃ nindayā niṣiddham | ato'nayorananyatvamityarthaḥ | yata eva jīvabrahmaṇorananyatvam, ata eva madhuni nānārasavat nadīsamudravacca nānayoravibhāgopapattimokṣaḥ ; vibhāge hi sā bhavati, nānanyatve ityabhiprāyeṇāha—ata iti | kiṃca P. 120-7. sāvayavānāmanyonyāvayavamiśratālakṣaṇo'vibhāgo bhavati | ātmā ca “niṣkalaḥ” iti śruteranavayavaḥ | ato nāsyāvibhāgopapattirityāha—niravayava iti | ekādaśendriyāṇi pañcamahābhūtāni ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ, tadrahitatvenātmano niṣkalaśabdenānavayavatvamuktam | tathā—“yathāgrejvalataḥ śuddhā visphuliṅgā vyuccaranti, evamevāsmādātmanaḥ” ityādiśruteḥ jīvasya brahmakāryatvam, kāryasya kāraṇabhāvāpattirbrahmaprāptirityayamapi pakṣo naityāha—nāpīti | sarvadā kāryamupādānakāraṇātmakam | ato'sya tadbhāvāpattirnityasiddhā, na vidyāsādhyā ; vidyāsādhyaśca mokṣaḥ śruta iti bhāvaḥ | atha kāryasya nityaṃ kāraṇātmatve'pi jaivasya kāryarūpasya vināśaḥ kāraṇe brahmaṇi layo mokṣaḥ ; tato jīvasyoccheda eva mokṣaḥ syāt | tatra “vidyayāmṛtamaśnute” iti śrutivirodhaḥ | tadā hi vidyayā ucchedaḥ prasajyeta, nāmṛtatvamaparatvam | kiṃca nātmana ucchedaṃ kaścidicchati | na ca niṣpramāṇaṃ phalaṃ bhavati | tatra niṣphalaiva vidyā syāt | apica jīvabrahmaṇostāvadākāśaghaṭākāśayoriva bhedasyaupādhikatvāt kāryakāraṇabhāvo nāstyeva | yatrāpyasau prasiddhaḥ, tatrāpi paramārthato nāstītyuktam | kiṃ svātantryeṇa ? na, vācārambhaṇaśrutyā vikārānṛtatvapratipādanāt ; tathāhi—vikāro ghaṭādiḥ ‘ghaṭaḥ’ iti nāmadheyaṃ ca vācā kevalamāramyate, na tu tathvato'sti | kāraṇamātrameva tu satyam, mṛttiketyeva tu satyamiti | vāgiti vyavahāro lakṣyate ; tena vyavahārasādhanamātramidaṃ ghaṭādi, na tathvato'stītyarthaḥ |
[p. 246]
P.120-13 na ca yogavyāghravadeva jīvasya brahmarūpeṇa pariṇāmo mokṣaḥ; anyasya brahmarūpeṇa pariṇāmo bhavati, yogina iva vyāghrarūpeṇa | na ca jīvabrahmaṇoranyatvamastītyuktam | apica pūrvāvasthāto viśeṣāpattiḥ pariṇāme; sā nāsti, caitanyabrahmarūpasya jīvānāmaviśeṣāt | pūrvaṃ śokamohādi caitanyamāsīt, caitanyamātrasvabhāvabrahmapariṇāme tu jīvānāṃ śokādyabhāvo bhūta ityasti viśeṣa iti cet, na; asminnapi pakṣe yadi śokādayo jīvānāṃ svabhāvabhūtāḥ, tato jīvānāṃ nityatvātte'pi jīvavadanucchedyāḥ | atha jīvebhyo'rthāntarabhūtāḥ kṣaṇikā guṇāḥ, tataḥ kṣaṇikatvādeva vina ṅkṛtyantīti na tadarthaṃ vidyākhyaṃ sādhanamapekṣitamiti vidyāvaiphalyam | na cāgāmināṃ śokādīnāmanutpattaye sādhanamapekṣitamiti vācyam | sa hi teṣāṃ prāgabhāvaḥ; sa cāsādhya ityuktam | ato na tadarthamapi sādhanāpekṣā | atha brahmaṇyaiśvaryaviśeṣo'sti; tatprāptireva brahmarūpapariṇāmo mokṣa ucyate; tathāhi—“sa svarāṅ bhavati” iti śrutirmuktasya svayaṃprabhutvamāha | tadasat, anekeśvaratvānupapatteḥ | tathāhi—brahmaṇo nyūnaṃ samaṃ vā muktānāmaiśvaryaṃ syāt; tatra nyūnatve na brahmarūpāpattiḥ, na ca svayaṃprabhutvam; mukteśvarāṇāṃ parameśvaraparatvadarśanāt | tathā sati cānyarājanyatvaṃ muktānāṃ syāt | anyarājanyatve “atha ye'nyathāto viduḥ, anyarājānaste kṣayyalokā bhavanti” iti śrutirmuktānāmaiśvaryasyāntavaccaṃ, tataśca svargiṇāmiva punarāvṛttiḥ syāt | ato brahmātmabhāvāt anyathā ye viurityanvayaḥ | anyo rājā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | na cāntavaccamanupapannam, loke parameśvarecchayā nyūneśvarāṇāmaiśvaryapracyutidarśanāt | brahmaiśvaryasamasya caiśvaryasya muktānāṃ yugapadanupapattireva; tathāhi—ekaśredīśvaraḥ sarvasya jagato'dhipatiḥ praśāsitā ca syāt, tato'parasya kartavyameva nāsti; sa kiṃ kurvannīśvaraḥ syāt? virodhācca samasyaiśvaryasya yugapadanupapattiḥ; na khalu yadekasyeśvarasyābhipretaṃ tadaparasyāpīti niyame heturasti | tatraikasminvastuni viruddhābhiprāyayordvayorīśvarayormadhye yadyekābhiprāyānuvidhāyitvaṃ tasya vastunaḥ syāt, tato yasyeśvarasyābhiprāyaṃ tadvastu nānuvidhaste na tadabhiprāyānurūpaṃ bhavati tasyānīśvaratvaṃ syāt | ubhayeśvarābhiprāyānuvartitvaṃ ca vastuno-
[p. 247]
'saṃbhāvyeva, virodhāt | atha ubhayorabhiprāyaṃ nānuvartate, yādṛśaṃ vastu P. 121-9 ubhayorabhiprāyaṃ tato'nyathaiva syāt | tathobhayorapyanīśvaratvaṃ syāt | athocyeta—syādevaṃ yadyaikamatye na heturasti; na tvevam | anaikamatye hīśvarāṇāṃ parasparavirodhāt kāryaṃ na sidhyet | tataśca kāryasiddhireva teṣāmaikamatye hetuḥ | bhavati ca kāryasiddhaye bahūnāmaikamatyam, yathā vyavahārādiṣu pariṣada iti cet; tanna; evaṃ hi pratyekamanīśvaratvaṃ syāt | etacca karmahetuke'pyaikamatye tulyam | atha pūrvoktavirodhābhāvāya paryāyeṇa teṣāmīśvaratvamucyate, tato yasyaiśvaryamuparataṃ tadīyasyaiśvaryalakṣaṇasya mokṣasyāntavaccaṃ syāt | anantaśrāptau, apunarādṛtiśruteḥ | itaśca na brahmatulyamaiśvaryaṃ muktānām, yato brahma jagatsṛjati na muktāḥ | te tu sṛṣṭyuttarakālamīśvaropāsanenāpadṛjyante | ataḥ sṛṣṭisamaye na santyeva; kutasteṣāṃ sraṣṭṛtvam? atasteṣāṃ sraṣṭṛtvābhāvāt na brahmatulyamaiśvaryam | tasmādyathā śuddhatvarūpasya sphaṭikasya japādyupādhikalpitarāgatirohitatvarūpasya rāgāpanaye vidyamānameva tvarūpamāvirbhavati, na tvapūrvaṃ janyate, tathā “taccamasi” “anena jīvenātmanā” ityādiśrutisiddhabrahmatvarūpasya jīvasyāvidyātirohitatadrūpasya vidyayā avidyāpanaye nijabrahmarūpāvirbhāvamātraṃ brahmaprāptiḥ, na rūpāntarāpattiḥ | tathā ca “svena rūpeṇa” iti śrutau svaśabda upapadyate, nānyarūpaniṣpattau | svaśabdo'yamātmātmīyavacanaḥ | tatrānyadapi rūpaṃ yadyabhinnaṃ niṣpadyate, tadā tvam, atha bhinnaṃ tadā tvīyam, ityabhedāt saṃbandhādvānyarūpaniṣpattāvapi svaśabdopapattiriti cet; na, ‘svena’ iti viśeṣaṇātarthakyaprasaṅgāt | saṃbhave vyabhicāre hi viśeṣaṇamarthavat | anyarūpasya niṣpadyamānasya svatvaṃ svīyatvaṃ vā saṃbhavatyeva, na vyabhicarati | ataḥ ‘svena’ iti viśeṣaṇānarthakyam | svatvāditi; svaśabda ātmātmīyavacanaḥ | nijasyaiva brahmatvarūpasyābhivyaktimātraṃ yadi muktiḥ, kathaṃ tarhi niṣpadyata iti? niṣpattirhi janma; taccābhūtaprādurbhāvaḥ, na tu vidyamānarūpābhivyaktimātram iti cenna; abhivyaktāvapyevaṃviśeṣaśabdaprayogadarśanāt; yathā—malāpakarṣaṇābhivyaktaśaucchye ‘ghaṭaḥ śuklo jātaḥ, niṣpannaḥ, bhūtaḥ’ iti cocyate | nenāyamapyadoṣaḥ | tathā “brahmaiva san brahmāpyeti” itīyamapi śrutiḥ
[p. 248]
jīvasya vidyamānabrahmarūpāvirbhāvameva mokṣamāha—anyathā “brahmaiva san” iti na ghaṭate | evaṃ mokṣasvarūpaṃ nirūpya ‘sādhyo mokṣo na cāparaḥ’ iti ślokārtha- P.121-19 mupapādayan vyācaṣṭe—tadevamiti | na ceti nakāraḥ kākākṣivat ‘na sādhyaḥ’ ‘na cāparaḥ’ ityubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | ‘na sādhyaḥ’ ityatra pūrvo vā vartate | yadvā ‘na ca’ ityasya sādhya ityanena saṃbandhaḥ | apara iti cānyo na bhavatītyarthaḥ | kvacittu ‘na nāparaḥ’ iti pāṭhaḥ; tatra na sādhyo nāpara ityarthaḥ | na cānyatvamiti; vidyāto mokṣasya nānyatvamityarthaḥ | etacca ‘nāparaḥ’ ityasya vyākhyānam | ananyatve hetumāha—yat iti | yato brahmasvarūpabhūtanityavidyāvirbhāva eva mokṣaḥ, ato na vidyāto'nyaḥ | anyacca sādhyaṃ bhavati | ato na vidyāsādhya iti prakṛtopayogaḥ | yadyapi ‘avidyāstamayo mokṣaḥ’ ityuktaṃ śloke, tathāpi vidyaivāvidyāstamaya iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt vṛttau vidyādhigamo muktiruktetyadoṣaḥ ! ‘avidyāvigamaḥ’ iti pāṭhe tvavirodha eva | ukteneti; sphaṭikanyāyena, ‘advayātmaprakāśo'sau’ iti vānenoktenetyarthaḥ | advayātmaprakāśarūpāyā vidyāyā mokṣatvamupapādayati—avidyeti | avidyānivṛttāvapi mokṣe ‘saṃsāra-nivṛttirmokṣaḥ’ iti prasiddherna doṣa iti | nanu tathāpyavidyānivṛttirmokṣaḥ, na vidyetyāśaṅkyāha—vidyaiveti | agraharūpā vā avidyā, viparyayajñānarūpā vā; tatra yadi tāvadagrahaṇamavidyā, tato vidyaiva tannivṛttiḥ, yato'grahaṇaprāgabhāvo grahaṇam | tasya grahaṇabhāva eva nivṛttiḥ, nānyā; ghaṭaprāgabhāvasyaiva ghaṭabhāvaḥ | atha viparyayajñānamavidyā, tathāpi tadvirodhitattvajñānodaya eva tannivṛttirityarthaḥ | etat dṛṣṭāntenopapādayati—na hoti | tatra śuktijñānāsādhyatve hetumāha—tayoriti | yadi śuktijñānena rajatajñānanivṛttiḥ sādhyā, tat śuktijñānarajatajñānanivṛttyorayaugapadyaṃ syāt; na ca tadastītyarthaḥ | prayatnāntarāsādhyatve hetumāha—prayatnāntara iti | na hi rajatajñānanivṛttaye śuktijñānodayāt pṛthak prayatata ityarthaḥ |
[p. 249]
vidyaivābhivyaktā brahmaprāptiḥ, na tatsādhyetīmarmarthaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā P. 122-3. ceti | pūrvabhāvi hi kāraṇaṃ bhavati; tatra yadi vidyāsādhyā brahmaprāptibhavati, tato ‘viditvā brahmarva bhavati’ iti vidyābrahmabhāvayāḥ, tathā ‘viditvā na vibheti’ iti vidyābhayanivṛttyoḥ, tathaikatvadarśanamohāmāvayoḥ paurvāparyaṃ śrūyeteti naikakālatā | iha ca ‘veda bhavati’ ‘vidvān vibheti’ ‘ko moho'nupaśyataḥ’ ityekakālatā śrūyate; tathāhi—‘veda’ iti viderleṅantasya “vido layo vā” iti tipo ṇaladeśātprayogaḥ; ‘vidvān’ iti ca “videḥ śaturvasuḥ” iti śaturvasvādeśāt, anupaśyata iti vyaktā vartamānatā | tadevaṃ vartamānaikakālatvānna vidyāprāptyoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ | “tadātmānam” ityatra tu ‘avet, abhayat’ ityanayobhūtārthatvādanekakālatā draṣṭavyā; tathāhi—‘avet’ iti viderleṅantāt “hallaṅghābbhyaḥ” iti tipo lopāt bhūtārthaprayogaḥ | nanu “tameva viditvātimṛtyubheti” iti paurvāparyaṃ śrūyata ityāśaṅkyāha—yatviti | nanu vidyābrahmaprāptyorekatve tulyakālatāpi na yuktā; bhinnayorhi sā bhavatīti codayati—nanviti | uttaramāha—neti | yathā kapālajanmaiva vaṭanāśaḥ, tathāpi tayorbhedena vyapadeśaḥ; tathaihāpi varatvabhede'pi vyapadeśamātrabheda ityarthaḥ | yadvā atrāpi bhāvābhāvarūpeṇaiva vyapadeśabhedaḥ | sa hi ‘vidvān vibheti’, ‘ko moha ekatvamanupaśyataḥ’ iti vyakta eva | “brahmaiva bhavati” ityatra tvavadhāraṇenābrahmatvābhāvo'bhipreyata iti dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayoḥ sāmyam | nanu yadi vijñānātmabo'dvayabrahmarūpasya tatsvarūpāvirbhāvarūpavidyodaya eva mokṣaḥ; tato yatraiva vidyodayastatraiva sa mucyeta; tato gatyabhāvāt “te'cirabhisaṃbhavantyaciṣo'haḥ” ityādyā mārgaśrutayaḥ katham? kathaṃ ca “loke yathā madhukṛtaḥ” iti “yathā saugya nadyaḥ” ityādyāśra jīvabrahmaṇorasati vibhāge vibhāgasyāpyayaṃ layaṃ śrāvayantyaḥ śrutayaḥ? kathaṃ cādvayātmasvarūpāvirbhāve “sa ekadhā bhavati, dvidhā” ityādyāḥ śrutayaḥ?— iti pṛcchati—kathamiti | atrottaramāha—tatreti | ācāryā ityadhyāhāryameva | tacca pūjārthamuktam | na svamataparyudāsāya | vidyā saguṇā
[p. 250]
nirguṇā ca | tatra mārgādiśrutayo na nirguṇavidyāviṣayāḥ ; tatra hi gatiniṣedhaḥ eva “brahmaiva san” iti śrūyate | brahmasvarūpasyaiva brahmabhāvaṃ vadantī śrutireṣā svarūpasya tatraiva deśe sulabhatvādanyatra gatiṃ niṣedhati | tathā “na tasmātprāṇāḥ” ityādyā ca śrutiḥ tasmādviduṣo dehāstra prāṇā utkrāmanti, apitu atraiva samavanīyante pralīyante iti vadantī viduṣo vijñānātmano gatiṃ niṣedhati | gatyarthaśca mārgopadeśaḥ | sa gatyabhāve nirguṇavidyāyāmanarthaka ityāśayaḥ | nanu prāṇānāmayaṃ gatiniṣedho na vijñānātmana P.122-14 ityāśaṅkyāha—prāṇeti | atra hetumāha—na hīti | yathā vimbasyaivādarśādyupādhervimbāṅgedena prativimbatvam, evaṃ brahmaṇa eva prāṇopādheḥ brahmaṇo bhedena vijñānātmatvam; tatra prāṇānāmagamane satyupādherabhāvādvijñānātmatvameva nāsti; kasya gamanaṃ bhavatviti arthātprāṇagamane vijñānātmana evāgatiruktā bhavatītyarthaḥ | atrāha—“na tasmātprāṇā utkrāmanti” iti jīvāpādānā prāṇānāmutkrāntiranena niṣidhyate, na dehāpādānā | tenāyamasyārthaḥ— jīvamujjhitvā prāṇā notkrāmanti, apitu jīvena sahaiva dehādutkrāmantīti | evaṃ ca “na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti” iti śākhāntaraśrutirapi ‘tasya prāṇāḥ kuto notkrāmanti? kiṃ dehāt? uta dehinaḥ?’ ityapekṣāyāṃ “na tasmāt” iti śrutibalāt dehina iti viśeṣe sthāpyate | kiṃca prāptipūrvakatvānniṣedhasya “tamutkrāmantaṃ prāṇā anūtkrāmanti” iti dehāntaragrahaṇāyāviduṣo yotkrāntiḥ śrutā, sā “na tasmātprāṇāḥ” ityanena viduṣi niṣidhyate, na parabrahmaprāptyarthā | taduktam—“dehāntarārthā yotkrāntiḥ sā tatra pratiṣidhyate” iti | tena viduṣo'pyutkrāntirastīti paravidyāviṣayā api mārgaśrutayaḥ | tathā ca tatprakaraṇe'pi sāḥ paṭhyanta iti | tadidamasaṃbaddham; tathāhi—yadyaviduṣo yotkrāntiḥ sā viduṣo niṣidhyate, tarhi sā dehācchrutā kathaṃ jīvānniṣidhyate? atha dehādeva sotkrāntiniṣiddhā; kiṃ tu dehāntarārthā, na brahmaprāptyarthetyucyate; tato'nena dehāntarārthotkrāntirviduṣo niṣiddhā; brahmaprāptyarthā tu kena vihitā? na tāvadanenaiva, vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | na cārthākṣepasiddhā, vinaiva tayā dehāntarotkrāntiniṣedhopapatteḥ, brahmaprāptyupapatteśca | tathāhi—yadanyat dūrasthaṃ ca, tadgatyā prāpyate | brahma tu jīvasya nijaṃ rūpaṃ sarvagataṃ ca; ato nānyat,
[p. 251]
na ca dūrastham | ataḥ kiṃ tatra gatyā, yena sārthādākṣipyeta ? kiṃca “atraiva samavanīyante” iti ca tatraiva deśe prāṇānāṃ pralayamabhivadantī śrutiḥ viduṣo'nuktānti sūcayati | atreti brahmocyate, na deśaviśeṣa iti cet ; mahābhijño bhavān śabdavṛttasya | na hi ‘rājā na gataḥ, atraiva tiṣṭhati’ ityukte yato na gataḥ, tato deśādanyat ‘atra’ ityucyate | tasmādviduṣo brahmaprāptyarthā notkrāntiriti vyarthā mārgaśrutayaḥ paraprakaraṇe'pi paṭhitāḥ | yatrārthavatyaḥ, tatra pūṣānumantraṇamantravat duṣyanta ityalaṃ prapañcena | kiṃviṣayā nāma tā mārgaśrutayaḥ ? ityapekṣāyāmāha—yatra tviti | P.122-16 saguṇavidyāviṣayāstā ityarthaḥ | na ca nirguṇavidyānyāyena tatrāpi tāsāmanupayoga ityabhiprāyeṇāha—tatra ceti | parabrahma hi jīvasya nijaṃ rūpam, tacca nityaprāptameva sadavidyayā karasthamuvarṇavadaprāptaṃ vidyayā avidyānivṛttau tatraiva sulabhamiti na gatimapekṣate ; tena yuktastatra mārgaśrutīnāmanupayogaḥ | kāryabrahmalokaḥ punaḥ itastṛtīyasyāṃ divi tiṣṭhati ; tena sa dūrasthaḥ ; tatprāptaye āmavat gatimapekṣata iti tatra mārgaśrutayaṃ upayujyanta ityarthaḥ | kāryabrahmalokāstitvaṃ śrautatvena darśayati—yatreti | kāryaṃ ca brahmalokaṃ ye'pi na parabrahmavidaste'pi yānti | tatra śraddhāsatyatapasāmupāsakānāṃ kāryabrahmalokaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | nanu saguṇopāsakānāmapi brahmaprāptireva kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkyāha—pareti | atra hetumāha—vidyayeti | mṛtyumatyetītyanvayaḥ | nanu saguṇopāsakānāṃ kāryabrahmalokaprāptiśret, tatastasya mahāpralaye nāśāt teṣāṃ punarāvṛttyā bhāvyam ; atha katham “imaṃ mānavamāvartaṃ nāvartante” ityanāvṛttiśrutiriti pṛcchati—apunarāvṛttīti | atra matabhedenottaramāha—atreti | tatprapañcayati—taditi ; kāryabrahmalokasthānaṃ nirdiśati ; tat kāryabrahmaloke sthānaṃ prāpya tatrotpannasarvopādhinirmuktabrahmatattvavijñānā mahāpralaye prāpte krameṇa paraṃ padamaśnuvate iti teṣāmapi punarāvṛttyabhāvāt apunarāvṛttiśrutyupapattiḥ | atra vākyaṃ paṭhati—yatheti | vedāntavijñānena suniścito'rtho yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ, utpannavākyārthamātrajñānāḥ,
[p. 252]
anutpannādvayātmasākṣātkārā iti yāvat | ata eva sahasā na mucyante, apitu kāryabrahmalokaṃ gatvānantaraṃ mucyante | “te brahmalokeṣu” iti parameva brahmocyate ; atastatprāptirevānena kathyate, na kāryabrahmalokaprāpti- P.123-14 rityāśaṅkyāha—brahmeti | paraṃ brahmaikamanavayavaṃ ca | atastasya na svato nāpyavayavadvāreṇa bahutvamavakalpate ; kāryabrahmalokastu yadyapyekaḥ, tathāpyavayavabahutvena tatra bahuvacanamupapadyate ; tena sa evocyate, na paramityarthaḥ | atra kecit grahaikatvavadvahutvamavidheyatvādavivakṣitam ; ataḥ paraṃ brahmaivātra brahmalokamityāhuḥ ; tadayuktam ; parāntakālapratīkṣaṇāt ; na hi viduṣo mahāpralayakāle parabrahmaprāptiḥ, apitu dehapātānantaramiti teṣāmapyabhimatam | tathāca sūtram—“pāte tu” iti | lokaśabdaśra bhogabhūmau mukhyo na parabrahmaṇi | ādhāratvamapi tasyaiva kāryabrahmalokasya nivāsasthānatvāt ghaṭate | tasmātkāryabrahmalokanirdeśa evāyam | evaṃ ca sati bahuvacanamapi kathaṃcidavakalpata iti tadapi liṅgasthānīyamuktamityadoṣaḥ | sarvamidaṃ kartrādi brahmetyupāsanāditi ; yogaśabdena upāsanāmātramucyate, na tacchajñānamityuktaṃ bhavati | ata eva kāryabrahmalokaprāptiḥ phalam | atraivārthe smṛtimāha—tathāceti | brahmalokaṃ gatāste sarve kṛtātmānaḥ saṃskṛtabuddhayaḥ parasya pradhānasya brahmaṇo'nte adhikārasamāptau yadā pratisaṃcaro mahāpralayo bhavati tadā brahmaṇā saha paraṃ padaṃ praviśantītyarthaḥ | anye tu anāvṛttiśruterimarthaṃ manyante—yathā karmiṇo dhūmādimārgeṇa candralokaṃ gatāste tatra patanakālaparyantaṃ sthitvā bhuktvā bhogāṃstasmin sthita eva nivartante, naiva sthite brahmaloke tatra gatānāṃ tata āvṛttiḥ ; apitu brahmalokapralayaparyantaṃ tatraiva siṣṭanti | na ca vācyam—tathāpi tatpralaye teṣāmāvṛtteḥ kathamanādṛttiśrutiriti ; yatastasmin sati na tata ādṛttirbhavati ; tatpralaye tu sa eva nāsti ; kutastata āvṛttiḥ ? ataścaivamanāvṛttiḥ śrūyate | yatra tu ‘tatpralaye hi tata āvṛttiḥ’ iti pāṭhaḥ, tatra tataḥ pralayāt prāgāvṛttirnāsti, tataścānāvṛttiḥ śrūyata ityarthaḥ | tathāca “imaṃ mānavam” iti “iha nāvartante” iti ‘imam iha’ iti viśeṣaṇam asyāṃ saṃnihitāyāṃ vartamānasṛṣṭāvanāvṛttimāha | sarvathānāvṛttau duryojametat | “apṛtatvayeti” iti cāpṛtatvaṃ ye prāṇinaḥ pralayādaryāgeva adhinte tānapekṣya |
[p. 253]
mahāpralaye mriyamāṇānāmapi teṣāṃ devānāmivāmaratvamupapadyate | tathā ca “ābhūtasaṃjñavaṃ sthānam” iti smṛtiḥ yāvadbhūtagrāmasyāvasthānaṃ tāvadbhūtatvam, pralaye mriyanta ityāha | yattu manyate—brahmaṇaḥ prādeśiko'yaṃ bhūtabhautikavikāro na sārvatrikaḥ | P. 124-5 tenāvikṛto'pyasya pradeśo'sti | tathāca “yadataḥ paraḥ” ityādikā śrutiḥ ato divaḥ parastājjaivikārairanabhibhūtamātmajyotirdīpyamānaṃ darśayati | tatraitasya ca muktiḥ | ato'sti mārgaśrutīnāṃ parabrahmaprāptādupayoga iti na kāryabrahmaviṣayateti | tannirūpyam—na tāvadanutpannādvayajñānasyāyaṃ mārgopadeśaḥ brahmavidaḥ śravaṇāt | yo'pyutpannādvayātmavidyaḥ, tasyāpi vidyayā niḥśeṣāvidyānivṛttau tajjasya bhedaprapañcasya sarvatra vilayādihāpyavikṛtaṃ brahma sulabhamiti na tatprāptaye'nyatra gatirupayujyate | ato na tadartho mārgopadeśa iti kāryabrahmalokaviṣayataivāsya yuktā | yāpi madhu-nadīnidarśanena jīvabrahmaṇoravibhāgaśrutiḥ, sāpi yathā madhuni kuṣumarasānāṃ yathā ca samudre nadīnāṃ saṃsarge dravyatvena parasparamavibhāgaḥ, tathā jīvabrahmaṇorityevaṃparā na bhavati, tayorasaṃsargidravyatvāt ; apitu yathā tatra vivekena jñānābhāvaḥ, tathā jīvabrahmaṇorapītyevaṃparā ; kiṃcitsādṛśyenāpi dṛṣṭāntopādānāt | tathā sṛṣṭiśrutīnāṃ sṛṣṭiparatve sṛjyamānaprapañcasatyatvādradvaitahaniśaṅkānivṛttyarthaṃ tā api prasaṅgato vyācaṣṭe—sargeti | tā api na sṛṣṭiparāḥ ; ātmaiko jagatastattvam, na tato'nyat dvitīyaṃ jagadastītyetatpratipattiparāḥ | tenātmaikatvānugrahastāsāmartho vyavasthāpyaḥ, nānyathā | ātmaikatvaṃ vākyārthībhūtaṃ virudhyate | kathaṃ tāsāmātmaikatvaparatvamucyate ? “kasminnu bhagavo vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati” ityupakramaḥ | yadi ca ātmaikaḥ sarvasya taccaṃ na taccāntaramasti, tadā ātmani jñāte sarvamidaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati, nānyathā ! tenātmaikatvenopakrama iti gamyate | tathā “sadeva somya” ityatra “ekamevādvitīyam” ityekatvenopakramaḥ | tathā “aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam” ityekatvenopasaṃhāraḥ | tenātmaikatvapratipattiparamidaṃ vākyam | tatra yadi bhūtasṛṣṭiparā satyatayā pratipādyeta, tato vākyabhedaḥ syāt | ato yathopāṃśuyājayākye “jāmi vā etadyajñasya kriyate
[p. 254]
yadanvaśvau puroḍāśau” ityupakramāt “upāṃśuyājamantarā yajatyajāmitvāya” ityupasaṃhārāccaikavākyatve avagate tanmadhyavartīni “viṣṇurūpāṃśu yaṣṭavyaḥ” ityādīni vacāṃsi vākyabhedadoṣāt pṛthagviṣatvamalabhamānānyupāṃśuyājaśeṣatvāt, tadānuguṇyena ‘īdṛśo'yamupāṃśuyājo mahābhāgo yarimannijyamāne viṣṇvādayo'pīṣṭā bhavanti’ ityevamupāṃśuyājastutyarthatayā vyavatiṣṭhante, tathā sṛṣṭiśrutayo vākyārthabhūtasṛjyamānaprapañcabhedapratipattiparāḥ | yata ekatvenaivopakramopasaṃhārau sarvatra śrutau, yena ca tau śrutau sa eva vākyārthaḥ, yaśca vākyārthaḥ sa eva pradhānam, yacca pradhānaṃ tadarthamevānyadyuktam | aphalaṃ ca bhedajñānam | ātmaikatvajñānaṃ tu dṛṣṭena ca śokādinivṛttiphalena phalavat | ataḥ ‘phalavatsaṃnidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgam’ iti nyāyenāpyaikātmyaparatvaṃ sṛṣṭiśrutīnām | tatra viśuddhetyādinā phalasya dṛṣṭatvamuktam | “tatra ko mohaḥ” ityādinā śrutatvamiti vivekaḥ | sargaśrutayaḥ ekatvopāyatayā P.125-7. vyavatiṣṭhanta ityuktam, tadupāyatvaṃ darśayati—upāyatvaṃ ceti | nedamasanmūlamiti “kathamasataḥ sajjāyeta” iti śrutyartha uktaḥ | advitīyaikasanmūlamiti tu “sadevedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam” iti śrutyarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ kāryakāraṇayorbhedāt kathamātmaikatvamityāśaṅkyāha—kāraṇeti | syādevaṃ yadi tayorvyatirekaḥ syāt; sa eva tu nāsti; ‘mṛdayaṃ ghaṭaḥ’ ityabhedapratīteriti bhāvaḥ | idamiti ; jagadityarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha— ghaṭādīti | yathā mṛdūpeṇa ghaṭādīnāmekatvam, evaṃ sadūpeṇa jagata ityarthaḥ | nanu yadi sanmūlaṃ jagat tato mukhya eva kāryakāraṇabhāva uktaḥ syāt | ataśca prapañcasatyatā | tatra cādvaitahānirityabhiprāyeṇa codayati— nanviti | pariharati—neti | etatprapañcayati—na svakhviti; ekatvāvatāro pāyatvaṃ vivṛṇoti | taduktam— “mṛddhohavisphuliṅgādyaiḥ sṛṣṭiryā coditā śrutau | upāyaḥ so'vatārāya nāsti bhedaḥ kathaṃcana ||” iti | bhedābhiniveśātiśayāt sahasraivābhedaṃ boddhumaśaktaḥ sadupādānatayā sadūpeṇābhinnaṃ jagat ghaṭādivanmṛdūpeṇeti pratibodhyata ityuktaṃ bhavati | nanu
[p. 255]
mukhya eva kāryakāraṇabhāve ekatvāvatāropāyo bhaviṣyati; tathāpi prapañcasatyatā na vyāhanyata ityāśaṅkyāha—sa ceti | sa ca mukhya ekatva- P.125-13 virodhī; tenāmukhyo gṛhyata ityarthaḥ | nanu mukhye'pi kāryakāraṇabhāve, kāryātmanā nānātvaṃ kāraṇātmanā caikṛtvamiti naikatvasya kaściddirodha ityāśaṅkya tanniṣedhayati—ekamiti | kiṃca yadi kāryātmanā nānātvaṃ tataḥ, kāryasyāpi satyatvāt “tatsatyam” iti tacchabdoktasya kāraṇasyāsādhāraṇyena satyatvaṃ śrutirnāvadhārayet; avadhārayati ca | ano na kāryātmanā nānātvamityāha—tatsatyamiti | nanu kārye'pi satyaśrutirasti: yathā—“mṛttiketyeva satyam” iti “prāṇā vai satyam” iti ca | ucyate—yathā antaratame mukhya ātmani vivakṣite “anyo'ntara ātmā prāṇamayaḥ” ityātmaśabdoktaṃ bodhayituṃ prāṇamayādiṣu prayujyate; na caitāvatā teṣāṃ mukhyamātmatvam, tathā mūlakāraṇamukhyasatyatvavivakṣayā mṛttikāyāmajñasiddhasatyatve kāraṇe prāṇeṣu cāsiddhasatyatveṣu “teṣāmeṣa satyam” iti mukhyasatyavivakṣayā satyaśabdaḥ prayukta ityadoṣaḥ | kāryātmanā nānātvābhāve śrutidvayamaparamāha—anyatreti | atraiva hetvantaramāha—bhedeti | ‘ekatvamavirodhena’ ityatra varṇitamatra smartavyam | tasmādetatsamastaśrutyavirodhena yathā bimbāpratibimbāni vikalpāḥ, varṇebhyaśca padāni vikalpāḥ, tathā jagadvikalpasyātmopādānam | tathā sṛṣṭiśrutirvarṇyata ityāha—etaditi | dṛṣṭāntadvayaṃ vivṛṇoti—yathā hīti | yathā bimbāt pratibimbāni saṃbhūtānītyucyante na ca tāni paramārthataḥ santi, tathā “ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ, ākāśādvāyuḥ” ityucyata iti | kiṃca svapne'pi sṛṣṭiśrutirasti; na ca tāvatā tatra rathādīnāṃ satyatvam | tadvadeṣāpi bhūtasṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati, ātmaikatvapratipattiratvādityāha—apiceti | kiṃca rathādapi bhūtasṛṣṭiḥ pāramārthikī, yadi svayameva vācārambhaṇaśrutyā “mṛttiketyeva satyam” iti kāraṇamātrasya satyatvamavadhārayantyā pāramārthikī soktā; na caitadastītyāha—svayamiti | evaṃ sṛṣṭiśrutīnāṃ gatimuktvā aiśvaryaśrutīnāmāha—aiśvaryeti | saguṇavidyāyāṃ svargādisthānīyaṃ phalamihānekadhādibhāvaḥ śrūyate; na tu mokṣaḥ; sa
[p. 256]
hi nirguṇavidyānimitta ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ punastatrāpi kvacidapunarāvṛttismṛti- P.126-10 rityāśaṅkyāha—yadapīti | dvayā gatiriti ; kramamuktyabhiprāyaṃ vā vartamānasargānāvṛttyabhiprāyaṃ vā anāvṛttiśravaṇaṃ vyākhyātamityarthaḥ | aiśvaryaśrutiṣu matāntaramāha—anya iti | yato brahma sarvātmakaṃ, sarveśvaraṃ, sarvopabhogānāṃ ca bhoktṛ, sarvajñaṃ, devādyānandāśra brahmānandasya mātrā, ato yatkiñcidīśvaratvādi brahmādisthāvarānteṣu bhūteṣu tatsarvaṃ brahmaṇi, yacca brahmaṇi tatsarvamāvirbhūtabrahmasvarūpo vidvān brahmaṇaḥ sarvātmatvāt āpnoti, tena “sarvānkāmānāmoti” ityādi tasya svarūpāvirbhāvalakṣaṇamokṣapraśaṃsārthamucyata ityarthaḥ | nanu yadyāvirbhūtabrahmasvarūpo vidvān sarvātmatvātsarvakāmānāmoti, tataḥ sarvātmatvādeva sarvabhūtagataśokādyavāmuyāt ; tataśca mokṣe duḥkhamapi syādityabhiprāyeṇa codayati—nanviti | pariharati—neti | brahmaṇo rūpaṃ vidvānāmoti ; śokādayastu na brahmaṇo rūpamityarthaḥ | atra hetumāha—avidyeti | jñānaiśvaryādayaśca brahmaṇo rūpam ; tena brahmaprāptau prāpyata iti yuktamityāha—jñāneti | kutaḥ punarjñānādīnāṃ brahmarūpatvaṃ gamyata ityāha—vijñānamiti | “nānyo'to'sti draṣṭā” iti ca jñānarūpatve, “etasya” ityānandātmatve, “sarvasyeśvānaḥ” iti caiśvaryātmatve śrutiriti vivekaḥ | tadevaṃ śokādīnāmavidyādhyastatvāt jñānādīnāṃ ca brahmarūpatvāt yuktaṃ yadāvirbhūtabrahmasvarūpo vidvān jñānādīnaśnute, na śokādīnityāha—tathāceti | atra codayati—nanviti | tatpariharati— naitaditi | dṛṣṭāntau trivṛṇoni—siddheneti | nanu prakāśyānadhīne'pi prakāśarūpe brahmaṇo'dvayatvādasati sarvasmin kathaṃ sarvajñatvamityāśaṅkyāha— tathāceti | tathāca sati prakāśyānapekṣe brahmaṇaḥ prakāśarūpatve sati, taccataḥ sarvābhāve'pi avidyāvasthāyāṃ sarvasya prapañcasya “tasya bhāsā” ityādiśrutisiddhātmacaitanyenāvagāsatvāt ātmanaḥ sarvajñatvamityarthaḥ | evamadvaye brahmaṇi sarvābhāve sarveśvaratvānupapattimāśaṅkya tadupapādayati— sarveti | atrāpyavidyāvasthāyāmeva brahmendrādīnāmīśvarāṇāṃ yathāyathaṃ sveṣu svepvīśitavyeṣu yadaiśvaryaṃ tatsarvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sarvātmatvāt tadaiśvaryeṇaiveti tasya sarveśvaratvamityarthaḥ | “eṣa bhūtāvipatiḥ” ityādāvapi eṣeva yojanetyāha—
[p. 257]
evamiti | tatrāpyavidyāvasthāyāmeva bhūteṣu lokeṣu ca brahmandrādīnāṃ yadai- P.127-14 śvaryaṃ tat karmopāsanāviśeṣeṇa parameśvarasyaiva sarvātmanā teṣvavacchinnamabhivyaktam | tenendrādīnāṃ yadaiśvaryaṃ tat “eṣa bhūnādhipatiḥ” ityādinā parameśvarasyoktamityarthaḥ | evamavirbhūtādvayabrahmasvarūpaṃ prati sarvasya svargādeḥ kāmasyābhāvāt sarvakāmāvāntiśruteranupapannatvamāśaṅkya tāmapyupapādayati— sarveti | atrāpyavidyābhūmāveva karmaṇo yānyānkāmānāpnuvanti teṣu teṣu ya ānandāḥ, te “etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrāmupajīvanti” iti śruteḥ brahmānanda eva | ataśca brahma prāpto vidvān tatsarvānandānāmotīti “sarvānkāmānavāmoti” ityucyate | na tu svarūpeṇaiva sarvakāmānavāmotītyarthaḥ | etāmeva yojanāṃ śrutyantare'pyatidiśati— evamiti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | dṛṣṭāntāsiddhimāśaṅkya tatsādhayati—na hīti | dvitīyena hi krīḍā mithunībhāvaśca bhavati | na cādvayaṃ brahma prāptasya dvitīyamasti | tenātra yathā mithunībhāvānandaprāptyā “ātmamithunaḥ” ityuktam, tathā “ramamāṇaḥ strībhiḥ” ityādyapi yathoktanyāyena tadānandaprāptyoktamityarthaḥ | brahmaṇa eva yāni jñānādīnyanavacchinnāni tānyeva kalpitāvacchedāni bhūteṣu, nānyānītīmamarthaṃ gītayā draḍhayati—yataśceti | yata eva brahmaṇa eva jñānānandādīni yānyanavacchinnāni tānyevāvacchinnāni sarvabhūteṣu | ata eva yatra yatra bhūteṣu jñānādīnāṃ nātyantamavacchedaḥ alpībhāvaḥ, tāni vyāsādibhūtāni jñānādīnāṃ samutkarṣāt bhagavataḥ pratyāsannāni, bhagavān “munīnāmapyahaṃ vyāsaḥ” ityātmatvenoktvā “munīnām” ityādi yaduktaṃ tat “yadyadvibhūtimatsatvam” ityupasaṃjaharityarthaḥ | paramātmajñānamevānavacchinnaṃ sat darśanādivyavahāraheturiti gamyata iti jñānaṃ prati viśeṣeṇāha—tatheti | tathā sarvabhūtadarśanaśravaṇādīnāṃ paramātmajñānanibandhanatvam “adṛṣṭaṃ draṣṭu” ityādiṣu śrutamityarthaḥ | evaṃ sarvajñatvādikamupapādya tatra matāntaramāha—anya iti | nāmarūpe śabdārthāvāśrayau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | dhyānamevādhyānam | tatra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | tadupapādayati—na hīti | svamahimapratiṣṭhasyeti | ādhārā- 17
[p. 258]
P.128-10 napekṣatvamāha | deśaviśeṣaḥ ādityamaṇḍalaṃ svarūpaṃ ca hiraṇyayādi | nanu parabrahmaṇa eva deśaviśeṣo rūpaṃ coktamiti kuto'yaṃ gamyata ityāha— para iti | śrutimāha—udita iti | pāpmaśabdena puṇyamapyucyate ; tena sarvebhyaḥ puṇyapāpebhya udita utkramyordhvaṃ vā gataḥ ; tairasaṃsṛṣṭa iti yāvat | puṇyapāpāsaṃsparśaśca “anyatra dharmāt” iti parasya śrutaḥ | tasmātparasyaivaiṣa deśaviśeṣo rūpaṃ ca śrutam | na ca te tasya vibhorarūpasya ca saṃbhavataḥ | tena dhyānaṃ tena rūpeṇa vidhīyate | tathā sarvajñatvādyapi draṣṭavyamiti | tadevaṃ svarūpāvirbhāvalakṣaṇe mokṣa mārgādiśrutīstadviśvārthāḥ tathā anyāśca prasaṅgāgatāḥ samādhāya tameva mokṣaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati— tadevamiti | brahmātmana iti ; brahmaiva ātmā yasya viduṣaḥ sa tathoktaḥ ; tadūpāvirbhāva ityarthaḥ | sā ca tatprāptirātmano jñānasvarūpatvādvidyaivetyāha—sā ceti | evaṃ sthite ‘na sādhyo mokṣo na ca vidyāyā anyaḥ’ iti yaduktaṃ tadupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | matāntaramāśaṅkayati—atheti | ukte'rthe pramāṇamāha—śrūyata iti | nanu pāpmamātrasya vidyayā dāhaḥ śrutaḥ, na puṇyasya ; tadapi ca vandhahetuḥ | ato nāśeṣavandhakṣayaheturvidyetyāśaṅkyāha—pāpmeti | atra hetumāha—sāvadyeti | sāvadyaṃ sadoṣaṃ phalaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ | doṣaśca saṃsārānuvandhitvaṃ patanādiheyavatvaṃ ca | pāpmaśabdena puṇyamapyucyata ityatrārthe hetvantaramāha—anyatreti | sukṛtaduṣkṛtopakramaḥ pāpmaśabdastatra dvayorapi tathorvartate yata ityarthaḥ | yadi ca karmakṣayamantareṇa mokṣaḥ syāt tato nāsau sādhyeta ; na caitadastītyāha—na ceti | phalaikavināśyatvāt karmaṇo nādattaphalasya nivṛttiḥ ; ataḥ kathaṃ vidyayā tatkṣaya ityāśaṅkyāha—prāyaścittānāmiti | govadhādidoṣasaṃyogena prāyaścittānyāgnāyante ; na ca tannimittamātramityuktam | ato doṣakṣayahetutvameṣāṃ gamyate | tena prāyaścittenādattaphalameva pāpaṃ karma kṣīyate | tathā vidyayā vandhahetukarmakṣaya ityarthaḥ | nanu vandhahetukarmakṣayaścet vidyāsādhyo na tārhe vidyāto muktirityāśaṅkyāha—saiveti | evaṃ ca svarūpāvirbhāvo mokṣa ityapi pratyuktam |
[p. 259]
nanu karmakṣayasya vidyākāryatve kāryatvāt ghaṭavat antavaccaṃ syāt ; ataśca punaḥ karmaprādurbhāvāt muktasya punarbandhaḥ syādityāśaṅkyāha—evamiti | na hi ghaṭadhvaṃso vināśī, ghaṭasya punaḥ prādurbhāvādarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | P. 129-5 - evaṃ paramatamupanyasya dūṣayati—tatreti | prāyaścitte yā nyāyadik tayā | bandhahetoḥ karmaṇa ucchedaḥ kṣayo na bhavati ; yataḥ sarvāvidyāpravilaye chedyaṃ karma nāvaśiṣyata iti | tatra hetumāha—bandheti | bandhahetuḥ karma avidyopādānatvāt avidyātvabhāvam | tatra vidyāyāṃ satyāmavidyā kātsnyena praṇaṣṭā | tenāvidyānivṛttyaiva tadātmabandhaheturapi nivṛtta iti na pṛthak chedyamavaśiṣyata ityarthaḥ | vidyotpattirevāvidyātmakabandhahetunivṛttiḥ | ato na sā tatsādhyeti bhāvaḥ | vṛttiḥ spaṣṭārthā | avidyocchedena karmocchede liṅgaṃ darśayati—yataśceti | avidyocchedena ucchidyamānābhyāṃ saṃśayaviparyayābhyāṃ saha karmaṇām “bhidyate hṛdayagranthiḥ” ityatra saṃkhyānamavidyocchedena teṣāmucchedaṃ sūcayatītyarthaḥ | hṛdayagranthirviparyayo'bhipretaḥ | atra codayati—nanviti | atra doṣamāha—tasyeti | utpannajñānasya tāvadeva ciraṃ kālavilambo yāvad dehena vimokṣyate | chāndasastakāralopaḥ | atha dehavimokṣānantaraṃ brahma saṃpatsyata ityarthaḥ | kasya nāmeyaṃ śrutirna bādhyate ityatrāha—yasyeti | yasya karmakṣayaheturvidyā śāstrāt gamyate, tasya śāstrānusāreṇa keṣāṃcitkarmaṇāṃ vidyayā kṣayaḥ, keṣāṃcidupabhogeneti vyavasthopapadyata ityarthaḥ | śāstrānusāramāha—tatreti ; tasmin pakṣa ityarthaḥ | tata iti ; vidyāta ityarthaḥ | aviśeṣeṇotsargato vidyayā sarvakarmakṣayaprāptau sa mukterdehapātāvaṣitvaśrutyāpodyata ityatra hetumāha—na hīti | sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ vidyayā kṣaye vidyodayānantarameva muktiḥ syāt ; ato dehapātapratīkṣā nopapadyeta ; śrūyate cāsau | ataḥ keṣāṃcit karmaṇāṃ bhogena kṣaya iti gamyata ityarthaḥ | ācāryajuṣṭaśrāyaṃ mārga ityāha—taduktamiti | anārabdhakārya apradattaphale vartamānadehānārambhake puṇyapāpe pūrvopāse vidyayā kṣayapitvā, 17A
[p. 260]
itare tu ārabdhakārye tadavadhermukterdehapātāvadhiśruterbhegena kṣapayitvā brahma saṃpadyata iti sūtrārthaḥ | yasya tu karmaphalavyavahāraṃ dṛṣṭenaiva vidyocchinatti, tasya vidyayā sarvāvidyocchintāvaviśeṣeṇa sarvakarmocchedādanantarameva mucyeta; ataśca P.130-6. dehapātāvadhiśrutirbādhyetetyāha—vidyayeti | tatpariharati—naiṣa iti | eṣa dehapātāvadhiśrutivādhadoṣo nāstītyarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha—na hīti | yadīyaṃ śrutirdehapātāvadhi cirakālatāviśiṣṭatvaṃ ca mukterbrūyāt, tataḥ syādeṣa doṣaḥ; na tvedastītyarthaḥ | kiṃ nāmāhetyapekṣāyāmāha—kiṃ tviti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | yathā kaścit 'kṣiprataraṃ gaccha' iti niyuktaḥ kṣiprataratvaṃ pratipādayannāha—'tāvadeva me ciraṃ yat snāto bhuñjānasya upānahau vā kramamāṇasyāśvaṃ vārohataḥ' iti, tathaivamapītyarthaḥ | yadi tu ciratvamātraṃ śrutau vivakṣitaṃ syāt tataściramityeva brūyāt, na tu tāvadevetyavadhiviśeṣam | dehapātāvadhiṃ tu darśayantyāḥ śruterayamabhiprāyaḥ—yadavidyājena dehena vidyayā avidyānivṛttāvanantarameva patitavyam, ataḥ kṣiprameva muktiriti | evaṃ kṣepyūparatā śruterlakṣyata ityāha—anyatheti | upasaṃharati—ata iti | na pratīkṣaṇīyamiti; bhogenārabdhakarmakṣapaṇaṃ na pratīkṣaṇīyamityarthaḥ | yadā cānantarameva muktiḥ, tadā dehapātamantareṇa tadasaṃbhavānnāntarīyakatvādehapāto'pyanantaraṃ bhavatyevetyāha—deheti | asyāstāvacchruterarthāntaramāha—athaveti | ubhayavidhau doṣamāha— anyatheti | tadevaṃ sati yaḥ śruterarthaḥ saṃpadyate tamāha—tatreti | anyataḥ prāpta kasyacinmukteściratvamanūdya dehavimokṣaṇamavadhirvidhīyata ityarthaḥ | tatra pūrvasmin śrutyarthaṃ codayati—nanviti | vidyodayānantaraṃ muktau sthitaḥ prājñaḥ patitadehatvānnāstyeva; kasya lakṣaṇamucyate? ityarthaḥ | dvitīye'pi śrutyarthaṃ codayati—dvitīye'pīti | vidyātaḥ sarvakarmakṣaye karmajasya śarīrasyānantarameva pātāla kasyacinmukteściratvamasti; ato na tadanuvādenāvadhiviśeṣavidhānamupapadyata ityarthaḥ | tatrāsyaṃ codyaṃ tāvatpariharati—ucyata iti | vigalitāyāṃ nikhilāyāmavidyāyāṃ siddhaḥ, vigalita^1^
[p. 261]
nikhilā avidyā yasyāṃ vidyāyāṃ tayā vā siddho vigalitanikhilāvidyāsiddhaḥ | yo yogābhyāsaparipākādutpannādvayātmasākṣātkāraprakāśo'nantarameva mucyate, na sa sthitaprajñaḥ; kiñcadvayātmasākṣātkāraprakārasya sādhaka evāvasthāviśeṣaṃ prāptaḥ siddheḥ kiñcidarvācīnāyāmavasthāyāṃ vartamāno yaḥ siddhabhrāyo nādyāpi sidhyati sa sthitaprajñaḥ | tasya cāpratitadehasya lakṣaṇābhidhānaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | dvitīyaṃ codyaṃ pariharati—na ceti | ārabdha- P.130-21 kāryakarmānapekṣaṇe yastidhyati, tadāha—tatreti | bhogena karmakṣayāpekṣāyāṃ sarvasya cireṇaivāpavargaḥ | tadanapekṣāyāṃ ca kasyacittatkālaḥ, kasyaciccireṇa; yasya cireṇa tasya ciratvamanūdyāvadhiviśeṣavidhānamupapadyata iti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadāpyārabdhakāryakarmakṣayaṃ bhogena na pratīkṣate, tadāpi vidyayā avidyājñakṛtsnakarmanivṛttau tajjanyadehasyānantarameva viyogaḥ; ataḥ kathaṃ kasyacit cirakālo'pavarga ityāśaṅkyāha—yatheti | tatsaṃskārāditi; tacchabdena bhayaṃ kampādi cocyate | bhujyamāne vipakve prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ phale 'ahaṃ śarīrī bhoktā, idaṃ bhogyam' iti yaḥ saṃskāro jātaḥ, tasyānuvṛttiḥ, tannibandhanetyarthaḥ | saṃskārātkāryānuvṛttau dṛṣṭāntamāha— kulaleti | atra codayati—nanviti | tatra ca doṣamāha—tathāceti | athaitaddoṣabhayāt bhoktādivibhāgadarśanasyāvidyāhetukasya vidyayā avidyocchedādananuvṛttirityucyate, tadā kṣaṇamapi na bhoktādivibhāgadarśanaṃ syāditi sa eva dehapātāvadhiśrutivirodhaḥ | saiṣā ubhayato doṣādubhayataḥspāśā rajjurityupamayocyata ityāha—atheti | tatrānanuvṛttipakṣamanabhyupagamyaivānuvṛttipakṣoktadoṣaṃ pariharati—ucyata iti | na ca mūlakāraṇeti | mūlakāraṇaṃ bhayaṃ tato yathā kampādi sābhiniveśaṃ prabalataraṃ tathā tatsaṃskārādityarthaḥ | etacca yathā karmaṇo mūlakāraṇasya phalaṃ sābhiniveśaṃ na tathā tatsaṃskāraphalamiti vakṣyamāṇaviśeṣopayogitayoktam | tata eveti; rajjutvadarśanādevetyarthaḥ | nanu kṣaṇikaṃ tat, ataḥ kathaṃ krameṇa nivṛttiḥ?
