vetyatra cakrabhramaṇasaṃskāro dṛṣṭāntaḥ | etacca sarvaṃ 'na ca bhayavigame'pyanuvṛttirityetāvatā cirakālā bhavati' ityetadupayogitayoktam | svayaṃ betyatra krameṇetyanuvartate | nanu kṣaṇikasya sahetuko nāśo dṛṣṭaḥ ; tatkathaṃ svayamityucyate ; cakrasaṃskāro'pi phalavināśyeva | atha karabhūbhāgādisparśavadvyasaṃyogādvā naśyatītyasiddho dṛṣṭāntaḥ | ucyate—ko vā brūte na sahetuka iti ? kāla eva heturbhaviṣyati ; ata eva krameṇetyuktam | dṛṣṭaṃ ca kālasyāpi sūtakadoṣādivināśakatvam | tathehāpi bhaviṣyati | kathaṃ tarhi svayamityuktam ? prayatnāntarānapekṣamātravivakṣayoktamityadoṣaḥ | evaṃ P.131-16 dṛṣṭāntaṃ prasādhya dārṣṭāntike yojayati—tatheti | karmasu cārabdhakāryeṣvanārabdhakāryeṣvityatra 'nivṛttāyām' iti liṅgavacanavyatyayena 'nivṛtteṣu' iti kṛtvā yojyam | rūḍhābhiniveśa iti ; rūḍhaḥ prādurbhūto'bhiniveśaḥ āsthātiśayo yasyeti vigrahaḥ | tenāyamarthaḥ—vidyayā avidyāyāṃ karmasu cāviśeṣādārabdhānārabdhakāryeṣu nivṛtteṣu ārabdho bhujyamāno vipākaḥ prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ tatsaṃskārāt vidvānāṃ śarīriṇaṃ bhoktāraṃ cātmānamābhāsamātreṇa āsthāvikalaṃ pratyeti | na tvavidvānivāsthātiśayopetamiti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yatprārabdhaphalaṃ karma, tasminnivṛtte'pi tatphale bhujyamāne yaḥ śarīritvādisaṃskāra udbhūtaḥ, tenottarakālamapi śarīriṇaṃ bhoktāramiva cātmānaṃ manyata iti | atrāntare vivakṣitamarthamasamāpyaiva sthitaprajñalakṣaṇābhidhānopapattaye sthitaprajñasvarūpaṃ tāvadāha—sa cāyamiti | pūrvaṃ sādhakaḥ sthitaprajña uktaḥ, adhunā siddha eveti viśeṣaḥ | tasya ca "prajñahāti yadā kāmān" "ātmanyevātmanā tuṣṭaḥ" "duḥkheṣvanuddhimanāḥ" ityādilakṣaṇaṃ saṃpādayitumāha—vigateti | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | uktamarthaṃ dārṣṭāntike yojayati—tatheti | kṣāṇā karmakṛtā avidyā yasya, kṣaṇi vā karmāvidye yasyeti vigrahaḥ | tatsaṃskārāditi tacchabdena karma parāmṛśyate ; dvitīye'pi vigrahe karmāvidye ; tadā cāvidyāsaṃskāro'pi sūcita iti mantavyam | chāyāmātreṇetyabhiniveśavaikalyaṃ darśayati | viduṣaḥ śarīrādiṣvidyamabhiniveśavaikalyaṃ śrutyā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | ahinirvayanī ahestvak, sā yathā pratyastā
[p. 263]
pratikṣiptā tyaktā satī ahaṃmānaśūnyatayā sā vikalā śeta iva, tathā viduṣaḥ saṃskāramātrānuvarti śarīramityarthaḥ | kimiti punaḥ karmeva śarīre saṃskāravartinyapyāsthā na syādityāśaṅkyāha—yata iti | atulyatvaṃ ca P. 132-3. duśmitaṃ bhayasaṃskāre | idānīṃ saṃskārāccedbhoktādivibhāgadarśanamanuvartate, dehapātāduttarakāle'pyanuvartateti yaduktaṃ tatpariharati—kampādīti | atra hetumāha—alpeti | nanu saṃskāraścedanuvartate tannivṛttaye hetvantaramupāsyam, na ca viduṣastadastītyāśaṅkyāha—tadvadeveti | “tatra tasyeva” iti saptamīsamarthādvatiḥ | tatra kampādī yathā saṃskāranivṛttaye na hetvantaramapekṣyate, tathātrāpītyarthaḥ | idānīmanenaiva prasaṅgena jīvanmuktimapi darśayati—sā ceti | saṃskārakṣayaḥ kathamavagamyata iti cet ata āha—prārabdheti | nanu kvacidbujyamānavipākasaṃskāra ityucyate, kvacitkarmasaṃskāra iti ; kimidaṃ viplutābhidhānam ? na viplutam, pakṣadvayasyāpi vikalpeneṣṭatvāt; tathā ca vakṣyati—labdhavṛttikāraṇasaṃskārādvā kāryaśeṣaḥ, kāryasaṃskārādeva veti | yadvā tatrāpi bhujyamāno vipāko yasya saḥ, tathā prārabdho vipāko yasyeti ca bahuvrīhiṇā prārabdhakāryaṃ karmavocyata ityavirodhaḥ | kathamityāha—taditi | tadaiva dehapāte prārabdhakāryakarmasaṃskārasya kṣīṇatvāt tannimittasya karmaphalasya vipākābhāsamātreṇāpi dehapātottarakāle darśanaṃ na śakyata ityāha—itīti | itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ kākākṣivadubhayatra saṃbadhyate | prārabdhakāryakarmasaṃskārakṣaye liṅgamuktam | upapattirucyate—yena hīti | yena karmaṇā yaccharīramārabdham, tatraiva tatphalaṃ dadāti, nānyatra | yatra ca tatphalaṃ dadāti, tatraiva saṃskārādapi tatphalaśeṣābhāsatā yuktetyarthaḥ | nanu yathā nivṛtte'pyārabdhaphale karmaṇi tatsaṃskāro'nuvartate, tathā anārabdhaphalānāmapi karmaṇāṃ saṃskāraḥ kasmānnānuvartate ? tadanuvṛttau ca dīrghaḥ saṃskāraḥ syāt; ataḥ sa eva dehapātāvadhiśrutivirodha ityāśaṅkyāha—anārabdheti | atra hetumāha—labdheti; labdhā kāryāya vṛttirbyāpāro yena tat tathoktam | etat dṛṣṭāntena sādhayati—na svalpiti | alabdhavṛttau ata evānupajanitakampādī iti hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ | na labdhā vṛttiryena,
[p. 264]
anupajanitaṃ kampādi yeneti vigrahaḥ ; bhayamanyapadārthaḥ | prakṛtamupasaṃharati— P.132-12 tasmāditi | etena ‘nanu brahmavido'pi cet’ ityādinā ‘nāvaśyaṃbhāvi kaivalyaṃ syāt’ ityantena yaccoditaṃ tatparihṛtam | idānīṃ sarvathābhimatasiddheḥ saṃskāraviśeṣāvadhāraṇaṃ pratyanādaraṃ darśayati—atra ceti | kāraṇaṃ karma, kāryaṃ ca tatphalam ; tena labdhavṛtteḥ prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ saṃskārāt tatkāryasaṃskārādvā viduṣo'pi kāryaśeṣo'nuvartata ityarthaḥ | tathā dṛṣṭānte'pyeṣaiva digiti darśayati—tatheti | tasmātsaṃskāraviśeṣo nādriyata ityapekṣāyāmāha—sarvatheti | kāraṇamatra bhayam ; kāryaśeṣaḥ saṃskārajakampādiḥ | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—ata iti | tena na dehapātāvadhiśrutivirodha iti bhāvaḥ | ‘etena kṣaṇamapi na syāt’ iti yaccoditaṃ tadapi parihṛtam | idameva svapakṣaṃ kāpilatantrasiddhatvena draḍhayati—taduktamiti | pravṛttabhogānāṃ karmaṇāṃ pravṛttavegasyeveṣośraktasyeva vānyena vidyayā pratibandho'śakyaḥ ; ato bhogena teṣāṃ kṣayapratīkṣeti sūktaṃ “bhogena tvitare kṣapayitvā” iti bhagavatpādīyamatamupanyasyati—ye tviti | taddūṣayati— taditi | pravṛttavego'pīṣuḥ kubjādibhiḥ pratibandhu śakyaḥ ; ato dṛṣṭānta evāsiddhaḥ | tena tatra yathā kubjādibhiḥ pravṛttavegasyāpīṣoḥ pratibandhaḥ, tathā karmaṇāmapi vidyayeti na bhogena tatkṣayapratīkṣā yukteti bhāvaḥ | āstāṃ tāvatpratibandhaḥ ; nāśo'pi yāvacchakyata iti pratibandhaṃ daṇḍāpūpanyāyasiddhaṃ darśayituṃ ‘nāśayitum’ ityuktam | dṛṣṭārthasyeṣoḥ pratibandhaḥ śakyaḥ, na tvadṛṣṭaphalasya karmaṇa iti cedata āha—svameti | svarārohaṇādisvamena gṛbhrapatanādyāpātena ca sūcitaḥ upasthitaḥ pratyāsanno vipākaḥ phalaṃ yasya, tacca vartamānadehabhogyaṃ ca tat karma ceti samāptaḥ | svapakṣamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | saṃskārādeveti prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ kṣayapratīkṣāṃ nivartayati | nanveva sati ‘sarvāvidyāpravilye na cchedyamavaśiṣyate’ iti yaduktaṃ tadvighaṭitam ; tanna, yataḥ saṃskārakāryaśeṣābhāsau svayameva pravilyete iti na pravilyāpye ityabhiprāyeṇa tāvat ‘na cchedyamavaśiṣyate’ ityuktam |
[p. 265]
tayośrāvidyātmanorapyakiṃcitkaratvāt, aputranyāyenāsattvamadhyāropya chedyābhiprāyeṇa 'sarvāvidyāpravilaye' ityuktamityubhayatra parihārābhiprāyaḥ | vilīnatulyatvadarśanārthaṃ hetumāha—akiṃciditi | yadi līnaṃ tadakiṃcitkaram, P. 138-6. abandhaḥ, svanivṛttaye hetvantarānapekṣaṃ ca | saṃskārakāryaśeṣāvapi tathā | atastāvapi vilīnakalpāvityarthaḥ | tatra svanivṛttaye hetvantarānapekṣatvaṃ tata eva krameṇa nivartate, svayameva vā ityatroktamityupekṣya akiṃcitkaratvābandhatvayorupapattyabhidhānapuraḥsaraṃ kāryaśeṣābhāsasya pravilīnaprāyatvamāha—viśuddhamiti | prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ kāryaśeṣābhāso vipākābhāsaḥ; sa viśuddhaṃ prapañcakāluṣyeṇāsaṃspṛṣṭamātmānaṃ paśyantaṃ na spṛśati | na ca tamasaṃspṛśat tasya kiṃcitkaroti, badhnāti ca tam | ataśchiddhābhramiva pralīnaprāya eva sa ityarthaḥ | yadyapi pūrvaṃ saṃskārasyāpyakiṃcitkaratvaṃ pratijñātam; tathāpi kāryaśeṣābhāsāveśamukhena tasyākiṃcitkaratā; atastasminnakiṃcitkare tasyāpyakiṃcitkaratā arthādukteti pṛthagihoktā | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | srcādyābhāsa ityatra pravilīna evetyanuvartate; pravilīnaprāya evetyarthaḥ | rāgādīnāmiti akiṃcitkaratvamuktam | dṛṣṭāntāntaramāha—tatheti | ātmānamiti, ātmaśabdena laukikaḥ śarīrātmocyate | avadātaṃ gauram | viparītam anavadātam | tatrāvadātasya bhukhasya khaḍgādau śyāmābhāso'navadātasya ca purvādau avadātāvabhāsa utpanno'pi mukhasya rūpatattvajñānādātmasaṃsparśaṃ pratyavakāśamalabhamānaṃ: pravilīnaprāya evetyarthaḥ | pravilīnaprāyatvameva draḍhayati—tathā ceti | etena karmasaṃskāranimittayoḥ śarīrasthityormahānviśeṣaḥ, viduṣo yathoktakarmaphalanivṛttyā karmakṣayo gamyate; tataśca karmaṇaḥ kṣīṇatvānna bhogena kṣayapratīkṣetyuktaṃ bhavati | viduṣo vipākābhāsasyānabhinandyamānādviṣyamāṇatve gītayā draḍhayati—tathā ceti | vidvānābhāsamātmasaṃsparśinaṃ na manyata iti yaduktaṃ tadapi gītayaiva draḍhayati—tathā ceti | atra vākyārthajñānādeva muktiriti manvānaśrodayati—nanviti | tasyeti; tattvajñānasyetyarthaḥ | pariharati—ucyata iti | samānajātīyaṃ hi virodhi bhavati, na vijātīyam; pratyakṣasya ca prapañcābhāsasya parokṣaṃ jñātuṃ
[p. 266]
taccajñānaṃ vijātīyam; tena tayoravirodhāt parokṣeṇa taccajñānenāparokṣasya prapañcābhāsasyātmāsaṃsparśitvamakicitkaratvamasaṃbandhatvaṃ vāpādayituṃ na śakyata | P.134.4. ityarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—yaśceti | yathā khaṇḍo madhuraḥ khaṇḍatvāt, prāganubhūtakhaṇḍavat ityanumānādāttavacanādvā pramāṇāntarāvagatamādhurye'pi khaṇḍadravye pratyakṣasaṃrūpa indriyajastiktāvabhāsaḥ sa khaṇḍadravyāsaṃsparśī nābhāsamātratayā anabhinandyamāno'dviṣyamāṇaścātmāsaṃsparśitayāvatiṣṭhate, apitu dviṣyata ityarthaḥ | etadeva draḍhayati—tathā ceti | duḥkhāya bhavatīti dviṣyamāṇatvamuktam | tathā ca dhūrutya tyāgādityuktam | upāsanāyāṃ tu satyāṃ tayā sākṣātkṛtātmataccasya viduṣo'parokṣeṇātmataccajñānenāparokṣasya prapañcābhāsasya samānajātīyatvena virodhaḥ; tataśca sannapi prapañcābhāsastenāsatsamīkṛto nātmasaṃsparśī na sukhāya vā bhavati | tenāstyupāsanopayoga iti bhāvaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha—devadatteti | itaścātmataccāparokṣāyopāsanāpekṣitavyetyāha—nityaśceti | ātmataccaprakāśaḥ ātmanaḥ “sutyaṃ jñānamanantam” iti jñānātmakasya prakāśaḥ “avināśī vāre'yamātmā” iti nityaḥ | atastasminnāvibhūte na punaḥ prapañcabhrāntyavabhāso bhavati | tenātyantikasaṃsāranivṛttirupapadyate | śābdaṃ tu jñānaṃ kṣaṇikam | atastasminnaṣṭe pramāṇānanusaṃdhāne punarapi prapañcabhrāntiravakāśaṃ labhate | tasya punarāvṛttiḥ syāt | tato nityātmaprakāśāvirbhāvāyopāsanā yuktetyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭaṃ caitat—pramāṇānanusaṃdhāne bhrāntirbhavati, sā ca duḥkhaheturityāha—dṛṣṭamiti | nanu pramāṇānanusaṃdhāne cetpunarbhrāntirbhavati, tato'trāpi śābdamevātmajñānaṃ saṃtatamanusaṃdhīyatām; tata eva punarbhrāntirna bhaviṣyati; kimupāsanayetyāśaṅkayati—aśceti | taddūṣayati— kimiti | saṃtatānusaṃdhānamevopāsanā, nāparā | atastaccedāśrīyate, kathamupāsanā neṣṭetyarthaḥ | evaṃ dehapātāvadhiśrutivirodhaṃ parihṛtya vākyārthajñānātiriktopāsanopāyaṃ coktvā ‘na cchedyamavaśiṣyate’ iti yaduktaṃ tadupasaṃharati—tasmāditi |
[p. 267]
evamavidyānivṛttimukhena dṛṣṭenaiva vidyā prārabdhānārabdhakāryapūrvakarmakṣayaheturiti prasādhya utpannātmatattvabodhasyottarakālīnena pramādajena pāpenālepamadṛṣṭaśaktyā vidyā karotīti ye manyante, tānnirākartuṃ tatrāpi dṛṣṭenaiva vidyā heturityatidiśati—evamiti | tatrottarāghāśleṣaśrutiṃ tāvatpaṭhati—yatheti | P.134-14 yathā ca tatra dṛṣṭenaiva vidyā hetustathā darśayati—kartriti | karturhi kartavyena karmaṇā saṃbandhaḥ ; ato yāvadevātmānaṃ kartāraṃ kartavyaṃ ca pāpakarmāvidyayā manyate, tāvadeva tacchepaḥ | vidyāyāṃ tūditāyāmavidyāvilayāt kartṛkartavyavibhāga eva nāsti ; kasya kena śleṣaḥ ? evaṃ dṛṣṭenaivottarāghāśleṣe'pi vidyā heturityarthaḥ | idānīṃ dṛṣṭenaiva vidyā pūrvottarakarmaṇoḥ kṣayāśleṣayorheturityasya svapakṣasya sādhutvaṃ darśayan adṛṣṭaśaktyā vidyā tayorheturityasya pakṣasyāsattāṃ sūcayati—evamiti | adṛṣṭaśaktivādināmadṛṣṭā śaktistāvat kalpyā ; sā ca pūrvottarakarmakṣayāśleṣayormedādaneketi kalpanāgauravam ; “na ca punarāvartate” ityādivartamānāpadeśānāṃ ca vipariṇāmena phalavidhitvakalpaneti doṣaḥ ; na tvasmākamityarthaḥ | nanvavidyātvāditarāvidyāvat saṃskārakāryaśeṣāvidyayā vinivartitavyam ; ataḥ kathaṃ pravilīnaprāyayorapi tayoranuvṛttirucyate ; anumānamidam ; taccābhāsānuvacyarthāpattidehapātāvadhiśrutibhyāṃ viruddhatvādapramāṇam | saṃskāraśca svahetunivṛttāvapyanuvartate, bhayādisaṃskāravat | idānīṃ tṛtīyāpratipattividhivādinā yaduktaṃ nānāpadārthasaṃsargātmikāyāḥ śābdapratipatteraprapañcaṃ brahma na viṣaya iti, tadupanyasya dūṣayati—yaditi | yadi vedāntaśabdajanitadhīgamyaṃ brahma na bhavati, tataḥ ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ iti brahmadhiyo vidhiraśakyaḥ ; yato nānirūpitapado'navagataviṣayo vidhirbhavati, na tattvamātramaviśiṣṭaṃ vidherviṣayaḥ, kiṃtu dhīviśeṣaḥ | viśeṣaśca svayamanākārāyā dhiyo'rthanibandhanaḥ | arthaścāsiddho'navagato dhīviśeṣasya prarūpāya nirūpaṇāya na kṣamaḥ | ato'rthe brahmaṇyavagate brahmajñānaṃ vidhātuṃ śakyam | na ca tasya śabdādanyato'vagatirasti | tasmācchabdagamyaṃ brahmaiṣṭavyamityarthaḥ | kiṃca yasya pratipattiviśeṣavidhiparā vedāntāḥ tasyātmatattvamapramāṇakameva syādityāha—api ceti | vidhiḥ niyogaḥ svasiddhiniṣṭhaḥ svakārya-
[p. 268]
P.135-9. tāyāṃ paryavasitaḥ; athavā kriyāsiddhiparaḥ, kriyātmano viṣayasya tataḥ kāryatā gamyate; tatrādye pakṣe vidhiḥ svātmani paryavasitaḥ, śabdo'pyadvayātmapratipattiviṣayo niyogaḥ kārya ityatraiva paryavasitaḥ | dvitīye viṣayasiddhau vidhiḥ paryavasitaḥ, śabdo'pi tatheti advayātmapratipattirahetukā apramāṇikā syāt | atha niyogāt “vijaro vimṛtyurviśokaḥ” ityādipadānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyāt kvacit, anyatrānyathā vā vaiyadhikaraṇyena vā saṃbandhāt ātmasvarūpādhigatirbhaviṣyatīti cedata āha—viniyogāditi | viniyogādātmasvarūpādhigatiryā, asyā ātmasvarūpādhigateḥ ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ iti yā jñānakriyā vihitā tadarthatā tatparatā, nātmasvarūpaparatā | ‘virajādisvarūpa ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ ityevaṃparā, na punaḥ ‘virajādirūpa ātmā’ ityevaṃparetyuktaṃ bhavati | ataśca jñānakriyāparyavasitātmādhigatiratatparā ātmasvarūpaparā na bhavantī nātmasvarūpe pramāṇam | yathā vidhistutiparā arthavādotthā buddhiḥ ‘pūjā kāryā’ ityatraiva paryavasitā, na punaḥ ‘advaya ātmā’ ityātmasvarūpe | tatrobhayathā advayātmarūpaparatvābhāvaḥ | yatparaśca śabdaḥ sa śabdārtho loke | ato'dvayātmapratipattirapramāṇikā; yathā “prajāpatirvapāmudakkhidat” ityādivṛttānteṣu | vṛttāvanyathā vetyasya vaiyadhikaraṇyena vetyarthaḥ | tatra ca “yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante” ityādyudāharaṇavidhirna svaniṣṭho nāpi kriyāsiddhiniṣṭhaḥ; kiṃtu ātmasvarūpameva vidhīyate | ataḥ śabdasya tatparatvāt sa eva tatra pramāṇamiti nādvaitātmapratipattirapramāṇiketi cedata āha—vidhiriti | anuṣṭheye hi viṣaye vidhiḥ puṃsāṃ pravartakaḥ; anuṣṭheyā ca kriyā; ataḥ kriyāgocaratvāt siddharūpatayā svaśabdenopāse'nuṣṭheye ātmadravye vidhirna vyāpārāya kṣamaḥ | vṛttau siddhavaditi svārthe vatiḥ | yathā cānyat ghaṭādi siddhaṃ svaśabdenopādīyate tathā ātmāpītyarthaḥ | nanu dravyaṃ cenna vidhīyate, kathaṃ tarhi “dadhā juhoti” ityādau guṇavidhiḥ? ucyate—tatrāpi kriyaiva vidhīyate; nanu sā “agnihotraṃ juhoti” ityataḥ prāptaiva | bādam; guṇaviśiṣṭā tu na prāptā | kathaṃ tarhi sa guṇavidhirucyate? prāptāprāpta-
[p. 269]
vivekena vidhimadhye guṇamātraphalatvādityadoṣaḥ | kiṃca īdṛśaṃ vastu abhūt asti bhaviṣyati vā iti kālatrayasyānyatareṇa saṃsṛṣṭayā sattvānvitasya dravyasya pratītiriti hetone kevalaṃ vidhidravyaṃ vidhātuṃ kṣamaḥ ; pratyuta duṣyati yāvat, svarūpahānāt | kālakartranavācchinnabhāvārthaviṣayatāsvarūpo nahi saḥ | na hi 'yajet' ityādau kālatrayasya madhye'nyatareṇa viśiṣṭā sattā pratīyate | nanu vihitamanuṣṭhāsyate, anuṣṭheyaṃ ca bhaviṣyati ; ataśca bhaviṣyatkālā tasya sattā pratīyate | naitat | na vihitamavaśyamanuṣṭhīyate ; anyathā hi tasyākaraṇaṃ na syāt ; ataśca "akurvanvihitaṃ karma" iti doṣaśrutiranārthikā syāt | tasmātmūkam—kālatrayānyatarasaṃsparśe sa duṣyatīti | nanu yathā "somena yajet" iti somaviśiṣṭahomakriyāvidhivākyameva tadviśeṣaṇe some pramāṇaṃ na pṛthak, tathā 'evaṃbhūta ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti pratipattikartavyatāpramāṇamevātmasvarūpe pramāṇam ; ataḥ kathamapramāṇikātmataccapratītirityāśaṅkyocyate—viśiṣṭeti | anyasyāṃ yāga- P.185-15 kriyāyāṃ somaviśiṣṭāyāṃ yo vidhiḥ sa eva viśeṣaṇe some pramāṇaṃ bhavati | 'evaṃbhūta ātmā pratipattavyaḥ' iti vihitā pratipattistu ātmasvarūpaviśiṣṭā pratipattiviṣayasyātmano vijarādirūpasya sattāṃ niyogato niyamena na sādhayati | kutaḥ? samāropeṇātadrūpe tadrūpādhyāropeṇāpi "asau vāva loko gautamāgniḥ" itivat saṃbhavādityarthaḥ | śrutau lokaśabdena bulokādirucyate | vaidikalaukikabhedena vidhāyakavartamānāpadeśabhedena vā viśeṣaṇaikatvānekatvabhedena vā vṛttau dṛṣṭāntadvayamuktam | samāropeṇa buddheḥ saṃbhavādityuktamasahamānaśrodayati—nanviti | no śabdo niṣedhārthaḥ | viparīto'rtho viṣayo yasyāḥ sā, tādṛśī anyadanyathā gṛhṇī dharmi bhavati | kutaḥ? ya evārtho yasyāṃ saṃvidi bhāti sa tasya viṣayo nānyat | tatra rajataṃ bhāti śuktiviṣaya iti pratītiviruddham | yadi ca jñānamanyadanyathā gṛhīyāt tato vyabhicāraśaṅkayā sarvatrānāśvāsaḥ 'tathaiva' iti niścayo na syāt | yadi śuktiḥ rajatatayā na bhāti, kiṃ nāmāya śuktau rajatapratyaya ityapekṣāyāmāha—rajateti | na tānnadidaṃ rajatajñānamindriyasaṃyogajam, asaṃnihitena rajatenendriyasaṃyogābhāvāt |
[p. 270]
nāpi liṅgādijam, liṅgāderabhāvāt | asti tu pūrvānubhavajaḥ smṛtihetuḥ saṃskāraḥ | tasyāt smṛteḥ kāraṇatadbhāvāt smṛtirevāyaṃ rajatapratyaya ityarthaḥ | kiṃca viṣayasya śukteḥ rūpaṃ cennānukaroti jñānam, śuktyākāraṃ na bhavati apitu rajatākāraṃ bhavati, tadā tajjñānaṃ nirālambanaṃ syāt | na caitaducitam | na vidyate ālambanaṃ viṣayo yasyeti vigrahaḥ | śuktyākhyaviṣayānanukārādaviṣayatvamuktam | anālambanamiti ca dṛṣṭāntalābhena vauddhānāṃ nirālambanānumānasyāvakāśaḥ syāditi vacanārthamuktam | agrāhyamiti ca karmavijñānakriyānupapattimūcanārthamiti vivekaḥ | nanu yadi nāma śuktirvedyatayā nālambanaṃ, tathāpi hetutayā bhaviṣyati; asti ca niraviṣṭhānabhramā- P.136-23 saṃbhavāt tasyā hetutvamityāśaṅkyāha—hetviti | hetumātrasyālambanatve cakṣurāderapi jñānālambanatā syāt | na ca taddṛṣṭam | tasmāddvayamevālambanam, na ca śuktistatheti nālambanamityarthaḥ | yadi ca hetumātratayālambanamartho na vedyatayā tathā sati jñānasyāvedakatvāt pramāṇāntarāpekṣādapi tato jñānādarthasiddhirna syāt, kimuta nirapekṣāt | ataśrānapekṣaṃ jñānaprāmāṇyaṃ na syādityabhiprāyeṇāha—tathā ceti | yadi viparītārthaṃ rajatajñānamidaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tarhīdaṃ rajatajñānamiti siddhāntī pṛcchati—kathamiti | apara āha—śuktīti | smṛterutpattihetumāha—sāmānyeti | śuktyāstaratvādirūpasya śuktirajatayoḥ sāmānyasya darśanāt sadṛśāṃ śuktiṃ dṛṣṭavataḥ saṃskāroddhodhāt rajate smṛtirjīyata ityarthaḥ | taduktam—“sadṛśādṛṣṭacintābhyāṃ smṛtijījasya bodhakaḥ” iti | yadi śukteḥ ‘idam’ iti grahaṇaṃ rajatasya ca smṛtiḥ, tatastayoryathārthatayaiva kathaṃ bhrāntitvamiti siddhāntī punaḥ pṛcchati—bhrāntiḥ kathamiti | para āha—pratyakṣeti | ‘idam’ iti pratyakṣasya śuktiśakalasya ye viśeṣā rajatādivivekahetavaḥ te'bhibhavānna jñātāḥ | śuktyāstaratvādisāmānyamātraṃ jñātam | smṛtyāpi manodoṣāt ‘tat’ ityullekharahitatayā rajataṃ gṛhyamāṇāt śuktiśakalāt pūrvadeśakālagatatvenāvivecitam | tadevaṃ pratyakṣasmayamāṇayoravivekāt rajatabuddhyā śuktau pravṛttasya vyavahāre visaṃvādādyathārthayorapi smaraṇagrahaṇayorbhrāntitvamityarthaḥ | nanu ‘idaṃ rajatam’ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratīterabhedagrahaṇa-
[p. 271]
miti kathamucyate, vivekāgrahaṇādityāśaṅkayāha—so'yamiti | atra P. 187-3. dṛṣṭāntamāha—yatheti | tathā ālokāgrahaṇe kṛṣṇatamorūpajñānābhimāno'ndhānāmudāhāryam | svapakṣamupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | tadetatsiddhāntī dūṣayati—atreti | yaduktaṃ nānyasyānyathā bhānamiti; yadi cakṣuḥsaṃyuktaṃ śuktiśakalaṃ rajatamiti na pratīyāt, rajataṃ vā deśāntaragataṃ tathā purovartitayā na pratīyāt, tadā rajatārthī rajatamādātukāmaḥ śuktiśakale na pravarteta; śuktiśakalasya rajatatvenānavagamāt | yaddhi rajatatvenānavagataṃ na tatra rajatārthī pravartate, yathā loṣṭhādiṣu | atha rajata eva smaryamāṇe sā pravṛttirityucyate, tanna; smaryamāṇasya purovartini deśe saṃnidhānābhāvāt purovartini deśe pravṛttirna syāt, apitu pūrvadṛṣṭa eva rajate pravarteta | atha 'tat' ityucchetsarahitayā smṛtyā rajatamātramupasthāpitam, na pūrvadeśasaṃbandhaḥ; tena rajatamātrapratīterasaṃnidheśrāpratīteḥ śuktau pravṛttirucyate, tanna; evaṃ hi sarvamidamanyatrāpyaviśiṣṭamiti tatrāpi pravarteta, na śuktāveva | śuktau tu yanniyamena pravartate, tattatra rajatasaṃnidhikāritam | tatsaṃnidhiśra bhavati yadi śuktau rajatatayā gṛhītā syāt, nānyathā | na hi tasya vastunaḥ saṃnidhirasti | tasmācchuktau rajatatayā gṛhyata iti bhāvaḥ | atha anyadeśeṣu vastuṣu saviśeṣeṣu pratyakṣeṇa gṛhīteṣu rajatadvivekasya bhedasya grahaṇādapravṛttiḥ, śuktau vivekāgrahaṇāt pravṛttiḥ; vivekāgrahaṇaṃ ca śukterbhāstrarūpasāmānyanibandhanāt sādṛśyāt vivecakānāṃ ca śukterviśeṣaṇānāmapratīterityucyate; evamapyadṛṣṭeṣvapi loṣṭhādiṣvadṛṣṭatvādeva vismṛteśrāvivekatvādrajatādviveko na gṛhīta iti teṣvapi pravarteta | atha teṣu rajatabuddhirnāsti, tena teṣu na pravartate; tarhi śuktāvapi rajatabuddhirnāsti, tatrāpi na pravarteta | athāsti śuktau rajatabuddhirityucyate, tato viparītārthākhyātirāpannā; katha sāpahvayet? viparīto'rtho yasyā iti vigrahaḥ | atha loṣṭhādiṣvadṛṣṭatvādapravṛttiḥ, tarhi śuktirapi rajatatayā na dṛṣṭeti tatrāpi rajatārthinaḥ pravṛttirna syāt, śuktiḥ śuklabhāstrarādisāmānyasvarūpeṇa dṛṣṭeti cet, tanna; na hi sāmānyarūpeṇa rajatārthinaḥ pravṛttikāraṇam,
[p. 272]
apitu rajatarūpeṇa | tena tadrūpeṇādṛṣṭaloṣṭhādivat śuktiśakalamapi na P.137-17 dṛṣṭam, tatastatrāpi pravṛttirna syāditi bhāvaḥ | dṛṣṭasmṛtayoḥ śuktirajatayoravivekāt pravṛttiḥ, tatrādṛṣṭeṣu loṣṭhādiṣu kutaḥ prasaṅga iti cet; tatrāpīdaṃ vicāryam—kiṃ śuktirajatayorvivekagrahaṇasya vivekakāriṇo'bhāvāt rajatasya saṃnidhau purovartitvena pratīteḥ śuktirajatayostaccasyābhedasya bodhaḥ; tataḥ śuktau rajatavedanāt pravṛttiḥ, utātaccasya bhedasyābodhāditi | tatra taccabodhāt pravṛttirityasminnāthe pakṣe śuktiśakalaṃ rajatatayā gṛhṇāti | ataḥ rajatamaterviparītārthatā syāt | parasminstu pakṣe yathā dṛṣṭāt śuktiśakalāt viveko rajatasya na gṛhyate, tathā loṣṭhāderadṛṣṭādapi | ato dṛṣṭādṛṣṭayorbhedo na syāt; dṛṣṭavadadṛṣṭe'pi pravṛttiḥ syādityarthaḥ | nanu viṣaye kāpi pravṛttiḥ; nāviṣaye | na cādṛṣṭaṃ loṣṭhādi viṣayaḥ | ataḥ kathaṃ tatra pravṛttiḥ? bāḍham; rajatamapi purovarti na dṛṣṭam, tava tatrāpi pravṛttirna syāt | yā tu dṛṣṭā śuktiḥ, sā rajatārtheno na pravṛtterviṣaya iti parasyāpyaviṣayaiva pravṛttiḥ | evaṃ cedadṛṣṭe'pi kiṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu dṛśyamānāviveko bhramaḥ; tat kathamadṛṣṭeṣu tatprasaṅgaḥ? syādeva yadi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭayorvivekāgrahaṇasya svarūpabhedaḥ syāt; sa tu tasya prāgabhāvatvānnāstīti manyate | yadapyucyate—bhramasamyagrajatajñānayorvivekāgrahaṇaṃ nāma sādhāraṇaṃ rūpamasti, atastena rūpeṇa tulyatvātsamyagrajatajñānatulyabhrame'pi vyavahārapravṛttiriti; tadapyayuktam; na hi yastriṃkācitsāmyena vyavahārasāmyam, mukhaduḥkhahetujñānayorapi jñānatvasāmyāt tatprasaṅgāt | tasmātsamyagrajatajñāne yatpravṛttinimittaṃ tatsāmye tacculyā pravṛttiriti yujyate | samyagrajatajñāne vivekāgraho na pravṛttinimittam, apitu vivekagrahaḥ; sa ca bhrame tava nāstīti kathaṃ tulyā pravṛttiriti | nanu dṛṣṭe darśanamidriyayogaśrāsti, nādṛṣṭa ityasti tayorbhedaḥ; kathamabheda ityāśaṅkyāha— neti | tathāhi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭābhyāṃ vivekāgrahasya tulyatvādindriyayogadarśane nopayoginī | na hyagrahaṇe tayoḥ kaścidupayogaḥ, tasya prāgabhāvarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ | kva nāma te upayoginī ityapekṣāyāṃ svapakṣe tayorupayogamāha—te hīti | rajatasya śuktau samārope upayoginī ityarthaḥ | atra
[p. 273]
hetumāha—neti | cākṣuṣo'yaṃ rajatabhramaḥ | sa ca cakṣuḥsaṃyukte sāmānyena P.187-22 ‘idam’ iti dṛṣṭe ca vastuni yujyate, nānyatra | atastatra svayogadarśane upayoginī ityarthaḥ | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—smṛtamiti | smṛtaṃ rajataṃ pratyakṣataḥ śuktiśakalādyudi sajñānāt rajatasmṛtirūpāttasya vivecanasāmarthyamahimnaiva minnaṃ viviktaṃ bhāti; tadā smṛtapratyakṣayoravivekaḥ kutaḥ? apitu viveka eva | tatra kuto rajatārthinaḥ śuktāvavivekātpravṛttiḥ | smṛtijñānasyetyādi pūrvoktavikalpavivaraṇamātram | atha tu smṛtiḥ svamahimnaiva svaviṣayaṃ na vivinakti, tata urdhvatvādisāmānyadarśane anyat sthāṇupuruṣādikaṃ smarataḥ sadā viparyayaḥ syāt; na caitat, ‘sthāṇurvā puruṣo vā’ iti saṃśayasyāpi darśanāt | tatra ca viparyayaśabdo vivekāgrahaṇalakṣaṇaṃ bhramaṃ vivekāgrahaṇasāmyenopacārādāha | na tu parasya viparyayo'bhimataḥ, yenāsau saṃprasajyate | ekasmṛtau purovartinastadavivekādbhramaḥ, anekasmṛtau tu smṛtadvayāvivekāt saṃśaya iti yadi manyate, tanna; yataḥ sthāṇupuruṣayorapi smṛtau kadācidanyatarabhramo dṛṣṭaḥ | sa tvatpakṣe na syāt | atha smṛtaṃ sajñānānna vivicyate, apitu purovartini śuktyādau ‘idam’ ityanirdhāritaviśeṣe sāmānyarūpeṇādhyakṣe pratyakṣeṇa dṛśyamāne yadrajatādibhyo vivekāpādakaṃ viśeṣajñānaṃ bhavati tata ityucyate, tanna; tadāpi hi asatyasminnadhyakṣagate viśeṣajñāne dvayoḥ smṛtau sadā saṃśayaḥ syāt, kathamekākāro viparyayaḥ? bhavati cāsāvapi kadācit | tasmānnāyamapi pakṣaḥ śreyāt | yadā tu smṛtiḥ svārthavivecanasvabhāvatayā smaryamāṇaṃ dṛśyādvivinakti, smṛteśca viparyayo minno'bhyupagamyate; tata ubhayasmṛtāvapi indriyādidoṣaviśeṣādavivekākāro viparyayo yujyate | tasmātsmṛtiḥ svaviṣayavivecanasvabhāvaiva, viparyayaśca tato minna evetyayameva pakṣaḥ śreyāt | itaśca svavijñānādeva smṛtilakṣaṇāt smṛtamapi vivicyate, na dṛśyamānasya viśeṣajñānāt, yato darśitamidaṃ vibhramaviveke—“yadūrdhvatādisāmānyadarśane'pi sthāṇuṃ puruṣaṃ vā ekāṃ koṭiṃ smarataḥ ubhayakoṭismaraṇābhāvāt na saṃśayaḥ, 18
[p. 274]
doṣāṇāmadṛṣṭatvācca na viparyayaḥ” iti | tatra yadi smṛtiḥ svaviṣayaṃ svayaṃ na pravinikti, tata ekāṃ koṭiṃ smaratastadviparyayaḥ syāt; na caivam | ataḥ svajñānādeva smṛtaṃ vivicyate na dṛśyamānasya viśeṣaparijñānāt | sāmānya-mātradarśanasamaye tasyābhāvāt | ataśca smṛteḥ svabhāvena smṛtavivekatvāt kutaḥ smaryamāṇagṛhyamāṇayoḥ vivekābhāvaḥ? yena vivekākhyātiśrama ucyate | kiṃca yadi smaryamāṇagṛhyamāṇayoraviveko bhramaḥ, tadā śaśini prāpnūḍhaṃ prasiddhamekatvaṃ smarato dvitvaṃ cāsmarataḥ timirādidoṣeṇa yo'yaṃ dvitvabhramo dṛśyate sa na syāt, smaryamāṇasya vivekāgrahāsaṃbhavāt | timirādidoṣādindriyavṛttirdvidhā bhinnābhinne jñāne janayati; tena jñānabhedā-vivekāt indriyavṛttibhedāvivekādvā candre bhedabhrama iti cet, tanna; tatrāvivekayojñānendriyavṛttyoranumeyatdenāpratyakṣatvāt | nāpi smaryamāṇāt, tayorasmaraṇāt | nanvanumānena gṛhyamāṇāt jñānāt indriyavṛttervā vivekā-graho bhaviṣyati | naivam | utpanne dvicandrānubhave paścādanumānam | ato na tena gṛhyamāṇābhyāmindriyajñānavṛttibhyāmavivekāt dvicandrabhramasaṃbhavaḥ | taccānumānajñānābhiprāyeṇoktam | anubhavastu yadyapi pratyakṣaḥ, tathāpi tasyānākārasya svayamanavagatabhedasyārthabhedādeva bhedāvagamānna tavārthabhedabhrama iti sūktam—‘te kathaṃ dvicandrabhramaḥ?’ iti | kiṃca yadi kaścitsalilaṃ pātu-bhicchan tadvājane śuktāvabahitacetā bhavati, tadā rajatasyāsmaraṇāt rajata-bhramo na bhavet; bhavati ca so'pi kadācit | bhavan vā bhramaḥ salila-smṛtestasyāścāpi svārthāvivecakatvāt salila eva bhavedityarthaḥ | kiṃca yadi śuktisādṛśyadarśanādrajate smṛtiḥ, sā ca trivekāgrahādbhrama ityevaṃ sādṛśyanibandhanabhrāntiriṣyate, tataḥ śvetapītayoḥ tiktamadhurayośca sādṛśyābhāvāt śvetapītabhramo na syāt; asti cāsau | tasmānna sarvathā bhrāntiniyogena niyamena sādṛśyanibandhanā | athocyate—na sarvatraiva smṛtiprāmāṇyenāsmākaṃ bhramo'bhimataḥ, yena sarvatra sādṛśyanibandhanaḥ sa syāt; apitu gṛhyamāṇavivekāgrahādapi kvacidbhavati | tenāntaraṃ netragataṃ pittaṃ pītaṃ saṃvedyate, rasanāgataṃ ca pittaṃ tiktam, na tu smaryate; tada-vivekācca bhrama iti | tathāhi—apisvavaiāpṇyaṃ dravyarahitaḥ pītimā taiktyaṃ
[p. 275]
ca gṛhyate, dravyaṃ cendriyadoṣāt svaguṇarahitaṃ rūphaṭikamaṇiriva japopahitaḥ | tadevaṃ dravyaguṇayoranyonyāpekṣayorasaṃbandhānavabodhāt amaḥ | indriyaṃ bahivyopārayitavyam | tatkatham? ucyate—bahiravyāpārāt nākṣigatamakṣaṇā dṛśyate, yathā añjanam | nanvañjanaṃ nāyanatejovṛttinirodhakam; pittena tu kāceneva (na) tejovṛttirnirudhyate; prasaracca nāyanatejo bhrāhakam, nāśrayastham | tadayuktam; indriyamātmasthapītaguṇaṃ cet gṛhṇāti, tato bahirarthapvavyāpṛtamapi gṛhṇīyāt | tataśca bahirindriyamavyāpāraprato'pi puṃsaḥ pītimādisaṃvedanaṃ syāt | na caivam | kiṃ nayanenāto nāvyāpṛtenāñjanaruddhena ca nayanapittapītimā gṛhyate? naivam | evaṃ hi svagataṃ kṛṣṇimānamakṣigataṃ ca jalaṃ gṛhṇīyāt, tayorapi tejovṛtti(nya)pratirodhakatvāt | tasmātprasaradapi nāyanaṃ tejo bahireva gṛhṇāti, na netrastham | śaṅkhasvaṇḍayorapi kenacidrūpeṇa pītatiktāmyāṃ kiñcitsādṛśyamasti; tena śaṅkhasvaṇḍāvupalabhya pītatiktasmṛtirbhrama iti cet, na; evaṃ hi kva nāma tathā sādṛśyaṃ nāstīti śaṅkhe svaṇḍe vā na kaścidbhramo na syāt, api tu sarvaprakāraḥ sa syāt | tadevaṃ parapakṣe doṣamuktamupajīvya svapakṣe sādhutvamāha—tasmāditi | kasyacidindriyadoṣasya kvacid ame sāmarthyamiti dṛgādīndriyadoṣatāmarthasya vibhāgātkvacitkasyacidbhrama iti ameṣu niyamo bhavati | yadi doṣādagrahaṇamātraṃ syāt na tu viparyayaḥ, tadā kāmilādidoṣabhedāt ameṣu niyamo na syāt | na hi sa amaniyamaḥ sādṛśyanimittaḥ, asadṛśe'pi pītaśaṅkhādau amadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | sajñānādeva smṛtaṃ vivicyate, tatra kutaḥ smaryamāṇagṛhṇāṇayoraviveka iti siddhāntinokte, satyaṃ sajñānādeva smṛtaṃ vivicyate; ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ— yadviśeṣagrahiṇyapi pratyakṣe dūratvādidoṣādūrdhvatādidarśane kadācidviśeṣāgraho yathā, tathā smṛtāvapi manodoṣādagrahaḥ kadācidbhavati | tadā vivekābhāvo āntitvaṃ ca | sati tu manodoṣābhāvādviśeṣagrahe purovartinīndriyasaṃyukte'pi sthāṇau puruṣe cordhvatāsāmānyadarśane'pi viśeṣajñānena vivecitatvāt tadavivekābhāvaḥ | tataścobhayakoṭismaraṇe saṃśayaḥ | ekasmṛtau tu na saṃśayo na viparyayaḥ | ataśca 'anyathā tu syāt sadā sāmānyadarśane | viparyayo 'nyaṃ smarataḥ' ityādinaṃ doṣo bhavatīti paramatamāśaṅkya, evamapi dvayo-
[p. 276]
viviktayoḥ smaraṇe viparyayo na syāt, bhavati cāsāvapi kadāciditi P.140-16 yadyapi doṣo'sti; tathāpi dūṣaṇāntaramāha—viśeṣeti | na ca smṛtau viśeṣāgraho'pi kadācidbhavati; yato na nirdhāritaviśeṣaṃ vastumātraṃ smaryate, apitu vastuviśeṣa eva rajatādiḥ | athocyate—na brūmo vastuviśeṣo na smaryata iti; kiṃtu smaryamāṇo'pyasau yadā pūrvādhigatadeśakālaviśeṣāpatraḥ smaryate, tadā gṛhyamāṇādvivicyate svajñānāt | yatra tu deśakālaviśeṣāgrahaḥ, tatra na vivicyate | tena deśakālaviśeṣātsmṛtaṃ vivicyate, tadabhāve tvavivekād bhrama iti, tatrāha—deśeti | atra hetumāha— anekeni | deśāgrahaṇamupalakṣaṇam, anantadeśakālādhigatam | tato deśakālaviśeṣāt smaryamāṇaṃ gṛhyamāṇaṃ na punarviviktaṃ gamyate; apitu svata eva smṛtirvivinakti | anyathā hi yadyapi saktadeśakālajñātasya rajatāderdeśakālasmṛtisaṃbhavaḥ, tathāpi anantadeśakālādhigatānāṃ rajatādīnāmanantadeśakālasmaraṇāsaṃbhavāt yasya rajatāderdeśakālaviśeṣaḥ smaryate, tata eva dṛśyaṃ vivicyate, nānyasmādasmaryamāṇadeśakālaviśeṣāt | ataśca yenānantadeśakālatthaṃ rajatādyadhigataṃ tasya sarvadeśakālaviśeṣasmaraṇāsaṃbhavāt sadā smṛteravivekatvād bhrāntiḥ syādityarthaḥ | yadi ca samuccitayordeśakālayorvivekatvaṃ, tadā taddeśādhigate tatraiva smaryamāṇe yadyapi kālabhedo'sti, tathāpi deśabhedābhāvānnityamavivekaḥ, tataśca sadā bhrāntitvaprasaṅgaḥ, na caivam | tasmāt deśakālaviśeṣo na vivekahetuḥ | tena tayoragrahāviveke bhrama ityapi na yuktam | atha ‘smarāmi’ ityullekhena smaryamāṇatājñānāt smṛtasya vivekaḥ, tadabhāve cāvivekaḥ; tena ‘smarāmi’ iti jñānaśūnyāni rajatādijñānāni bhrāntitvahetava ityucyate, tatrāha—smṛtiriti | rajatasmṛteranyadeva rajate ‘smṛtiriyam’ iti smṛtitvagrāhi ‘smarāmi’ iti jñānamudāhṛtam | atastadabhāve'pi rajatasmṛtiḥ saṃpūrṇeva; na tathā svaviṣayasya kiñcinna gṛhītam, yena tasyāḥ pramoṣaḥ syāt | evaṃ ca svayameva sā svaviṣayaṃ gṛhyamāṇāt vivinakti | kutaḥ smaryamāṇagṛhyamāṇayoravivekaḥ ? yato bhrāntiḥ syādityarthaḥ | vṛttau viparyayaśabdau bhrāntimātraparau |
[p. 277]
athoktadoṣaparijihīrṣayā smṛtiḥ svaviṣayavivecanasvabhāvā na bhavatītyucyate, tadānyeṣāṃ sthāṇupuruṣādīnāṃ smṛtāvūdhvetāsāmānyadarśane ca smṛtidarśanayorapyavivecakatvātsadā bhramaḥ syāt | na caitat, saṃśayasyāpi darśanāt | etacca 'anyathā tu' syāt ityatroktam | kiṃca 'smarāmi' ityapi smṛtijñānaṃ pramāṇaphalādanubhavāt smṛtaṃ phalaṃ bhinnaṃ viviktaṃ yadi syāt tataḥ sidhyati, na tu tasmādvinā | tasmāt smṛtiḥ smṛtaṃ viviktamevānubhāvayatīti kuto gṛhyamāṇādaviveko yato bhramaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | vṛttau na smarāmīti; 'naiṣa doṣaḥ smarāmīti vivekāccet' iti . grantha P.141-21 cchedaḥ | sa eveti; sa eva 'smarāmi' iti vivekaḥ smṛteḥ svaviṣayāvivecakatve sati na syādityarthaḥ | kiṃca yadi 'smarāmi' iti jñānādvivekaḥ, tadabhāvācca smṛtāvivekādbhramaḥ, tato'yamanayorekaḥ 'smarāmi' ityucchikhyamāne smṛtiviveke sati aparatra 'sa evāyam' iti yaḥ pratyabhijñābhramo dṛśyate sa na syāt | na cātrāpi 'smarāmi' iti jñānena smṛtaṃ vivecitemeva | pratyakṣagrāhyasya tu purovartinaḥ smṛtādvivekābhāvāt bhrama iti | yataḥ smṛtasya cetpratyakṣādvivekaḥ, tataḥ pratyakṣasyāpi smṛtādvivekaḥ siddhaḥ | na hyaviviktāt vivekajñānasaṃbhavaḥ | kiṃcaiva sati smṛtādāvāvivekāt smṛtibhramo'yaṃ syāt | pratyakṣabhramastvayaṃ prasiddhaḥ | tasmānna 'smarāmi' ityucchetsvapramoṣādavivekaḥ | athāsaṃnihito rajatādyarthaḥ saṃnidhimattayā purovartitvena vedyaḥ sannavivikta ityucyate, evaṃ satyasaṃnihitatayā darśayantyā viviktasmṛterviparītārthatā syāt | ataśca viparītārthakhyātiḥ syāt, nākhyātiḥ | viparīto'rtho viṣayo yasyā iti vigrahaḥ | atha nāsaṃnihitasya saṃnidhimattayā pratītiravivekaḥ; kiṃtu asaṃnidheraparigraha ityucyate, tanna ; yato'saṃnihitaścet so'rthaḥ, kasmāttasyāsaṃnidhirna jñātaḥ? manodoṣāditi cet, evaṃ tarhi yatra svamādijñāne manaso doṣairupaghātastatra bhavatyevam | yatpunaḥ svasthamanasa eva timirādidoṣopaghātāt bahirindriyajñā bhrāntiḥ, tatra manaso doṣairanupaghātānnāsaṃnidheraparijñānam | yasmātkāraṇāt doṣo grahaṇābhāve hetuḥ, sa ca manasastatra nāsti, tasmāttatrāsaṃnidhyaparijñānāsaṃbhavāt bhramo na syāt; asti cāsau | kiṃca atra pakṣe śuktāveva pravṛttiḥ, nānyatretyapi pravṛtti- 19
[p. 278]
niyamo na syāditi 'asaṃnidhānābodhāccet pravṛttiniyamaḥ kutaḥ' ityaloktam | yadi ca smṛtaṃ sarvātmanā na smṛtam, śuktirapi sarvātmanā na gṛhītetyetāvanmātreṇa mithyātvaṃ syāt, tataḥ sarvāṇyeva jñānāni mithyā syuḥ | na hi kaścidarthaḥ kenacit jñānena sarvātmanā gṛhyate | kiṃca P.142-5 yadi vivekāgrahaṇamālaṃ bhramaḥ syāt, tadā āptavākyānumānādinā pramāṇā ntareṇa ca 'ekaśrandro nānekaḥ, prācīyaṃ dik nodīcī' iti jñātavivekasya puṃso dvicandradibbohādiviparyayān anuvṛttimato'nuvartamānān paśyan svasthātmā svasthāntaḥkaraṇaḥ ko'pahnute? satyapi viveke dvicandrādibhramo'nuvartate | tasmānna vivekāgrahaṇaṃ bhrama ityuktaṃ bhavati | itaśrākhyātipakṣo durghaṭa ityāha—prasakteti | yadi śuktau 'rajatam' iti jñānaṃ syāt, tatastena rajatasya prāptau satyāṃ prasaktapratiṣedhātmā prasaktapratiṣedharūpā 'nedaṃ rajatam' iti khyātijñānaṃ prakalpate ghaṭate | na smṛtadṛśyayorvivekāgrahamātre bhramaḥ | yataḥ prāptaḥ pratiṣedhyate | na cāvivekāgrahaḥ prāpakaḥ, sa hi vivekagrahaṇābhāvaḥ, na cābhāvaḥ prāpakaḥ, abhāvatvādeva | kiṃ nāma prāpakamityapekṣāyāmāha—prāpiketi | śukti rajatatayā rajataṃ vā purovartitayā yā darśayati sā viparītārthā dhīḥ prāpikā | nanu vivekāgrahaṇapakṣe'pi rajatasmṛtiḥ rajatasya nayanasaṃyukte vastuni 'idam' iti jñānaṃ purovartinaḥ prāpakamastyeva; atastābhyāṃ prāpitayoḥ rajatedamaṃśayorayaṃ pratiṣedho bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkyāha—ye ceti | rajate nayanasaṃyukte ca śuktivastuni smṛtigṛhītirūpe khyātī jñāne, tābhyāmupanītasyārthasya nāyaṃ 'nedaṃ rajatam' iti pratiṣedho'vagamyate | apitu śuktau rajatatvasya, rajatasya vā purovartitvasyaiva pratiṣedhaḥ | na cāprāptasyāsau saṃbhavati | na cāgrahaṇamabhāvatvātprāpakam | tasmācchuktau rajatatvasya rajatasya vā purovartitvasya prāpikā viparītakhyātirupāsanīyā | na ca smṛtadṛśyayorvivekāgrahaṇameva 'nedaṃ rajatam' ityeṣā pratītirapabādhate; tathā santi sarvāsāṃ ghaṭādipratītīnāmagrahaṇabādhena janman utpatteḥ sarvaiva ghaṭādipratītirīdṛśī prasaktapratiṣedharūpā syāt, na caivam | kiṃca 'nedaṃ rajatam' iti rajatamatra niṣedhyaṃ bhāti, nāgrahaṇam | ataḥ pratītiviruddhaśrāyaṃ pakṣaḥ | na ca
[p. 279]
smṛtipratyakṣayoḥ 'idaṃ rajatam' iti, vivekajñānamidaṃ 'nedaṃ rajatam' iti ; na tu prasaktapratiṣedhadhīreṣāḥ ato na pūrvoktadoṣatrasaṅga iti vācyam | evaṃ hi dhārmyādbudvāṇaḥ svāṃ pratītimapahute | yasmādaśrāntasya śuktirajatayoḥ P.142-14 krame sati rajatamupalabhya paścācchuktitāṃ viviktāṃ paśyato yogapadye vā yugapacchuktirajate vivikte paśyato yā vivekamatirbhavati sā 'nedam' itīdṛśī na bhavati ; apitu vivikte ete īdṛśī, iyaṃ tu 'nedam' itīdṛśī jāyeta ityasau pratipadyate ; tathāpyapahute | na caitatpratītyanusāriṇo'nurūpamiti bhāvaḥ | śuktirajatayoraviviktayorgrahaṇe syādīdṛśīti cet, idamasmākamapītyāha—prāpteriti | śuktirajatayoraiveyasyābhedasya prāpikāyām 'idaṃ rajatam' iti viparītasaṃvittau satyāṃ rajatasya purovartitvena prāpteḥ prasaktapratiṣedhātmā 'nedam' iti pratītiḥ syāt ; na tu tayorbhedasyāgrahaṇe, tasyābhāvarūpasyāprāpakatvena prāptyabhāvādityarthaḥ | itaśca vivekabuddhiḥ pratiṣedhātmikā na bhavatītyāha—rajatasyeti, karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | rajataṃ smaran cakṣuḥsaṃyuktasya ca śuktivastunaḥ śuktamāsvarādirūpasāmānyaṃ dṛṣṭvā viparyayānudaye'pi smṛtādrajatācchukterviviktatāṃ cakṣuṣo'prasādāt prathamamabuddhā paścāccakṣuṣaḥ prasādādvaiśeṣikarūpajñāne sati 'śuktiriyam' iti rajatācchuktiṃ vividhan 'nedaṃ rajatam' ityevamanyaniṣedhena na pratyeti, śuktirajatayoravivekagrahaṇābhāvāt, aprasaktasya ca pratiṣedhānupapatteḥ | ataśca tatra yathā vivekajñānamapratiṣedhātmakam, evaṃ ca abhe'pi syāt, na caivam | nanu sāmānyadarśane yatrāpi viparyayo na bhavati tatrāpi saṃśayaḥ syāt ; tadapanodakaṃ ca nirṇayajñānamekakoṭiniṣedhātmakameva ; ataḥ kathamucyate—'vividhatraivaṃ pratyeti' iti, ityāśaṅkyāha—sāmānyeti | syādevaṃ yadi niyogato niyamena sāmānyadarśane saṃśaya eva syāt | sa tu koṭidvayasmaraṇotthaḥ ; tadabhāve kacinna bhavatyapi | tena tadabhiprāyeṇāsmābhiridamuktam—'vividhatraivaṃ pratyeti' iti | nanu sāmānyadarśane yadi saṃśayo na bhavati, viparyayastarhi syāt | tadapavādhakaṃ ca jñānaṃ niṣedhātmakameva | ataḥ kathamucyate—'vaivaṃ pratyeti' iti ityāśaṅkya vṛttāvuktam—na ca
[p. 280]
P.143-20 viparyaya iti | ‘bhavati’ ityādinā ‘na caikatvam’ ityantena viparyayābhāva eva darśitaḥ | yattu ‘nānyatvam’ ityuktam, tadviviktatāmabuddhetyasya viṣayapradarśanārthaṃ prasaṅgāduktamiti mantavyam | tasmādyatra sāmānyadarśane saṃśayaviparyayayau na staḥ, tatra vivekajñānamātram | na ca tatprasaktapratiṣedhātmakam, śuktirajatayorekatvāpratīteḥ, śuktau rajatasya prasaktyabhāvāt | vivekagrahaṇābhāvāditi | smṛtasya ca rajatasya vartamāne kāle kālato vartamānatve ; kālagrahaṇamupalakṣaṇam, deśataśca ; saṃnidheḥ saṃvittitaḥ rajatasya purovartitvat purovartito vā rajatavadavabhāsāt rajatasya prāptau pratiṣedhopapattaye'bhyupetāyāmasaṃnihitasya saṃnidhipratītyabhyupagamādanyathāgraho viparītā khyātiriṣṭā syāt | yadi paramasaṃnihitatvasya śuktitvasya vā agrahastatrānyathāgrahe nimittaṃ syāt, tasminsati bhāvāt tadabhāve cābhāvāt, etacceṣṭameva na iti bhāvaḥ | athavā nāsaṃnidhyagraho nimittam, abhāvasyānimittatvāt ; apitu saṃnidhigraha eva bhāvarūpatvānnimittam | krīdṛśo'sau nimittamityapekṣāyāmāha—bhedeti | saṃnidhigrahasya hi ‘na rajataṃ saṃnihitam, apitu śuktiḥ’ iti bhedasya vivekasya graho'pavādaḥ ; sa yāvaccāpatati tāvattena rahitaḥ saṃnidhigraho'nyathāgrahasya nimittam ; nāpavādenāpoditaḥ | kiṃca viparyayasya viparītakhyāteranabhyupagame, vivekāgraha eva amaḥ ; so'grahanibandhana iti bruvāṇena agraho'grahanibandhana uktaḥ syāt | etacca śabdaikagranthaṃ, na tu vastudūtenaikasyaiva nimittanaimittikatvabhedo'stīti bhāvaḥ | vṛttau suvyāhatamityupālambhavacanam | kiṃca smaryamāṇagṛhyamāṇayorvivekāgrahaśret amaḥ, tataḥ svapne gṛhyamāṇasya kasyacit dvitīyasyābhāvāt na smaryamāṇasyāviveko vivekāgraho vā amaḥ syāt | atha gṛhyamāṇābhāve'pi smṛtaṃ manodoṣāt smṛtatvenāviviktaṃ sat gṛhyamāṇaṃ svapne bhāti | na caivamanyathāsyātiprasaṅgadoṣaḥ, yato grahaṇaviśeṣa eva gṛhītagrahaṇaṃ smṛtirnānyā | ato gṛhītāṃśatvapramoṣe gṛhītiravatiṣṭhate | tena svapne tadviṣayasya gṛhyamāṇatvenāvabhāmo mithyā | taduktam, “grahaṇasya viśeṣo hi gṛhītagrahaṇaṃ smṛtiḥ | so'gṛhītāṃśamoṣeṇa gṛhītiravatiṣṭhate ||” iti |
[p. 281]
tatra ca smṛtatvena vivekajñānaṃ bādhakamiti cedataāha—smṛtatveneti | P. 144-5. smṛtatvenāviveke'bhyupagamyamāne yathā smṛtāvajñātasya smṛtatvadharmasya jñānaṃ bādhakaṃ tattvadharmasya jñānaṃ vā, yathā ca tatra smṛtatvarahitaṃ taddharmamātraṃ gṛhṇatī smṛtirbhramaḥ, tathā dūrādanirūpitarūpaviśeṣe dharmiṇi devadattādau jñāte punaḥ samīpe 'gauro'yam' iti dharmāntarapratītebādhikatvaṃ pūrvasyāśca dharmimātrapratīterbhrāntitvaṃ syāt | kiṃca smṛtiḥ parokṣārthagrāhiṇī; sā gṛhītāṃśamoṣe'pi parokṣārthagṛhītirūpeṇāvatiṣṭheta | tvame cāparokṣārthavadavabhāsastava na syāt | na cāgṛhītirityeva sarvāgṛhītiraparokṣārthā, anumānādau vyabhicārāt | tasmādasadetat | itiḥ ślokārthasamāptau | yaccoktaṃ rajataṃ pratīyate, śuktirviṣaya iti pratītiviruddhamiti; sa tvadoṣa evāsajjitaḥ; yataḥ 'sitamidaṃ purovartidravyaṃ rajatam' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena bodhādanayorabhedapratītirasti; sā vivekāgrahavādino virudhyata ityāha—ya iti | agre śuktitvasyājñānāt śuklamātrasyedamaṃśasya jñānāt 'sitam' ityuktam | tatra yadyapyekamidamabhedagrāhi sāmānādhikaraṇyajñānaṃ pratibhāti, tathāpyanyasyānyabhāvānupapattyā smṛtigṛhītirūpe bhinne ime jñāne iti karaṇyiṣyāma ityāśaṅkyāha—jñāneti | sāmānādhikaraṇyapratītisiddhaṃ cet, anyasyānyathābhāve ko virodhaḥ? na hi pratītisiddhe virodho'sti; apratītinibandhanatvāttasyeti bhāvaḥ | nanu nedaṃ rajatajñānamindriyajam, asaṃnihitenendriyasaṃnikarṣābhāvāt; nāpi liṅgādijam, liṅgāderabhāvāt; asti tu pūrvānubhavajasaṃskāraḥ pratītihetuḥ | tasmātsmṛteḥ kāraṇasadbhāvāt smṛtireva rajatajñānamiti pratīmaḥ | maivaṃ vādīḥ | indriyajamevedaṃ rajatajñānam | nanu rajatendriyasaṃnikarṣo nāsti, asaṃnidhānāt | bāḍham | kathaṃ tarhi jñānamanindriyajam? śuktīndriyasaṃnikarṣāt | ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ— yadaduṣṭamindriyaṃ tattathā, duṣṭastvanyathāvabhāsayati doṣasāmarthyādityadoṣaḥ | idānīmanāśvāsācceti yaduktaṃ tatpariharati—anāśvāsa iti | ekatrānyathātvadarśanāt sarvatra jñāyamāne'rthaṃ tathātvaṃ pratyanāśvāso yaḥ, sa tajjñānenaiva tathātvaparicchedakena bādhyate | tathātvaparicchedakaṃ jñānamaniruddhānyathātvāśaṅkaṃ notthānamarhatītyuktaṃ bhavati | tathāhi—anāśvāsanivṛttyarthamapi
[p. 282]
yadayaṃ paraṃ jñānamapekṣate, tadapyanāśvāso'petaṃ tathātvaparicchedakameva | asti ca tādṛśaṃ jñānam | atastasya ko'parādhaḥ? yenānyanmṛgyate | nanu parasyānyathākhyātimanicchato'nāśvāso nāstyeva; ataḥ kathaṃ taṃ pratīdamuktam? tasyāpyekatra vivekāgrahāt sarvatra vivekāgrahaśaṅkayā anāśvāsastukhya iti bhāvaḥ | athaikatra vyabhicārayogāt sarvatrārthaparicchedaṃ pratyasamarthaṃ jñānamapramāṇaṃ P.144-13 syāt, tathā sati cānāśvāsamapi na nirudhyādityucyate, tatrāha—vyabhicārāditi | vyabhicārādarthaparicchede pramāṇasya nāsāmarthyam, arthaparicchedasya tatkāryasya lābhataḥ pramāṇasya paricchedaphalatvādeva siddhatvāt | ataśca paricchedaścedasti, tataḥ satyapi vyabhicāre prāmāṇyaṃ siddham; yataḥ paricchedāparicchedābhyāṃ prāmāṇyāprāmāṇye; na tu vyabhicārāvyabhicārābhyām, prāmāṇyāprāmāṇye prati tayoratantratvāt; tathāhi—dhūmāderavyabhicārasyāpi saṃbandhasmaraṇādivaikalyātkadācitparicchedamakurvato'sati paricchede na prāmāṇyam; cakṣuṣaśca kṛṣṇādiviṣaye vyabhicāravato'pi sati paricchede prāmāṇyaṃ dṛśyate | kiṃca bādhakāraṇadoṣāveva sarvatra prāmāṇyopadhātinau dṛṣṭau, na vyabhicāraḥ, satyapi vyabhicāre cakṣuṣaḥ pramāṇatvāt; uktaṃ ca ‘yatra ca dṛṣṭaṃ karaṇam’ ityādi | nanu—vyabhicārādapyaprāmāṇyaṃ dṛṣṭam; yathā śabdasya nityānityatvayoḥ sādhyayoḥ sādhāraṇānaikāntikasya prameyatvādeḥ | maivam, na tatra vyabhicārādaprāmāṇyam; api tu paricchedākhyaṃ kāryameva tatra na labhyate, liṅgābhāvāt | yasmādavyabhicāri liṅgaṃ bhavati; prameyatvādi ca na tathā | yatra tu cakṣurādāvavyabhicāraḥ paricchede na kāraṇam, tatra sati paricchede prāmāṇyaṃ vyabhicāre'pyavivādam | jñānaṃ cakṣurādivadeva vyabhicāryapi paricchede nimittam, na tu liṅgavadavyabhicāri | athocyate—vyabhicāraḥ ‘arthastathā, anyathā vā’ iti saṃśayamutpādayan prāmāṇyamupahanti; yathā ūrdhvatvasya sthāṇupuruṣadvayagatatvena dvaividhyaṃ saṃśayahetuḥ, tathā jñānasyāpi tathātvānyathātvobhayakoṭisaṃsparśitayā dvaividhyaṃ saṃśayahetuḥ syāditi | tanna ; yato jñānamutpattyārthaṃ ‘tathaiva’ iti paricchinatti; tannāsato dvaividhyasya kutaḥ saṃśayahetutvam? na hi sthāṇau paricchinne
[p. 283]
dvaividhyādūrdhvastre saṃśerate | atha 'tathaiva' ityavadhārake sati mā bhavatu P.145-6. saṃśayaḥ ; anavadhārake tu syādityucyate | tadayuktam ; yatastadapramāṇameva ; pramāṇaṃ cātra kvaciddhyabhicārāt 'pramāṇam', apramāṇaṃ vā' iti vicāryamāṇatayādhikṛtam | tatra kuto'pramāṇasya prasaṅgaḥ | pramāṇācca 'tathaiva' ityavadhāraṇam | na ca tataḥ 'anyathā vā' ityanavadhāraṇaṃ yujyate, vipratiṣedhāt | tadevamasmākaṃ tāvadanāśvāso nāsti ; pratyuta ya eva sarvajñānānāmavyabhicāramāśvāsanārthaṃ prārthayate, tasyaivānāśvāsaḥ | nanu jñānasyāvyabhicāritvajñānaṃ yat, satyameva tat ; ataḥ kathamanyasyānāśvāsaḥ ? maivam, sarvajñānānāmavyabhicāritvagrāhiṇāpi jñānenāśvāsāt | nanvavyabhicāragrāhi jñānaṃ sāmānyena sarvajñānānāmavyabhicāraṃ gṛhṇāti ; tataśca svayamapyavyabhicāri sarveṣāmavyabhicāraṃ gṛhṇāti ; tataśca svayayadi tat svayaṃ siddhāvyabhicāraṃ syāt, tataḥ svasya jñānāntarāṇāṃ cāvyabhicāraṃ gṛhṇāti ; tataśca svayaṃ siddhāvyabhicāre tasmin anyāvyabhicāranirṇayaḥ | tannirṇaye ca tasya siddhāvyabhicāritvamitītaretarāśrayaṃ syāt | atha jñānāntarāt tasyāvyabhicārasiddhiḥ, tatastatrāpyevamityanavasthā | kiṃca jñānasyārthaparicchedakatvalakṣaṇāt svarūpādyadi nāśvāsaḥ, tato'rthasyaivāsiddhatvānna tena saha jñānasyāvyabhicāro gṛhyata | tasmāccayāpi jñānarūpādevārthāśvāsa iti vācyam | evaṃ cedasmākamapi tathā bhaviṣyati | kiṃca jñānasya yathābhūtārthaparicchedakatvaṃ nāma yat svarūpaṃ tadviparītarūpātāvayathārthaparicchedakatvādvirudhyata ityevaṃ jñānasvarūpādeva jñānasyāvyabhicāraḥ kathyate | tataścāvyabhicārārthamapi cet jñānasvarūpamāśrayitavyam, tathā sati tata evārthasiddhirbhaviṣyati ; tataḥ kuto nāśvāsaḥ ? yena tannirṇaye ca tataśca svayaṃ siddhāvyabhicāraḥ prārthayate | na ca vācyaṃ vyabhicāre sati prāmāṇyameva na syāditi, yato vyabhicāraḥ prāmāṇyaṃ nopahantītyuktamityarthaḥ | kiṃca udvekasya kāryādhikyasya kāraṇadoṣāstimirādayo na hetavaḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? kāraṇasya kārye yatsāmarthyaṃ tasya dhātinaḥ | atasteṣāṃ saccāt kāraṇasya grahaṇākhyakāryasāmarthyapratighātādagrahaḥ | asaccācca kāryasāmarthyāpratighātāt graha ityevaṃ doṣasadasaccaṃ smṛtahṛdayayovivekāgrahaṇe ame 'atra
[p. 284]
vivekāgrahaḥ, atra na' iti vivekasya nirṇayasya yatkāraṇaṃ parasyeṣṭaṃ tadviparyayadarśane'pi agre na daṇḍairnivāryate | ato viparyayadarśane pramāṇatadā- P.145-16 bhāsavivekānnābhāsavyabhicāreṇa pramāṇe'pyanāśvāsa ityabhiprāyeṇāha—api ceti | atra hetumāha—viparyaya iti | yataḥ kāryasāmarthyapratighātino doṣā iti yaduktaṃ tadviparyaye agre'pi sutarāṃ prakalpate ghaṭate | kathamityapekṣāyāmāha—dṛṣṭeni | agrahaṇe agre doṣebhya iṣṭasya grahaṇākhyakāryasya nirodhamātraṃ bhavati | viparyayalakṣaṇe tu agre na kevalaṃ tattvagrahaṇasya doṣebhyo nirodhaḥ ; apitu tatpratipakṣaviparītajñānodayaśca bhavati | tena tattvagrahaṇākhyamiṣṭaṃ kāryasvarūpataḥ pratipakṣotthāpanena ca doṣairatyantaṃ niruddhamityatiśayārthaḥ | tasmādagrahaṇe agre doṣāṇāṃ sattvādagrahaḥ ; doṣāsattvācca graha iti grahāgrahaviveko yaḥ, sa viparyayalakṣaṇe'pi agre ghaṭata ityāha—tasmāditi | yatra doṣo'san, tatrāduṣṭādindriyāt tattvagraha ityāśaṅkānivṛttirityuktam ; taddūṣayati—doṣa iti | atra hetumāha— na hīti | yadi kāraṇasadbhāve kāryasattā niyogato niyogena syāt, tato nirdoṣendriyasadbhāve tattvagrahākhyakāryabhāvādagrahāśaṅkā nivarteta, na tvetadasti | tathāhi—satyapi nirdoṣe cakṣuṣi vistite jñānānudayo dṛśyate | na ca cakṣurmātraṃ kārakam, apitu sāmagryapi ; sā ca tatra nāstīti kāryānudaya iti vācyam | yataḥ pratyekavartinī kārakaśaktiḥ, na samāhāravartinī | kārakāṇāṃ hi kāryasiddhye samāsaḥ, na militānāṃ karaṇatvam | tasmāccakṣurapi kāraṇam | na ca tasminnaduṣṭe'pi kāryasiddhiriti sūktam—‘na hi kāraṇasadbhāve’ iti | ato na parasya grahāgrahavivekasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | kiṃca evaṃ sati viparyayaḥ syādityāha—pratyakṣamiti | pratyakṣayā hi saṃvityā kāraṇamanumīyata iti yuktam ; parasya tu pratyakṣaṃ saṃvittilakṣaṇaṃ kāryamaduṣṭena karaṇenānumīyata iti viparyayaḥ | anyathākhyātau tu doṣasya hetorabhāvāt kāryasyābhāvo yujyate | atastatra doṣābhāvāt viparyayadarśanākhyaphalābhāvānumānaṃ yuktamityāha—viparyaya iti | tathā viparyayalakṣaṇāt phalāddhetoliṅgāt taddhetudoṣasaṃbhavānumānamapi yuktamityapi draṣṭavyam | ato'smākaṃ doṣābhāvabhāvābhyāṃ pramāṇatadābhāsavivekasiddhiḥ, na parasyeti bhāvaḥ |
[p. 285]
anyathākhyātāvanālambanaṃ syāditi parokto doṣaḥ parasyāpyastītyāha—anālambaneti | yathā anyathākhyātau śukterākārādākārāntarasya P.146-10 rajatasya samarpaṇāt jñānasyānālambanatāpattiḥ, evamakhyātivādinastavāgrahaṇe'pi agre śuktisrarūpasya viśiṣṭasya 'idam' iti jñāne'narpaṇāt anālambanatāpattirbhavedityarthaḥ | atha tatra viśeṣānarpaṇe'pi 'idam' iti jñānena śuktamāsvaratvādisāmānyāṃśasya kasyacidarpaṇamasti ; sa ca śukteḥ svākāra iti tasmin gṛhyamāṇe śuktirevālambanamāpanneti 'viśeṣāṃśāgrahe'pi na nirālambanatāpattirityucyate, tadanyathākhyātāvapi tulyam, yatastatrāpīdamaṃśakhyātirastyevetyāha—kasyaciditi | anyā vyākhyā—atha kasyacidanyasya ghaṭādervastunaḥ svena rūpeṇa grahaṇamastīti kasyacit śuktivastunaḥ śuktirūpeṇānarpaṇe'pi na nirālambanamityāśaṅkyāha—kasyaciditi | vikalpamātrametat | svākāratvādityasyānantaraṃ 'nānālambanatvam' iti yojyam | keṣāṃcitsvākārāṇāmiti śuktamāsvaratvādīnāmiti prathamavyākhyāyāṃ tulyatvahetuḥ | keṣāṃcit ghaṭādivastūnāmavabhāsanāditi ca dvitīyavyākhyāyāṃ tulyatvaheturūhyaḥ | yadvā keṣāṃciditi ghaṭādivastūnāṃ svākārāṇāmiti ca śuktamāsvarādīnāmavabhāsanāditi vyākhyādvaye hetudvayamuktam | atha tatra vivekāgrahaṇe taccakṣuḥsaṃyuktaṃ na tathā ālambanamiṣyata ityucyate, parasyāpyanyathākhyātivādinaḥ tatrānyathākhyātau evaṃ cakṣuḥsaṃyuktasya nālambanatvamiṣṭam | atha tatrānyathākhyātau tathā sthiteḥ nirālambanasya sthitatvāt sarvatra samyagjñāneṣvapi tathābhāvo nirālambanatā syāditi śaṅkyetetyucyate, tatastvāpi viviktaśaktyagrahaṇe nirālambanatā dṛṣṭeti sarvatra śaṅkyeta etadyathetyādinā viṣṭatam | tatra viparyayadarśane'nimittatvānnirālambanatvādyathā samyagjñāne'nimittatā nirālambanatā śaṅkyete, tathā śaktyagrahe sā nirālambanatā nirūpitā sarvatra ghaṭādigrahe'pi śaṅkyete, atastatvāpi sarvatrānāśvāsastulya iti bhāvaḥ | atha ghaṭādijñāne jñānādevārthaparicchedakādarthasiddherarthasya nimittatvamālambanatvam, etadanyeṣāmapyanyathākhyātivādināṃ na viśiṣyata ityarthaḥ | kiṃca rajatakhyātirapi śuktimevālambate, saiva hi rajatātmanā gṛhyate | na ānyathālambyamāne'rthe jñānasya 20
[p. 286]
nirālambanatvaṃ bhavati | evaṃ ca sati hetumātrasyānālambanatvāt cakṣurādivaditi yaduktaṃ tadapi pratyuktam | na vayaṃ hetumātramālambanaṃ brūmaḥ, P.147-11 apitu yattathā anyathā vā ālambyate | cakṣurastanyathāpi nālambyata iti na bhrānterālambanam | itaśca śuktiranyathālambyate, yato na yadākāraṃ yat jñānaṃ tattasyālambanam, jñānasya nirākāratvāt | apitu yasmin jñāne sati yat yathā vyavahārayogyaṃ bhavati, tasya tadālambanam | śuktiśca rajatajñāne rajatatayā vyavahārayogyā bhavati, na śuktitayā; tathāca rajatabuddhyaiva bhrāntastathā vyavaharani, na śuktibuddhyā | tasmānna śuktiḥ svarūpeṇa rajatajñānasya lambanam, apitu rajatatayeti sthitam | vṛttau 'rajatarūpeṇa' ityatra ālambitetyanuvartate | ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’. iti vidhau jñānakriyāniṣṭhe sati ātmasvarūpaparatvābhāvāt apahatapāpmānandāderātmasvarūpasyāsiddhimāśaṅkya tatsiddhye kaścidāha—avaghāteti | “brīhīnavahanti” “brīhīnprokṣati” ityādau dṛṣṭādṛṣṭasaṃskārayorbrīhyarthatā yathā sthitā, brīhīniti dvitīyayā cepsitatamatvena prādhānyāvagamāt, tathā jñānaṃ svabhāvena “ātmā vāre draṣṭavyaḥ” iti cātmanaḥ karmasvena nirdeśāt jñeyabhūtātmadravyārtham | ato jñānaviṣayo'pyātmajñānavidhiḥ ātmasvarūpamānandādi spṛśati; na jñānamātre paryavasyati; yasmādātmasvarūpadaimarthyenaiva jñānasya kartavyatā niyogādavagamyate | ato niyogenātmasvarūpamapyānandādyākṣiptamiti tasya siddhiriti bhāvaḥ | atra paraḥ śaṅkayati—bhavatviti | bhavatu jñānasyārthatā, tathāpyapahatapāpmānandādiviśiṣṭasyābhipretasya siddhiḥ kutaḥ pramāṇāt? na tāvattatra ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ iti śabdaḥ pramāṇam | sa hi ‘apahatapāpmādirūpa ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ ityātmārthatayā jñānasya kāryatve paryavastitaḥ; ‘evaṃ rūpa ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ iti hi tadā codyate, na ‘evaṃrūpa ātmā’ ityātmasvarūpaniṣṭhā; yatparaśca śabdaḥ sa śabdārthaḥ | tasmācchabdastāvadapahatapāpmādyātmasvarūpasiddhau na pramāṇam | na ca tatra pramāṇāntaraṃ prakramate | tasmādevamapi nātmasvarūpasiddhirityāśayaḥ | evamāśaṅkyāha—jñāneti |
[p. 287]
jñeyārthatvena jñānaṃ kartavyatayā ceditam ; na ca tatrākiṃcitkarasya tādṛśyena kartavyatāsiddhiryujyate | tasmāt jñānena jñeye kaścidarthaḥ kartavyaḥ | sa ca jñeyabhūtātmasvarūpaprakāśanāt ko'nyaḥ ? apitu tadeva | na hi P.148-11 tasya dṛṣṭasya saṃbhave satyadṛṣṭakalpanecitā | tasmādyathā prokṣaṇādividhetrīṃścārthaprokṣaṇaviṣayādapi trīhārvākiṃcitkarasya prokṣaṇasya tādṛśyānupapattyā trīhiṣvadṛṣṭā saṃskṛtirgamyate, tathā jñānavidherātmārthajñānaviṣayādapyātmano'pahatapāpmādi rūpaṃ gamyata ityarthaḥ | yathā svargakāma | ityāderchattigranthasyāyamarthaḥ—yaḥ khalu yatkāmaḥ, sa tadupāyaṃ kartavyatā pratyeti, anutiṣṭhati ca ; nānupāyamityanubhavasiddham | anupāyo hi tatkāmena nānuṣṭhitaḥ syāt, anyakāmasyānyatrānuṣṭhānādarśanāt | ataśca svargānupāye yāge niyogānuṣṭhāne svargakāmasya na bhavataḥ | tasmātsvargayāgayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvo yathā yāgaviṣayādapi vidheravagamyate, tathā apahatapāpmādirūpamātmānamuddiśya jñānaniyogo na bujyate yadi tathāvidha ātmā jñānena na pratīyata iti jñānaviṣayādapi vidherātmasvarūpaṃ bodhyate | tadetastiddhāntī dūṣayati—ukteti | satyamevamātmā jñānena pratīyata iti siddhyati, na tu svarūpeṇeti ; yasmātpararūpeṇāpi jñānamarthasya bhāsakamanantaramevānyathājyātipratiṣṭhāpanena pradarśitam | tatra kiṃ svarūpeṇātmā jñānena pratīyate, pararūpeṇa veti śabdānna niścayaḥ syāt | na cātra pramāṇāntaramasti, yato niścayaḥ syāt | avaśyaṃ caivamabhyupeyama—yadātmano'nyathāpratītirastīti ; anyathā jñānavidherna kiṃcitkalaṃ syāt | sa hi niṣprapañco'pyātmā śabdātmakaprapañcarūpeṇa prakāśata iti tadavabodhanena taccapratipattyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭā śvasvarūpabhūtarajatāvabodhanena śuktitvapratīti | ataśca niṣprapañcasyātmanaḥ prapañcātmanā pratītirastīti nāvaśyamātmano'pahatapāpmādirūpeṇa jñeyatayā tadūpasiddhiḥ ; adhyāropeṇāpi “vācaṃ dhenumupāsīta” itivat tadupapatterityuktaṃ bhavati | atha matam—dvividheyamavidyā ; ekā taccājñānātmikā bījabhūtā taccamācchādayantyācchādiketyucyate ; yathā rajjutataccājñānātmikā rajjutaccamaprakāśāyantī | anyā ca tatkāryabhūtā rajjumeva sarpadaṇḍādirūpeṇa ātmā- 20A
[p. 288]
namaprapañcaṃ prapañcarūpeṇa vistārayantī vikṣepikatyucyate | tatrādyā bījamātrarūpeṇa suṣupte tiṣṭhati | dvitīyā ca svapnajāgaritayoḥ prapañcātmanā | tatra P.148-24 yadyapi vikṣepikāyā nivṛttirnātmajñānavidheḥ phalam, tathāpyācchādikāyā nivṛttiḥ phalaṃ bhaviṣyatītyato na jñānavidhernipphalatvamityāśaṅkyāha—laya iti | sāṃsārikavividhaduḥkhanivṛttaye prekṣāvato jñānavidhyaye pravṛttiḥ | vikṣepaśca prapañcātmakaḥ svapnajāgaritayoḥ śokādiduḥkhahetuḥ, na laya ācchādikā avidyā | na hyasau suṣuptagatā duḥkhahetuḥ | duḥkhahetuśca nivartyaḥ | ato na layastathā nivartyo yathā vikṣepaḥ | sa hyaduḥkho duḥkhaheturna layaḥ | sa tu duḥkhātmakatvāt na kevalaṃ na nivartyaḥ, pratyuta prārthanīyaḥ | tathāca suṣupte vividhaduḥkhanivṛtterlaya ānandavāsanākṛta śrutau śrutaḥ | apica avidyādvaividhyamapi prasiddhamakhyātimātrabhramavādinastava na syāditi dūṣaṇāntaramāha—layeti | layaḥ taccāgrahaṇam, vikṣepo viparyayadarśanam; tayorbhedo na syādagrahamātre bhrame | tasmādagrahaṇaviparyayagrahaṇalakṣaṇe dve avidye abhyupetavye | te ca pūrvā kāraṇam, uttarā kāryamityeva vyavasthite | tathāca rajjutatvāgrahaṇāt sarpādiviparyayo dṛśyate | evaṃ sati ca pūrvā suṣupte, parā ca svapnajāgaritayorityevamavidyāvibhāgopapattiḥ | tathāca gauḍapādairdarśitam—“kāryakāraṇabaddhau” iti | kāryaṃ vikṣepā tmikā prapañcāvidyā, kāraṇamagrahaṇātmikā avidyā; tābhyāṃ baddhāvubhau viśvataijasaśabdavācyau jāgaritasthānasvapnasthānāvātmānāviṣyete | prājñastu suṣuptasthāna ātmā kāraṇena taccāgrahaṇātmikayā avidyayā baddhaḥ | ubhe tu te avidye turye pūrvoktasthānatrayāpekṣayā turye “caturaśchayatāvādyakṣaralopaśca” iti smaraṇāt caturthe muktisthāne na sidhyata ityarthaḥ | nanu anyathaivāyamagrahaṇāsamyagrahaṇabhedenāvidyābhedo bhaviṣyati, kiṃ viparyayeṇa ? tatrāgrahaṇaṃ suṣupte, jāgaritasvapnayostvasamyagrahaṇamiti vyavasthāsiddhiḥ | nanvasamyagrahaṇāgrahaṇayoḥ kiṃ bhedo'sti ; bāṭam ; tathāhi—abhinnasyaikasya prapañcabhedaiḥ rahitasyātmavastuno'pradeśasya vibhogyagrahaṇamasamyagrahaṇam, tathābhūtasyābhinnasyāpradeśasya vā agrahaṇamagrahaṇam | tadevaṃ grahaṇāgrahaṇa rūpatvāttayoḥ pṛthaktvena pratītiḥ | ato'styanayorbhedaḥ | naitatsāram ; yato
[p. 289]
yadi tāvadviparyajñānādanyadviparyayāṃśaśūnyagrahaṇamātramasamyagrahaṇamiṣyate, taḥ P. 160-7. tkimavyaktāvabhātitvam ? athavā kenacidrūpeṇārthasya grahaṇaṃ, na sarvātmanā ? tatra yadyādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadā dūrasthe sūkṣme cārthe bhavatyavyaktāvabhāttakajñānam; na ca tadavidyā | tathā dvitīye pakṣe tatraiva dūrasthe sthāṇāvūrdhvatāsāmānyamātraṃ jñātaṃ viśeṣajñānānna sarvātmanā jñātamityavidyā syāt; na caivam | kiñcaivaṃ sati sarvameva jñānaṃ na sarvātmanārthasya grāhakamiti sarvajñānānāmasamyajñānatvānmithyātvamāpatet | tasmādasamyagrahaṇamavidyeti yaduktaṃ tattāvadyuktam | yadapyabhinnasyātmano'pradeśasyābhedagrahaṇaṃ dvitīyāvidyoktā; tatrāpi yadyabhedāgrahaṇamātraṃ prapañcagrahaṇarahitamavidyā, tadā tatrāvidyāyā nivartanīyaṃ nāsti | na hi abhedāgrahaṇaṃ nivartyam, yato duḥkhatrayahetutayā yaḥ puruṣasyāparādhyati sa tena nivartyaḥ; na cāgrahaṇaṃ tathā | kiṃtu, prapañcabhedaḥ; sa hi dviviṣaduḥkhāyatanam | tathācānyathākhyātirabhyupetā syāt | atha bhinne bhedena bhātīti na brūmaḥ, yenānyathākhyātiḥ syāt; kiṃtvabhinnamapyātmatattvaṃ prapañcabhedena bhinnamiva bhātīti, tatro'cyate—kimatra bhinnena sahābhinnasya sādṛśyam ? yadivaśabdasyārthaḥ syāt | yadi yathā bhinnamaprakāśamānaikatvaṃ prakāśate, tathātmatattvamabhinnamapyaprakāśamānaikatvaṃ prakāśata itīdaṃ sādṛśyamivārthaḥ, tadaikatvarahitasyātmatattvasya grahaṇāt kenacidaṃśena grahaṇaṃ na sarvātmanetyekalakṣaṇamasamyagrahaṇamuktaṃ syāt; tatra ca na kiñciñcānaṃ sarvātmanārthasya grāhakamiti sarvajñānamithyātvāpāta ityuktam | athābhinnamātmatattvaṃ bhinnaṃ prakāśata iva, na tu prakāśata eva; ato'nyathākhyātiprasaṅga ityucyate, tadāpi bhinnaprakāśena sahābhinnaprakāśasya kiṃ sādṛśyaṃ vācyam | yathā bhinnaprakāśāt bhedavyavahārapravṛttiḥ, tathātmatattvasyābhinnasya bhedavyavahārādidaṃ sādṛśyamiti cet; na, abhinne'pi cedātmani bhedavyavahāro bhavati, asti tarhi tatrāpyātmani bhedaprakāśanam | yato bhedaprakāśo bhedavyavahārasya kāraṇam | na cāsati kāraṇe kāryaṃ bhavati | tataścābhinne'pyātmani bhedajñānodayāt āpannā anyathākhyātiḥ | athābhinnasyātmatattvasyābhedāgrahe'bhimānamātramidam, na tu bhedagrahaḥ; etadeva vyavahārahetuḥ; ato nānyathākhyātiprasaṅga iti ceducyate, tadasat; abhedāgraho hi grahaṇābhāvaḥ | na cāsāvabhāvamaprameyamabhyupagacchatāṃ svarūpeṇa pararūpeṇa vā grāhyaḥ | na cāgṛhīte tasmin bhedagrahābhimāno'vakalpate | na
[p. 290]
brahmahītāyāṃ śuktau rajatagrahābhimāno bhavati | kiñcābhinnasyātmanaḥ prapañcātmanā bhedaśrendra prakāśot, tataḥ kuto bhedagrahaḥ ? nanu na bhedagrahaḥ, kiṃtu tadabhibhāna ityuktam | satyamuktam; kiṃtu bhedagrahādanyo nābhimāno nāma nirūpyate | ato bhedagraha evāsau | sa ca bhedāprakāśane na yukta iti sūktam | kiñca abhinna ātmatattve prapañcātmanā bhedābhimāno viparītajñānamavidyetyasmatpakṣaṃ parityajyābhinnasyātmatattvasyābhedāgrahe bhedagrahābhimānamaṅgī kurvatā viparītakhyātirevāṅgīkṛtā | ataḥ kiṃ tena parihatam ? tasmāt 'layavikṣepabhedaśra na syādagrahamātrake' iti sūktam | vedāntānāṃ jñānavidhiparatve apahatapāpmādirūpamātmano na sidhyati; P.151-1. yato nātmasvarūpeṇāvabhāsakaṃ jñānamityuktam; tatra codayati—nanviti | arūpeṇānātmarūpeṇa apahatapāpmatvādinā ātmano'vabhāse dṛṣṭe prayojanābhāvādadṛṣṭakalpanā syāt | apahatapāpmāderātmarūpasyāvabhāse tatsiddhirnāma dṛṣṭaphalam | yato'nyatrāpi jñeyasvarūpasiddhireva jñānaphalam | na ca dṛṣṭaphale saṃbhavatyadṛṣṭakalpanocitā | tasmāt jñānavidhiparatve'pi vedāntānāmātmano'pahatapāpmādirūpasiddhirityarthaḥ | atrottaramāha—na dṛṣṭamātrāditi | dṛṣṭamātrādhataḥ kutaścidapuruṣārthādapi dṛṣṭānna dṛṣṭārthaṃ jñānaṃ bhavati; kiṃtu puruṣārthena vinā vidheranairākāṅkṣyāt puruṣārthānugaṃ puruṣārthasaṃsparśitat dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ bhavati | na cāpahatapāpmāderātmarūpasyānātmarūpasya vāvabhāse puruṣārthasya bhāvo bhidyate viśiṣyate; ubhayatrāpi puruṣārthaphalatvamaviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ | aviśeṣaṃ prapañcayati—yatheti | yathā prapañcaśūnyatvapakṣe apahatapāpmādiviśuddhādvyātmatattve jñānena prakāśite śokādiduḥkhasya karmaṇāṃ rāgādeśca kleśasya hānaṃ tyāgaḥ, tathāpahatapāpmādāvatattve'pi jñānena prakāśite teṣāṃ hānaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ | taduktam— “tasmādbhūtamabhūtaṃ vā yadyadevātibhāvyate | bhāvanāphalaniṣpattau sphuṭakalpā ca dhīḥ phalam ||” iti | yaśca vṛttau jīvanmuktaḥ sa manorapi saṃmataḥ | tathā cāha— “kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucalamatsahāyatā | samatā caiva bhūteṣu etanmuktasya lakṣaṇam ||” iti |
[p. 291]
na cātra rāgādimukto mukto'bhimataḥ, viśeṣasyānupādānāt | tasmādagṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt sarvabandhamukto'tra mukta ukta iti gamyate | bandhasya hi mūlaṃ kāraṇamavidyā | tasyāṃ ca nirlepamapahatāyāṃ kāraṇanivṛttyaiva bandhasya nivṛtterupapannā viduṣo jīvata eva muktiḥ | yā tu prapañcasya bādhitasyāpyābhāsamātreṇānuvṛttiḥ, tadupapattiḥ pūrvamukteti na punarucyate | atra cāvirbhūtādvayātmaprakāśo jīvanmukto'bhipretaḥ, na vākyārthamātraviditi viśuddhādvayādirūpamātmataccamevātmano jñānena prakāśyata iti | idamevātra yuktam | evaṃ hi dvayagrāhiṇāṃ pratyakṣādīnāṃ virodho na bhavati | karmavidhānaṃ ca sādhyasādhanetikartavyatādibhedaviṣayaṃ satyārthaṃ bhavati | paramārthe hi prapañcaśūnyatve prapañcagrāhīṇi pratyakṣādīni bādhyeran | karmaviṣayaścābhūtaviṣayāḥ syuḥ | tasmāt “vācaṃ dhenumupāsīta” ityādivat abhūtātmādvaitaviṣayajñānavidhirayamiti yujyate | anaupacārikārthā iti; anaupacārikaḥ kalpito'rtho viṣayo yeṣāmiti vigrahaḥ | abhūtasya kalpanā, saivopādānaṃ yasya, sa cāsau vyavahārasiddhaśvāsāvarthaśca, tadvocarā iti vṛttau vigrahaḥ | nanu jānātistaccabodhaviṣayaḥ; ataḥ satyamevādvayādirūpamātmano jñānaviṣaya iti śaṅkayati—jānātiriti | tatrottaramāha—neti | jānātistaccabodhe na vartate | kutaḥ ? mithyājñānaṃ samyagjñānaṃ saṃśayajñānamiti mithyādibhirjñānasya viśeṣaṇāt | anyathā samyagjñānamityatra viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇapadayoḥ paunaruktyaṃ syāt | mithyājñānaṃ saṃśayajñānamityatra virodhaḥ | nanu ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ ityātmajñānaṃ vidhīyate | tatra yadyāropitenātmarūpeṇa viśuddhādvayādīnāṃ jñānaṃ syāt, tathā sati nātmā jñātaḥ syāt | ātmarūpeṇa hi pratīta ātmā jñāto bhavati, nānyathā | tataścādvayādirūpeṇātmajñānavidherātmanastadrūpasiddhirityāśaṅkayati—nātmeti | tatrottaramāha—na tadrūpeti | yadi ‘ātmā jñātavyaḥ’ ityetāvadidaṃ vākyaṃ syāt; tena cātmasvarūpajñānaṃ vidhīyate, tataḥ syādevam; na tvetadasti; ātmanaḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhatve tasyānuvādatvāt, anirjātasvarūpasya jātumaśakyatvāt | ataścātmanaḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhatve, anyathā pramāṇāntarasiddhatve ca sutarāmātmasvarūpasyāvidhānam | yadi “apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ” ityādiśabdatamapitena rūpeṇa tasyātmano jñānaṃ vidhīyate, tadā tenāpahata
[p. 292]
pāpmādirūpeṇa taccenāpyadhyāropitenāpi “vācaṃ dhenumupāsīta” iti vadasyātmano jñānamavakalpate | tena jñānavidherapahatapāpmāderātmasvarūpasya tattvatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | P.152-7. kiṃca na tāvadadvayātmaprakāśalakṣaṇaṃ jñānamadṛṣṭārtham | na hi tena kiñcidadṛṣṭaṃ janyate | tatra yadi dṛṣṭārtham, tatrocyate—jñānamiti | tasyopāyo na jñātaḥ, tenopāyājñānāt dṛṣṭaphale'pi jñāne vidhimantareṇa puruṣo na pravartata iti jñānaṃ vidhīyata ityāśaṅkayati—taditi | atrottaramāha—tasyeti | atha dṛṣṭārthamapi jñānamavadhātādivat vidhāsyata ityāśaṅkayati—avadhāteti | tatrāpi taṇḍulagataniyamādṛṣṭamasti; avadhātādvilakṣaṇastuṣādivimokaḥ, tato vilakṣaṇastaṇḍulāḥ, tato vilakṣaṇaṃ piṣṭam, tato vilakṣaṇaṃ puroḍāśa ityevaṃ praṇāḍyā pradhānāpūrvasiddherevādṛṣṭaṃ tatrāstīti bhāvaḥ | athādvayātmajñānamadṛṣṭapūrvamalaukikam; ato'dṛṣṭārthamapi tadvidhīyata iti, tatrāha—alaukikamiti | kimarthaṃ tadvidhīyate ? na tāvadātmasvarūpasya siddhaye pratipattaye; yato na pratipattimātraphalo vidhiḥ, apitu kartavyatā anuṣṭhānaṃ vidhito bhavati | sā ca dṛṣṭārthaṃ jñāne dṛṣṭādeva phalāt bhojanādivat prasidhyati; kiṃ vidhinā ? ato'laukike apahatapāpmādirūpe ātmatattve'rthaṃ śabdena prathamaṃ parokṣatayā pratipādite tasyāparokṣajñāne tamaparokṣīkartuṃ tasyāparokṣajñānasya jñeyabhūtātmopāyatvāt svayamevānmukhaḥ pumān | tathāhi—kasyacidvākyādāśrayeśrūtaṃ gandharvanagarādi śrutvā tadaparokṣīkaraṇe svayameva pumān unmukho bhavatīti | ataḥ kiṃ vidhineti bhāvaḥ | kva nāma vidhiryujyata ityāha—taditi | upasaṃharati—niyogeti | ato'pahatapāpmādivacasāṃ jñānaniyogānupraveśo bhūte siddharūpe'rthaṃ teṣāṃ pramāṇatāṃ vihanti, atatparatvāt | itihūtau | bhūtasya siddhasyāpahatapāpmāderarthasya gatimicchatā niyogānupraveśo nopāsyo na manasi kārya ityarthaḥ | tadeva prāyuktāsu tisrapu pratipattipu madhye prathamā śābdī pratipattiḥ, tṛtīyā cādvayātmaprakāśarūpā na vidhīyata ityuktam | adhunā upāsanākhyā thā dvitīyā pratipattiḥ, tasyāḥ pratipattervidhirnirasyate—ata pūrveti |
[p. 293]
bhūte'rthaṃ pramāṇābhāvaprasaṅgādityarthaḥ | ‘niṣṭhatā’ ityasyānantaraṃ siddhānāṃ vedāntānāmityadhyāhāryam | ata evetyupajīvitamarthaṃ darśayati—tatrāpīti | P 153-16 pūrvavaditi; yathā tṛtīyavidhiparatve atasparatvādvākyasya nātmasvarūpāvagatisiddhiḥ, tathetyarthaḥ | yadi tvapahatapāpmādivākyaṃ svarūpaniṣṭhameva na niyoganiṣṭham; tena tasmātpramitasyātmasvarūpasya “nididhyāsitavyaḥ” ityādervaco'ntarādupāsanavidhānamiṣyate, tat syādeva; na kaściddoṣaḥ | tathāca “vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīta” ityatra ‘vijñāya’ iti klāpratyayenātmataccajñānasya vedāntajasya prajñākaraṇāt pūrvasiddhatāṃ darśayatītyāha—svarūpeti | tadapi copāsanavidhānaṃ dyathā, vidheraprāptārthatvāt; asya ca dṛṣṭārthatayaiva bhojanādivat prāpterityāha—prāpteriti | dṛṣṭārthatvamāha—abhyāseneti | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yadi svargādivanmuktiradṛṣṭaphalaṃ syāt, tatastatphalānucintanamadṛṣṭārthatvādaprāptaṃ vidhīyeta; kiṃtu svarūpāvirbhāvamātraṃ muktiriti varṇitam | svarūpāvirbhāvaśca śabdātparokṣatayāvagatasyādvyātmanaḥ sākṣādbhāvaḥ | sa cānucintanasya bhojanasyeva tṛptirdṛṣṭaṃ phalam, jñānābhyāsena pratyayaprakarṣadarśanāt | tathāhi—abhūtamapi yadanubhāvyate tatra sākṣādanubhavamāpadyata ityuktam; kiṃ punarbhūtam | tadevaṃ dṛṣṭārthamanucintanam | na ca dṛṣṭārthaṃ dṛṣṭanirākāṅkṣatvādadṛṣṭaṃ phalamākāṅkṣate | tasmādṛṣṭārthatayā prāptārthamanucintanamavidheyamityupasaṃharati—tasmāditi | vṛttau ‘yadapi’ ityādinā pūrvapakṣopanyāsaḥ | ‘taccamasi’ iti taccapadārthayorbrahmajīvayorabhedaṃ dhārayedityācāryaṇokte śiṣyābhiprāyeṇa ‘sadasmi’ ityuttamapuruṣanirdeśaḥ | dhārayediti dhāraṇamanucintanamucyate | “taccamasi” - ityatrāśruto'pi vidhiḥ “pūṣā prapiṣṭhabhāgaḥ” ityatreva kalpyata iti paro manyate | śravaṇāditi; vedāntaśravaṇopalakṣitāt vedāntavākyārthajñānādanyadityarthaḥ | “sa kratuṃ kurvīta” ityādiśrutiṣu kratuprajñāśabdābhyāmanucintanamuktam | dhyāyatinā tayorbhede tulyavannirdeśānupapattirmā bhūditi abhedadarśanārthamanucintanaṃ dhyānamiti paryāyanirdeśaḥ | pratyayapravāha iti ca dhyānavivaraṇam | karmavaditi; yathāgnihotrādi karma svargādiphalaṃ tathetyarthaḥ |
[p. 294]
P. 154-8. bāśropāsanāviṣāyakāḥ śrutaya uktāḥ, tāsu “sa kratuṃ kurvīta” ityeṣā tāvacchrutiḥ sakalaviśeṣanirmuktasya nirupādherātmatattvasyopāsanaṃ nirviśeṣabrahmībhāvaphalaṃ na vidhatte; kiṃtu “manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīro bhārūpaḥ” ityanuvṛttermanomayādiviśeṣopādhikasya sopādherātmano'nucintanamaiśvaryaphalam; aiśvaryamanubhūya paścāt krameṇa vā svarūpāvirbhāvalakṣaṇaṃ muktiphalaṃ vidhatte, na tu sākṣānmuktiphalam | ato na tatra nirviśeṣātmavādinām “apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ” ityādīnām “asthūlamanaṇu” ityādīnāṃ vākyānāmanvayo ghaṭate | nāmarūpe śabdārthāvupādhī yasya manomayādirūpasyātmanaḥ, tasyānudhyānamiti vigrahaḥ | tathā “tameva dhīro vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīta” ityatrāpi ‘raktaḥ paṭo bhavati’ itivat upapadasaṃkrānto vidhyarthaḥ | tena nāsyopāsanāvidhau tātparyam; apitu ‘tameva’ ityanyanivṛttau | tathāhi— tatrākāśāditattvānāṃ bhedaṃ pratipādyātmābhedaḥ pratipāditaḥ | tathāca “virajaḥ para ākāśāt” iti pūrvārthe ākāśātparatvamuktam | tatra tathaiva prajñākaraṇaṃ prāpte ‘tameva’ ityevakāreṇa ‘nākāśātparatvenātmani prajñā kartavyā, apitvākāśamantarbhāvyaiva kevalaḥ’ ityevakārārthatātparyam | yadi tu naiva vyākhyāyate, tadā evakāro'narthakaḥ syāt | na cāsya nivartyāntaramasti | tathāca kevala evātmā dhyeyo nākāśādīti | etadeva “nānudhyāyādbrahmabrahmabrahma vāco viglāpanaṃ hi tat” ityanena prajñākaraṇavākyatāpannena prapañcayate | na ca vācyam—śabdasyānudhyānaniṣedho'yam, nārthasyeti; yataḥ śabdamātrasyānudhyānamaprasaktatvānna niṣedhyam | ataḥ śabdapūrvakatvāt śabdārthānudhyānaniṣedha evāyamiti | tathā “ātmetyevopāsīta” ityayamapyevakāraśravaṇādanyanivṛttiparo vidhiḥ, nopāsanāparaḥ | tathā “nididhyāsitavyaḥ” ityayamapi nopāsanāvidhiḥ; yasmāt “ātmanastu kāmāya” ityupakramāt “idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā” ityupasaṃhārācca ātmasvarūpaparamekamidaṃ vākyam | ato yathā “jāmi vā etatkriyate” ityupakramāt “upāṃśuyājamantarā yajate'jāmitvāya” ityupasaṃhārādekavākyatve sthite, tadantargatānāṃ “viṣṇurūpāṃśu yaṣṭavyaḥ” ityādīnāṃ na pṛthagvidhitvam, evaṃ na kevalaṃ “nididhyāsitavyaḥ” ityādayastadantargatā na pṛthagvidhayaḥ, apitu yasmādīdṛksvarūpo'yamātmā, tasmāt darśanādikamarhatītyevam arhe kṛtya-
[p. 295]
smaraṇāt stutiparaḥ | pṛthagvidhiparatve tu teṣāmupakramopasaṃhārayorekārthānuvṛttāvagatamekatvaṃ hīyetāpi | “tasvamasi” ityatrāśrutavidhau svarūpaniṣṭhe yā vidhikaraṇnā, sāpyadhyāhārādipramāṇābhāvāt svamatikalpitetyupekṣā | ^12. 155-6^ “pūṣā prapiṣṭhabhāgaḥ” ityatra tu nākṛte peṣaṇe pūṣā prapiṣṭhabhāgo bhavatītyarthāt peṣaṇakartavyatāvagamaḥ | tasmāttisraḥ svalu brahmaṇi pratipattaya ityatroktasya vividhasyāpi jñānasya vidhyasaṃbhavāt vidhiṃ vināpi vedāntavākyādarthapratīterapauruṣeyatvena vānapekṣatvādbhūte'pyarthe vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyasiddhirityayamanantarakāṇḍoktakṛtsnārthopasaṃhāraḥ | idaṃ tu sūtrakṛto'pi saṃmatam | “tattu samanvayāt” iti tuśabdena codanālakṣaṇāddharmād brahma pṛthak ; tattu, samanvayaḥ padānāmanyonyasaṃsargo viniyogavācyaḥ, tadgranthaṃ na codanāgamyamityuktam | nanu vedādhyayanānantaram “athāto dharmajijñāsā” iti dharmasyopalakṣakṣaṇatvātkṛtsna eva vedārtho jijñāsyatayā pratijñātaḥ | tena kṛtsna eva vedārthe codanālakṣaṇasūtreṇa codanā pramāṇatvenoktā | itthaṃ ca kṛtsne vedārthe codanā pramāṇaṃ bhavati, yadi mantrārthavādopaniṣadāṃ codanaikavākyatvenāpṛthagvedatvaṃ syāt ; nānyathā | evaṃ ca kṛtsna eva vedaśrodanā | sā ca kāryaikaviṣayetyaviśeṣeṇa kārya evārthe kṛtsno vedaḥ pramāṇamiti sūtrakṛtoktam | ataḥ sūtraviruddhamupaniṣadāṃ bhūte'rthe prāmāṇyam | naitatsāram | evaṃ hi codanāprāmāṇyādhīnā cet vedārthasya kāryātmatā, tataścodanāprāmāṇyāt prāgasiddhasya kāryātmano vedārthasyānuvādāsaṃbhavāt na codanāprāmāṇyānuvādenaiva vedārtho vicāryatayā pratijñeyaḥ | siddhaṃ cānūdyate | tatrānantaramautpattikasūtreṇa codanāprāmāṇyapratipādanaṃ na yujyeta | kiñcaiva santi prameyalakṣaṇaṃ syāt, na pramāṇalakṣaṇam | tatra dvitīyādhyāyādau “prathamādhyāye pramāṇalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttam” iti yaduktaṃ tadapi na yujyeta | kiñcatraiva yadi kṛtsnavedasya kāryārthatā nirṇītā, tato mantrārthavādayoḥ kāryārthatve vipratipattirupariṣṭhāstra syāt | sā copariṣṭhādvedārthavādādyadhikaraṇena nirasiṣyate | tasmādevamādidoṣaduṣṭatvādasya pakṣasya, dharmamālajijñāsaiva tatra pratijñātā ; tatraiva codanā pramāṇamuktam, na kṛtsnavedārtha ityalamatiprasaṅgena |
[p. 296]
kiṃca ananuvādatvamapauruṣeyāgamajatvenāduṣṭakāraṇajatvaṃ nirapekṣatvaṃ ca kārye prāmāṇyakāraṇatrayaṃ, tasmiddhe'pyarthe samānam | atastatrāpi prāmāṇyaṃ P.155-18 yuktamityāha—yatheti | anyeṣāṃ pratyakṣādimānānāṃ, vyatibhedaḥ saṃsparśaḥ, tena muktā matiḥ apauruṣeyāgamalabdhajanmā kārye'yaṃ yathā nirapekṣatvāt tprāmāṇyamupaiti, tathā siddhe'pyartha ityarthaḥ | tatrānyamānetyanenānanuvādatvamuktam ; apauruṣeyeti kāraṇadoṣarahitatvam ; nirapekṣabhāvāditi saṃvādakapramāṇānapekṣatvamiti vivekaḥ | iti śrībrahmasiddhivyākhyāne śaṅkhapāṇikṛte niyogakāṇḍavyākhyānaṃ samāptam ||
[p. 297]
brahmasiddhivyākhyā śaṅkhapāṇikṛtā | caturthaḥ (siddhi)kāṇḍaḥ | idānīmaprāmāṇyavādī codayati—nanviti | na tāvadbrahmādipadāni P. 156-4. brahmaṇi pramāṇam, teṣāṃ saṃbandhajñānāpekṣitvāt; pramāṇāntareṇa pūrvasiddhe'rthe jñānahetutvenāsiddhe brahmaṇi sāmarthyābhāvāt | na ca vākyam; na hi tatsākṣādvācakam, apitu guṇapradhānabhāvena parasparānvitapadārthadvāreṇa viśiṣṭārthabodhakam | anyathā hyapadārthajño'pi vākyārtha pratīyāt | na tvevam | ataśca padārthā eva parasparasaṃspṛṣṭā vākyārtha iti sthite brahmaṇo'padārthatvena na vākyagamyatvam | padārthatve tu padānāmadhigataviṣayatvādanya ekaḥ pramāṇāntaragocaro brahmapadārthaḥ syāt | ataśca āmrāyaikagamyatvapratijñā hīyetetyabhiprāyaḥ | na saṃsṛjyeranniti; saṃsargo vākyārtha iti matenoktam | sa ca padārthānāmanyonyamanvayaḥ | na bhidyeranniti; bhedo vākyārtha iti matenoktam | sa cātadbyāvṛttiḥ; yathā 'nīlamutpalam' iti anīlānutpalayoḥ | codanā ityapauruṣeyabaccanābhiprāyam | na hi codanātra vicāryatayā prastutā | parasya vā pūrvoktanyāyena kṛtsna eva vedaśrodanātmaka iti tadabhiprāyeṇedamuktam | apūrvapadārthāvagama iti pramāṇāntarāpūrvasya brahmapadārthasyāvagama ityarthaḥ | sā hītyādinā kārya evāpauruṣeyaṃ vacaḥ pramāṇamiti siddhamityuktaṃ bhavati | nanvaprāmāṇye niṣphalatvādvedāntānāṃ traividyaparigrahaḥ kathamityāśaṅkya, traividyetyādinā prayojanamuktam | evamapadārthatvāt brahma na vākyārtha iti codite pratibidhīyate— sāmānyeneti | yathā sāmānyena loke pakṣiṇaḥ pramāṇāntarādhigatasya saṃbandhajñānasaṃbhavātpadārthatve siddhe maratakapādatvādibhirananyapakṣisādhāraṇairguṇairapūrvasya pramāṇāntarānadhigatasya pakṣiviśeṣasya vākyena pratipādanā bhavati; tathā kāraṇamātrasya sanmātrasya vā sāmānyena kāraṇapadārthatve satpadārthatve vā lokasiddhe, asya jagatkāraṇaviśeṣasya sthūlādisakalaviśeṣarahitasya “yato
[p. 298]
vā imāni” “asthūlam” iti ca vākyadvayena lokasiddhapadārthakena pratipādanā śakyatyarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati—yathā sāmānyena lokasiddhasya pakṣipadārthasya marakatapadādibhirlokasiddhaireva padārthaiḥ saṃsargādapūrvapakṣiviśeṣa-pratipattiḥ, tathā lokasiddhasya kāraṇasāmānyasya “yato vā imāni bhūtāni” “asthūlam” ityādibhirlokasiddhaiḥ padārthaiḥ saṃsargādapūrvaṃ jagataḥ kāraṇamapetasamastaviśeṣaṃ pratīyate | ato nānavabodhakatvaṃ na cānuvādakatvaṃ vedāntānāmiti | P.167-10 parihārāntaramāha—sarveti | para evaikaḥ prakāśo'vidyākalpitena tena tena nīlādirūpeṇa sarveṣu pratyayeṣu vedyate bhāsate; vedya ityavivakṣitakarmaka-nirdeśaḥ; tathāhi—sarvatra nīlādiviśeṣe prakāśasāmānyamanugatamekamavabhāsate | na brahmaprakāśamāne prakāśe kiñcitprakāśate | tathāca “sa eṣa neti netyātmanaḥ” iti bhedopasaṃhāreṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ sāmānyarūpaṃ brahma satyaṃ pradarśitam | atha “vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttikatveva satyam” iti dṛṣṭāntāt sarvabhedānusyūtasya mṛttikāvatkāraṇamāvasya satyatvaṃ darśitam | tena mṛttikeva ghaṭādiṣvatyantaṃ prasiddhaṃ brahma | ataḥ padārthatvādvākyārthatvamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu sarvapratyayasiddhaṃ cedbrahma, kiṃ tarhi śabdaiḥ pratipādyata ityā-śaṅkyāha—prapañcasyeti | saprapañcaṃ tatsiddham, aprapañcaṃ tvasthūlādiśabdena pratipādyate | na cātrāpratītapadārthatvāt saṃsargāpratītiḥ, navarthasya sthūlā-deśya prāsaddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ pramāṇāntaragocaratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prāptam, ataḥ kathamātrāyaikagocaratvaṃ pratijñātamityāśaṅkyāha—pravilīneti | prapañca-rūpeṇa mānāntarātsiddham, aprapañcarūpeṇa na siddhamiti pramāṇāntaragocara-mucyata ityarthaḥ | paraḥ punaranyathā pratyavatiṣṭhate—nanviti | tatrocyate—pramāṇasyeti | upekṣāpi pramāṇaphalamiṣṭam | na ca puruṣārthavattā pramāṇalakṣaṇam, apitu paricchedakatvam; taccātrāpyastīti bhāvaḥ | etacca parāskandanamātram; tacca-tastu nādhyayanavidhiviṣayasya tulyasāṃpradāyikasyopekṣāphalatvamucitam | na japārthatādhyayanavidheḥ, arthavibodhārthatvādityaparitoṣātparihārāntaramāha—api-ceti | nanu jñānādanyatpuruṣārthātmakaṃ phalaṃ dṛśyate, yatra jñānaṃ svayam-
[p. 299]
puruṣārthaḥ | idaṃ tu nirdoṣasvabhāvātmataccajñānamajñānotthaśokādyaniṣṭhanivartakatvādiṣṭasyānandasyāvirbhāvācca svayaṃ puruṣārtho matam | ato nāpuruṣārthatvam, na ca puruṣārthāntarāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ | viruddhabhāvotpattireva pūrvanivṛttiḥ | ato jñānamevājñānotthaśokādinivṛttirnanyeti tannivṛttirjñānamiti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśaḥ | na ca vācyam—bādhitamapi śokitvādimithyājñānamavidyāsaṃskārādanuvartate, tatastasya nivṛttireva nāsti ; kathaṃ sā āśāsyātmataccajñānasya phalamiti ; yato yadyapi tadanuvartate, tathāpi yathā satyatvenābhimanyamānaṃ tadavidyāvantaṃ puruṣamabhiniveśayadviṣṭāvayati, naivamutpannajñānadṛḍhīkṛtaśokitvādijñānamithyātvaṃ śāṭhyamātmano'śokitvādijñānamanusaṃdadhānam | na hi māyāpuruṣārthāntarāpekṣeti yathā puruṣamabhiniveśayati, tathā bādhitam | na ca śokitvādimithyājñānaṃ saṃskārādanuvartamānaṃ sanna nivartata eva, apitu krameṇa nivartate | bhavati ca vyavahitamapi phalam, yathā svargādi | dṛṣṭaśca jñānotpatteḥ puruṣārthatvamityāha—kasminniti | P. 158-8. yathāścaryaviśeṣamatiśayena didṛkṣoḥ kasyacitkṛtūhalinaḥ taddarśanākulitasya duḥkhinastadarśanameva taduḥkhāpanodi puruṣārthaḥ, tathetyarthaḥ | kiṃca yadi pravṛttinivṛttiviṣayasyaiva puruṣārthateti niyamaḥ syāt, tato bhūtārthaniṣṭhānāṃ vedāntānāmapuruṣārthatā syāt ; na caivamasti, pratyakṣādip vyabhicārādityāha— akṣādāviti | pravṛttigrahaṇamupalabdhān nivṛttirapi draṣṭavyā | atha pratyakṣādijñeyaṃ salilādivastu tatra puruṣārthaḥ ; tena hi puruṣasya snānapānādyupakāraḥ kriyate ; ataḥ pratyakṣādīnāṃ siddhārthaniṣṭhānāmapi svaprameyadvāreṇa puruṣārthatetyucyate, tatrāpi vedāntajanyataccajñānena jñeyaṃ brahma puruṣajarādiduḥkhopaśamānandātmakatvācca suprasannaṃ svayameva puruṣārthaḥ | salilādeḥ puruṣārthahetorviśeṣapradarśanārthaṃ svayamityuktam, yat eva ca brahma svayaṃ puruṣārtho labdho bhavatīti śvetaketumukhenānuśāsanīyāya śiṣyāya “tasvamasi” ityabhinnaṃ brahma śiṣyane upadiśyate | tathā coktam—“ātmalābhānna paraṃ vidyate” iti | abhinnaṃ hi ityādivṛtterayamarthaḥ—taddhiśiṣyāt bhinnam, abhinnamapyavidyayā bhedena pratīyamānam, ubhayathāpi na puruṣārthaccam | abhede tu yathoktasya brahmaṇaḥ ‘tadevāham’ iti śiṣyasya tādātmyajñāne ca, śiṣyasya tathābhāvān yathoktabrahmabhāvān sarveṣāmādhyā-
[p. 300]
P.159-9. tmikādiduḥkhātmakānāmaśivānāmakalyāṇānāmupaśamāt , paramasya cānandākhyaśibasya ca bhāvāt puruṣārthaṃ āso bhavatīti | duḥkhopaśamasukhe pratyekamasaṃpūrṇāvanyatra puruṣārthau ; iha tūbhayamapi ; ataḥ pūrṇaṃ ityuktam | parasyāmuttarakālikyāṃ hānopādānādipravṛttāvaṅgatvātpratyakṣādīnāṃ prāmāṇyamiti cet , evaṃ tarhi śabdātpramite brahmaṇi tatsākṣātkaraṇāyopāsanādipravṛttervedāntajjñānasya tadaṅgatve iṣyatāmapuruṣārthānāṃ vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyamiti bhāvaḥ | itiḥ granthakaraṇaparisamāptau || iti śrībrahmasiddhivyākhyāne śaṅkhapāṇikṛte caturthaṃ(siddhi)kāṇḍavyākhyānaṃ samāptam || || brahmasiddhivyākhyānaṃ samāptam ||
